# Vidhiviveka of Maṇḍanamiśra with the Nyāyakaṇikā of Vācaspatimiśra
# Source: SARIT, vidhiviveka-and-nyayakanika.xml (TEI; data entry Aurorachana, Auroville; prepared by Liudmila Olalde; SARIT/DFG-NEH 2013-2017), CC BY-SA 4.0.
# Edition: Vidhiviveka of Śrī Maṇḍana Miśra with the Commentary Nyāyakaṇikā of Vāchaspati Miśra, ed. Mahaprabhu Lal Goswami (Varanasi). Page markers [p.N] = Goswami's pages.
# Converted Devanāgarī → IAST with sanscript on 2026-09-23 (Kengo Harimoto / Claude); XML notes dropped; the Vidhiviveka mūla is embedded in the commentary as in the source. Avagraha appears as an apostrophe.
#

[p.1]
## atha vidhivivekaḥ
śrīgaṇeśāya namaḥ
sādhane puruṣārthasya saṅgirante trayīvidaḥ |
bodhaṃ vidhau samāyattamataḥ sa pravivicyate ||
atha nyāyakaṇikā
śrīsarasvatyai namaḥ
parāmṛṣṭaḥ kleśaiḥ kathamapi na yo jātu bhagavān
na dharmā'dharmābhyāṃ tribhirapi vipākairna ca tayoḥ |
paraṃ vācāṃ tattvaṃ yamadhigamayatyomiti padaṃ
namasyāmo viṣṇuṃ tamamaragurūṇāmapi gurum || 1 ||
bhuvanabhavanasthemadhvaṃsaprabandhavidhāyine
bhavabhayabhide tubhyaṃ bhetre purāṃ tisṛṇāmapi |
kṣitihutavahakṣetrajñā'mbhaḥprabhañjanacandrama-
stapanaviyadityaṣṭau mūrtīrnamo bhava vibhrate || 2 ||
ajñānatimiraśamanīṃ paradamanīṃ nyāyamañjarīṃ rucirām |
prasavitre prabhavitre vidyātarave namo gurave || 3 ||
ācāryakṛtiniveśanamapyavadhūtaṃ vaco'smadādīnām |
rathyodakamiva gaṅgāpravāhapātaḥ pavitrayati || 4 ||
prakaraṇā''rambhe prekṣāvatpravṛttyaṅgaṃ viṣayaprayojanahetukaṃ prakaraṇasya
vidhiviṣayavivecanaṃ prayojanamāha—
sādhane puruṣārthasya saṅgirante trayīvidaḥ |
bodhaṃ vidhau samāyattamataḥ sa pravivicyate ||
iha kecidāhuḥ | yadaprayojanaviṣayaṃ na tatprekṣāvatpravṛttigocaraḥ | yathā
kākadantaparīkṣā | tathā caitanmīmāsāprakaraṇamiti vyāpakaviruddhopalabdhiḥ |
[p.2]
na ceyamasiddhā | prayojanaṃ khalviha vidhervedaprāmāṇyasiddhirvā, padārthasaṃsarga
pratītirvā, hitā'hitasādhanatā'vagamo vā pravṛttinivṛttinimittam | na tāva
tprāmāṇyasiddhirantareṇā'pi vidhiṃ tatsambhavāt |
pūrvapakṣaḥ—syādetat | vidhivirahe kāryaśūnyatayā bhūtā'rthā'vagāhino
bhūtasya ca mānāntaraviṣayatayā tadapekṣatvādvedasya prāmāṇyaṃ vihanyeta, puruṣa
vākyavat | tadanupapannam | atredaṃ tu bhavān pṛṣṭo vyācaṣṭām yadetatpuruṣa
vacasāṃ mānāntarā'pekṣatvam, tatkiṃ bhūtārthatayā, āhosvit puruṣabuddhiprabhava
tayā ? na tāvadbhatārthatayā | pratyakṣādīnāmaprāmāṇyaprasakteḥ | tānyapi hi
bhūtārthānyeva | caityavandanādivākyasya ca kāryā'rthā'bhidhāyino nirapekṣatayā
prāmāṇyaprasaṅgāt | viniyogamātraparametanna kāryaparamiti ca svatantrasiddhānta
śraddhāmātravijṛmbhitaṃ na prāmāṇikamiti nivedayiṣyate | tasmādvaktṛdhī
prabhavatvameva sāpekṣatve hetuḥ | tacca bhūtārthe'pi vaidike nāstīti nāsti
sāpekṣatā | sā ca kāryaniṣṭhānāmapi puruṣavacasām | tanna, prāmāṇyasiddhirartho
vidheḥ |
nā'pi padārthasaṃsargapratītiḥ | vinā'pi vidhinā sthālyāmodanaṃ pacati
caitra iti padārthasaṃsargapratīterupapatteḥ | yadi manyeta vivakṣā'dhīnaracanāni puṃso
vacanāni, vivakṣā ca mānāntarā'vadhāraṇā'dhīnā, mānāntaraṃ ca padārthasaṃsarga
gocaraṃ na padārthamātragocaramiti tanmānāntaraṃ prabhavatayā puṃsyānyapi
padārthasaṃsargameva gocarayanti | na tvevaṃ vedavākyānyapauruṣeyāṇīti padārthasaṃsarga
siddhyartha vidhireṣitavya iti | tadayuktam | vidhāvapi tadanupapatteḥ | tasmin
vidhau sati tadvidhikāṅkṣānibandhanaḥ sambandhaḥ padārthānāmiti cet ? tadā
kāṅkṣaiva kutaḥ tadaparyavasānāditi cet ? na | kriyādiṣvapi sāmyāt |
yathā hi śabdā'bhihito vidhirviṣayaniyojyādibhirvinā na paryavasyati
tathā kriyādayo'pi padārthāḥ kārakādibhirvineti ta eva padairupāttā ākā
ṅkṣāyogyatāsannidhisadhrīcīnāśca saṃsarga gamayiṣyantīti kṛtaṃ vidhinā |
viśiṣṭā'rthapratītiprayuktatvena ca padānāṃ samabhivyāhārasya loke vidita
lokatadupāyatvācca vaidikā'rthapratīteḥ |
nā'pi hitā'hitasādhanatā'vagamastasya prayojanaṃ sambhavati |
santāpamapanayati ca taptasya salilā'vasekaḥ, dahati ca jvalanajvālākalā
pā''liṅganamiti vartamānā'padeśādapi hitā'hitasādhanatā'vagamāt |
tataścepsājihāsābhyāṃ pramāṇāntarādiva pravṛttinivṛttyorupapattervyartho 
[p.3]
vidhiriti siddhā vyāpakaviruddhopalabdhiḥ , ato nā''rambhaṇīyametatprakaraṇa
miti | tān pratyāha—sādhane puruṣārthasya saṅgirante trayīvidaḥ | bodhaṃ vidhau
samāyattamiti | mā bhūvannanyāni prayojanāni vidheḥ | puruṣārthasādhanatā
'vabodhastu vidhinibandhanaḥ | na hi 
svargakāmo yajete-ti vidhau asati svargo
bhāvanāyā bhāvyaḥ sambhavati | sā hi bhāvyamātrā'pekṣiṇī | bhavitā ca
bhāvyo yāgādayaśca bhavitāra iti samānapadopādānatayā prakṛtyarthatayā ca
tadanuraktāyā eva bhāvanāyāḥ pratīteḥ svargasya ca padāntaropādānatayā
puruṣaviśeṣaṇatayā ca vyavasthānāttamapahāya yāgādīneva bhāvyatayā bhāvanā
gṛhṇīyāt | kartrapekṣitopāyatārūpavidhyupahitamaryādā punariyaṃ bhāvanā samāna
padopādānamapi prathamā'vagatamapi duḥkhākaratayā kartranapekṣitamapahāya
dhātvarthamasamānapadopādānamapi puruṣaviśeṣaṇamapyapekṣitatayā svargameva
bhāvyamavalambate | samāsāditatayāvidhabhāvyā ca yāgādīn karaṇatayā
svīkarotīti puruṣārthasādhanatā teṣāṃ siddhā bhavati | kathaṃ tarhi vartamānā'pa
deśebhyo loke puruṣārthasādhanatā'vagatiḥ ? pramāṇāntaravaśānna śabdasāmarthyāt |
yathā caitattathā dravyasaṃskārakarmasviti cā'sādhakaṃ tu tādarthyāditi ca
vyācakṣāṇa upapādayiṣyati svayamevā''cāryaḥ |
nanveṣa vidhiḥ śabdātprasiddho vā syādaprasiddho vā ? sa cetprasiddho,
nā'nena prakaraṇena pratipādanīyaḥ | śabdapratipannasyā'pratipitsitatvāt |
niṣpāditakriye karmaṇi sādhanasya tatrā'nupapatteḥ | aprasiddhaścennitarāṃ vedārtha
tayā pratipādyaḥ | tasmādanarthakametadviṣayaṃ prakaraṇamiti | ata āha—ataḥ sa
pravivicyate iti | satyaṃ śabdā'vagamyo'pi vidhirvipratipatteḥ sādhakabādhaka
pramāṇavirahe sati saṃśayamāpannaḥ san pratipitsita upapattyanupapattibhyāṃ
parīkṣya śaṅkitasamāropitarūpavyavacchedena yathāvatpratipādyate | taducyate —
vivicyate iti |
nanvasya viveko'pi vārtikakṛtprabhṛtibhirakārīti so'pi vyartha
evetyata āha—preti | kṛto'pi tairviveko'kṛtakalpa eva | abhidhābhāvanā
māhu rityādisaṅkīrṇaśabdaprayogāt | iha tu karturiṣṭā'bhyupāya iti
[p.4]
sa khalu śabdabhedo vā, tadvyāpārā'tiśayo vā'rthabhedo vā ? yada
bhidhānācchabdo'pi tathā vyapadeśyaḥ |
pramāṇatvādaniyamātpravṛtteḥ saṃvidāśrayāt |
samabhivyāhṛteḥ śabdo na vidhiḥ kāryakalpanāt ||
pramāṇaṃ hi śabdaḥ pratijñāyate | bodhakaṃ ca pramāṇam | tatra pravṛttihetuṃ
kañcanā'rthātiśayamavagamayan śabdaścodanātvena pramāṇatāmaśnute | svayameva
tu pravṛtteḥ kārakastāṃ pramāṇatāmapajahyāt | na hi kārako hetuḥ pramāṇam |
api tu jñāpakaḥ |
niṣkṛṣyā'bhidhānāt tasya ca vyutpādanātprakarṣopapatterarthavadetatprakaraṇamiti |
tatra prathamaṃ tāvadvipratipattinimittaṃ vimarśamāha—sa khalu śabdabhedo vā |
liṅādiḥ śabdāntarādbhidyate iti bhedaḥ | tasya khalu liṅāderayaskānta
maṇeriva vastusvabhāvajo'tiśayo yena lohamiva cetanaṃ pravartayati ?,
tadvyāpārā'tiśayo vā, pravṛttiheturupeyate arthabhedo vā vakṣyamāṇabhedaḥ | ?
yadyarthabhedaḥ, kathaṃ tarhi codaneti kriyāyāḥ pravartakaṃ vacanamiti vidhinā tveka
vākyatvāditi cetyata āha—yadabhidhānācchabdo'pi tathā vyapadeśyaḥ |
tatra śabdasvarūpasya tāvadvidhibhāvaṃ niṣedhati—
prāmāṇatvādaniyamāt pravṛtteḥ saṃvidāśrayāt |
samabhivyāhṛteḥ śabdo na vidhiḥ kāryakalpanāt ||
kārikāṃ vyācaṣṭe—pramāṇaṃ hi śabdaḥ pratijñāyate codanālakṣaṇo'rtho
dharma iti | bodhakaṃ ca pramāṇam | abādhitā'nadhigatā'sandigdhā'rthapramājanakam |
tatra ca pravṛttihetuṃ kañcanā'rthātiśayamavagamayan śabdaścodanātvena pramāṇatā
maśnute śabdasvarūpavidhivādināṃ mate | svayameva tu pravṛtterapramāyāḥ kārakastāṃ
pramāṇatāmapajahyāt | nanvapramāyā api pravṛtteḥ kārakaḥ kasmānna pramāṇamata
āha—na hi kārako hetuḥ pramāṇam | mā bhūd vījādīnāmaṅkurādikārakāṇāṃ
prāmāṇyam | kiṃ tarhi pramāṇakamityata āha—api tu jñāpakaḥ | indriyādau
tathābhāvāt |
nanu mā nāmavādītpravṛttihetuṃ kañcanā'rthātiśayam, astu śabdaḥ
svayameva pravṛttihetuḥ | tathāpi yatra pravṛttiranena janayitavyā tadviṣayaḥ puruṣasya
[p.5]
viṣayā'vabodhanānna doṣa iti cet ? na | tanmātrasyā'nyatrā'pi
tulyatvāt | codanālakṣaṇo'rtho dharma ityabhyupagamā'narthakyāt | śabdasvā
tantrye ca niyogato'vaśyaṃ pravṛttiḥ syāt | tathā cā'kurvan vihitaṃ karmeti
nirviṣayaṃ syāt | na hi tadānīṃ balavadanilasalilaughanudyamānasyevecchā'pi
tantraṃ pravṛttiṃ prati puruṣasya | jñāpakaśca svarūpakarmasambandhaviṣayajñānama
pekṣate | liṅādisvarūpaṃ ca pravṛtteḥ kārakamityanupayuktasvarūpatatkarmasambandha
viṣayasaṃvido 'pi puṃsaḥ pravṛttiprasaṅgaḥ |
vyāpāro bodhyate iti kārakahetorapi viṣaye pramājanakatvātpramāṇatopa
patsyate | aṅkurasyeva pacādihetoḥ svākāraṇaṃ pramāṣayata iti śaṅkate—
viṣayā'vabodhanānna doṣa iti cet ? | nirākaroti—neti | kutaḥ ? tanmā
trasyā'nyatrāpi vartamānāpadeśe'pi caitraḥ pacatītyādau tulyatvāt | na hi
tatra bhāvanā yatra nā'bagamyate | astu tulyatā, kā no hānirityata āha—
codanālakṣaṇo'rtho dharma ityabhyupagamā''narthakyāt | pravartakatvaṃ codanātvaṃ
pravṛttihetuṃ kañcanā'rthā'tiśayamavagamayannanena rūpeṇa prāmāṇyamaśnute, na bhāvanā
mātravacanatvena | tasyā'nyatrā'pi tulyatvāt | tasmādyena rūpeṇa prāmāṇyaṃ
na tena codanā, yena codanā na tena prāmāṇyam, tasya pravṛttiṃ prati kārakatvā
dityarthaḥ | pramāṇatvāditi hetuṃ vyākhyāya aniyamāt pravṛtteriti vyācaṣṭe,
śabdasvātantrye ca puṃsaḥ pravṛttau niyogato'vaśyaṃ pravṛttiḥ syāt | bhavatu, ko
doṣa ? ityata āha—tathā cā'kurvan vihitaṃ karmeti dharmasūtrakāravacanaṃ
nirviṣayaṃ syāt |
nanvicchādhīnapravṛttitvātpuṃsāṃ tasyāśca kādācitkatvāt na vihite
'pyavaśyambhāvaniyamaḥ pravṛtteriti sambhavati vihitākaraṇena nirviṣayatā
vacanasya, ityata āha—na hi tadānīṃ balavadanilasalilaudhanudya
mānasyevecchā'pi tantraṃ pravṛtti prati puruṣasya | saṃvidāśrayāditi vyācaṣṭe—
jñāpakaśca svarūpatatkarmasambandhaviṣayajñānamapekṣate, na tu kārakaḥ | liṅādi
svarūpaṃ ca pravṛtteḥ kārakamityanupayuktasvarūpatatkarmasambandhaviṣayasaṃvido'pi
puṃsaḥ pravṛttiprasaṅgaḥ | etadukta bhavati, svakārye saṃvidāśrayatvaṃ jñāpakatvena
[p.6]
nanu śaṅkhadhvanivadetat syāt ? yathā hi śaṅkhaśabdāt pravarti
tavyamityupayuktasvarūpasambandhasaṃvida eva pravartante, netare | na ca śaṅkhaśabdaḥ
pravṛtterabhidhāyako'nyasya vā kasya citpravṛttihetoḥ, yena jñāpakaḥ syāt |
vārtametat | na hi śaṅkhadhvaniḥ pravṛttyupayuktasaṅgatitayā pravṛttikāraṇaṃ
śaṅkhaśabdāt pravartitavyamiti, api tu pravartitavyamiti | prakṛtyarthā'ti
rikte pratyayārthe ca vidheyasya puṃso'bhiprāyabhede kṛtasaṅketastamanusmārayati |
vyāptam, dhūmādau tathā darśanād vījādau cā'nupalabdheḥ | tadiha tadviruddhakāra
katvopalabdhirūpā'nupalabdhireva saṃvidāśrayatvaṃ nitrartayediti |
codayati nanu śaṅkhadhvanivadetat syāt ? | tadeva sphuṭayati—yathā hi
śaṅkhaśabdāt pravartitavyamiti pravṛtyupayukta svarūpasambandhasaṃvida eva pravartante
netare | na cā'sya tatra jñāpakatvam, anabhidhānāt | api tu kārakatva
mevetyāha—na ca śaṅkhaśabdaḥ pravṛtterabhidhāyako'nyasya vā kasyacitpravṛtti
hetoḥ, yena jñāpakaḥ syāt | anabhidhāyako'pi śaṅkhadhvanirna pravṛttau kārako
'pi tu pravṛttihetumabhiprāyaviśeṣamanuvidheyasyā'vagayaman jñāpaka eveti na
vyabhicāra iti | pariharati—vārtametat | na hi śaṅkhadhvaniḥ pravṛttyupayukta
saṅgatitayā pravṛteḥ kāraṇam | kutaḥ ? śaṅkhaśabdāt pravartante ityasmādapi
svakārya pravṛttiṃ prati kāraṇatvamasyā'vagamyate | hetau pañcamīvidhānāt |
tataścā'nena prakāreṇa viditasvakāryaśabdācchaṅkhadhvanerevā'bhiprāyaviśeṣā
'numānasiddhāt pravṛttiprasaṅgāt | kathaṃ tataḥ pravṛttirityata āha—
api tu pravartitavyamiti prakṛtyarthā'tirikte pratyayārthe'nuvidheyasyā'bhiprāya
bhede ājñādau kṛtasambandhaḥ kṛtasaṅketastamanusmārayati | tamabhiprāyaviśeṣa
mupalakṣaṇīkṛtyā'numātumuddiśyeti yāvat | smārayati sambandham | tataḥ
kṛtasambandhasmaraṇasahakāriṇaḥ śaṅkhadhvanerevā'bhiprāyaviśeṣā'numānasiddhiḥ |
kvacitpāṭhaḥ | kṛtaṃ sambandhamanusmārayati tāvat | atha tatsmaraṇa
sahakārī sannabhiprāyabhedamanumāpayatīti draṣṭavyam | etena śaṅkhadhvanerjñāpaka
tvād vyabhicāra iti darśitam | bhavatvevaṃ, pravṛttistu kuta ityata āha—
tato 'bhiprāyabhedādanumitāttadanuvidhāyinaḥ pravartante |
[p.7]
tatastadanuvidhāyinaḥ pravartante | aviditaviṣayatayā nā'nadhigatasamayāḥ
pravartante ityapi mithyā | tanmātrasaṅgatijñasyā'pi tatprasaṅgāt | apratyaya
pūrvāyāṃ ca śabdaparatantrasya pravṛttau viṣayavedanā'nupayogāt | prabalapavane
ritasyevā'ndhapaṅgvāderna vedanaṃ pravṛttyaṅgam | kārako api prāṇistho
'hidaṃśo jñānamapekṣate | naivam | jñānasyaiva tatra kārakatvāt | vinā'pyahidaṃśaṃ
śaṅkāviṣeṇā'pi maraṇadarśanāt |
yo'pyāha—abhidheyavijñānaṃ prati kārakaheturapi śabdo yathā
śaktijñānamapekṣate tathā pravṛttiśaktisaṃvidamapīti | tenā'pi na jñāpakādanyatra
jñānā'pekṣā pradarśyate | jñānakarakasyaiva jñāpaka ityākhyānāt |
nanu bhavatu pravṛtteḥ kārako yatra tvanena pravartayitavyaḥ puruṣaḥ sa
viṣayo'jñāto na pravṛttiviṣayo bhavatīti tatra liṅādirjñāpako 'bhyupagantavyaḥ |
tathā cā'sya saṃvidāśrayatvamupapannam | pravṛttikārakasyā'pi pravṛttiviṣaye
prāmāṇyādityata āha—aviditaviṣayatayā nā'nadhigatasamayāḥ pumāṃso
liṅādibhyaḥ pravṛttikārakebhyo'pi pravartante ityapi mithyā | kutaḥ ?
tadviṣayamātrasaṅgatijñasyā'pi tatpravṛttiprasaṅgāt | dūṣaṇāntaramāha—apratya
yapūrvāyāñca śabdaparatantrasya pravṛtau viṣayavedanā'nupayogāt | kasmādupayogo
nāstītyata āha—prabalapavaneritasyeva nā'ndhapaṅgvādervedanaṃ pravṛttyaṅgam |
codayati—kārako'pi prāṇistho 'hidaṃśo jñānamapekṣate iti vyabhicāraḥ |
pariharati—naivam | jñānasyaiva tatra kāraṇatvāt | kutaḥ ? vinā'pyahidaṃśaṃ
śaṅkāviṣeṇā 'pi maraṇadarśanāt | daṃśasyaiva maraṇakāraṇatve'sati tasmin
na śaṅkāviṣeṇa preyāt | na jātu bījavijñānamaṅkuramasati bīje janayati |
bālānāmapi prāgbhavīyabhāvanāprabodhādahidaṃśavijñāne sati bhayasambhavānma
raṇamiti mantavyam |
yo'pyāha—abhidheyaviśeṣavijñānaṃ prati kārakaheturapi śabdo yathā
śaktijñānamapekṣate tathā pravṛttiśaktisaṃvidamapīti | tenā'pi na jñāpakā
danyatra jñānā'pekṣitā pradarśyate | jñānakārakasyaiva jñāpaka iti ākhyānā
diti na sapakṣeṇa vyabhicāra iti bhāvaḥ | sarvasyaiva jñāpakasya kāryajanakatā
svarūpatacchaktisaṃvidāśrayabhāvitokte ti matvā bhrāntaścodayati | nanu
[p.8]
nanu jñānakārako'pi cakṣurādirna śaktijñānamapekṣate | kimataḥ ?
śabdasyā'kārakatvaprasaṃgaḥ | ayamaparo doṣaḥ | kutastarhyarthabodhaḥ ?
abhyāsāt kaśā'ṅkuśavat | uktañca—
abhyāsātpratibhāhetuḥ sarvaḥ śabdaḥ samāsataḥ |
bālānāṃ ca tiraścāṃ ca yathā'rthapratipādane ||
jñānakārako'pi cakṣurādirna śaktijñānamapekṣate | samādhātuṃ nigūḍhā'bhi
sandhirāha—kimataḥ ? āntare patrārśvasthaḥ prasaṅgamāpādayati—jñānaṃ prati 
śabdasyā'kārakatvaprasaṅgaḥ | jñānakārakacakṣurādivaidharmyāt | atrā'nantara
tayā pārśvasthaṃ prati tāvaduttaramāha—ayamaparo doṣaḥ | na vayaṃ mīmāṃsakāḥ,
yena śabdārthayoḥ svābhāvikaṃ sambandhamācakṣmahe iti bhāvaḥ | nanu yadyavācakaḥ
śabdaḥ, kutastarhyarthabodhaḥ ? na ca nāstīti vaktavyaṃ, vacanena bhavato 
'smatpratipādanāyā'pravṛttiprasaṅgāditi bhāvaḥ | uttaram—adhyāsāt
kaśāṅkuśā'bhighātavat | nā'bhidhānā'dhīno'rthabodhaḥ śabdādapi tva
bhyāsā'dhīnaḥ | yathā hi khalu kaśā'ṅkuśā'bhighātādayo'nabhighāyakā api
vājigajādīnāmabhyāsāt svataḥ pravṛttinivṛttihetumarthapratibhāsāṃ pratibhāṃ
bhāvayantaḥ pravartakā nivartakāśca tathā saṃkṣepataḥ sarvaḥ śabdo vyutpannānā
mavyutpannānāṃ ceha janayatītyarthaḥ | uktañca—
abhyāsātpratibhāhetuḥ sarvaḥ śabdaḥ samāsataḥ |
 vyutpannānāṃ punararthābhidhānataḥ | abhyāsajanitāṃ bhāvanāma
bhyāsaśabdenāha | kārye kāraṇopacārāt | ayaṃ ca prāgbhavīyo'bhyā
so'vaśyā'bhyupeya ityatra dṛṣṭāntamāha—
bālānāṃ ca tiraścāṃ ca yathā'rthapratipādane |
na hi teṣāṃ prāgbhavīyabhāvanāmantareṇa pravṛttinivṛttihetubhūtā'rthaprati
bhāsa pratibhotpādanasambhavaḥ | arthā''bhāsapratibhotpādanena cā'rthaprati
pādanamuktam | etacca siddhānte vistareṇa vakṣyate |
[p.9]
anāgamaśca so'bhyāsaḥ samayaḥ kaiścidiṣyate | iti |
na ca brūmaḥ sarvo jñāpako jñānā'pekṣaḥ, kintu sa eva, netaraḥ |
bījādiṣvadarśanāt |
na ca dṛṣṭaviparītasvabhāvakalpanā liṅādīnām anupapattyā |
tādṛśā'nusāreṇā'pyupapatteḥ | pratyayaśca sahā'bhidhānātprakṛtyā, tadanuvādādvā,
anāgamaśca so'bhyāsaḥ
āgamyate prāpyate'smāditi kāraṇamāgamaḥ | avidyamāna āgamo
yasminnasāvanāgamo'nādirityartha |
samayaḥ kaiścidiṣyate |
samaya ityādimattāmāha | naiyāyikādayo hīśvaranirmāṇaṃ śabdārtha
sambandhaṃ samaya ityācakṣate | sa cā'bhyāsasya bhāvanāyā viṣaya ityabhyāsa
uktaḥ | evaṃ pārśvasyaṃ nirākṛtya prakṛtaṃ codakaṃ pratyuttaramāha—na ca brūmaḥ,
sarvo jñāpako jñānā'pekṣaḥ kintu sa eva jñāpaka eva, netaraḥ kārakaḥ |
kutaḥ ? bījādiṣvaṅkurādikārakeṣvadarśanāt jñānā'pekṣā'vadhāryate sa tu
jñāpakaḥ etena nigūḍho'bhisandhirudghāṭitaḥ |
nanu jñāpakatve samāne'pi kecidanāśritasaṃvido jñānaṃ janayanti, yathā
cakṣurādayaḥ | kecitpunarāśritasaṃvidaḥ , yathā dhūmādayo dhūmadhvajādivijñānam |
evaṃ yadyapyanye kārakāḥ hetavaḥ svakāryajananaṃ pratyanāśritasaṃvidastathā'pi
śabdapravṛttikārako'pi saṃvidamāśrayiṣyate anyathā pravṛttyanupapattirityata
āha—na ca dṛṣṭaviparītasvabhāvakalpanā liṅādīnāmanyathā'nupapattyā |
kutaḥ ? pravṛttihetubhūtā'rthajñāpakatayā dṛṣṭā'nusāreṇā'pyupapatteḥ | prakṛti
pratyayayoḥ sahā'bhidhānam | tathā ca pratyayaśca sahā'bhidhānātprakṛtyā saha
samabhivyāhāramarhati nā'nyathā | sahā'bhidhānaprakāraṃ darśayati—tadanuvādādvā
[p.10]
taddyotanādvā, tatsambandhyarthāntarā'bhidhānādvā tayā samabhivyāhāramarhati |
avācakasya tu pravṛtteḥ kārakasya sa na yuktaḥ | saṃkhyādyabhidheyāntaranibandhanaḥ
sa iti cet ?
astu | pravṛttestu pratyayenā'pratipādyamānāyāḥ kathaṃ pravatyabhisambandhaḥ ?
abhidhayaiva pratyayasya prakṛtyarthā'nuraktā pravṛttiriti cet ? na tāvadbhāvanā
kāryarūpā liṅādyabhidheyeti śabdavidhivādibhiḥ śakyaṃ vaktum | tasyāṃ tarhi
tādṛśaliṅādeḥ kārakatvena saṅgatiḥ | samabhivyāhṛtistu tasyā | vācyaṃ
prakṛtyarthā'nuvādāt | yathaiko dvau bahavaḥ iti | taddyotanādvā prakṛtyarthadyotanādvā |
yathā kumārīti īpratyayaḥ strītvaṃ dyotayati | tatsambandhyarthāntarābhidhānāt |
yathā pacati pācako daṇḍīti tivake nayaḥ prakṛtyarthaviśiṣṭabhāvanāḥ
kartṛsambandhārthāḥ | astu, prastute kimāyātamityata āha—avācakasya tu
liṅādeḥ pravṛtteḥ kārakasya na samabhivyāhāro yuktaḥ | sahā'bhidhānā
'sambhavāt | śaṅkate—abhidheyāntaranibandhanaḥ sa iti cet ? | na hi
vayamanabhidheyatāṃ sarvathaiva liṅādīnāṃ brūmaḥ, yena sahā'bhidhānā
sambhavāt prakṛtyā samabhivyāhṛtirna syāt | kintu kartṛsaṃkhyādayo
hi tasyā'bhidheyāḥ | te ca kathañcitkṛtyā sambaddhā iti
samabhivyāhāropapattirityarthaḥ | nirākaroti—astu samabhivyāhāramātram,
viśeṣastu na sidhyatītyāha—pravṛttestu pratyayenā'pratipādyamānāyāḥ kāryāyāḥ
kathaṃ prakṛtyarthasambandhaḥ ? na khalu samabhivyāhāramātraṃ liṅādīnāṃ prakṛtyā
sahā'vagamyate, api tu kartavyatopahitasīmānaṃ pravṛttibhāvanāṃ prakṛtyartha
viśiṣṭāmabhidadhatām | na ca kartṛsaṃkhyādyabhidhāne satya pyayaṃ viśeṣaḥ seddhu
marhatītyarthaḥ | śaṅkate—abhidheyaiva pratyayasya prakṛtyarthā'nuraktā pravṛttirbhāvanā |
tena samabhivyāhāraviśeṣa upapanna iti cet ? nirākaroti—na tāvadbhāvanā
kāryarūpā liṅādyabhidheyeti śabdasvarūpavidhivādibhiḥ śakyaṃ vaktum |
kartavyatā ''kārāyā eva bhāvanāyāḥ pravṛttihetutayā vidhitvena śabdasvarūpasya
tattvā'nupapatteḥ | tasmātpravṛttimātraṃ liṅādyartho na punaḥ kartavyatārūpeti
tairabhyupagantavyam | tathā ca tasyāṃ tarhi pravṛttau tādṛśyāṃ kartavyatā''kārāyāṃ
liṅādeḥ kārakatvena saṅgatirna punarabhidhāyakatveneti prakṛtyarthā'nuraktakārya
[p.11]
lakṣyaṃ vā tathāvidhena sambandhamupāśnute | apratītayorghaṭanā'yogāt | kāryāyāṃ
tu pravṛttau na niyamanimittaṃ samasti anabhidheyasya pravṛttihetoḥ | api
cā'śrutaphaleṣu phalā'dhyāhāraḥ, kvacit kratūpakārakalpanā, śrutānāmapi
svargādīnāṃ phalatvā'dhyavasāya iti sarva eṣa mahimā vidheḥ | sa śabdasya
tadbhāve'nupapannaḥ | na hi tadā śabdādanavagamyamānamanyathā gamyate | na hi
liṅādisvarūpasyaivā'nyathā'nupapattiḥ | pravartakasyeti cet ? na | tasyā'pi
pavanādivartina ivopapatteḥ phalarūpaṃ kārakaṃ vinā | tasmānna vidhiḥ śabda
stadvyāpāro vā |
rūpapravṛttyabhidhāyakatvena rūpeṇa | viśiṣṭasamabhivyāhṛtistu kutaḥ sā
kimityahetuketyata āha—vācyaṃ lakṣyaṃ vā tathāvidhena vācyena lakṣyeṇa vā
sambandhamupāśnute | śabdādapratītayorghaṭanā'yogāt | śābde prameye pravṛttimā
trasyā'bhidheyatvāt | kāryāyāṃ tu kartavyatā''kārāyāṃ pravṛttāvanabhidheyāyāṃ
na niyamanimittaṃ viśiṣṭasamabhivyāhāranimittaṃ samasti anabhidheyasyā'
vidyamānaviśiṣṭā'bhidheyasya śabdasvarūpavidhivādino mate pravṛttiheto
rliṅādeḥ | kāryakalpanāditi vivṛṇoti—
api cā'śrutaphaleṣu piṇḍapitṛyajñādiṣu svargādiphalā'dhyāhāraḥ |
kvacit kratūpakārakalpanā samidhādau | śrutānāmapi puruṣaviśeṣaṇatayā
svargādīnāṃ phalatvā'dhyavasāya iti sarva eṣa mahimā vidheḥ | sa śabdasya
tadbhāve vidhibhāve'nupapannaḥ | kasmāt ? na hi tadā śabdādanavakalpyamāna
manyathā phalaṃ kratūpakāraṃ vinā kiñcidgamyate | yacchabdā davagataṃ phalādinā
vinā'nupapadyamānaṃ phalādīnāṃ kalpanābījaṃ bhavet | mā bhūd liṅādigamyasyā
'nyathā'nupapattiḥ | liṅādireva seti cettatrāha—na hi liṅādeḥ svarūpasyaivā'
nyathā'nupapattiḥ | na hi tadrūpasya śrotragrāhyasya sānnidhye phalādi vartate, yena
tadvinā nopapadyate | śaṅkate—pravartakasyeti cet ? liṅādayaḥ khalu puṃsāṃ
pravartakāḥ | na caite niṣphale pravartayituṃ puruṣamīśate iti tadanyathā'nupapattyā
phalakalpanetyarthaḥ | nirākaroti—na | tasyā'pi pravartakatvasya pavanādivartina
ivopapatteḥ | na hi yo yaḥ pravartayati sa sarvaḥ phalamapekṣate | pavanādīnāṃ
pravartayatāmapi tadanapekṣatvadarśanādityarthaḥ | kārikārthamupasaṃharati—tasmāddhetu
pañcakānna vidhiḥ śabdaḥ | tadvyāpāro vā na vidhiḥ | ata eva hetupañca
[p.12]
nanvanabhidheye ete doṣāḥ, abhidheya eva tu liṅādiśabdānāṃ puruṣa
prati prayojakavyāpāra ātmīyaḥ pravartanā | evaṃ hyaktam | abhidhābhāvanā
māhuranyāmeva liṅādayaḥ | tathā ca jñāpakatvātprāmāṇyam | pravṛttihetuṃ
buddhvā puruṣasyecchayā pravṛttiḥ saṃbandhabodhā'pekṣā ca abhidheyasambandha
niyamaśca | abhidheyatvāt | pratītapravartanā'nyathā'nupapattyā kāryakalpaneti |
naitatsāram |
na prayogā'nirūpyatvādvaiyarthyātpūrvadoṣataḥ |
apravṛtteḥ phalā'yogādrūpoktervyāpṛtiḥ śruteḥ ||
vidhirityanuṣajyate | na hi preraṇā'dhyeṣaṇā'bhyanujñālakṣaṇaḥ śabdasya
kāñcodayati—nanvanabhidheyaśabdavyāpāre ete doṣāḥ pūrvoktāḥ | abhidheye
eva tu liṅādiśabdānāṃ puruṣaṃ pravartamānaṃ prayojyaṃ prati prayojakavyāpāra
ātmīyaḥ pravartanā | na caitatsvotprekṣitamityāha—evaṃ hyuktam—
abhidhābhāvanāmāhuranyāmeva liṅādayaḥ | iti |
abhidhāyāḥ śabdasya bhāvanāṃ pravartanāmāhurityarthaḥ | evaṃ ca yatkāra
ṇatvenā 'prāmāṇyamāsañjitaṃ tatsvakāryapravartanājñāpakatvena parihṛtaṃ bhavatī
tyāha—tathā ca jñāpakatvāt prāmāṇyam | icchāsahakāriṇaśca pravartakatvāt
icchāyāścā'vaśyambhāvā 'niyamāta | pravṛttiniyamo'pyupapanna ityāha—
pravṛttihetuṃ buddhavā puruṣasyecchayā pravṛttiḥ | jñāpakatve sati saṃvidāśrayatvamapyu
papannamityāha—sambandhabodhā'pekṣā ca | prakṛtya ntarā'nuraktapravartanā'bhi
dhāyinā ca pratyayena saha prakṛteḥ samabhivyāhāraviśeṣo'pyupapanna ityāha—
abhidheyasambandhaniyamaśca | abhidheyayordhātvarthapratyayārthapravartanayoḥ sambandha
niyamātprakṛtipratyayayoḥ samabhivyāhāraviśeṣo'pyupapanna ityarthaḥ | kutaḥ ?
pravartanāyā abhidheyatvāt | śabdavyāpāraścā'bhihita icchāsahakārī pravarta
yatīti | kāryakalpanā'pyupapannetyāha—śabdapratītapravartanā'nyayā'nupapattyā
kāryakalpaneti | tadetad dūṣayati—naitatsāram | tathā hi |
na prayogā'nirūpyatvādvaiyarthyātpūrvadoṣataḥ |
apravṛttaḥ phalā'yogādrūpoktervyāpṛtiḥ śruteḥ ||
vedhirityanuṣajyate | sa ca na vyāpṛtiḥ śruteriti niṣidhyate |
prayogā'nirūpyatvāditi vivṛṇoti—na hi preṣaṇā'dhyeṣaṇā'bhyanujñālakṣaṇaḥ
[p.13]
vyāpāro nirūpyate | tasya puruṣadharmatvāt | nāpyanyastatsamarthācaraṇalakṣaṇaḥ
kārīṣādivat | anupalabdheḥ | tata evopalabdhiḥ sambandhā'pekṣaṇāda
samīcīnā | na ca ṇijvatpreṣaṇādyatirikte tatsamarthācaraṇamātre liṅādayo
nirūpitaprayogāḥ | kārīṣādistu śītāpanodanādinā śaktatāṃ sampādayan
pravṛttihetuḥ | śabdastu kathamiti na nirūpyate |
śabdasya prayogo vyāpāro nirūpyate | nanu śabdoccāraṇā'nantaraṃ tadavagamā
ttenoktamiti kathaṃ preṣaṇādilakṣaṇaḥ śabdaprayogo na nirūpyata ityāha—tasya
puruṣadharmatvāt | satyaṃ śabdavijñānā'nantaramupalabhyate | na tvasau śabdasyā
'bhiprāyabhedatvāt | preṣaṇāderacetanatvena śabde'sambhavādityarthaḥ |
atha mā bhūvan preṣaṇādayaḥ puruṣadharmāḥ śabde, yathā tu kārīṣo'gnira
dhyāpayatītyadhyayanapravṛttau samartha śītā'panodanamadhyeturbhāvayan kārīṣo
'gniradhyayane pravartayati puruṣam evaṃ liṅādayo'pi pravṛttisamarthaṃ kañcidupakāraṃ
puruṣasyā''carantaḥ svavyāpāreṇa pravartayiṣyantītyata āha—nāpyanyaḥ preṣaṇā
dibhyastatsamarthā''caraṇalakṣaṇaḥ kārīṣādivat | kutaḥ ? anupalabdheḥ |
pramāṇāntareṇa |
yadyucyeta, mā jñāyi prāmāṇāntareṇa, śabdādeva sa vyāpāro'vagaṃsyate 
ityata āha—tata evopalabdhirasamīcīnā | kutaḥ ? sambandhagrahaṇā
'pekṣaṇāt | ayamarthaḥ | gṛhītasambandhaḥ khalu śabdaḥ svārthamavabodhayati |
sambandhabodhaśca sambandhisaṃvedanā'dhīnaḥ | śabdādeva cedarthaḥ sambandhī prati
patavyo, duruttaramitaretarāśrayaṃ prasajyeta; sambandhabodhe śabdo'rthamavabodhayati,
tadavabodhe ca sambandhabodha iti | nanu yathā ṇicpratyayaḥ kārīṣādīnāma
dhyayanapravṛtyanukūlaṃ vyāpāramabhidhatte abhidhāsyante tathā liṅādayaḥ svavyā
pārameva puruṣapravṛttyanukūlāṃ pravartanāmityata āha—na ca ṇijvat preṣaṇā
dyatirikte tatsamarthā''caraṇamātre liṅādayo nirūpitaprayogāḥ mānāntareṇa
liṅādīnāṃ vyāpārasyā'nupalabdherityuktam | ṇijvācyastu kārīṣādīnāṃ
vyāpāro na tathetyata āha—kārīṣādistu śītā'panodanādinā śaktatāṃ
sampādayan pravṛttihetuḥ | śabdastu kathamiti na nirūpyate, vaiyarthyāditi
[p.14]
syānmatam—pavanādiriva liṅādi prerayati puruṣam | tadasat |
abhidhānavaiyarthyāt | apratītavyāpārasyā'pi vāyvāderiva svabhāvata
prerakatvāt | pūrvoktadoṣāpātācca | na hi pravṛtti prati kārakatve śabdasya
sadapi tadvyāpārā'bhidhānamaṅgam | anabhihitavyāpārasyā'pi tasya kāraka
tvāt | kārakasyā'napekṣitajñānatvāta | upetyāpi tu śabdasya prayoga
brūma | na pravartena puruṣa | pravatayato'pi śabdasyā'nanurodhyatvāt | na hi
sarvasmin pravatayitari pravṛtti prekṣāvatām, api tvanuvidheye | na
cārthā'nathaprāptiparihārādyanuvidhānakāraṇa svāmyādāviva śabde samasti |
phalātpravṛttau tadvayathyam | tata eva phalā'vagamādavaiyarthyamiti cet ? tanna |
vivarītu śaṅkate—syānmatam | pavanādiriva liṅādi prerayati puruṣam, ki
mānāntaratastadvyāpāragrahaṇeneti | nirākaroti—tadasat | abhidhānasya
vaiyarthyāt | kuta ? apratīta vyāpārasyā'pi liṅādervāyvāderiva svabhāvata
prerakatvāt, pūrvoktadoṣā''pātācca | apramāṇatvādidoṣaprasaṅgādityartha |
nanvanabhidheyatve vyāpārasyaite doṣā na tvabhidheyatve ityuktam, ata
āha—na hi pravṛttikārakatve śabdasya sadapi tadvyāpārā'bhidhānamaṅga
pravṛtti prati | anabhihitavyāpārasyā'pi tasya kārakatvāt | kuta ?
kārakasyā'napekṣitajñānatvāt | etena vaiyarthyātpūrvadoṣata iti ca vivṛte |
apravṛtteriti vivṛṇoti—upetyā'pi tu śabdasya prayoga brūma, na pravarteta
puruṣa | kuta ? pravartayato'pi śabdasyā'nanurodhyatvāt | na hi sarvasmin
pravartayitari pravṛtti prekṣāvatām, api tvanuvidheye | athā'nuvidheya śabda
kasmāttena bhavatītyata āha—na cārthā'nathaprāptiparihārādyanuvidhāna
kāraṇa svāmyādāviva śabde samasti | ādiśabdābhyā karuṇādu khino
rgrahaṇam | yadyucyeta, phalaprāptiranuvidhānakāraṇamastīti, tatrāha—phalā
tpravṛttau tadvaiyarthyam | taditi śabdavyāpāra parāmṛśati | śaṅkate—tata eva
śabdavyāpārāt śabdena kṛtā'vabhāsanāt phalā'vagamādavaiyarthyamiti cet ?
tanna | phalā'yogāt, svavyāpāreṇa kṛtā'vabhāsanena | śabde pravartayitari
phalasyā'yuktatvamāha—āpte hi sapratyayakriye hitakare eva pravartayitari
anyathā'nupapattyā phalā'vagama | pramāṇena hitā'hitaprāptiparihāropāyata
ttvā'vagatirāpti, tayā saha vartate ityāptastasmin | sa ca hitā'hita
prāptiparihāropāyatattvā'vagatāvapi kadācidakāruṇikatayā nopadiśati |
ata uktama—hitakare pareṣām | ete ca tattvajñānakāruṇya parātmavartinī iti
[p.15]
phalā'yogāt | āpte hi sapratyayakriye hitakare eva pravartayitari anyathānu
papattyā phalā'vagama | śabde tu kimanupapannam ? pravartanāmātrasya vāyvā
dāviva viparīte'pyupapatte |
puruṣaśreyo'rtha pravṛtte śāstrasya naivamiti cet ? na | pramāṇā'bhāvāt |
prāk śāstratvā'siddhe | liṅādivyāpāre ca vidhau svarūpā'bhidhāna
prasaṅga | anyathā śabdaviśiṣṭavyāpārā'pratyayātsannidhānādviśiṣṭatve'rthasyāpi
tatprasaṅgāt | yogyatvasyobhayatrā'parijñānāt | tasmānnā'bhidheyā bhāvanā
vidhirūpam |
kuto'vagamyate ityata uktam—sapratyayakriye | pratyaya upadiṣṭasyā'rthasya
mānāntareṇa savādastena saha vartate iti sapratyayā kriyā yasyopadeśarūpā
sā yasyo padeṣṭu sa tathokta, tasmin |
etadukta bhavati | yadeva yasmai hitamupadiśati kama tasya tatpravṛttau
hitaprāpte savādadarśanādupadeṣṭṛvartinī api tattvajñānakāruṇye īṣatkarajñāne
eveti tadetasmin pravartayitari tatpravartanā'nyathānupattyā phalā'vagama iti
yuktam | śabde tu kimanupapannam ? pravartaneti cet ? tatrāha—pravartanā
mātrasyā'' ptā'nāptapraṇetṛkasya vāyvādāviva viparīte'pyupapatte | śaṅkate—
puruṣaśreyo'rthapravṛtte śāstrasya naivamiti cet ? śāsanāddhi śiṣyāṇā śāstra
mucyate, śāsana ca śaṅkitasamāropitarūpavyavacchedena hitā'hitaprāpti
parihāropāyatattvajñāpanam, tataśca śāstratvādeva puruṣaśreyo'vagatirityartha |
nirācaṣṭe—na pramāṇā'bhāvāt | prāk phalā'vagamācchāstratvā'siddhe |
phalā'vagamācca śāstratve parasparāśrayaprasaṅgāditi bhāva |
tadanena phalā'yogāditi vivṛtam | rūpokteriti vyācaṣṭe—liṅādi
vyāpāre ca vidhau liṅādisvarūpā'bhidhānaprasaṅga | kasmāt ? anyathā yadi
liṅādisvarūpa nā'bhidhīyate tatastannā'vagatamiti viśiṣṭavyāpārā'pratyayāt
liṅāde sanidhānāt tena viśiṣṭatve'rthasyā'pi tadvyāpāravattvaprasaṅgāt |
sanidhānā'viśeṣāt | ayā'rthasya pravartanā prati yogyatva nā'vadhṛtamiti
sanidhāne'pi na tasya pravartanā vyāpāra iti tatrāha—yogyatvasyobhayatrā
'parijñānāt | na hi liṅāderapi yogyatvamanyato'vadhṛtamityarthaḥ |
kārikārthamupasaharati—tasmānnā'bhidheyā bhāvanā vidhirūpam |
[p.16]
atha matam—abhidhaiva bhāvanā vidhirliṅgādyartha iti | atrocyate—
pravṛtteḥ sarvato'rthe vā prasaṅgātkāryato gateḥ |
asthānānniyaterhetorabhāvāccā'bhidhaiva na ||
vidhirityanuṣajyate | abhidhā cedvidhiḥ sarvaśabdānāṃ yathāsvamabhidheyeṣu
tadbhāva iti ghaṭādiśabdebhyo 'pi pravṛttiprasaṅgaḥ | asyā aviśeṣāt |
abhidheyaviśeṣādbhede sa eva pravṛttiheturanvayavyatirekābhyām | ananyalabhyastu
śabdārtha iti sthitau kāryonneyāyā abhidhāyāḥ śabdārthatvamayuktam |
atha mataṃ, nā'bhidheyā bhāvanā'bhidhābhāvaneti, api tvabhidhaivā'rthā
'bhidhāvyāpāraḥ śabdasya bhāvanā vidhirliṅādyartha iti | tasya ca śabdā'rtha
saṃvedanāphalaprathitasadbhāvasya pravṛttijananaṃ prati sāmarthyamātramunneyamiti
bhāvaḥ |
atrocyate—
pravṛtteḥ sarvato'rthe vā prasaṅgātkāryato gateḥ |
asthānānniyaterhetorabhāvāccā'bhidhaiva na ||
vidhirityanuṣajyate | sa ca nābhidheti niṣidhyate | asyāṃ pratijñāyāṃ
pravṛtteḥ sarvataḥ prasaṅgāditi hetuṃ vivṛṇoti—abhidhā cedvidhiḥ sarva
śabdānāṃ yathāsvamabhidheyeṣu tadbhāvo'bhidhāvyāpārasadbhāva iti ghaṭādi
śabdebhyo'pi pravṛttiprasaṅgaḥ | kutaḥ ? asyā abhidhāyā aviśeṣāt |
yadyucyeta, arthābhidhātvamātreṇā'viśeṣe'pi abhidheyaviśeṣādbhedo bhaviṣya
tyabhidhāyāḥ | tādṛśaḥ khalvabhidheyaviśeṣo liṅādīnāṃ, yena liṅādyabhidhaiva
pravartayati, na tu ghaṭādiśabdā'bhidheti, tatrāha—abhidheyaviśeṣādbhede sa
evā'bhidhāviśeṣa eva pravṛttiheturnā'bhidhā | kutaḥ ? anvayavyatirekābhyāmabhi
veyaviśeṣā'vagame sati pravṛtteḥ | satyāmapyabhidhāyāṃ ghaṭādiśabdebhyastadabhāve
pravṛtterabhāvādityarthaḥ |
tadanenā'rthe veti vivṛtam | kāryato gateriti vivṛṇoti—ananya
labhyastu śabdārtha iti sthitau satyāmarthapratītilakṣaṇakāryonneyāyā abhidhāyā
[p.17]
abhidhāyāścā'bhidhā'pekṣaṇe'navasthāpātāt | arthā'bhidhaivā'pekṣate iti
cet ? na | niyāmakasya hetorabhāvāt | arthā'bhidhānasya ca | na hyatra
heturasti | arthābhidhaivāpekṣate nā'bhidheti | abhidhīyate'bhidheti puruṣasya
ca pravṛttiriti kiṃ kena saṅgatam ? tasmādabhidhā na vidhiḥ |
astu tarhyarthabhedaḥ | naitadapi |
apauruṣeye preṣādirnṛdharmo nā'vakalpate |
anyalabdhatvācchabdārthatvamayuktam | asthānāditi vivṛṇoti—abhidhāyāścā
'bhidhā'pekṣaṇe'bhidhā'bhidhā'pyabhidhātvenā'bhidheyā, evaṃ tadabhidhā'pītya
navasthāpātaḥ | śaṅkate—arthā'bhidhaivā'pekṣate'bhidhāṃ nā'bhidheti cet ?
nirākaroti—na | niyāmakasya hetorabhāvāt |
api cā'rthā'bhidhā'bhidhīyamānā tayaivā'bhidhīyetā'bhidhāntareṇa
vā ? yadā'bhidhāntareṇa tadā tayoyaugapadyaṃ kramo veti vikalpā upaplavante |
tatra na tāvatprathamaḥ kalpaḥ | svātmani vṛttivirodhenā'pāstatvāt | nā'pi
yaugapadyam | phale phalāntarā'nupapatteḥ | na hyasti sambhavaḥ, pākaḥ, pacyate,
chidā cchidyate iti | kvacidasti, yathā jñānaṃ jñāyate iti cet | na |
phalāvasthāyāṃ tadanupapatteḥ phalāntarotpādasamaye ca tasya jñeyatayā karmakāra
katvena phalābhāvā'bhāvāt | astu tarhi kramaḥ | prathamaṃ liṅādibhiḥ svārtho
'bhidhīyate, atha svārtho 'bhidheti | tanna | śabdabuddhikarmaṇāmanāvṛttānāṃ viramya
vyāpārā'nupapatteḥ |
etenaiva kramaśo'bhidhānaṃ pratyuktam | tadanenā'bhisandhinā''ha—
arthā'bhidhānasya ca | arthā'bhidhāyā api hetorabhāvādityarthaḥ | anena
niyaterhetorabhāvācceti vyākhyātam | cetyabhidhānā'bhāvahetusamuccayāditi |
niyamahetvabhāvamāha—na hyatra heturasti | arthā'bhidhaivā'pekṣate'bhidhāṃ nā'bhi
dhā'bhidheti | anyalabhyasyā'pi śabdārthatvā'bhyupagamāditi bhāvaḥ | api
cā'bhidhīyate liṅādibhiḥ svārthā'bhidheti ca prekṣāvatāṃ hitā'hitaprāpti
parihārārthanāṃ pravṛttiriti kiṃ kena saṅgatam ? na khalu prāsādaḥ śveta iti
pipāsavo dahanamāharanti | upasaṃharati—tasmādabhidhānamapi na vidhiḥ |
astu tarhyarthabheda eva liṅādīnāṃ vidhiḥ naitadapi | kutaḥ ?
apauruṣeye praiṣādirnadharmo nā'vakalpate |
[p.18]
na ca pravartanāmātramaviśeṣamakartṛkam ||
loke hi pratītaḥ preṣaṇā'dhyeṣaṇā'bhyanujñālakṣaṇo'bhiprāyā'tiśayaḥ
prayoktṛdharmo liṅarthaḥ | tasyā'pauruṣeyeṣu vedavākyeṣvasambhavaḥ | pratīteḥ
sambhava iti cet ? na | pauruṣeyatvāpatteḥ | vedātmano'bhiprāya
ityatisthavīyaḥ | tenā'saṃdṛbdhatve'bhiprāyā'vagatyabhāvāt, saṃdṛbdhatve pauru
ṣeyatvadoṣāt |
na ca pravartanāmātramaviśeṣamakartṛkam ||
liṅādyarthabhedaścedvidhistasya lokā'dhīnā'vadhāraṇatvālloke ca
preṣaṇādhyeṣaṇā'bhyanujñālakṣaṇasya tasyopalabdheḥ sa eva vidhiḥ syāt |
tasya ca pauruṣeyatayā'pauruṣeyeṣu vedavākyeṣvasambhava ityāha—loke hi
pratītaḥ praiṣaṇā'dhyeṣaṇā'bhyanujñālakṣaṇo'bhiprāyā'tiśayo'nuvidheyapuruṣadhaureya
dharmatayā'tiśayaḥ prayokturdharmo liṅarthaḥ | tasyā'pauruṣeyeṣu vedavākyeṣvasambhavaḥ |
śaṅkate—lokā'vagatasāmarthyālliṅādervede'pi śrūyamāṇātpreṣaṇādipratīteḥ
sambhava iti cet ? na hi dṛṣṭe'nupapannaṃ nāmeti bhāvaḥ | nirākaroti—na,
pauruṣeyatvāpatteḥ | ye'pi vedāntavādino'pauruṣeyamaṅgīkṛtya vedaṃ vedaśarīri
ṇamantaryāmiṇamāsthiṣata athā'dhidaivaṃ yaḥ sarveṣu vedeṣu pratiṣṭhan sarvebhyo
vedebhyonantaro yaṃ sarve vedā na viduryasya sarve vedāḥ śarīraṃ yaḥ sarvān vedā
nantaro yamayati eṣa ta ātmā'ntaryāmyamṛta iti śruteḥ; tān pratyāha—
vedātmano'bhiprāya ityatisthavīyaḥ | ceṣṭendriyārthāśrayaḥ śarīramiti hi
tadvidaḥ | na ca kasyacidātmanaśceṣṭādīnāṃ vedarāśirāśrayo bhavitumarhati |
na jātu devadattādiśarīravadasmin buddhipūrvāḥ santi kriyāḥ | na ca pārthivā
''pyataijasavāyavīyā'dṛṣṭopanibaddhakarṇanemimaṇḍalā'vacchinnanabhobhāgātmanāmi
ndriyāṇāmamūrttavedādhāratā sambhavati | na ca manomātrasya bāhyendriyanira
pekṣasya bhogasādhanatvamūpapadyate |
etena viṣayāśrayatvamasya pratyuktam | tasmāddehalakṣaṇavirahādvedātmano
vedadehasyā''tmano'bhiprāya ityatisthavīya iti | api ca yadā loke
gāmabhyājetyādivākyebhyo vakturājñādayo'bhiprāyabhedāḥ pratīyante nā'nyasya
tadā yadyantaryāmī vedasandṛbdhā tatastena tasyā'sambaddhatvena tadabhiprāyā'va
gatyabhāvāt, sandṛ bdhatve pauruṣeyatvadoṣādayuktametat
[p.19]
yadapi matam—anekasāmarthyaparikalpanādoṣādvyabhicārācca praiṣādīnāma
vācyatvādavyabhicārātpravartanāmātraṃ loke liṅādyarthastasya vede'pyupapatti
riti | idamapya caturasram | nirviśeṣasāmānyā'yogāt | akartṛkatve vyā
pārā'nupapatteśca | na tāvat praiṣādayo viśaṣā vede sambhavinaḥ | nā'pyanyo
viśeṣaḥ kaścidupadarśyate | tadupadarśane vā sāmānyā'bhidhānaprasādhanamasminna
vasare vyartham | tadetadapāstasakamabhedaṃ pravartanāsāmānyaṃ brāhmaṇyamiva
samujjhitakaṭhādibhedaṃ syāt | pravartanā ca pravartayiturvyāpāraḥ sa
tamantareṇeti nā'tivirājate | puruṣasyā'bhāvāt | śabdasya ca pravarta
katvaniṣedhāt pravartayiturabhāvaḥ |
yadi manyeta phalaṃ pravartakaṃ tadvyāpāraḥ pravartanā, phalārthinaḥ puruṣasya
yadapi mataṃ, tattatpreṣaṇādyabhiprāyaviśeṣā'bhidhāne'nekasāmarthyaparikalpanā
doṣādvyabhicārācca praiṣādīnāmavācyatvādavyabhicārātpravartanāmātraṃ loke
liṅartho , na punaḥ praiṣādayaḥ | tasya cā'pauruṣeye vede'pyupapattiriti |
abhiprāyabhedāḥ khalvamī praiṣādayaḥ puruṣanāntarīyakatayā tamavagamayanti, na
pravartanāmātram | tasya tannāntarīyakatvā'bhāvāditi bhāvaḥ | nirācaṣṭe—
idamapyacaturasram | kutaḥ ? nirviśeṣasāmānyā'yogāt | akartṛkatve
vyāpārā'yogācca |
prathamaṃ hetu vivṛṇoti—na tāvatpraiṣādayo viśeṣā vede sambhavinaḥ,
apauruṣeyatvavyādhātāt | nā'pyanyo viśeṣaḥ kaścidupadarśyate | tadupadarśane
vā sāmānyā'bhidhānaprasādhanamasminnavasare vyartham, viśeṣasyaiva pravartakatvāt |
tadetadapāstasakalabhedaṃ pravartanāsāmānyaṃ brāhmaṇatvamiva samujjhitakaṭhādibhedaṃ 
syāt | gaganakusumāyamānaṃ syādityarthaḥ |
dvitīyaṃ vivṛṇoti—pravartanā ca pravartayiturvyāpāraḥ, sa taṃ pravartayitāra
mantareṇeti nā'tivirājate | nanu puruṣaḥ pravartayitā bhaviṣyatītyata āha—
puruṣasyā'bhāvāt | nāpi śabda ityāha—śabdasya ca pravartakatvaniṣedhā
dadhastāt | tasmātpravartayiturabhāvaḥ, tadabhāvāttadvyāptāyāḥ pravartanāya
apyabhāvaḥ | yadi manyeta phala pravartakamiti tadvyāpāro'rthitā prītyātma ta
[p.20]
tatsādhane pravṛtteḥ | anyathā 'bhāvāt | na kaścidvyāpāraviśeṣaḥ pravartanā'pi
tu pravṛttisamarthaṃ vyāpāramātraṃ ca prayojakavyāpāraḥ | bhikṣā vāsayati; kārīṣo
'gniradhyāpayatīti darśanāt | tadasat |
arthitā vyāpṛtiḥ puṃso niyamaḥ kiṃnibandhanaḥ |
phalasādhanatā karmaniśceyā sādhyatā kadā ||
phalārthitā cet pravṛttihetuḥ secchā tadyogo vā icchāsamavāyo vā |
kṛttaddhitasamāseṣu sambandhā'bhidhānaṃ tvatalbhyāmiti vacanāt puruṣadharma iti
na phalaṃ vyāpṛtiḥ | atha tadicchopahāramukhena phalasya pravṛttiheturdharmaḥ prītyā
tmatā phalavyāpāraḥ pravartanā ? sā'pi tatraiva na karmaṇi | phalavyāpārācca
vā pravartanā | ka smāt ? phalārthinaḥ puruṣasya phalasādhane pravṛtteḥ | anyathā
phalavyāpāramantareṇā'bhāvāt | na hi kaścideva viśeṣo vyāpāraḥ pravartanā,
api tu pravartana samartha vyāpāramātraṃ prayojakavyāpāraḥ, pravartanāsama rthaścā
yamarthitālakṣaṇaḥ prītyātmatā lakṣaṇo vā phalasya vyāpāraḥ | bhikṣā
vāsayati, kārīṣo'gniradhyāpayatīti darśanāt, annopādānaśītā'panoda
nābhyām | tad dūṣayati—tadasat |
arthitā vyāpṛtiḥ puṃso niyamaḥ kiṃ nibandhanaḥ |
phalasādhanatā karmmaniśceyā sādhyatā kadā ||
phalārthitā cetpravṛttihetuḥ secchā, tadyogo vā icchāsamavāyo vā
kṛttaddhitasamāseṣu sambandhā'bhidhānaṃ tvatalbhyāmiti vārtikakāravacanāt
puruṣadharma eva, na phaladharma iti na phalaṃ vyāpṛtiḥ | na khalvanyatra samaveto
'nyavyāpāro bhavitumarhati, atiprasaṅgāt | śaṅkate—atha tadicchopahāramukhena
phalasya pravṛttiheturdharmaḥ, prītyātmatā phalavyāpāraḥ pravartanā, iṣṭalakṣaṇaṃ phalam,
sukhā'nuśāyinī ceccheti phalena sukhātmanā bhavitavyam, sukhatvena tadicchāmupa
harati, icchā ca prayatnaṃ prasūte, itīcchopahāramukhena phalasamavāyinī prītyātmatā
pravṛttihetuvyāpāratvād bhavati pravartanetyarthaḥ | nirācaṣṭe—sā'pi prītyātmatā
'pi tatraiva phale eva | na ca phalaṃ pravṛttiviṣaya ityuktam, phalena sākṣātkaraṇe
kartuśceṣṭeti | yatra ca phalasādhane karmmaṇi pravṛttiḥ tasya ca duḥkhatayā na
prītyātmatetyāha—na karmaṇi |
[p.21]
pravartamānaḥ sarvatra pravarteta | niyamanimittā'bhāvāt | tatsādhanatvāt karmaṇyeva
pravartate, na sarvatra | tata eva tarhi tatsādhanatva pravṛttihetuḥ karmaṇi
na phalarūpam, tacca karmasamavāyīti karma pravartakaṃ syāt | evaṃ tarhi tatsādhyatā
pravṛttihetuḥ, sā ca phalasamavāyinīti na doṣaḥ | tathā hi | samabhilaṣitasya
tṛptyādeḥ karmaviśeṣeṇa sādhyatvāttatraiva pravṛttiḥ | kā punariyaṃ sādhyatā ?
yadi rūpaṃ phalasya, sarvatra pravattiprasaṅgaḥ | kārakaviśeṣaḥ | sa hi phalasya
sādhananiyataḥ śaktibhedo rūpādivat | anyathā kārakatvā'bhāvāt | yathā hi
yadyucyeta, phalasamavāyino'pi prītyātmatāvyāpārātpuruṣaḥ karmaṇi
pravartsyati, tatrāha—phalavyāpārācca pravartamānaḥ sarvatra pravarteta | kutaḥ ?
phalādanyatvena tatsādhanasyā'nyeṣāṃ cā'viśeṣānniyamanimittā'bhāvāt |
codayati—tatsādhanatvātkarmaṇyeva pravartate | sarvatra sarveṣāṃ phalasādhanatvā
'bhāvāt | pariharati—tata eva tatsādhanatve sati pravṛttibhāvādeva | bhavatu
tarhi tatsādhanatvaṃ pravṛttihetuḥ karmaṇi, na punaḥ phalarūpam | bhavatu ko doṣa ?
ityata āha—tataśca karmasamavāyi na phalasamavāyīti karmaiva pravartakaṃ syāt |
codayati—evaṃ tarhi tatkarmasādhyatā pravṛttihetuḥ, sā ca phalasamavāyinīti na
doṣaḥ | nanūktaṃ phalasamavāyinī sā kathamanyatra karmaṇi pravṛttiheturatiprasaṅgā
diti ? tatrāha—tathā hi samabhilaṣitasya tṛptyādeḥ karmaviśeṣeṇa sādhyatvātta
traiva karmaviśeṣe pra varttanā'nyatra |
etaduktaṃ bhavati | phalasamavāyinyapi asau sādhyatā sādhanā'dhīnā
nirūpaṇatayā sādhanamapi gocarayati, na punarasādhanamapi | tenaiva tasmā
dviśeṣātsādhane eva pravartayati, na tu sarvatreti | tadetad dūṣayati—kā punariyaṃ
sādhyatā ? yadi rūpaṃ phalasya, tatastasya sādhanā'dhīnanirūpaṇatvā'bhāvānna sādhane
pravartayet | pravartayedvā sarvatraiva, anyatvā'viśeṣāt | codayati—kārakaviśeṣaḥ |
viśeṣyate vyāvartyate karmakārakaṃ gagana kusumādibhyo yena śaktibhedena sa
tathoktaḥ | nanu tathā'pi kutastasya sādhane pravartakatvam ? ityatrāha—sa hi phalasya
sādhananiyataḥ śaktibhedaḥ | ataḥ sādhane pravartate ityarthaḥ | nidarśanamāha—
rūpādivat | kutaḥ śaktibhedapratyayaḥ ? ityata āha—anyathā yadi phalasya śakti
bhedo na syāttato'sya laukikaparīkṣakaprasiddhasya karmakārakatvasyā'bhāvāt |
śaktimatkārakamiti tadvidaḥ | dṛṣṭāntadārṣṭāntike vibhajate—yathā hi rūpādīnāṃ
[p.22]
rūpādīnāṃ śaktiviśeṣād grahaṇe sādhananiyamaḥ tathā phaleṣvapi draṣṭavyaḥ |
gaganatatkusumāderanutpādāt, tṛptyādeśca karmamātreṇa | kadā punarayaṃ śaktibhedaḥ
sādhyatā'bhidhānaḥ ? phalasya bhāvasamaye na tāvat | vaiyarthyādapravṛttihetutvācca |
na khalūtpannasyotpādo, yadyoginī śaktirarthavatī | nāpi siddhe phale tatsādhane
kaścitpravartate | abhāvakāle'pyasatkathaṃ śaktimat khapuṣpavat ?
nanu sadeva kāryamutpadyate ?
karmakārakāṇāṃ śaktiviśeṣādgrahaṇe cakṣurādisādhananiyamaḥ, tathā phaleṣvapi
draṣṭavyaḥ | sambhavati khalu sa tādṛśaḥ phalasya karmakārakasya sāmarthyā'tiśayo
yenā'bhavanadharmaṇaśca gaganakusumāderbhavanadharmaṇaśca ghaṭādestathā sādhanā'
viśeṣācca bhedaḥ sidhyatotyāha—gaganakusumāde ranutpādāt | tṛptyādeśca
phalasya karmamātreṇa | tadetadvikalpya dūṣayati—kadā punarayaṃ śaktibhedaḥ sādhya
tā'bhidhānaḥ ? phalasya bhāvasamaye na tāvat | kutaḥ vaiyarthyādapravṛttihetutvācca |
vaiyarthyāditi hetuṃ vivṛṇoti—na khalutpa nnaphalasyotpādaḥ yadyoginī śakti
rarthavatī | apravṛttihetutvācceti vivṛṇoti—nā'pi siddhe phale tatsādhane
kaścitprekṣāvān pravartate | phalasyā'bhāvakāle'pyasat kathaṃ śaktimat ?
khapuṣpavat | codayati—nanu sadeva kāryamutpadyate ?
idamatrā''kūtam | yadasattanna kriyate yathā śaśaviṣāṇam | asaccetkāraṇe
kāryaṃ tadapi na kriyeta, asattvāt | kriyate ca, tasmātsat | tathā hi | kriyamā
ṇatvaṃ bhavanakartṛtayā vyāptaṃ, sā cā'sato vyāvartamānā svavyāpyaṃ kriyamāṇatvaṃ
vyāvartayantī satyeva vyavasthāpayatīti pratibandhasiddhiḥ | api ca kārakatvo'
viśeṣe'pi kasmāt tantvādyātmakā eva paṭādayo, na kuvindādyātmakāḥ ?
na hyasattve kaścidviśeṣaḥ | tantvādyātmānaścopalabhyante | tasmātantvādiṣu
santaḥ paṭādayaḥ | api ca yadi tantvādiṣvasantaḥ paṭādayaḥ, kasmāttantvādibhya
eva bhavanti, na tu vāra ṇādibhyaḥ ? paṭa eva vā tantubhyaḥ kasmājjāyate,
na jāyante kaṭādayaḥ ? ato na khalvasattve kaścidviśeṣaḥ | so'yamubhaya
vidho niyamaḥ svakāraṇe kāryasya sattvamasatvamanyatrā'vagamayati | api
cā'śaktādaśakyakāryasyā'nutpatteratiprasaṅgāt śaktādeva kāraṇātkāryeṇa
[p.23]
idaṃ tāvadasatkāryavādino'nunāthya vaktavyam |
śakyenotpattavyam, śaktiyuktaśca śakyaḥ, śaktiśca sambandharūpā
saṃyogavadubhayāśrayā śakyā'bhāve na bhavatīti śakyabhāvo'bhyupeyaḥ |
api ca kāraṇaṃ nāma kāryopahitamaryādaṃ yadi tadabhāve nā'rhati bhavitum na
hi yadyadupahitamaryādaṃ tattadabhāve bhavati; yathā kuṇḍalopahitasīmā kuṇḍalī
na kuṇḍalā'bhāve | tasmātkāraṇabhāvāttatra kārya sat | tadidaṃ satkārya
sādhanamavīta pañcakamācakhyuḥ sāṅkhyāḥ | pariharati—idaṃ tāvadasatkāya
vādino'nunāthya vaktavyam |
idamākūtam | na tāvatkriyamāṇatvamatra pramāṇaṃ bhavitumarhati, asādhā
raṇatvāt, anupalabdhivirodhācca | yathā hi khalvasada śakyakriyamevaṃ sadapi
na jātu satīti śaktirapariṇāminyanantā kriyāgocaraḥ | api ca ida ma
mantrauṣadhamindrajālamāryeṇa śikṣitaṃ yadidamajātā'vinaṣṭarūpā'tiśayam
vyavadhānamanatidūrasthānaṃ tasyaiva tadavasthendriyādereva puṃsaḥ kadācitpratyakṣa 
parokṣaṃ ca, yena kadācidasya pratyakṣamupalabdhiḥ, kadācidanumānam, kadā
cidāgamaḥ | tasminnevā'natiśaye'mīṣāṃ prakārāṇāṃ virodhāt | yadi manyeta,
nā'natiśayamekā'tiśayotpattyā parā'tiśayanivṛttyā ca vyavahārabhedopa
labdheḥ | tisraḥ khalvimā bhāvapariṇatividhā bhavanti sāṅkhyānām, dharmalakṣa
ṇā'vasthābhedāt yathāsvaṃ dharmidharmalakṣaṇā'dhikaraṇāḥ | tadyathā suvarṇatattvamekaṃ
dharmi, tasya pariṇāmāḥ, svastikarucakādayo dharmā upajanā'pāyadharmāṇa
steṣāñca lakṣaṇapariṇāmaḥ, tathā hi | yadā khalvayaṃ hemakāraḥ svastikaṃ
bhaṅttvā rucakaṃ racayati tadā svastiko vartamānatālakṣaṇaṃ hitvā'tītatā
lakṣaṇamāpadyate | rucakastvanāgatatālakṣaṇaṃ hitvā vartamānatāṃ pratipadyate |
tathā'vasthāpariṇāmo lakṣaṇagataḥ | pratikṣaṇamutpattinirodhadharmāṇo'bhinava
tamā'bhinavatarā'bhinavapurāṇapurāṇatarapurāṇatamatvādayaḥ | prayatnarakṣitasyāpi
vastrādeḥ prānte purāṇatamatvopalambhāt so'yaṃ trividhaḥ pariṇāmo'tiśaya
iti | athā'yamatiśayastrividho'pi dharmiṇi sanātanaḥ kādācitko vā ?
yadi sanātanastadā svastikādayaśca traikālyaṃ ca navapurāṇatvādayaścā'paryāyaṃ
dharmiṇyekasmin suvarṇe upalabhyeran | kādācitkatve tu kathaṃ nā'satā
mutpādaḥ ? teṣāṃ śaktyātmanā sattvādayamadoṣa iti cet ? na | śaktirati
[p.24]
api ca nirupādānakāryā'nutpādāttadatyantavyatirekā'bhāvādupādeyasyo-
śayaśca kimekameva tattvaṃ nānā vā ? tatraikatve janmā'janmanivṛttiranivṛttiḥ
pratyakṣatāparokṣatā'rthakriyāsūpayogo'nupayogaśceti kathamekatra niṣparyāyaṃ
parasparaparāhataṃ yujyate ? nānātve vā sattve'pi śakterasannatiśayaḥ kādācitka
iti kathaṃ nā'sata utpattiḥ ? sarvadā'tiśayasya sattve vā kathaṃ na pūrvokta
doṣaprasaṅgaḥ ? atiśayasya vyaktyavyaktibhyāmavirodha iti cet ?
vyaktyavyaktī apyatiśayasya sadātanyau na vā ? tatra sadātanatve tadavasthaiva
virodhaprasaktiḥ | kādācitkatve vā kathaṃ nā'sata utpādaḥ ? iti bhavyatva
ñcā'sata upapādayiṣyate'dhikāranirūpaṇaprastāve | asattve'pi ca kāraṇa
sāmarthyaniyamāt kāryaniyama upapadyate | anyathā bhavanmate'pi pradhāno
pādānatvādviśva sya pradhānasya cā'nvayitayā sarvatraikarūpatvādupādānātma
katvādupādeyasya kāryajātasya sarva sarvataḥ sarvatra sarvadā sarvathā saditīdamato
nedamidamiha nedamidamidānīṃ nedamidamevaṃ nedamiti niyamo na syāt |
kasyacidapi rūpasya kathañcitkadācit kvacidvivekahetorabhāvāt | sarvatra
sattvā'viśeṣe'pi hetusāmarthyaniyamādabhivyaktiniyama iti cet ? hantā'sata
utpatyā kimaparāddham, yena tasyāmapi niyamo na syāt ? bhavatāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ
sarvātmakatvādabhivyaktibhedā'nupapatteśca duradhigamo niyama ityuktaprāyam |
etena kuvindādisamavadhāne'pi paṭasya niyamena tantvātmakatvena 
satkāryasādhanaṃ pratyuktam | śaktiśca śakyaviṣayā'pi śaktāśrayā vināpi
śakyasadbhāvaṃ jñānamiva jñeyaviṣayamapi jñānāśrayamantareṇāpi jñeyamupapatsyate |
kāraṇatvañca kāryaṃ prati kāraṇasya śaktibhedaḥ | sa ca kāraṇāśraya eva, 
na kāryakāraṇāśrayaḥ | nirūpaṇamapyasya kāryā'vagamādhīnaṃ, na tu kāryasattvā
'dhīnamiti nā'vītapañcakaṃ satkāryasādhane prabhavati | tasmāttantvādayaḥ
svavyāpārātprāgasatkāryamutpādayanti kāraṇatvādabhivyaktikāraṇavaditya
satkāryavādino darśayāmbabhūvuḥ | tena tānanunāthyaitadvaktavyam, sadeva kārya
mutpadyate iti |
api ca samānatīrthā api svāyambhuvā ekāntā'nabhyupagamāditi
vadanto na sarvātmanā sata utpattimabhyupayanti, api tūpādeyasyopādānarūpeṇa
sato, na punarupādeyarūpeṇāpi sata ityāha—api ca nirupādānakāryā'nutpā
[p.25]
pādānarūpeṇa sata utpattirucyate | sarvātmanā tu sattve tadanupapattervaiyarthyācco
pādeyavat | sādhyaṃ nopādānam | kathaṃ tarhi karmma kārakam ? naiva kamakārakaṃ
kārakāśritamiti kecit | vikāryaprāpyayorvā sattvādadoṣaḥ | traividhyaṃ
tarhi hīyate | kāmaṃ, na tvasataḥ kārakatvaṃ śakyā'dhyavasānam | kathaṃ tarhi
 ghaṭaṃ karotīti prayogaḥ ? upādeyaśrutyā upādānanirdeśāt | buddhisthasya vā
dāttadatyantavyatirekā'bhāvādupādeyasyopādānarūpeṇa sata utpattirucyate |
niṣkrāntamupādānātkārya nirupādānam, tantvādibhyo bhinnadeśamiti yāvat |
etaduktaṃ bhavati | yathā kuvindādibhyo bhinnadeśe etadupalabhyate, naivaṃ
tantvādibhyaḥ, so'yaṃ dharmadharmibhāvo naikāntikabhede bhavitumarhati, gavāśvasyeva |
nāpyabhede aikāntike, dharmirūpasyeva | tasmātkathañcidbhedaḥ kathañcidabheda
eṣitavyaḥ | so'yamanekāntā'bhyupagama ityarthaḥ |
athopādeyasvarūpeṇāpi sataḥ kimityutpattirnocyate ? ityata āha—
sarvātmanā tu sattve tadanupapatteḥ | tasmādutpatteranupapatteḥ | hetvantaramāha—
vaiyarthyācca sādhanānām | kutaḥ ? upādeyavat | upādeyarūpeṇa tadvat tadanvita
miti yāvat | sādhyaṃ sādhanārha nopādānam, yato niṣpāditakriye karmaṇya
viśeṣādhāyinaḥ sādhanasya sādhananyāyā'tipātāt | nanu yadyasata utpattista
tastasyā'sattvena kriyāṃ prati nimittabhāvā'bhāvātkathaṃ karmakārakam ?
ityāha—kathaṃ tarhi karmakārakam ? tadekadeśimatena pariharati—naiva kārakaṃ
karmakārakāśritamiti kecit | yathāhuḥ kārakādutpatteḥ karme-ti | tade
tatkārakā'dhikāre karmasaṃjñāvidhānaviruddhamityanādṛtya pakṣāntaramupanyasyati—
vikāryaprāpyayorvā sattvādadoṣaḥ | codayati—traividhyaṃ tarhi vaiyākaraṇapravara
saṃmataṃ karmaṇo hīyeta | nirvartyasyā'sattvenā'karmatvāt | pariharati—kāmam,
na tvasataḥ kārakatvaṃ śakyā'dhyavasānam; tatrabhavato'bhiyuktasya pāṇinervirodhe
kātyāyanasyā'sadvāditva miti bhāvaḥ | nanu yadi na nirvartyasya karmatā, kathaṃ
tarhi ghaṭaṃ karotīti dvitīyāprayogaḥ ? tasmāllaukikaprayogavirodhātpāṇine
revā'sadvāditvamityarthaḥ | samādhatte—upādeyasya ghaṭasya śrutyā | ghaṭaśabdeneti
yāvat | upādānasya kapālānāṃ tādarthyanimittopacārāt | upādānasya ca
sattvena kārakatvopapatterdvitīyāprayogasiddhiḥ |
etaduktaṃ bhavati, prayogo laukiko ghaṭaṃ karotīti, na punastasya
mukhyatvamapi, aupacārikasyā'pi tasya loke darśanāt; mañcāḥ krośantīti
[p.26]
kriyāsūpayogādyukto nirvartyasya kārakabhāvaḥ | yathoktam—
vyaktau padārthe śabdāderjanyamānasya karmmaṇaḥ |
kārakatvaṃ tathāsiddhaṃ buddhirūpaprakalpitam ||
etena sādhyasādhanayoḥ parasparā''nukūlyaṃ parāstam | astu tarhi karmma
pravartakam | abhimatasādhanatā tasya pravartanā, pravṛttiheturūpatvāt | na | tasya
viṣayatvāt | api ca—
prajñāyate liṅādīnāṃ vyañjanīyā pravartanā |
prayoktṛdharmmo na ṇico yathā'niyatakartṛkā ||
lokāddhi śabdārthā'dhigamaḥ | tatra ca pravartanā niyatādhārā prayoktṛsaṃśrayaiva
yathā | tadiha kārake karturīpsitatamaṃ karma, karmaṇi dvitīyetyabhiyuktasmṛteraupa
cārika iti niścīyate | ato yasya smṛtyā virodho mukhyatvasya, na tasya
laukikatvam, yasya tu prayogasya laukikatvam, na tasya virodha iti | astu vā
mukhyatvam, tathā'pyavirodha ityāha—buddhisthasya vā kriyāsūpayogāt | kriyā
nimittatvopapattau yuktau nirvartyasya kārakabhāvaḥ | yathoktam—
vyaktau padārthe śabdāderjanyamānasya karmaṇaḥ |
kārakatvaṃ tathā siddhaṃ buddhirūpaprakalpitam ||
ākṛtau hi padārthe tasyā nityatvājjanyamānatvānupapatterityarthaḥ |
tasmānna phalasya śaktibhedaḥ sādhyatā | etena phalagataśaktibhedasādhyatānirā
karaṇamātreṇa sādhyasādhanayoḥ siddhi prati parasparā''nukūlyaṃ sambandhaḥ sādhyatā
parāstā veditavyā | tatrā'pi sāmyātkadeti praśnasya |
astu tarhi karma pravartakam | abhimatasādhanatā tasya pravartanaprayojaka
vyāpāraḥ | kutaḥ ? pravṛttiheturūpatvāt | tathā khalvabhimata sādhane karmaṇi
svayaṃ duḥkharūpe'pi puruṣaḥ pravartate | tadetad dūṣayati—na, tasya viṣayatvā
tpravṛttikartuḥ prayojakaḥ pravartakaḥ, siddhaśca sa bhavati, tadiha siddhaṃ cetkarma
pravṛtteḥ prāk pravṛtterbhāvanāyā viṣayo na bhāvyam | na jātu gaganamasyā bhāvyaṃ
bhavitumarhati | viṣayaścetkarma asiddhatvātkathaṃ pravartakamityarthaḥ | api ca—
prajñāyate liṅādīnā vyañjanīyā prarvatanā |
prayoktṛdharmo na ṇico yathā'niyatakartṛkā ||
lokasiddho hi śabdasāmarthyā'dhigamaḥ | tatra ca pravartanā niyatādhārā
prayoktṛsaṃśrayaiva liṅādyartho'vagamyate | ṇijarthastu sā aniyatādhārā |
[p.27]
liṅādyartho'vagamyate | ṇijarthastu sā'niyatādhārā | na ca ṇijarthavalliṅā
dyarthopi bhavitumarhati | yathālokaprajñānaṃ śabdārthavyavasthānāt |
yasya tvevaṃ sati puruṣaprayatno'nuvādo homā'nugatastu vyāpāraḥ
śrutyocyate iti darśanāccetanakartṛvyāpārātmikā, itarathā vā spandā'spanda
sādhāraṇyenaudāsīnyapracyutyātmavyāpāramātraṃ bhāvanā vidhiśabdaparyāyā liṅartho
'parāmṛṣṭakālabhedā | li ṅādiṣu tu sattvepi kātaviśeṣeṇa vidhirūpaprati
bandhādapravṛttihetutvāt ya dyādibhiriva liṅabhidheyāyā iti darśanam;
tampratyucyate—
naikaṃ vibaddhaṃ na ca tadviśeṣaścetsa kathyatām |
kālabhedā'parāmarśaḥ kāmaṃ nā'smātpravartate ||
liṅādiṣu kālabhedena vidhirūpasya pratibandhe bhāvanājñānā'bhāvo-
na ca ṇijarthavalliṅartho'pi bhavitumarhati, yathālokaprajñānaṃ śabdārthavyavasthā
nāt | sugamam | yasya tu darśanamiti pareṇa sambandhaḥ kṛtaḥ | kiṃ tadityāha—
evaṃ sati puruṣaprayatno'nuvādaḥ, homādhāragatastu vyāpāraḥ śrutyocyata iti
teṣu teṣu śāstrapradeśeṣu bhāṣyakāravyavahāradarśanāccetanakartṛvyāpārātmikā
prayatnaśabdapravedanīyā | itarathā spandā'spandasādhāraṇyenaudāsīnyapratyupa
lakṣyamāṇā''tmavyāpāramātraṃ bhāvanā vidhiśabdaparyāyā liṅarthaḥ |
nanu yadi bhāvanaiva vidhiḥ pacatyapākṣītpakṣyatītyatrā'pyasti bhāvaneti
pacedityanena tulyārthatvaprasaṅga ityata āha—aparāmṛṣṭakālabhedā pravṛtti
heturbhāvanā vidhiraparāmṛṣṭakālabhedā taddhetuḥ | sā ca liṅartha | na tvevaṃ
laṅādyartha ityarthaḥ | laṅādiṣu sattve'pi bhāvanāyāḥ kālaviśeṣeṇa vartamānā
dinā vidhirūpapratibandhādapravṛttihetutvānna liṅāditulyārthatā, ya dyādi
bhiriva liṅabhidheyāyā iti | yathā yadi rathantarasāmā somaḥ syā, dyasyo
bhayaṃ havirārtimārcchatī ,-tyevamādiṣu; taṃ pratyucyate—
naikaṃ vibaddhaṃ, na ca tadviśeṣaścetsa kathyatām |
kālabhedā'parāmarśaḥ kāmaṃ nā'smāt pravartate ||
yadi hi bhāvanā vidhirityekameva tattvaṃ tato liṅādiṣu kālabhedena
vidhirūpapratibandhe bhāvanājñānā'bhāvaḥ | vidherbhāvanāyā abhedāt | avibaddhe
[p.28]
'bhedāt | avibaddhe bhedaprasaṅgāt | na khalvekaṃ vibaddhamavibaddhaṃ ca kalpate |
viśeṣasya pratibandho na bhāvarūpasya | viśeṣarūpabhāvā'bhāvā'nuvidhānāt |
pravṛttibhāvā'bhāvayoḥ | sa tarhi viśeṣo vidhiḥ | na bhāvo bhāvanā
vidhirupadeśaḥ preraṇamityekam | nidarśanīyaścāsau | kartavyateti cet ? na |
tasyā eva bubhutsanāt |
nanu kālabhedaparāmarśaviraha eva viśeṣaḥ kimanyena ? vartamānādirūpāyā
ananuṣṭheyatvāttadabhāve'pyanuṣṭheyatvapratīteḥ | bhavatveṣa viśeṣo na tu puruṣasya
pravṛttihetuḥ | kāmaṃ pratyetu kālabhedaviviktāṃ bhāvanām ? anuṣṭhānaṃ tu
kutaḥ ? na hi vasturūpamātrapratipattiḥ pravṛttinimittam | na khalu ghaṭa iti
pratipadya tato'rtha tāvatyeva tasminpravartate | sādhyatayā pravartata iti cet ?
vā bhāvanāyā vibaddhā'vibaddhaviruddhadharmā'dhyāsād bhedaprasaṅgāt | viruddhadharmā
'dhyāsā'sambhavamekasya darśayati—na khalvekaṃ vibaddhamavibaddhañca kalpate, ghaṭate |
codayati—viśeṣasya pratibandho na bhāvarūpasya | bhāva iti ṇyantādbhavatere
rajityaci kṛte bhāvanāmāha | pariharati—viśeṣarūpabhāvā'bhāvā'nuvidhānāt
pravṛttibhāvā'bhāvayoḥ | sa tarhi viśeṣo vidhiḥ | ato na bhāvo bhāvanā
vidhirupadeśaḥ preraṇamityekaṃ vastu | nidarśanīyaścāsau | śaṅkate—bhāvanāyāḥ
kartavyateti cet ? nirākaroti—na | tasyā eva bubhutsanāt | satyam, kartavyatā
vidhiḥ, saiva tu kīdṛśīti jijñāsyata ityarthaḥ | codayati—nanu kālabhedaparā
marśaviraha eva viśeṣo bhāvanāyāḥ kartavyatāvidhiḥ, tatkimanyena viśeṣeṇa ?
atraivā'nvayavyatirekau darśayati—vartamānādirūpāyā bhāvanāyā ananuṣṭheyatvā
ttadabhāve cā'nuṣṭheyatvapratītervidhinibandhanatvācca'nuṣṭheyatvasya kālabhedā
'parāmarśa eva bhāvanāyāḥ kartavyatā vidhiriti sāmpratam | tad dūṣayati—
bhavatveṣa viśeṣaḥ, na tu puruṣasya pravṛttihetuḥ, ataddhetuśca kathaṃ vidhiḥ ? nanva
nuṣṭheyatvā'vagatereva pravṛttiḥ sā ca bhāvanāyāḥ kālabhedā'parāmarśe āpata
tītyata āha—kāmaṃ pratyetu kālabhedaviviktāṃ bhāvanāmayaṃ pumān, anuṣṭhānaṃ
tu kuto hetoḥ ? nanu kālabhedā'parāmarśabhāvā'bhāvā'nuvidhānādanuṣṭheyatvā
'vagatibhāvā'bhāvayoḥ sa eva viśeṣaḥ pravṛttiheturityuktam, tatkiṃ hetupraśnena ?
ityata āha—na hi vasturūpamātrapratītiḥ pravṛttihetuḥ | kasmāt ? na hi ghaṭa
iti pratipadya tato ghaṭaśabdādarthakālabhedaparāmarśarahitaṃ tāvatyeva tasmin ghaṭe
pravartate | tasmādanvayo vyabhicārītyarthaḥ | śaṅkate—sādhyatayā pravartate iti
[p.29]
keyaṃ sādhyatā ? yadyasiddhatvam, khapuṣpam | mṛtpiṇḍā'vasthāyāṃ ca ghaṭo nāstī
tyapi prasaṅgaḥ | atha kārakaviṣayatvaṃ tairviṣayīkṛtāyāṃ pravṛttikārikāyāṃ
kimarthaṃ pravarteta ? sādhyāyāṃ cā'nena pravartitavyamiti kutastyam ? phalavattā
'vagamātpravartata iti cet ? | sa eva kutaḥ ? anuṣṭheyatvā'vagamāditi cet ?
na | siddhe'nuṣṭheyatve phalakalpanā tataścā'nuṣṭheyatvamitītaretarāśrayatvāt |
evamevā'pravṛttasyādyā pravṛttiḥ ākūtaṃ cikīrṣā adhyavasāyo vā liṅādyathaḥ |
uttarakālā pravṛttiḥ prayatno vā dehavacanaceṣṭā vā laṅādyartha iti
viśeṣā'bhidhānaṃ pratyūḍham | taddhetvabhāvāt | puruṣarūpamātrapratīterapravṛtteḥ,
hetvantarā'pratīteśca | tāṃ ca bhūtavartamānau pratibadhnīto, netaraḥ kālo,
bhaviṣyatyanuṣṭheyatvā'virodhāt |
cet ? vikalpya nirākaroti—keyaṃ sādhyatā ? ta tāvat kālabhedaparāmarśa
viraho, vyabhicārādityuktamadhastāt | tathā ca sati yadyasiddhatvamatyanta
prāgabhāvābhyāṃ khapuṣpam | mṛtpiṇḍādyavasthāyāñca ghaṭo nāstītyapi sādhya
miti pravṛttiprasaṅgaḥ | atha kārakaviṣayatvam, tairviṣayīkṛtāyāṃ pravṛttikāri
kāyāṃ kimarthaṃ pravarteta ? sādhanapravartakatvaṃ hi kartṛvyāpāraḥ; pravṛttāni
cetsādhanāni svavyāpāre kṛtaṃ tarhi pravartakene tyarthaḥ | api ca sādhyāyāñca
bhāvanāyāmanena prekṣāvatā hitā'hitaprāptiparihārārthinā pravartitavyamiti
kutastyam ? śaṅkate—phalavattā'vagamātpravartata iti cet ? nirakaroti—sa
evā'ntaraṅgatayā dhātvarthabhāvyāvaruddhāyāṃ bhavanmate kutaḥ ? anuṣṭheyatvāvagamā
diti cet ? na | siddhe'nuṣṭheyatve phalakalpanā, phalakalpane cā 'nuṣṭhānamitī
taretarāśrayam | evamevā'pravṛttasyā''dyā pravṛttiḥ | tāmeva vibhajate—ākūtaṃ
cikīrṣā'dhyavasāyo vā prayatnastatpūrvo liṅarthaḥ, uttarakālā pravṛttiḥ prayatno
vā dehavacanaceṣṭā vā laṅādyartha ityācāryadeśīyānāṃ viśeṣābhidhānaṃ pratyuktam |
evamityatidiṣṭaṃ hetumādarśayati—taddhetvabhāvāt | tadityādyāṃ pravṛttiṃ parā
mṛśati | sa eva kutaḥ ? puruṣasya rūpamātrapratīterapravṛtterhetvantarasya sādhyatāyā
vā phalasya vā'pratīteḥ | api ca sa nāma kālabhedaparāmarśastyajyatām, yaḥ
sādhyatāṃ vihanti bhāvanāyāḥ, atītavartamānau ca tathā; na tvanāgataḥ | tasya
tayā saha virodhā'siddheḥ | tena pakṣyatītyādāvanupahatasādhyabhāvā bhāvanā'va
gamyate iti vidhitvaprasaktirityāha—tāñca bhūtavartamānau pratibadhnīto,
netaraḥ kālo, bhaviṣyatyanuṣṭheyatvā'virodhāt |
[p.30]
yadapi samarthanam—aprāptasambandhayā kriyayā ātmanaḥ sambandhasya
pratītyā pravṛttiḥ yathā'dya tavedaṃ karmmeti loke | atrāpi tu ḷṅante śrotuḥ
pramāṇāntareṇā'nadhigatamarthamavagamayati vidhitvaprasaṅgaḥ |
na | nimittā'ntarā'prāpte tasya tadabhidhānaṃ, tatra nimittāntaraprāptasyaiva
bhaviṣyattvenā'bhidhānādarthasya liṅādibhistu tathā'bhidhānam | ataścā
'jñātajñāpanamapravṛttapravartanamubhayavidhaprāptipratiṣedhenā'prāptakriyākartṛsambandho
vidhiriti vidhividāmudgārāḥ |
naitatsāram | yasmāt—
na pravṛttiryogadhiyo loke'bhiprāyavedanāt |
mṛṣā bhavettathā kāmaṃ kiṃ mudhaiṣa prayasyati ||
yadapi samarthanam, aprāptasambandhayā kriyayā''tmanaḥ sambandhasya
pratītyā pravṛttiḥ | yathā'dya tavedaṃ karmeti loke | svāminā hi yadā bhṛtyaṃ
pratyucyate caitra! adya tava nagaragamanaṃ karmeti tadā khalvayaṃ caitra ātmano
nagaragamanasambandha svāmivacanādanyato'nadhigatamavagamya gamane pravartate,
tathehāpi svargakāmāderyāgādikriyāsambandhaṃ liṅāderanyato'nadhigatamavagamya
svargakāmasya yāgādau pravṛttirityaprāptakriyākartṛsambandha eva vidhirityarthaḥ |
ekadeśī tu prasaṅgamāpādayati—atrā'pi samarthane pakṣyatītyādau ḷṅante
śrotuḥ pramāṇāntareṇā'nadhigatamarthamavagamayati vacane vidhitvaprasaṅgaḥ |
pariharati codakaḥ—na | nimittāntarā'prāpte kriyākartṛsambandhe tasya
liṅādestadabhidhānam, sambandhābhidhānam, tatra ḷṅādau nimittāntaraprāptasyaiva
bhaviṣyattvena rūpeṇā'bhidhānādarthasya sambandhasya | liṅādibhistu tathā
'bhidhānaṃ nimittāntarā'prāptatayā'bhidhānam | aprāpte hi dvividhā prāptiḥ
pratiṣidhyate, utpattiḥ pramitiśca | tatra mānāntarā'nadhigataṃ sambandhama
vagamayati ḷṅante yadyapi pramitirūpā prāptirna samasti, samastyutpattirūpā,
nimittāntarādutpatsyamānasyaiva tasya tatrā'bhidhānāt | na tvevaṃ liṅante 
tadvākyārthā'vagatinibandhanaṃ puruṣaprayatnamantareṇā'nyato yāgādisambandhā
'siddherityarthaḥ | yata evamataścā'jñātajñāpanamapravṛttapravartanamubhayavidhaprāpti
pratiṣedhenā'prāptakriyākartṛsambandho vidhiriti vidhividāmudgārāḥ | tadetad
dūṣayati—naitatsāram | yasmāt—
na pravṛttiryogadhiyo loke'bhiprāyavedanāt |
mṛṣā bhavettathā kāmaṃ kiṃ mudhaiṣa prayasyati ||
[p.31]
pratipadyatāṃ nāmā'yamātmanaḥ kriyāyogaṃ śabdāt taṃ ca tathābhāve tatheti
niścinotu, viparyaye naitadevamiti | pravartate tu kasmāt ? loke tvadya tavedaṃ
karmati vacanādadhigatavaktrabhiprāyo yo yadabhiprāyā'nurodhī sa pravartitumarhati,
anyathā sarvasya pravṛtteḥ | nanvihāpi śabdā'nurodhī pravartsyati | na śabdā'nu
rodhe kiṃcana nimittam | nanvasti prāmāṇyam ? tacca pravṛtto samarthitaṃ bhavati |
anyathā mṛṣātvā''pātāt | katham ? bhūtavartamānayo sambandhayorabhāvāt |
bhaviṣyatyapi na cetpuruṣaḥ pravartate na tatsyāditi mṛṣātvāpātaḥ | puruṣa
pravṛttyā tarhi prāmāṇyam, bhavatu kāmaṃ mṛṣā, kimartho'yamasya śabdapramāṇī
karaṇaprayāsaḥ ? bahutaraṃ ca tasyaivamāyasitavyamāpatati | sakalamithyājñāna-
pratipadyatāṃ nāmā'yamātmanaḥ kriyayā yogaṃ śabdaḥ, tañca tathābhāve
mānāntareṇa saṃvāde ca tatheti niścinotu | viparyaye mānāntareṇa bādhe
naitadevamiti | pravarttate tu kasmāt ? nanūktaṃ yathā tavedaṃ karmeti loke, ityata
āha—loke tu adya tavedaṃ karmeti vacanādadhigatavaktrabhiprāyabhedo yo
yadabhiprāyā'nurodhī sa tadabhiprāyā'vagamāt pravartitumarhati | na hi tadā
kriyākartṛsambandhabodha pravartayati, api tvanuvidheyā'bhiprāyabheda ājñādiri
tyarthaḥ | kutaḥ ? anyathā yadi kriyākartṛsambandhabodhamātraṃ pravṛttihetuḥ, na
tvanuvidheyā'bhiprāyabhedabodhaḥ, tataḥ sambandhabodhaḥ sarvasādhāraṇa iti sarvasya
pravṛtteḥ | codayati—nanvihāpi śabdā'nurodhī pravartsyati | yathā loke
'bhiprāyānurodhī | dūṣayati—na śabdā'nurodhe kiñcinnimittam | yathā
'bhiprāyā'nurodhe'nuvihitabhāvaḥ khalvayaṃ svāmī hitaṃ tu bhṛtyāya prayacchati,
ahitaṃ vā na vidhatte | cādayati—nanvasti prāmāṇyam, pravṛttihetubhāvastasya
katham ? ityata āha—tacca pravṛttau samarthitaṃ bhavati | kasmāt ? anyathā
pravṛttyabhāve kartuḥ kriyāsambandhaviraha ātyantika iti tadabhidhāyino
vedarāśermṛṣātvā''pātāt | pṛcchati—kathaṃ punaḥ pravṛttyabhāve sambandhā
'bhāvaḥ ? uttaram—bhūtavartamānayoḥ sambandhayorupalambhā'rhayoranupalabdhera
bhāvāt | bhaviṣyatyapi sambandhe na cetpuruṣaḥ pravartate tatastasya kālatraya
viveke gaganakusumavadātyantikamasattvamiti tadviṣayasya na tatsyāditi
na tatprāmāṇyaṃ syāditi | so'yaṃ mṛṣātvāpātaḥ | tadetad dūṣayati—puruṣa
pravṛttyā tarhi prāmāṇyam | bhavatu ko doṣa ? ityata āha—tataḥ kāmaṃ
bhavatu mṛṣā śabdaḥ, kimartho'yamasya puṃsaḥ śabdapramāṇīkaraṇaprayāsaḥ ? na
khalvasya prekṣāvatastatpramāṇīkaraṇe hitaprāptirahitanivṛttirvā | yadi
[p.32]
pramāṇīkaraṇāt | nanu na śābdaṃ mithyājñānam | dvividhahetvabhāvāt | yadyapi
hetudoṣo na syāt, bādhakastu durvāraḥ | bhūtavartamānayoḥ saṃbandhayorupalabdhi
gocarayoranupalabdheḥ | bhaviṣyato hetvabhāvāt | śabdaprāmāṇyaṃ hetuḥ | na |
pravṛttau tasya bhāvāditaretarāśrayatvāt | nanu svataḥ prāmāṇyādvijñānasya
netaretarāśrayam | na | pravṛttihetvabhāvena tasyā'pavādāt | pramāṇasya
paricchedakatvātsvato'pravṛttikāraṇatvāt | pravṛttihetusadbhāvādeva prāmāṇya
siddheḥ |
nanu ca svataḥ prāmāṇyātpravṛttihetuḥ kalpayiṣyate tasmātpramitasiddhāvanya
paricchedāt | anyā'paricchittyā ca pramitatāhānāt | kaḥ ? phalam | na |
prāmāṇyasiddhimātramīhitaṃ tatra bahutaraṃ ca tasyaivamāyasitavyamāpatitam,
sakalamithyājñānapramāṇīkaraṇāt | codayati—nanu na śābdaṃ mithyājñānaṃ
dvividhahetvabhāvāt | tasmātprāmāṇyameva śabdasya puruṣapravṛttimākṣepsya
tītyarthaḥ | dūṣayati—yadyapyapauruṣeyatayā hetudoṣo na syād bādhastu
durnivāraḥ | kutaḥ ? bhūtavartamānayoḥ sambandhayorupalabdhigocarayorupalambha
kapratyayāntarasākalyasvabhāvaviśeṣaśālinīranupalabdheḥ | bhaviṣyatī hetva
bhāvāt | hetvabhāve ca kāryā'bhāvaḥ | anyathā kādācitkatvā'nupapatteriti
bhāvaḥ | codayati—śabdaprāmāṇyaṃ bhaviṣyataḥ sambandhasya heturasti | dūṣayati—
na | pravṛttau tasya prāmāṇyasya bhāvāt prāmāṇye tu sati pravṛttisiddheritareta
rāśrayāt | codayati—nanu svataḥ prāmāṇyādvijñānasya na pravṛttinibandhanaṃ
prāmāṇyamiti netaretarāśrayam | nirācaṣṭe—na | pravṛttihetvabhāvena tasya
prāmāṇyasyautsargikasyā'pyapavādāt | nanu pramāṇameva pravṛttihetuḥ, svataḥ
prāmāṇyā'nyathānupapattyā tadākṣepakatvādityata āha—pramāṇasya pariccheda
katvāt, svato'pravṛttikāraṇatvāt | pravṛttihetusadbhāvādeva prāmāṇyasiddheḥ |
idamākūtaṃ, ko'yamākṣepo nāma ? na tāvatprajñāpanam | tasyā ahetu
kāyāḥ khapuṣpavadatyantā'satyāḥ pravṛtteḥ prajñāpane śabdaprāmāṇyā'nupapatterarthā
dutpādakatvameṣitavyam | tatra cokto doṣaḥ | codayati—nanu ca svataḥ
prāmāṇyātpravṛttihetuḥ kalpayiṣyate | kasmāt ? svataḥ siddhapramāṇabhāvāt
pramāṇāt pramitasya siddhau tadanyathā'nupapattyā'nyasya tadupapādakasya
paricchedāt | anyathā'nupapattimeva pramitasya darśayati—anyā'paricchittyā
ca pramitatāhāniprasaṅgāt | ko'sāvanyaḥ pravṛttihetuḥ ? uttaraṃ phalam |
[p.33]
phalavatyapyarthino'pravṛtteḥ | kriyāsaṃbandhasyā'viśeṣeṇa pravṛtteḥ | kalpite'pi
tasmiṃstadanupapatteḥ |
nanu svargakāmādaya eva kriyāsambandhinaḥ śrūyante ? satyaṃ, na tu sarvatra
viśvajitā yajeta, yāvajjīvamagnihotraṃ juhuyāditi | ata eva sarvasyā
'bhilaṣitamekatra phalam anyatrā'pāyaparihāraḥ kalpyate | satyam | na tveva
mapi śrutopapattiḥ | tathā hi—
arthino'pi niyogena na kriyā na nimittinaḥ |
ālasyādantarāyebhyo vihitā'kṛtyabhāvataḥ ||
na khalu phalakāmo nimittavānvā'pāyaparihāraprayojano niyogataḥ
kriyāsambandhamanubhavati, yena śrutakriyāsambandhopapattiḥ syāt phalā'pāya
parihārakalpanayoḥ satoḥ | yato hyavaśyaṃ kriyāsambandhaḥ sa hetustamupapādayituṃ
kṣamate | kathaṃ punaḥ phalakāmasyā'pāyabhīrorvā na kriyā ? alasatayā | na
tadetad dūṣayati—na phalavatyapyarthino'pravṛtteḥ | nanu mā bhūdarnārthanaḥ pravṛttiḥ
arthī tu pravartsyatītyata āha—kriyāsambandhasyā'viśeṣeṇā'rthino'narthinaśca
pravṛtteḥ | kalpite'pi phale tasminnaviśeṣapravṛttasya kriyāsambandhasyā
'nupapatteḥ | pramitaṃ khalvanupapadyamānamupapādakamupakalpayati, na punaranupapadyamāna
mātramanupapādakam | anupapādakañca phalamaviśeṣapravṛttasya kriyākartṛ
sambandhasyetyarthaḥ—codayati | nanu svargakāmādaya eva kriyāsambandhinaḥ śrūyante
na tu puruṣamātram | dūṣayati—satyam, kvacinna tu sarvatra | viśvajitā yajeta,
yāvañjīvamagnihotraṃ juhuyāditi | codaka āha—ata eva sarvasyā'bhilaṣita
mekatra viśvajiti svargaḥ phalamanyatra naimittike'pāyaparihāraḥ prayojanaṃ
kalpyate, na phalaṃ tatkāmasyā'nadhikārāditi sarvaśaktau pravedayiṣyate | tade
tad dūṣayati—satyam, na tvevamapi śrutopapattiḥ | tathā hi—
arthino'pi niyogena na kriyā na nimittinaḥ |
ālasyādantarāyebhyo vihitā'kṛtyabhāvataḥ ||
na khalu phalakāmo nimittavānvā'pāyaparihāraprayojanaḥ kartā niyo
gataḥ kriyāsambandhamanubhavati prāpnoti, yena śrutakriyāsambandhopapattiḥ syāt,
phalopāpayarihārakalpanayoḥ satoḥ | kasmātpunarna syād ? ityata āha—yato
hyavaśyaṃ kriyāsambandhaḥ sa hetustamupapādayituṃ kṣamate—pṛcchati | kathaṃ punaḥ
phalakāmasya viśvajidādāvapāyabhīrorvā naimittike nāvaśyaṃbhāvinī kriyā ?
[p.34]
tarhyarthitā | na pratyātmavedyā'rthitā kriyā'bhāvena śakyanihnavā | tathā
maraṇādibhyo'ntarāyebhyo'pravṛttiḥ | yadi ca niyogataḥ pravṛttiḥ, vihitā
'karaṇā'bhāvaprasaṅgaḥ | api ca—
nimittāntarataḥ prāptirmohādeśca prakalpate |
na copadeśakaḥ śabda udīkṣeta tataḥ pumān ||
phale hi pravṛttihetau kalpyamāne na śabdanimittaḥ kriyāsambandhaḥ, api tu
śābdapariprāpita iti nimittāntarā'prāptatā'nupapattiḥ | pramāṇāntarā'nadhi
gatatvādupapattiryadi; ḷḍante'pi prasaṅga ityuktam | api ca mohayadṛcchādau
sambhavini kriyāsambandhahetau naikāntataḥ phalavattvaṃ parikalpyam | śāstrasya
puruṣaśreyo'bhidhāyakatvātphalakalpaneti cet | na | aśāstratvāt | upadeśakaṃ
uttaram—alasatayā | praṣṭurvacanaṃ na tarhyarthitā | na hyasti sambhavastadarthitā ca
tadakriyā cetyarthaḥ | uttaram—na pratyātmasaṃvedyā manasā saṃyuktasamavāyādiṣṭā
'rthitā kriyābhāvenā'numānena śakyanihnavā | anumānasya mānasapratyakṣabādhita
viṣayatayā'nutpādādirtthaḥ | tathā maraṇādibhyo'ntarāyebhyo'rthino'pravṛttiḥ |
yadi ca niyogataḥ pravṛttistato vihitā'karaṇā'bhāvaprasaṅgaḥ | tathā
cā'kurvanvihitaṃ karmeti nirviṣayaṃ prasajyetetyuktam | api ca—
nimittāntarataḥ prāptirmohādeśca prakalpate |
na copadeśakaḥ śabda udīkṣeta tataḥ pumān ||
phale hi pravṛttihetau kalpyamāne sati na śabdanibandhanaḥ kriyayā sambandha
prasaṅgo api tu śābdaphalapariprāpita iti nimittāntarā'prāptatā'nupa
pattiḥ | yadi manyeta, bhavatu phalaṃ nimittamasyotpādakatayā, jñāpakatayā tu
sambandhasyā'nyato'prajñātasya śabdo nimittaṃ bhaviṣyatīti śaṅkate—pramāṇā
ntarā'nadhigatatvādupapattiryadīti | nirākaroti—ḷṅante'pi vidhitva
prasaṅga ityuktam | api ca mohayadṛcchādau sambhavini kriyāsambandhahetau
naikāntataḥ phalavattvaṃ kriyāyāḥ śakyaparikalpanam | auṣadhādāviva mohādapi
pravṛtterupapatteḥ | kriyākartṛsambandhasya yadṛcchāheturapi kriyā karakisalaya
kalitalīlākamaladalasaṅkalanādilakṣaṇā lokasiddhaiva | śaṅkate—śāstrasya
puruṣaśreyo'vidhāyakatvāt mohayadṛcchādipariharaṇāt phalakalpaneti cet ?
nirākaroti—na | aśāstratvāt | tad darśayati—upadeśakaṃ hi śāstram,
[p.35]
hi śāstram | na caiteṣāmupadeśakā yajetetyādayaḥ śabdāḥ | na hyebhiridaṃ
kurvityupadiśyate | api tu svargakāmāderyāgasambandho'vagamyate, daṇḍyādi
śrutibhiribādaṇḍādisambandhaḥ | sa ca bhūto vartamāno vā 'nupalabdhinirākṛta
iti bhaviṣyannavaśiṣyate |
api caivaṃ na pratyayapūrvaṃ puruṣaḥ pravartate | sāmudravidākhyātamivā'nirūpita
nimittaṃ kriyāsambandhamudīkṣeta | tathā ca vacanavaiyarthyam, puruṣakārā'bhāvāt |
yadapi darśanam—pramāṇāntarā'gocaraḥ śabdamātrā''lambano niyukto
'smīti pratyātmavedanīyaḥ sukhādivat aparāmṛṣṭakālatrayo liṅādīnāmartho
vidhiriti |
na caiteṣāmupadeśakā yajetetyādayaḥ śabdāḥ, yasmānna tairidaṃ kurvityupadiśyate,
api tu svargakāmāderyāgena sambandho'vagamyate yajetetyādiśabdaiḥ | gamyata
iti ṇici rūpam | daṇḍyādiśrutibhirivā'nupadeśikābhirdaṇḍādisambandho
devadattādeḥ | sa ca bhūto vartamāno vā'nupalabdhinirākṛta iti bhaviṣyannava
śiṣyate | tatra ca phalamiva mohayadṛcchādayo'pi hetavaḥ sambhavina iti
naikāntataḥ phalakalpanāsambhava iti |
api caivaṃ na pratyayapūrva puruṣaḥ pravartate yathopadeśakādidaṃ kurviti vākyāt,
kintu sāmudravidākhyātamivā'nirūpitanimittaviśeṣaṃ kriyāsambandhamudīkṣeta |
nanūdīkṣatām ko doṣa ? ityata āha—tathā ca vacanavaiyarthyam | kutaḥ ?
puruṣakārā'bhāvāt | yatra hi vacanādartha pratipadya pravartamānaḥ samīhitaṃ
kiñcidāpnoti tatrārtho vacanasya, tadabhāve tvapratīterapravṛtteḥ | samīhita
prāptyanupapatteḥ yadā tu vacanādartha pratipadyā'pi puṃsodīkṣitavyam, na
pravartitavyam, tadā'narthakaṃ vacanamityarthaḥ |
samprati ṭīkākārīyaṃ matamupanyasyati—yadapi darśanam pramāṇāntarā
'gocaro na ca niṣpramāṇakaḥ | yataḥ śabdamātrālambanaḥ, śabdamātramālambana
māśrayaḥ pratipādakatayā yasya sa tathoktaḥ | kathampunarasya śabdapratipādyatvam ?
ityata āha—yato liṅādiyuktavākyaśravaṇasamanantaraṃ svargakāmādinā
niyojyena niyukto 'smīti pratyātmavedanīyaḥ sukhādivat | yathā hi santāpa
dūnaścandanā'nulepanā'nantaramāntaramapi manomātrapravedanīyamāmīlitalocanaḥ
sukhabhedamananyapramāṇakamanubhavati, evaṃ niyogamapi liṅādivākyaśravaṇā
'nantaramityarthaḥ | pramāṇāntarā'gocaratve hetumāha—aparāmṛṣṭakālatrayaḥ |
[p.36]
atrocyate—
padārtha eva vākyārtho na ca so'nanyagocaraḥ |
nā'kartṛkā kriyā kālaviviktañca khapuṣpavat ||
sa khalu śabdapramāṇako vākyārthaḥ padārtho vā | tatra padārthasyaiva padārthā
ntaropakalpitaviśeṣasya vākyārthatvādapadārthatve tadanupapattiḥ | sambandha
vākyārthavādino'pi tadviśeṣo vākyārthaḥ sambandhinaśca padārthā eveti nā'pa-
sadupalambhanāni hi pratyakṣādīni mānāntarāṇi, kālabhedasambhedaśca sattā 
tadviṣayāṇām, ayaṃ punarniyogo'tītā'nāgatavartamānādīnāmanyatamenāpyātmanā
na parāmṛśyata iti kāryatayā na mānāntaragocaraḥ | kāryapratipakṣatayā ca
vidyamānopalambhanatvam, nā'tītā'nāgatapratipakṣatayā | tenā'numānādiṣvatī
tā'nāgataviṣayeṣvapi tadastīti na bhāgā'siddhatā hetoḥ | so'yaṃ liṅādīnā
martho niyogo vidhiḥ | evaṃvidhārthatve ca codanāyā anapekṣatayā prāmāṇya
siddhiriti |
acocyate—
padārtha eva vākyārtho na ca so'nanyagocaraḥ |
nā'kartṛkā kriyā kālaviviktañca khapuṣpavat ||
sa khalu śabdapramāṇako niyogo vākyārthaḥ padārtho vā syāt |
apadārthasya tasya vākyārthatvaṃ dūṣayati—tatra padārthasyaivaikasyetarapadārthāntaropa
kalpitaviśeṣasya vākyārthatvālloke tathā darśanāt | apadārthatve tadanupapattiḥ |
sambandhavākyārthavādino'pi mate na tāvatsambandhamātraṃ vākyārthaḥ | tasya
viśeṣanibandhanavyavahārā'naṅgatayā vākyaśrāviṇaḥ kvacit kutaścit
pravṛttinivṛttyoranupapatteḥ | tasmādetadviśeṣo vākyārthaḥ | na ca niyogasta
dviśeṣaḥ, asambandharūpatvāt | sambandhaśca viśeṣyamāṇaḥ sambandhibhirvi
śeṣaṇīyaḥ | tasya sambandhinirūpaṇatayā svarūpeṇa viśeṣā'nupapatteḥ |
sambandhinaścāsya padārthā iti niyogenāpi sambandhinā satā padārthenaiva
bhavitavyam, nā'padārthena | na ca sambandhamātraṃ vākyārtha ityāha—
sambandhamātramaviśiṣṭaṃ sambandhibhiḥ padārthaiḥ padārtha eva sambandhā
'nvayavyatiṣaṅgādipadagocaramātrameva, na tu viśiṣṭavyavahārārhamityuktamiti |
tasmātsambandhavākyārthavādino'pi mate nā'padārtho niyogo vākyārtha
upapadyate | apadārthasyāpi sambandhabhedasyaiva vākyārthatvāt | apadārthabhūtasya
[p.37]
dārtho vākyārthaḥ |
astu tarhi padārthaḥ ? naivam | ananyapramāṇagocarasya tattvā'navakḷpteḥ |
sambandhasaṃvedanasāpekṣatvāt | tasya ca sambandhiparicchedapuraḥsaratvāt |
pramāṇādṛte ca tasyā'yogāt |
nanvasti śabdaḥ pramāṇam ? na | itaretarāśrayatvāt | pramite hi śabdena
niyoge sambandhagrahaḥ, sati ca tasmin śabdena tasya pramā | avidinasaṅgateḥ
padasya prakṛteḥ pratyayasya cā'pratyāyakatvāt |
niyogasyā'sambandhitayā tadavacchedakatvā'nupapatterityuktamityarthaḥ |
dvitīyaṃ vikalpamabalambate—astu tarhiṃ padārthaḥ | nirākaroti—
naivam | ananyapramāṇagocarasya tattvā'navakḷpteḥ | padārthatvā'nupapatteḥ |
kutaḥ sambandhasaṃvedanasāpekṣatvātpadārthatvasya | bhavatu saṃvedanasāpekṣatā, tataḥ
kim ? ityata āha—tasya ca sambandhasaṃvedanasya sambandhiparicchedapurassara
tvāt | evamapi kim ? ityāha—pramāṇādṛte kāryasambandhipariccheda
syā'yogāt | paricchedo hi pramāṇasya phalam, tanna tadabhāve bhavitumarhati,
aṅkura iva vījābhāve ityarthaḥ | codayati—nanvasti śabdaḥ pramāṇam |
pariharati—netaretarāśrayatvāt | tad darśayati—pramite hi śabdena niyoge tena
saha sambandhagrahaḥ, sati ca tasmin sambandhagrahe śabdena tasya niyogasya pramā |
yastu manyate, asti khalu liṅādestādṛśaḥ sāmarthyā'tiśayaḥ, yenā'gṛhīta
sambandho vilakṣaṇaviṣayaḥ katipayaśravaṇasahakārī janayati vilakṣaṇa
viṣayamavabodham | katipayaśravaṇasamanantaraṃ tadbhāvāt | na cā'nyenā'gṛhīta
saṅgatitayā śabdāḥ svārthamabhidadhatītyanenāpi tathā bhavitavyam | mā
bhūdanyeṣāṃ na sākṣātkāryārthatetyasyāpi tathābhāvaḥ |
tannirākaroti—pramāntarā'viditasaṅgateḥ padasya prakṛteḥ pratyayasya
cā'pratyāyakatvāt | ayamabhisandhiḥ | prathamaśravaṇādiva saṃbandhā'grahe śataśo
'pi śrutāt liṅādeviṃlakṣaṇaviṣayabodhā'nutpatteḥ, vyavahāradarśanasya cā'rtha
rūpasambandhidarśanagarbhatayotpatterupapatternā'dṛṣṭacarasāmarthyā'tiśayaparikalpanādau
naisargika śabdamātravarttisambandhā'pekṣā'pavādo'sati balavadbādhakopanipāte
yujyata iti |
[p.38]
praiṣādīnāṃ loke pratītestadgatasya tadupādheḥ sambandhagrahaḥ |
atra codayati—praiṣādīnāṃ loke pratītestadgatasya tadupādheḥ sambandhagrahaḥ |
idamākūtam | kāryā'bhidhāyitā tāvalliṅādīnāmavagatā ācāryavākyeṣu
māṇavaka! samidhamāharetyādiṣu | tathā hi | etadvākyaśravaṇā'nantaraṃ pravartamānaṃ
samidāharaṇe māṇavakamupalabhya pārśvastho vyutpitsurevamavadhārayati, buddhipūrveya
masya pravṛttiḥ, svatantrapravṛttitvāt, madīyapravṛttivat, yaccāhaṃ buddhvā pravṛttasta
devāyaṃ madaviśeṣāt | ahaṃ ca na kriyāmātrā'vagamānnāpi phalamātrāva
gamānnāpi phalasādhanatvā'vagamāt; kintu kāryatā'vagamāt | na khalvantato
'rbhakaḥ stanapānādikāmapi kriyāṃ kāryatayā yāvannā'vagatavānasmi na tāva
ttasyāmapi pravṛttaḥ | na ca yā śreyaḥsādhanatā saiva kāryatā | parasparapari
hārasthitilakṣaṇavirodhādhāratvāt | na hyasti saṃbhavaḥ, yadeva samīhataṃ prati
sādhanatvaṃ tadeva kṛtiṃ puruṣaprayatnaṃ prati sādhyatvamiti | kleśātmanastu
karmaṇaḥ phalasādhanatā'nvayinī kāryateti kecidabhedaṃ pratītavantaḥ | tathā
sukhasya phalasādhanatāṃ vinā ca kāryatā'vagatirasti | ataḥ phalasādhanatā
'tiriktakāryatābodhāt pravṛttirmameti māṇavako'pi tadavabodhādeva pravṛtta
iti niścinoti | tadababodhaścāsyā''cāryavacanā'nantaramupajāyamānastanni
bandhana eveti kāryā'bhidhāyitāṃ śabdasya kalpayati tacca kāryyamanvitama
bhidheyamiti sthitau satyām agnihotraṃ juhuyātsvargakāma ityādiṣu vākyeṣu
ṣaṣṭhādyarāddhāntā'nusāreṇa svargakāmapadena niyojyatayā svargakāmaḥ samarpaṇīya
iti sthitam | yaśca kāryamavabudhyate sa niyojyaḥ | svargakāmaśca sādhyasvarga
kāmaviśiṣṭaḥ | sa ca tasminneva kārye niyojyatayā'nveti, yadeva tasya
kāmyopāyatāmanubhavituṃ śaknoti | na ca kriyā kṣaṇabhaṅginī kālāntarabhāvi
phalaheturiti na kāryyatayā niyojyena sahā'nveti | yatkālāntarasthāyi kāryaṃ
tadeva tena sahā'nvayaṃ gantumarhatāti kriyā'tirekimānāntarā'vedyameva liṅādayaḥ
kāryamabhidadhatīti | tacca mānāntarā'pūrvatayā apūrvamiti ca kāryatayā
'vagamyamānamātmani puruṣaṃ niyuñjānaṃ niyoga iti ca vyapadiśyate | na ca
kriyā'nantaraṃ bhavadapi deśakālādisahakāryapekṣayā anantarameva svargādi
bhāvayati | na ca phalajanakatayā guṇabhāvādapūrvasya vākyārthatvahāniḥ, svā'nu
guṇaniyojyalābhāya phalānukūlatā'valambanātsvamivat | yathā''tmana eva
saṃvidadhānaḥ svāmī garbhadāsasyopakaroti, tathaitadapi | na caivaṃ vede eva
[p.39]
teṣāmeva tarhi pratīteḥ sambandhajñānaṃ na niyogasya | upādhayaste na
śabdārthāḥ, vyabhicārāt | pravartakasteṣvanuyāyī śabdārthaḥ | sarvatrā'pari
tyāgāt |
naitatsāram | upādhayaścenmānāntarasiddhāḥ naikasteṣvanvayī pravartako
vyutpattiḥ | lokavyutpattereva vedavākyā'nusāreṇa apūrvaparyantagamanāt | na
cā'nekārthā'bhidhāyitā liṅādīnāṃ na pratīyate | vadati hi māṇavaka ācārya
coditaḥ pravarttitaḥ kāryamavabodhita iti yāvat | na khalvanyathā cetanaḥ
pravartayituṃ śakyate | yatastadevaṃ praiṣādīnāṃ loke pratīteḥ kāryā'vabodhamantareṇa ca
pravṛttyanupapattestadgatasya sambandhagrahastadupagrahamapīdya ca vede paryavasānāt |
nanu praiṣādayo'pyavagamyante loke, tatkathaṃ kāryā'bhidhāyakatvameva
liṅādīnām ? ityata āha—tadupādheḥ | praiṣādyupādheḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati,
upādhaya ete, na tu śabdārthāḥ | ta eva copādhayaḥ ye taṭasthāḥ prayogadarśana
mātrācchabdārthaṃ viśeṣe vyavasthāpayanti, yathā haripadā'bhidheyasya paśutva
mupādhiḥ | tathā hi | paśutvaṃ taṭasthamevā'bhidhānā'viniveśāditi haripadārtha
iti hartṛtvaṃ paśāveva niyacchati | evaṃ praiṣādayo'pyabhidhānā'viniveśina
eva kārya viśeṣe vyavasthāpayanti, na punarabhidheyā iti sambandhagrahaṇasambha
vādupannaṃ niyogā'bhidhānaṃ liṅādīnāmiti |
tadetad dūṣayati—teṣāṃ praiṣādīnāmeva tarhi pratīteḥ sambandhajñānaṃ na
niyogasyā'pūrvasya | nanūktamupādhayaste na śabdārthāḥ, vyabhicārāt | na hi
praiṣādayo liṅarthāḥ, samaṃ pratyāmantraṇe'pi tasyāprayogāt | nāmantraṇām,
jyāyāṃsaṃ pratyadhyeṣaṇe'pi prayogāt | nā'dhyeṣaṇam, hīnaṃ prati preṣaṇe'pi
prayogāt | so'yaṃ vyabhicāraḥ |
pravartakasteṣvanuyāyī śabdārthaḥ | kutaḥ ? sarvatra'parityāgāt | sarveṣveva
praiṣādiṣu kartavyataikā saṃbhavet | anyathā svatantrapravṛtteranupapatterityuktam |
tasmātkarttavyatā'bhidhāyino liṅādayaḥ svargakāmapadasambandhānniyojyaviśeṣa
ṇībhūtasādhyasvargādyanukūlasyā''śutaravināśiyāgādyatiriktasyā'pūrvasyā'bhidhā
yinaḥ | tadevaṃ pramāṇāntarā'gocaratā liṅādyarthatā niyogasyeti siddham |
tad dūṣayati—naitatsāram | upādhayaścenmānāntarasiddhā, naikasteṣvanvayī
[p.40]
niyogaḥ | pramāṇāntaragocaratvātteṣāṃ sambandhajñānaṃ na tasya | avidita
viṣayasya sambandhasya pratyetumaśakteḥ | ta eva ca śabdārthā viditasaṅgati
tvānnetare |
pravartako niyogaḥ | apūrvamiti yāvat | ataḥ pramāṇāntaragocaratvātteṣā
mupādhīnāmeva sambandhajñānaṃ liṅādivācyatvajñānam | na tasyā'pūrvāparanāmno
niyogasya | kutaḥ ? aviditaviṣayasya sambandhasya pratyetumaśakteḥ |
bhavatūpādhiṣu sambandhajñānam, mā ca bhūdapūrve, kimetāvatā ? ityata āha—
ta eva śabdārthā viditaniyogasaṃgatitvāt netare niyogasaṅgatisaṃvedanavira
hāt | idamatrākūtam | satyaṃ kartavyatā'bhidhāyino liṅādayaḥ, kartavyatā
tu kriyāyā eva, nā'pūrvasya, lokādhīnā'vadhāraṇatvācchabdasāmarthyasya |
tasyā eva ca kāryatayā pravṛttihetutvena svātmani mānāntareṇa viditatvāt |
nanvapūrvasya tasya mānāntarā'gocaratvāt kartavyatāmātraviditasaṅgatīnāṃ
loke liṅādīnāṃ svargakāmapadasambandhādvede'pūrvaviśeṣapratilambha iti cet ?
astu tāvadasyaiva tu kartavyasya liṅādyarthatā, na punaḥ kriyāyā api laukikyā
iti kutastyam ? anekārthatvasyā'nyāyyatvāt | lakṣaṇasyāpi ca karta
vyāyāṃ kriyāyāṃ laukikasya liṅādīnāṃ prayogasyopapatterapūrvakartavyatā
nāntarīyakatvāt | kriyākartavyatāyā asādhitāyāḥ kriyāyāstatsā
