dhanatvā'nupapatteḥ | laukikaprayogā'virodhāt | apūrvakartavyatāyāstu
laukikakriyākartavyātānāntarīyakatvā'nupalabdhervaiparītyā'nupapatteratyantaprayo
gābhyāsāllākṣaṇikasyāpi vācakatvā'bhimānādapūrvā'bhidhānaniyogasiddhariti
cet ? nanvanekārthatvamanyāyyamityetadeva kutaḥ ? anekaśaktikalpanāgauravā
diti cet ? nanvapūrvamapi naikavidhapradhānāṅgavarti nāneti kathamaikārthya
liṅaḥ ? kṛtivyāpyatāyāḥ pravṛttinimittasya sarvatrā'viśeṣādrūpabhede'pyapūrvā
ṇāmekaśaktirasyā liṅa ekahāyanyādiśabdavaditi cet ? hanta bhostapa
svinyā kriyayā kimaparāddham ? yadasyāṃ kartavyatārūpāyāmabhidhānāyakaḥ
patiriva liṅādiḥ śaktyantaramutkocaṃ rocayate | śabdasāmye'pi kartavyatā
bhedādayamadoṣa iti cet ? na | bhedā'bhāvāt |
yadi manyeta, yadyapi kartavyatāśabdasāmyaṃ tathā'pi kṛtiṃ pratīpsita
tamatā kartavyatā'pūrvasya, taduddeśena puruṣaprayatnasyeti kṛteḥ pravṛtteḥ saiva ca
liṅādipravṛttinimittam | na caivaṃrūpā kriyākartavyatā, tasyā duḥkhatayā tāṃ
[p.41]
prati pradhānabhāvā'nupapatteḥ odanādyarthapravṛttakṛtivyāpyatālakṣaṇaudanādi
hetostasyāḥ kartavyatā, sā tvanyatvānna śaktyantaramantareṇa liṅādigocara
iti | tanna | viśeṣā'bhāvāt kriyākartavyatāyāḥ | na khalvapūrvamuddiśya
kṛtiḥ pravartitumarhati | sā hi puruṣaprayatnaḥ | sa cecchānuśayīti tadevo
ddiśyeta | yadicchākarma tadeva ca tasyāḥ karma yatsukhaṃ sukhānuśayitvādi
cchāyāḥ | na cā'pūrva sukhamiti kathamiṣṭam ? aniṣṭaṃ cet kathaṃ taduddeśena
kṛteḥ pravṛttiḥ ? śabdāttathā'vagateḥ | asukhamapyaniṣṭhamapyapūrva kṛtiṃ prati
pradhānamita cet ? tatkiṃ śabdo'vagamayatītyanagnirapyastu dhūmaḥ ? ānanda
svabhāvaṃ vā vijñānam, vijñānamānandamiti śruteḥ | pramāṇāntaravirodhānnaiva
miti cet ? na | ihā'pi tasya sāmyāt | samidādiviṣayasyā'pūrvasyā
'nanuṣṭheyasyā'nuṣṭheyā'dhikārā'pūrvaparatvena kartavyatātvā'bhyupagamanāt |
svargādyanukūlatvādapūrvasyā'sukhasyāpīcchāviṣayatvamiti kuto mānāntaravirodha
iti cet ? na | kriyāyāmapi samīhitasādhanatāyāstathābhāvāt | naimittika
niṣedhā'pūrvayośca phalavirahiṇostadanupapatteḥ | evaṃ ca naimittikaniṣedhā
'pūrvayornā'pūrvasiddhirliṅādīnām | nimittavato niṣidhyamānakriyākartuśca
siddhaviśeṣaṇāvacchinnasya niyojyasya kriyākartavyatā'vagamā'virodhāt |
pradhānā'ṅgagrāmasahabhāvasyā'śabdārthabhūtakalpitā'pūrvadvāreṇāpyupapatteḥ | api ca
sādhyasvargaviśiṣṭo 'pi niyojyaḥ kasmāt kriyāmeva nāvaiti kartavyām ?
tasyā āśutaravināśinītvena tadanukūlatvā'bhāvāt | taddhi tadutpādā'nukūlaṃ
yasmin sati yadutpadyate, nā'sati | na cā''muṣmikaṃ svargādi sati karmaṇi
bhavati ciravinaṣṭe | tasmiṃstadupapatteriti cet ? kimidānīmāgneyādiṣvapi
na sādhanatvamadhikārā'pūrvasya, bhākto vā sādhanabhāvasteṣām ? na hi tasya
bhāktatve itikartavyatāsambandho ghaṭate | na jātūpacaritavahnibhāvo māṇavako
dāhapākayorupayujyate | teṣāṃ ca viṣayatvamekaṃ nānā ca karaṇatvaṃ bhavadbhirabhyu
peyate | tayorbhinnaprasthānopadhārūpatvāt | pratipattyanubandho hi viṣayo 
yathāpratīti vyavatiṣṭhate | tatroccāritāścā''gneyādayo darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ
svargakāmo yajeteti sakṛdavagatestathaiva viṣayabhāvamanubhavantītyekamevaiṣāma
dhikārā'pūrva prati viṣayatvam utpattyanubandhastu karaṇatvam | sa ca svarūpā
'dhīnaḥ, na pratipattyadhīnaḥ | svarūpaṃ caiṣāmāgneyādīnāṃ yadāgneyo 'ṣṭākapāla
ityādibhyo bhinnabhāvamavagatamiti tadanuvartinī sādhanatā'pi bhinnaiva |
[p.42]
na caivamanyonyanirapekṣāṇāmupāyabhāvaḥ | yathāśrutakaraṇā'dhīnasiddhitvāt
paramā'pūrvasya | bahūni cā'sya karaṇāni śrūyante, iti naikopāyasādhyatā
bhavitumarhati | anekakaraṇasādhyatvaṃ caikasyā api kriyāyā loke'pi
dṛṣṭameva | yathā'śvena dīpikayā khaṅgapāṇibhiragresaraiḥ saṃcarate rājā pradoṣa
iti | avāntaravyāpārabhedādaśvādīnāṃ sādhanānāṃ bheda iti cet ?
ihāpyutpattyapūrvāṇyevā'vāntaravyāpārāḥ | te ca pratyāgneyādi bhidyante iti
samānam | evaṃ cā''jyauṣadhasāṃnāyyeṣu na saṅkaro dharmāṇām | paramā'pūrva
prayuktatve'pyavāntaravyāpārotpattyapūrvabhedena sādhanabhāvasya bhedādapūrvasādhana
niveśitatvācca teṣām | anyathā tadabhede saṅkaraprasaṅgāt | tasmādyathaivādhi
kārā'pūrvotpattisamaye āgneyādīnāmasatāmapi śabdā'vagatasādhanabhāvanā
mutpattyapūrvāṇyavāntaravyāpārāḥ śaktayo vā tathā phalaṃ pratyavagatasādhanabhāvāyāḥ
kriyāyā avāntaravyāpāraḥ śaktirvā apūrva bhaviṣyati | kathamanyasamavetā
'nyasya śaktiḥ, asati vā vyāpāravatyavāntaravyāpāra iti cet ? āgneyā
dīnāmapi paramā'pūrvasādhanānāmavāntaravyāpāreṣu śaktiṣu votpattyapūrveṣu
tulyatvātparyanuyogasya | anapekṣaśabdā'vagatasādhanabhāvanāmāgneyādīnāmasaha
bhuvāmanyasamavetānyapyasamānakālānyapyutpattyapūrvāṇi vyāpārāḥ sāmarthya veti
cet ? tatkiṃ darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ svargakāmo yajete-ti lokā'nusārataḥ
sādhyasvargavaiśiṣṭyena kriyā kāryā nā'vagamyate, svaviśeṣaṇaṃ vā sādhyarūpaṃ
pratyayasyā'nānukūlyam, yena tadghaṭanāyā'vāntaravyāpāraḥ sāmarthya vā kiyāyā
nā'pūrvamāsthīyeta ?
phalasādhanasādhanaṃ na phalasādhanaṃ kriyetyapi mithyā | phalapravṛttāyā eva
tasyāstatsādhanatvāt | yathā paramā'pūrvasādhanapravṛttānāmāgneyādīnāmutpa
ttyapūrvasādhanatvam, sasyāgamo ddeśena pravṛttasyeva kṛṣikarmaṇo bhūpāṭanādi
sādhanatvam | na ca paramā'pūrvaṃ pratyutpattyapūrvāṇāmeva sādhanatvam, nā''gneyādī
nāmiti sāmpratam | na hi darśapūrṇamāsābhyāmiti ca yajeteti cotpattya
pūrvāṇāmabhidhānamapi tu ṣaṇṇāmāgneyādīnāṃ kālaviśeṣasambandhenotpannā
nāmanūditānāṃ samudāyinām | na caiṣāmutpattivākyānyeva yathāsvamutpattya
pūrvāṇi prati kāraṇatvamadhigamayitumīśate | adhikāravākyasannidhisamāmnā
tānāṃ tadutpattivākyānāmadhikārā'pūrvapratyabhijñāśaṅkayā drāgivā'pūrvāntara
pratyayā'jananāt | utpannānūditānāṃ tu paścādāgneyādīnāmadhikārā'pūrvaṃ
[p.43]
atha mānāntarātsiddhiḥ ? na | mānāntaraviṣayatvenā'napekṣatvasiddheḥ |
api ca niyoktṛvyāpāro niyogo na niyokturvinā avakalpate | na
pratyadhigataviṣayakaraṇa bhāvānāmaparasparaṃ sambhavatāṃ tadanupapatteḥ | tatsiddhyartha
mutpattyapūrvāṇi kalpitāni pratyayavācyānyapi kāryarūpāṇyapi nā''gneyā
dīnāṃ sādhanānāṃ pradhānaṃ prati svakaraṇatāmāpādayitumarhanti | tairāgneyādibhiḥ
paramā'pūrve janayitavye'vāntaravyāpāratayā tatkalpanāt | tathaiva ca liṅartha
tvavyavasthāpanāt prādhānyā'nupapatteḥ | pradhānadvayasambandhasyaikatra duradhigama
tvāt | tasmādyathā tavā''gneyādīnāmutpattyapūrvā'vāntaravyāpārāṇāṃ paramā
'pūrva phalaṃ caikakṛtikarma tathā vivṛddhaṃ sādhyaṃ pratyeka eva karaṇabhāvaḥ, tathā
mamāpyapūrvā'vāntaravyāpārāyā eva kriyāyā api svargā'nukūlateti sādhya
svargaviśiṣṭo'pi śakta eva kriyākartavyatāmavaboddhum | na ca yo yatkāmaḥ
sa tadupāyameva kāryyamavaiti, nā'nupāyamityasyaikāntikatvamiti niveda
yiṣyate | darśitaṃ ca bhāvanāviveke | kartṛsamīhitopāyasya kartavyatājñānā
lambanatāyāṃ phale cā'prasaṅga ityupariṣṭānnivedayiṣyate | tasmānna svarga
kāmādisambandhānmānāntarā'pūrvā'bhidhānasiddhirliṅādīnām |
nā'pi loke māṇavaka! sandhyāmupāsvetyācāryavacanā'nantaraṃ māṇavakasya
pravṛttimupalabhya tasyāśca kāryā'bagamā'dhīnatvātsandhyopāsanakarmaṇaśca
duḥkhatayā tadanupapatteḥ tadatirikte kārye mānāntarā'vedye liṅādeḥ saṅgatigraha
iti caturasram | kriyāyā eva kartavyatopapatteḥ | duḥkhatayā'nupapattiriti
cet ? evaṃ tarhi varaṃ tasyā eva hitā'hitaprāptiparihārayoranyataropāyatā
parikalpanam, tādṛśyā eva tasyā ātmani pravṛttihetorupalabdheḥ tadetadapyati
pelavam | tasmānmānāntarā'viṣayatvenā'padārthatvācca naiva vākyārthatvaṃ
niyogasyā'pūrvasyeti siddham |
yadi tu niyogasya padārthatvalipsayā'nabhimatamapi mānāntaragocara
tvamāsthoyate tato mānāntaraviṣayatvenā'napekṣatvaṃ codanāyāḥ syādityāha—
atha mānāntarātsiddhiḥ ? na | mānāntaraviṣayatvenā'napekṣatvasiddheścoda
nāyāḥ | api ca niyoga iti pravartaneti ca preraṇeti ca niyokturvyāpāre
nirūḍhavṛttayaḥ śabdāstaṃ gamayanti | na cā'sau niyokturvinā avakalpate,
kāraṇā'bhāve kāryanivṛtterityāha—api ca niyokturvyāpāro niyogo na
niyokturvinā avakalpate | astu tarhi niyoktetyatrāha—na cā'syā'sti
[p.44]
cā'sya sambhavaḥ, apauruṣeyatvā'bhyupagamāt | śabdasya purastāt pratiṣedhāt |
kurviti tadaparāmarśādananyagocaratvam | tadyadi śabdato ghaṭādāvapi prasaṅgaḥ |
na hi ghaṭādiśrutayo'pi bhūtādīnāmanyatamamāmṛśanti | arthā'rthataḥ ? atyantā
'sattvaṃ khapuṣpādivat | etallakṣaṇatvādatyantā'sattāyāḥ | nityānāṃ ca
vartamānatvādavyabhicāraḥ | yadītarapratiyogitā vartamānatā na nityeṣu sā |
sambhavaḥ | nanu puruṣo niyoktā bhaviṣyatītyata āha—apauruṣeyatvā'bhyu
pagamāt | puruṣā'bhāvādvede | astu tarhi śabdo niyoktetyata āha—śabdasya
purastāt pratiṣedhāt | pramāṇatvādityādinā | yadapi pramāṇāntarāṇāṃ kālavi
parivṛttyarthaviṣayagocaratvāt kurviti tadaparāmarśāt kālatrayā'parāmarśātka
riṣyasi, akārṣīḥ, karoṣītyabhinnārthatvaprasaṅgādananyagocaratvam | tadyadi
śabdato ghaṭādāvapi mānāntarā'gocaratvaprasaṅgaḥ | na hi ghaṭādiśrutayo'pi
bhūtādīnāmanyatamamāmṛśanti | na hi ghaṭa iti bhūtādīnāmanyatamāmarśe
ghaṭo'bhūdasti bhaviṣyatīti prayogasambhavaḥ | virodhapaunaruktyābhyāṃ trayāmarśe
cā'paunaruktyamevetyarthaḥ | athā'rthatastraikālyā'nāmarśo, na punaḥ śabdamā
trāt | dūṣayati—atyantā'sattvaṃ khapuṣpādivat | yathā khapuṣpādayo nā'bhūvanna
bhavanti na bhaviṣyanti tathā cenniyogo'pi na syāttebhyo viśeṣaḥ | na
hyatyantā'sattāyā anyallakṣaṇam | ataḥ kālatrayavivekādityāha—etalla
kṣaṇatvādatyantā'sattāyāḥ | kālā'tiriktasya hi sattvaṃ kālā'vacchedena
vyāptam, sa tadavacchedo kālātmano niyogānnivartamānaḥ sattvamapyasya
nivartayati, nivartamānā vṛkṣateva śiṃśapātvamekaśilāmayāt giripradeśā
dityarthaḥ | nanu kālā'tiriktāḥ paramāṇvākāśādayaḥ santo na ca kālā
'vacchinnāstasmādvyabhicāra ityata āha—nityānāṃ ca vartamānatvāda
vyabhicāraḥ | atītā'nāgatābhyāṃ vyapavṛttayā'stikriyayā svā'yogaṃ nityeṣu
niyogena vyavacchindantyā upahitānāṃ nityānāṃ vartamānatā sadātanītyarthaḥ |
śaṅkate—yadītarapratiyogitā vartamānatā, na nityeṣu sā, yā khalvanā
gatatā kāraṇadvāreṇopamṛdya bhavati yā ca atītatayā sva nāśakakāraṇa
dvāreṇopamṛdyate, sā vartamānatā | na ceyaṃ nityeṣu sambhavati | prāk
pradhvaṃsā'bhāvā'vinābhāvitayā nityatvavirodhinītvādityarthaḥ | tadetatpāri
bhāṣikaṃ vartamānatvaṃ na lokasiddham | asti hi prākpradhvaṃsā'bhāvarahite
[p.45]
kālā'saṃsṛṣṭamapi tarhi mānāntaraviṣaya iti vyarthaḥ kālavivekopanyāsaḥ |
pramāṇagrāhyatā sattvalakṣaṇam | ato nā'tyantāsattvam | na | abhāva-
ṣvapyākāśādiṣu vartamānatānisargā'nubhavo laukikānām vidyate vyomā
''kāśo'stī tyādiprayogadarśanāt | na ca bhāktaḥ prayogaḥ, viśeṣābhāvāt |
na khalvātmā'sti, vijñānamastīti viśeṣo'vagamyate | aviśeṣe api 
caikasya bhāktatvakalpane tadatiriktasyāpi bhāktatvaprasaṅga iti |
satyapyatra dūṣaṇe sthavīyasi doṣāntaramāha—kālā'saṃsṛṣṭamapi tarhi
mānāntaraviṣaya iti vyartho niyogasya kālavivekopanyāsaḥ |
etena yadāhuḥ kārakavyāpāro hi kālatrayā'vacchedyo nā'dhikāra iti,
tadapyapāstaṃ veditavyam | yathā'vyāpārātmatayā'dhikāro niyogaḥ kāryyam,
na kālatrayasaṃbhinnaḥ tathā ghaṭādayo'pi | na jātu te'pi vyāpārā na ca 
mānāntarāgocarāḥ | tathā'dhikāronavacchinno'pi kālena mānāntara
gocaraḥ syāditi vyarthaḥ kālavivekopanyāsaḥ | nanu na kālasambandhaḥ sattā,
nāpi tadvayāptā, kālasya tadasambandhasyā'sattvaprasaṅgāt | nā'pi sāmānyam,
pūrvavimarśā'bhāve tadanupapatteḥ | yathā kālākṣīmīkṣitavataḥ svastimatyāṃ gavi
tadavamavarśo, naivaṃ himaśailādiṣu ca trasareṇvādiṣu ca rūpādiṣu cotkṣepaṇādiṣu
gotvādiṣu ca tadavamarśaḥ samasti etena dravyatvādayo'pi pratyuktā veditavyāḥ |
tasmānna sāmānyamapi sattā, api tu pramāṇagrāhyatā sattvalaṇam | sā ca
traikālyā'navacchinnasya kālasyeva niyogasyā'pi śāstrapramāṇaprayogavedanī
yasyā'stīti nātyantā'sattvam |
gaganakusumādayastvatītasamastamānakarmabhāvā atyatā'santa evetyā
śayavān paricodayati—pramāṇagrāhyatā sattvalakṣaṇam, ato nā'tyantā'sattvaṃ
niyogasya | dūṣayati—nā'bhāvasyāpi prameyatvāt | na khalu prameyatā sattā,
tadanuvṛttāvapi sattāyā abhavād vyāvṛtteḥ | yaddhi yasmin anuvartamāne vyāvartate
tattato'nyadeva, yathā sūtrāt kusumāni | sadasaditi ca parasparavyāvṛtte
lokasiddhe prameye | yadāhuḥ prāmāṇikāḥ 
sat saditi gṛhyamāṇaṃ yathābhūta
maviparītaṃ ca tattvaṃ sat | asadasaditi gṛhyamāṇaṃ yathābhūtamaviparītaṃ tattvamasa
[p.46]
katham ? yadā nā'bhāvaḥ prameyaḥ kaṇṭakādīnāmiti niraṭaṅki, kathaṃ tarhi nā
syāpi prameyatvāt | abhāva prameya iti suvyāhṛtam | nāsti pramīyate ceti
'stīti vyavahāraḥ ? viṣayāvacchedāt | avacchinnaviṣayaṃ hi pramāṇaṃ tadava
bodhayadanyadanavabodhayannāstīti vyavahārayati | kathamanyaviṣayo vyavahāro
'nyaviṣayātpramāṇāt ? nā'yamanyaviṣayaḥ | tasya pramāṇaviṣayā'vacchedaviṣaya
tvāt | tasyā viṣayāntaratvāt | tathā hi—nāstītyayamartho neda pratīyate
diti | tadetadasattvena nityaṃ parasparavyāvṛtaṃ sadasattvaṃ vyacchinnamanubhūyate |
codakaḥ sopahāsamāha—abhāvaḥ prameya iti suvyāhṛtam | upahāsavījaṃ
darśayati—nāsti pramīyate ceti katham ? na hi yathā dravyaguṇasāmānyānya
nyo'nyavilakṣaṇāni svabhāvabhedavanti ca tadbhedāścakāsati tathā 'yamabhāvo
'pi kaścit parasparavyāvṛtaśca svabhāvabhedavāṃścā'nubhava eva ca pramāṇamārgā
saṅgato lokastattvālokanāt, na punaḥ puruṣapravādamātram | vipravādo hi
sambhavet savaṭayakṣavat | tasmānnāstīti vyapadeśa'pramīyamāṇatāmātrā'
'lambano na punarabhāvaṃ vigrahavantaṃ gocarayati | tathā ca ghaṭo nāsti, na
pramīyata ityarthaḥ | tasya kathaṃ pramīyata ityanena saṅgati ? virodhādityarthaḥ |
yadyevamabhāvavyavahārasya bhūtale kaṇṭakādi nāstīti caraṇanyāsādilakṣaṇasya
pravṛttyātmakasya ghaṭādi nāstīti ca nivṛttyā''tmakasyā'satyaṃbhāve kiṃ
bījam ? ityāha | yadā nā'bhāvaḥ prameyaḥ kaṇṭakādīnāmiti niraṭaṅki, kathaṃ
tarhi tadānīṃ nāstīti vyavahāraḥ ? uttaramāha—viṣayā'vacchedāt | pramāṇasya
bhūtalagrāhiṇaḥ | tadeva sphoṭayati—avacchinnaviṣayaṃ hi pramāṇamekākibhūtalagrāhi
na ghaṭakaṇṭakādisadvitīyabhūtalagrāhīti yāvat | tadavabodhayad bhūtalamanyadanava
bodhayat ghaṭakaṇṭakādi nāstīti vyavahārayati |
tadetadviśadīkartuṃ śakyate—kathamanyaviṣayo vyavahāro ghaṭādyabhāva
viṣayo'nyaviṣayād bhūtalādiviṣayāt pramāṇāt ? abhāvavyavahārastāvadayaṃ
sarvajanīno na nihnavamarhati, bhāvavyavahāravat | tadanenā'bhāvādevotpattavyam,
anyavyavahārasyā'nyata utpattāvatiprasaṅgādityarthaḥ | nirākaroti—nāyamanya
viṣayaḥ | na vyatiriktā'bhāvaviṣayaḥ, tasyā'bhāvavad vigrahavato'pratīteḥ |
kiṃviṣayastarhi ? ityata āha—tasyā'bhāvavyavahārasya pramāṇaviṣayā'vaccheda
viṣayatvāt |
nanvevaṃ viṣayāvacchedo'pi bhāvarūpādviṣayād bhinna iti kathaṃ nā'nya
viṣayo'yamabhāvavyavahāraḥ ? ityata āha—tasyā viṣayāntaratvāt | na
[p.47]
bhūtalaṃ pramīyate na ghaṭa iti | atastatrā'pramitiḥ pramāṇamityalaukikamiti |
ucyate—anirūpitavyavahāramatrabhavantaṃ paśyāmaḥ | idaṃ hi bhavānnirūpayatu,
ya eṣa laukikānāṃ kvacinnāstīti pravṛttinivṛttilakṣaṇo vyavahāraḥ kimaya
maparicchedād, utā'nyaparicchedāt uta tadabhāvaparicchedāt ? yadyapari
cchedāt, neṣṭasyā'bhāvaṃ jijñāseran, aparicchedādeva nivarteran, aniṣṭasyā
'paricchedādeva pravarteran | pramāṇaviṣayā'paricchedanibandhana eva vyavahāraḥ |
na | viśeṣā'bhāvāt | na hi taditarayoraparicchedo viśiṣyate | apratyaya-
hyavacchedo nāma bhūtalabhāvād bhinnaḥ, ki tarhi dṛśye pratiyogini ghaṭakaṇṭa
kādau bhūtalamātrolambhaḥ | sa caikākibhūtalaviṣayaḥ | yasmindidṛkṣite yasmin
pratiyogini dṛśye bhavati tasyābhāva ucyate, pravartayati ca tadabhāvavyava
hāram | nanu bhavatu nāmaitadviṣayo'yamabhāvavyavahāro nāstīti vyapadeśo
laukikānāṃ bhāvā'tiriktamarthamabalambiṣyata ityata āha—tathā hi | nāstī
tyayamartho nedaṃ pratīyate, bhūtalamekāki pratīyate | na ghaṭa iti vyapadeśo'pi
pramāṇaviṣayāvacchedaviṣaya eva, na nirviṣayaḥ | pramāṇaviṣayāvacchedaśca
vyākṛtaḥ | tasmādupapanna upahāsa ityāha—atastatrā'pramitiḥ pramāṇaṃ
cetyalaukikam |
tadetaddūṣayitumupakramate—ucyate | anirūpitavyavahāramatrabhavantaṃ
paśyāmaḥ | nirūpaṇe tvabhāvavyavahārasyā'nupapannametaditi tannirūpaṇe
pravartayati | idaṃ hi bhavānnirūpayatu, ya eṣa laukikānāṃ kvacinnāstīti
pravṛttinivṛttilakṣaṇo vyavahāraḥ kimayamaparicchedātsarvopākhyāvirahalakṣa
ṇāt, utā'nyaparicchedāt, uta tadabhāvaparicchedāt ? tatra prathamaṃ kalpaṃ
dūṣayati—yadyaparicchedāt, neṣṭasyā'bhāvaṃ jijñāseran | kiñcā'paricche
dādeva nivarteran aniṣṭasyā'paricchedādeva pravarteran | jijñāsamāno'pi
khalvaparicchedamevā''pnuyāt | tadatiricyamānadehasya prameyā'bhāvasyā'nabhyu
pagamāt | sa ca prāpta eveti prekṣāvatāmabhāvajijñāsā na syāt |
śaṅkate—pramāṇaviṣayā'paricchedanibandhana eva vyavahāraḥ | nā'paricche
damātramatra vivakṣitam, api tu dṛśyasya pratiyogina ityarthaḥ | nirākaroti—
na | viśeṣā'bhāvāt | tamevāha—na hi tattaditarayoraparicchedo viśiṣyate |
na hi dṛśyatve'dṛśyatve vā pratiyoginoraparicchedasya prasajyapratiṣedhātmanaḥ
sarvopākhyāvirahiṇaḥ kaścidviśeṣaḥ sambhavati | tatsambhave tu tucchatvā
'nupapatteḥ |
[p.48]
pūrvike ca pravṛttinivṛttī ityasamīcīnam |
vastvantarapratyayāt pravartante nivartante ceti mithyā | bhāve'pi
pratiyoginastatprasaṅgāt | bhāve ghaṭakaṇṭakayorjñāyamānayostannibandhe eva
pravṛttinivṛttī | hanta tarhi tadabhāvādviparyayaḥ, na vastvantarabhāvāt | iṣṭā
'niṣṭasādhanajñānasya tadāpi bhāvātpurasvācca |
dūṣaṇāntaramāha—apratyayapūrvike ca pravṛtinivṛttī ityasamīcītam |
na khalu gaganakusumāyamānastuccho'paricchedaḥ pratyayaḥ kāraṇaṃ vā vijñānaṃ
vā sambhavati | tataścā'paricchedāt prekṣāvatpravṛttinivṛttī bruvāṇa ākasmi
katvaṃ vā tayoravijñānapūrvakatvaṃ vā vācoyuktyantareṇā'bhidadhīta, kadācitka
tvaprekṣāvatpravṛttinivṛttitvayoranupapatterityarthaḥ |
nanūbhayī bhūtalādau saṃvit, sadvitīyabhūtalaviṣayā caikākibhūtalaviṣayā
ceti | uttarā 'pi ca dvayī, dṛśye pratiyogini adṛśye ca | dṛśyatvaṃ cāsyo
palambhāya pratyayāntarasākalyaṃ, svabhāvaviśeṣaśca | sa ca svabhāvaviśeṣa
ucyate | yasminsati satsvanyeṣūpalambhapratyayeṣu sadbhāvo'nubhūyate | evaṃ ca yā
dṛśye pratiyogini bhūtalapratītiḥ saivā'bhāvavyavahārasādhanī | yasyāścā
'tucchatvāt sambhavati viśeṣaṇayogaḥ | tatsiddhyartha ca yujyate sūkṣmakīṭakaṇṭa
kādijijñāsetyata āha—vatsvantarapratyayāt pravarttante ceti mithyā | kutaḥ ?
bhāve'pi pratiyogino ghaṭāderbhūtalādau tatprasaṅgāt | ghaṭo nāstīti nivṛtteḥ
kaṇṭako nāstīti pravṛtteḥ prasaṅgāt |
śaṅkate—bhāve ghaṭakaṇṭakayorjāyamānayostannibandhane eva pravṛtti
nivṛttī; na bhūtalajñānanibandhane | nirācaṣṭe—hanta tarhi tadabhāvāt ghaṭa
kaṇṭakayorabhāvādviparyaya iṣṭā'niṣṭasādhanajñānasya ghaṭakaṇṭakajñānasya | atha
bhūtalabhāva eva kasmādiṣṭā'niṣṭasādhanajñānaviparyayaheturna bhavati ? ityata
āha—na vastvantarabhāvāt | na bhūtalabhāvādiṣṭā'niṣṭasādhanajñānaviparyayaḥ |
kasmāt ? tadā'pīṣṭā'niṣṭasādhanajñānasya samaye'pi bhāvād bhūtalasya |
etaduktaṃ bhavati | yadīṣṭā'niṣṭasādhanajñānavirodho bhūtalasyā'bhaviṣyat
tatastasmāttadanaṅkṣyat na tvanayorvirodhaḥ | tajjñānasantatyā saha tasya
ciramavasthiteriti |
[p.49]
na ceṣṭasādhanajñānā'sahabhuvo'pi bhūbhāgasya tajjñānaviparyayavirodhitā
'pītyāha—purastācca | bhūtalasya bhāvāt | iṣṭā'niṣṭasādhanajñānasya
ghaṭakaṇṭakajñānasya purastāttadabhāve dṛśyamānaṃ bhūtalaṃ na tatsahabhāvaniyatamityarthaḥ |
atredamākūtam | yo'yamabhāvavyavahāro laukikānāṃ ghaṭo nāstīti
nivṛttilakṣaṇaḥ, kaṇṭako nāstīti pravṛttilakṣaṇaḥ, sa na tāvad dṛśye ghaṭakaṇṭa
kādau bhūbhāgagrahanibandhanaḥ, dṛśyamāne'pi tadgate ghaṭādau prasaṅgāt | na
hyadṛśyaḥ, dṛśyamānatvāt | kevalabhūbhāgagrahaṇanibandhanatvādayamadoṣa iti
cet | kiṃ punarasya kaivalyam ? svarūpamiti cet | na | ghaṭakaṇṭakādigrahe 'pi
tatsadbhāvāt | taddharma iti cet ? sa tarhi ghaṭādibhāvavirodhī bhūmikāntareṇa
tadabhāva eveti siddhaṃ naḥ samīhitam | nanvabhāvaprameyavādināmapi bhūtala
grahaṇasadbhāvajñānakāraṇameṣitavyam | yadāha—
gṛhītvā vastusadbhāvaṃ smṛtvā
ca pratiyoginamiti | tatra na tāvat saṃsṛṣṭabhūtalagrahastaddheturvirodhāt | na
khalvasti sambhavaḥ, ghaṭasaṃsṛṣṭabhūtalagrahaśca tatra ghaṭā'bhāvaniścayaśceti |
nāpi ghaṭā'bhāvavadbhūtalagrahaḥ | ātmāśrayaprasaṅgāt | na khalu sa eva tasya
heturiti sāmpratam | tasmādadṛśye pratiyogini ghaṭādau bhūtalamātragrahaṇaṃ
ghaṭā'bhāvaparicchedaheturityakāmenā'pi ghaṭābhāvādanyat kaivalyaṃ bhūtalasya
svīkaraṇīyam | tadeva cā'bhāvavyavahāranibandhanamiti kimantargaṅunā ghaṭā
'bhāveneti |
tadetadayuktam | vastusadbhāvagrahaṇamātrasyā'bhāvajñānahetutvā'nabhyupaga
māt | api tu tulyopalambhayogyasya pratiyogino'nupalambhane satyasya tatkā
raṇatvamiṣyate | tadāha—smṛtvā ca pratiyoginamiti | anenāgṛhyamāṇatāmasya
sūcayati | tattve smṛtyanupapatteḥ | na caikajñānasaṃsargibhūtalopalambha eva ghaṭā
nupalambha iti sāmpratam nopalabdhacaraghaṭa saṃsṛṣṭabhūtalopalambhe'pi ghaṭā'nu
palambhaprasaṅgāt | tadā'pi bhūtalopalambhasya bhāvāt | ghaṭā'nupalambhasya ca
tadavyatirekāt | nanvayamanupalambhaḥ pratīto vā syādabhāvajñāne heturapratīto
vā ? na tāvat pratīto'navasthā''pātāt | so'pi hyupalambhā'bhāva
rūpatvādanulambhāntareṇa pramātavyaḥ, evamanupalambhāntaramapītyanavasthānāt |
apratītatve tu sattayā hetuḥ syāt | tathā ca yadā'yaṃ pramāṇopalambhacaracatvara
bhūtalapramitsā'bhāvāt tasminnapratītacare caitrā'bhāvaṃ tvaciraprāptacaitra
viśiṣṭacatvaratalopalambhavān pṛcchyate maitra! prāk kimāsīdiha caitra iti
[p.50]
sa tadā tatpraśnasamuditajijñāsaḥ prāktanīṃ dṛśyamānasya caitrasyā'sattāṃ tadānī
mevā'vadhāryottaramāha, āyuṣmannāsīdiha caitraḥ samprāpta iti |
tannopapadyate | tadānīntanenopalambhena tadanupalambhā'pavādāt | na ca
prāktano'nupalambhastasya sampratitanīmabhāvadhiyamādhātumarhati | na khalu
nimīlitanayanayugalaḥ prācyā nayanasattayā samprati rūpaṃ paśyati | tasmādeka
jñānasaṃsargavastvantaropalambha evā'bhāvajñānakāraṇameṣitavyam | tathā ca
svasaṃvedanasiddhaḥ sa evā'bhāvavyavahārasādhanāya paryāpta iti kṛtamabhāvena
prameyeneti |
atrocyate | na tāvadvinā ghaṭā'nupalambham bhūtalopalambhamātramabhāva
vyavahārasādhanam | na ca bhūtalopalambha eva tadanupalambha iti coktam |
ato'nupalambho varaṃ pratiyogina eṣitavyaḥ | na cā'sāvapratītastadabhāva
vyavahārasādhanam, prekṣāvadvyavahārasyā'buddhipūrvakatvā'nupapatteḥ | tadabhāva
jñānasādhanaṃ tu bhavet | ajñātasyā'pi jñānopāyatvadarśanāccakṣurādivat |
nanvevaṃ sattāmātreṇopāyatvamabhyupetaṃ bhavati | taccā'nupapannam | asatya
nupalambhe tadabhāvabuddhisamutpādaprasaṅgādityuktam | na | anupalambhā'bhāva
syā'siddheḥ | nanvatra catvaratale kimayamāsīccaitra iti praśnasamaye tadupa
lambhamānaḥ sa eva maitraḥ kathaṃ tadupalambhavān ? satyam, gṛhṇāti caitram, na tu
smaratyasya prāktanīṃ tatra sattām, catvaratalasyeva | na ca smṛtiranupalambha iti
tadabhāvo'pyanupalambha eva | tena yathā grahaṇārhasyā'grahaṇaṃ tadabhāvavīkṣā
sādhanam , evaṃ satyapi samprati tasya grahaṇe prāktanī caitrasya sattā catvara
syeva tatra madhyandine smaraṇārhā na smaryata iti mādhyandinasmaraṇā'bhāvaḥ
sanniti prāktanā'bhāvaviṣayaṃ mādhyandinaṃ pratyayaṃ śakta eva janayitum | na
ca pratyakṣā'nupalabdhirevā'bhāvajñānasādhanamiti sāmpratam | yogyapramāṇā
'nupalabdhestatsādhanatvāt | atatsādhanatve tvanumānādinivṛtteḥ | makaragatasya
saviturmīnādiṣu sattāyāśca caityavandanādidharmatāyāśca darvihomā'pūrvavattvasya
ca dhvanyāgamanamātropapannaśravaṇaśabdasya sajātīyaparamparārambhaṇasya nivāra
ṇā'bhāvāt | pramāṇāntarāṇāmivā'pramāṇasyā'pi smaraṇasya svaviṣaye
yogyatvopapatte | pratyayāntarasākalyalakṣaṇasya ca dṛśyatvasya viṣayakāraṇaka eva
pratyaya upapatteḥ | tadabhāve cā'numānādinivṛtteranumeyādyabhāvasādhanatvāt |
[p.51]
na hyanumānādyekaviṣayāṇāṃ prameyāṇāṃ kvacidabhāvastadvyavahāro vā viṣayā
'tiriktapratyayāntarasākalye sambhavati | tasmin sati sadvirodhibhāvapratyaya
vyavahāropapatteḥ na ca viṣayakāraṇe'pi pratyayāntarasākalyasambhavaḥ | indriyā
rthasannikarṣasyaivā'satyarthe'nupapatteḥ | tasmātsaṃskārā'bhāve'pi smaraṇā'rhatvamupa
pannam | na copalambhā'nupalambhayorvirodhaḥ, asamasamayavṛttibhāvā'bhāvasādhana
tayā bhinnaviṣayatvāt |
syādetat | sattāmātreṇā'bhāvasādhane tvanupalambhasya dṛśyatvamapyasya
tatheti tajjñānasiddhyartha sūkṣmakīṭakaṇṭakādijijñāsā na syāt | maivam |
dṛśyatvasiddhyarthaṃ hi sā | ajijñāsitasya tattvā'nupapatteḥ | na tu dṛśyatva
jñānārtham | bhavatu vā dṛśyatvajñānaṃ hetuḥ, bhūtalajñānavat, kimāyātamanupa
lambhasya ? na hyāloko jñāyamānaḥ kāraṇamiti cakṣuṣā'pi tathā bhavi
tavyam | na ca dhūmo'vagato'numānajñānakāraṇamiti mano'pi tatkāraṇaṃ
jñātavyam | tasmātsattayā'nupalambho'bhāvajñānakāraṇamiti nā'navasthā |
nā'pi prameyā'bhāvā'tiriktakaivalyā'ṅgīkāraḥ | catvaratalopalambhasya caitrā
'nupalambhe sati tatkāraṇatvāt | tadayamabhāvavyavahāro vastusadbhāvagrahaṇatat
pratiyogitatasmaraṇa tadbubhutsā'tiriktanimittajanmā | yathā satsvapi tantuṣu
kadācitpaṭaḥ prādurbhavaṃstadatiriktatatsaṃyogabhedajanmeti | dṛśye pratiyogini
smaryamāṇe vastusadbhāvagrahaṇe satyabhāvavyavahāro 'vaśyambhāvīti heturasiddha iti
cenna | gṛhyamāṇe'pi pratiyoginyasmaryamāṇe kimāsīditi praśnā'nantaraṃ
prāktanatadabhāvavyavahārapravṛtteḥ | bhavatu tarhi bubhutsite pratiyogini bodhārhe
vastusadbhāvajñānamabhāvavyavahārasādhanam | asti ca gṛhyamāṇe caitre kimā
sīditi praśnasamanantaraṃ tatra tadīyaprācīnasadbhāvasusmūrṣā | tadetasyāṃ satyāṃ
prākkālavarticatvaratalasmṛtirevā'vaśyaṃ tadabhāvavyavahārapravṛttinimittamitya
siddhatā hetoḥ | na | etasyāmapyavaśyambhāvaniyamā'bhāvāt | pratītismaraṇā
'nubhavayoḥ satorapi nitāntaballabhyatayā susmūrṣā'nububhūṣayoravirāmāt |
tathā ca pratiyogino'nubhave smaraṇe ca tatra tatra tadabhāvavyavahāraprasakteḥ |
asaṃsmaraṇe'nanubhave ceti viśeṣaṇānna doṣa iti cet ? aṅgīkṛtaṃ tarhi
tadatiriktaṃ nimittamabhāvaḥ | tathāpyupalambhā'bhāvarūpatvādasya na prameyā
'bhāvasiddhiriti cet | atrottaramanantarameva vakṣyati svayamevā''cāryaḥ |
nanvabhāvo nāma vigrahavānna kaścidanubhūyata ityuktam, tatkimayamabhāva
vyavahāro nāsti, nāsti nimitto vā ? na tāvannāsti nimittaḥ | kādā
[p.52]
tasmānnā'nyapramitinibandhane nāpyapramitinibandhane anabhimatamabhimataṃ
nāsti, abhimataṃ nāstīti pravṛttinivṛttī, api tvabhāvā'dhigamanibandhane |
nāstijñānasya ko viṣayo nirūpitaḥ ? nanūktam, kimaparāddhaṃ bhavato viṣayā
'bhāvena ? yattamatilaṅghya pramāṇā'bhāvo viṣaya ityucyate | jñeyā'bhāvo na
citkatvā'nupapatteḥ | na ca nimittāntaramasyā'stītyuktam | na ca nāsti,
bhāvavyavahārasyā'pi apahnavaprasaṅgādityuktam | niḥsvabhāvasya kathamanūbhūya
mānatā ? iti cenna | sadvyāvṛttirūpasya tasya pratīyamānatvātsvato'vāntara
viśeṣā'nupalabdheḥ | niḥsvabhāvateti cet ? na | jñānaśaktyādīnāmapi
tatprasaṅgāt | teṣāmapi hi na svato bhedo nirūpyate | jñeyaśakya bhedā'dhīnaṃ
tadbhedanirūpaṇamiti cet ? na | ihāpi pratiyogibhedena tadbhedanirūpaṇāditi
sarvamavadātam |
prakṛtamupasaṃharati—tasmānnā'nyapramitinibandhane nā'pyapramitinibandhane'
nabhimataṃ nāsti, abhimataṃ nāstīti pravṛttinivṛttī, api tu pāriśeṣyāda
bhāvā'vagamanibandhane | na kevalaṃ vyavahārā'nyathānupapatterabhāvasiddhiḥ, api
tu ghaṭo'stīti jñānavattadabhāvajñānamapyavagamyate | na ca jñānaṃ nirālambana
miti nivedayiṣyate | tadasyā''lambanaṃ nā'bhāvādanyadastītyāśayavān
pṛcchati—nāstijñānasya ko viṣayo nirūpitaḥ ? apara āha—nanūktam,
nedamiha pratīyata ityarthaḥ |
tatra na tāvadadṛśye pratiyogini vastusadbhāvajñānam, pratiyo
gino'pyapramitiḥ sambhavatītyuktam | atastadvyatiriktā'nupalambhā'bhāvo
vaktavyaḥ | tathā ca kimaparāddhamatrabhavatī viṣayā'bhāvena ? yattaṃ pramīyamāṇama
tilaṅghya pramāṇā'bhāvo viṣaya ucyate |
śaṅkate—prātisvikabhedā'nupalabdherjñeyā'bhāvo na prameyaḥ | nirācaṣṭe—
bhavatu yadyasti jñānajñeyā'bhāvayorviśeṣaḥ | na hi jñānā'bhāvo'pyājānato
bhedavān | tadbhedasyā'pi pratiyogibhedā'dhīnanirūpaṇatvāt | tathā ca caitro
nāstīti pratītistadabhāvā'vabhāsalakṣaṇakāryasamadhigamanīyā, na kukavi
kṛtiriva kleśena kathaṃcitpramāṇā'bhāvaviṣayatayā vyakhyātumucitā | athāpi
vaijātyāt ucyate, na ca tattvato nāstīti buddhivyavahārau staḥ, kintu
[p.53]
prameyaḥ | bhavatu yadyasti tadviśeṣo hetuḥ | jñānā'bhāve jñānabhramo vyavahārā
'bhāve vyavahārabhramaḥ | ālokā'darśane'ndhakārabhramavat |
na | suṣuptyādyavasthāsu prasaṅgāt | apramite ca bhramā'yogātsuṣuptyā
divat |
caitradarśanā'bhāve caitro nāstīti jñānaṃ bhramaḥ, caitrocitavyavahārā'bhāve vyava
hārabhramaḥ |
atraiva nidarśanamāha—ālokā'darśane'ndhakārabhramavat | na kilā'ndha
kāro nāma tattvato 'sti, tatpratyayo vā | tathā hi tad dravyādīnāma
nyatama meva bhavet | taireva viśvasya saṅgrahāt | tatra dravyatve tamaḥpṛthi
vyādīnāmanyatamamanyadvā ? na tāvadanyatamam | kṣitisalilātmasu caturdaśa
guṇeṣu, tejasi caikādaśaguṇe, mātariśvani ca navaguṇe, manasi cā'ṣṭaguṇe,
nabhasi ca ṣaṅguṇe, dikkālayośca pañcaguṇayoravināśinoranantarbhāvāt |
antarbhāve vā tāvadguṇatvaprasaṅgānnityatvāpatteśca | nā'pi dravyāntaram
nirguṇasya tattvā'nupapatteḥ | nīlotpalapalāśaśyāmalamupalabhyamānaṃ mahacca
tamo na nirguṇam | ato dravyāntaramiti cet ? na | kṛṣṇarūpasya gandhādye
kāryasamavāyinastadvyāptasya tadabhāve'bhāvāt | tathā ca nikhilaguṇavirahiṇo
na dravyatvam | guṇakarmasāmānyabhāvo'pyanāśrayasya na sambhavati |
na ca pṛthivyādidravyamasyāśrayaḥ | tadāśrayatve tadguṇānāṃ tatsahacaritā
nāmupalabdhiprasaṅgāt | na cāsya tamasa upalabdhirupapadyate |
upalambhakā'bhāvāt | na cendriyāṇāmetattamo locanaṃ gocarayati |
tadabhāve tadanupalabdhiprasaṅgāt | asti ca nimilitalocanasyā'pi
tamaḥ paśyāmītyabhimānaḥ | ghrāṇādīnāṃ gocarastama iti sthavīyaḥ | mano
'pi cā''ntaragocaraniyataṃ bahirasatīndriyaliṅgādau davīya eva | na ca
kāraṇā'bhāve kāryamutpattumarhati | tasyā''kasmikatvena kādācitkatvā
'nupapatteḥ | tasmānna tamo nāma kiñcidasti vastu | nāpi tajjñānam |
atha ca tamastamālanīlamupalabhāmaha ityabhimāno laukikānāmālokā'darśane |
so'yaṃ viplavaḥ | evamabhāvadarśanaṃ tadvyavahāraśca viplava eveti bhāvaḥ |
tadetannirākaroti—na | suṣuptyādyavasthāsu prasaṅgāt | yadi hi jñāna
vyavahārayorabhāve tadvibhramaḥ, suṣuptyādyavasthāsvapi tathā prasaṅgaḥ | na hi
tadā jñānaṃ, nā'pi vyavahāraḥ | samastavijñānopasaṃhṛtilakṣaṇātvātsuṣuptyā
dyavasthāyāḥ | hetvantaramāha—apramite ca bhrāntyayogāt suṣuptyādivat |
[p.54]
tamodarśanaṃ tu bhūcchāyādarśanam |
pramitasya hi bhāvasya pramita eva bhāve samāropabhrāntirna punarasato nāpi
jñānā''kārasya, nāpyagraha ityupapāditaṃ vibhramaviveke | atrāpi
sūcayiṣyati | na ca jñāvnayavahārābhāvau kvacidupalabdhapūrvau | tadupalambhe
vā kṛtamatra bhramopanyāsena | tasmādapramite bhrāntyanupapatterayuktametadityarthaḥ |
dṛṣṭāntastu sādhyavikala ityāha—ata eva tamodarśanaṃ tu bhūcchāyā
darśanam | ayamatrā'bhisandhiḥ | pramīyate tāvattamastamālamālāśyāmam | na
cā'pramitāveva pramitivibhramasambhavaḥ | na cāsya bādhakamasti pramāṇam |
na ca tadabhāve pramīyamāṇamapahnavamarhati | atiprasaṅgāt | na ca tamasaḥ
pramāṇā'nupalabdheḥ pramā prathamānāpyapahnotumucitā | rūpādyupalabdherapi
tatprasaṅgāt | na hi tatpramāṇamindriyādyupalabhyate | rūpādyupalabdhiliṅgaka
manumānamasyopalabdhiriti cet ? na | ihāpi samānatvāt | nanu cakṣurādi
gocaratvamasyetyuktam | tarhi varamastu tamasi samastajanasākṣātkārā''spade
pramitikāryadarśanāt pramāṇāntaramanupalabdhapūrvamapi, na punastasyā'nyato
'nupalambhāt spaṣṭadṛṣṭakāryaviparyayo yujyate | astu vā cakṣurevā'sya
kāraṇam | na ca nimīlitākṣasya paṭalavato vā tannāsti, api tu pihitam |
kathaṃ tarhi pihitaṃ ghaṭādāviva bāhye tamasi pravartteteti cet ? mā
pravarttiṣṭa | kinnaśchinnam ? paricchetsyati hi tacchāyāṃ pakṣmaṇoḥ paṭalasya
vā'tyāsannāmapi pidhātṛṇī paṭalapakṣmaṇī pidadhatīṃ tāmevekṣate cakṣuḥ, na
tu paṭalapakṣmaṇī | ālokāpyāyitaṃ tatpṛthivyādigrahe prabhavati, na tu tacchā
yāyāṃ tadvirodhinyām, kevalasyaiva tatra sāmarthyāt | kāryadarśanonneyatvāttasya
sūkṣmamapi ceṣīkatūlādi cakṣurācchādakamāsannamanubhūyamānamatimahadanavadhi
cakāsti tatheyamapi | na cā'ndhānāmitaragātrā'vayavavad golakādhiṣṭhāne
tatkāryopalambhasambhavaḥ | tadbhāve vā varamupalambhakāntarakalpanetyuktam |
katamatpunardravyādīnāṃ tamaḥ ? nanu dravyameva, kālimaguṇaśālitvāt
spandavattvācca | tathā hi | kālimaivā'sya rūpamupalabhyate | aptejasoriva
śvetimā | evaṃ saṃṅkhyā 'pyekatvādikā parimāṇam, taccaturvidham | pṛthivyā
dyaṇūnāmiva tamo'ṇūnāmapyanumānāt | pṛthaktvasaṃyogavibhāgaparatvā'paratva
saṃskārāśca pañcavidhamapi karmā'dhyakṣamīkṣate | yathāhā'trabhavānvārtikakāraḥ |
[p.55]
na nu nā'bhāvamātrasya tamastvaṃ vṛddhasaṃmatam |
chāyāyāḥ kārṣṇyamityevaṃ purāṇe bhūguṇaśrūteḥ ||
bhūguṇasya kārṣṇyasya cchāyāyāṃ dravyāntaraśruterityarthaḥ |
dūrā''sannapradīpādidehaceṣṭā'nusāriṇī |
āsannadūradīpādimahadalpacalā'calā |
dehānuvarttinī cchāyā na vastutvādinā bhavet || iti |
na ca pṛthivyādīnāmanyatamam | na tāvatkṛṣṇarūpasya tamasastoyādi
bhāvasambhavaḥ | tīyatejasoḥ sitatvādevaṃ pavanādīnāṃ cā'rūpatvāt | na ca
paropadhānāt kālindīsalilasyeva tamasaḥ kālimeti sāmpratam | anupahi
tasya kadācidapi svābhāvikarūpāntaraśālino'nupalabdheḥ, kṣetrasamuddhṛ
tasyeva kālindīvāriṇaḥ svabhāvasvacchadhavalasya |
nāpi pārthivaṃ tamaḥ | tadguṇānāṃ gandhādīnāmabhāvāt | nanu tathaiva
gandhādivyāptaṃ kṛṣṇamapi rūpaṃ tannivṛttāvasadityuktam | tatkiṃ pavane'nuṣṇa
śītasparśo'sanneva ? gandhādivyāptasya tasya pṛthivyāmupalabdheḥ | pavane ca
teṣāmabhāvāt | pākajasya sparśasya gandhādivyāptatvam | ayaṃ tvapākajaḥ
sparśo vāyavīya iti cenna | ihāpi sāmyāt | na hi tamaso'pi kālimā
pākajaḥ | pratyakṣaṃ cobhayatrā'pi samānam | tamaḥparamāṇavaśca pārthivādi
paramāṇava iva dvyaṇukādikrameṇa mahāntaṃ tamo'vayavinaṃ pārthivamivārambhante |
tacca rūpaviśeṣe satyanekadravyatvānmahattvādvā cākṣuṣamiti na tadutpattyana
vakḷptiḥ | na ca tasya divā''rambhasambhavaḥ śāśvatikavirodhe sati tejasi |
na jātu sparśavadvegavanmudgarādighāte paripanthini kumbhārambhāya bhavanti
mṛdavayavāḥ bibhrati vā kumbhamārabdhamiti | ata eva hi divāpi nirasta
tejasi giriguhāyāmārabhante eva |
syādetat | na tāmasaḥ paramāṇavo 'vayavinamārabhante | sparśavirahā
dātmamano vyoma vat | na cā''rambhe rūpavattvaṃ prayojakam | arūpāṇāmapi
pavanaparamāṇūnāṃ sparśavaddravyārambhadarśanādasparśānāṃ cā''tmamanasāṃ tadabhāvāt |
tatkimatra bhavatāṃ tamaḥparamāṇavaḥ santi na vā ? santi cetkathantadavayavi
nāmabhāvaḥ ? tadbhāvalakṣaṇatvāt paramāṇubhāvasya | asattve vā tamaḥparamāṇūnāṃ
kathaṃ na hetorāśrayāsiddhiḥ ? hetorasparśasyā'sadrūpatvādasadāśrayatvamadoṣa iti
cenna | tucchasyā'hetutvātkalpanāropitatadbhāvasya ca vastuno'sādhakatvāt |
[p.56]
na jātu samāropitadhūmabhāvā mihikā kṛśānusādhanaṃ bhavati | prāmāṇikasya
tvabhāvasya bhāvā'dhikaraṇamantareṇā'yogāt | tamaḥparamāṇuṣvasatsvāśrayā
'siddhatayā bhavatyasiddhiḥ, na tu svato'prasiddhasya satyāmapi paraprasiddhau
sādhanāṅgatvam, svayamapratītasya parapratyāyanā'narhatvāt | yathoktam |
yo'pi tāvatparā'siddhaḥ svayaṃsiddho'bhidhīyate |
bhavettatra pratīkāraḥ svato'siddhe tu kā kriyeti ||
api cā'sparśasya dravyā'nārambhakatvena na svābhāvikaḥ saṃbandho manasaḥ
śakyo niścetuṃ, vaiya rthyasyopādhervidyamānatvāt | dharmā'dharmanimittatvādviśva
kāryanirmāṇasya | sākṣātpāramparyeṇa ca sarvaṃ kāryajātaṃ bhogasādhanaṃ sattannirmāṇaṃ
syāt | na ca mano'vayavinaḥ śarīratayā viṣayatayā vā bhogasādhanatā
sambhavati | indriyā'nadhiṣṭhānatayā dehabhāvā'bhāvāt | apārthivasya ca
tadbhāvā'nabhyupagamāt | viṣayabhāvastvananubhūyamānatayā'nupapannaḥ | indriya
bhāvastvaṇuta eva manasaḥ | tamasastu rūpavato viṣayabhāvena bhavati bhogasādha
natvamiti na vaiyarthyam | paramamahatāṃ punarasamavāyikāraṇasaṃyogavirahādadhika
parimāṇakāryadravyā'nupapatteśca kāraṇasamānanyūnaparimāṇakāryā'darśanāccā'dhi
kyā'yogācca paramamahattvavyādhātācca na svasamavāyidravyārambhakatvamiti so
'yamupādhiḥ | na caupādhikaḥ sambandho'numānāṅgam | mā bhūdupādhyāyadarśanā
dasakṛdavagatasahabhāvasyā'numānaṃ śiṣyasya | na cā'nanyathāsiddhe nīlā'ndha
kārā'nubhave paripanthini sphurati pratyakṣe bādhitaviṣayamanumānamudetumutsahate |
mā bhūdanuṣṇatvaṃ tejaso dravyatvātpṛthivyādivadityanumānam | dravyāntarasya ca
dravyāntarāśrayatvaṃ dṛṣṭam | tadyathā ākarajasya tejasaḥ suvarṇādeḥ pārthiva
dravyopaṣṭambhaḥ, anyathā patanādi na bhaved gurutvā'bhāvāt | yathā cā'bhijāta
mahānīlādimaṇisamāśrayaṃ tejaḥ na ca tadvadetad | bhramaṇata steja iti sāmpra
tam | tejasastadāśrayasyā'ntataḥ khadyotādigatasyāpi kayācinmātrayā rūpa
prakāśanavyāptatvāttamasaśca tadvaiparītyāt | tasmāttamo dravyāntaramapi pṛthi
vyādyanuvidhāsyati, tejasaśca vinaṅkṣyati | tadapagame ca pṛthivyādibhyaḥ punaḥ
prabhaviṣyati | catuṣkādapavarakapraviṣṭasya katipayā''lokā'vayavasambhedavirale'pi
tamasi sāndratā'vagamo bhramaḥ | indriyopahateḥ | kālaparivāsavaśāt | upahati
vigame tu yathāvatpaśyatyapavarakavartinaḥ padārthān, alpāṃ ca tatra cchāyām |
[p.57]
ālokā'bhāvadaśanaṃ cetyasamānam |
sā ca sāndrā pidhatte padārthān, na tejasā viralīkṛtā | na cā''lokābhāva
mātraṃ tama iti sāmpratam, tasya nīlatvā'nupapatterasmṛtā'navalokitā'lokasya
ca tamaḥpratyayā'nupapattiprasaṅgāt | tathā ca parispandādayastadvartino 'bagamya
mānāḥ kathaṃcit kleśena na vyākhyāmarhanti | tasmātpratyakṣasiddhamasati bādhake
dravyāntaramekādaśaṃ tamo navaguṇaṃ ceti siddham |
nādṛṣṭau darśanaṃ chāyā navā'bhāvo'smṛtau gateḥ |
rūpādupāyasadbhāvād dravyaṃ dravyāntarā'nugam ||
iti saṅgrahaślokaḥ |
alaṃ vā naiyāyikairvivādena | ālokā'bhāva eva tamaḥ | tathā'pi
siddhatyeva naḥ samīhitamityāha—ālokā'bhāvadarśanaṃ ceti | tasmādasamānaṃ
dārṣṭāntikeneti | tadyadi pramāṇagrāhyatā pramāṇasaṃbandhaḥ, yadi vā tadarhatā,
ubhayyapyasāvabhāve sadā'stīti tasyāpi sattvaprasaṅgaḥ | atha svarūpavarta
mānatā , na sā'bhāve'sti | na | atucchatvopapādanāt | na caivamapi
pratibhāvaṃ bhedavatī satsaditi buddhivyapadeśayorabhinnā'rthāvagāhinorgocaro
bhavitumarhati | abhede vā'bhidhānāntareṇa sāmānyameva tat | pūrvā'bhimarśa
mantareṇa sāmānyamaprāmāṇakamiti cet ? na | vikalpā'sahatvāt | kaḥ
khalvayaṃ pūrvā'bhimarśo'bhimata āyuṣmataḥ, yāvadupalabdharūpaparāmarśaḥ, āho
svit katipayānāmapi rūpāṇām ? tatra prathamasya na kvacidapi sambhavaḥ |
na khalūpalabdhacaraśilābhrakasyā pi varṣāsu śaradi punaḥ paśyatastādṛśaḥ
pūrvā'vamarśo'sti | tasyā'pi tadabhāvādanyatvaprasaṅgaḥ | katipayaparāmarśo'trāpi
samānaḥ | asti khalu katipayānāṃ rūpāṇāmanugamastrasareṇukareṇvorvividha
bhedasamavāye'pi vā dravyatvādinā | tasmādyathā sadbuddhivyapadeśayorananya
yāsiddherguṇavattvasya ca pratiguṇavadbhedena tadupapādanā'sāmarthyāt evaṃ dravya
guṇakarmasāmānyānāmapi atyantabhinnānāmapi sadityabhinnabuddhivyapadeśa
bhājāmanusyūtamasti tatsattvam | yasya ca traikālyā'sambandhādatyantā'santaḥ
śaśaviṣāṇādayaḥ, yasya cā'yogavyavacchedādatyantasantaḥ paramāṇvākāśādayaḥ,
yasya cātyantā'yogavyacchedātkadācideva santaḥ paṭādayaḥ, yasya ca sambandhād
dravyaguṇakarmasāmānyāni svā'bhāvavyavacchedena svā'nubhavād bhāsante, sāmā
[p.58]
astu vā pramāṇagrā hyatā, varttamānatā tu sattvamucyate |
arthā'bhāvo niyogasya śābdyāṃ bhūtādyapākṛtau |
ananyagocaratvaṃ na pramāṇāntarajanmani ||
aśābdaścenniyogo nābhūnnāsti na bhaviṣyatīti bodhayati na niyukto
na niyujyate na niyokṣyate vā svargakāma iti vākyārthaḥ syāditi
sunirūpito'dhikāraḥ | atha na śābdo bhūtādipratikṣepaḥ kiṃ tu tasyaudā
sīnyānmānāntaratastathā'vasāyaḥ | na tarhyananyagocaraḥ | mānāntareṇa
tathātvasyā'vasāyāt śabdapramitasya viśeṣastataḥ | pratyakṣasyeva gireragnimattā
nyarūpabhūyastvā'lpīyastvanibandhanaśca tatra vailakṣaṇyā'vagatibhedaḥ | yathā
''hācāryaḥ |
sāmānyarūpabhūyastve tasmāttattvaṃ prakāśate |
vilakṣaṇatvamalpatve cakāsti tadanudbhavāt || iti |
tadetad brahmasiddhau kṛtaśramāṇāṃ sugamamiti neha prapañcitam |
abhyupetyocyate | astu vā pramāṇagrāhyatā sattvaṃ, tathā'pi vartamānatā
tu sā | kasmāt ? atītā'nāgatayoḥ pramāṇagrāhyayorapyasatpratītivyavahārā
spadatvāt | vartamānataiva hi sattvamucyate | tathā ca vastutaḥ kālā'navacchinno
niyogaḥ pramāṇagrāhyo'pyasanneva sattve ca na mānāntarā'gocara iti siddham |
api ca—
arthābhāvo niyogasya śābdyāṃ bhūtādyapākṛtau |
ananyagocaratvaṃ na pramāṇāntarajanmani ||
api cāsya traikālyā'navacchedaḥ śabdena vā pratipādyate, mānāntareṇa
vā ? yadi śabdenā'tyantā'sattvam | mānāntareṇa tu traikālyavyavacchede
mānāntaragocaratvaprasaṅgādvivakṣitārthabhāvanamityāha—aśābdaścenniyogo nā'
bhūnnāsti na bhaviṣyatīti bodhayati tathā sati na niyukto na niyujyate na
niyokṣyate svargakāma iti vākyārthaḥ syāditi sunirūpito'dhikāraḥ | atha
na śābdo bhūtādipratiṣedhaḥ, tasya śabdasya teṣu bhūtādiṣu niṣedhyeṣvaudā
sīnyāt, kintu mānāntaratastathā'vasāyaḥ, traikālyavyavacchedenā'vasāyaḥ |
na tarhyananyagocaraḥ | kutaḥ ? mānāntareṇa tathātvasya traikālyā
vacchedasyā'vasāyāt |
[p.59]
'numānāt | na | viśiṣṭā'paricchede viśeṣaṇasyā'viṣayatvāt | api ca
anyato'pi tathātvā'dhigame'niyoga eva svargakāmaḥ | tasyāpi pramāṇatvāt |
atattve vā bhūtādivivekā'bhidhānaṃ pralāpaḥ | nanūpādhayo bhūtādayaḥ, na
copādhinivṛttāvupādhimato nivṛttirapratītirvā | bhavatvanyopādhiṣvevamiha
tvetallakṣaṇamevā'tyantā'sattvam | nityā'nityatvopādhinivṛttau ca nopādhi
matsambhavaḥ | pratyakṣaṃ tvāpātajanmakāle bhedopādhiviviktarūpamātragrāhīti na
tato viṣayabhedaḥ | avarttamānasyā'rūpatvādvarttamānaviṣayaṃ tat | satyaṃ vastuto
śaṅkate—śabdapramitasya niyogasya viśeṣastraikālyavivekastato mānānta
rāt | na punarmānāntaraṃ niyoge pravartata iti | atraiva nidarśanamāha—pratyakṣa
syeva gireragnimattāviśeṣo'numānāt, na tu tadagnimattāgocaraṃ girimapi
gocarayitumarhati | nirākaroti—na | viśiṣṭā'paricchede viśeṣasyā
'viṣayatvāt | na hyasti sambhavo yadviśiṣṭaṃ parvatādi na paricchidyate,
tadviśeṣaścā'gnimattvaṃ gamyata iti | na cā'pratyakṣīkṛtaparvatastadviśeṣamanu
mātumarhatīti sāmpratam | anumānasamaye pratyakṣajñānasyā'tītatvena girera
paricchedātsmṛtyanupapatteśca | tadā yugapad jñānotpatteranupapatterantaḥkaraṇasya
pratyayaparyāye sāmarthyāt | anyathā samānagocarabuddhidhārā''nupapatteḥ | sakṛdeva
yāvatkartavyakaraṇāt | samarthasya kṣepā'yogāt | pratyakṣā'numānayośca dharmi
dharmarūpamātragocaratayā sahasthitāvapi viśiṣṭatvā 'navabhāsanāt | api
cāsyā'nyato'pi tathātvā'dhigame'niyoga eva svargakāmaḥ | tasyā'pi
pramāṇatvāt | kālatrayavirahāt | tattve vā bhūtādivivekā'bhidhānama
pramāṇikaṃ pralāpaḥ | codayati—nanūpādhayo viśeṣaṇāni vastuto bhūtādayaḥ
karṇasyeva kuṇḍalādayaḥ | na punaḥ svabhāvaḥ | tataḥ kim ? ityata āha—
na copādhinivṛttau satyāmupādhimato'pi nivṛttirapratītirvā | na khalu
kuṇḍalanivṛttau karṇo nivartate, na pratīyate vā | pariharati—bhavatvanyopādhiṣu
kuṇḍalādiṣvevam, iha tvetallakṣaṇaṃ traikālyavivekalakṣaṇamevā'tyantā'sa
ttvam | na khalu śaśaviṣāṇādīnāmatyantā'sattvamanyat | atastraikālya
vivekāt | na kevalamayamanye'pi santi te upādhayo ye nivartamānā vastusattāṃ
nivartayantītyāha—nityā'nityatvopādhinivṛttau nopādhimatsambhavaḥ | api
ca śabdatastraikālyā'navamarśe pratyakṣaṃ tvāpātajanmaviśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvā
'navamarśāt kālabhedopādhiviviktarūpamātragrāhīti na tataḥ pratyakṣādviṣaya
bhedaḥ śābdasya jñānasya bhavet |
[p.60]
na pramititaḥ | vastuto vartamānādiviveke nirātmakatvamatyantā'pramitito na
viṣayabhedātpramāṇāntarā'napekṣatvaṃ śabdasya | saiṣobhayataspāśā |
nanu pratīyate yajeteti kāryo'rtho, na sa bhaviṣyanmānā'bhāvāt ekāntā
'navadhāraṇāt | kāryyatvādeva netarakālā'nupātī | na ca nāsti | pratīteḥ |
uktottarametat | sātmakatve na vastutaḥ kālabhedaḥ, pramititaścenna viṣayabheda
iti nairātyam | kathaṃ tarhi pratītiḥ ? ata eva pratibhāmātraṃ vikalpamātraṃ vā
śābdajñānamiti vipaścitaḥ | pratibhānibandhanaśca vyavahāraḥ | pratibhā'nu
gṛhītāni ca pramāṇāni vyavahārā'ṅgamiti |
śaṅkate—avartamānasyā'rūpatvādvartamānaviṣayaṃ tat | nirācaṣṭe—satyaṃ
vastuto na pramititaḥ | uktamarthaṃ saṅkṣipati—tatra niyoge vastuto vartamānā
diviveke nirātmakatvamatyantā'pramitito na viṣayabhedāt pramāṇāntarā
'napekṣatvaṃ śabdasya, saiṣobhayataspāśā rajjuḥ |
codayati—nanu pramīyate yajeteti kāryo 'rtho na sa bhaviṣyan, bhaviṣya
tyarthe pramāṇā'bhāvādekāntā'navadhāraṇāttattvā'niścayāt | mā bhūd bhaviṣyan,
vartamāno'tīto vā syādityata āha—kāryatvādeva netarakālā'nupātī |
gaganakusumāyamānastarhītyata āha—na ca nāsti | kutaḥ ? pratīteḥ | na
hyekāntā'sataḥ pratītiviṣayabhāvaḥ sambhavatīti | pariharati—uktottara
metat | uktaprāyamasyottaram | taddarśayati—sātmakatve na vastutaḥ kāla
bhedavivekaḥ pramititaścenna viṣayabhedaḥ, pratyakṣād ghaṭādiśabdācceti |
tasmānmānāntarā'gocaratvamicchato niyogasya traikālyasambhedavirahamantareṇa
tadanupapatternairātmyamāpatet |
kathaṃ tarhi pratītiratyantā'sataḥ ? uttaram—ata evā'sadgocaratvā
deva pratibhāmātraṃvikalpamātraṃ vā śābdaṃ jñānamiti vipaścitaḥ | asatpratīti
reva pratibheti vā vikalpa iti vā matabhedena vyapadeśabhedavatī | etaduktaṃ
bhavati, nā'tyantā'satpratīteragocaro 'pi tu pramiteḥ | nanvasadviṣayā pratītiḥ
kathaṃ vyavahārā'ṅgam ? na khalu khadyotaṃ dahana iti manyamānastena dahati
pacati veti | ata āha—pratibhānibandhanaśca vyavahāraḥ | nā'styayaṃ niyamaḥ,
asadarthavijñānaṃ na vyavahārāṅgamiti | saṃśayaviparyayau hi tathā | tābhyāṃ
vyavahārā'nupalabdheḥ | pratibhā punarvyavahārahetuḥ | tadbhāvā'bhāvā'nuvidhānād
dravyavyavahārabhāvā'bhāvayorityarthaḥ | yadyevaṃ na tarhi vyavahārasādhanatvaṃ pramāṇā
nām, pratibhāta eva tatsiddherityata āha—pratibhā'nugṛhītāni ca pramāṇāni
vyavahārāṅgam | etaccopariṣṭāt spaṣṭayiṣyatīti | itiḥ samāptau |
[p.61]
api ca—
vedārtha itthamāśaṅkā niyogasya na kāryatā |
niyuktasya pravṛttiśca na niyogaikabandhanā ||
yat khalu na bhūtaṃ na varttamānaṃ bhāvi na vetyanavadhāritaikāntaṃ tadā
śaṅkājñānagocaro, yathā varṣamanatipatitamapravṛttamatinīlabahalajaladodayadarśana
sāmarthyena pratyayapathamanupatadvyabhicāradarśanādaniścīyamānaikāntam | imāṃ
cā'vasthāṃ vata vedārthaḥ prajñāśālibhirupanīto'tivyākhyayā |
api ca viṣayaḥ kāryyo na niyogaḥ | kāryaśca vedaprameyaḥ itarapadārthānāṃ
tadanvayitvāt | tasyaiva kālaviviko mānāntarā'gocaratayā'napekṣatvasiddhaye-
api ca—
vedārtha itthamāśaṅkā niyogasya na kāryatā |
niyuktasya pravṛttiśca na niyogaikabandhanā ||
yatkhalu na bhūtaṃ na vartamānaṃ bhāvi na vetyanavadhāritaikāntaṃ tadāśaṅkā
jñānagocaraḥ | tadyathā varṣamanatipatitamabhūtamapravartamānamatinīlabahala jaladoda
yādbahuladarśanasāmarthyena pratyayapathamanupatatkvacid vyabhicāradarśanādaniścīya
mānaikāntam | tataḥ kim ? ityāha—imāṃ cā'vasthāṃ vedārthaḥ prajñāśālibhi
rupanīto 'tivyākhyayā | niścayaphalaṃ hi pramāṇam | ayaṃ ca svārthe śaṅkāma
niścayātmikāṃ vidadhāno'pramāṇaṃ syādityarthaḥ |
tadanena vedārtha itthamāśaṅketi vivṛtam | niyogasya na kāryateti
vivṛṇoti—api ca viṣayaḥ kāryo na niyogaḥ | ājñādiṣu hi niyogeṣu
svāmyādiniyoktṛkeṣu viṣayaḥ kāryo'vagamyate niyojyena | ca cā''jñādayo
niyogā ityarthaḥ | tataḥ kim ? ityāha—kāryaśca vedaprameyo lokā'nu
sārāt | nanu dravyādayo'pi tatpadebhyo'vagamyanta iti kathaṃ tasyaiva vedaprame
yatā ? ityata āha—itarapadārthānāṃ tadanvayitvāt | kāryānvayitvāt | eta
duktaṃ bhavati | sarvameva padajātaṃ kāryaparam, tatraiva vyutpatteḥ, tatra kiñcida
bhidhāyakatayā, yathā liṅādi | kiñcittu tadarthasvārthā'bhidhāyakatayā yathā
dadhnetyādi | bhavatvevam, prakṛte tu kimāyātam ? ityata āha—tathā ca tasyaiva
kāryasyaiva vedārthasya kālaviveko mānāntarā'gocaratayā''mnāyasya sā'pekṣatva
siddhaye'bhidhānīyo na tu niyogasya, tasyā'kāryatvena vedārthatvā'bhāvāt |
na cāsau kālarahitaḥ | tasya kārakavyāpāraviṣayatayā traikālyaparāmarśa
sambhavāditi bhāvaḥ |
[p.62]
'bhidhānīyaḥ | nanu niyoga eva kāryyatvapratyayaḥ | evaṃ hi vyapadiśanti,
ācāryyaniyogaḥ kartavyo rājaśāsanamanuṣṭheyamiti | naitat | niyojyasya
niyoktṛtvaprasaṅgāt | niyogasya hi kartā niyokteti | api ca | yāge
niyogātkartavyatā tasya kutaḥ ? na hyeṣa tatra niyujyate |
svarūpeṇaiva sa kāryaḥ, na tatrā'nyāpekṣā viṣayavat | kiṃ tu yajeteti
yājayedityarthaḥ syāt | tathā ca svargakāmo'nyaniyogamātreṇa kṛtaśāstrārthaḥ
syāditi sādhu susaṃpāditamatrabhavatā | niyogārthe ca kārye svargayāgayoḥ
parasparā'sambandhastadanvayāt |
codayati—nanu niyoge eva kāryatvapratyayo laukikānām | kutaḥ ?
evaṃ hi vyapadiśanti, ācāryaniyogaḥ kartavyaḥ, rājaśāsanamanuṣṭheyamiti |
pariharati—naitat | kutaḥ ? niyogaścenniyojyasya kāryaḥ, tathā ca sati
niyojyasya niyoktṛtvaprasaṅgāt | kasmāt ? niyogasya hi karttā niyoktā |
yathā svāmyādirājñādeḥ | api ca yāge viṣaye niyogātkartavyatā tasya
niyogasya | kutaḥ kartavyatā ? yadyucyeta, niyogāditi, tatrāha—na hyeṣa tatra
niyoge niyujyate | niyogāntarā'bhāvāt | svātmani ca viṣayaviṣayibhāvā
'nupapatteḥ | bhedā'dhiṣṭhānatvāttasya |
śaṅkate—svarūpeṇaiva sa niyogaḥ kāryaḥ | na tatrā'nyā'pekṣāniyogā
ntarāpekṣā viṣayavat | yathā hi viṣayo viṣayiniyogakartavyatākṣepātkartavyaḥ,
yathāha—
yattu tatsiddhyarthamupādīyate tadvidheyamiti tantre vyavahāra iti, naivaṃ
niyogasya kartavyatā, svabhāvata eva tasya tattvādityarthaḥ | pariharati—
bhavatu, kintu yajeteti yājayedityarthaḥ syāt |
ayamabhisandhiḥ | na tāvadapūrvaṃ bhāvārthaviṣayaṃ kāryatayā 'vagamyamāna
svātmani niyojyaṃ prerayanniyogo liṅādyartha ityupapāditamadhastāt | tathā
cā''jñādaya evā'vaśiṣyante iti yajeteti liṅā kārya niyogamabhidadhatā
yāgaviṣayā''jñādirarthaḥ kartavya ityukta syāt | tasya cā'pauruṣeye vede 'pi
sambhavaḥ | na cā''jñādayo viṣayā'nuṣṭhānamantareṇā'jñāpayitrā kartumaśakyāḥ |
na khalu rājā viṣṭiṃ niyuñjānaḥ śibikāvahane bahati svayaṃ śibikām |
tataśca svargakāmo yāganiyogakaraṇamātreṇa kṛtaśāstrārthaḥ syāditi sādhu
sukaraṃ sampāditamatrabhavatā |
ataśca na niyogaḥ kārya ityāha—niyogārthe ca kārye svargayāgayoḥ
parasparā'sambandhaḥ | kutaḥ ? tadanvayāt | kāryarūpaniyogānvayādubhayoḥ |
[p.63]
ekaniyogasambandhinoraruṇaikahāyanīvanniyamāt | satyam | nā'nyakāmo
yāganiyoge | svargakāmaniyogo na yāgādanyatra | sādhyasādhanabhāvastvayuktaḥ |
nanu na niyogo viṣayaniyojyasambandhamakurvan kartavyatā'vagamāyā'lam,
rūpā'bhāvāt kaṃcitkvacinniyuṅkta iti hi niyogasya rūpamasyedaṃ kartavya
miti | na ca sambandho guṇapradhānabhāvādṛte sambhavatīti neṣṭaṃ paraniṣpattaye iti
svargasyā'parārthatvādyāgasya tādarthyam | iṣṭasaṃyukte tadarthā'nvayapratīteḥ |
karotu niyojyaviṣayasaṃbandhaṃ niyogaḥ, viṣayaniyojyaviśeṣaṇasaṅgatistu
kutaḥ ?
codayati—ekaviniyogasambandhinoḥ svargayāgayoraruṇaikahāyanīvanniyataḥ
parasparasambandhaḥ syāt | etaduktaṃ bhavati | na viniyogopamarddī niyogaḥ |
tadviṣayatayā tadavirodhenā'vasthānāt | pariharati satyam, nā'nyakāmo
yāganiyoge | svargakāmaniyogo na yāgādanyatra | sādhyasādhanabhāvastvayuktaḥ |
svargayāgayorekaniyogasambandhinorapi svargakāmayāgayoreva sambandho 'vagamyate,
na punaḥ svargayāgayoḥ | anavagamyamānaśca viniyogo na niyogaviṣaya
ityarthaḥ |
codayati—nanu na niyogo viṣayaniyojyasambandhamakurvan svayaṃ kartavyatā
'vagamāyā'lam | kutaḥ ? tadasambandhe rūpā'bhāvāt | niyogatvopādhika 
rūpaṃ darśayati—kaṃcinniyojyaṃ svargakāmādi kvacidviṣaye yāgādau niyuṅkte
iti hi niyogarūpaṃ viṣayaniyojyābhyāmasya nirūpyamāṇatvāt | tābhyāṃ
nirūpyamāṇatvaṃ darśayituṃ svarūpamapyāha—kāryatā''kāreṇā'pyubhayanirūpyatva
masyedaṃ kartavyamiti | na vā tayā 'pi niyojyadvārā svargasya yāgasambandho
guṇapradhānabhāvādṛte sambhavatīti neṣṭa paraniṣpattaya iti svargasyā'parārtha
tvādyāgasyā'niṣṭhatvena tādarthyaṃ svargārthatvam | tasmātsiddhaḥ svargayāgayoḥ
sādhyasādhanasambandhaḥ | nanvayaṃ tādarthye sati, tadeva kutaḥ ? ityata āha—
iṣṭasaṃyukte vākye svayamaniṣṭasya tadiṣṭārthā'nvayapratīteḥ | dūṣayati—
karotu niyojyaviṣayasambandhaṃ niyogaḥ, viṣayaniyojyaviśeṣaṇasaṅgatistu
kutaḥ ? yayorekaniyogasambandhastayorbhavatu parasparā'nvayaniyamaḥ svargakāma
yāgayoḥ, svargayāgayostu kimāyātam ? na hi svargo yāga iva niyogena
sambaddhaḥ, api tu niyojyaviśeṣaṇamityarthaḥ |
[p.64]
viśiṣṭasambandhāccet | jīvanāderapiprasaṅgaḥ | kimataḥ ? aṅgatvaṃ vā
nimittasya prādhānyaṃ vā | aṅgatve 'tideśaprasaṅgo nimittasya |
prādhānye kāmyanaimittikādhikārayoraviśeṣaḥ | ubhayoḥ kāryaniṣṭhatvānna
naimittikaḥ prayoganiṣṭhaḥ syāt | tathā cobhayatra sarvāṅgopasaṃhāro na vā |
niyojyabhedasamarppaṇena kṛtārthasya jīvanāderna viṣayasambandhaḥ syāt |
ārthastu nimittanaimittikabhāvaḥ | tasmiṃśca sati tatrā'dhikārāt svargakāmo
yajetetyapi tarhyevaṃ syāt | na bhaviṣyati | sādhyasyaiva viśeṣaṇatvāt |
kāmyatvāt | tathāpi na viṣayasambandhe pramāṇam | anyasādhyasyāpi
codayati—viśiṣṭasambandhāccet | yadyapi na sākṣātsvargo niyogena
saṃbaddhaḥ, tadviśiṣṭastu niyojyo niyogasaṃbaddha iti svargo'pi tanmukhena
niyogasaṃbaddha ityekaniyogasambandhādyāgenāpi saṃbadhyate | tathā ca sādhya
sādhanabhāvasiddhiranayorityarthaḥ | dūṣayati—jīvanāderapi nimittasya niyojya
sambandhadvāreṇa niyogasambandhe yāgādisambandhaprasaṅgaḥ | codakaḥ pṛcchati,
kimataḥ ? uttaram—aṅgatvaṃ vā nimittasya niyogaviṣaye yāgādau prādhānyaṃ
vā | tatrā'ṅgatve vikṛtau samidādivadatideśaprasaṅgo nimittasya | prādhānyaṃ
tu yāgaṃ prati sādhyatvalakṣaṇaṃ jīvanādernimittasya siddhasya na sambhavatyeva |
abhyupetyocyate | prādhānye kāmyanaimittikā'dhikārayoraviśeṣaḥ | kutaḥ ?
ubhayoḥ kāryaniṣṭhatvāt | tataśca na naimittikaḥ prayoganiṣṭhaḥ syāt | mā
bhūt, kā naḥ pīḍā ? ityata āha—tathā cobhayatra kāmye naimittike ca sarvā'ṅgo
pasaṃhāro na vā ? codaka āha—niyejyabhedasamarpaṇena kṛtārthasyaiva jīvanāderna
viṣayasambandhaḥ syāt | na hyasya viṣayasaṃbandhamantareṇa vaiyarthyam, niyojyāva
cchedakatayā'rthavattvādityarthaḥ | yadi niyojyabhedasamarpaṇena caritārthaṃ jīvanaṃ
na saṃbandhāntaramarhati, hanta bhornimittanaimittikabhāvamapi na bhajetetyata
āha—niyojyabhedasamarpaṇaparasyaiva jīvanādeḥ | ārthastu nimittanaimittika
bhāvaḥ | ārthatāṃ darśayati—tasmin sati tatrā'dhikārāt | dūṣayati—
svargakāmo yajetetyapi tarhyevaṃ syāt | niyojyaviśeṣaṇaṃ kāmo nimittatvena
saṃbadhyate, na sādhyatayetyarthaḥ | codaka āha—na bhaviṣyati kāmasya
nimittabhāvaḥ | siddhasya nimittatā bhavati, kāmasya tu sādhyasyaiva niyojya
viśeṣaṇatvāt | sādhyataivā'sya kutaḥ ? ityata āha—kāmyatvāt | yo hi
yadicchati tattasya sādhyam | icchāprayatnayorvaiyadhikāraṇyā'bhāvāditi bhāvaḥ |
dūṣayati—tathā 'pi na viṣayasaṃbandhe kāmasya pramāṇamasti |
[p.65]
viśeṣaṇatvopapatteḥ | icchāmātradṛṣṭeśca | nanu kāmino'dhikārā'vagatiḥ
kāmopāye'vakalpate, nānyathā tat niyojyena kṛtaṃ syāt | kāminā kṛtaṃ
niyojyena kṛtaṃ syāt |
na tu kāmaḥ pravṛttihetuḥ, adhikārahetustu | adhikārahetukā ca
pravṛttiriṣyate na phalahetukā, prāgadhikārāttadajñānāt |
nanu sādhyaṃ sādhanā'pekṣīti sannidhānādviṣayaḥ sādhanatayā'nena
sambhantsyata ityata āha—anyasādhyasyā'pi viśeṣaṇatvopapatteḥ | ayamabhi
sandhiḥ | samānapadopādānatayā niyogenaiva svaviṣayo bhāvārthaḥ kāraṇatayā
svīkṛto nā'nyatra tadbhāvamanubhavitṛmarhati | sādhyadvayasamavāyasyaikatrā'sambhavād
vākyabhedaprasaṅgāt | na ca svarganiyogayoḥ sādhyatvamanyathā nopapadyate, yena
sādhyavivṛddhiḥ kalpyeta | anyasādhyasyāpi svargasya niyojyā'vacchedakatayā
niyogasādhyatvapratipatterupapatteriti | api cā'bhyupetya sādhyatvaṃ kāmasyeda
muditam, paramārthatastu sādhyatā'pi na pratīyata ityāha—icchāmātradṛṣṭeśca |
prayatnavyāpyatā hi sādhyatā, na punaruddeśyatāmātram, nā'pi tadavinābhā
vinī sā | na khalu kāraṇānyavaśyaṃ kāryavanti vyabhicārāditi bhāvaḥ |
codayati—nanu kāmino'dhikārā'vagatirniyogā'vagatiḥ, pradhānaṃ
kārya pratyātmanastairaścīnyavijñānamiti yāvat, kāmopāye svargādyupāye
eva dhātvaviṣayiṇaḥ kāryasya niyogasya kāmyotpādā'nukūlatve satyavakalpale |
yasmānnā'nyathā kāmya'nupāyatve tatkarmma kāminā niyojyate sādhanaṃ kṛtaṃ
syātkintu puruṣamātreṇa | phalānukūlaniyogā'vagame hi vivṛddhe sādhye
ekasminniyogaphalarūpasādhanatayā'bagamya karmma prayujyeta | tadavaśyamevaṃ
vidhaniyogapratītisāmarthyādbhāvārthasya kāmopāyatvamabhyupeyamityarthaḥ |
uttaram—kāminā kṛtaṃ niyojyena kṛtaṃ syāt | kāmopāyastarhi
karmetyata āha—na tu kāmaḥ pravṛttihetuḥ | yadi hi pravṛttihetuḥ kāmo bhavettato
nā'nupāye pravarttate kāmīti pravṛttiviṣayasya tadupāyatvaṃ syāt, na tvasya
pravṛtihetutetyarthaḥ | kimayamahetureva kāmaḥ ? tathā ca vyarthamasya śravaṇamityata
āha—adhikārahetustu | niyogā'vagamanahetustu niyojyāvacchedakatayā |
kutastarhi pravṛttiḥ ? ityata āha—adhikārahetukā ca pravṛtiriṣyate, na
phalahetukā | kutaḥ ? prāgadhikārāttatphalā'jñānāt | niyogā'bagamasya punaḥ
[p.66]
nanu kāmino'dhikāraḥ | kāmā'nupāye ca na kāmanimittaḥ | adhikāro
na nirnimitto na vā'nyanimittaḥ | kāmanimittaḥ | satyasminnadhikriya
māṇatvāt |
nanvanupāye'dhikriyamāṇasya na phalārthitā nimittam | upāye
hyapeyārthitā niyoganimittam | siddhe tarhyupāyopeyabhāve niyogaḥ | na
niyogāttatsiddhiḥ | siddha evā'sau niyogātpratīyate | na | sāpekṣatvāt |
tathā hi—yadi viśiṣṭapuruṣaniyogaḥ śabdārthaḥ kathaṃ sa niyogād gamyate ?
atha phalaviśeṣanimittako niyogaḥ, nāsau prāgasiddhe tadbhāve iti prāksiddha-
purastāccetphalamavagantavyam, kṛtaṃ phalā'bagamena, niyogā'vagamādeva pravṛtti
siddherityarthaḥ |
punaścodayati—nanu kāmino'dhikāraḥ svargakāmādipadasambandhā
davagamyate | tathā ca kāmo nimitamadhikārasyetyarthaḥ | bhavatu, kiṃ tataḥ ?
ityāha—kāmā'nupāye ca na kāmanimitto niyogaḥ | nāpi nirnimitta
ityāha—adhikāraḥ kāryarūpa iti | na nirnimittaḥ | svarūpahāniprasaṅgāt |
na vā'nyanimittaḥ | kāmapadavaiyarthyāditi bhāvaḥ | uttaram—kāmanimittaḥ |
kutaḥ ? satyasmin kāme'dhikriyamāṇatvāt |
codakaḥ svābhiprāyaṃ kathayati—nanvanupāye'dhikriyamāṇasya phalārthitā
na hi niyogāvagamasya nimittam | kutaḥ ? upāye hyapeyārthitā niyogā
'vagamanimittam | dūṣayati—yadyupeyārthitā'nupāye niyogā'vagamananimittam,
siddhe tarhyapāyopeyabhāve niyogaḥ | omiti bruvantaṃ pratyāha—na niyogātta
tsiddhiḥ | tataśca seṣyate iti bhāvaḥ | codaka āha—siddha evāsau niyogā
tpratīyate | ayamarthaḥ | na tadavagamapuraḥsaraṃ niyogavijñānamapi tu niyojyasya
svargakāmasya yāge niyogā'vagamo'paryavasyan svargayāgayoḥ sādhyasādhana
bhāvamākṣipati | tena siddha evopāyopeyabhāvaḥ | kāraṇāntarānniyogena
jñāpyata iti | tad dūṣayati—na | sāpekṣatvaprasaṅgāt | tad darśayati—tathā
hi | yadi viśiṣṭapuruṣaniyogaḥ śabdārthaḥ | yo hi puruṣaḥ svarga upāyena
me sādhya ityavagacchati tadīyo niyogaḥ śabdārthaḥ | tarhi niyogātprāgayaṃ
viśeṣaḥ puruṣasyā'vagantavya iti kathaṃ sa viśeṣo niyogād gamyate ? atha
phalaviśeṣanimittako niyogaḥ, yāgasādhyatā svargasya viśeṣaḥ, tannimittako
niyogaḥ śabdārthaḥ | nirākaroti—nāsau prāgasiddhe'navagate tadbhāve yāga
[p.67]
tadbhāvā'pekṣaḥ śabdenocyata iti pramāṇāntarā'pekṣatvaprasaṅgaḥ |
upeyasyaiva cā'dhikāranimittatve jīvanādiṣvadhikārā'bhāvaḥ |
nimittatvapratītihetuśabdā'bhāvāccā'yuktamevaitat | niyojyaviśeṣaṇa
meva tu tasminsati niyogādarthānnimittamucyate | yadi ca svargakāmādayo
sādhyatve phalasya bhavitumarhatīti mānāntareṇa prāksiddhatadbhāvā'pekṣaḥ śabdeno
cyata iti pramāṇāntarā'pekṣatvaprasaṅgaḥ |
idamatrā''kūtam | na tāvatsādhyasvargā'viśiṣṭasya yāgaviṣaya
kāryā'vagatiryāgasya sādhyaṃ svargaṃ prati sādhanatāmākṣeptumarhati, niyogaprā
dhānyavirodhena pratyuta pratikṣepārhatvāt | pradhānabhūtaniyogasiddhyanukūlaniyojya
siddhyarthamanupraveśāt | cikīrṣitasyā'pi phalasya sādhyatā na niyogarūpakārya
prādhānyavirodhinī, tadadhīnapratītitvāt, kintu niyogakārye prādhānyā
'nukūlatayā tādarthyenaivā'vatiṣṭhate | garbhadāsopakāravat, iti cenna |
cikīrṣitā'cikīrṣitasamavāye cikīrṣitārthena cetanavyāpāreṇa taditarasya
tādarthyena vyāpārāt | aprādhānyāpādanena tadanukūlatā'nupapatteḥ | tādarthyena
niveśāttadānukūlyamiti cenna | svabhāvavirodhena tadanupapatteḥ | na hi jātu
santāpadūnasyopakartumupanidhīyamāno dahanastadanukūlatāmācarati | na cā'sukha
masukhasādhanaṃ vā'pūrva cikīrṣāgocara ityāveditam |
yattu garbhadāsavadityuktam, tadayuktam | dvau hi tatra prayatnau cetanadvaya
samavāyinau | tatra svāminaḥ prayatnaḥ svārthamuddiśya garbhadāsopakārāya
pravarttate | garbhadāso'pi ca svayogakṣemasamavahitasvāntavṛttireva pravarttate
svāmyarthe | garbhadāsasthānīyaścāyaṃ niyojyaḥ kāmī niyogaṃ kṛtyā vyāpnuyāt |
tathā ca kāmoddeśenā'sya kṛtiḥ pravṛttā garbhadāsasyeva svārthoddeśena svāmyartha
miva niyogaṃ vyāpnuyāt, na punaḥ prādhānyena | na ca svāmīva cetanāntara
matrā'sti, yatkṛtiṃ prati prādhānyaṃ niyogasya bhavet | tasmātkāmo pāyatā
bhāvārthasya na niyogā''kṣepalabhyeti sāmpratam |
yadi manyeta, niyoganimittatvamanupeyasya kāmasya na sambhavatīti yāgaṃ
pratyupeya evetyatrāha—upeyasyaiva cā'dhikāranimittatve jīvanādiṣvadhikārā'
bhāvaprasaṅgaḥ | api cā'dhikāranimittatāṃ kāmasyā'bhyupetyedamuktam |
paramārthatastu nimittatvapratipattihetuliṅādiśabdā'bhāvādayuktamevaitannimittatvaṃ
kāmasya |
codayati—mā bhūnnimittapratipattihetuḥ śabdo niyojyaviśeṣaṇameva |
[p.68]
nimittaparā na tarhi niyojyaparāḥ | tatrā'dhikārā'pratīteḥ prayogā'bhāvaḥ |
athā'rthādadhikārasiddhiḥ ? mudhā kartṛviśeṣaṇapakṣanirāsaḥ |
yathaiva hi kāmitayā'dhikāro'vagamyasāna upāyabhūte dhātvarthe tathā
kāmitayā kartṛtvaṃ nā'nupāyo yasya ca kāmopāyastasyedaṃ karmeti arthāt
kartṛniyamavidheradhikārasiddhiḥ | adhikāravidheriva kartṛniya siddhiḥ |
tasmiṃśca sati niyogādarthānnimitta mityucyate | pariharati—yadi ca
svargakāmādayo nimittaparāḥ, na tarhi niyājyaparāḥ | ca tvarthaḥ pūrvapakṣaṃ
vyāvarttayati | ubhayaparatve vākyabhedaprasaṅgāt | tatra nimittaparatve'dhikārā
'pratīteḥ prayogā'bhāvaḥ | atha nimittaparādapi śabdādāpekṣitatvādarthānni
mittavato'dhikārasiddhiḥ pratītiḥ, anyathā'nuṣṭhānā'bhāvena kāyasya
tattvavyāghātāditi | nirākaroti—adhikārasya siddhyartha tarhi mudhā
kartṛviśeṣaṇapakṣanirāsaḥ | na tāvat kartṛviśeṣaṇapakṣe kartteva kriyāṅgatayā
'vaśyaṃ tadviśeṣaṇamupādātavyam, yena samudramanodhyānavat phalepsā upādā
nasya sarvān pratyaviśeṣāt kartṛviśeṣo na labhyeta, api tu yathā rāja
tvajātiḥ śucitvādivatprayatnā'nirvarttyatayā'nupādeyā karttāramavacchinatti |
rājā rājasūyena svārājyakāmo yajete-ti | yasya cā'sāvanupādeyāpi
rājatvajātirasti, tasyā'rthādadhikāro, na tu brāhmaṇavaiśyayoḥ, na jāti
virahiṇoḥ | evaṃ phale nisargasundaratayā svabhāvādicchākarmaṇīcchā puruṣasyā
'nupādeyā'pi sambhavatīti tayā samudācarantyā prasuptatanuvicchinnecchebhyo
vyavacchettuṃ karttā cetanāntarebhyaḥ śakyata iti na vyavacchedyā'bhāvaḥ | na ca
caramayā kriyayā kartṛsaṅkhyayā vā''khyātapadagocareṇa karttureva prathamamu
pasthāpitatvāt kāryeṇa prathamamapekṣitatve'pi cā'dhikāriṇaḥ sāṅgaprayoga
jñānā'dhīnanirūpaṇatvād na drāgityeva pratītirityapekṣāyāmapi na prathamamabhi
saṃbandha ityupariṣṭānnivedayiṣyate |
evamapi kutaḥ siddhiradhikāriṇa ? ityata āha—yathaiva bhavanmate
kāmitayā 'dhikāro'vagamyamāna upāya bhūte dhātvarthe, tathā'smākamapi tathā
karttṛtvaṃ nā'nupāye | yo hi yadicchati sa tatkarotīti nyāyasyā'trāpi
sambhavāt | nanvevamapi kuto'dhikāraviśeṣapratilambha ? ityata āha—yasya
ca kāmopāyastasyedaṃ karmetyarthāt kartṛniyamavidheradhikārasiddhirasmākam,
[p.69]
iṣṭasaṃyukte ca vākye iṣṭārthe'nvayo na niyogārthe'nvayaḥ | ubhayārthe'nvaye
vākyabhedāt |
nanu na viniyogopamardī niyogaḥ | virodhā'bhāvāt | yathāviniyogaṃ
tatpratyayāt | tatra parasparā'nvitāḥ padārthā niyogaviṣayatāmāpadyante | na
cā'nvayo guṇapradhānabhāvamantareṇa | tatreṣṭatvātsvargaḥ pradhānaṃ karma guṇata iti
karmopasarjane svargakāmaḥ puruṣo niyujyate iti na niyogapradhānatā hāsyate |
karmaphalasambandhaścopapatsyate |
na ca kāme svataḥ pravṛtto niyojyapuruṣaḥ | upāyarahite'rthino'pya
pravṛtteḥ | na ca prāṅniyogādupāyopeyajñānam | na khalu viniyoganibandhanā
bhavatāmadhikāravidheriva kartṛniyamasiddhiḥ | dūṣaṇāntaramāha—iṣṭasaṃyukte
ca vākye iṣṭārthe'nvayo, na niyogārthe 'nvayaḥ | athobhayārthaḥ kasmānna
bhavatī ? tyata āha—ubhayārthe'nvaye vākyabhedāt | garbhadāsanyāyaśca na
sambhavatītyuktamadhastāt |
samprati jaratprābhākaramatamupanyasyati—nanu na viniyogopamarddī
niyogaḥ kutaḥ ? virodhā'bhāvāt | tamevāha—yathāviniyogaṃ tatpratyayāt |
tatra paramparā'nvitāḥ padārthā niyogaviṣayatāmāpadyante | na cā'nvayo
guṇapradhānabhāvamantareṇa, tatreṣṭatvāt svargaḥ pradhānam, karma guṇataḥ, guṇaḥ |
sārvavibhaktikastasiḥ iti karmopasarjanaṃ yasyā'sminsvarge yāgasādhye svargakāmaḥ
puruṣo niyujyata iti na niyogapradhānatā tasya hāsyate, karmmaphalasambandhaśco
papatsyate | sugamam |
nanu yāgopasarjane kāme na puruṣo niyojyaḥ | icchāta evāsya
pravṛttatvāt | apravṛttapravarttanāvyāpyasya ca tadbhāvādityata āha—na ca kāme
svataḥ pravṛttau na niyojyaḥ puruṣaḥ | kutaḥ ? upāyarahite'rthino'pyapravṛtteḥ |
satyāmapi kāmanāyāṃ pravṛttiraśakyā | kāmayanta eva hi sarvajñatāṃ pumāṃso
na punariyamanupāyā śakyaprāptiriti nāsyāṃ pravarttante | evamupāyajñānasya
purastāt kāmyā api svargādayo na pravṛttigocarā iti niyogastadupāyama
vagamayaṃstatra pravartayati tadarthinamityarthaḥ | na ca prāṅniyogādupāyopeyajñānam,
tasmādeva tadavagaterityarthaḥ |
[p.70]
svargayāgasaṃgatiriṣyate | tārtīyī sā, naiyogikī tveṣā | bhāvārthaśca niyoga
viṣaya iti pratyuddhṛtameva syāt | niradhikāratā ca syāt | viṣayasyānadhi
kārahetutvāt | viśvajidādau tadakalpanaprāpteḥ | vidheyatvalakṣaṇatvayorvirodhāt
prāptyaprāptibhyām | kartṛtayā cā'nvayātprāṅniyogānniyojyatvā'bhāvāt |
avaśyakartavyatāprasaṅgaśca | kāmasyā'tantratvāt | tantratve'pīcchā'pyavaśya-
tadetatsvatantrasiddhāntā'virodhena dūṣayati—na khalu viniyoga
nibandhanā svargayāgasaṅgatirupāyopeyalakṣaṇeṣyate bhavadbhiḥ | kutaḥ ? tārtīyī
hi sā viniyoganibandhanā saṅgatirnaiyogikī tveṣā bādaryadhikaraṇasya tṛtīye
'dhyāye sākṣātsaṅgatimanicchataṣṭīkākārasya saṃmateti rāddhāntavyākopaḥ |
api ca sopāyaphalaviṣayaṃ niyogamabhyupagacchata idamaparamaniṣṭamāpadyata
ityāha—bhāvārthaśca kevalo niyogaviṣaya iti pratyuddhṛtameva syāt | api
ca yadā phalaṃ niyogaviṣayastadā niradhikāratā ca syāt | kutaḥ ? viṣaya
syā'nadhikārahetutvāt | athā'dhikārahetureva viṣayaḥ kasmānna bhavatī ?
tyata āha—viśvajidādau tadakalpanaprāpteḥ | viṣayasyā'dhikārahetorvidya
mānatvāt |
nanu prayojanametat, pramāṇaṃ tu vaktavyam, tadidamucyate—vidheyatva
lakṣaṇatvayorvirodhāt | niyogaviṣayaḥ khalu vidheyaḥ, adhikārahetuśca lakṣaṇam,
tena hi lakṣitaḥ puruṣastadalakṣitebhyo vyavacchinno'dhikārabhāgī ceti |
athaitayoreva kuto virodhaḥ ? ityata āha—prāptyaprāptibhyāṃ lakṣaṇasya
prāptatvātprasiddhatvāt | aprasiddhasya tadanupapatteḥ | aprāptasya ca vidheyatvena
vidhiviṣayatvātprāptau tadanupapatterityarthaḥ | api ca viniyogaviṣayaścenni
yogaḥ, tathā sati tadviṣayānniyoganirūpaṇātprāgviniyogo nirūpaṇīyo tathā
bhāvārthaḥ | no khalvanavagata eva bhāvārtho niyoganirūpaṇāya prabhavati | yathā
ca yāgasvargakāmasambandhalakṣaṇo viniyogo niyogātprāṅnirūpito viṣaya iti
sa nirūpaṇīyaḥ | niyogā'vagamācca prāk svargakāmaḥ kriyayā saṃbadhyamānaḥ
kartṛtayaiva saṃbadhyate, paścāttu niyogapratītau niyojyāyeti karturadhikārāt
punarapi rāddhāntavyākopa evetyāha—kartṛtayā cā'nvayā tprāṅniyogānni
yojyatvā'bhāvāt | idaṃ cā'paramaniṣṭamāpadyata ityāha—yāgāvacchinnaviṣayaka
kartavyatā'vagateḥ | kartavyatayā ca svaviruddhasvabhāvavyavacchedādavaśyakarttavyatā
prasaṅgaśca sopāyasya svargādeḥ |
[p.71]
kāryā syāt | tasmātparasparamananvitayornniyogā'nvayaḥ | tadanvaye parasparaṃ
vākyabhedāt | ekaniyogasambandhādapi sādhyasādhanabhāvaḥ pratyuktaḥ |
nanu niyogādasyedamupakārakamityavagamyate, kathaṃ tasya karma syāt ?
ka ivā'syopakāro'nyatra kāmyāt ? upakārāntare'rthipadamanarthakaṃ syāt |
upakāryopakārakatve tarhi niyogārthaḥ | kimataḥ ? upadeśakatvaṃ hīyate |
sthitavastvanuvādāt sāpekṣatvaṃ ca | tasmādasyedaṃ kartavyamiti niyogārtho-
nanvayaṃ kāmopahitamaryādaḥ svargādiḥ, kāmaśca nā'vaśyaṃbhāvīti
kathamavaśyakarttavyate?tyata āha—kāmasyā'tantratvāt | niyogādavaśyakartta
vyatā, tayā ca svaviruddhasvā'bhāvavyavacchedādavaśyakartavyatā phalasya pratīya
mānā na kāmayanā saṃbandhamarhati nityā'nityasaṃyogavirodhāditi pradhāna
niyogā'nurodhenā'tantratā kāmanāyāḥ sambhavatu, na pramādapāṭha ityarthaḥ |
atha tu tantratā''stheyā ? tatrāha—tantratve'pīcchā'pyavaśyakāryā syāt |
tadviṣayatvānniyogasya phalādivat | upasaṃharati—tasmātparasparamananvitayo
ryāgasvargakāmayorniyogā'nvayaḥ | tadanvaye ca na parasparaṃ tantram, phalasyā'pi
sādhyatayā dvayorvākyabhedāt | ekaniyogasaṃbandhādapi sādhyasādhanabhāvaḥ
pratyuktaḥ | garbhadāsanyāyasyātrā'sambhavā diti bhāvaḥ |
nanu mā nāma bhūnniyojyaviśeṣaṇatayā sādhyatvena svargasya yāgasaṅgatiḥ,
adhikārapratītisāmarthyāttu karmaṇi svargasādhanatvamasya saṅgaṃsyata iti
codayati—nanu niyogālliṅāderasya puruṣasyedamupakārakaṃ karmeti siddhamava
gamyate, gamyatām, kimetāvatā ? ityāha—kathaṃ ca tasyā'dhikāriṇaḥ karma
syād, yadi nopakārakaṃ karma | karmajanitamupakāraṃ bhajamāno hi cetanaḥ karmaṇi
svāmitāṃ pratipadyate nā'nyathetyarthaḥ | tathā'pi svargalakṣaṇasyopakārasya
kimāyātami ? tyata āha—ka ivā'syopakāro'nyatra kāmyādupakārāt |
nanu jagati kimayamevopakāra ? ityata āha—upakārāntare kalpyamāne
'rthipadamavivakṣitārtha syāt | tathā ca śrutahāniraśrutakalpanā ceti doṣadvaya
māpatitamityarthaḥ | dūṣayati—yadi liṅāderasya puruṣasyedamupakārakaṃ karmeti
siddhamavagamyate, upakāryopakārakatve tarhi niyogārtho liṅarthaḥ | codakaḥ
pṛcchati—kimataḥ ? uttaram—upadeśakatvaṃ prasiddhaṃ hīyate | kutaḥ ?
sthitavastvanuvādāt | tataḥ sāpekṣatvaṃ cā'prāmāṇyanimittaṃ mūrddhābhiṣiktaṃ
prasajyeta | upasaṃharati—tasmādasyedaṃ karttavyamiti niyogārtho, na punarasye
[p.72]
'nuṣṭhānapratīteḥ, avyabhicārācca | naimittikaniṣedhā'dhikārayorupakārā'nā
śrayaṇāt | na ca niyojyārtha eva karmaṇi niyogaḥ | ata eva laukike cā'dhye
ṣaṇādāvabhāvāt | nanu viśeṣaṇaṃ svargo nopalakṣaṇam | bhavatu niyoge'syā
'nvayaḥ | na tu niyoga eva yāgasambandhaṃ vidadhāti | tathā nāma īdṛśaḥ puruṣo
niyujyata iti | yasyā'gnirgṛhāndahediti yathā | nanviṣṭasiddhyupādhau
damupakārakamiti | kutaḥ ? anuṣṭhānapratīteḥ, prasiddhopadeśabhāvā'virodhāt
avyabhicārācca | upakāryopakārakayostu niyogārthatve vyabhicāraḥ syāt |
kutaḥ ? naimittikātiṣedhā'dhikārayorupakārā'nāśrayaṇāt | na cā'pūrvotpatti
rupakāraḥ | tasyā'sukhasya hitā'hitaprāptiparihārā'sādhanasyā'nukūlave
danīyatā'bhāvenopakārabhāvā'bhāvāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanu jvaritaḥ pathyamaśnīyādityādau niyojyārthe karmaṇi niyogo
loke dṛśyate, tadupāyatvācca vede'pi niyogaviṣayasya karmaṇo niyojyārtha
karmatīcitetyata āha—na ca niyojyārtha eva karmmaṇi niyogaḥ | kasmāt ?
ata eva naimittikaniṣedhā'dhikārayorvyabhicārata eva | nanu loke pratīti
sāmarthyānnaimittikaniṣedhādhikārayorupakāraṃ kalpayiṣyāma ityata āha—
loke cā'dhyeṣaṇādāvabhāvānniyojyārthatāyāḥ karmmaṇaḥ | punarapi svarge
sādhyatāyāmupalakṣaṇavailakṣaṇyena niyojyaviśeṣaṇatāṃ pramāṇayati—nanu viśeṣaṇaṃ
svargo nopalakṣaṇam, yenātmānaṃ na kārye niveśayediti cet | kathaṃ na sādhya
tayā'nviyāt ? atha neti cet ? kathamasya na yāgaḥ sādhana ? mityataḥ
parasparasaṅgatiḥ svargayāgayorityarthaḥ | pariharati bhavatu niyoge svargā'nvayo,
yathā''ruṇyasya, yāgasya tu kathaṃ ? nanu niyoga eva svargasambandhaḥ svargasya
svaviṣayeṇa yogena sambandhaṃ vidadhāti | tathā ca svargayāgayoḥ sambandhaḥ 
sādhyasādhanabhāva iti | pariharati—tathā ca svargakāmayāgayoḥ sambandho
nāma niyogāt na tu svargayāgayoḥ | na hyetāvatā niyogasaṅgatistayorna
kalpyata iti tatsvarūpaṃ darśayati—īdṛśaḥ svargakāmaḥ puruṣo'sminyāge
niyujyata iti | īdṛśatve dṛṣṭāntamāha—yasyā'gnirgṛhān dahet sa yajeteti |
yathā āhitāgnergṛhadāhavata iṣṭau niyogo neṣṭergṛhadāhaṃ prati sādhanatāma
vagamayati, tathā svargakāmasyā'pi yāganiyogo na yāgasya tadviśeṣaṇaṃ prati
sādhanabhāvamavagamayitumarhatītyarthaḥ |
[p.73]
bhāvārtha niyogātphalasiddhiḥ syāt ? syādyadīdṛśo bhāvārthaḥ | na tvayame
vetyuktaṃ bhāvanāviveke |
naimittike ca phalaprasaṅgaḥ | yathā cā'smindarśane na karmaphalasaṃbandhasta
thoktaṃ bhāvanāviveke | 'ataḥ svargayāgayorasambandhānna puruṣārthasādhanajñānam |
puruṣārthasādhanaṃ ca jijñāsante prekṣābantaḥ | lokācca śabdārthā'bagamānna
codayati—nanviṣṭasiddhyupādhau bhāvārthe niyogātphalasiddhiḥ syāt |
idamabhisaṃhitam | yadyapi dhātuḥ karmmarūpamātraṃ gocarayati, pākacchedādi
padebhyastanmātrā'vagamāt | ākhyātopasaṃdhānāttu tadarthaḥ pūrvā'parībhūtapracaya
rūpaḥ pratīyate | na caikamupasaṅgrāhakamantareṇa tadbhāva iti phalamasyopa
saṃgrāhakam | tadidamākhyātamāprārambhādāphalotpatteḥ pūrvā'parībhūtaṃ samāna
jātīyakriyāsantānaṃ vā samānajātīyakriyāsamudāyaṃ vā'nyotpādā'nukūlatayā
bhāvātmānamavacchinatti, pacatītyabhidadhāti | tathā ca yajetetyabhimataphalā
vadhini devadattasmaraṇadravyā'vamarśanatadviṣayasvatvatyāgātmani yāgakarmaṇi
niyogātphalabhedā'pekṣāyāmadhikṛtaviśeṣaṇasyā'pi svargasya yāgasādhyatayā
samanvaya iti |
pariharati—syādyadīdṛśo bhāvārthaḥ | na tvayamevamityuktaṃ bhāvanā
viveke | tādṛśaṃ cet karmma niyogaviṣayastarhi tanniyogātprāgavagantavyam |
na khalvanabagatagocaro niyojyastadgocaraṃ niyogamavagantumutsahate | na ca
svargā'vadhiko yāgaḥ prāṅniyogā'vagamānmānāntareṇa pāka ivaudanā'badhiḥ
śakyā'bagamaḥ | na ca niyogāttadavagamaḥ | parasparāśrayadoṣaprasakteḥ | sati
niyogā'vagame tannirūpaṇam, tasmiṃstu sati tadabagama iti | yadyapi yāgarūpaṃ
pramāṇāntarasiddham, tathā'pi bhāvātmanā na tatsiddham | tena ca tadviṣayo, na
punā rūpamātreṇa | tanmātreṇa vā viṣayatvasamarthane nāsya svargasambandhaḥ syāditi
bhāvaḥ | naimittike ca phalakalpanāprasaṅgaḥ | iṣṭopādherbhāvārthasya kāmyavatta
trā'pyaviśeṣāt | na cā'pūrvamicchāgocara ityasakṛdāveditam | yathā
cā'smin darśane prakārāntareṇāpi na karmmaphalasambandhastathāktaṃ bhāvanāviveke |
nanu bhavatvasambandhaḥ, kā naḥ pīḍetyata āha—tena svargayāgayora
sambandhād na puruṣārthasādhanaṃ jñānam | tataḥ kimityāha—puruṣārthasādhanaṃ ca
jijñāsante prekṣābantaḥ |
tadanena prabandhena svargayāgayoḥ parasparā'saṅgatiprasaṅgād niyogasya
kāryatā pratiṣiddhā | punastāmeva prakārāntareṇa niṣedhati—lokācca śabdārthā
[p.74]
niyogapradhānatā | tatsiddhāvaniyogāt | nāpi pratipattā pratyeti tām |
viṣayasiddhāvapyananuṣṭhānāt | na hi gāmānayeti niyogasiddhirvivakṣitā vaktu
rapi tu viṣayasiddhiḥ |
anyalloke śabdavṛttamanyadvede | vivakṣāparo hi samanvayo loke
śābdānām, niyogaparo vede | ayamaparo gaṇḍasyopari sphoṭaḥ | yadi khalu
vivakṣāpare'nvaye śabdasāmarthyaṃ lokato'vagamyate nā'nyaparatvam | ananyapramāṇa
katvācchabdasāmarthyasya | vakturabhāvāditi cet | ayameva vivakṣāparo'nva
yastamanumāpayediti prāptaṃ pauruṣeyatvam | pratipattyabhāvo vā bhavantī vā
pratipattirnna śabdanimittā sāmayikī syāt, sāmānyatodṛṣṭajā vā |
tasmāllākṣaṇikaprayogaja eva tatrabhavato vibhramo niyogaḥ kāryya iti | api
'dhigamānna niyogapradhānatā niyojyakṛtiṃ prati | kutaḥ ? tanniyogasiddhāva
niyogānniyoktureva tatsiddheḥ | nā'pi pratipattā pratyeti tāṃ niyokturvākya
yogasiddhiṃ svakṛtervyāpyatayā | kasmāt ? viṣayasiddhau satyāmananuṣṭhānānni
yojyasya pratipatuḥ | na hyanyenā''nītāyāṃ gavītarastadānayanamārabhate |
tasmādviṣayasiddhau niyoktṛniyojyayoḥ sampratipatteḥ saṃbaddhaviṣayaparāmeva
sādhanatāmadhyavasyati, na tu niyogaparāmityarthaḥ |
codayati—anyalloke śabdavṛttamanyadvede | tadeva sphoṭayati—
vivakṣāparo hi samanvayo loke śabdānāṃ padārthānām, niyogaparo vede | tena
tatra niyogasyaiva kāryatetyarthaḥ | dūṣayati—ayamaparo gaṇḍasyopari sphoṭaḥ |
tamāha—yadi khalu vivapakṣāpare'nvaye śabdasāmarthyaṃ lokato'bagamyate tato
na vivakṣitādanyaparatvaṃ vedasya | kasmāt ? ananyapramāṇakatvāllokapramāṇa
katvācchabdasāmarthyasya nopāyāntaraṃ tadavagamāya vede sambhavatīti | śaṅkate—
vakturabhāvānna vivakṣāsambhavo veda iti cet ? nirākaroti—ayameva
vivakṣāparo'nvayastamanumāpayediti prāptaṃ pauruṣeyatvam | so'yamasmin pakṣe
lokavirodhalakṣaṇasya gaṇḍasyopari pauruṣeyatvaprasaṅgalakṣaṇaḥ sphoṭaḥ | atha
dṛḍhataramapauruṣeyatvaṃ tataḥ pratipattyabhāvo vā sphoṭaḥ | tadupāyā'bhāvāt |
atha svā'nubhavasiddhā vedavākyaśravaṇasamanantarā pratītirnā'pahnavamarhati,
tatrāha—bhavantī vā pratipattirna śabdanimittā sāmayiko | svataḥ siddheṣveva
vedavākyeṣu puruṣakṛtasaṅketanibandhanā taduccāraṇādarthapratītirityarthaḥ | yadi
tu sambandhurasmaraṇānna saṅketakriyāsambhavaḥ, tataḥ sāmanyatodṛṣṭarūpajā
vā | gavādipadayuktaṃ hi vākyamarthaviśeṣapratyayanimittamupalabdham, vaidikaṃ ca
tatheti tadapi pratyāyakamiti sāmanyatodṛṣṭādarthapratyayo vaidikādapi vākyā
dityarthaḥ |
[p.75]
ca na loke niyogamātraṃ pravṛttihetuḥ, sarvasya niyogādapravṛtteḥ | anuvidheya
niyogācca tadbhāvāt | anuvidheyatvaṃ niyokturanyaniyogāt pravṛtihetuḥ |
anuvidhānakāraṇaṃ cā'rthā'narthaprāptirihārādi pramāṇāntarasiddham | na ca vede
niyoktā'pi | kutaḥ punaranuvidheyaḥ ? nanu śabdaḥ | bhavatu nāma niyoktā, na
tvanuvidheyaḥ | hetvabhāvāt | na hyarthā'narthaprāptiparihārayoḥ śabdā'nuvidhāne
pramāṇamasti | niyoga iti cet | na | tasya prameyatvāt | niyogajñānaṃ tarhi |
na | tasya niyogaviṣayatvāt | tasya ca tābhyāmanyatvāt | na cā'rthādākṣepaḥ |
yatta niyogakarttavyatāyāmācāryaniyogaḥ kartavyaḥ, rājaśāsanamanuṣṭheya
miti laukiko vyapadeśaḥ pramāṇamupanyastam, tadapahasannanyathāsiddhyati—
tasmāllākṣaṇikaprayogaprabhava eṣa tatrabhavato vibhramo niyogaḥ kārya iti |
niyogaśabdena svābhidheyaviṣayabhāvārthalakṣaṇāditi pūrvārddha vyākhyāya kāri
kāyāḥ paścārddha vyācaṣṭe | api ca na loke niyogamātraṃ pravṛttihetuḥ |
kutaḥ ? sarvasya niyogādapravṛtteḥ | anuvidheyaniyogācca tatpravṛttibhāvāt |
tasmādanuvidheyatvaṃ niyokturanyaniyogātpravṛttihetuḥ | anuvidhānakāraṇaṃ
cārthā'narthaprāptiparihārādi pramāṇāntarasiddham | vede'pyanuvidheyo'stu
lokavaditi cet | ataḥ āha—na ca vede'pauruṣeye'pi niyoktā'pi dṛśyate |
kutaḥ punarayamanuvidheyaḥ ? dharmasyā'tyantā'siddherityarthaḥ |
codayati—nanu śabdo niyoktā'nuvidheyaḥ | śabdaniyoktṛtvaṃ nyarāsi
prathamameva pramāṇatvādityādinā | abhyupagamyāpyāha—bhavatu nāma niyoktā,
na tvanuvidheyaḥ | anuvidhānahetvabhāvāt | nanvarthā'narthaprāptiparihārāvanu
vidhānakāraṇaṃ bhaviṣyatītyata āha—na hyarthānarthaprāptiparihārayoḥ śabdā
'nuvidhāne pramāṇamasti | śaṅkate—śabdā'nuvidhāne satyanuvidhātuḥ puruṣasya
hitaprāptirahitanivṛttirvā bhavatītyatra niyogaḥ pramāṇamiti cet | nirā
karoti—na | tasya niyogasya prameyatvāt | na khalu prameyaṃ tattvena pramāṇaṃ
bhavatīti | punaḥ śaṅkate—yadi prameyatayā niyogo na pramāṇam, niyogajñānaṃ
tarhi pramāṇamastu | nirākaroti—na | tasya niyogaviṣayatvāt | tasya ca
niyogasya tābhyāmarthā'narthaprāptiparihārābhyāmanyatvāt |
yadi manyeta, mā nāma bhūnniyogaḥ | prameyatayā'rthānarthaprāptiparihārayoḥ
pramāṇaṃ śabdāvagatestvayaṃ cetanapravṛttiṃ vinā'nupapadyamānastasyāścārthā
'narthaprāptiparihāramantareṇā'sambhavāt pravṛttimarthānarthaprāptiparihāraprayojana
mākṣepsyate ca tadapekṣayā prameyamapi pramāṇatām, kārakaśabdānāmāpekṣikapravṛtti
tvādityata āha—na cā'rthādākṣepaḥ | kutaḥ ? hitā'hitaprāptiparihārarahita
[p.76]
anāptaniyogadarśanāt | kṣemyo'yaṃ panthāḥ, gacchatu bhavānanenaiveti yathā |
padārthā'nvayaniṣṭho niyogo loke tasya ca pramāṇāntaraviṣayatvāttadapekṣatvā
ttannibandhanā pravṛttirloke, niyoganiṣṭhastu vede, ato nā'nyā'pekṣā | uktottara
metat | api ca na niyogamātraṃ pravṛttihetuḥ | mithyā tarhi niyogajñānam |
na ca tadyuktam | kāraṇadvayā'bhāvāt | na vayaṃ niyogamavajānīmahe yena
mithyātvaṃ syāt | satyapi tasminpuruṣaḥ pravartate loka iva yasya kasya cinni
yogāt | na hi tatra niyogo nāsti | atatparatve tātparyamapramāṇakamityuktam |
nanu kartavyatā'vagamāt pravṛttiḥ | avagacchati ca niyukta idaṃ mama kartavya-
syā'nāptaniyogasya darśanāt | apathameva supanthānamākhyāya tatra prarttayatya
nāptaḥ kṣemyo'yaṃ panthāḥ, gacchatu bhavānaneneti yathā | codayati—
padārthā'nvayaniṣṭho'rthā'narthaprāptiparihāropāyatālakṣaṇasamanvayaniṣṭho niyogo
liṅādirniyujyate'neneti vyutpattyā loke tasya sādhyasādhanabhāvasya
pramāṇāntaraviṣayatvāt | pramāṇāntarā'pekṣatvāttannibandhanā sādhyasādhanabhāvā
'bagamanibandhanā pravṛttirloke | anāptavākye ca niyogasya mānāntareṇa
bādhitatvādapravṛttiḥ | mānāntarā'gocaraniyoganiṣṭhastu padārthānvayo vede |
sa ca pratītaḥ pravṛttau hitā'hitaprāptiparihārasādhanatāmanuvidhānakāṇaṃ
nā'pekṣate | pravarttanakarasatvāt | ato nā'nyā'pekṣā | na hi pramāṇaṃ
śabdastadavabodhane mānāntaramapekṣate | nā'pi niyogaḥ puruṣapravṛttau hitā'hita
prāptiparihārasādhanatāmityarthaḥ | tadetad dūṣayati—uktottarametat | vivakṣā
paratvanirākaraṇaprastāve |
yattu pravarttanaikarasatvamabhimataṃ niyogasya, tadapi na mṛṣyāmaha ityāha—
api ca laukiko bhavatu, alaukiko vā, sarvathā niyoga ityeva na niyogamātraṃ
pravṛttihetuḥ | codayati—na cetpravarttayati, mithyā tarhi niyogajñānam | na
caitaducitam, kāraṇadvayā'bhāvāt | pariharati—na vayaṃ niyogamavajānīmahe,
yena mithyātvaṃ vedasya syāt | satyapi tasminpuruṣapravṛttirloka iva yasya kasya
cinniyogāt | na hi tatra niyogo nāsti | atha na tanniyogaparaṃ lauki
kamapi tu viniyogaparamityata āha—atatparatve niyogaparatve laukikasya
lokā'dhīnā'vadhāraṇārthasya vaidikasya tātparyaṃ niyogaparatvamaprāmāṇika
mityuktam |
nanu na vayaṃ niyuktimātraṃ niyogaṃ brūmahe, api tu karttavyatām | sā
ca nirapekṣā pravṛttaheturvedādavagamyata ityāha—nanu karttavyatā'vagamātpravṛttiḥ |
[p.77]
miti | yastvagacchannapi anuṣṭheyaṃ nā'nutiṣṭhati sa sattve'pyarthā'narthaprāpti
parihārayorananutiṣṭhanniva na daṇḍairvāryyate | tadetadavimṛṣṭaniyogarūpam | tathā
hi | pravartanāmātraṃ niyogaḥ | sa ca liṅādyartha iti pravarttito'hamatreti
pratipattiḥ śabdāt | kartavyatā'vagamastu niyokturanuvidheyatvāt |
anuvidheyaniyoge mamedaṃ kartavyamityadhyavasāyāt | itaratra tu pravarta
nāmātrapratīteḥ | uktaṃ ca kartavyatāviṣayo niyogo na niyogaḥ kartavyatāmāha |
avagacchati ca niyukta idaṃ mama karttavyamiti | yastvavagacchannapyanuṣṭheyaṃ
nā'nutiṣṭhati sa sattve'pyarthā'narthaprāptiparihārayorananutiṣṭhanniva na daṇḍai
rvāryate | idamabhisaṃhitam, cetanāpravṛttilakṣaṇakāryonneyaśaktayo liṅādayaḥ
pravṛttihetvābhidhāyinaḥ | taddhetuśca svātmani karttavyatā'vagamo vidita iti
karttavyatāmabhidadhāti | tathā ca tataḥ karttavyatāmavagamyā'pyapravarttamānā
hitā'hitaprāptiparihāropāyatāmiva na daṇḍaiḥ parāṇudyanta iti | dūṣayati—
tadidamavimṛṣṭaniyogarūpaṃ vacaḥ | tathā hi | lokā'nusārādājñādirūpitaṃ
pravarttanāmātraṃ niyogaḥ | sa ca liṅādyartho, na punaḥ pravṛttihetuḥ karttavyatā |
tadavabodhe 'pi karttavyatā'navabodhāt | ananuvidheyaniyogena hi tatra niyoga
pratyayo nāstiḥ api tu karttavyatā'vabodhaḥ | tadavabodhe satyasati vidhura
pratyayo panipāte pravṛtteravaśyambhāvaniyamāditi | tasmātpravartito'hamiti
pratipattiḥ śabdātpravṛttihetuḥ | karttavyatāpratipattistu prekṣāpūrvato niyojyasya
niyokturanuvidheyatvāt | kasmāt ? anuvidheyaniyoge mamedaṃ karttavyamityadhya
vasāyāt | itaratra tu karttavyatā'dhyavasāyarahitapravarttanāmātrapratīteḥ |
atraiva jaratprābhākaronnītārthaṃ gurorvacaḥ saṅgacchata ityāha—uktaṃ ca,
karttavyatāviṣayo niyogaḥ pravarttanā niyokturddharmmaḥ, tamabhidadhāti liṅ, na
niyojyasya dharmaṃ karttavyatvaṃ pravarttanāyā viṣayamāha, viṣayasya viṣayiṇo
'nyatvāt |
navīnāstūnnayanti, anirūpitaniyogavyāpārasyedaṃ codyamityupakramyeda
muktam | karttavyatāviṣayo hi niyogo na karttavyatāmāheti | yāgakarttavyatā
'nuṣṭhānaṃ viṣayo yasya niyogasya sa tathā | tena hi niyogaḥ sādhyate, na
