punarniyogo yāgasyā'nuṣṭhānaṃ jñāpayati |
etaduktaṃ bhavati sākṣānniyoga eva karttavyatayā'bagamyate liṅādibhyaḥ |
sa tu yāgaviṣayastadanuṣṭhānamantareṇa karttavyatāyā aparyavasānāt tadanuṣṭhāna
[p.78]
tadviṣayatvādeva hi tarhi kartavyatā'vagamaḥ | na tāvadayaṃ śābdaḥ | śabdena
niyogamātrasyā'bhidhānāt | nāpyārthaḥ | sarvaniyogeṣvabhāvāt | tasmādi
damapi darśanamavimṛśyamānasundaramiti nādriyante vṛddhāḥ | astu tarhyanuvidheyo
niyoktā bhūtānām | sākṣātkṛtā''nuśra vikaśreyaḥsādhano hitakāmaḥ sarvajñaḥ |
mākṣipati | so'yamākṣepā'paranāmopādānavyāpāro niyogasyā''sthīyata
iti | tadetadapūrvātmani kāryarūpe niyoge satyupapadyate | tadabhāvaśca nipuṇa
taramupapāditaḥ | tadāstāṃ tāvat |
codayati—niyogasya tadviṣayatvādeva tarhi karttavyatā'vagamaḥ |
viṣayiṇo viṣayanāntarīyakatvāditi bhāvaḥ | nirākaroti—na tāvadayaṃ
śabdaḥ | kasmāt ? śabdena niyogamātrasyā'bhidhānāt | nanūktaṃ nāntarīya
katvāditi, tatrāha—nāpyārthaḥ | kutaḥ ? sārvaniyogeṣvabhāvāt | karttavya
tā'vagamasya | na khalvayamananuvidheyena śataśo'pi niyujyamāno'vabudhya
māno'pi niyogaṃ karttavyatāmavagaṃsyata ityuktam | upasaṃharati-tasmādidamapi
darśanamapi dṛśyamānasundaramiti nādriyante bṛddhāḥ | tatsiddhametat | vivādā
dhyāsitā liṅādayo na mānāntarā'pūrvakāryā'bhidhāyinaḥ, tenā'gṛhīta
saṅgatitvāt, yadyenā'gṛhītasaṅgati na tattadabhidhāyi, yathā vājipadaṃ kramela
kasyeti | agṛhītasaṅgaterabhidhāyakatve vyavasthāhetvabhāvāt | sarvasya sarvama
bhidhāyakaṃ sambhavedityavyavasthā | tadidamagṛhītasaṅgatitvaṃ vyavasthāhetvabhāvena
vyāptaṃ svavyāpakaviruddhavyavasthopalabdherabhidhāyakatvānnivarttamānamanabhidhāya
katva evāvatiṣṭhata iti pratibandhasiddhiḥ |
sampratyanyathā pratyavatiṣṭhate paraḥ | astu tarhyanuvidheyo niyoktā
bhūtānām, bhiṣaja iva tasya bhagavato niyogādarthā'narthaprāptiparihārārthena
pravartsyanti nivartsyanti ceti | kasmādityāha—yataḥ sākṣātkṛtā''nu
śravikaśreyaḥ sādhanaḥ | nanu sākṣātkṛtā''gamagamyaśreyaḥsādhano'pi kadā
cidanyathā brūyāt, na vā brūyāt | tatkathamasyā'nuvidheyatā ? ityata āha—
hitakāmaḥ pareṣāṃ svārthībhūta viśvavyāpikāruṇyo hi bhagavān vairāgyā
'tiśayasaṃpanno mātrayā'pi rāgādibhiḥ kleśairupakleśaiśca madamānādibhira
parāmṛṣṭaḥ | tadayamatimahati saṃsāraduḥkhapaṅke nimajjataḥ prāṇinaḥ paśyan
kathaṃ nāmaitānuddhareyamiti tadduḥkhena kathaṃ na dūyamāno vā hitā'hitaprāpti
parihāropāyatattve vidvān karaṇapāṭave sati kathamanyathopadiśet, nopadiśed
[p.79]
vā | athaivambhāva evāsya kutaḥ ? ityata āha—sarvajñaḥ prahīṇarāgādyāvaraṇa
tattvajñānaḥ khalvayamanavayavena viśvaṃ karatalā''malakamiva sākṣādīkṣamāṇaḥ
sarvajñaḥ sākṣātkṛtā''nupravikaśreyaḥ sādhano bhavati, bhavati ca hitakāmaḥ |
duḥkhato jagaduddharttukāmasyāsya sarvajñatāmantareṇa tadanupapattestatra pravṛtteḥ |
na ca sarvajño nāsti | tathā hi | tadabhāvavyavahāro bādhakapramāṇa
sadbhāvādvā sādhakapramāṇā'bhāvādvā bhavet | na tāvadvādhakapramāṇasadbhāvaḥ |
na khalu pratyakṣamasya bādhakaṃ rajatasyeva samāropitasya śuktitattvaṃ viṣayaḥ
sambhavati | tadviruddhopasthānā'sambhavāt | sarvajñatāyā atyantaparokṣāyāḥ
kenacidapi saha pratyakṣapratītena virodhā'navagateḥ | nāpyanumānamasya
bādhakam | taddhi vidhiniṣedhasādhanatayā dvividham | tatra vidhisādhanaṃ
svabhāvahetukaṃ kāryahetukaṃ vā na niṣedhe sādhye prabhavati | anyasādhanatve'nya
sādhanasyā'prasaṅgāt | anupalabdhiliṅgakaṃ tu pratiṣedhasādhanaṃ bhavet | tacca
svabhāvā'nupalambhasādhanādanyadiha sarvajñaviruddhārthāntaravidhānena vā tatkāraṇa
vyāpakaniṣedhena vā sarvavidabhāvaṃ sādhayet | na tāvatsvabhāvaviprakarṣiṇā 
sarvavidā saha kasyacidapi pratīyamānasya virodho dṛṣṭapūrvaḥ | virodhi
pratipattināntarīyakatayā virodhā'vagateḥ | virodhipratipattau vā nā'tyantāya
tanniṣedhaḥ | na ca pratyakṣā'nupalambhasādhanaṃ kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ svabhāvavi
prakarṣavati sarvavidi śakyā'vagamaḥ | ata eva vyāpakatvamapi kasyacid
duravasāyameva sarvavidi | nāpi vipakṣe bādhake pramāṇapravṛttiḥ kṣaṇikatvasyeva
sattve sarvavidvyāpakā'bhāvā'bagama -nibandhanam | tadabhāvāt | bhāve vā
niṣedho nāsti atyantāyatasya | na khalu khapuṣpaṃ kasyacidvyāpyaṃ sambhavati
vyāpakaṃ vā | na cā'nyamātranivṛttāvanyasya nivṛttiḥ | atiprasa
ṅgāt | yathāhuḥ—
anyasyaiva nivṛtyā'nyavinivṛttiḥ kathaṃ bhavet |
nā'śvavāniti martyena na bhāvyaṃ gomatā'pi kim ? || iti |
na ca sattvajñeyatvādayo sarvajñatāsahabhuvaḥ sahasraśa upalabdhā vyāptā
vivādādhyāsitasya puruṣadhaureyasya viruddhopasthāpanena sarvajñatāṃ pratīkṣipantīti
sāmpratam | sattvāderasarvavidvyāptyasiddheḥ | na khalu darśanā'darśanamātra
nibandhanaṃ vyāptivijñānam, mā bhūccaitraḥ śyāmo maitrītanayatvāt paridṛśya
mānamaitrītanayastīmavadityanumānam | tattanayatāyā aśyāmatayā virodhā
'bhāvena sandigdhavyatirekitvānnānumānametaditi cet ? na | ihā'pi
[p.80]
samānatvāta | na hi sarvavittayā saha sattvādeḥ kaścidvirodho, yenāsau na
sandigdhavyatirekaḥ syāt | dvayī hi virodhā'vagatiḥ sahā'navasthānaṃ vā,
chāyā''tapavat parasparaparihāro vā bhāvā'bhāvavat | na tāvadavikalakāraṇasya
sarvajñatvasyālokasyeva sattvādeḥ santamasasyeva bhāve'bhāvaḥ, yataḥ sahā'nava
sthitirūpavirodhasaṅgatiḥ syāt | tathā ca bhāve vā nātyantābhāvaḥ sarvajña
tāyā ityuktam |
nā'pyuttaraḥ | na khalu sarvajñatāsattvādiparihāreṇa sattvādayo vā tatpa
rihāreṇa bhāvā'bhāvavat pratyayapathamavataranti | na ca sarvajñatayā teṣāṃ vyāpti
rastītyuktam |
syādetat | upadeśapraṇayanameva bādhakaṃ sakalavastutattvadarśanasya | tathā
hi | upadeśo vikalpavijñānayonirvikalpaśca samāropitagocaratayā mithyeti
na tathā tathāgatavijñānaṃ bhavitumarhati | tadayamupadeśo niṣedhyasarvaviṣayatattva
vijñānaviruddhasamāropitagocaravikalpavijñānakārya iti viruddhakāryopalabdhyā
buddhādīnāṃ sarvajñatvā'bhāvasādhanam | upadeśā'bhāve vā vṛthā tatprasādhanaṃ tatsa
nnidhānamātrādvā caityavandanādicodanānāmaniḥsṛtatvābhyupagame'nāśvāsa iti |
tanna | vikalpasya sarvavijñānatvā'virodhāt | dvividho hi vikalpo viparyā
sapūrvo'tatpūrvaśca | tatra viparyāsapūrvo bhavādṛśām atatpūrvastatrabhavataḥ sarva
vedinaḥ | na khalvasau bhrānto vikalpavānapi tu viditasamastavastutattvavistāraḥ
karuṇāparādhīnamānaso duḥkhina upadeṣṭukāmaḥ | vacanamantareṇa tadanupapatteḥ
vikalpaṃ vinā tadasambhavāt tasyā'tāttvikatvaṃ vidvānapi tamāharati | yathā
nairātmyatattvaṃ vidvānapi vikalpya samāropitamātmānaṃ niṣedhati, nāstyātmeti |
yathā vā vaiyākaraṇapravaro gāvyādiśabdānasādhūn jānāno'pi pṛthagjanaprati
pipādayiṣayā prayuñjāno'pi nā'paśabdakārī | tadidamāhāryamasya rūpaṃ na
tāttvikamiti tattvavido nopadeśavirodhaḥ | na ca rāgādimattā | na khalu kṛpā
rāgapakṣe varttate | nanviyaṃ paraduḥkhaprahāṇeccheti rāga eva | maivam | na hīcchā
mātraṃ rāgaḥ, api tu cittamalamātmano darśanā''varaṇamabhūtaguṇā'bhinandanaṃ
rāgamācakṣate | na hyasati nityātmadarśane svārthatṛṣṇāpariplutaḥ kaścitpari
spandate | yathā hi cetraḥ svato bhinnaṃ maitramudīkṣamāṇaḥ svagatasukhatṛṣṇāpa
ripluto na maitrasukhāya ghaṭate | tathā pūrvā'parakṣaṇaviviktaṃ vijñānamātramātmānaṃ
sākṣādīkṣamāṇaḥ sannatipatitatadviparītalakṣaṇāntaropakāra karmaṇi pravarttate,
pravarttate ca svārthatṛṣṇāpariplutaḥ | tenā'vagacchāmo'vagacchati nūnamayamahamiti
[p.81]
na | tasyā'nupapatteḥ, ajñānācca | tathā hi—
hetvabhāve phalā'bhāvātpramāṇe'sati na pramā |
cakṣurādyuktaviṣayaṃ paratantraṃ bahirmanaḥ ||
yadyapyātmapratyakṣanivṛttirviprakarṣavatāmabhāvaṃ vyabhicarati | sarvapratyakṣa-
vibhinnānapi svacittakṣaṇānekatayā | tadidaṃ sahajamātma 'darśanaṃ nidānaṃ rāgā
dīnām | yathā'haṃ sukho bhaveyaṃ duḥkhī ca mā bhūvamiti ca mā bhūvamiti ca
tṛpyato yaivāhamiti dhīḥ saiva sahaja ta ttvadarśanamiti | na ca sarvavidaḥ karuṇā
'hamupakuryā parānityevamākārāpi sattvadarśanā'vayavā yasmānnātmagāmo phalā
bhilāṣā'yamapi tu svārthamanapekṣya paraduḥkhaprahāṇecchetyuktam | na ca sarve citta
kṣaṇāḥ pratyekaṃ paramupakuryāmahamahamityahamahamikayotpadyamānā apyātmadarśanā'
bayavāḥ | paropacikīrṣāyāstaṃ vinā'pyupapatteḥ | tattvadarśane copadeśasambhavā
tsvagatatā mantareṇāpi paropakārasya samīhitatvādapekṣitalakṣaṇatvācca phalasya
satsambandhasya pravṛtteḥ prekṣāvattā'vidhātāt | tasmādupadeśatattvavijñānayora
virodhātsandigdhavyatirekitvādvacanamapi na sarvajñatāyā bādhakamiti na bādhaka
pramāṇasadbhāvastadabhāvavyavahārasādhanam | nā'pi sādhakapramāṇā'bhāvaḥ | tathā
hi | pratyakṣā'bhāvo vā sakalapramāṇābhāvo vā syāt | pratyakṣābhāvo'pi
cā''tmanaḥ sarveṣāṃ vā, yadātmanastadā'pi dṛśyatāviśeṣaṇo nirviśeṣaṇo vā |
na tāvadātmapratyakṣanivṛttirdṛśyatāviśeṣaṇā sarvajñā'bhāvasādhanī | tasya svabhāva
viprakarṣiṇastatvānupapatteḥ prasiddhatvānnirviśeṣaṇāyāśca vyabhicārāt samasta
pratyakṣanivṛtteścā'siddhatvāt | na khalvarvāgdṛśaḥ sakalapratyakṣanivṛttiḥ |
asiddhatvāt | na ca pramāṇaṃ prameyasya vyāpakaṃ kāraṇaṃ vā, yena tannivṛttau
nivarteta | tasmānna tadabhāvasiddhiḥ | nāpi tadbhāvasandehaḥ | tatsādhanena
tatsadbhāvasya nivedayiṣyamāṇatvāt | tasmādanuvidheyapuruṣadhaureyaniyogādeva
pravṛttinivṛttī iti sāmpratam |
tadetannirācaṣṭe—na | kutaḥ ? tasyā'nu vidheyasya sarvavido'nupapatteḥ |
ajñānācca | pātite pramāṇadāruṇi yathāyathaṃ pariśodhayati—tathā hi |
hetvabhāve phalā'bhāvāt pramāṇe sati na pramā |
cakṣurādyuktaviṣayaṃ paratantraṃ bahirmanaḥ ||
tadetadvyākhyātumanā bhūmikāmāracayati—yadyapyātmapratyakṣanivṛtti
rviprakarṣavatāṃ trividhena kāladeśasvabhāvaviprakarṣeṇa tadvatāṃ rāmasumeruparamāṇvā
[p.82]
nivṛttirasiddhā | asarvadṛśaḥ sakalapramāṇanivṛttyā ca nā'rthā'bhāvasiddhiḥ |
avyāpakanivṛttyā'vyāpyanivṛtteraniyamāt | avyāpakatvaṃ cā'hetutvāt |
tanmātrānubandhā'bhāvācca | anyathā sarvasya sarvadarśitvaprasaṅgāt,
aviśeṣācca | vacanādayaśca yadyapyavirodhādanivartakāḥ tathāpi kāraṇanivṛttyā
kāryanivṛttipratīteḥ pramāṇā'nupapattyā tatkāryāyāḥ sarvārtheṣu saṃvido'bhāvamanu
mimīmahe | tathā hi | na pratyakṣaṃ cakṣurādijanma tāvatsarvārtheṣu, teṣāṃ viṣaya
niyamāt | kiṃcideva hi vartamānaṃ saṃbaddhaṃ ca tadviṣayo na sarve'rthāḥ |
yadyapi cā'nekavidhaprameyasaṃvedanādrūpato niyamo durnirūpaḥ | paṭumandatā-
dīnāmabhāvaṃ vyabhicarati | sarvapratyakṣanivṛttirasiddhā | asarvadṛśo'smadādeḥ
sakalapramāṇanivṛttyā ca nārthā'bhāvasiddhiḥ | kasmāt ? avyāpakanivṛttā
bavyāpyanivṛtteravaśyambhāvā'niyamāt | avyāpakatvameva kuta ? ityāha—
avyāpakatvaṃ cā'hetutvādakāraṇatvāttanmātrapratibandhā 'bhāvācca | hetudharmma
mātrā'nubandhe hi sādhyadharmo'sya vyāpakaḥ | yathā vṛkṣatvaṃ śiṃśapātvasya |
na ca prameyamātrā'nubandhi pramāṇam | anyathā prameyā'nubandhitve
pramāṇasyā'smadādeḥ sarvasya sarvavittvaprasaṅgaḥ | tathā satyaviśeṣā'smadādīnāṃ
bhavadabhimatena saha savavidā | prameyamātrā'nubandhipramāṇaśālitā hi
sarvajñatā | sā cā'smadāderapītyaviśeṣaprasaṅgaḥ | vacanādayaśca yadyapyanivarttakāḥ
sarvārthatattvavido vipaścitaścā'virodha stayā saha vacane sattvādīnām, tathā
'pi kāraṇanivṛttyā kāryanivṛttipratītiḥ | na hyasti sambhavo, nāsti dhūma
ketanaḥ, asti ca dhūma iti | pramāṇaṃ ca karaṇaṃ pramāyā iti pramāṇā'nupapattyā
tatkāryāyāḥ sarvārthasaṃvido'bhāvamanumimīmahe |
pramāṇā'nupapattimeva darśayati—tathā hi | na pratyakṣaṃ cakṣurādijanma
tāvat sarvārtheṣu sambhavati | kutaḥ ? teṣāṃ cakṣurādīnāṃ viṣayaniyamāt |
niyamabhedamāha—kiñcideva varttamānaṃ saṃbaddhaṃ tadviṣayaḥ | na hi varttamānamātraṃ
tadviṣayaḥ | svabhāvaviprakarṣavatāṃ paramāṇvādīnāmapi tadviṣayatvaprasaṅgāt |
ata uktam—kiñcidevāvayavi dravyādīti | tathā'pi kālavyavahitadeśavi
prakṛṣṭaṃ ca tadviṣayaḥ prasajyeta, ata uktaṃ sambaddhamiti |
etasmiṃśca niyame na sarvaviṣayaṃ vijñānamindriyajamityāha—na ca
sarve'rthā deśakālasvabhāvadhiprakarṣaśālinaḥ | tathā niyamāntare tu dūṣaṇamanabhyu
pagamādeva paribhūtamityāha—yadyapi cā'nekavidhaprameyasaṃvedanādrūpato niyamo
[p.83]
dibhedataśca nānādeśaparimāṇārthaparicchedānna deśataḥ saṃbandhaniyamaḥ |
parimāṇato niyamaśca | kālatastu nirūpyate | prāpyakāritve'vartamānasya
prāptyabhāvāt | aprāpyakāritve'pyarthasāmarthyasavyapekṣatvāt |
jñānotpattāvajātanivṛttayoranupākhyeyatvāt asāmarthyāt | tathā
durnirūpaḥ | tathā hi | na tāvadrūpavaccākṣuṣam, arūpasyā'pi saṅkhyādeścākṣu
ṣatvāt | nāpi rūpisamavetam | tadasamavetasya nīrūpasya kālāderapi
cākṣupatvāditi na rūpato niyamaḥ | nāpi pāṭavaṃ vā rūpaśabdādīnāmudbhava
samākhyātam, sphītā''lokamadhyavarttitā'rthasya aindriyakatvaniyamahetuḥ |
anudbhūtānāṃ mandānāṃ rūpaśabdādīnāṃ cakṣurādigatānāmandhakārasthasya ca
ghaṭāderagrahaṇāditisāmpratamityāha—paṭumandatādibhedataśca grahaṇā'grahaṇaniyamo
durnirūpaḥ | indriyapāṭavatāratamyena mandamandataramandatamagrahaṇāt | vṛṣadaṃśā
dīnāṃ ca nirālokavartipadārthagrahaṇāt | nāpi deśasaṃnidhimahattve dravyagrahaṇa
niyamakāraṇe ityāha—nānādeśaparimāṇārthaparicchedānna deśataḥ sambandha
niyamaḥ | cakṣuretāvati deśe saṃnikṛṣyate, naitāvatīti nāyaṃ niyamaḥ sambhavatīti
tulyavadbhūmyādigatakumbhādiśanaiścarādigrahaṇadarśanāt | parimāṇato niyamaśca
durnirūpaḥ | sūkṣmasūkṣmatarasūkṣmatamagocaratvasyopalabdheḥ | aṇoranupalambha
ityacodyam | yadeva bhavadabhimataṃ tryaṇukamokṣāmahe tadeva paramāṇumācakṣmahe |
tadavayavā'nupalambhāt tatkalpanāhetvanupapatteśca | dṛśyamānā'vayavasya ca
sthavīyasastaireva kṣodīyobhirārambhasambhavāt | amahato'pi ca guṇādeścākṣu
ṣatvānna mahimaguṇaśalitā taddhetuḥ | tadekārthasamavāyasya vā taddhetutve
tryaṇukasyā'grahaṇaprasaṅgaḥ syāt, tadabhāvātparasparā'pekṣobhayakāraṇatve vā
tryaṇukasya ca guṇādeścaikaikakāraṇaśālinoracākṣuṣatā syāt | parasparā'napekṣa
kāraṇatve tu tṛtīyasyāpi prakārasya sambhavo durvāraḥ | bhavatu vā svabhāva
viśeṣa eva cākṣuṣatve hetureka iti na parimāṇato niyamopapattiḥ | tasmānni
rūpaṇāntarā'bhāvāt kālatastu nirūpyate niyamaḥ |
tathā hi—indriyāṇi prāpyakārīṇi na vā, ubhayathāpi teṣāṃ varttamāna
viṣayataiṣitavyetyāha—prāpyakāritve prāpte ubhayāśrayatvenā'varttamānasyā'saṃta
āśrayatvā'yogāt | praptyabhāvāt aprāpyakāritve yadyapi prāpyāśrayatvena
nā'pekṣyate'rthaḥ tathāpi indriyāṇāmasatyarthe vijñānā'jananāt arthasāmarthyā'pekṣa
tvāt | jñānotpattau kārye ajātanivṛttayoranupākhyeyatvāt hetorasāmarthyam |
[p.84]
cā'tiśayānāmapi cakṣurādayaḥ svaviṣayameva deśataḥ parimāṇataḥ saṃkhyāto
vā'dhikaṃ bodhayeyuḥ nā'viṣayamajātamativṛttam | taduktaṃ vidyamānopalambha
natvāt | tathā hi | pratyakṣe rūpādivedane taccakṣurādiparatantraṃ tanniyamādeva
na khalvatīto'jātaśatruḥ samprati saṃtarpayati viprānannapānairutthāpayati vā yūpaṃ
mahāsaṃpannimittamanāgataśaṅkhaścakravarttī | yadyucyeta mā nāma bhūtāmarvāgdṛśāṃ
locanagocarāvatītā'nāgatau, yogavimalāñjanojjvalīkṛtalocanāstvāloca
yiṣyanti sakṛdeva jagadudaravartti vṛttavarttiṣyamāṇamarthamātram | acintyo hi
indriyāṇāṃ sāmarthyā'tiśayaḥ, prabhāvā'tiśayaśca yogasya |
yathā hi na cakṣurādīnāṃ deśataḥ parimāṇataḥ saṃkhyāto vā niyamaḥ tathā
kālato'pi mā bhūditi tatrāha—yadā kālato niyama iti vyavasthāpitaṃ tadā
atiśayānāmapi siddhāñjanādibhiryogināṃ cakṣurādayo'rvāgdṛśāmindriyāṇi
ca svaviṣayameva deśataḥ parimāṇataḥ sakhyāto vā'dhikaṃ bodhayeyuḥ, nā'viṣayama
jātamanivṛttaṃ vā | etaduktaṃ bhavati | yathaiva kāryamahetukaṃ na bhavati kādāci
tkatvavyāghātāt evamaniyatahetukamapi | na khalu aniyatahetukatvamahetu
katvādviśiṣyate | kāraṇasya hi kāryaprāgbhāvaniyamaḥ kāraṇatvam, taccā
'niyatanimittatve'nupapannam | na hi jātu yavabījamatyantaparikarmitamapi
kalamo'ṅkurāya parikalpate | nānākāraṇamekaṃ vṛścikādī ti ca sthūladṛśaḥ |
kuśāgrīyadṛśastu praṇihitamīkṣamāṇāḥ paśyanti tatrāpi kamapi jātibhedam |
tasmādyathaivakāraṇāntarāṇisvakāryaniya tasāmarthyāni svakārya evā'tiśayamanu
bhavanti nā'kārye nā'pyanyakārye tathendriyāṇyapi na svā viṣaye nā'pyanyaviṣaye
iti siddham |
atraiva pāsmarṣaṃ sūtrā'vayavaṃ yojayati—taduktaṃ vidyamānopalambhanatvā
diti | nanu mā pravarttiṣata bāhyānīndriyāṇi, manastu pravartsyatītyata āha—
mānasamapi pratyakṣaṃ na sarvaṃ bodhayati | bahirmanaso'svatantratvāt | yatra hi
manaḥ sthitaṃ tatra tena pravarttitavyam, svapratyayā'nuvidhānāt | āntare ca sukhādau
tat svatantramiti sukhādiviṣayaṃ tat | na ca sukhādayaścititayā svasaṃvedana
pratyakṣā iti sāmpratam | vijñānādanyatvāt | vijñānā'bhinnahetutayā tattva
meṣāmiti cet | na | abhinnahetujatvasyā'siddheḥ | indriyārthasanikarṣo hi
candanavijñānasya hetuḥ, sukhasya tu tadvijñānam, tadabhāve'pi vijñānamātrādeva
svapne sukhotpādadarśanāt | satyapi candanā'nulepe hemante tadanutpādādityeṣā
dik | tasmātsukhādiviṣayaṃ mānasaṃ bahirviṣayaṃ bhavituṃ nā'rhati, tatra tatpratya
yasya manasa indriyāditantratvādindriyādīnāṃ ca tadagocaratvāt |
[p.85]
niyatam | anyathā'ndhādyabhāvaprasaṅgāditi | mānasamapi pratyakṣamāntarasukhādi
viṣayam | bahirmanaso'svatantratvāt | svātantrye'ndhabadhirādyabhāvaprasaṅgā
dityuktam | tajjanmasamanantarapratyayasahakāryyapekṣaṇādyadi nā'ndhādyabhāvaḥ
sarvathā pāratantryaṃ na nivartate | tadviṣayatvāt | anyathendriyāntarajasahakā
riṇo'pi pravṛtteḥ sa evā'ndhādyabhāvaḥ | na ca tulyajātīyaviṣaya eva samananta-
kasmānna svatantraṃ bahirmana ? ityata āha—svātantrye rūpādau manaso
'ndhabadhirādyabhāvaprasaṅgāt | na khalvandhādayo nirmanaskāsteṣāṃ smṛtyādya
bhāvaprasaṅgāt | manaśceccakṣurādinirapekṣamīkṣeta rūpādīnna kaścidandhabadhirādiḥ
prasajyeteti | tadeva sphoṭayati—tathā hi | pratyakṣe rūpādivedane tanmanaśca
kṣurādiparatantraṃ tanniyamādeva niyatamanyathā niyamādyabhāve'nvādyabhāva
prasaṅgāditi |
uktaṃ śaṅkate—tajjanmasamanantarapratyayasahakāryapekṣaṇādyadi nā'ndhādya
bhāvaḥ | ayamarthaḥ | na khalu mano nāma cakṣurādivat kiṃ cidindriyāntaraṃ
rocayāmahe, kiṃ tu pūrvameva vijñānaṃ caramajñānasyopādānamācakṣmahe | tadeva hi
samaṃvijñānatvenā'nantaraṃ cā'vyavahitatvena samanantarapratyaya ityucyate | tatra
yadindriyajavijñānasamanantarapratyayasahakāriṇā tadindriyajaviṣayakṣaṇopādānena
rūpekṣaṇāntareṇendriyajavijñānasamānakālena janitaṃ svajanakakṣaṇaviṣayaṃ viśadā
bhamuparatendriyavyāpārasya jñānaṃ tanmānasaṃ pratyakṣam | na tadindriyajam, tadvyāpā
roparame bhāvāt | nā''ntaraviṣayam | indriyajaviṣayavijñānaviṣayakṣaṇajanita
kṣaṇāntaragocaratvāt | na cā'ndhavadhirādyabhāvaḥ | teṣāmindriyarahita
tayā tajjavijñānā'bhāvenopādānavirahāt | na cā'pratyakṣam, viśadābhā
satvāditi |
nirākaroti—sarvathā nendriyāntarapāratantryaṃ nivarttate svotpattau
mānasasya pratyakṣasya | kutaḥ ? yadindriyajavijñānaviṣayā'nantarakṣaṇo grāhyaḥ
tadindriyaviṣayajaviṣayajaviṣayatvāt anyathā tadindriyajaviṣayajaviṣayā'na
pekṣaṇe svotpattau mānasasyendriyāntarajavijñānasahakāriṇo'rthātpravarttamānasya
rūpavijñānasya sa evā'ndhādyabhāvaḥ prasajyeta | sahakāribhiḥ saha svopādeya
mupādānamapi karotīti sahakārītyucyate | yadyucyeta tulyajātīyaviṣaya eva
samanantarapratyayo bhavati nā'sajātīyaviṣayaḥ, tenā'yamaprasaṅga iti, tatrāha—na
ca tulyajātīyaviṣaya eva samanantarapratyayaḥ | kasmāt ? jātyantarasaṃcārā'
[p.86]
rapratyayaḥ | jātyantarasaṃcārā'bhāvāt | na khalveṣa jñānadharmo, jātyantara
saṃcārāt | na pratyakṣadharmo'vitarkitarūpādyapekṣaṇāt | na mānasadharmaḥ |
sukhādiṣu tadadarśanāt | api ca viṣayaniyamātpāratantryamastu sahakāriviśe
ṣā'pekṣayā veti pāratantrya yuktaṃ smaraṇe tathā darśanāt | ekendriyagrāhye ca
sajātau rūpāntaragrahaṇaprasaṅgaḥ | abāntarajātyapekṣaṇe bhinnasantānā''pātaḥ |
tadatatsantānavṛttyorjjātyabhedāt | tasmādekasantānakṣaṇe samanantarapratyayatāṃ
bhāvaprasaṅgāt | na tāvannirupādānaṃ vijñānaṃ bhavitumarhati | na cā'niyatopā
dānam | uktametat, aniyatahetukatvamahetukatvānnā'tiricyata iti | tadiha
pūrvabuddhibhāvā'bhāvā'nuvidhānāduttarabuddhibhāvā'bhāvayoḥ saivopādānamiti
yuktam | tataśca śuklavijñānā'nantaramupajāyamānaṃ ghanasāraśalākāyāṃ śīta
sparśavijñānaṃ śuklavijñānopādānameṣitavyam | anyathā śuklavijñānajātīyasya
saṃtānasya śiśirajñānajātyantarasaṃcārā'bhāvaḥ syādityarthaḥ |
tadetadvibhajate—na khalveṣa jñānamātrasya dharmaḥ | kutaḥ ? jātyantara
saṃcārāt | na pratyagdharmaḥ | kasmāt ? avikalpitarūpārthā'pekṣaṇāt |
avikalpitasya rūpasya sākṣātkārāttadviṣayapūrvajñānasaṃbhave tu śabdādismṛtau
satyāṃ vā''locanamātraṃ bhavedityarthaḥ | na mānasapratyakṣadharmaḥ | kasmāt ?
sukhādiṣu tadviṣayapūrvajñānā'darśanāt |
evaṃ tāvaduktena krameṇa sahakāriviśeṣā'pekṣayā kṣaṇāntaraviṣayatve'pi
pāratantryamāpāditaṃ manasaḥ samprati vimarśapūrvakamindriyaviṣayatayaiva pāratantrya
mavadhārayati—api ca viṣayaniyamātpāratantryamastu sahakāriviṣayā'pekṣayā
veti vimṛśyā'bhimatamavadhārayati—pāratantryaṃ yuktam | ya eva yadindriyaviṣayaḥ
tatraiva manastadindriyatantraṃ pravartata iti yuktamityarthaḥ | kasmāt ? smaraṇe tathā
darśanāt | api ca tulyajātīyaviṣayaṃ samanantarapratyayaṃ mānasapratyakṣakāraṇaṃ
bruvāṇo na tāvadekendriyagrāhyatayā tulyajātīyatāṃ vaktumarhati, atiprasaṅgā
dityāha—ekendriyagrāhye ca sajātāvabhyupagamyamāne rūpāntaragrahaṇaprasaṅgaḥ,
pītadarśanamapi cākṣuṣaṃ mānasapratyakṣaṃ cakṣurasannikṛṣṭe'pi nīle prasuvīta, tadi
ndriyagrahaṇā'rhatvasyobhayatrā'viśeṣādityarthaḥ | yadi tvavāntarajātirāsthīyeta
na cāsau rūpāntara iti nā'tiprasaṅgastatrāha—avāntarajātyapekṣaṇe hi bhinna
santānajñānā''pātaḥ | ekanīlotpalasantānavartyekanīlotpalakṣaṇaviṣayamindriyā
'sannikṛṣṭasaṃtānāntaravartinīlotpalakṣaṇaviṣayamapi viśadaṃ mānasaṃ janayet |
kutaḥ ? tadasantānavṛttyā nīlotpalakṣaṇayorjātyabhedāt | upasaṃharati—tasmāde
kanīlotpalasantānavarttikṣaṇaviṣayendriyajajñāne samanantarapratyayatvaṃ tadindriya
[p.87]
vadatā cakṣurādiviṣayagrāhitvamevoktaṃ bhavati | kṣaṇavivekapratibhāsanādabheda
pratibhāsanāt kṣaṇikatvapratikṣepāt |
viṣayajñānaviṣayajaviṣayasya mānasasya vadatā'kāmenāpi cakṣurādiviṣaya
grāhitvamevoktaṃ bhavati | tathā cā'syendriyapāratantryamiti siddham |
na caitāvatā'pi cakṣurādiviṣayatvameva bhaviṣyati, na cakṣurādiviṣaya
grāhitvamityata āha—kṣaṇavivekapratibhāsanāt | idamabhisaṃhitam | kṣaṇikatve
hi siddhe bhāvānāmeva tadbhavedapi, na tvetadasti | tathā hi | asya sādhanaṃ
pratyakṣamanumānaṃ vā bhavet | pramāṇāntarā'bhāvāt | pramāṇasya sato'traivā'nta
rbhāvāt | anantarbhāve tattvā'nupapatteriti sthiteḥ | tatra na tāvatpratyakṣaṃ
laukikaṃ nīlotpalādivartikṣaṇikatāṃ gocarayati | nanu na nīlādanyat kṣaṇikatvaṃ
nāma vastusat | arthakriyābhede hi tathā syāt sitādivat | na ca kṣaṇi
katvasya nīlasādhyāyā arthakriyāyā bhinnā samasti arthakriyā, yā svalakṣaṇa
bhedenā'vatiṣṭheta | kṣaṇikatvaṃ pītādivannīlasādhyayaiva cedarthakriyayā tattatvato
nīlādbhidyate | na bhidyate cennūnaṃ nīlagrāhiṇā pratyakṣeṇaiva kṣaṇikatvaṃ gṛhītaṃ
tadavyatirekāt | tadanupapannam anubhavā'nārohāt na khalvanubhavo vyākhyāna
marhati | sa ca nīlamiti na tu kṣaṇikavacanamiti | tadavyatirekāttadgocaratve
sthemno'pi tadabhinnasya tattvaprasaṅgaḥ | mānāntarāttu kṣaṇikasya tadbhedasiddhau
pratyakṣasiddhyabhidhānaṃ pralāpaḥ | tatra tasyā'vasāyā'janakatvāt | avasāyā'
nanugatavyāpārasya pratyakṣasya pravṛttiviṣayaṃ prati prāmāṇyā'nabhyupagamāt |
syādetat | anubhavārūḍhaiva kṣaṇamātravartitā nīlādīnām | tathā hi
pratyakṣavijñānaṃ kṣaṇikaṃ svasamayenaivā'vacchinnaṃ paricchinatti | tadyathā |
nīlā''kāramālocanaṃ nīlaṃ paricchindat tadabhāvaṃ vyavacchindat tadabhāvā'
vyabhicāriṇaḥ pītādīnapi vyavacchinatti | tathā'stasamayā'vacchinnaṃ nīlaṃ
paricchindat svasamaya varttitā'bhāvamapyasya vyavacchindat tadabhāvā'vinā
bhāvinīṃ tatpūrvā'parasamayavartitāmasya vyavacchinatti | tadavyavacchede pītā
dīnāmapyavyavacchedaḥ | tathā ca nīlasādhyā'rthakriyā'rthino na kvacideva
niyamena pravarteran | tadanubhavasiddhaiva kṣaṇikatā bhāvānāmiti |
tadanupapannam | nīlamātragocaratvādālocanasya | na khala tadātmānamapi
gocarayati, davīyasī tvasya svasamayagocaratā | svasaṃvedanā'bhyupagame'pyātmā
'nātmaprakāśanamātraṃ nātmānaṃ samayenā''tmanā vā nīlaṃ samayena vā ghaṭayet |
[p.88]
ālocanamātratayā viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvā'viṣayatvāt | ālocanasyāpi
tatsamayā'vicchede'pi nīlādīnāṃ naikakṣaṇamātravarttitvasiddhiḥ | ālocana
syāpi dvitrādikṣaṇavartitvāt ekakṣaṇamātrā'vasthānā'siddheḥ | na caikasya
kramavadanekasambandho virudhyata iti nivedayiṣyate | tatsiddhaṃ pratyakṣeṇa kṣaṇa
vivekā'pratibhāsanānnendriyaviṣayajaviṣayatā mānasasya kintvindriya 
viṣayagrāhitvameveti |
nanu mā nāma kṣaṇabhedaḥ pratibhāsatām, tadabhedastu kuta ? ityata āha—
abhedapratibhāsanāt | so'yaṃ sphaṭika iti pratyabhijñaiva bhagavatī sthāpayiṣyati
sphaṭikam | nanviyaṃna sphaṭikasya pūrvā'parakṣaṇā'vasthānalakṣaṇaṃ sthiratvaṃ
yocarayitumarhati | na khalvetadvijñānamekam, kāraṇabhedād viṣayabhedāt svabhāva
virodhācca | tathā hi | sa iti pārokṣyamayamiti ca sākṣātkāraḥ | na
tvanayoḥ svabhāvaviruddhayordahanatuhinayoriva śakyā śakreṇāpyekatā''pāda
yitum | trailokyasyaikyaprasaṅgāt | satyapi ca sphaṭika iti vyapadeśā'bhede
vaiśakālabhedād viruddhadharmmasaṃsargādanayorgocaro bhidyate | na ca saṃskāraḥ pūrvā
'nubhavayonitayā tadgocaraniyataḥ sampratitanīmananubhūtacarīmasya sattāmavaga
mayitumarhati | na cendriyāṇi sannihitaviṣayāṇi prāktanīṃ gocarayanti
tattāspadasya sattāmasannihitām | na ca kāraṇabhede'pi kāryā'bhedasambhavaḥ |
kāraṇabhedā'bhedā'dhīnatvāttadbhedā'bhedayoḥ anyathā''kasmikatvā''patteḥ | tatte
dantāspade ca svagocaramātracāriṇī smaraṇagrahaṇe na parasparasya gocaraṃ
sambhintaḥ | na cā''bhyāmanyadekaṃ vijñānamubhayaviṣayā'saṃbhedasamarthaṃ sambhava
tyuktādeva kramāt | na cā'sya prāmāṇyam, vikalpatvenā'vastunirbhāsatvāt
smārttādaviśeṣācca | tasmātpratyabhijñāṃ sthāpayati bhāvāniti manoratha
mātram |
atrocyate | ekamevedaṃ vijñānaṃ pratyabhijñāsamākhyānam | nanūktaṃ
svabhāvavirodhānneti, tatkimidānīṃ bhavatāmanumānavijñānamapi naikam ?
tadapi hi pratyakṣamapratyakṣaṃ vā vikalpo'vikalpaśca samāropo'samāropaśca |
svā'nubhavā'vasthāpitā'bhedasya svarūpasya tadgrāhyabhedā'pekṣayā pratyakṣatvādī
nāmavirodha iti cet | na | ihāpi sāmyāt | na khalvetadapi vijñānaṃ
tattedantā'dhikaraṇamekamābhyāmanuraktaṃ sphaṭikaṃ gocarayati, yadabhinnaṃ nā
nubhūyate anubhūyate vā | ekatve'pi ca vastunastadanurañjakatattedantābhe dāpekṣayā
[p.89]
pratyakṣatāparokṣate na virotsyete | sahasambhavādvijñānaikatvasya ca pramāṇasiddha
tvāt | na ca sa iti pūrvadeśakālasaṃsargo'yamiti ca sannihitadeśakālasaṃsarga
ekasya virudhyate | ato yuktaṃ yatpadmarāgasya svarūpe paricchidyamāne tadabhāvo
vyavacchidyata iti | tadavyavacchede tatsvarūpā'paricchedāt svapracyutivyavaccheda
svabhāvatvāt padmarāgabhāvasya | tadavyavacchede tatparicchedā'nupapatteḥ | kasmā
tpunastadanye puṣparāgādayo vyavacchidyante ? tadabhāvā'vinābhāvāditi cet,
sa eva kutaḥ ? pratyakṣaṇa kadācidapi puṣparāgadmarāgayostādātmyā'nu
balambhāditi cet | yatra tarhi satastādātmyapratītiḥ tatra na tadabhāvā'vinā
bhāvaḥ | samasti ca so'yaṃ padmarāga iti deśakālabhedā'nugatamekaṃ padmarāga
tattvamavabhāsayantī sākṣātkāravatī pratītiḥ | tena bhavatu deśakālayostatsaṃ
sargayorvā parasparamatadātmatvaṃ na tu tadavacchinnasya padmarāgasya | tasya
bhāvātsaṃsargābhyāṃ cā'nyatvāt | tato'nyatve tatsaṃsargayoḥ kutastadīyatvamiti
cet | svabhāvādeveti saṃsargaparīkṣāyāṃ nipuṇataramupapādayiṣyate | tasmāttatte
dantā''spadasya padmarāgasya tadavabhāsino'nubhavasya caikatvamanubhavādvā
anumānādvā tadavabhāsalakṣaṇaphalā'bhedajanmanaḥ siddham | viśadatarakārya
siddhau cā'pratīyamānamapi kāraṇaṃ kalpanīyaṃ na punarapratīyamānakalpanābhayāt
kāryavaiśadyamapahnotumucitam | rūpādivijñaptīnāmapyapahnavaprasaṅgāt | tadiha
yadyapīndriyaṃ kevalamasamarthaṃ yadyapi ca saṃskāramātraṃ saṃskārasadhrīcīnaṃ tvindriyaṃ
bhāvayiṣyati pratyabhijñātadbhāvā'bhāvā'nuvidhānāt pratyabhijñābhāvā'bhāvayoḥ |
na hi nā'jījanad bījamaṅkuramiti mṛdādisahitamapi na janayati | tena
bhavatu sthirasya bhāvasya sahakāripratyaya samavadhānasamāsāditā'tiśayasya
kāryakāritā, bhavatu vopasarpaṇaṃ pratyayaparamparālabdhatathātvādabhedasya, ubhayathā
'pi locanādīnāṃ saṃskārasahitānāmanubhavavāsanābhedasahabhuvāṃ vā pratyabhijñāna
jananasāmarthyasambhavaḥ | anubhavavāsanāmātrajatve'yamityanupapatterindriyamātra
jatve vā sa iti anarthajatve vā arthasaṃnidhānā'saṃnidhānābhyāṃ tatpratibhāsabhedā
'nupapatteḥ | na caivamatītakālasambandhitendriyāṇām | tadasambandhe'pyatīta
kālatāyāstatpāramparyasambandhasaṃskāradvāreṇā'babhāsopapatteḥ | na caivamatītakā
latā na pratyakṣeti sāmpratam | indriyajavijñānaviṣayatvāt tanmātrānubandhi
tvācca pratyakṣatāyā indriyasambandhatve | tanmātrā nubandhitve tu tajja
[p.90]
vijñānā'viṣayāṇāmapi tadgandhādīnāṃ paramātmamanaḥprabhṛtīnāmapratyakṣatva
prasaṅgaḥ | tasmānna kāraṇā'bhāvaḥ | nāpi sāmagrībhedaḥ, yataḥ kāryabhedaḥ
syāt | tasmin janayitavye niyamena tatpūrvabhāvināṃ saṃskārendriyādīnāmekatra
sāmagrībhāvāt | tatsiddhamabhedadarśanaṃ pratyabhijñānātmakamekamanekadeśakālā
'vasthāsambandhaṃ padmarāgādi gācarayatīti | ca cedaṃ smārttam, pūrvā'paradeśakālā
'vasthānugateradhikārāta | na ca vikalparūpatayā'syā'prāmāṇyam, abhilāpa
saṃsargayogyapratibhāsaprāmāṇyayoravirodhāt | samāropitagocaratvasyā'sati
vādhake'nupapatteḥ | na canadanusandhānamātraṃ yathā bhinnānāmapi yajamānānāmekī
bhūtā vayamiha satre iti vyapadeśamātraṃ tatkenacidupādhinā na tvekatvāvagamaḥ |
iha tu deśakālā'vasthāsaṃsargabhede'pi paśyantyekaṃ padmarāgatattvamabhinnadeśakāla
miva laukikaparīkṣakāḥ | tatsiddhamabhedapratibhāsanāditi |
nanvastvabhedapratibhāsaḥ, na punarayamakṣaṇikatāmāvahati bhāvādīnām |
bādhake sati samāropitagocaratvāt | asti hi bādhakaṃ yat sattat kṣaṇikaṃ
yathā ghaṭādi | saṃśca vivādā''spadībhūtaḥ śabdādiriti svabhāvahetuḥ |
bhāvamātrā'nubandhisādhyaviṣayatvāt | kathaṃ punariha sattāmātrā'nubandhasiddhiḥ
kṣaṇikatāyāḥ pūrvā'parabhāgavikalakālamātrā'vasthitilakṣaṇāyāḥ ? | ittham |
tathā hi | sarvopākhyārahitaviparītarūpanirūpaṇā sattā bhāvānāṃ nā'rthakriyā
kāritāyā atiricyate | no khalu samastā'rthakriyā'nupayogibhāvasaṃbhavaḥ |
tathā hi—svarūpamātrā'nubandhisārvajñavijñānagocaro na vā ? agocaraścenni
rupākhyo na bhāvaḥ | tadviṣayatāvyāptasya vasturūpasya tannivṛttau nivṛtteḥ |
tadgocaratve vā kathamanālambanam ? ālambanaṃ cet kathamapratyayaḥ ? pratyaya
ścetkathamarthakriyāyāṃ nopayujyate ? tadarthakriyākāritaiva sattā bhāvānām |
sā ca kramā'kramābhyāṃ vyāptā | tṛtīyaprakāravirahāt |
tathā hi | bhāvānāṃ tāsu tāsvarthakriyāsu kramā'kramau paraspara
parihāravantau tyakṣata eva cakāstaḥ | tathā ca prakārāntaramapi kramā
'kramātmakamīdṛśābhyāmevā'nubhūtā'vasitābhyāṃ kramā'kramābhyāṃ vyatirikta
maṅgīkriyeta, tato dṛśyamānayostayorapi kramā'kramayoḥ parasparasaṅkara
prasaṅga iti dṛśyamānaḥ kramo'kramātmako'kramaśca kramātmaka iti tathaivā'nu
bhavakriye prasajyeyātām | tadevaṃ parasparaparihārabantau kramā'kramau paricchindat
[p.91]
pratyakṣameva pratikṣipati, na mṛṣyati ca prakārāntaramīdṛkkramā'kramasaṃkīrṇa
mekam | tadanupalambhaśca dṛśyaviśeṣaṇarahito'pi dṛśyamānatādātmyaniṣe
dhātmakatayā tadabhāvavyavahārāya kalpate | dṛśyamānastambhatādātmyena svayama
dṛśyānāṃ piśācaplavaṅgamādīnāmaviśeṣeṇā'nupalambhamātreṇā'sadvyavahārasiddheḥ |
anevaṃbhūtakramayaugapadyavyatirekavati tu prakārāntare kramayaugapadye eva na
saṃbhavataḥ | nanu mā bhūtāmastvanya eva kramā'kramā'bhidhānayaugapadyādanyaprakāro
yamāsthāya arthakriyāsūpayokṣyata iti cet | nanveṣāṃ prakārāntaravatkimarthakriyā
piśācāyamānaiva ? tathā cetkiṃ naśchinnam | dṛśyamānāstvanubhūtā'vasita
parasparavyāvṛttakramayaugapadyarūpaprakāradvyasamāliṅgitaśarīrā arthakriyā na
prakārāntarād bhavitumīśate | na ca tadapi | tathā hi | yatra yatprakāravyava
cchedena yaditaraprakāravyavasthānaṃ tatra prakārāntarasabhavaḥ | tadyathā nīla
prakāravyavacchedenā'nīlaprakāravyavasthānaṃ pīte | asti ca kramayaugapadyayora
nyataravyavacchedena taditaraprakāravyavasthānaṃ vyavacchidyamānaprakārāntarā
'viṣayīkṛte arthakriyā sarvatra kāryakāraṇa iti viruddhopalabdhiḥ | niṣidhya
mānaprakārāntarasambhavaviruddhaṃ hi dvayoranyataraniṣedhe'nyataravyavasthānamiti
prakārāntarā'bhāvād bhāvānāmarthakriyā kramā'kramābhyā vyāptā, tau ca
sthirādvyāvarttamānau arthakriyāmapi vyāvarttayato vṛkṣateva śiṃśapātvameka
śilāmayādacalapradeśāt |
tathā hi | na tāvatkrameṇā'rthakriyāsambhavaḥ, svenaiva hi rūpeṇa bhāvā
arthakriyāsūpayujyamānā dṛśyante yato yadyatkāryānvayavyatirekā'nuvihita
bhāvā'bhāvaṃ tattatkāryaprasavasamartham, rūpaṃ ca teṣāṃ kāryeṇā'nukṛtā'nvayavyati
rekamiti tadeva samartham | tacca dvitīyādiṣviva kṣaṇeṣu prathame'pi kṣaṇe saditi
dvitīyādikṣaṇajanyaṃ kāryakalāpaṃ prathame eva kṣaṇe janayet samarthasya kṣepā'yogā
diti nā'kṣaṇikasya kramavadvyāpārasambhavaḥ |
syādetat | asamarthopi kramavatsahakārisaṃnidhānāt krame ṇa karotīti |
tadanupapannam | vikalpā'sahatvāt | kimasya sahakāriṇaḥ kaṃcidupakāramāda
dhati na vā ? ādadhānā api bhāvādbhinnamabhinnaṃ vā ? tatra bhinnopakārā''
dhāne bhāvarūpe satyapyalabdhajanane satyapyasminnanupalabdherutpattimata evopakā
rātkāryasya niṣpatterna tatkārī bhāvaḥ syāt | na copakārasahakāriṇaḥ kārya
jananamiti sāmpratam | upakārasyopakārāntarajanane'navasthāpātāt | tata eva
[p.92]
copakāryasyotpatterbhāvasyā'tatkāritvaprasaṅga ityuktam | abhinnopakārādhāne
bhāva evā'bhidhīyeta, na ca sa prāk siddhaḥ śakya ādhātum | anupakārakatve
vā sahakāriṇo bhāvena nā'pekṣyerannityutpannamātrameva bījamaṅkuraṃ janayet |
anapekṣasya kṣepā'yogāt | kṣepe vā paścādapi na janayet | yadi manyeta anupa
kārakā api bhavanti sahakāriṇaḥ, yasmāttaiḥ sahabhāvaḥ kārya karoti | na ca
nā'pekṣyante | tairvinā kāryasyā'nutpatteriti | tanna | svarūpeṇa cetkāryajanako
bhāvaḥ kasmānnaitānantareṇa janayati ? | tebhyaḥ prāgapi rūpasadbhāvāt | saha
kārirūpeṇa vā janakatve ta eva janakā na bhāvāḥ | anyarūpeṇa vā'nyasya
janakatve gagananalinasyāpi sattvena sadvyavahāragocaratvaprasaṅgaḥ | na cobhaya
bhāvā'bhāvā'nuvidhānādarthakriyābhāvā'bhāvayorekaikasmācca vyabhicārādasmā
dubhayā'dhīnajanmateti sāmpratam | caramabhāvini sati hetau utpattimati
kāryotpatteravaśyambhāvaniyamena vyabhicārā'bhāvāt | na ca svakāraṇātkati
payakālakalāvilambena kāryajananasvabhāvo bhāvo jāta iti svabhāvādeva
vilambakārīti sāmpratam | vikalpā'sahatvāt | kiṃ katipayakālavilambe'sya
vilambanakāritāsvabhāvo nivṛttau na vā ? nivṛttaścet kathaṃ sa bhāvo na
nivṛttaḥ ? svabhāvanivṛtteḥ | anivṛttau vā kathamasya svabhāvaḥ ? nivṛttā
'nivṛttarūpaviruddhadharmasaṃsargāt | atatsvabhāvatve vā na vilambata iti janmā
'nantarameva kṛtakṛtyaḥ prasajyeta | anivṛttau katipayakālakalā'tikrame'pi na
kāryaṃ janayet | vilambakāritāyā rūpasya tādavasthyāt | taduktena krameṇa
na krameṇā'rthakriyākāritā sambhavati bhāvasya | nāpi yaugapadyena yujyate |
tasmādyāvatkāryaṃ kimapi prathame kṣaṇe tenā'kṣaṇikena svenā''tmanā saṃpāditā
tāvatsarva dvitīyādikṣaṇe saṃpādayet | tāvatkāryasaṃpādanayogyasyā''tmano
dvitīyādikṣaṇeṣu saṃnidhānāt | tathā ca saṃpāditasya saṃpādanā'nupapatteḥ
tadanyatve dvitīyādikṣaṇasaṃpādyasya na yaugapadyena nikhilā'rthakriyānirvarttana
makṣaṇikasya yujyate | tadanena paryāyeṇa nā'kṣaṇikasya kramā'kramābhyāmartha
kriyākāritā kalpyata iti | vipakṣe akṣaṇikatve vyāpakā'bhāvāttato
vyāvarttamānaṃ sattvaṃ kṣaṇikatvena vyāpyata iti pratibandhasiddhiḥ |
syādetat | kṣaṇiko'pi bhāvo'rthakriyājananasvabhāvo na vā ? na
cedasanneva | janakasvabhāvaścet kimasya sahakāribhiḥ ? ityutpannamātrameva
bījamaṅkuraṃ janayet | nanūtpanno bījakṣaṇaḥ samartho janayatyevā'ṅkuraṃ kutaḥ
punastasyotpattiḥ pūrvasmādvījakṣaṇāt tarhi tatsaṃtānapatitānāṃ sarveṣāṃ bījatvā
'viśeṣādaṅkurajananasāmarthyamiti labdhajanmamātrameva bījamaṅkuraṃ janayet |
[p.93]
maivam | pūrvapūrvakṣitibījapavanādikṣaṇasamavadhānotpannā'tiśayavattaduttarottara
kṣaṇaparamparālabdhajanmā'nyo bījakṣaṇaḥ samarthaḥ samarthakṣityādikṣaṇasahabhūto
'napekṣa evāṅkaraṃ janayati | na cā'sya kṣityādisahabhuvastadanapekṣasyāpi
tairvinā kāryakaraṇam | tadekasāmagryādhānasya tadabhāve abhāvāt | na
cā'sahakāritā kṣityādīnām | taireva sahā'ṅkurajananāt | teṣāmapi
tatpūrvabhāvitvasya niyamavattvā'viśeṣāt, tanmātratvādeva ca kāraṇatāyāḥ |
na cā'napekṣāṇāmapi parasparaṃ kāryāntarā''rambhaṇām, tanmātrasya tebhya upalabdheḥ
tatraiva sāmarthyaniyamāt | na ca kṛtakaratayetareṣāmakriyā, sahakriyāyāṃ
kṛtamityasaṃbhavāt | na ca svakāraṇabalalabdhaparasparasaṃnidhayo'prekṣāvantaḥ
śakyamidamekenāpyasmāsu karttamiti kṛtaṃ saṃnidhineti nivartitumīśate |
na ca sahakārikāraṇabhedaḥ kāryabhedahetuḥ, api tu sāmagrībhedaḥ | tasmin
sati kāryabhedopalabdheḥ | abhinnā ceha sāmagrīti na kāryabhedasambhavaḥ |
parasparasamavadhānaṃ copasarpaṇapratyayebhyaḥ kṣityādīnāmiti na kṣaṇikasyā'rtha
kriyāvirodhādasādhāraṇatā hetoḥ | na ca sādhyadharmiṇi dṛśyamāne śabdādau
vyāptiprasādhanādeva sādhyasiddherasādhanā'ṅgahetuvacanam | na khalu sarvopa
saṃhāravatī vyāptirdṛśyamātraviṣayā bhavitumarhati | śakyaṃ hi śaṅkituṃ pareṇa
dṛśyamānānāṃ sattvamakṣaṇikatvānna vyāvarttitaṃ tvayeti sattvamanaikāntikaṃ na
kṣaṇikatvasādhanam iti | tasmādyatsad dṛśyamadṛśyaṃ vā tatsarvaṃ kṣaṇikamiti
darśanīyā vyāptiḥ | nanvevamapi śabdādervivādā''spadībhūtasya vyāptirddarśana
balādeva siddhā kṣaṇikateti tadavasthamevā'sādhanāṅgatvaṃ hetuvacanasya | maivam |
asatyapi śaśaviṣāṇādau yatsad dṛśyamadṛśyaṃ vā tatsarvaṃ kṣaṇikamiti yathā
sarvopasaṃhāravatī vyāptiḥ sidhyati | na ca śaśaviṣāṇādayo bhavanti
kṣaṇikāḥ | evamasatyapi vivādāspadīkṛte śabdādau vyāptisiddhāvapi na
sidhyati kṣaṇikatetyavaśyaṃ darśayitavyameva teṣāṃ kṣaṇikatvasādhanāya sattvamiti
nā'sādhanāṅgatā hetuvacanasya | api ca kṛtakā bhāvāḥ svahetubhya upajāya
mānā vinaśvarā jāyante avinaśvarā vā ? vinaśvarāścedudayānantara
mevā'pavṛjyeranniti siddhiḥ kṣaṇabhaṅgaḥ | athā'vinaśvarāḥ, na kadācidapi
naśyeyuḥ | na khalvavinaśvaro vinaṣṭumarhati | na jātu svakāraṇādutpannaṃ
nīlaṃ pītaṃ bhavati | na ca hetvantarā'dhīnajanmā vināśa eṣāmiti sāmpratam |
vikalpā'sahatvāt | sa hi hetvantareṇa janyamāno vināśo bhāvādbhinno'bhinno
vā | na tāvadabhinnaḥ kāraṇabhedāt | bhinnatve tu bhāvasya tādavasthyāt
[p.94]
pūrvavadupalabdhyādiprasaṅgaḥ | tena bhāvastirodhīyata iti cet | na | tirodhāna
syāpi bhede bhāvatādavasthyam | abhede vā hetubhedā'nupapattiḥ | tasmāda
bhāvasyāpi samastarūpavirahiṇo vikalpitasya kriyā'nupapanneti abhāvaṃ
karotīti karotikriyāyā abhāvena niḥsvabhāvena saṃbandhā'nupapatteḥ | nañā
saha sambandho bhāvaṃ na karotīti | yaśca na karoti tasyā'kārakasyā'hetu
tvamiti na vināśahetavaḥ |
kecit tu tasmātsvahetorayamekakṣaṇasthitidharmā bhāvo jāta iti
dvitīyakṣaṇe svayameva na bhavati, na punaratra hetumapekṣate | na khalu svakāraṇataḥ
svabhāvavyavasthitaṃ nīlamupajāyamānamapītādibhāve hetumapekṣate paryanuyujyate 
veti | api ca kṛtakānāṃ bhāvānāmavaśyambhāvī vināśaḥ pratīyate tenāpi
śakyā kṣaṇikatā'numātum | tathā hi | yadyeṣāṃ dhruvaṃ bhāvi tatra teṣāṃ hetva
ntarā'pekṣā nāsti yathā rūpādīnāṃ lohamayatve | dhuvabhāvī ca kṛtakānāṃ
bhāvānāṃ vināśa iti viruddhavyāptopalabdhirvināśasya hetvantarāyattatāṃ
pratikṣipati | dhruvabhāvitā hi niṣidhyamānahetvantarā'pekṣatvaviruddhatannira
pekṣatvavyāptyā labdhā hetvantarā'napekṣatvamupasthāpayati | tacca svaviruddhaṃ
hetvantarā'pekṣatvamapākaroti | yathā hi | ye ye hetvantarāpekṣā na te dhruvabhāvinaḥ
tathā vāsasi rāgādayaḥ | yathā yadi bhāvā api svahetubhyo hetvantaraṃ vināśaṃ
pratyapekṣeran tatastasya saṃnidhānaniyamapramāṇā'bhāvāt kaścitkṛtako'pi na
vinaśyet | soyamavaśyambhāvī kṣaṇikānāṃ bhāvānāṃ vināśaḥ svahetubhya
evaikakṣaṇā'vasyāyināmutpattiṃ sūcayati | tathā ca svayameva na bhavanti bhāvā
dvitīye kṣaṇe iti siddhaḥ kṣaṇabhaṅga eteṣām |
sadṛśaparā'parotpattivipralabdhabuddhayastu kṣaṇānāmanyo'nyasya bhedama
paśyanto'jñādivadarvāgdṛśastadevedaṃ stambhādītyadhyavasyantītyāśaṅkyāha—
kṣaṇikatvapratikṣepāt |
idamākūtam | naiva tāvatsattvaṃ kṣaṇikatvasādhanasamarthaṃ kṣaṇikatve
vyavasthā'nupapatterasādhāraṇatvāt | tathā hi | antyakṣaṇaprāptānāṃ kṣaṇānāṃ
kāryajananamupeyate | tatredaṃ bhavān pṛṣṭo vyācaṣṭāṃ kimekasmādekaṃ kāryamupa
jāyate utā'nekam ? evaṃ kimanekasmādekaṃ kāryamutā'nekamiti ? na tāva
dekasmādekakāryeṇa khalvanukṛtā'nvayavyatirekaṃ kāraṇaṃ taducyate yasya kāryo
tpādātprāṅ niyamavān bhāvaḥ | anukārayanti ca bahava eva kṣitibījādayaḥ
svā'nvayavyatirekāvaṅkuraṃ yat niyamena tadutpādasya purastāt sahabhavantaḥ |
[p.95]
kākatālīyanyāyena tu kṣityādikṣaṇānāṃ prāgbhāvo na niyamavān bhavet | na
cā'ṅkuramātraṃ bījakṣaṇasya parastādavaśyambhāvi | kṣityadīnāmapi
tathābhāvāt | tānyapi khalvaṅkura iva bījakṣaṇasya parastādavaśyambhāva
niyatāni | na ca paurvāparyaniyamādanyaḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ | api ca
ekaikakāraṇatvā'bhāve na niyanenāṅkurādisahabhuvo bhūmyādayo bhaveyuḥ |
tasmā danekasmānnaikam ? yadāha na kiṃcidekamekasmā'diti | nāpyekasmādane
kam, anekakṣitibījatejo'nilasalilā'nantarabhāvaniyamādaṅkurādīnāmaneke
ṣām | nāpyanekasmādekasyaivāṅkurasya janmeti sāmpratam | aṅkurādbhinnānāṃ
bhūmyādīnāṃ tena sahaikahetukatvā'bhāve tatsahabhāvaniyamā'bhāvaprasaṅgāt |
tatsahabhāvaniyamasya cā'ṅkuravatteṣāmapyaviśeṣāt | tadanekasmādanekotpattiḥ
pariśiṣyate |
tatredamālocanīyam | kiṃ pūrvakṣaṇā utareṣāṃ kṣaṇānāṃ parasparanirapekṣā
eva janakā āho svitsāpekṣā ? iti | nanūktamantyakṣaṇaprāptāḥ paraspara
nirapekṣāḥ kāryāṇi janayantīti tatkimanabhyupetāropitapratiṣedhaprapañcena |
tatkimidānīṃ samarthabījakṣaṇajanako'pi tajjanane iti kṛtaṃ sahakāribhiḥ |
nanūktaṃ naite prekṣābantaḥ ki tu svapratyayā'dhīnaniyatasaṃnidhayo na vyavadherīśate
iti | kimayaṃ kṛṣībalo'pi na prekṣāvān yaḥ kusūlādapanīya bījamāvapati
bhūmau parikarmitāyāṃ kṣityādisahabhāvina evā'tiśayotpādaparamparayā'ṅkara
jananasāmarthyadarśanāt | bījasya kusūla sthasya vatsaraśatenāpi tadadarśanāt |
tatrāvapatīti cet | atha kimayaṃ na svasaṃtānamātraṃprabhavasamartho bījakṣaṇaḥ ?
tathā cet kathaṃ saṃtānāntaraṃ sahakāri nāpekṣeta kāryakaraṇe ? nanvapekṣata eva
caiṣa svotpāde na svakārye tatra cā'syā'napekṣatvamucyate na tu svotpatau |
nanūtpattāvapyasya svasaṃtānavartī jāgarti pūrva eva nirapekṣaḥ kṣaṇaḥ | evaṃ tasya pūrvaḥ
pūrvaḥ svasaṃtatipatita evā'napekṣo jāgarti janane iti kusūlanihitabīje eva
syātkṛtī kṛṣībalaḥ, kṛtamasya prekṣāvataḥ kṛṣikarmaṇalā duḥkhākareṇeti |
yadi manyeta dvividhamiha kāryam | ekaṃ sahakāribhirāhitā'tiśaya
viśeṣamaparamparāprasavadharmakaṃ dvitīyaṃ tu sahakāryatiśayanirapekṣajananam |
tatrā'ṅkure āhitā'tiśayaviśeṣaṃ parā'pekṣajanma | ādyo'tiśayastu sahakāri
kṛtā'tiśayaparamparānirapekṣotpādaḥ | na caitadubhayavidhamapi kārya bījasya
[p.96]
saṃtānamātrādutpattumarhatīti kṣityādisaṃtānāntarā'pekṣameva svasaṃtānavarti tvā
tkādācitkaṃ kāryaṃ karotīti kalpate kṛṣikarma | saṃtānāntarāṇi tu kādāci
tkāryotpattau ye sahakāripratyayāḥ ta eva yathā'ṅkurotpattau sahakāriṇo bījasya
sāmarthyaprabodhahetavaḥ kadācittveṣāṃ kṣityādīnāṃ ye melanahetavasta eva sāmarthyā
'vabodhahetavo bhavanti tathā bījānāmādyā'tiśayotpādane sāmarthyaprabodhahetava
upasarpaṇapratyayāḥ | sarvathā saṃtānāntarairanāhitā'tiśayasāmarthyaṃ na bījaṃ kārya
viśeṣaṃ kādācitkaṃ karoti | sāmarthyaprabodhahetavaśca dvaye sahakāripratyayā upa
sarpaṇapratyayāśceti |
tadanupapannam | tathā hi | bhavatu dvividhaṃ kāryam, astu ca tat kādāci
tkatvātsaṃtānāntarā'pekṣam | apekṣā'rthastu vaktavyaḥ | kāraṇasya svasāmarthya
prabodha iti cet | nanu sāmarthyatatprabodhayoḥ samarthabhāvatatprākaṭyā'vyati
rekāt samarthapratyayotpāda ityuktaṃ syāt | tathā ca svotpāde apekṣeta kāraṇaṃ
sahakāripratyayān | satyam | nā'bhyupagama eva doṣatvena codyate | athā'ya
māṅkurasamartho vā kṣaṇa ādyā'tiśayo vā kiṃ nirapekṣeṇa svasaṃtānamātra
janmanā pūrvabījakṣaṇena janyate nirapekṣeṇa na punaḥ svasaṃtānamātrajanmanā | tasyāpi
pūrvasmādeva puñjādutpatteḥ | nanu samānakusūlajanmasu bahuṣu bījasaṃtāneṣu
kasmātkiṃcideva bījaṃ paramparayā'ṅkurajananā'nuguṇamupajanayati bījakṣaṇām,
nā'nye bījakṣaṇā bhinnasaṃtāne patitāḥ | na khalu upasarpaṇapratyayātprākteṣāṃ
samānā'samānasaṃtānavarttināṃ kaścitparasparā'tiśayo bījakṣaṇānām | athopa
sarpaṇapratyayātprāk na tatsaṃtānavarttino janayanti paramparayāpyaṅkurajananā'nuguṇaṃ
bījakṣaṇam | bījamātrajananāttu teṣāṃ kasyacid bījakṣaṇasyopasarpaṇapratyaya
saha bhuva ādyātiśayotpādaḥ | hanta tarhi tadabhāve satyutpanno'pi janayedeva |
na cā'syopasarpaṇapratyayena sahabhāvaniyamaḥ | tatsantānānāṃ kusūlavartināṃ bīja
kṣaṇānāṃ tena saha samānayogakṣemāṇāmapyupasarpaṇapratyayasahabhāvā'bhāvāt |
kṣaṇasya caikasya kṣaṇāntareṇaikena kāryakāraṇabhāvaniścayā'yogātsāmānyaviṣaya
tvādanvayavyatirekayostatraivopapatteḥ | na khalu kāraṇabhedena | tathā ca kevalānāṃ
vya bhicārasambhavādādyā'tiśayotpādamaṅkurotpādaṃ vā prati kṣityādīnāṃ para
sparā'pekṣāṇāmevotpādakatvamakāmenāpyaṅgīkaraṇīyam |
api cā'ntyakṣaṇaprāptaṃ bījamanapekṣmaṅkarā'vanipavanapāthastejāṃsi
janayet | kiṃ yenaiva rūpeṇāṅkuraṃ janayati tenaiva taditarāṇyapi kiṃ vā rūpānta
reṇa ? na tāvattenaiva | kṣityādīnāmapyaṅkurasvābhāvyāpatteḥ | na khalu kāraṇā
[p.97]
'bhede bhedavatkārya bhavitumarhati | kāryabhedasyā''kasmikatvaprasaṅgāt | yadāha—
ayameva hi bhedo bhedaheturvā yadviruddhakarmā'dhyāsaḥ kāraṇabhedaśce ti | nāpi
sāmagrībhedaḥ | tasyā apyekatvāt | anyathā sahabhāve niyamā'bhāvāt |
rūpāntareṇa tadireṣāṃ janakam | tathā hi | bījamaṅkurādi janayadupādānaṃ tadvi
kriyayā tadupapatteḥ | taditareṣu punarasya sahakāripratyayatā | teṣāṃ tu pūrve
pābanādayo yathāsvamupādānāni, tadvikriyayā tadutpādāditi cet | nanu sahakā
ritopādānateti kimekaṃ tattvaṃ nānā vā ? ekaṃ cetkathaṃ rūpāntareṇa janakam ?
nānātve'pyanayorbījādbhedo'bhedo vā ? bhede kathaṃ bījasya janakatvam ? tābhyā
mevā'ṅkurādīnāmutpatteḥ | abhede vā kathaṃ bījasya nānātvaṃ ? bhinnatā
dātmyāt | etayorvaikyamekatādātmyāt |
yadyucyeta kṣityādau janayitavye tadupādānabhūtameva kṣityādibījasya
rūpāntaramiti | na tarhi bījaṃ tadanapekṣaṃ kṣityādīnāṃ janakam | tadanapekṣatve
teṣāmaṅkurādbhedā'nupapatteḥ | na cā'nupakārakāṇyapekṣyanta iti tvayaivoktam | na
ca kṣaṇikasyopakārasaṃbhavo'nyatra jananāt | tasyābhedyatvāt | tathā cā'rtha
kriyākāritāyā ekā'nekatvasā'pekṣatvavyāptatvāt | tṛtīyaprakāravirahādana
yoranyataraniṣedhasyā'nyataravidhānanāntarīyakatvāt kṣaṇikatve ca tadvyāpaka
yorekā'nekatvaprakārayorabhāvāduktena krameṇa sattvaṃ na nivṛttamityasādhāraṇā
'naikāntikaṃ gandhavattvavaditi |
yadi manyeta anupakārakā api bhavanti sahakāriṇo'pekṣaṇīyāśca
kāryeṇā'nuvihitabhāvā'bhāvatvātsahakāryakaraṇācca | nanvanena krameṇā'kṣaṇiko
'pi bhāvo'nupakārakānapi sahakāriṇaḥ kramavataḥ kāryeṇā'nukṛtā'nvayavyati
rekānapekṣiṣyate kariṣyate ca kramavatsahakārisamavadhānavaśātkrameṇa kāryāṇīti
vyāpakā'nupalabdherasiddheḥ saṃdigdhavyatirekamanaikāntikaṃ sattvaṃ kṣaṇikatvasiddhā
viti | nanvayamakṣaṇikaḥ svarūpeṇa kārya janayati | tacca svarūpaṃ tṛtīyādiṣviva
kṣaṇeṣu dvitīye'pi kṣaṇe saditi tadā'pi janayet | akurvanbā tṛtīyādiṣvapi
na kurvīta sthirasya tādavasthyāt | atādavasthye vā tadevāsya kṣaṇikatva
miti |
atrocyate | satyaṃ svarūpeṇa kārya janayati, na tu tenaiva, sahakārisahi
tasya tasya janakatvāt | kramavatsvapratyayā'pekṣiṇastatsahakārisamavadhānasya
dvitīye kṣaṇe bhāvāt svarūpavat sahakārisamavadhānamapyātmano'nvayavyatirekā
[p.98]
vanukārayati tatkāryotpādane | na cetāvatā sahakārisamavadhānādevotpattimataḥ
kāryasiddheḥ kṛtameteṣāṃ svarūpeṇeti sāmpratam | tatsahitādeva tatastadutpatti
darśanādrūpe satyapyanutpādāt | samavadhāne tu utpādādeva kāryasya tadeva hetuḥ na
punā rūpamapīti cet | nanvevamahetutve samāne'pi bīje rūpasya rāsabhādibhiḥ
saha kasmādvījarūpe satyevāṅkarotpādo na rāsabhādiṣu ? satsveva bījādi
svarūpasamavadhānasyaiva hetutvāditi cet ? taddhetutve kathaṃ tanniveśi svarūpamapi
na hetuḥ ? ahetutve vā kathaṃ na rāsabhāditulyatā ? na cā'varjanīyatayā
rūpaniveśaḥ | samavadhānamātrasya tadantareṇāpi bhāvat | viśeṣastu rūpameva |
tasmādyathobhayā'dhīnanirūpaṇā vyāptirvyāpakasya vyāpye bhāva eva, nābhāva
iti vyāpyasya tu vyāpaka eva bhāvo, nā'nyatreti nirūpyate tathā kāryakāraṇa
bhāva ubhayādhīnanirūpaṇo'pi kāraṇasya kāryātprāgabhāva eva nābhāva iti
kāryasya kāraṇe satyeva nā'sati bhāva iti anubhavā'nusārānniścīyate |
tathaiva laukikaparīkṣakāṇāṃ kāryakāraṇabhāvā'vagateḥ | na ca kāraṇataiva sattā
svarūpaṃ nīlāderanubhavā'vasitam | taddhi tenaiva svarūpeṇa svabhāvavyavacchinnaṃ
tāsu tāsvarthakriyāsūpayujyate, na punastatkāritaivāsya rūpam | mā bhūdaparyā
yeṇā'nekārthakriyākāraṇe rūpabhedaḥ, mā ca bhūduṣādhyapekṣamasyāpi nirūpaṇaṃ
kāryatvasyaiva sattvasya śuddhasyaiva nirūpyamāṇatvāt kāraṇatve tu sattve yathā
kāryatvaṃ bhāvasvabhāvo'pi vastutaḥ kāraṇādhīnanirūpaṇaṃ tathā kāraṇatvamapi
svasambandhikāryamantareṇā'śakyanirūpaṇamiti sattvaṃ śuddhaṃ na nirūpyeta |
tataścopādhyapekṣaḥ śuddho vā nāśe kāryatvasattvavaditi vyāhanyeta | tathā ca
yadyasti sarvajñaḥ tathā'pi siddhaṃ sadrūpa eva bhāvastadvijñānālambanapratyayatvamanu
bhaviṣyati | atha nāsti, tathā'pi svasvahetuto labdhasvarūpa evāpātato
'kiñcitkaraḥ samartho'pi tattatsahakārisaṃnidhestāsu tāsvarthakriyāsūpayokṣyate |
na ca prathamataḥ kāryā'nupalabdherasāmarthya, tathā sati rāsabhāderapi kṣityādi
samavadhānādaṅkarādyutpattiprasaṅga ityuktam | nanu janakatvamajanakatvaṃ ca viruddhau
dharmau kathamekatra samāviśataḥ ? tathaikatraiva bīje'ṅkurajanakatvaṃ tadajanakatva
lakṣaṇaṃ ca kṣityādijanakatvaṃ kasmānna viruddhe ? virodhinī cetkathamabhedaḥ ?
bhedaścennaikaṃ jagati kiṃciditi nā'nekamapi syāditi sunirūpitamatrabhavatā
bhāvānāṃ tattvam | sakṛdupalabhyamāne kāryabhede'pi bījarūpasya janakasyā'bhedā
vagateḥ pratyakṣeṇā'bheda iti cenna | ihāpi samānatvāt | na khalvatrāpi
kramavatkāryabhede'pi bījarūpamanyadanyadapekṣyate | na ca nañaḥ prayogā'prayogamā
trābhyāṃ virodhā'bagatiḥ | atiprasaṅgāt | na ca bījasyāpi yadaiva janakatvaṃ
tadaivā'janakatvam | na ca janakatvamasya svabhāvo'pi tu bījaṃ janakatvaṃ na
[p.99]
api ca manovaiguṇye cakṣurādyabhāve ca rūpādyavedanādubhayavedyatā'vasīyate |
kevalamanovedyatā tu kutaḥ ? dvitīyakṣaṇopalabdheriti cet | na | tatra cakṣurā-
taditarapratyayasamabadhānaṃ vā sāmarthyaṃ vā dharmabhedaḥ | samavadhānaṃ co sarpaṇapratyayā
'dhīnamasya | na ca na dharmmadharmiṇorabhedaḥ | tasya tato'nyatvāt | anyatve'pi
ca tadāśritanvāttasyolabdheḥ tena ca sahakāryakaraṇāttatsambandhaḥ | tatsiddha
makṣaṇikasyāpi kramā'kramābhyāmarthakriyākāritopapatteḥ sandigdhavyatirekitvā
danaikāntikaṃ sattvamasādhanāṅgaṃ kṣaṇabhaṃguratve bhāvānāmiti |
yacca vikalpitaṃ, svabhāvato bhāvā vinaśvarā avinaśvarā vā jāyanta
iti, tatrocyate | na vayaṃ vinaśvaratvamavinaśvaratvaṃ vā jānīma, kintu santaḥ
svahetubhyo jāyante dhvaṃsante va hetvantarebhyaḥ svapratyayā'dhīnasaṃnidhānebhya iti |
dhvaṃsaśca dhvaṃsyamānā'bhinno hetubhedātsvabhāvabhedācca | upapāditaśca svabhāva
bhedo niyogaparīkṣāyām | nanvasminnutpanne'pi bhinnamūrtau kimāyātaṃ bhāvasya ?
na kiñcidāyātam | astyayamāyuṣmān bhāvaḥ | astu svakāraṇādhīnajanmanā,
tadabhāvena tatsvarūpavirahalakṣaṇena svalakṣaṇaśālitā svā'nurūpaṃ vyavahāraṃ
pravarttayatā nirastasamastārthakriyāḥ kiṃ nacchinnam ? na ca dhruvabhāvitā
vināśasya hetvantarānapekṣatāmāvahati | asiddhatvādanaikāntikatvācca | idaṃ hi
bhavānnirūpayatu, kiṃ ghaṭasantānātsaṃvibhāgā kapālasaṃtatirdhruvabhāvinī na vā ?
na cedvināśo'pi ghaṭasyā'dhruvabhāvī | na khalu saṃvibhāgakṣaṇotpādamantareṇa
ghaṭavināśamīkṣāmahe'pihitacakṣupaḥ | tathā cā'siddhā dhruvabhāvinā vināśasya |
atha dhruvabhāvinī, tasyāmeva vibhāgasaṃtatau dhruvabhāvinyāmapi mudgarāpekṣi
ṇyāmanaikāntikaṃ dhruvabhāvitvaṃ nā'napekṣatvena vyāptamiti hetvantarā'pekṣatvaṃ
niṣeddhumarhati | tasmāt kṣaṇikatvasādhanā'numānapratikṣepādavādhitaṃ pratyabhijñānaṃ
sthāpayati bhavāniti siddham |
tadevaṃ kṣaṇikatvapratikṣepānnendriyajavijñānasamayabhāvī tadviṣayajanmā
kṣaṇo'sti yo manovijñānagocaraḥ syādityuktam | astu vā, tathāpyayamapi
cākṣuṣo, na tu manomātragocaraḥ | ubhayabhāvā'nuvidhānāttadvijñānabhāvā'bhāvayori
tyāha—api cā'nyaviṣayavyāvṛttamanasāṃ manovaiguṇye'praṇihitamanasāmandhādīnāṃ
cakṣurādyabhāve ca rūpādyavedanāt itarathā ca vedanāt ubhayavedyatā'numīyate |
kevalamanovedyatā kutaḥ ? śaṅkate—prathame eva kṣaṇe indriyasāmarthyopakṣayāt
dvitīyakṣaṇopalabdheḥ kevalamanovedyatā'numīyate iti cet ? nirākaroti—na |
kutaḥ ? tādṛśatajjātīyakṣaṇāvadhṛtasāmarthyānāṃ cakṣurādīnāṃ sāmarthyaparikṣaye
hetvabhāvāt na caikasminneva kṣaṇe sāmarthyameteṣāṃ yena kṣaṇāntare tanna syādi
[p.100]
dīnāṃ sāmarthyaparikṣaye hetvabhāvāt | tajjñānadhārāvāhikatvadarśanācca | smaraṇe
tu pratītameva pāratantryamanumānādiṣu liṅgādyabhāve pratyayāt pāratantryamiti
na kva cidapyanāntare manaḥsākṣātsvatantraṃ pravartata iti āntara eva sākṣādviṣaya
statraiva sākṣād vṛttyā prakarṣamanubhavet nā'viṣaye |
ityāha—tajjñānadhārāvāhikatvadarśanācca | nanu smaraṇe kevalameva manaḥ
pravartate, andhādīnāmapi rūpādismaraṇopalabdherityata āha—smaraṇe tu
pratītameva pāratantryaṃ manasaḥ | pramāṇāntarā'dhigatagocaratvāt |
pramāṇāntarajanmā'nubhavā''hitabhāvanāsahitaṃ hi manaḥ smaraṇe hetuḥ, na
nirapekṣamityarthaḥ | na cā'numānādijñāneṣu svatantramityāha—anumānā
diṣu liṅgādyabhāve pratyayātpāratantryaṃ manasaḥ pratītamiti | tasmānna kva
cidanāntare manaḥ sākṣātsvatantraṃ pravarttata ityāntara eva sukhādirasya sākṣāda
vyavadhānena saṃyuktasamabāyalakṣaṇena saṃnikarṣeṇa svatantrasya viṣayo na tu
bāhyam | tena sākṣāccakṣurādivadasaṃbandhādasvatantratvācca | tena tanmanastatraiva
sākṣādvṛttyā prakarṣamanubhaved na tadaviṣaye bāhye | tatsiddhametat vivādādhyāsi
tāni cakṣurādīni nā'tītā'nāgataviṣayavijñānajanakāni viṣayasahakāriṇāṃ
tatkāryajanakatvād yadyatsahakāri yatkāryajanakaṃ tattadabhāve na kārya janayati
yathā bījamaṅkarajanakakṣityādisahakārikṣityādāvatīte'nāgate vā nā'ṅkrāya
kalpate | na cā'rthasahakāritvamindriyāṇāmasiddhaṃ, vijñānajanane tadasahakāritve
'smadādīnāmapyatītā'nāgatārthapratyakṣatvaprasaṅgāt | siddhauṣadhamantratapaḥ samā
dhīnāṃ caṃkṣurādyatirodhāyakatve'pi na svaviṣayā'tilaṅghanamityuktam | na
ca saṃdigdhavyatirekitā | tathā hi | sahakāritvamapekṣaṇīyatayā vyāptaṃ
tadabhāve tu kāryajanane apekṣaṇīyatvaviruddhamanapekṣaṇīyatvaṃ vyāpakaviruddho
palabdhyā sahakāritāmapi nivarttayatīti pratibandhasaddhiḥ | evaṃ vivādā''spadaṃ
mano na cakṣurādinirapekṣaṃ bāhye pravarttate tattantrapravṛttitvādyad yattantrapravṛtti
na tattannirapekṣaṃ tatra pravarttate yathā''lokāpekṣaṃ cakṣū rūpe na saṃtamase iti
viruddhopalabdhiḥ | na ca bāhye na cakṣurāditantraṃ mana iti sāmpratam |
andhabadhirādyabhāvaprasaṅgāt iti vipañcitam | nanu mā bhūdindriyajaṃ mānasaṃ
ca pratyakṣaṃ sarvaviṣayaṃ, bhāvanāmayaṃ tu bhaviṣyati | tathā hi | śrutamayena
vijñānena samastavastuviṣayanairātmyādi gṛhītvā yuktimayena ca bhūtatāmasya
[p.101]
vyavasthāpyā'sakṛccetoniveśanarūpabhāvanāprakarṣaparyanta-janmapratyakṣaṃ vijñāna 
manavayavenā''tmādirūpaviśvālambanaṃ karatalā'ravindaviṣayamivā'tiviśadaṃ
bhāvayiṣyati tathāgataḥ | na cā'yamīdṛśaḥ pratyayo na sambhavati bhāvanā
prakarṣāt | tathā hi | bhāvanā'bhāvādvā tatprakarṣā'bhāvādvā tatsāsarthyā'bhāvā
dvā na bhaved | na bhāvanā' bhāvāt sarvadharmanairātmyabhāvanā'sambhavaḥ | sa
khalvevaṃ na bhaved yadi jagati duḥkhaṃ nāma na syāt, sadvā na jihāsitaṃ syāt,
jihāsitaṃ vā na śakyahānam, aśakyahānatā ca tannityatvādvā, tadupāyā
'parijñānādvā, upāyā'parijñāne'pi ca śakyahānatve'pi ca nairātmyabhāvanāyā
anupāyatvādvā, laghutaropāyāntarasambhāvādvā | na tāvat duḥkhaṃ nāma nāsti
jagati, pariṇāmatāpasaṃskāraiḥ khalvanavayavena pañcāpi skandhā bhavanti
saṃsāriṇām | na caite svasya pareṣāṃ vā nā'paninīṣitāḥ | śrāvakabodhiprati
niyatena sā naisargikakaruṇāparādhīnacetasā vā puruṣadhaureyakeṇa paraduḥkhaduḥkhinā
pratikūlavedanīyatvāt | nāpyaśakyahānatā | na hi nityo nāma bhāvaḥ saṃbhavī,
tasya samastakriyāvirahiṇo vyomotpalāyamānatvāt | nāpyupāyā'parijñānam |
tathā hi | duḥkhasya samutpādakā doṣāḥ rāgadveṣādayo madamānādayaśca te
cotpādakā api tadavayavānnivartamānāstadapi nivarttayanti, yathā kaphodbhavo
jvaraḥ kaphanivṛttyā nivarttate | doṣāśca nityātmādidarśanajanmānaḥ tadavaya
vāstadavibhāgavarttinastadabhāva na bhavanti | tadabhāvaheturna tatpratipakṣasākṣā
tkārādanyaḥ | tasyaiva tadvirodhitvāt | tathā hi | yatra yadviruddhasamavadhānaṃ
tatrā'vaśyaṃ taditaranivṛttiḥ | yathā viruddhavahnisaṃnidhāne śītanivṛttiḥ |
saṃbhavati ca citte sthirā''tmadarśanaviruddhasarvadharmanairātmyasvātmībhāva iti sva
bhāvaviruddhopalabdhiḥ | evaṃ ca taddheturāgādīnāmapyatyantanivṛttiḥ | tathā hi |
ye saṃbhavatsvahetuviruddhā'tyantavivṛddhayaḥ te saṃbhavadatyantasamucchedāḥ yathendralupta
nitāntavivṛddhayaḥ keśāḥ | saṃbhavatsvahetuviruddhātyantavivṛddhayaśca rāgādaya
iti svabhāvahetuḥ | sarvadharmmanairātmyaṃ ca tatpratipakṣastatsākṣātkāraśca na
bhāvanāprakarṣādanyataḥ | na cā'sau bhāvanāmantareṇeti | na ca sarvadharmmanairātmya
vijñānaṃ prati ca samartho bhāvanāprakarṣaḥ |
tathā hi | yanmanovijñānaṃ sambhavadbhāvanāprakarṣa tadbhāvanā''dheyajñeyā''
kāravaiśadyaṃ yathākāmārttādyavasthāsu manovijñānaṃ kāminīvastvādyālambanaṃ
[p.102]
bhāvanāprakarṣa''dheyaviśadabhāvam | sambhavadbhāvanāprakarṣaśca nairātmyavikalpa
iti svabhāvahetuḥ, bhāvanāprakarṣamātranimitatvāt | pratibhāsavaiśadyasya yasya
bhāvanāprakarṣastasya viśadapratibhāsatvaṃ niyatasaṃvidhānamiti sambhavadbhāvanā
prakarṣamātrānubandhinā viśadapratibhāsateti satyapi bhāvanāprakarṣasambhave yadi
pratibhāsavaiśadyaṃ notpadyeta nimittāntarasāpekṣaṃ syāt | nirapekṣatayā ca
vyāptaḥ prakarṣo dṛṣṭaḥ, tato vyāpakaviruddhopalabdhyā vipakṣād vyāvartamāno
bhāvanāprakarṣo vaiśadyena vyāpyata iti na saṃdigdhavyatirekaḥ | nāpyasiddhaḥ |
kāruṇyena duḥkhabhayādvā bhāvanāprakarṣaprārambhasambhavāt | na ca laṅghanodakatāpa
vadvyavacchinnotkarṣatā sarvadharmmanairātmye cetaso yena kvacinna vivādaḥ
sambhavet | yato yaḥ sthirā''śrayo dharmo yatra yathotpanno yathāhitasya bhāgasya
pravṛttaye na punaryatnamapekṣate so'tyantaṃ niṣṭhāṃ tatra gacchati | tadyathā kala
dhautasya puṭapākaprabandhā''hitā viśuddhiḥ raktasāratām | sthirāśrayaśca sarvadharma
nairātmyabodhe utpanne viśeṣe punaḥ prayatnā'napekṣaḥ sambhavadatyantā'bhyāsaḥ
puṃsāmiti svabhāvahetuḥ sthirāśrayatvaṃ paralokasiddheḥ | apradhānamapi bhāvādbhinna
mabhinnaṃ vā ? tatrā'bhinnopakārā'bhāvarūpe satyapyalabdhajanmanaḥ satyapyasminna
nupalabdherutpattimata evā'pakarāt kāryaniṣpatteraśaktādbhāvaḥ syāt | na
copakāriṇaḥ kāryajananamiti sāmpratam | upakārasyopakārāntarajanane
'navasthāpātāt | tata eva kāryasyotpatterbhāvasyā'tatkāritvaprasaṅga ityuktam |
abhinnopakārā''dhāne vā bhāva evādheyaḥ | na ca sa prāksiddhaḥ śakya
ādhātum | anupakāritve vā sahakāribhāvena nāpekṣerannityutpannamātrameva
bījamaṅkurādi janayet | anapekṣasya kṣepā'yogāttatkṣepe vā paścādapi na
janayedaviśeṣāt | yadi manyetā'nupakārakā api bhavanti sahakāriṇo
yasmāttaiḥ sahabhāvaḥ kārya karotīti | na ca nā'pekṣyante, tairvinā kāryasyā
'nupapatteriti | tatra svarūpeṇa cet kāryasya janako bhāvaḥ tasmānnaivā'bhāva
yorekaikasmād vyabhicārādubhayā'dhīnajanmateti sāmpratam | hetoruktakāryo
tpatteravaśyambhāvaniyamena vyabhicārā'bhāvāt | na ca svakāraṇāt kati
payakālakalāvilambena kāryajananasvabhāvo bhāvo jāta iti svabhāvādeva
vilambakārīti sāmpratam | vikalpā'sahatvāt | kiṃ katipayakālakalā
vilambanakāritāsvabhāvo nivṛtto na vā ? nivṛttaścet kathaṃ bhāvo na
nivṛttaḥ ? svabhāvanivṛtteḥ | anivṛttau bādhakamasya svabhāvo nivṛttarūpa
viruddhadharmasaṃsargāt | atatsvabhāvatve vā na vilambata iti janmānantarameva
bhāvaḥ kṛtakṛtyaḥ prasajyeta | anivṛttau katipayakālakalātikrame'pi na kārya
janayet | vilambakāritāyā rūpasya tādavasthyāt | taduktaṃ 'na krameṇā'rtha
[p.103]
kriyākāritā sambhavati bhāvasya nāpi yaugapadyena yujyate yasmādyāvatkāryaṃ
kimapi prathame kṣaṇe tenā'kṣaṇikatvenātmanā sampāditaṃ tāvatsarva dvitīyādi
kṣaṇeṣu sampādayet tāvatkāryam | kṣaṇikasya tu kramā'kramābhyāmarthakriyā kalpata
iti | vipakṣe kṣaṇikatve vyāpakābhāvāttato vyāvarttamānaṃ sattvaṃ kṣaṇikatvena
vyāpyata iti pratibandhasiddhiḥ |
syādetat | kṣaṇiko'pi bhāvo'rthakriyājananasvabhāvaḥ | na cedamatraiva
jananatvā'bhāvaścet kimasya sahakāribhirityutpannamātrameva bījamaṅkuraṃ
janayet | nanūtpanno bījakṣaṇaḥ samartho janayatyeva, kutaḥ punastasyotpattiḥ pūrva
smādbījakṣaṇāt ? tarhi tatsantānapatitānāṃ sarveṣāṃ bījatvā'viśeṣādaṅkura
jananasāmarthyamiti labdhajanmamātrameva bījamaṅkuraṃ janayet | maivam | pūrva
kṣitibījapavanādikṣaṇasamavadhānotpannā'tiśayavattaduttarottarakṣaṇaparamparālabdha
janmā'nyo'pi kṣaṇasamarthaḥ samarthakṣityādikṣaṇasahakṛto'napekṣa evā'ṅkuraṃ
janayati | na cā'nyakṣityādisahabhuvastaistadanapekṣitasyāpi tairvinā kāryakaraṇaṃ,
tadekasāmarthyā'dhīnasya tadabhāve'bhāvāt | na cā'sahakāritā kṣityādīnāṃ
siddheti nodakatāpavadvyavasthitotkarṣatā | tatrā'tyantatāpena tadāśrayāṇamapā
muparaterasthirāśrayatvāt | nāpi laṅnavadvyavasthitotkarṣam | yathāhitasya
bhāgasyotpattau tatra punaryatnāpekṣatvāt | na hi tatra pūrvasiddhā''lambanamātrā
satī laṅghanāntarāya kalpate, api tvasau vinaṣṭā | laṅghanāntaraṃ tu pūrva
laṅghanavadbalaprayatnābhyāṃ pravartate | sarvadharmanairātmyaprakāśanasvabhāvastu cetasaḥ
svarasavāhīti na punaḥ prayatnamapekṣate | tasya hi pūrvabhāvanā''hitā'dhi
kā'dhikottaraviśeṣapratilambhenā'tyantaprakarṣotpatterāśrayasvabhāvabhūtatvāt | iha
prakarṣaviśeṣaṇaṃ laṅghanaṃ tu na dehasvābhāvaḥ | satyapyāśraye punaryatnāpekṣatvāt |
evamudakatāpo'pi nodakasvabhāvaḥ | tadvināśatvāt | bhāvanāṃ vā pakṣīkṛtyo
cyate | tathā hi | yā yā''daranairantaryadīrdhakālā''sevitā bhāvanā sā sarvā
karatalā'malakāyamānā''lambananirbhāsavijñānaphalā | tadyathā kāmāturasya
kāminībhāvanā viśeṣaṇatrayavatī kāminīviṣayavijñānaviśadābhatāhetuḥ |
tathā ca samastavastunairātmyabhāvanāviśeṣaṇatrayaśālinīti svabhāvahetuḥ |
manovijñānaṃ hi viśadābhamiyamīdṛśī bhāvanā svā'nvayavyatirekāva
pyanukārayantī kāraṇāntaranirapekṣā kāmāturādiṣu dṛṣṭā | tadyadi satyāmapya
syāṃ viśadābhatvaṃ samastavastunairātmyagrāhiṇo vijñānasya nopajāyeta, kāraṇā
ntarasāpekṣatvamasyāḥ prasajyeta | tathā ca dṛṣṭaṃ nirapekṣatvaṃ bhāvanāyā vyāpakaṃ
[p.104]
bhāvanāmayamapi vijñānaṃ śrutā'numitaviṣayam akasmādbhāvanā'yogā
dāgamā'numānaparatantram | pūrvajñānabhūtārthatvaviparyyayā'nuvidhānāt sāpekṣatvāda
pramāṇam |
vyāhanyeteti vipakṣe vyāpakaviruddhasya sāpekṣatvasya darśanāddhīvaiśadyajananād
vyāpakaviruddhopalabdhyā vyāvartamānā bhāvanā vaiśadyajanmaniyatā'vaśiṣyata iti
bhāvanāviśeṣā'nuvidhānātsphuṭatvotpādanya na bhāvanāmātrānubandhiteti sva
bhāvahetuḥ, hetośca samagrasya kāryotpādanasāmarthyā'numānam | tathā ca
satyāmeva tasyāṃ sāmagryāmapratibandhāyāṃ niyamena phalamutpadyata iti na pākṣika
phalasambhavaviṣayatvaṃ sāmagryā niścīyata iti niścayānnā'numānatvavirodhaḥ |
na cā'siddho hetuḥ | sambhavati hi bhāvanā viśeṣaṇatrayavatī sāṃsārikaduḥkha
nimittād vibhyatāṃ śrāvakabodhipratiniyatānāṃ nikhiladuḥkhanimittaśamanī
kāruṇikānāṃ ca sakalāneva duḥkhina ātmatayā'bhyupagacchatāṃ dānādipāra
mitābhyāsatanūkṛtāntarmalānāmīdṛgbhāvanāprakarṣajanmavijñānamabhrāntaṃ ca |
pramāṇasiddhavastuviṣayatvāt | avikalpaśca viśadābhatvāt | samastavastuviṣayaṃ
ca tannairātmyasākṣātkārarūpatvāt | na ca nairātmyaṃ nāma bhāvebhyo bhinnaṃ kiṃ
cidasti, yadapratyakṣagocareṣvapi teṣu pratyakṣagocaraṃ syāt | vikalpārūḍhaṃ ca sphuṭaṃ
nā'vikalpya vijñānaṃ pratibhāsi | tadetadbhāvanādheyavaiśadyaṃ sadviśadabhāvātma
naiva syāt | tathā ca viśvanairātmyavaiśadyaṃ viśvavaiśadyameveti | yadidaṃ sārvajña
tadbhūtārthabhāvanāyā evā''daranairantaryadīrghakālā''sevitāyāḥ sarvā''kāra
sakalavastusākṣātkārahetoḥ sambhavādasiddho hetvabhāvo na phalā'bhāvasiddhā
vaṅgamityāśaṅkyāha—bhāvanāmayamapi vijñānaṃ śrutā'numitaviṣayam | kutaḥ ?
akasmādbhāvanāyā ayogāt | āgamā'numānaparatantraṃ tadviṣaya eva sākṣād
vṛttyā prakarṣamanubhaved na tadaviṣaya iti pūrveṇa sambandhaḥ | na ca tadapi
prakarṣavatpramāṇamityāha—pūrvajñānabhūtārthatvaviparyayā'nuvidhānācca sā'pekṣatvā
dapramāṇam |
etadatrārthatattvaṃ vivakṣitamācāryasya | satyaṃ, śrutā'numānagocaracāriṇī
bhāvanā viśadābhavijñānaheturiti nā'vajānīmahe, kiṃ tu yadviṣaye jātaṃ tadeva
viśadapratipattigācarā na jātu rūpabhāvanāprakarṣo rasaviṣayavijñānavaiśadyāya
kalpate | nanu na viṣayāntaravaiśadyahetubhāvaṃ bhāvanāyāḥ saṅgirāmahe, ki tu
śratā'numānaviṣayavaiśadyahetutāmeva | tadviṣayaṃ ca samastavastunairātmyamiti
[p.105]
tadbhāvanāprakarṣaḥ samastavastunairātmyaṃ viśadayan samastavastuviśadatāmantareṇa
tadanupapatteḥ samastavastuvaiśadyamāvahatī -tyuktam |
satyamuktam | ayuktaṃ tu tat | tathā hi | nā''gamā'numānagocaratva
nirātmanāṃ vastubhedānāṃ paramārthasatām | na hi te eteṣāmanyanivṛttimātrāva
gāhinī paramārthasatsvalakṣaṇaṃ gocarayitumarhati | nāpi tadviṣayā bhāvanā
tadagrāhyamapi svalakṣaṇaṃ tadadhyavaseyatayā tadviṣaya iti tadyonirapi bhāvanā
tadviṣayeti tatprakarṣavaiśadyaheturiti cet | na | tadavaseyasyāpi paramārthasattvā
'bhāvāt | tathā hi | yadanupānena gṛhyate yaccā'vasīyate dve apyanyanivṛttī
na vastunī | svalakṣaṇā'vagāhitve abhilāpasaṃsargayogyapratibhāsatvā'nupapatteḥ |
mā bhūttayoḥ svalakṣaṇaṃ viṣayaḥ | tatprabhavabhāvanāprakarṣajanmanastu
viśadābhasya cetaso bhaviṣyati kāminīvikalpaprabhavabhāvanāprakarṣādiva
kāmo''turasya kāminīsvalakṣaṇasākṣātkāraḥ | karikumbhakaṭhorakucakalaśe
hariṇaśābalola locane campakadalā'vadātagātralate lāvaṇyasarasi nirantara
lagnalalitadoḥkandalīmūlamāliṅganamaṅgane preyasitare prayaccha sañjīvaya
patito'smi tava caraṇa nalinayoriti vacanakāyaceṣṭayorupalabdheḥ | asti
ca vikalpā'vikalpayāḥ kathaṃcitsamānaviṣayateti nā'tiprasaṅga iti | satyam |
sambhavatyanubhavo na punarasyā'rtha prāmāṇyasambhavaḥ | atadutpatteḥ atadātma
nastadavyabhicāraniyamā'yogādatādātmyaṃ cā'rthasya vijñānādatirekāt
anatireke'pi ca vijñānānāmanyonyasya bhedatādātmyāt ekasya vijñānasyetara
vijñānavedanā'nupapatteḥ | vijñānasvalakṣaṇaikatvā'bhyupagame tannityamekamevā
'dvitīyaṃ brahmā'bhyasanīyamiti kṣaṇikanairātmyā'bhyāsā'bhyupagamo datta
jalāñjaliḥ prasajyeta | tanna tādātmyādasyā'vyabhicāraḥ | nāpi tatkārya
tvāt | bhāvanāprakarṣakārya khalvetanna viṣayakāryam |
yadyucyeta pāramparyeṇa tatkāryamanumānavat | yathā hi vahnisvalakṣaṇād
dhūmasvalakṣaṇaṃ tato dhūmā'nubhavastatā dahana vikalpastataścā'numānamutpannamiti
pāramparyeṇa vahnipratibandhātprāpakaṃ ca vahnerddāhapākakāriṇaḥ, tathaitadapyanumāna
janitabhāvanāprakarṣaparyantajaṃ pāramparyeṇā'rthaprabhūtatayā tadavyabhicāraniyamānna
pramāṇamiti cet | kimanumānena vahniṃ vyavasthāpya bhāvayato yadvahniviṣaya
[p.106]
viśadaṃ vijñānaṃ tatpramāṇamiti ? omiti bruvāṇasya parvatanitambārohaṇe
sati indriyasaṃnikarṣajanmano dahanavijñānasya bhāvanā''dheyaviśadābhavijñānena
saha samvāda niyamaprasaṅgaḥ | visaṃvādaśca bahulamupalabhyate | lakṣaṇayogini
ca vyabhicārasambhave tallakṣaṇameva vādhitamiti viśadābhamapi prātibhamiti
saṃśayākrāntamapramāṇam | tadbhāvanāyā bhūtārthatvaṃ na tajjanmaviśadavijñāna
prāmāṇye heturvyabhicārāt | evaṃ ca prasarppakasyeva saktukarkarīprāptimūlalābha
manorathaparamparānīto draviṇasambhārasākṣātkārastathāgatasya nirātmakasamasta
vastusākṣātkāra ityāpatitam sarvārthavastubhāvanāparikarmitacittasantānavartti
jñānaṃ pratyālambanapratyayatvamarthasya | tathā ca tadutpattestadavyabhicāraniyama iti
cet | nārthasyā''lambanapratyayatvaṃ vijñānaṃ pratīndriyā'pekṣatvena vyāptaṃ, taccā
'smādviruddhopalabdhyā vyāvartamānamālambanapratyayatāmapyarthasya nivarttayati |
na khalvindhanaviśeṣo dhūmaheturiti vināpi dahanaṃ sahasreṇāpi saṃskārairdhūmamā
dhatte | tadādhāne vā samastakāryahetutā'numānocchedaprasaṅgaḥ | bhāvanāyāśca
bhūtārthāyā arthā'napekṣāyā eva viśadavijñānajananasāmarthyamupalabdhaṃ kāmāturā
divarttinyā iti bhūtārthāpi tannirapekṣaiva samartheti nā'rthasyālambanapratyayatvaṃ
śakyā'vagamam | api cā''lambanapratyayā api ta evā'sya kṣaṇā yujyante,
ye tasya purastāttanā avyavadhānāḥ | tathā ca ta evā'sya grāhya na punaḥ
pūrvatarāstatkālā anāgatāśceti na sarvaviṣayatā | atha dṛśyamānā dhātutraya
paryāpannāḥ prāṇabhṛto janmāntaraparivṛttau yatrā'tītā'nāgatasvandhakadambako
pādānopādeyātmāna iti taddarśanaṃ dṛśyamānatādātmyena tadviśeṣaṇatayā'tītā
'nāgatānyapi gocarayati | na cā'smadādidarśanasyāpi tathā prasaṅgo,
rāgādimalāvṛtatvāt | tasya hi bhagavatī nirmṛṣṭanikhilakleśopakleśamalaṃ
vijñānamanā varaṇaṃ paritaḥ pradyotamānaṃ svālambanaṃ pratyayaṃ sarvā''kāraṃ
gocarayattasya ca sākṣātparamparayā ca kathaṃcitsarveṇa sambandhāddeśakālavipra
kārṇavastuviśiṣṭasvabhāvatayā tathaiva gocarayet | na ca sarvagrahaṇamantareṇeti
sarvaviṣayamasya vijñānamanāvaraṇaṃ siddham |
tadanupapannam | vicārā'sahatvāt | tathā hi iyamālambanapratyayasya
sarvaviśiṣṭatā svābhāvikī cenna tāvatsarvasminnālambanapratyaye caikā bhavati |
ekasyā'nekavṛttitvā'nupapatteḥ | nānā cedālambanapratyayāśca sarve ceti tattvam |
tathā ca na sambandha iti na tadgrahaṇe sarvagrahaṇavikalpāropitatathātvavilpakaṃ
vastuviṣayaṃ sarvatra pratīyata iti subhāṣitam | svālambanapratyayamātragocaramevā
[p.107]
'vikalpakaṃ samastavastuviśiṣṭālambanā'dhyavasāyajananam | tenā'dhyavasāyā'nu
gatavyāpāramavikalpakamapi samastavastuviṣayaṃ bhavati | yathā''ha—vyavasya 
nti kṣaṇādeva sarvākāraṃ mahādhiya iti ceta | atha katipaya vastvālambanasyā
'nubhavasya kutaḥ sa eṣa mahimā ? yataḥ samastavastvavasāya iti | sagādyāva
raṇavigamāditi cettarhi yathāvastu tāni paśyet, na punarasmādapārthatvameveti |
tadayuktam | vikalpanirmāṇakauśalamasya yujyate | tattvā''varakatā hi malānāṃ
keśādīnāṃ na punarvikalpanirmāṇapratibandhatā | tasmādbhāvanāprakarṣamātrajatvāda
rthā'vyabhicāraniyamā'bhāvādviśadābhamapi saṃśayākrānnatvādapramāṇamapratyakṣaṃ
ceti sāmpratam |
yadapi sadarthaprakāśanaṃ buddheḥ svabhāvo'sadarthatvaṃ tvāgantukamityasati
bādhake sadarthatvameveti | tadayuktam | anumitabhāvitavahniviṣayaviśadā
bhavijñānādiprāmāṇyaprasaṅgāt | tadviṣayasya kvacidvādhadarśanādaprāmāṇya
mihāpi samānam | anyatrā'bhiniveśāt | tadiha yadi viśadābhavijñāna
hetutvaṃ bhāvanāyā viśeṣaṇayogena sādhyate tataḥ siddhasādhanam | bhavatu
tathāgatastathābhūtavijñānavān, na tattve tadvijñānamasya pratyakṣamapramāṇatvāt |
tathā cā'pakṣadharmatayā hetorasiddhatā, prasiddhadharmiṇo'jijñāsitaviśeṣaṇatayā
'numeyatvā'bhāvāt | atha pratyakṣavijñānahetutā bhāvanāyāḥ paraṃ pratyakṣasiddhā
sādhyate, tathā sati sādhyaviparyayavyāpterviruddhatā hetoḥ | viśeṣaṇatrayavatyā
api bhāvanāyā viśadābhabhrāntivijñānajanakatvāt | dṛṣṭāntasya ca sādhyahīna
tvāt | yadā ca bhūtārthabhāvanājanitatve'pi nā'sya prāmāṇyamabhūtārthatvāttadā,
yaducyate
nirupadravabhūtārthasvabhāvasya viparyayaiḥ |
na bādho'yatnavattvepi buddhestatpakṣapātataḥ || iti,
tadanupapannam | bhūtārthatve hi buddhestatpa kṣapātitve'bhūtārthaiḥ pratipakṣairbādho na
bhavet | abhūtārthā tviyaṃ svātmā'bhāvamāpannāpyātmīyadṛṣṭiriva sambhavad
bādhā | tasmātpratipakṣavivṛddhimātram | na cā''tyantikī vivṛddhiḥ sambhavati |
yathā samūlakāṣaṃ kaṣitā doṣā na punarudbhaviṣyanti ata evā'sthirāśrayatve
'pyapunaryatnāpekṣitve'pyasya nā''tyantikī niṣṭhā sambhavati | ātmīyadṛśa iva
virodhipratyayasambhavāt | tatsambhavaścā'bhūtārthatvāt | tacca na | śrutā numitavi
ṣayaṃ tu pratyakṣaṃ na sambhavatyeva | tayoḥ parokṣarūpā'vagāhitvāt | pratyakṣasya ca
[p.108]
nanu na kā lato'pi niyamaścakṣurādīnām | avartamānarajatā dyupa
labdheḥ | na hi sā śuktikādyupalabdhiḥ | anyākārasaṃvido'nyaviṣayatvā
'yogāt anāśvāsāt |
tadviparītā'rthatvāt tadgatabhūtārthatvā'nuvidhāyitvena svaviṣa ye śrutā'numāna
jñānāpekṣatayā prāmāṇyā'nupapatteśca | tatsiddhametadbhatārthabhāvanāprakarṣaparyantajaṃ
vijñānamapratyakṣam, arthe pramāṇatvāt yadapramāṇaṃ tadapratyakṣamarthe | yathā kāmā''
turasya kāminīvijñānam | apramāṇaṃ ca tat | nitāntaviśadābhatve sati
bhāvanājanyatvāt | yannitāntaviśadābhatve sati bhāvanāprakarṣajaṃ vijñānaṃ
tadapramāṇaṃ, yathā'numitabhāvitavahniviṣayaviśadābhavijñānamiti samānahetukatvaṃ
samānarūpatayā vyāptam | yathāha tadatadrūpiṇo bhāvāstadatadrūpahetujā iti |
tadasyāḥ prāmāṇyaṃ nivartamānaṃ tulyahetujatvamapi nivartayati | na caiṣā bhūtārthe
bhāvanāprakarṣaparyantā'tīndriyasaṃnikṛṣṭā'numitabhāvitavahnivaiśadye ca nirātmaka
samastavastuvaiśadye'pi ca viśiṣyate | na ca rāgādyāvaraṇaviraho vā viśeṣaḥ |
na khalvete kambalādivadāvarakā vijñānasya, ki tu tadākṣiptamanā vividha
viṣayabhedatṛṣṇādiparipluto na śaknoti bhāvayitumiti bhāvanā''daramātre eva
tadvirahopayogaḥ | asti cehāpi śiśiratarasaṃbhūtajaḍimamantharatarakāyakāṇḍa
syā'numitavahnibhāvanābhiyoga iti na hetubheda iti pratibandhasiddhiḥ | na
caikapārthivā'ṇusamavāyikāraṇajanmabhirabhinnauṣṇyā'pekṣaikavahnisayogā'samavāyi
kāraṇairgandharasarūpasparśairnānābhāvairvyabhicāraḥ | sāmarthyavaicitryādekatve'pi pārthi
vasya paramāṇoḥ | tadvaicitryaṃ ca kāryavaicitryopalambhāt | tacca nityasamavetaṃ
nityaṃ kāraṇasāmarthyakrameṇa ca pārthivā'vayavini kārye jāyata ityavadātam |
pariśiṣṭaṃ granthavyākhyānasamaye vakṣyāmaḥ | tadāstāṃ tāvat |
samprati vyavasthāpitonmūlanena kaścitprābhākaragandhī pratyavatiṣṭhate—nanu
na kālato'pi niyamaścakṣurādīnām | kutaḥ ? idaṃ rajatamityavarttamānarajatādyu
palabdheḥ | nanvidaṃ rajatamiti purovarttinīṃ śuktimavalambamānā kathamasaṃnihita
deśakālarajatopalabdhi ? rityata āha—na hi sā śuktikādyupalabdhiḥ |
kasmāt ? anyā''kārāyāḥ saṃvido'nyaviṣayatvā'yogāt | tadyoge viṣaya
tvasyā'niyamāt | sarvaṃ vijñānaṃ sarvaviṣayamityayatnasiddhā sarvajñatā syāditi
phalitaṃ tathāgatamanorathena | tasmādyadākāravijñānaṃ sa evāsya viṣaya eṣi
[p.109]
adhigatasvātantryaṃ ca manaḥ svapne | na ca svapnajñānaṃ smṛtiḥ | atadrū
patvāt | naitatsāram | tathā hi—
arthā'pekṣā mudhā tulyamanyākārapravedanam |
dehino vartamānābhā svapne spaṣṭā'pi hi smṛtiḥ ||
cakṣurādayastāvadvarttamānameva śuktikāśakalādyarthaṃ darśayanti, tada
pekṣaṇāt | anyathā vaipha lyāt tatsannidheḥ tadasannidhāvapi rajatavedanaprāpteḥ |
tavyo niyamāya | rajatā''kāraṃ cedamiti tadevāsya viṣayo na punaḥ śuktiḥ |
avedanāt anāśvāsācca | yadi hyanyā''kārā saṃvidanyaviṣayā bhavettadā
saṃvidaḥ svārthavyabhicārāt sarvatraivā'nāśvāsaprasaṅga iti na prekṣāvān kva
cit kutaścidvā pravarteta nivarteta vā | tasmādapi rajataviṣayameva | na cedaṃ
rajatamiti sāmānādhikaraṇyapratheyamapi tvasāmānādhikaraṇyā'graho doṣa
vaśāt | na ca smṛtiḥ | sākṣātkārā''spadatvāt | na ca nāsti | svasaṃvedana
siddhatvāt | tadasya kṛṣtasāmarthya cakṣurvā mano vā kāraṇamakāmenā'pyabhyupa
gantavyam | na ca rajataṃ varttamānamityavartamānamapi cakṣurmano vā gocarayati |
tathā ca na kālato'pi niyamaḥ |
paratantraṃ bahirmana ityākṣipati—adhigatasvātantryaṃ ca manaḥ svapne |
tenā'nyatrāpi bāhye svatantrameva | yadyucyeta svapne'pi pūrvā'nubhavabhāvanāsahaṃ
kāri smṛtiṃ janayati manaḥ, tenā'svatantrameveti, tatrāha—na ca svapnajñānaṃ
smṛtiḥ | kasmāt ? atadrūpatvāt | anubhūtaviṣayā'saṃpramoṣo hi smṛte rūpam |
asaṃpramoṣaścā'nubhūtatayā'nubhāvyasyā'nusandhānam | na caivaṃvidhaṃ svapnajñātaṃ,
tasmānna smṛtiḥ | na ca cākṣuṣaṃ, cakṣurvyāpārā'bhāvāt | tatsiddhametadbāhye
manaḥ svatantramiti |
tadetannirākaroti—naitatsāram | tathā hi—
arthā'pekṣā mudhā tulyamanyākārapravedanam ||
doṣato varttamānātvaṃ svapne spaṣṭā 'pi hi smṛtiḥ ||
yattāvaduktam anītā'viṣayaṃ cakṣurādīti, tatrāha—cakṣurādayastāva
dvarttamānameva śuktikāśakalādyarthaṃ darśayanti | kutaḥ ? tadapekṣaṇāt | śuktikādya
pekṣaṇāt | anyathā tadanapekṣaṇe vaiphalyāttatsaṃnidheḥ śuktisaṃnidhānasya |
kuto'sya vaiphalyaprasaṅga ? ityata āha—śuktikā'napekṣaṇe tadasaṃnidhāvapi
rajatavedanaprāpteḥ | ayamabhisaṃdhiḥ | na tāvaccakṣurādivadrajatavijñānaṃ kāraṇa
[p.110]
rajatatā'vabhāsanaṃ yadi na śuktiviṣayaṃ vartamānābhāsanamapi tarhi nā'varttamāna
gocaram |
tāmātreṇā'pekṣate śuktiśakalam | cakṣurādyabhāva iva tadabhāve'nyatra rajatasaṃni
dhāvapi rajatavijñānā'bhāvaprasaṅgāt | tasmādviṣayatayaivā'pekṣate rajatavijñānaṃ
śuktiśakalamiti |
yaccoktamanyā''kārāsaṃbinnā'nyaviṣayeti, tatrāha—rajatāvabhāsanaṃ yadi
na śuktiviṣayaṃ vartamānābhāsanamapi tarhi nā'vartamānagocaram |
ayamabhisandhiḥ | atītarajatagocaraṃ cedetadvijñānamatītatayaivā'smin
rajataṃ prapadyeta, tathā ca tatprepsavo na pravarteran, atītasya prāptyabhāvāt | na
khalvatītaṃ tattayā'vagamya śabdādvadānyapreṣṭhaṃ yudhiṣṭhiraṃ kṣutkṣāmātmā'pi bhikṣuko
yācituṃ pravartate | atītasyāpi rajatasya doṣatastatrā'navabhāsanādvartamānasya
ca śuktikeya miti tattaya gṛhītasya rajatasādhāraṇarūpavedanāt | tadbhedahetu
śuktikātvasāmānyaviśeṣā'navabhāsācca | samīhitasaṃnihitarajatamatitulyatā
bhedā'nadhyavasāyasāmarthyā'bhyāsāt | tataśca rajatamidamiti sāmānādhika
raṇyavyapadeśaḥ samīcīnaṃ saṃnihitarajatapratyayatulyavyavahārapravartanaṃ ca śuktikeya
miti bhedadhiyaḥ svaviṣayaviruddhabhedā'grahanivāraṇena pūrvapratyayaprasañjitarajato
citavyavahāranirākaraṇādbādhakatā'pyupapanneti cenna | bhedā'grahamātrasya
tathābhūtavyavahārapravartakatvā'nupapatteḥ | sannihitarajatā'vabhāso hi tadvyavahā
raheturna punaratathā'navabhāsaḥ | tattulyatayā so'pi tadvyavahārāya kalpate |
bhedā'navabhāsaśca tattulyateti cet | yadyevamavartamānā'vabhāsanayā'tītarajatā
'vabhāsavijñānatulyatā'pyasyāstīti tattulyavyavahārapravṛttirapyetasya syāt |
tadayaṃ rajatavijñānavān purovarttini śuktikāśakale pravartteta nivartteteti tava
viruddhaṃ kriyādvayamāpannam | purovarttivastvavabhāsa eva raja vijñānādagṛhītabhedaḥ
samīcīnasaṃnihitarajatā'vabhāsasadṛśavyavahāraheturiti nā'yaṃ prasaṅga iti
cet | atha rajataṃ tadidaṃ tu śuklabhāsvaramiti buddhervyavahāraṃ kasmānna
pravarttayati ? yathaiva hi samīcīnasaṃnihitarajatadhītulyatayā'sya bhedā
'nadhyavasāyāttathā tadrajatamidaṃ tu śuklabhāsvaramitītyetattulyatā'pyabhedā
nadhyavasāyāt | na cā'dhyavasāyayoḥ kaścidviśeṣaḥ, yenaika eva svocitaṃ
[p.111]
sāmyaṃ saṃpādayati nā'paro'pīti | so'yamasya tadavastha eva viruddhakriyādvayasya
samāveśaprasaṅgaḥ | na ca cetanavyavahāropāyatulyatā jñānopāyānāṃ, yena
jñānahetoreva caramavastuvyavahāra ityucyeta | ajātarūpatatkāryatatsaṃbandhānāmapi
cakṣurādīnāṃ jñānopāyanvadarśanāt | cetanavyavahārasya tu vijñānajatvaniyamena
paryanuyogā'narhatvāt | tatsiddhametadvivādādhyāsitaṃ rajatavijñānaṃ dṛśyamāna
purovarttivastugocaraṃ tadupāyā'viṣayatve'pi sati tavarthinastatraiva niyamena
pravartakatvāt | tadupāyā'viṣayaṃ sadyatraiva yadarthinaṃ pravartayati tattadviṣayaṃ,
yathobhayasiddhasamocīnarajatavijñānamiti | tathā hyayaṃ pravṛttiniyamo hetumattayā
vyāptaḥ sa cā'tadviṣayatvād vyāvartamānaḥ svavyāpyaṃ pravṛttiniyamamapi vyāvartta
yati tadviṣayatve eva vyavasthāpayatīti pratibandhasiddhiḥ | na cā'sāvatadviṣayatve
'pi bhedā'grahamātrādbhavitumarhati | abhedāgrahaṇasyāpi svocitavyavahārapravarta
nena virodhikriyāsamāveśena vyavahartuḥ pravṛttinivṛttiprasaṅgādityuktam |
tasmādanyasyā'nyathāgrahaṇamepitavyam | evaṃ ca vartamānatayā'vabhāsanamupapadyate |
tathā ca rajatā'vabhāsanaṃ yadi na śuktiviṣayam anyatvādvarttamānā'vabhāsanamapi
tarhi nā'varttamānagocaram | anyatvatādavasthyāt |
yadyucyeta, avartamānagocaraṃ bhaviṣyati vartamānā'vabhāsaṃ vijñānadoṣa
sāmarthyāt | na ca doṣāṇāṃ kāryakāraṇasārthyavihatimātre vyāpāro na
kāryāntaraviṣayaśakyā''dhāne, na jātu yavabījamupahataṃ doṣaiḥ śālyaṅkurāya
kalpate iti sāmpratam | bhasmakādidoṣavaśādudarajasya vahnerbahuvidhā'nnapānapāca
nopalambhāt vyabhicārāt | aśitapītāhārarasabhāvaḥ kāryamudarajasya teja
sastatpratighātena bhasmakasyāpi doṣabhāva iti na vyabhicāra iti cenna |
ihāpi samānatvāt | indriyādīnāmapi paramārthavijñānajananasāmarthyaṃ svābhā
vikamapavādhya viparyayavijñānahetutāmādadhānā doṣā na cyavante tattāyā |
na cā'navabhāsanāt avartamānaṃ na viṣaya iti yujyate | vikalpā'kṣamatvāt |
kimavartamānaṃ rajatamavarttamānatayā na viṣaya iti sādhyate āho svit
rajatatayā'pi viṣaya ? iti | tatra prathame kalpe na vipratipadyāmahe | rajatatayā
tvaviṣayatve sādhye tattvenā'navābhāsanamasiddhaṃ na heturbhavati | tathoktamācāryeṇa
ābhāsate kathaṃcicca tannātyantaṃ na bhāsate, tena nāvediketi | tasmā
ddoṣavaśādavarttamānamapi varttamānatayā pratīyata evetyāha—doṣatastathātvamavartta
mānagocarasya vijñānasya varttamānā'vabhāsanamitarasyāpi śuktikāgocarasyāpi
rajatā'vabhānaṃ tulyameva |
[p.112]
doṣatastathātvamitarasyāpi tulyam | vartamāne cā'vartamānā'vabhāso
nā'vartamāne vartamānā'vabhāsaḥ | arthasannidheḥ sāphalyāt | nedaṃ rajatamiti ca
prasaktasya pratiṣedhopapatteḥ | taimirikasya keśoṇḍrābhaṃ jñānamālokāṃśeṣviti
nā'rthāpekṣo vyabhicāraḥ | svapne'pi kiṃ smṛtervartamānā 'vabhāsatvam, uta
manasaḥ svātantryamiti ? smṛtervartamānā'vabhāsatā yuktā | anantara
divasā'nubhūtasyā'vikalā'nadhikasya vispaṣṭasmṛteḥ vartamānavadvedanāddoṣo
paplavena | na tvadṛṣṭaṃ manasaḥ svātantryam | doṣataśca na sāmarthyā'tireko,
evamāpātatastulyatāmabhidhāya svā'bhimatasyāsya viśeṣamāha—vartamāne
śuktiśakale cā'yamavarttamānarajatāvabhāso nā'vartamāne rajate vartamānā'va
bhāsaḥ | purovarttivastuviṣayaṃ nā'tītaviṣayaṃ rajatamidamiti vijñānamityarthaḥ |
kasmāt ? arthasaṃnidheḥ sāphalyāt | kutaśca nedaṃ rajatamiti prasaktasya prati
ṣedhopapatteḥ | aṅgulīnirddeśena śuktikāśakalameva rajatatayā pratiṣedhanti
vipratipattāraḥ | tattatra rajataṃ prasañjitaṃ pūrvavijñāneneti gamyate | na ca
vivekā'grahaṇena tatprasañjanaṃ, jīvatyavivekā'grahe paripanyini | tasmādviparī
tagrahe tatprasañjakaṃ kalpate nānyathā | na ca purovarttitvamanenā'tīte prasañji
tam | idantayā tasya pratiṣedhā'sambhavāt | tatpurovarttivastuviṣayameva tadvi
jñānamityavadātam |
nanu taimi rikasya keśābhavijñānamupajāyate | na ca tatrāsti keśānāṃ
saṃnidhiḥ | nāpyanyasya kasyacit | yena tattadālambanaṃ bhavet | tasmādasaṃni
hitakeśābalambanameṣitavyaṃ tadvijñānam | tataścā'rthāpekṣo vyabhicāraścakṣu
rādīnāmityata āha—taimirikasya keśoṇḍrābhaṃ vijñānamālokāṃśeṣviti
nā'rthāpekṣo vyabhicāraḥ |
yaccoktamadhigatasvātantryaṃ manaḥ svapnā'vasthāyāṃ bāhya iti tanni
rācikīrṣurvimṛśati svapne'pi kiṃ smṛtervarttamānā'vabhāsitvamuta tasminmanaḥ
svātantryamiti ? vimṛśya svapakṣaṃ pratijānīte—smṛtervarttamānā'vabhāsatā
yuktā | hetumāha—anantaradivasā'nubhūsyā vikalānādhikasyāpi spaṣṭasmṛte
rvarttamānavadvedanāddoṣopaplavena | na tvadṛṣṭaṃ manasaḥ svātantryaṃ bāhye yuktam |
atha kasmāddoṣa eva manaso nādhatte tathāvidhasāmarthyā'tiśayaṃ ? yena bāhye
svatantrameva tadvartetetyata āha—doṣataśca na sāmarthyā'tiśayo dṛṣṭo yena manaḥ
[p.113]
doṣasya tadapanayanahetutvāt | satyasvapnadarśanaṃ tu yadi vartamānasya tathā
grahaṇāttadadṛṣṭam | prabodhe svapnākhyānārthasaṃvādadarśanād dṛṣṭamiti cet | na |
ākhyānasaṃvādā'bhāvāt | ākhyānasaṃvādastu kākatālīyaḥ | bhaviṣyatastu
vartamānavaddarśanaṃ kathaṃ satyam ? mā bhūt satyam | asti tāvat svātantryaṃ
manasaḥ | tadapi tadvividhasyā'nubhūtasya smaraṇam | itarathā manaso'dṛṣṭa-
svatantraṃ tadbāhye tattvajñānaṃ prasuvīta | viparyāsastu saṃskāratantrādeva manasa
