ityuktam | kasmānna doṣato manasaḥ sāmarthyā'tireka ityata āha—doṣasya
tadapanayanahetutvāt | taditi tacca vijñānajananasāmarthyaṃ parāmṛśati |
yadi manyeta asti khalu satyaṃ svapnadarśanamapi | tadyathā | vallabhā
liṅgitamātmānama nubhūya svapne jāgarāyāmapi tathaiva paśyati | na ceyaṃ varta
mānā'vabhāsā smṛtiriti sāmpratam | asati bādhake tadanupapatteḥ | tadabhāvaśca
saṃvādadarśanāt | tasmādatra manasaḥ svātantryamupetavyamiti, tatrāha—satyasvapna
darśanaṃ tu yadi vartamānasya tathāgrahaṇāt tadadṛṣṭam | na khalvayamapi pratyayaḥ
saṃvādī | tathā hi svapne jāgarā'vasthā''pannamayamātmānaṃ vallabhāvāhukadalī
balayitamupalabdhavān, vinidranayanotpalastu paśyannapi priyā''liṅganaṃ nidrā
ṇo'hamāsaṃ sampratyeva prabuddha ityavagacchati | kuto'sya svapnadṛṣṭā'rthasaṃvāda ?
ityarthaḥ |
śaṅkate—prabodhe svapnadṛṣṭārthasaṃvādadarśanānmanasaḥ svātantryaṃ dṛṣṭamiti
cet | yadā khalu svapne śuklamālyāmbaradharo brāhmaṇāyanamāha 'somaśarmannā
yuṣman pañcame'hani prātareva bhūpatirurvarāprāyabhuvā grāmavareṇa mānayiṣyati
tvāmiti, sa ca pañcame'hani prātareva tathā saṃmānitaḥ somaśarmā bhavati
satyasvapnavānityarthaḥ | nirākaroti—na | kasmāt ? ākhyānasaṃvādā'bhā
vāt | ākhyānena hi prabuddhaḥ somaśarmā smarannapyākhyātāraṃ brāhmaṇāyanaṃ na
paśyati tatpraṇītatāṃ cā''khyānasya smṛtyārūḍhasya manyate iti | maivaṃ bhūdā
khyānasaṃvādaḥ svapnasyāstīti, tathā ca satyatetyata āha—ākhyānasaṃvādastu
kākatālīyo daivaprāpto na svapnajñānaṃ pramāṇayati, yathāvidhasyaiva tatsāma
grīkasya visaṃvādavato bahulamupalabdheḥ | saṃvādasya daivā'dhīnatvāt | nanu
yatrā'tītasya varttamānavadbhāsanaṃ tatra saṃskāratantramastu mano bhāvanāyoni
pūrvā'nubhavāt | yatra punaranāgata eva vartamānatayā'vabhāsate svapne kutastada
nubhavaḥ kutastarāṃ ca tadbhāvanā kutastamāṃ ca mana sa stattantrate ? tyata
āha—bhaviṣyatastu vartamānavaddarśanaṃ kathaṃ satyam ? codaka āha—mā bhūtsatya
[p.114]
sāmarthyasya kalpyatvāt | dharmaviśeṣaścedyathārthadarśanaprasaṅgāt | tasya kāryaṃ
pratyadṛṣṭasāmarthyasya kalpyatvāt | yadi ca na smṛtiḥ svapnajñānaṃ pramāṇaṃ tarhi
na pramāṇamapi | bahistathā grāhyasyā'bhāvāt | na tarhi bahirarthe svatantraṃ
manaḥ | svapne tasyā'grāhyatvāt | grāhyatve vā'nubhūtasya grahaṇaṃ, tathā
dṛṣṭatvāt | na doṣato'dṛṣṭasāmarthyā'tirekakalpanetyubhayathā na svatantraṃ
bahirmanaḥ | etena timirataḥ keśādidarśanaṃ pratyuktam |
masti tāvatsvātantryaṃ manasaḥ saṃskārasya bhaviṣyatyasambhavāt | samādhātā
svā'bhiprāyamudghāṭayati—tadapi tadvidhasyānyatrā'nubhūtasya smaraṇam | na
hi sarvathā'nanubhūtamupalabhyate svapne | parasparaviruddhānāmapi kvacitkadācitkaṃthaṃ
cidupalabdhānāṃ sahānubhavavibhramasambhavaḥ | kasmātpunaḥ kleśena sarvatra smaraṇa
māśrīyate ? ityata āha—itarathā manaso'dṛṣṭasvātantryakalpanāprasaṅgāt |
yadyucyeta svapne'pi preyasīsaṃbhogajanmanaḥ sukhodbhavasyopalabdherasti
samudācārād vṛtti rasya dharmaḥ, tena tadviśiṣṭameva manaḥ priyānubhavaṃ bhāva
yiṣyatīti kṛtaṃ saṃskāreṇeti, tatrāha—dharmmaviśeṣaśced yathārthadarśanaprasaṅgāt |
dharmmaviśeṣo hi yathārthadarśanaheturavagata āgamataḥ kathaṃ mithyājñānāya kalpate ?
api ca laukikaṃ kāraṇamasādhāraṇamāśritya svakāryaṃ janayati | na ca tanmanasaḥ
svātantryaṃ prati kiṃcidupalabhyate | tena dharmaviśeṣa evā'sahāyaḥ kāraṇamiti
kalpanīyaṃ, taccā'dṛṣṭapramāṇenetyāha—tasya dharmaviśeṣasya manaḥsvātantryaṃ
kāryaṃ pratyadṛṣṭasāmarthyasya kalpanāprasaṅgāt | yadi ca na smṛtiḥ svapnajñānaṃ
bhavanmate pramāṇaṃ tarhi svatantrasya manasastadviṣaye pravṛttiḥ | atha na pramāṇamapi |
kutaḥ ? svapnajñāne bhāsate vastu, tasya bādhake sati bahistathā grāhyā'bhāvāt |
uttaraṃ—na tarhi bahirarthe svatantraṃ manaḥ, svapne tasya tathāvidhasyā'
grāhyatvāt | prakārabhedarahitavasturūpagrāhyatve vā bhāvanaiva jāgarti smaraṇahetuḥ |
tasyāśca pūrvā'nubhavamantareṇā'sambhavādanubhūtasya grahaṇam | tathā siddhopaplava
baśātsaṃskāratantrasya manaso bāhyasmṛteḥ kāraṇatvena dṛṣṭatvāt | na tu
doṣato'dṛṣṭasāmarthyā'tirekakalpanā | tasmādyadi grāhyatvaṃ bāhyasya yadi
vā'grāhyatvamubhayathā'pyasvatantraṃ bahirmanaḥ | etena manasaḥ svapnavijñāne
svātantryapradarśanamārgeṇa timirataḥ keśādijñānaṃ mānasamiti pratyuktam |
alokāṃśālambanatayā tadvijñānasya doṣavallocananimittatvāt |
[p.115]
yattūktamanāśvāsāditi tadanyatrā''cāryeṇa—
bodhādeva pramāṇatvamiti mīmāṃsakasthitim |
vidannavyabhicāreṇa tāṃ vyudasyatyapaṇḍitaḥ ||
ityādinā prabandhena dūṣitamiti neha dūṣitam | tathā dūṣaṇakraṇikeha
sūcyate |
kimavyabhicāritaiva prāmāṇyamatha tatkāraṇaṃ tadvyāpikā vā ? yena
kvacidvyabhicāradarśanāttadabhāve sati jñānamātre'nāśvāsaḥ syāt | na tāvada
vyabhicāritaiva prāmāṇyam | avyabhicāriṇāmapi vahnyādau dhūmādīnāṃ
kutaścinnimittādanupajanitakṛśānupratyayānāmaprāmāṇyaṃ syāt | vyabhicāri
ṇāmapi cakṣurādīnāṃ nīlādibhede tadviṣayavijñānahetūnāṃ prāmāṇyamiti
sāmpratam | pramitikriyāṃ prati sādhakatamatvasambhavāt | anyathā kāṣṭhādī
nāmapi pākādāvapi asādhanatvaprasaṅgāt | nāpi prāmāṇyakāraṇam | ata
evā'nyathā cakṣurādīnāṃ nīlādibhedavyabhicāriṇāṃ tatpratimitihetūnāma
prāmāṇyaprasaṅgāt | kecideva hi dhūmādayo vahnyādibodhajananāyā'vyabhicāra
mapekṣante, nā'pekṣante ca cakṣurādayaḥ | svabhāvaniyamāt | etena vyāpakatvama
vyabhicāritāyāḥ pramāṇyaṃ pratyuktaṃ veditavyam | jñānenā'vyabhicāro'pekṣyate
svakārye iti cet | na | vikalpā'sahatvāt | athāpi syādavyabhicāritopetaṃ
vijñānaṃ pramāṇaṃ tasyā'bhāvastattvam | taddhi tathāvidhaṃ satsvakārye paryāptaṃ
mithyājñānasya tatrā'sāmarthyāditi | tannāsti | vikalpā'nupapatteḥ | tathā hi |
vijñeyā'vabhāso'sya kāryamucyate svagocarocitavyavahārapravarttanaṃ vā | tatra na
tāvat jñeyā'vabhāse'sya svabhāvapratilambhamātrā'dhīnajanmani sambhavatyavyabhi
cāragrahā'pekṣā | na hi vayamutpannavijñānaviṣayā'vabhāsāyā'sya svaviṣayasya
svaviṣayā'vyabhicāramudīkṣamāṇāḥ sma | bhūtireva hi vijñānasya viṣayā'vabhāse
kriyeti | yathāha—'tena janmaiva viṣaye buddhervyāpāra iṣyate | tadeva ca pramāṇa
rūpamiti |
'na hi tat kṣaṇamapyāste jāyate vā pramātmakam |
yenā'rthagrahaṇe paścādvyāpriyetendriyādivat || iti ca |
atha svagocarocitavyavahārapravarttane jñānamavyabhicāritāmapekṣate | na
hi vijñānaṃ vijñānamityeva vyavahāraṃ pravartayati, mithyājñānādapi tatprasaṅgāt |
tasmānmithyājñānād vyāvṛttamasya saṃvādakatvamarthakriyāprāpaṇasāmarthyamavyabhi
cāritālakṣaṇaṃ prekṣāvatpravṛttyaṅgamavagantavyam | na khalvavyabhicāramantareṇa
[p.116]
tatastatprāptiḥ svārthavyabhicāraśca | tato'nyasya jñānasya tasmādanutpatteḥ |
anyathā'tiprasaṅgāt | yathāha—
arthasyā'saṃbhave bhāvāt pratyakṣe'pi pramāṇatā |
pratibandhasvabhāvasya taddhetutve samaṃ dvayam || iti |
tad dvividhaṃ pratyakṣajñānam | arthakriyāsādhananirbhasikaṃ pravartakaṃ pramāṇam,
abhyāsadaśā''pannaṃ tadviparītaṃ ca | tatra tadviparītasyā'rthakriyānirbhāsādvi
jñānātprāmāṇyamabhyupagantavyam | yathāha pramāṇato'rthapratipatau pravṛttisāmarthyā
darthavatpramāṇamiti | tathā pramāṇamavisaṃvādi jñānamarthakriyāsthitiḥ,
asaṃvādanamiti | tadarthanāntarīyakā'rthāntaradarśanādabhyāsadaśāpannāt | tadyathā |
ārādvahnivijñānasya vahnikiśukanicayasaṃśayena tirohitaprāmāṇyasya dhūma
darśanāt prāmāṇyaviniścayaḥ | arthakriyānirbhāsasya jñānasyā'gṛhītaprāmāṇyasya
na pravartakavijñānaprāmāṇyaniścāyakatvaṃ, gṛhītaprāmāṇyasya vā tanniścāya
katvenā'navasthāprasaṅga iti cet, na | arthakriyānirbhāsasya jñānasya svarūpe
ṇeṣyamāṇatayā phalatvenā'pravartakatayā pramāṇalakṣaṇā'yogitayā vyabhicāra
niścayasyā'nupayogāt | tadrūpasya ca svasaṃvedanasiddhasya śaṅkā'nāspadatvāt |
yadāha—svarūpasya svato gatiriti | nibandhāro'pyāhuḥ—
arthakriyā sukhādyātmajñānameva tathāvidham |
pramāṇalakṣaṇā'yogi svato'dhigataśaktikam || iti |'
anyonyasaṃśrayānnaivamiti cet | na | pravṛttidvaividhyāt | tatraitatsyāt |
arthakriyānirbhāsādvijñānāccedavyabhicāraniścayaścetasastadeva kutaḥ ? prekṣāvatāṃ
pravartakavijñānānniścitā'vyabhicārāditi cet prāptamanyonyasaṃśrayamiti tatra
prekṣāvatpravṛttidvaividhyāt | dvayī khalviha pravṛttiḥ prekṣāvatāṃ, tatrā''dyā
pravarttakavijñānaparīkṣārthā, dvitīyā tu parīkṣitasādhananirbhāsasya vijñānasya
tajjātīyādvijñānādarthakriyāprāptyarthā | yathā kila kṛṣīvalasya karparasthitāyāṃ
kṛṣiparikarmitāyāṃ bījā''vapanalakṣaṇā pravṛttirādyā bījaparīkṣārthā | sa tu
tatrā'ṅkurotpādanādavagatabījatattvaḥ sasyā'dhigamakāmyayā dvitīyāṃ pravṛtti
mārabhate parikarmitāyāṃ bhuvi tajjātīyabījā''vapanalakṣaṇāṃ, tatheyamapi
pravṛttirdvayīti nā'nyonyasaṃśrayaḥ | na ca prekṣāvattvavyāghātaḥ | parīkṣopāye
pravartamānasyā'nupāye saṃdigdhopāye vā'pravṛtteḥ | pravartatāṃ vopāyasaṃdehādapi
tathāpi na prekṣāvattvavighātaḥ | arthasaṃśayasya prekṣāvatpravṛttyaṅgatvāt nivṛttya
ṅgatvāccā'narthasaṃśayasya | abhyāsadaśāpannasya tu pratyakṣasya prāpitā'rthakriyā
[p.117]
jñānasādharmyādanumānāt prāmāṇyaviniścayaḥ | anumānasya tu nāntarīyakā'rtha
darśanajanmatayā niścitavyabhicārasya svata eva prāmāṇyaniścayaḥ | tatsva
gocarocitavyavahārapravarttanāyā'stvavyabhicāraniścayā'pekṣā pramāṇasyeti |
tadidamasāmpratam | arthakriyānirbhāsino hi vijñānasyā'rthāvyabhi
cārasya svārthe viniścāyakatvaṃ durlabhaṃ, kimaṅga punaḥ pūrvavijñānasya prāpaṇa
sāmarthye svasaṃvedanasiddhasya tasya prayojanatayā kimarthā'vyabhicāranirūpaṇena ki
ca prāmāṇyenetyuktamiti cet | tatkimidānīṃ yatra svapne kāmukasya svocitā
'rthakriyāsāmarthakāminīvijñānaṃ tadanantaraṃ pravṛttijñānamatha saṃbhogavijñānamantaraṃ
sukhaṃ caramadhātuvisargaśceti tatra tāvadādyaṃ kāminīvijñānamastu prāmāṇyaṃ, na
hyanyatrā'rthakriyānirbhāsaṃ nāsti vijñānam | tadanantaraṃ tasya bhāvājjāgarāyāṃ
tadarthakāriṇaḥ kāminīrūpasya bādhanāt | sukhacaramadhātuvisargayośca manomātra
yonitayā kāminyāmasatyāmapi tajjñānamātrādutpādanāt jāgarāyāmanuvṛttera
virodhāt | anyathā kāminīvastusādhyā'dharapallavanakhakṣatādivadvyāvṛtti
prasaṅgādayamadoṣa iti cet | na | prakṛte'rthakriyānirbhāsasya jñānasyā'vyabhi
cāritvā'graheṇā'rthakriyāyā aniścayena pravarttakasya jñānasyā'rthakriyāsamartha
vastupariprāpaṇalakṣaṇaprāmāṇyā'niścayena svapnavijñānasāmyāt |
yadi manyeta arthakriyānirbhāsaṃ jñānaṃ svataḥ pramāṇam | arthakriyā
niścayātmakatvāt | na ca svapnādau prasaṅgaḥ | tatrā'vasthāntare puruṣāntare
kālāntare vā bādhakotpādādaprāmāṇyāt | tatpravarttakeṣvapi jñāneṣu samāna
manyatrā'bhiniveśāt | api cā'vyabhicāritvaṃ nāma jñānasyārthena saha | na ca
tadarthagrahaṇādṛte śakyā'vasānam | na khalvagṛhītadahano dhūmasya tadavyabhicāra
mavadhārayitumarhati | kṣaṇikatvamapi jyotirvidāgameṣu pūrvā'parabhāgarahitakāla
kalāmātrasya prasiddhestanmātravarttitvaṃ bhāvasyeti nā'tyantā'prasiddhaṃ yena sattvasya
tadavyabhicāro na śakyā'vagamo bhavet | mā bhūdvā kāpi na naḥ kṣatiḥ | na
cā'pramāṇamapramāṇena vā 'navadhṛtaprāmāṇyena tadgrahaṇamavyabhicāragrahaṇā'ṅgam |
na khalvapramāṇenā'gṛhītapramāṇyena vā pramāṇena viṣayīkṛto vahnirdhūmā'vyabhi
cāragrahaṇāya kalpate | tasmādavyabhicāragrahaṇamantareṇa nā'rthagrahaṇaṃ, na cā'rtha
grahaṇamantareṇā'vyabhicāragrahaṇam iti parasparāśrayaprasaktiḥ | evaṃ ca dṛṣṭa
sāmarthyasādharmyā'numānenā'bhyāsadaśāpannapratyakṣajñānānāṃ svārthavyabhicāragrahaṇaṃ
parāstaṃ veditavyam |
yaccā'numānasya svataḥprāmāṇyamukta tasmin darśane pratyakṣasya svā'rthā
'vyabhicāragrahaṇāduktena krameṇa tatpūrvakatvā'numānasyodaya eva durlabhaḥ kuto
[p.118]
'sya prāmāṇyaṃ kutastarāṃ ca tat svataḥ ? nanvavyabhicārā'grahe jñānamātrāda
nāśvāsaḥ prekṣāvatāmityuktam | satyamuktamidaṃ tu vaktavyaṃ, ko'yamanāśvāsaḥ,
kiṃ saṃśaya āhosvidajñānam ? na tāvadajñānaṃ bodhasyaiva jñānatvāt | nāpi
saṃśayaḥ | viśeṣasmṛtyapekṣavimarśaṃ trikāraṇaṃ pañcakāraṇaṃ vā saṃśayaṃ rocayante 
samānā'nekadharmopapatteḥ | vipratipatterupalabdhyanupalabdhya vasthātaśca viśeṣā
'pekṣo vimarśaḥ saṃśaya ityācakṣāṇāstadvicakṣaṇāḥ | na cā'gṛhīte viśeṣe 
smaraṇasaṃbhavaḥ | na ca saṃśayātmaiva tadgrahaṇaṃ smṛtihetuḥ | smṛtau satyāṃ
yadbhāvāttasmiṃśca ssṛtyupapatteranyonyāśrayaprasaṅgāt | saṃskārajatve'pi ca
prasaṅgatādavasthyāt | anāditāyāśca gatyantarasambhave'nāśrayaṇāt | tatsiddhaṃ
nā'vyabhicāraniścayo jñānasya svā'rthaniścayanimittaṃ, tasyotpattau tada
pekṣatvāt | yatsvarūpasyotpattau yadapekṣyate na tattasya hetuḥ | yathā'numeyavijñānaṃ
liṅgavijñānasya | na cā'siddhatā hetoḥ | uktaṃ hi—svā'rthaniścayāpekṣaṃ
tadavyabhicāravijñānamiti | kāryātprāgbhāvitvena hi kāraṇatvaṃ vyāptaṃ,
tadvipakṣādviruddhopalabdhyā vyāvartamānaṃ kāraṇatvamapi vyāvartayatīti pratibandha
siddhiḥ | arthaprāpaṇa śaktirapi tasyā'rthatvapradarśanānnā'nyā | na hi jñānamātmīya
haste vastūpanayati, api tu tatra pravartayati jñātāram | na cāsya tatra pravarttana
manyadato vastutattvopadarśanāt | na ca vastutattvamarthasyā'nyadataḥ svarūpāt |
taccāsya rūpamanubhavaḥ svabhāvata evā'nubhavati, na punaravyabhicārādyapekṣate |
tadidamanubhavasyā'rthe svataḥprāmāṇyamucyate | yatpunaranubhūyamānādrūpādarthasyā'
nyathātvaṃ na tadanubhavatyanubhavaḥ | rajatavibhrameṇa tadanyathātvagrahaṇe rajatārthi
nastatrā'pravṛtterbādhakā'nupapatteśca | tataśca jñānāntarā'vaseyamanyathātvaṃ, tena
tatrā'nyadapekṣata iti tadasya parata ityucyate | yatpunarasya tattvā'babodharūpatva
vijñānasya tadapi tadgrāhiṇaḥ pratyayāntarānmānasādānumānikādvā'vagamyamānaṃ
svata ityucyate | tatsvarūpagrāhiṇaḥ pratyayātpratyayāntarā'napekṣaṇāt | na cāsya
bādhakāśaṅkāyāmāprāmāṇyamāśaṅkanīyam | bādhā'narhe tadāśaṅkā'nupapatteḥ | santi
hi kānicidvijñānāni svasthendriyasvāntasya puṃsaḥ sphītā''lokamadhyavarti
prameyaviṣayāṇi, yatra na manāgapi bādhā dṛṣṭacarī kathaṃ tatra bādhārhatā, yatrāpi
nipuṇena pratipatrā bhavitavyam | sa yadi prayatnenā'nusarannapi nā''pādayatyasya
bādhāṃ tadautsargikamasya prāmāṇyamavihatamanapekṣamavatiṣṭhate | yadi tu paśyetta
tastadapohitamiti nānyatra tadutprekṣitavyam | yathoktam—
[p.119]
liṅgādyābhāsajaṃ loke pratibhānamaniścayaḥ |
sarvataḥ pūrṇavijñānaḥ pūrṇe nyūne na kaścana ||
yadapi kila śvo me bhrātā''ganteti pratibhāyāmakṣaliṅgādyapekṣā'bhāvād
bahiḥ svatantraṃ mano dṛṣṭaṃ, sarvaṃ ca sarvamiti vā sadasatpadārthā dravyādayaḥ
tasmādbodhātmakatvena prāptā buddheḥ pramāṇatā |
arthā'nyathātvahetūtthadoṣajñānādapodyate |
dvāveva ninditau loke niraśaṅkā'tiśaṅkitau ||
evaṃ tricaturajñānajanmano nā'dhikā matiḥ |
arthyate tāvadevaikaṃ svataḥ prāmāṇyamaśnute ||
utprekṣate hi yo mohādajñānamapi bādhakam |
sa sarvavyavahāreṣu saṃśayātmā kṣayaṃ vrajet || iti |
na cedaṃ śāpamātram | uktaṃ hi—na bādhā'narhe tadāśaṅkā yujyate
atipraṅgāditi | bādhakāraṇadoṣābhyāmaprāmāṇyajñānāttadabhāvagrahā'pekṣaprāmā
ṇya niścaye, na tu svata iti cet | na ekahetutvakalpanāyāṃ kāryotpattāvaneka
hetutvakalpanā'nupapatteḥ | bādhakāraṇadoṣagrahaṇasya cā'prāmāṇyaniścayahetubhāva
mātreṇa prāmāṇyopahantṛtvena tadabhāve bodhamātrādeva prāmāṇyopapattau na tada
bhāvo'pekṣaṇīyaḥ | sa ca satyāmāśaṅkāyāṃ, sā ca yādṛśe viṣaye dṛṣṭacarī
tādṛśe eva na sarvatretyāveditam | tatsiddhaṃ svataḥ sarvapramāṇānāṃ prāmāṇyaṃ,
tadanyā'napekṣaṇāt | yadyato'nyatrā'pekṣate tattasya svata eva | tadyathā vahnerda
hanasāmarthyamiti | tadanyā'napekṣatvaṃ ca sādhitamuktena prabandhena | tadanyā'pe
kṣasya hi tatsvabhāvā'nadhīnatve tadāśritatvaviruddhatadanāśritatvaprasaktibādhakaṃ
pramāṇamiti na sandigdho vyatirekaḥ | tadidamuktam—
svataḥ sarvapramāṇānāṃ prāmāṇyamiti gṛhyatām |
na hi svato'satī śaktiḥ karttumanyena śakyate || iti |
tasmānnā'nāśvāsa iti siddham |
samprati naiyāyikā'bhimatayogijñānaviṣayatvaṃ viśvasya nirākaroti—
liṅgādyābhāsajaṃ loke pratibhānamaniścayaḥ |
sarvaḥ saṃpūrṇavijñānaḥ pūrṇe nyūne na kaścana ||
yadapi kila śvo me bhrātā āganteti laukikyāṃ pratibhāyāmakṣa
liṅgādyapekṣā'bhāvād bahiḥ svatantraṃ mano dṛṣṭam | nanu tatra bhrātuḥ svarūpasya
[p.120]
prakṛtipuruṣau pañcaskandhā ityādi vā'bhedena lokapramāṇavaśena manaso viṣaya
ityapi svātantrye bahiḥ sarvajñatvasiddhiḥ | atrocyate—
lokottaraṃ tāvat pratibhānaṃ vivādapadaṃ, laukikaṃ tu priyā'priyagocaraṃ
prāyaḥ sahapravṛttahṛdayaprasādodvegādiliṅgādyābhāsaprabhavamata evā'niścayātmakaṃ
tathā vyapadiśanti pratibhānameva, na tu niścayaḥ | ubhayakoṭisaṃsparśitvā
ttulyatvācca na saṃśayātmakam | pratibhaiva | na pramāṇam | aniścayaphalatvāt |
pramāṇāntarāpekṣaniścayatvāt | evaṃ liṅgādijātīyā'pekṣatvāt tatrāpi
prāgupalabdherbhavatvevamiha tu viśveṣāṃ bhāvānā deśakālā'vasthāviśeṣavipra
kīrṇānāṃ na rūpato'pyupalambhasambhava iti kathamatra manasaḥ svātantryamityata
āha—sarvaṃ ca sarvamiti vā prakārarahitena rūpeṇa vyūḍhaṃ prakārabhedavatā yato
nedānīṃ vyūhe hetuprakārānnaiyāyikaḥ svaparā'bhimatānāha—sadasatpadārthā dravyā
dayaḥ prakṛtipuruṣau pañcaskandhā ityādinā vā bhedena lokapramāṇavaśena manasā
viṣayīkṛtaṃ bhāvanāmayasya sphuṭā'vabhāsasya lokottarasya pratyakṣasya viṣaya
ityapi sarvajñatāsiddhiḥ svātantrye manasaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati | niṣprakāraṃ
saprakāraṃ vā laukikena pramāṇena gṛhītvā bhāvayato bhāvanā'panītā''varaṇaṃ manaḥ
svatantrameva viśvamavagāhate iti |
atrocyate | lokottaraṃ tāvatpratibhānaṃ sattayā vivādapadameveti na tatra
manasaḥ svātantryaṃ svarūpeṇa pratiṣedhanīyam | laukikaṃ tu priyā'priyā'gocaraṃ
na svatantramanojanyaṃ, yataḥ prāyaḥ sahapravṛttahṛdayaprasādodvegādilakṣaṇaliṅgādyā
bhāsaprabhavamata eva liṅgādyābhāsaprabhavatvādeva tadaniścayātmakam | tathā ca
vyapadiśanti evaṃ pratibhānaṃ na tu niścaya iti | ubhayakoṭisaṃsparśasyā
'tulyatvācca na saṃśayātmakam |
nanu yadi na saṃśayo nāpi niścayaḥ tarhi tadvijñānatattvaṃ vācyamityata
āha—pratibhaiva | na hyetadvijñānatulyavadupaplavamānaviruddhakoṭidvayaviṣayo yena
saṃśayātmakaṃ, nāpyekakoṭiviṣayāmapi pramāmavadhārayati yena niścayaḥ syāt |
na caikakoṭiviṣayaṃ na ca prakāśate yena na syāt | tasmātsiddhaṃ pratibhaiveti |
tarhi svayamaniścayātmāpi niścayajananādastu pramāṇaṃ cakṣarādivadityata
āha—na pramāṇam | kasmāt ? aniścayaphalatvāt | nanūpajāte pratibhābhede
yatra tadanantaraṃ niścayo bhavati tatra niścayaphalakaḥ pramāṇabhedaḥ kasmānna
pramāṇam ? ityata āha—pramāṇāntarā'pekṣaniścayatvāt | na tāvatpratibhāyā
evā'napekṣayā eva niścayajanakatvaṃ, sarvatra tathābhāvaprasaṅgāt | liṅgādya
[p.121]
pāratantryam | anyathā tadanapekṣo manomātrasādhano bahirarthasaṃbandhaḥ sarvasya
sarvasmin sarvadā syāditi | adṛṣṭaviśeṣādakṣaliṅgādya napekṣaṃ manaḥ kasyacid
bāhye'pyarthe'sati cakṣurādyārambhavaiyarvyaprasaṅgaḥ | tata evā'dṛṣṭaviśeṣā'bhisaṃskṛ
tāt sarvapratipattisiddhestasyāpi vaiyarthyāt | akaraṇasyaiva karturātmano'dṛṣṭa
viśeṣādeva paricchittikriyāsiddheḥ | na khalyanupāyamadṛṣṭamapyarthaṃ sādhayati
śarīrādyāra mbhāt na tarhi akṣaliṅgādyanapekṣaṃ caibhirmanasaḥ pravṛttiḥ svataḥ |
ata eva yogajāddharmāt sarvārtheṣu na mānasaṃ pratyakṣaṃ, saṃnikarṣopāyatvāt siddheḥ |
pekṣāyāṃ ca taddhetutve kṛtamanayā, liṅgādīnāṃ tannirapekṣāṇāmeva tatra tatra
sāmarthyapratīteriti |
upasaṃharati—evaṃ liṅgādijātīyā'pekṣatvālliṅgādyābhāsā'pekṣatvāt
tatrāpi pratibhāne'pi laukike paratantraṃ manaḥ | anyathā svātatrye talliṅgā
dyābhāsā'napekṣamanomātrasādhano bahirarthasaṃbandho manaso nityasaṃnidhānāt
sarvasya puṃsaḥ sarvasminnarthe sarvadā syāditi sarvaḥ sarvadā sarvadarśī syāt |
adṛṣṭaviśeṣād bāhye manaḥ svatantraṃ kadācitkasyacideveti nā'tiprasaṅgaḥ |
tadāha—adṛṣṭaviśeṣādakṣaliṅgādyanapekṣaṃ manaḥ kasyacitkvacidbāhye'pyarthe
cakṣurādyārambhavaiphalyaprasaṅgaḥ | kasmāt ? tata eva manaso'dṛṣṭaviśeṣā'bhi
saṃskṛtātsarvarūpādipratītisiddheḥ | āsatāṃ cakṣurādayaḥ, tasya manaso'pi
vaiyarthyam | kutaḥ ? akaraṇasyaiva kartturātmano'dṛṣṭaviśeṣādeva rūpādipari
cchittikriyāsiddheḥ |
yadyucyeta, na khalvanupāyamadṛṣṭamapyartha sādhayati | kutaḥ ? śarīrādyā
rambhāt | na tarhyakṣaliṅgādyanapekṣā bahirmanasaḥ pravṛttiḥ | tato'dṛṣṭata
evā'nupāyasyā'sādhanatvādeva yogajāddharmātsarvārtheṣu na mānasaṃ pratyakṣaṃ, yatta
ttārakamityācakṣate svāyambhuvāḥ | kutaḥ ? indriyā'rthasaṃnikarṣopāyatvātta
dbāhyaviṣayapratyakṣatvasiddheḥ |
yadyucyeta nendriyārthasaṃyoga eva, tadupāyaguṇādijñānasyā'pratyakṣatva
prasaṅgāt | nā'pīndriyasamavāya eva | śabdajñānasyaiva pratyakṣatvāpatteḥ | api
tu saṃyuktasamavāyādayo'pi tadupāyāḥ | ata eva sarvā'virodhārthamindriyārtha
saṃnikarṣotpannamityāha smaṃ tatrabhavānakṣapāṣaḥ | tadguṇādigraha iva saṃyuktasama
vāyādihāpi samādhijadharmmasaṃskṛtasya manaso bāhyendriyasya vā tatsaṃyukta
[p.122]
saṃyuktasaṃyogā'nabhyupagamāt | anyathā kuṅyādivyavahitā'rthopalabdhiprasaṅgāt |
anumāne'pi pratyakṣatvā''pātāt | ajātā'tivṛtteṣu tasyāpyayogāt | ata
eva nendriyā'rthayoḥ siddhayoḥ sannikarṣaḥ | atītā'nāgatānāṃ tadāpyasattvād
vyavahitā'rthopalabdhiprasaṅgāt | anumānādiṣu ca pratyakṣatvā''pātāt |
sannikarṣagrahaṇā''narthakyācca | nanvatītā'nāgatānāṃ deśādiviśeṣaṇatvena
jñānājjñānasya triśeṣadeśādyālambanatvāt taiścakṣuvo manaso vā sannikarṣa
sambhavāt | teṣāṃ ca sattvātsannidhānādavyā hatamanutpannā'tivṛttajñānasyendri-
saṃyogādirāśrayiṣyate tattadvyavahitādigrahaṇasiddhyarthamityarthata āha—saṃyukta
saṃyogasya pratyakṣajñānahetoranabhyupagamād bhavadbhiḥ |
na kevalamanabhyupagato'nupapannaścā'tiprasaṅgādityata āha—anyathā
kuṅyādisaṃyogavyavahitā'rthopalabdhiprasaṅgādasmadādīnām | na ca samādhi
saṃskṛtasya manaso locanādervā sāmarthyamevaṃvidhaṃ sambhavatītyuktaṃ bauddhasarvajña
parīkṣāyām | tataḥ sarvasya sarvajñatvaprasaṅgāt asti hi kathaṃcitsarvasya sarveṇa
sambandha | tathā'numāne'pi pratyakṣatvāpātāt | indriyārthasannikarṣotpannatvena
hyanumānāt pratyakṣaṃ vyāvartate | yadā tvanumānamapi tathā tadapi pratyakṣaṃ syāt |
tathā hi | dhūmādi yāvaccakṣurādisaṃyuktaṃ dhūmādiśca vahnyādinā tadanena
prakāreṇā'sti kaścitkathaṃcidindriyasya saṃnikarṣa iti tadutpannamanumānaṃ pratyakṣaṃ
prasajyeta | na ca pratyutpannacakṣurādinirapekṣamatra dhūmādyeva kāraṇamiti yuktam |
saṃnikarṣasyaiva tatra tatra kḷptasāmarthyasya sambhave liṅgādisāmarthyakalpanā'nu
papatteḥ | na caitāvatā sarvajñatā sidhyatītyāha—ajātā'tivṛtteṣu tasyāpya
yogāt | ata eva nendriyā'rthayoḥ sadbhāvo jñānajananaṃ prati parasparā''nukūlye
nā'vasthānaṃ saṃnikarṣaḥ |
ata eveti vibhajate—atītā'nāgatānāṃ tadā vijñānasamaye'sattvādvya
vahitārthopalabdhiprasaṅgāt | anumānādiṣu ca pratyakṣatvā''pātāt | hetvantara
māha—saṃnikarṣagrahaṇā''narthakyaprasaṅgācca | indriyārthotpannamityetāvataiva tayoḥ
sadbhāvasiddheḥ | asata utpādakatvā'yogāt |
codayati—nanvatītā'nāgatānāṃ deśādiviśeṣaṇatvena jñānāt | tadyathā
iyamatītā'nāgatabahurājā jagatīti tajjñānasya virodhideśādyālambanatvātta
syaivā'ṅgulyā nirdeśāt tatra pravṛtteśca deśādibhirviśeṣyairyathāyathaṃ cakṣuṣo
manaso vā'vadhṛtapratyakṣatā na hetubhāvasya, saṃnikarṣasambhavāt | kutaḥ sambhava ?
ityata āha—teṣāṃ ca viśeṣyāṇāṃ deśādīnāṃ ca sattvāt saṃnidhānādavyāhata
manutpannā'tivṛttajñānasyendriyārthasaṃnikarṣajatvā'viśeṣeṇā'navabhāso'pi viśeṣya
[p.123]
yā'rthasannikarṣajatvam | anumānādilakṣaṇā bhāve ca indriyā'rthasannikarṣotpannaṃ
jñānamiti nā'numānādiṣu tattvam | yadi tāvadyaḥ kaścidarthastataḥ smṛtau
pratyakṣatvaprasaṅgaḥ | atha prameyo deśādiprajñānaṃ bhavatu pratyakṣaṃ, tanmātrasannikarṣāt
na tu viśeṣaṇajñānam | viśiṣṭajñānaṃ pratyakṣamiti tadākṣepāt viśeṣaṇamapi
pratyakṣamiti cet | na tasyā'sannikarṣāt | na hyasati viśeṣaṇe viśiṣṭo
vartamāno'pi bhavati | sarvasya tathātvaprasaṅgāt | kathaṃ tarhi ativṛttāyāṃ
mātari devadattā putro yajñadatta ucyate | kimatra ? na sa tayā tadā viśiṣṭa
tayā vyavahāramanupatediti | yadi tarhyavarttamānaṃ na vyavahārāṅgaṃ devadattā'pi
syaivārthatvāditi bhāvaḥ | nanvevamapi parvatāderindriyasaṃnikṛṣṭasyā'numā
naviṣayatvādanumānasyāpi pratyakṣatvāpāta ityata āha—pṛthaganumānādilakṣaṇa
praṇayanasāmarthyādanumānādilakṣaṇābhāve indriyā'rthasaṃnikarṣotpannamiti pratyakṣa
lakṣaṇamiti nā'numānādiṣu tattvaṃ pratyakṣatvam | tallakṣaṇavirahāt |
tadetadvikalpapya dūṣayati—yadi tāvadyaḥ kaścidarthaḥ tena sahendriyā'saṃni
karṣādyadvijñāna tatpratyakṣaṃ, tataḥ smṛtau pratyakṣatvaprasaṅgaḥ | sāpi khalūdbuddha
saṃskāreṇā''tmanā saha manasaḥ saṃnikarṣādupajāyate | na caitasyāḥ pāramarṣa
pṛthagasti lakṣaṇam | atha prameyaḥ sa eva khalu arthyamānatayā'rtho, na cā''tmā
smṛtāvarthyamānaḥ, ki tu pūrvā'nubhūto deśādiriti dūṣayati—deśādimātrajñānaṃ
bhavatu pratyakṣaṃ, tanmātrasannikarṣāt, na tu viśeṣaṇajñānam |
śaṅkate—viśiṣṭajñānaṃ pratyakṣamiti tadākṣepādviśeṣaṇamapi pratyakṣamiti
cet ? nirākaroti—na | tasyā'vidheyasya viśeṣaṇavadasaṃnikarṣāt | kasmātta
rhyasaṃnihitaviśeṣaṇo'tītaviśeṣaṇo viśiṣṭāvarttamāno bhavati rūpamātrasya
varttamānatve'pi | kutaḥ ? sarvasya tathātvaprasaṅgāt | asaṃnidhānā'viśeṣāt |
codayati—deśādyavartamānaviśeṣaṇe viśiṣṭo varttamānaḥ, kathaṃ tarhyativṛttāyāṃ
mātari devadattāputro yajñadatta ityucyate ? samadhātuṃ nigūḍhā'bhisandhiḥ pṛcchati
kimatra kathaṃ ? codaka āha—na sa tayā tadā viśiṣṭa iti na devadattāputra iti
hi viśiṣṭatayā vyavahāramanuvarteta | avartamānasya vyavahārā'naṅgatvāditi |
samādhātā''ha—yadi tarhyavarttamānaṃ na vyavahārā'ṅgaṃ devadattā'pyatītayo
[p.124]
viśeṣaṇatayā na vyavahārā'ṅgaṃ syāt | atha bhūtapūrveṇa viśeṣaṇena vyavahāraḥ,
viśiṣṭatayāpi tādṛśyā na daṇḍavāritaḥ | na cedṛśaṃ viśeṣaṇamupalakṣaṇaṃ tu,
rūpato bhinnasyā'sannidhānāt | sannidhyasannidhikṛta eva caitayorbhedaḥ | tasmā
nnā'sanihitaviśeṣaṇo viśiṣṭaḥ sannihitaḥ | yadi viśeṣaṇaṃ prameyaṃ jātādyarthaśca
prameyaḥ | anarthasannikarṣajamapi pratyakṣaṃ prāptam | na ca tathā | tallakṣaṇavirahā
dindriyasyā'rthāpekṣatvāt |
atha deśaviśeṣa eva prameyo na tadviśeṣaṇaṃ tathā | na ca tallakṣaṇavirahaḥ |
arthyamānasaṃnikarṣāt | na tarhi deśādyālambanajñānenā'nutpannādivedino
dhyāyinaḥ | prameyaikadeśasannikarṣācca pratyakṣajñānodaye sarvaḥ sarvapratyakṣadarśī
bhayasiddhā na tadvyavahārāṅgaṃ syāt | samādhātaivā''śaṅkya tulyatāmāpā
dayati—atha bhūtapūrveṇa viśeṣaṇena vyavahāro na viśiṣṭatayā'pi tādṛśyā
daṇḍena vāritaḥ | api cā'bhyupetya viśeṣaṇatvamevaṃvidhasyedamuditaṃ, paramārthatastu
nedaṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ devadattādikaṃ nāmetyata āha—upalakṣaṇaṃ tu, rūpato bhinnasya
svalakṣaṇasyā''kāriṇa iti yāvat | asaṃnidhānādviśeṣyasyā'bhimatavastva
nākāratvāditi | atha mā bhūt saṃnidhānaṃ, tathāpi viśeṣaṇaṃ kasmānna bhavatī ?
tyata āha—saṃnidhyasaṃnidhikṛta eva etayorviśeṣaṇopalakṣaṇayoryathākramaṃ bhedaḥ |
kasmāt ? nā'saṃnihitaviśeṣaṇo viśaṣṭaḥ saṃnihitaḥ | svarūpeṇa saṃnihito'pi
tadasaṃnihitaviśeṣaṇasya vastunaḥ svarūpaṃ saṃnihitasya tanmātraṃ pratyakṣaṃ na
punarviśiṣṭatvamapīti siddham |
yadi ca viśiṣṭaprameyatāsiddhyarthaṃ viśeṣaṇamapi prameyamaṅgīkriyeta tadanta
reṇa viśiṣṭaprameyatā'nupapatteḥ | tato jātādyarthaśca prameya iti anarthasaṃnikarṣaja
mapi jñānaṃ pratyakṣaṃ prāptam | viśeṣaṇasyā'rthyamānasya tatra saṃnikarṣā'bhāvāt |
na ca tathā sambhavati | kutaḥ ? pratyakṣalakṣaṇavirahāt | indriyārthasaṃnikarṣo
tpannamiti hi tallakṣaṇam | atha mā bhūt lakṣaṇaṃ viśeṣyendriyasaṃnikarṣamātrā
ttajjanniṣyate, lapsyate ca pratyakṣaṃ vaiśadyādityata āha—indriyasyā'rthā'pekṣa
tvāditi tattvajñānotpattau yattadavocāma tattvavijñānajananamindriyāṇāmartha
sahakāritayā niyatamiti |
tadetatsamutthānaṃ śaṅkate—atha teṣu deśaviśeṣa eva prameyo na tadviśeṣaṇam
atītādi | tathā ca na pratyakṣalakṣaṇaviraho'rthyamānendriyasaṃnikarṣāt | uttaraṃ—
na tarhi deśādyālambanena jñānenā'nutpannādivedino dhyāyinaḥ | yadi manyeta
prameyaikadeśasaṃnikarṣādeva dhyāyino dhyānasaṃskṛtasvāntavṛttayo yuñjānā viśvameva
[p.125]
syāt | sarvatyendriyasannikṛṣṭaṃ prati kathaṃcidviśeṣaṇatvopapatteḥ | anumāna
lakṣaṇā'bhāve'pi pratyakṣavidyamānā'rthasmṛtau pratyakṣaprasaṅgaḥ | saṃskārastatra
kāraṇaṃ nā'rthasattā | asatyapyarthe bhāvāt | itaratrāpi tulyam | yogajadharmmasya
hetutvāt | na hi tatrā'rthopayoge pramāṇam | sannihite'saṃnihite, vyavahite
'vyavahite, vartamāne'vartamāne ca sarvatra yogipratyakṣotpādā'bhyupagamāt |
vartamānadeśādyālambanatvaṃ liṅgādyabhāve iti vadatā saṃplavo nirastaḥ syāt |
utsūtraṃ ca kalpitamanumānāditi bādhanāsūtraṃ kalpitam | anumānādibādhanā-
varttamānā'varttamānaṃ kathaṃcidindriyasaṃnikṛṣṭaṃ prameyaikadeśaviśeṣaṇatayā sākṣā
tkariṣyantīti, tatrāha—prameyaikadeśasaṃkirṣāttu kathaṃcidviśeṣaṇībhū tāvarttamāna
viśvaviṣayapratyakṣajñānodaye sarvaḥ sarvapratyakṣadarśī syātsarvasyendriyasannikṛṣṭamarthaṃ
prati kathaṃcidviśeṣaṇatvopapatteḥ | uktaṃ hi—nā'nyatra yogaḥ prabhavatīti |
anumānalakṣaṇā'bhāve ca anumānalakṣaṇā'navaruddhe viṣaye pratyakṣalakṣaṇaṃ vyavasthā
pyamāne pratyakṣe kasmiścit sadṛśe gavayādau dṛṣṭe saṃskārabodhātsadṛśāntarasya
gavāderarthasya vidyamānasya smṛtau pratyakṣaprasaṅgaḥ |
sambhavati hi gavāderapi hi vidyamānasya kathaṃcit pratyakṣa gavayādi
viśeṣaṇatvamiti śaṅkate—saṃskārastatra smṛtau kāraṇaṃ nā'rthasuttā | kutaḥ ?
asatyapyarthe sati saṃskāre smṛterbhāvāt | uttaram—itaratrāpi tulyam | kutaḥ ?
arthasattānirapekṣasya yogajadharmasya hetutvāt | taddarśayati—na hi tatra yogināṃ
sarvaviṣayapratyakṣajanmani samastārthopayoge pramāṇam | kasmāt ? saṃnihite
'saṃnihite, vyavahite'vyavahite, varttamāne'varttamāne ca sarvaprakāre yogipratya
kṣotpādā'bhyupagamāt śāstre |
yadyucyate atītādiviśiṣṭasaṃnihitavarttamānadeśādyālambane tatrā'to tā
digocaramiti, tatrāha—varttamānadeśādyālambanatvaṃ yogijñānasyātītādi
viśiṣṭā'rthagrāhiṇaḥ pratyuktamadhastāt | api ca pramāṇata iti sarvavacanopa
saṃgrahārtha tasiṃ prayuñjānena tadartha copariṣṭāttu viśadayatā tatra bhavatā pakṣila
svāminā pramāṇena pramāṇābhyāṃ pramāṇaiścaikasyaiva prameyasyā''tmādeḥ pramitiriti
pramāṇasaṃplavo darśitaḥ, tadvyākopaśca prasajyetetyata āha—liṅgādyabhāve
pratyakṣamiti vadatā saṃplavaḥ pramāṇānāṃ nirastaḥ syāt | utsūtraṃ ca kalpitam |
na khalu liṅgādyabhāva iti uddeśo lakṣaṇaṃ parīkṣāsūtraṃ vā samasti |
[p.126]
nnotsūtramiti cet | śabdanivṛttyarthamavyapadeśyapadavyākhyānamayuktam | ṣaḍvi
dhasannikarṣābhyupagama indriyagrahaṇaṃ cā'numānādinivṛsyarthamityādi parāhataṃ
syāt | tasmādakṣaliṅgādiparatantraṃ manaḥ | sarva ca paripūrṇaṃ cellaukikapramāṇa
gocaro laukikapramāṇa vyavahāryyapi paripūrṇajñāna iti na lokottarajñānā
'bhimatasya viśeṣaḥ | spaṣṭā'vabhāsaṃ jñānaṃ yadi, na tatra tasyopayogaḥ | atha
niścayaḥ, sa itarasyāpi pramāṇatulyaḥ | pramiteḥ pratyakṣadarśinaḥ sarvārtheṣu
śaṅkate—anumānādilakṣaṇapraṇayanavādhanānnotsūtramiti cet ? sūtrā'
'hitā'rthasāmarthyalabhyamapi tadartha eva, tanmūlatvādityarthaḥ | nirākaroti—
śabdanivṛttyarthamavyapadeśyapadavyākhyānamayuktam | bhāṣyakāro hi śābdajñāna
nivṛttyartham avyapadeśyapadaṃ varṇayāṃvabhūva | tathā hi | yatra pratyakṣeṇa dṛśyamāne
kesarādimati vastuni nāgarikeṇā''raṇyakaṃ pratyucyate turaṅgo'yamiti tatrā'
'raṇyakastasya vastunasturaṅgamapadavācyatāmavaiti, mānadvayavyāpārāt | tathā
hi | turaṅgamapadā'bhidheya ityetāvatā śabdaḥ purovartini ca viśeṣe tadabhidheya
tāśraye indriyatadupadeśasya tatra śabdasya pravṛtteḥ | taccaitadubhayajamapi jñānaṃ śābda
meva, śabdasya tatra sādhakatamatvāt | satyapīndriyā'rthasaṃnikarṣe śabdā'bhāve
tadanupapatteḥ | asati tu tasminyadā sadeva vastvanubhūtamāraṇyakenā'nusmarya
saṅketaṃ pratipadyate sa kesarādimān padārthasturaṅgama iti tadā vākye sati
tadavabodhāt | tasmādubhayajasya vijñānasya śabdasya nivṛttyarthamavyapadeśya
padavyākhyātamayuktaṃ syāt | liṅgādyabhāve pratyakṣalakṣaṇavyavasthāpanāt, ubhaya
jasya śābdatvena pratyakṣatvaprasaktyabhāvāt |
etaduktaṃ bhavati | tadeva sūtrā'bhihitasāmarthyena labhyate na yatsūtrā'vaya
vamupahanti na tūpaghātakamapīti, tathā deśakālaviprakīrṇamātraviṣayapratyakṣa
jñānā'bhyupagamo liṅgādyabhāve pratyakṣalakṣaṇam, indriyagrahaṇaṃ cā'numāna
nivṛttyarthamityādi parāhataṃ syāt | tadviṣayaṃ pratyakṣamityādiśabdasūcitaṃ parāhataṃ
veditavyam | prakṛtamupasaṃharati—tasmādakṣaliṅgādiparatantraṃ bahirmanaḥ | api
ca sarvaṃ paripūrṇa laukikaṃ pramāṇagocaro nyūnaṃ vā ? tatra sarva ca paripūrṇaṃ
cellaukikapramāṇavyavahārye'pi paripūrṇajñāna eveti na tato lokottarajñānā'bhi
matasya viśeṣaḥ |
śaṅkate—spaṣṭā'vabhāsaṃ jñānaṃ yadi viśeṣaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati | pūrṇa
viṣayamapi laukikamaviśadābhamalaukikaṃ tu bhāvanā''dheyaviśadābhamityasyā
stato viśeṣa iti | nirākaroti—na | tatra sarvasmin sampūrṇagṛhīte na
[p.127]
nairākāṅkṣyaṃ netarasya | apratyakṣamapi yadi paripūrṇaṃ mānāntareṇa mitaṃ kimanya
dākāṅkṣediti | na hi pramāṇāntarā''kāṅkṣā, prameyasiddhyarthatvāttadākāṅ
kṣāyāḥ | punaḥ prameyasiddhyarthaṃ pramāṇāntarākāṅkṣā | na | pūrvasmādapyasa
kṛttatsiddheḥ | siddhasya punaḥ siddhyapekṣāhetvabhāvācca | upāyāntarasadbhāvaśco
pāyāntarākāṅkṣaiva syāt | prīti viśeṣaścetpūrvapramāṇajāddarśanātsiddhyatīti
spaṣṭā'vabhāsasyopayogaḥ | laukikapramāṇā'nvitamarthamavagamayatā vispaṣṭaṃ tena
piṣṭaṃ piṣṭaṃ syāt |
śaṅkate—atha niścayaḥ ? nirākaroti—sa itarasyāpi laukikasya
pramāṇatvena tulyaḥ | śaṅkate—pramiteḥ sarvasyā eva pratyakṣaparatvāt sarvārtheṣu
nairākāṅkṣyaṃ netarasya pramāṇāntareṇa samastavastudarśino laukikasyā'pratyakṣe
sarvasminnairākāṅkṣyam | tathā hi pramātā āgamādarthamavagamya anumānāt
bubhutsate anumāya ca taṃ pratyakṣeṇa didṛkṣate | atha sākṣātkṛtya nirākāṅkṣatā
māpadyate | seyaṃ sarvaiva pratyakṣapramitiḥ pramātṝṇām | nirākaroti—apratyakṣamapi
yadi mānāntareṇa paripūrṇamitaṃ kimanyadākāṅkṣediti | yatra khalvanumāyā'pi
pratyakṣeṇa didṛkṣate na nūnaṃ tatrā'numānena sampūrṇaprameyā'dhigatirabhūt tadbhāve
didṛkṣā'nupapatteḥ | yathāhuratrabhavanto vārttikakāramiśrāḥ |
yāvadajñātasandigdhaṃ meyaṃ tāvatpramitsyate |
pramite tu pramātṝṇāṃ pramotsukyaṃ vihanyate || iti |
atha mānāntaramākāṅkṣati tatrāha—na hi pramāṇāntarā''kāṅkṣā |
kutaḥ ? prameyasiddhyarthatvāttadākāṅkṣāyāḥ | hitā'hitaprāptiparihārārthino
hi tadupāyamarthayante, sa ca pramāṇā'dhīnanirūpaṇa iti pramāṇamanusaranti, sa cet
kutaścitpramāṇātsiddhaḥ kṛtaṃ tarhi pramāṇāntarānusaraṇena teṣām | tathāpi
cedanusaranti nūnaṃ prameyāntaramapyamī bubhutsante ityarthaḥ | atha tataḥ prameya
siddhāvapi tasyā''śutaravināśitvena punastatprameyasiddhyarthaṃ pramāṇāntarā
kāṅkṣāṃ nirākaroti—na | pūrvasmādapyasakṛttatsiddheranekajanmaparamparā''yāsa
sādhyayogajavijñānagocaraprayatnā'nupapatteḥ | kutaścitsiddhasya punaḥ pramāṇānta
rātsiddhyapekṣāhetvabhāvācca | tadanubhavabhāvitabhāvanodbodhavaśādetadviṣayasmṛ
tisantānasambhavāt |
śaṅkate—vicitrā'bhisandhitvāt pramātṛṇāmupāyāntarasadbhāvastasmi
nprameye viśeṣaṇatayā bubhutsite cet | nirākaroti—tarhi prāptā'prāpta
vivekena upāyāntarā''kāṅkṣaiva syāt na prameyāntarā''kāṅkṣā | śaṅkate—
[p.128]
vyarthaṃ pramāṇāntaram | upāyāntarasadbhāvācca punarjijñāsamānaṃ dṛṣṭamapītarai
rjijñāseta, viśeṣaniścayātmakaṃ pratyakṣamaniścayātmakamanumānāditi vyabhi
cārāt | apramitatvādeva tarhyākāṅkṣā | vyabhicāriṇo'niścayātmakasyā'
pramāṇatvāt | bhānādiyogyaṃ pratyakṣaṃ sannikarṣānnā'numānādi | sannikarṣasādhanaṃ
tarhi gamanādyapekṣatāṃ na tu pramāsādhanaṃ, siddhatvātpramāyāḥ pramite ca sannikarṣasya
bhānādihetutvāt | prakṛtaṃ ca pratyakṣanasannikṛṣṭagocaramiti na nairākāṅkṣyā
yā'lamityaviśeṣa eva laukikalokottarayoḥ pramātroḥ | anumānādeḥ sāmānya
viṣayatvātpratyarthaniyatātmaviśeṣā'navadhāraṇāt sākāṅkṣatvaṃ laukikasya
prītiviśeṣaścet | yathā hi śvaśrūrekena gavākṣeṇa vīkṣya jāmātaramapareṇa
vīkṣate prītiviśeṣāt tathedamapītyarthaḥ | nirākaroti—sukare pūrvapramāṇa
jādapi punarddarśanātsidhyatīti vyarthaṃ pramāṇāntaram | atyantaduṣkaramapi
copāyāntarasadbhāvācca punarjijñāsamānaṃ pratyakṣadṛṣṭamapītarairanumānādibhirayaṃ
vicitrā'bhisandhiḥ pratipattā jijñāseteti na pratyakṣaparā sarvapramitiḥ |
pratyakṣaparatvamicchatā vācyo viśeṣaḥ pratyakṣasya mānāntarebhyaḥ | athā'ya
mucyate niścayātmakaṃ pratyakṣaṃ viśadatvādaniścayātmakamanumānādi vyabhicāra
darśanāt | tadidamanupapannam | anumānāderapi yathoktalakṣaṇopapannasya vyabhi
cārā'bhāvāt | ābhāsavyabhicāra stu pratyakṣe'pi tulya ityanabhidhāya
sthūlanirākarāṇāntaramāha —apramitatvādeva tarhyākāṅkṣā | kutaḥ ? vyabhi
cāriṇo'ta evā'niścayātmakasyā'pramāṇatvāt | śaṅkate—bhānādiyogyaṃ
pratyakṣam | kutaḥ ? sannikarṣādviṣayasya sannihitatvāt asannidhāne tadanu
papatteḥ | na punarevamanumānādi | tasya deśakālaviprakīrṇavastugocaratvāt |
nirākaroti—sannikarṣasādhanaṃ tarhi gamanādyapekṣatāṃ na tu pramāsādhanam
pramāṇam | kutaḥ ? anumānādereva siddhatvātpramāyāḥ | na ca pramaiva
bhānādiheturityāha—sannikarṣasya pramāṇa mātrasahāyasya bhānādihetutvāt |
api ca prakṛtaṃ pratyakṣaṃ deśakālaviprakīrṇavastumātraviṣayamasannikṛṣṭavastugocara
miti na nairākāṅkṣyāyā'lamiti | tasmādaviśeṣo laukikalokottarayoḥ
pramātroḥ sarvapramāyām |
punaḥ śaṅkate—gṛhītasambandhaliṅgādiprabhavatayā viśeṣe ca sambandha
saṃvedanā'nupapatteḥ anumānādeḥ sāmānyaviṣayatvātpratyarthaniyatā'nantaviśeṣā'
[p.129]
pramātuḥ | laukikottarasyā'pi kālarūpā'vadhāraṇānnairākāṅkṣyamiti
nā'viśeṣaḥ | yadi tarhi taukikaṃ pramāṇaṃ vikalavastuviṣayaḥ lokottaramapi
bhāvanāmayaṃ tatparatantraṃ na tadgocarayitumarhati | svātantryaṃ tu bahirmmanaso
netyāveditameva | tasmātsarvaṃ manaso viṣayā iti pralāpaḥ | api ca—
mithyaiva vartamānābhaṃ na tu pratyakṣamanyathā |
kramā'kramau na kalpete na prameyaṃ nirūpyate ||
kālatrayaparivṛttyarthagocarāṇi mānāntarāṇi vartamānaviṣayaṃ pratyakṣaṃ tatra
vartamānātmanā paśyato mithyājñānaṃ, sarvasyā'tathābhāvāt | yathārthaṃ tu paśyato
na pratyakṣam | atītā'nāgataviṣayatvā'bhāvāt tasya | atha vā'vaśyamanena
bahuvidhasya pratyakṣasyaikaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ vācyam | anyathā pramāṇāntaratvā'bhidhānama-
navadhāraṇāt sākāṅtvaṃ laukikasya pramātuḥ | lokottarasya tvavikala
rūpasarvā'vadhāraṇādapramitasya vastuno 'bhāvānnairākāṅghyamiti nā'viśeṣaḥ |
dūṣayati—yadi tarhi laukikaṃ pramāṇaṃ nā'vikalavastuviṣayaṃ, lokottaramapi
bhāvanāmayaṃ bhavānayā kṛtaṃ tatparatantram, bhāvanā ca vikalaviṣayalaukika
pramāṇaparatantreti na tadavikalaṃ gocarayitumarhati | na ca bhāvanā''hitasaṃskāraṃ
mano bahiḥ svatantraṃ pravartata ityāha—svātantryaṃ tu bahirmanaso netyāveditameva |
etacca bauddhasarvajñaparīkṣāyāṃ nipuṇataramupapāditam | prakṛtamupasaṃharati—
tasmātsarvaṃ manaso viṣaya iti pralāpaḥ | api ca |
mithyaiva vartamānābhaṃ na tu pratyakṣamanyathā |
kramā'kramau na kalpete na prameyaṃ nirūpyate ||
yathā lokapratītikālatrayaparivṛttyarthaviṣayāṇi mānāntarāṇyanumānā
dīni vartamānaviṣayaṃ pratyakṣaṃ tatra sarvajñasya pratyakṣeṇa sarvaṃ vartamānātmanā
paśyato mithyājñānam | kutaḥ ? sarvasyā'tītā'nāgatavarttamānasyā'tathā
bhāvāt vartamānatvā'bhāvāt | yathārthaṃ tu paśyato'tītāditvena paśyato na
tadviṣayaṃ jñānaṃ pratyakṣaṃ syāt | atītā'nāgataviṣayatvā'bhāvāt tasya pratyakṣasya |
pratyakṣatā hi vartamānaviṣayatayā vyāptā | vartamānaviṣayatā cātītā'nāgata
viṣayādvijñānānnivartamānā pratyakṣatvamapi nivartayatītyarthaḥ |
na tu pratyakṣamanyathetyasyā'nyā vyākhyā | atha vā avaśyamanena
bahuvidhasya pratyakṣasyaikaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ vaktavyam anyathā yadyekaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ nocyeta tasya
pratyakṣasyā'parigṛhītasyaikasyā'numānādibhyaḥ pramāṇāntaratvā'bhidhānamapratyaya
kāraṇakaṃ syāt | yadi punarasālakṣaṇye'pi pratyakṣamekaṃ pramāṇamabhyupeyeta tataḥ
[p.130]
pratyayaṃ pramāṇāntarā'bhyupagamo vā paribhāṣāprayojanā'bhāvāt | bhinnalakṣa
ṇeṣvapi keṣucillaukikāḥ pratyakṣaśabdaṃ prayuñjate iti mithyālokottare
tadayogāt | tatra nā'kṣajatvaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ manaso'tivyāpteḥ | tasmādindriyārtha
sannikṛṣṭā'rthajatvaṃ tallakṣaṇam | yathoktam | arthasāmarthyena samudbhavādindri
yā'rthasannikarṣotpannaṃ satsaṃprayoga iti | manaso'kṣatve sukhādijñānavyāpteḥ
arthasāmarthyajatvena anumānādivyavacchedāt | na ca sūkṣmaviprakṛṣṭavyavahitānāṃ
jñānotpattau sāmarthyamiti na sarvaviṣayapratyakṣasyopapattiḥ | kalpanā'poḍhatvaṃ
pramāṇāntarā'bhyupagamo vā pratyayaḥ syāt | bhinnalakṣaṇānāmapi pratyakṣatve
'numānādīnāmapi pratyakṣatvaprasaṅgāt na mānāntarāṇi tānyabhyupeyerannityarthaḥ |
yaducyeta bhinnatvā'viśeṣe'pi dravyaguṇakarmmaṇāmevā'rthaśabdā'bhidheyatā pari
bhāṣitā na sāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyānām | evaṃ kāsucideva jñānavyaktiṣu
pratyakṣaśabdaḥ paribhāṣito nā'numānādivyaktiṣvityata āha—paribhāṣā
prayojanā'bhāvāt | aprayojanasya paribhāṣaṇe'tiprasaṅgādityarthaḥ | bhinnala
kṣaṇeṣvapi keṣucillaukikā vācakameva pratyakṣaśabdaṃ prayujjate | na khalvete
paryanuyojyāḥ | yathā jātitraye akṣaśabdamekamityapi mithyā | kutaḥ ?
lokottare bhavadabhimate lokottaratvādeva tasya laukikaprayogasyā'yogātaṃ |
tadavasthitaṃ pratyakṣalakṣaṇamekaṃ vaktavyamiti |
nanūcyate akṣajatvaṃ nāma pratyakṣāṇāmekaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ, tathā ca sarvajñajñānaṃ
mano'bhidhānā'kṣaprabhavamiti nā'tivartate pratyakṣatāmityata āha—tatra nā'kṣa
jatvaṃ lakṣaṇam | kutaḥ ? tathā hi—manonāmā'kṣaṃ na vā | tatra manaso'kṣatve
'numānādyapi manojanmaiveti tasyāpi pratyakṣatvaṃ prasaktamityativyāpterna
lakṣaṇam | anakṣatve vā sukhādijñānasya bāhyendriyā'janitasyā'pratyakṣatva
prasakteravyāpteḥ | tasmādindriyā'rthasāmarthyajatvameva tatpratyakṣalakṣaṇamāstheyaṃ
sarvaireva vādibhiḥ ||
atraiva sarveṣāmanumati darśayati—yathoktamarthasya sāmarthyena samudbhavāt |
andhādīnāmanupapatterindriyasyetyapi śeṣaḥ | tathendriyārthasannikarṣotpannaṃ tathā
satsamprayoga iti | evaṃ ca sati nā'vyāptiḥ | manaso'kṣatve sati sukhādi
jñānavyāpternā'tivyāptiḥ | arthasāmarthyajatvenā'numānādivyavacchedāt | nanve
vamapi sarvaviṣayaṃ vijñānaṃ kasmānna pratyakṣam ? ityata āha—na ca sūkṣmavi
prakṛṣṭavyavahitānāmarthānāṃ vijñānotpattau sāmarthyamiti na sarvaviṣayapratyakṣato
papattiḥ |
[p.131]
pratyakṣalakṣaṇamiti cet | keyaṃ kalpanā ? yadi śabdābhāsā pratītiḥ, śrautraṃ
jñānamapratyakṣam athā'bhilāpasaṃsargayogyapratibhāsā |
śaṅkate—kalpanāpoḍhatvaṃ pratyakṣalakṣaṇamiti cet | idaṃ khalvanumānā
dibhyo vikalparūpebhyaḥ pratyakṣatāṃ nivarttayati vyavasyāpayati ca sukhādiṣu
jñātarūpatayā svasaṃvedanasiddheṣvapoḍheṣu kalpanāyāḥ pratyakṣatvaṃ, saṅgṛhṇāti ca
sarvajñavijñānamakhilaṃ kalpanānirmuktamityarthaḥ |
tadetadyathāsambhavaṃ vikalpya dūṣayati—keyaṃ kalpanā yasyā apoḍhatva
mucyate pratyakṣatvaṃ ? yadi śabdā''bhāsā śabdā''kārapratītiḥ śrautraṃ jñānaṃ
śabdaviṣayaṃ viśadābhamapratyakṣaṃ prasajyeta | athā'bhilāpasaṃsargayogyapratibhāsā
pratītiḥ kalpanā |
ayamarthaḥ | abhilāpasaṃsargayogyaḥ pratibhāsa ākāro yasyāḥ pratīteḥ
sā kalpanā | etaduktaṃ bhavati | sa evā'bhilāpasaṃsṛṣṭo bhavati yatrā'yama
bhilāpaḥ kṛtasaṅketaḥ | sa eva ca tathā yo deśakālā'vasthā'nugataḥ prati
bhāsate | na ca vastusataḥ svalakṣaṇasyā'nugatiḥ sambhāvinī | na ca jātyā
dayo vastusantaḥ sambhavanti | vicārā'sahatvāt | ata eva tadvantopi bhedā
na tāttvikāḥ | tadabhāve tadvattā'nupapatteḥ | tasmāt svā''bhāsaṃ vā alīkaṃ
vā bāhyaṃ paśyanti yādṛśam eko govikalpaḥ tādṛśameva govikalpāntaramapi |
na ca sva viṣayavikalpāntaraviṣayādbhedastenaiva gṛhyate nāpi vikalpāntareṇeti
bhedā'grahādeko vikalpaviṣayaḥ | tadekatvādvikalpānāmapyekatvam | tadekavikalpa
nirmāṇasāmarthyāccā'nubhavānāmapyekatvam | tadekatrā'taddhetūnāṃ vyaktīnāpyekatā
yathā''ha—
ekapratyavamarśasya hetutvāddhīrabhedinī |
ekadhīhetubhādena vyaktīnāpyabhinnatā || iti |
taccā ''ropyamāṇaṃ rūpamanyavyāvṛttisvabhāvaṃ bhāvā'bhāvasādhāraṇa
tvāccā'tyantavisadṛśānāṃ sādṛśyā''pādākatvāccā'bāhyasādṛśyācca | tathā
hi | yadbhāvā'bhāvasādhāraṇaṃ rūpaṃ tadanyavyāvṛttiniṣṭhameva yathā mūrttatvādi |
tathā ca gavādi vikalpaviṣaya iti svabhāvahetuḥ | sādhāraṇaṃ khalvetadgotvādi
bhāvā'bhāvayoḥ, asti gaurnāsti gauriti vikalpāspadatvāt | asādhāraṇye
tadanupapatteḥ virodhapaunaruktyābhyām | tathā yadatyantavisadṛśānāṃ sādṛśya
[p.132]
sāmānyākārasvasaṃvedanepyapratyakṣatvam | atha vastuni vastvantarayojanā,
sāmānyā''kārapratīteḥ pratyakṣatvam | sāmānyasya vastvantarasyā'nabhyupagamāt |
māpādayati tadanyavyāvṛttiniṣṭhameva, yathā varāhamahiṣamātaṅgādīnāmatyanta
visadṛśānāmakramelakatvam | tathā ca atyantavisadṛśānāṃ śābaleyādisvasva
lakṣaṇānāṃ gotvamiti svabhāvo hetuḥ | tathā jñānapratibhāsasya vā alokasya
vā bāhyena vā paramārthato vā'tyantā'sadrūpeṇa sādṛśyamanyavyāvṛttikṛtameva
kalpate nā'nyathā | ata eva jñānā''bhāso vā'līkaṃ vā gotvaṃ prati vikalpaṃ
vidyamānaṃ sadṛśatayā'nākalitabhedamapyaśvādi vikalpaviṣayavyāvṛttameva
bāhyatayā''ropyata iti nā'nyavyāvṛttirūpatā'sya na prathate | na cā'nirūpite
nā'gavā tadvyāvṛttirūpagonirūpaṇād gonirūpaṇā'dhīnatvāccā'gonirūpaṇa
syā'nyonyāśrayamiti sāmpratam | anyatarasyā'nyataḥ prasiddheḥ | gorūpasya
vijñānā''kārasya vā alīkasya vā'nyato vyāvṛttirna punarasya tadadhīnaṃ
nirūpaṇam | anyathā sāmānye'pi pāramārthike doṣasya sāmyāt | tasmāt
jñānākāro vā alīkaṃ vā bāhyatvenā'vasitaṃ saṃketasambhavādbhavati śabda
saṃsargayogyaṃ tadviṣayā vyutpannānāṃ ca pratītiḥ kalpaneti | tajjñānākāravādināṃ
kalpanāṃ dūṣayati—sāmānyākārasvayaṃvedane'pyapratyakṣam |
idamabhisaṃhitam | jñānā'tiriktasya vedanā'nupapatteranyavyāvṛttirūpaṃ
sāmānyaṃ jñānākāra iti nirūpitaṃ sā''kāravādibhiḥ | na ca tadbāhyatvaṃ
vikalpasya grāhyamadhyavaseyaṃ vā sambhavatīti nivedayiṣyate na nirālambanaṃ
jñānamityatrāntare | tatpāriśeṣyāt jñānā''kārasāmānyasya svasavedane'pi
vikalpanīyatayā syādapratyakṣatā prāgeva tādbāhyatāyāstasyā agrāhyāyā
anadhyavaseyāśca sarvathā vikalpagocaratvāditi |
samprati nirākārajñānavādināṃ matakāśaṅkate—atha vastvantarasya raja
tādeḥ purovartini śuktikādau yo jano rajatamidamiti vijñānakalpanāṃ nirā
karoti sā sāmānyā''kārā pratītiḥ sāmānyamākriyate vyajyate yayā
pratipattyā sā pratyakṣaṃ syāt kutaḥ ? sāmānyarūpasya vastvantarasyā'nabhyu
pagamāt | yathāha—
tasyāṃ yadūṣamābhāti bāhyamekamivā'nyataḥ |
vyāvṛttamiva nistattvaṃ parīkṣābhaṅgabhāvataḥ || iti |
[p.133]
athā'bhidhānayojanā, avyutpannā'bhidhānasya tathā syāt |
athā'rūpasyā''ropaḥ kalpanāpoḍhaṃ rūpamātravedanaṃ pratyakṣam | na tarhya
jātā'tivṛtteṣu yogino'nyasya vā jñānaṃ pratyakṣam | jñānapratibhāsino rūpasya
teṣvabhāvāt | bhāve vā pratyutpannatvā''pātāt |
śaṅkate—athā'bhidhānayojanā | abhidheye kalpanāṃ nirākaroti—
avyutpannā'bhidhānasya puṃso yā'bhilāpasaṃsargayogyapratibhāsā pratītiḥ
śabdayojanārahitā sā tathā pratyakṣaṃ syāt | na ceṣyate | śaṅkate—athā'
rūpasyā'līkasyā''ropo bāhyatvena kalpanā | yathoktaṃ bhadantena buddhyā kalpi
tayā viviktamaparairyadrūpamullikhyate tadbuddhirna bahiriti | iyaṃ ca kalpanā
vyutpannānāmavyutpannānāṃ ca sādhāraṇī | tena tadapoḍhaṃ rūpamātravedanaṃ pratyakṣam |
tathā ca noktadoṣaprasaṅga iti | nirākaroti—na tarhyajātā'tivṛtteṣu
yogino'nyasya vā jñānaṃ pratyakṣam | kutaḥ ? jñāne pratibhāsino rūpasya teṣva
jātā'tivṛtteṣvabhāvāt | kasmādabhāva ? ityata āha—bhāve vā pratyutpannatvā'
'pātāt |
ayamabhisandhiḥ | na khalu vikalpānāmalīkaprakāśane'pi samānaviṣayatva
sambhavaḥ | prativikalpamalīkasyā'bhedāt | nanūktaṃ bhede'pyalīkānāṃ yādṛśameko
govikalpaḥ kalpayatyalīkaṃ tādṛśamevā'para iti bhedā'grahādalīkānāṃ bhedā 
'vasāya iti | satyamuktamidaṃ tvālocanīyam | kimalīkātmanāmasti tatti
rohitaṃ svarūpaṃ yadeṣu prathamānepvapi na prathate, nāsti vā ? na tāvadavastusatāṃ
kalpanāmātrā'dhīnamūrtīnāṃ kalpanā'nupadarśitarūpasambhavaḥ | tasyā'kalpi
tatvena vastusato'līkarūpatvā'nupapatteḥ | tasmādaprathamānaṃ nāstīti vedi
tavyam | yathā ca nāstitaiva pāramārthiko teṣām | astitā punaḥ kalpanā
mātramāpatet | nanu bhedāṃ'grahādabhedā'dhyavasāyo vikalpaviṣayāṇāmityanya
evaiṣāṃ viṣaya āśrayaṇīyaḥ | yaḥ śabdaḥ saṃsargayogyaḥ syāt prativikalpaṃ
tvalīkānāṃ bhedātsvalakṣaṇavadaśakyasamayatayā abhilāpasaṃsargayogyatvā'bhāvena
vikalpagocaratvā'nupapatteḥ | na cā'bhedasyāpi sambhavaḥ | na khalu kalpanā
mātrā'dhīnasattvasya tadbhedāt ekatvamupapadyate | taddhi tasyāṃ tadiva tajjanmani
jātamiva tadvināśe vināśi ceti kathaṃ tadbhedo na bhidyeta | bhedaścedaśakyasa
mayatayā'bhilāpasaṃsargayogyatvā'bhāvenā'līkaṃ tadbāhyabhāvāt svalakṣaṇavanna
vikalpagocara iti nā''tmākāro nā'līkaṃ na tadbāhyatā na svalakṣaṇaṃ buddhe
[p.134]
rviṣayaḥ | sarveṣāmaśakyasamayatayā'bhilāpasaṃsargā'narhatvāt | na cā'govyā
vṛttirūpatayā'līkānāmabhedaḥ keṣāṃciditi sāmpratam | tatsvabhāvatve teṣāma
nyonyāśrayadoṣeṇa grahaṇā'bhāvaprasaṅgāt | yathāhā'trabhavānvārttikakāraḥ |
siddhaśca gaurapohyeta gonivṛttyātmakaśca saḥ |
tatra gaureva vaktavyo'nyatāyāḥ pratiṣidhyate |
gavyasiddhe tvagaurnāsti tadabhāve na gauḥ kutaḥ || iti |
na ceyamanyavyāvṛttirapi kalpanānirmitā tadbhede bhedavatī sādhāraṇī
bhavitumarhati | ataeva ca vidhirūpamapi vikalpate gotvam | yatkila vijātī
yamātravyāvṛttaṃ paramārthasataḥ sajātīyā'sajātī yavyāvṛttātsvalakṣaṇāt
bhinnamapyabhedenā'basitam | yathoktaṃ yathā'bhiniveśā''yattatvāt yathātattvaṃ
ca samīhitatvāditi | tadapi na vikalpāspadam | asyāpi jñānā''kāra
syā'līkasya vā prativikalpaṃ bhedena vikalpanīyatā'nupapatteḥ |
yadi manyeta yathaivāsad rūpaṃ sadātmanā prathate tathaikatvarahitamapye
katvena prakāśate | na caitāvatā'sya bhedaḥ | tasyāpi vastvadhiṣṭhānatayā
kalpite'nupapatteḥ | tasmādetadgotvādi na bhinnaṃ nāpyabhinnam, saṃbhinnaṃ tu
bāhyatayā prakāśate | tathā ca siddhā'bhilāpasaṃsargayogyatā'syeti | tatkiṃ ya
evaikasya vikalpasya viṣayaḥ sa eva vikalpāntarasyāpi ? omiti cet |
tasyāpi vikalpanā'dhīnadehatvāt | idameva hi kalpitasya kalpanā'dhīnatvaṃ
yatkalpanābhedā'bhedādyanuvidhānaṃ nāma | tadanadhonatve tu kalpitatvā'nupapatterana
līkatvaprasaṅga ityuktam | yaccā'līkasya na bhāvike nā'nalīkatve iti |
tatra sarvapratīyamānā'vasthāyāṃ tvāpādayāmaḥ, na bhinnakalpanā'nuśayi śakyame
katvena pratipattum | tadanupātitvā'nupapatteḥ | kalpanāyāṃ prathanāntareṇa tasya
tattvā'sambhavāt |
syādetat | kalpanaiva svahetostathābhūtopajātā yayā svaviṣayaḥ keṣu
cideka iti kebhyaścidvyāvartata iva pradṛśyate | nanvevaṃvidho'pi kiṃ vikalpāntara
viṣayo na ? na cet nā'bhilāpasaṃsargayogyaḥ | tasyā'dṛṣṭapūrvasyā'śakya 
samayatvāt vikalpāntaraviṣayatve vā tadadhīnatvā'nupapattirityuktam | api
cā'tyantā'sato gotvādeḥ svabhāvabhedabhājo na prakāśasambhavaḥ | gaganana
linanaraviṣāṇādīnāmapi nātyantā'sattā | sasargabhedamātrasyā'sattvāt |
saṃsargiṇāṃ tu gaganādīnāṃ nalinādīnāṃ ca tatra tatra sadbhāvāt | saṃsargamā
trasya ca kvacitsadbhāvāt tatrāpi ca kathaṃcidupapatteḥ | atyantā'satāṃ
[p.135]
āropitarūpā'nukāritvāttatra kalpanātvaprāpteḥ | na hyavidyamāna
rūpānukārādanyo jñānasyā''dhyāropaḥ | nanu niradhiṣṭhāna āropo'to vastuno
'nyarūpapratītiḥ kalpanā | niradhiṣṭhānaṃ tarhi taimirakasya keśādijñānaṃ
saṃsargabhedānāṃ sadrūpasaṃsargibhedā'dhīnanirūpaṇatayā parasparavyāvṛttarūpā'babhāsa
sambhavāt | iha tu svalakṣaṇajñānā'dhīnanirūpaṇānāṃ gotvādīnāṃ svalakṣaṇaṃ,
tadvijñānavyaktīnāmatyantavyāvṛtteḥ | bhede satyabhinnatvā'navabhāsanāt artha
kriyāyāścaikatvā'nupapatteḥ | atadvyāvṛtteśca parastādāśrayaprasaṅgenā'nava
kalpanāt | na ca kalpitasya gotvasya vidhirūpasyā'nyavyāvṛttirūpasya vā
'bhilāpasaṃsargayogyatā | nanu paramārthasatsāmānyā'bhidhāne'pi bhāvā'bhāva
sādhāraṇyaṃ na kalpate | tadāśrayāṇāṃ vyaktīnāṃ tallakṣitānāṃ deśakālaviprakīrṇa
tayā śabdārthasya bhāvā'bhāvasādhāraṇyasaṃmbhavāt | na cā'tyantavaidharmyaṃ
vyaktīnāṃ, yenā'nyavyāvṛttireva sādharmyamamūṣāmāśrīyeta | sāmānyasyaiva vastu
sata āśrayabhūtā'vasitasya samānasya dharmasyopapatteḥ | tadvādhānirāsaśca
nivedayiṣyate saṃsargaparīkṣāyām | tatsiddhametannā'bhilāpasaṃsargayogyatā jñānā
''kārā'līkabāhyatvayorabhilāpenā'śakyasamayatvāt | yadabhilāpenā'śakya
samayaṃ na tattatsaṃsargayogyaṃ, yathā sukhādīnāmātmeti | yathoktam—
aśakyasamayo hyātmā sukhādīnāmananyabhāk |
teṣāmataḥ svasaṃvittirnā'bhijalpā'nuṣaṅgiṇī || iti |
śakyasamayatayā khalvabhilāpasaṃsargayogyatā vyāptā | anyathā'tipra
saṅgāt | sā jñānā''kārā'līkabāhyatābhyāṃ nivartamānā'bhilāpasaṃsarga
yogyatāmapi nivarttayatīti pratibandhasiddhiḥ | tadidaṃ prasiddhataratayā dūṣaṇama
nudbhāvya prakṛtasyāpi siddhirudbhāvitā''cāryeṇa dūṣaṇatayeti |
nanvāropitarūpā'nukāriṇī kalpanā | na cā'tītā'nāgateṣu vijñānaṃ
yoginastatheti na tatkalpanetyata āha—āropitarūpā'nukāritvāt tatrā'
jātā'tivṛttajñāne kalpanātvaprāpteḥ | nanvāropitarūpā'nukāritvamevā'syā'
siddhamityata āha—na hyavidyamānarūpā'nukārādanyo jñānasyā'dhyāropaḥ | na
cedaṃ vidyamānam | uktaṃ hi bhāve vā pratyutpannatvā''pātāditi |
codayati—nanu niradhiṣṭhāna āropaḥ | yathoktam āropaviṣayārope
vā jñānaṃ rajatabhramamiti | tataḥ kim ? ityata āha | ato vastunaḥ svalakṣaṇasya
paramārthasato'nyarūpeṇa sāmānyarūpeṇa pratītiḥ kalpanā | tathā ca nā'jātā
'tivṛttaḥ sākṣātkāro yoginaḥ kalpanā | na hi tatrā'jātā'tivṛtte sāmānya
rūpatayā cakāsta iti | atraikadeśī pariharati—niradhiṣṭhānaṃ tarhi taimiri
[p.136]
pratyakṣam | atha tatra kiṃcidadhiṣṭhānamucyate | khapuṣpamiti tu jñānamakalpanā
pratyakṣaṃ syāt | sāmanye ca sāmānyā''kārā pratītiḥ svasaṃvittāviva tatrā'
nāroparūpatvāt | atha rūpamātrakalpanā, atītā'jātādiṣu jñānaṃ na kalpanā
poḍham | atha sāmānyākārapratītirvastuni kalpanā tatpratyakṣaṃ sāmānye tu
bhrāntatvāt |
kasya keśādijñānaṃ pratyakṣaṃ prasajyeta | codakamutthāpya dūṣayati—atha tatra
kiñcidālokāṃśādyadhiṣṭhānamucyate | paramārthadūṣaṇamāha—khapuṣpamiti tu
jñānamavikalpanā yataḥ pratyakṣaṃ syāt | na hi tatra kiṃcidadhiṣṭhānaṃ sambhavati |
na khalu bauddhānāṃ mūrtā'bhāvādanyadasti gaganaṃ nāma | abhāvaśca sarvopākhyā
virahalakṣaṇo nā'dhiṣṭhānaṃ kasyaciditi | api ca sāmānye sāmānyā''kārā
pratītiḥ pratyakṣaṃ syāt | nanviyaṃ jñānā''kāramalīkaṃ vā bāhya madhyavasyantī
samānā''ropa eveti kathaṃ na kalpane ? tyata āha—svasaṃvittāviva tatra
sāmānye anāroparūpatvāt | yathā hi vikalpavijñānaṃ vikalpanīyapravaṇamapi
svasaṃvittāvakalpanā evaṃ sāmānyamalīkaṃ vā jñānā''kāraṃ bāhyamadhyavasyadeva
kalpanā | na tadrūpamātre nā''ropamapahāya tatsāmānyarūpamātrā''vedanaṃ kadā
cidapīti cet | atha vikalpanīyamapahāya saṃvidrūpatāvedanamapi kiṃ kadācidvi
kalpanīyaṃ, samānasamayatayā tu tadvedanaṃ bāhyasamāropasamayasāmānyavedane'pi
samānaṃ tat | tasmāt svasaṃvittāviva sāmānyarūpamātra vikalpatayā pratyakṣaṃ
prasajyetetyarthaḥ |
śaṅkate—athā'vidyamānasvarūpasāmānyādeḥ rūpamātrakalpanāpi bhavati
kalpanā | na caivaṃ sati svasavitau kalpanātvaprasaṅgaḥ | vikalpavijñānasvarūpasya
vidyamānatvāditi bhāvaḥ | nirākaroti—atītā'jātādivijñānaṃ kalpanāpoḍhaṃ
tatpratibhāsino rūpasya jātyāderivā'vidyamānatvāt |
śaṅkate—atha sāmānyā''kārā pratītirvastuni kalpanā samā''roparūpa
tvāt na pratyakṣam | atha mā bhūdvastuni, sāmānye tu syādityuktaṃ, tatrāha—
sāmānye tu bhrāntatvāt | asatkhalvaitatsāmānyaṃ vicārā'sahatvāt | tatta
dviṣayā pratītirasadarthatayā vibhramaḥ | na khalu pratyakṣaṃ kalpanāpoḍhamanyanirdiṣṭa
lakṣaṇamiti praṇayato diṅnāgasyaiva kalpanāpoḍhamātraṃ pratyakṣalakṣaṇamapi tu tade
vā'bhrāntatvasahitaṃ pratyakṣalakṣaṇamiti manyate sma kīrttiḥ | yathāha—pratyakṣaṃ
[p.137]
kā punirayaṃ bhrāntattāṃ yadyasadarthatā tulyeyaṃ sarvajña vedane athā'tyantā'sa
darthatā, taimirikasvapnādijñānaṃ pratyakṣam | athārthakriyāsaṃvāditvamabhrāntatvaṃ,
yadyarthanibandhanau sukhaduḥkhaprāptiparihārau upādānaparityāgā'yogyavedanasya
kalpanāpoḍhamabhrāntamiti | etenābhrāntaviśeṣaṇopādānena viradhiṣṭhāne
keśādijñāne bhrāntatva mapratyakṣatayā vyākhyātam | tadetadvikalpya dūṣayati—kā
punariyaṃ bhrāntatā ? yadyasadarthatā tulyeyaṃ sarvajña vedane | ajātā'tivṛttayora
sattvāt | athā'tyantā'sadarthatā taimirikasvapnādijñānaṃ pratyakṣaṃ prasajyeta |
tasyā'rthasyā'tyantāsattvā'bhāvāt | athā'rthakriyāsaṃvāditvamabhrāntatvam |
yathāha—pramāṇamavisaṃvādi jñānamarthakriyāsthitiravisaṃvādanamiti, tadvikalpya
dūṣayati—yadyarthanibandhanau jñānaviṣayanibandhanau yathākramaṃ sukhaduḥkhaprāpti
parihārau arthakriyāsaṃvāditvamiṣyate tata upādānaparityāgā'yogyaviṣayaṃ
yadvedanaṃ tasya candratārakārādiviṣayasya vijñānasya bhrāntatvaprasaṅgaḥ | athotta
rottarajñānanirvṛttirekasminnarthe saṃvādaḥ, sa cāsti candratārakādiviṣayasyāpi
vijñānasyeti tadapyabhrāntaṃ pratyakṣaṃ ceti na vyabhicāraḥ | dūṣayati—kṣaṇika
viṣayasya vijñānasya bhavatāmasmākamāśutaravināśividyudādiviṣayasya
vijñānasya tathātvaṃ bhrāntatvaṃ prasajyeta | na hi tatraikasmin viṣaye uttarottara
jñānotpattisambhavaḥ |
yadi manyeta upadarśitā'rthaprāpaṇasāmarthyameva prāpakatvamavisaṃvādakatvaṃ,
pravartakatvameva ca prāpaṇasāmarthyamupadarśakatvameva pravartakatvam | na hi arthakriyā
samarthavastūpadarśanādanyad vijñānasya pravartakatvaṃ prāpakatvaṃ vā sambhavati | na ca
deśakālā''kāraniyataṃ candratārakā'nyasāmarthyamarthakriyāsu svocitāsu ca na
tādṛśamupadarśita vijñānena | na ca na tādṛśaṃ prāpyeta yadi na deśato viprakṛṣyeta
vidyudādi vā na kālata iti | tadayuktam | vikalpā'sahatvāt | kimartha
mātraprāptiḥ saṃvāda āho yathopadarśitā'rthaprāpti ? na tāvatpūrvaḥ kalpaḥ |
vijñānamātrādarthamātraprāpteravisaṃvādasambhavena tanmātrasyā'bhrāntatvaprasakteḥ |
nāpyuttaraḥ | sā''kāranirākāravijñānayorvicchinnasthūlā'vabhāsasya pratyakṣatayā
niyatasyā'bhrāntatvena tattvā'navakḷpteḥ | na khalu sthaulyaṃ nāma paramārtha
sadvijñāne vā bāhye vā sambhavati | anekavidhaviruddhadharmasaṃsargāptatayobhayatrā
pyapāstatvāt | yathāha—
tasmānnā'rthe na vijñāne sthūlā''bhāsastadātmanaḥ |
ekatra pratiṣiddhatvād bahuṣvapi na sambhavaḥ || iti |
[p.138]
bhrāntatvaprasaṅgaḥ | athottarottarajñānanirvṛttiḥ, kṣaṇikavijñānasya tathātvam |
tasmādatadātmani tādātmyapratītirbhrāntiḥ | tathā ca sarvajñajñānaṃ na kalpanā
poḍhamabhrāntamiti na pratyakṣalakṣaṇamanveti | avā'nubhavā''kārapravṛttaṃ jñāna
mato'nvetyeva | yathoktaṃ mānasamapyanubhavākārapravṛttaṃ pratyakṣamiti | atha
keyamanubhavākāratā ? yadi bodhātmakatā, sarvaṃ tathā jñānam | atha vyaktyanta
rā'vabhāsatā, prathamopanipātyapi dūratvādinā'vyaktapītādiparicchedaṃ na pratyakṣaṃ
syāt | athā'dūrāntarā'tirohitavedanā, sāmānyavedanasya pratyakṣatvaprasaṅgaḥ |
na ca nirākāravijñānanaye bahavo rūpaparamāṇavo nirantarotpādavantaḥ
samarthā ekavijñānopārohiṇaḥ sthūlā''bhāsabhājaḥ | yathāha—apratibhāsadharmo
'sti sthaulyamiti | pratibhāsanakāladharmmaḥ pratibhāsadharmma iti | na ca na te
rūpaparamāṇavaḥ, na ca na nirantaramutpannāḥ, na ca naikavijñānopārohiṇaḥ, na
ca na tādṛśasthūlavyapadeśabhrāja iti kasmāt bhrāntateti sāmpratam ?
nairantaryā'nupapatteḥ | tadgrahasya bhrāntatvāpātādantarālā'grahe nitānta
vicchinneṣu taruṣvekaghanavanapratyayavat | na hi na te taravaḥ | na ca naikasminvijñāne
bhāsante | nirantarā'nutpādaśca tulyaḥ | na khalu tarava iva rūpaparamāṇavo
nirantaramutpannāḥ, vijātīyairgandharasasparśaparamāṇubhirantaritatvāt tādṛ
śasyāpi cā'bhrāntatve dvicandrā'lātacakracaladvṛkṣādidarśanānāmapyabhrānta
tvaprasaṅgaḥ | tadiyamabhrāntatā bhavatsveva praharatītyupekṣitā ācāryeṇa |
upasaṃharati—tasmātpariśeṣyādatadātmani tādātmyapratītirbhrāntiḥ |
bhavatvetad bhrāntilakṣaṇaṃ, tathāpi kā naḥ pīḍe ? tyata āha—tathā ca bhrānti
kalpanayoḥ samāroparūpeṇaikye sati sarvajñajñānaṃ na kalpanāpoḍham | avidyamāna
rūpayoratītā'nāgatayoḥ rūpavatvenā''ropāt | tadavedane pratyakṣatvā'nupapattera
sākṣātkāratvaprasaṅgāt | nāpyabhrāntamarūpā''ropādeveti na pratyakṣa
lakṣamanveti |
lakṣaṇāntaraṃ pratyakṣasya śaṅkate—athā'nubhavā''kārapravṛttaṃ pratyakṣam,
ato'nvetyevā'tītā'nāgataviṣayaṃ sarvajñajñānam | yathoktaṃ mānasamapyanubhavā
''kārapravṛttamiti | tadvikalpya dūṣayati—keyamanubhavākāratā ? yadi bodhā
tmakatā tataḥ sarvaṃ tathābodhātmakaṃ jñānamiti sarvameva pratyakṣaṃ prasaktam | atha
vyaktyantarā'vabhāsatā prathamopanipātyapi nirvikalpakaṃ dūratvādinā doṣeṇā
'vyaktapītādiparicchedaṃ na pratyakṣaṃ syāt | athā'dūrāntarasyā'tirohitasya
ca vedanā'nubhavā''kārapravṛttirasti cā'vyaktaparicchedasyāpyadūrāntarā'tiro
[p.139]
yadi manyeta sannihitavarttamānā'vabhāsā'nubhavā''kārapravṛttiḥ, tathābhūtaṃ ca
sarvārtheṣu yogināṃ tathāgatānāṃ ca darśanamiti | tadasat | pūrvadoṣā'prativṛtteḥ |
api ca tathābhūte'tathādarśanād vartamānaṃ mithyājñānamevaḥ taimivikadarśanavat |
na tu pratyakṣamanyayā bhavati, tadindriyārthasāmarthya janyalakṣaṇamitarathā timi
rādyupaplavajakeśādidarśanaṃ kasya hetorajākīryyata ? sarvameva pratyakṣamasaṃnihi
tā'vabhāsam | arthajñānayoḥ kāryyakāraṇabhāvena kālabhedāt | kiṃ tarhīdaṃ
hitagocaratvaṃ dūrāntaratve'tirohitatve ca tadgocarasyā'vedanāprasaṅgāditi
dūṣayati ca | tadgosāmānyasyā'vastusato na dūrāntaratvaṃ nāpi tirohi
tatvam | ubhayorvastudharmmatvāt | tatastadvedanaṃ pratyakṣaṃ syāt |
yadi manyeta saṃnihitavartamānā'vabhāsā khalvanubhavākārapravṛttiḥ |
na ca sāmānyamalīkaṃ saṃnihitaṃ ca varttamānaṃ ca | tathābhūtaṃ ca sarvārtheṣu yogināṃ
katipayabhūmisamārūḍhānāṃ tathāgatānāṃ ca daśabhūmigatānāṃ ca darśanamiti |
dūṣayati—tadasat | kutaḥ ? pūrvadoṣā'nativṛtteḥ | yadyapi sāmānyamaparamārtha
katayā'saṃnihitamavarttamānaṃ ca tathāpi tathā'vabhāsata ityanubhavā''kārapravṛttyā
tatpratipattiḥ pratyakṣaṃ prasajyeteti pūrvadoṣā''pattirityarthaḥ | atraiva kārikāyāḥ
pūrvārddhena dūṣaṇāntaramāha—api ca tathābhūte vartamāne'tīte'nāgate ca tathā
bhūtadarśanāt | tathābhūtatvamāha—vartamānaṃ mithyājñānameva, taimirikadarśana
vat | kasmātpunarna pratyakṣam ? ityata āha—na tu pratyakṣamanyathā bhavati |
kutaḥ ? tadindriyārthasāmarthyajanyalakṣaṇaṃ pratyakṣaṃ yatkhalu tatrā'rthyamānaṃ tasya
cendriyasya ca sāmarthyājjāyamānaṃ pratyakṣaṃ vijñānamiti tasya lakṣaṇaṃ, na
punaranubhavā''kārapravṛttistvadabhimatā lakṣaṇamityarthaḥ | kasmātpunaridaṃ na
lakṣaṇam ? ityata āha—anyathā hi timirādyupaplavajakeśādidarśanaṃ kasya
hetoḥ pratyakṣatāyā avā'kīryata ? na hi bhavatāmanubhavā''kārapravṛttitvaṃ
pratyakṣalakṣaṇamicchatāṃ taimirikajñānasya pratyakṣatvamabhimatamityarthaḥ |
samprati sā''kārajñānavādī pratyavatiṣṭhate—sarvameva pratyakṣamasaṃni
hitā'vabhāsaṃ tatsarvata eva mā nivartiṣṭa pratyakṣateti nā'yamanyathāgraho'pratyakṣa
tāmāvahatītyarthaḥ | kasmādasaṃnihitā'vabhāsaṃ sarvajñānam ? ityata āha—
arthajñānayoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvena kālabhedāt | yathāsaṅkhyamatra neṣyate |
dūṣaṇavādī tu gūḍhā'bhisandhi pṛcchati—kiṃ tarhīdaṃ nīlādi saṃnihitadeśakālaṃ
[p.140]
prakāśate ? jñānā''kāram | na hīdānīṃ sarvaikadeśasyāpi pratyakṣadarśī | jñānā
kāre'rthe vedanā'dhyavasāyenaiva vyavahāradarśanānnā'rthadarśanahāniḥ | kathamanyavedane
'nyā'dhyavasāyaḥ ? nirūḍhatvādanāderarthagrahā'bhiniveśā'pasmāralakṣaṇāyā
avidyāyāḥ | svasti tarhi sarvajñatvāya bhūyiṣṭhā'vidyā'pasmārāya, varaṃ
kanīyo'vidyamekadeśadarśitvameva | api ca paribhāṣayāpi tāvadekadeśa eva
pratyakṣaṃ syāt | grāhyatvaṃ hetutvameva jñānākārā'rpaṇakṣamamiti na punaḥ sarvaḥ |
tasyā'hetutvāddeśataḥ kālato vā'saṃnidhānāt | anyathā sarvasya sarvadarśitva-
prakāśate ? codaka āha—jñānākāram | praṣṭā sopahāsaṃ svā'bhiprāyamā
viṣkaroti—na hīdānīṃ sarvaikadeśasyāpyartharūpasya pratyakṣadarśī vidyata iti |
sādhu samarthitaḥ sarvārthapratyakṣadarśī codayati—jñānākāre'rthavedanā'dhyavasāyenaiva
vyavahārānnā'rthadarśanahāniḥ | dvividho hi viṣayaḥ pramāṇānāṃ grāhyaścā'dhya
vaseyaśca | tatra svā''kāro grāhyaḥ | tasya tu bāhyatvamadhyavaseyam | yathāha—
svapratibhāse 'narthe'rthādhyavasāyena pravṛttiriti | na hyasmindarśane prakārāntareṇa
grahaṇamupapadyate | tena jñānā''kārasya bāhyatvenā'dhyavasāya eva sarvavedanam |
na caitāvatā bhrāntaḥ | na khalu cittena grahaṇavyāpāreṇa gṛhṇan bhrānto bhavati
kadācidanyathā gṛhṇan kadāciducitādupāyāt upāyāntareṇā''bhimukhyena
pravṛtto bhrāntaḥ | na tvetadubhayamapi sarvavidi samastīti kathaṃ bhrāntaḥ ?
dūṣayati—kathamanyavedane svā''kāravedane anyavedanasya bāhyavedanasyā'dhyava
sāyaḥ ? codaka āha—nirūḍhatvādanāderarthagrahā'bhiniveśā'pasmāralakṣaṇāyā
avidyāyāḥ | sopahāsaṃ dūṣayati—svasti tarhi sarvajñatvāya bhūyiṣṭhā'vidyā
'pasmārāya, varaṃ kanīyo'vidyamekadeśadarśitvameva | na hi svā''kāramanarthamapya
dhyavasyannananyopāyatayā'pi bhavatyabhrāntaḥ | na ca bhrāntatvaṃ nyāyasiddhaṃ samasta
pratyakṣabhrāntatvaprasaṅgabhiyā śakyamapahnotuṃ, pramāṇasiddhasya prayojananirodhenā
'śakyanihnavatvāditi bhāvaḥ | adhyavasāyakartvanirākaraṇaṃ ca darśayiṣyate
na nirālambanaṃ jñānamityatra | api ca paribhāṣayā'pi tāvadekadeśa eva
pratyakṣaṃ syāt | paribhāṣāmāha—grāhyatvaṃ hetutvameva jñānā''kārārpaṇakṣama
miti | yathāha sautrāntikanaye |
bhinnakālaṃ kathaṃ grāhyamiti ced grāhyatāṃ viduḥ |
hetutvameva tad yuktaṃ jñānā''kārā'rpaṇakṣaṇam || iti |
taccetadgrāhyatvamalaukikatayā pāribhāṣikam | tena caikadeśa eva
pratyakṣo na punaḥ sarvaḥ | kutaḥ ? tasya sarvasyā'hetutvāt | tadeva kutaḥ ?
[p.141]
prasaṅgāt | athavā pratyutpannākārameva jñānamatītā'nāgatā''kāramapi vā |
pūrvatra sarvasyā'tathābhāvānmithyā | uttaratrā'nītādirūpakalpanāpravṛttatvānna
pratyakṣam | sarvaṃ vā jñānakāle pratyutpannātmanā jñāyeta, tathāvasthaṃ vā | pūrva
sminmithyātvam | uttaratra na sarvaṃ pratyakṣamadasthāntarā'pratyakṣīkaraṇāt |
api ca sarvaṃ na krameṇa śakyā'vagamamānantyāt | na hi pūrvā'parakoṭivira-
deśataḥ kālato vā tasyā'sannidhānāt | ato hi te bhāvane ye deśataḥ kālato
vā viprakṛṣyete na tu tathā sarvamityarthaḥ | athā'sannihitaḥ kasmānna hetuḥ ?
ityata āha—anyathā saṃnidhivirahiṇo'pi hetutve sarvasyā'smadāderapi
sarvadarśitvaprasaṅgaḥ | yogasya ca sāmarthyamatra nirastameva yathā pūrvārddhe |
asyā'nyā vyākhyā | atha vā pratyutpannā''kāraṃ vartamānā''kārameva vā
sarvasmiṃstraikālyabhāji vastuvistāre vijñānamatītā'nāgatākāramapi vā |
tatra pūrvasmin kalpe sarvasyā'tathābhāvānmithyā sarvajñavijñānaṃ syāt | uttaratra
tvatītā'nāgatādirūpakalpanāpratītatvā nna pratyakṣam | kalpanārahitasya
tattvāt | na tāvadatītā'nāgate rūpamātreṇa prameye nirvikalpakaprameyasya rūpa
mātrasya tayorvarttamānādaviśeṣāt | na hi vartamānamapi vartamānatāviśiṣṭaṃ
nirvikalpake prathate, api tu rūpamātreṇa | tasmādatītatvādinā viśiṣṭatva
manayorjñātavyam | tathā ca kalpanātvaṃ viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvena grahaṇameva hi
tattvaṃ kalpanānām | tathaivā'bhilāpasaṃsargā'rhatvāt | na tu śabdā'rthayoreka
vijñānopārohe, nirvikalpake'pi tathā saṃbhavāt | tasmādatītādiviśiṣṭā
lambanasya jñānasya kalpanātvādapratyakṣatetyupapannam |
yastu manyate astu varttamānaviṣayameva pratyakṣaṃ, tathāpi sarvaviṣayamatītā
'nāgatayorapyatītā'nāgatatvābhyāṃ vartamānatvāditi tannirāsāyā'syaiva
vyākhyāntaramāha—sarvaṃ vā jñānakāle pratyupannātmanā jñāyeta tathā'vasthaṃ vā |
yo yadavasthaḥ sa tathetyarthaḥ | tatra pūrvasminmithyātvam, anyāvasthasyā'nyathā
grahaṇāt | uttaratra na sarvaṃ pratyakṣam | kutaḥ ? avasthāntarā'pratyakṣīkaraṇāt |
etaduktaṃ bhavati | yastu jñānakāle'tītaḥ pratyutpanno'nāgato vā sa tathaiva
pratyakṣīkarttavyaḥ | sa ca svajñānakāle'tīta āsīdanāgato vartamānaśca tathā
svajñānakāle varttamāno'bhūdanāgataḥ bhavitā cā'tītaḥ | evaṃ svahīnakāle'nā
gatasya bhavitārau varttamānatvā'tītatve iti svajñānasamayavatyaṃvasthābhedavadvastu
grahaṇe'vasthāntaramasyāmagṛhītamiti na sarvajñaḥ syāt |
[p.142]
hiṇo jñeyasyotpādavataḥ pariniṣṭhā'sti | na yaugapadyena, ānantyādeva |
iyattā'navadhāraṇe sarvaikadeśapratipattyoraviśeṣāt | nanvanavadhārayannapīyattāṃ
rūpato yaugapadyena sarvamavaiti | naitatsāram | na hīyatā'bhavadhāraṇe sarvamavadhāritaṃ
bhavatyekadeśo vā | anyatarā'vyavacchedāt | bahudravyasamavāye iyattāvadhāraṇā
deva sarvapratipattiḥ, sā ca parimite sambhavati nā'parimite | jñānā''kārā
'dhīnaśca jñeyā'dhigamaḥ | na caikavijñānavarttināmākārāṇāṃ yugapadbhuvāmā-
paścārddha vivṛṇoti—api ca | sarva na tāvatkrameṇa śakyā'vagamam |
kasmāt ? ānantyāt | tadeva darśayati—na hi pūrvā'parakoṭivirahiṇo
jñeyasyotpādavataḥ pariniṣṭhā'sti | nityasyāpi tāvatparamāṇvādervyavasthita
rūpasyāpi neyattā kimaṅga punaravyavasthitarūpasyotpādavat ityarthaḥ | na yauga
padyena | kutaḥ ? pūrvoktādānantyādeva iyattā'bavadhāraṇāt | sākalyavacano
hyaṃ sarvaśabdaḥ, sākalyaṃ ceyattāniḥśeṣatāyāmāpatet, sā cennā'vadhāritā
nā'vadhāritaṃ sarvamityarthaḥ | atheyattā'vadhāraṇā'dhīnaṃ sarvā'vadhāraṇamityetadeva
kuta ? ityata āha—tadiyattā'navadhāraṇe sarvaikadeśapatipattyoraviśeṣāt |
etaduktaṃ bhavati | bahūnāmekadeśaprattipattau hi na sarvapratipattiriti loka
prasiddhaṃ bravanti vaktāraḥ, ekadeśameṣāṃ jānīmo na sarvamiti | yadi rūpa
mātrameva sarvaṃ bhavet tatastasyobhayatrā'viśeṣāt sarvaikadeśapratipattyorabhedaprasaṅga
iti |
codayati—nanvanavadhārayannapīyattāṃ rūpato yaugapadyena sarvamavaiti |
aniyatameva ca sarvamiti kathaṃ tadaparimāṇarūpā'vadhāraṇe sambhavatīyattā
prameyasyā'parādhyate | yadaniyataṃ na punastadviṣayaṃ vijñānaṃ tasya yathāprameyama
vasthānāt, anavasthāne vā prāmāṇyā'nupapatterityarthaḥ | nirākaroti—
naitatsāram | na hīyattā'navadhāraṇe sarvamavadhāritamekadeśo vā | kasmāt ?
anyatarasyaikadeśasya parimitarūpamātrā'grahaṇe avyavacchedāt | anyatarā'vya
vacchedena cā'nyatarapratipatteḥ | vyatirekaṃ darśayitvā'nvayamāha—bahudravya
samavāye iyattā'vadhāraṇādeva sarvapratipattiḥ naikasminnasaṃbaddheṣu vā parasparaṃ
bahuṣvapi sarvatvamekadeśatvaṃ vā sambhavatīti bahudravyasamavāya iyuktam | sā
ceyattāniḥśeṣatā parimite sambhavati nā'parimite, tatsiddhamānantyādaniḥ
śeṣatā | tathā ca na sarvā'vabhāsakṛt | sarvagrahaṇameva yaugapadyagrahaṇamiti
tasyāpyabhāva iti | itaśca nā'nantasya sarvasya yugapadgraho yatsautrāntikanaye
jñānākārā'dhīnaśca jñeyā'dhigamaḥ | na caikavijñānavarttināmākāraṇāṃ yugapadbhu
vāmānantyaṃ samasti | kutaḥ sarveṣāṃ pratyupannatvāt |
[p.143]
nantyaṃ samasti | sarveṣāṃ pratyutpannatvāt | avyavacchinnadeśakālā hyajātā'ti
vṛttapratyutpannā anantāḥ sahā'pekṣamāṇā anītāśca, pūrvakācakoṭeḥ anavacche
dāt, avacchede'pi purastāt | pratyutpannāstūbhayakroṭivyavacchinnā yāvanta
utpannāstāvanta eveti kathamānantyam ? ato nā'nantā''kāramekaṃ jñānama
nantāni vā yugavad jñānāni | kiṃ cedaṃ sarvaṃ yadvidvān sarvavit ? na tāvatpratyeka
marthāḥ, nāpyanyasahitāḥ kenacitsāmānyena vaśīkṛtāḥ kiyanto'pi | na
cetthaṃbhāvo'nanteṣu sambhavati | mukhyamanantameva sarvamiti cet | na | anyatara-
atha pratyutpannānāmapi kasmānnānantayam ? ityata āha—avyavacchinna
deśakālā hyajātā'tivṛttapratyutpannā anantāḥ | nanu pratyekaṃ bhāvānāṃ nātmā
vacchinnadeśakālatvamapotyata āha—sahā'pekṣamāṇāḥ samuccīyamānāḥ na kevalaṃ
trayaḥ, kiṃ tu atītāścā'nantāḥ | nanvekayaiva kālakalayā tasyāḥ purastāttanāḥ
sarve evā'tītā'vacchinnā iti kathamatītā anantā ? ityata āha—pūrvakāla
koṭeranavacchedāt avacchede'pi purastāt | na ceyaṃ vidhā ekā'nekavijñāno
pārohiṇāmākārāṇāmasti, yenā''nantyameva teṣāṃ bhavedityāha—pratyutpannāstū
bhayaka ṭivyavacchinnā yāvanta utpannāstāvanta eveti kathamānantyam ?
upasaṃharati—ato nā'nantākāramekaṃ jñānamanantāni vā yugapat jñānāni,
ubhayatrāpyubhayakoṭivyavacchedāt |
na prameyaṃ nirūpyate iti kārikārddhārthaṃ vivṛṇoti—kiṃ cedaṃ sarva
yadvidvān sarvavit ? na tāvatpratyekamarthāḥ pratyekamanyanirapekṣeṣu sarvaśabdā'prayo
gāt | nāpyasambaddhā'nyasahitāḥ | mā bhūdekasminnapi devadattaputre yaṃ kaṃ cidanya
mapekṣya sarvaśabdaprayogaḥ, api tu kenacitsāmānyena vaśīkṛtāḥ kiyanto'pi |
yathā sarve brāhmaṇāḥ sarve gāva iti | bhavatvevaṃ ko doṣaḥ ? ata āha—na
cetthaṃbhāvo'nanteṣu sambhavati |
śaṅkate—mukhyamanantameva sarvamitiṃ ceta | tathā hi | prakaraṇādāvasati
niyāmake ayaṃ kathamasaṃkucitavṛttiḥ sarvaśabdo'nanteṣu vartata ityananta evāsya
mukhyo'rtha ityarthaḥ | nirākaroti—nā'nyatarakālakoṭibhājāmatītānāmanāga
tānāṃ vā''nantye'pyatītayā tāvatyaivaikayā'pyupahitānāmasarvatvāt | pūrvā'pa
rā'vyavacchinnānāṃ ca pratyutpannānāṃ pratyutpannatopahitānāṃ sarvatvāt | api
cā''nantyameva sarvatve tadavadhāraṇaṃ na sambhavati | tathā hi | sarvā vyaktayo'va
dhāritā na vā ? avadhṛtāścet tāvatya eva nā'nantāḥ | anavadhāraṇe
hyanantatā tāsām | tathā cā'navadhāraṇetyāha—anavadhāraṇaṃ cā'nantamiti
katham ? tasya sarvasyā'vadhāraṇāt | na cā'navadhāryamāṇaṃ prameyamiti bhāvaḥ |
[p.144]
kālakoṭibhājāmānanye'pyasarvatvāt pūrvā'parā'vyavacchimānāṃ ca sarvatvāt |
api cānatyameva sarvatve tadavadhāraṇaṃ na sambhavati | tathā hi | sarvā vyakta
yo'vadhāritāścettāvatya eva nā'nantāḥ | bhanavadhāraṇe hyanantatvaṃ tāsāṃ
tadanavadhāraṇaṃ cā'nantapiti kathaṃ tadavadhāraṇam ? kālatrayā'vacchinnāstarhyarthāḥ
sarve, na, teṣvavacchedā'bhāvādekadeśā'viśeṣāt | tasmātkramā'kramā'tiricyamāna
vidhā'bhāvāttābhyāṃ vyāpterjñānasya tadanupattāvanupapattiḥ | prameyā'bhāvācca |
pratyakṣapūrvatvāccā'numānādīnāmapi sarvasminna kāraṇatvam | pratyakṣeṇa
sākṣātpāramparyeṇa vā sambandhagrahaṇā'bhāvānnā'numānam | na śabdo'ta eva |
padārthāntaropahitaviśeṣapadārthasyeva vākyārthatvāt | nā''rthāpattiḥ | tadabhāve
'navakalpamānadarśanā'nupapatteḥ | nopamānam | sādṛśyaviṣayatvāt |
śaṅkate—kālatrayā'vacchinnāstarhyarthāḥ sarve | vayamanantatāmeva sarvatāṃ
brūmaḥ, kiṃ tvekenaiva kālenopādhivaśalabdhā'tītādyarthavyavasthābhedenā'vacchi
nnā'bhāvātsarvamityarthaḥ | nirākaroti—na teṣvavacchedā'bhāvāt | hetoreka
deśā'viśeṣāt sarvapratipatteranupapatteḥ | na hyekenopasaṃgṛhītā ityetāvantaḥ
kiṃ tu etāvatā | na ca yāvadayameko doṣagrahaṇā'navacchinno nityaḥ kālo'nta
vāniti kathaṃ tadavacchinnopyetāvān | na cā'tītā'nāgatapratyutpannatvānyapyasya
samuccitānyantavantītyarthaḥ | kārikāyāḥ paścārdhaṃ pramāṇayannupasaṃharati—
tasmāt kramā'kramā'tiricyamānavidhā'bhāvāddhetostābhyāṃ vyāpteḥ jñānasya
tadanupapattāvanupapattiḥ | prameyā'bhāvācca prameyaṃ ca na nirūpyate | prameyā'ni
rūpaṇādeva ca sarvagrahaṇasāmartthyamātreṇa sarvajñatā pratyuktā veditavyā | nanu mā
nāma sarva pratyakṣeṇa jñāsīt, anumānādibhistu jñāsyatītyatrāha—pratyakṣapūrva
katvāccā'numānādīnāmapi sarvasmin akāraṇatvam pramāṇatvam |
pratyakṣapūrvakatvaṃ pramāṇānāṃ pratyekamādarśayati—pratyakṣeṇa sākṣātpārampa
ryeṇa vā sambandhasya pakṣadharmatāniyamalakṣaṇasyā'grahaṇaṃ hetustasya ca sarvasminna
bhāvannā'numānam | na śabdo'ta eva pratyakṣeṇa sambandhagrahaṇalabdhajanmatvāt | nanva
gṛhītasambandhaṃ sarva mā bhūtpadārtho vākyārthastu bhaviṣyatītyata āha—padārthānta
ropahitaviśeṣasya padārthasyaiva vākyārthatvāt | kṛtopapādanametanniyogaparīkṣā
yām | nā'rthāpattiḥ | kutaḥ ? tasya sarvasyā'bhāve'navakalpamānasyā'ghaṭamānasya
kasyacit darśanā'nupapatteḥ | nopamānam | dṛṣṭasya smṛtyārūḍhasya gavāde
rdṛśyamānena gavayādinopameyatvāt | sarvasya kvacidadarśanāt | sādṛśyaviṣaya
tvācca | sarvasya cā'sādṛśyarūpatvāt | na pramāṇā'bhāvastasya prameyā'bhāva
[p.145]
nā'bhāvaḥ | abhāvaviṣayatvāt | pratyakṣasyeva cetareṣāmapi pramāṇānāṃ
kramā'kramau na kalpete prameyaṃ na ca nirūpyate | evaṃ tāvadanupapattiḥ | jñānamapi |
sarvādṛśāmanyavittamindriyāṇāṃ na gocaraḥ |
ata eva na sarvajñajñānakāryyaṃ prasiddhyati ||
na tāvadanatīndriyadṛśaḥ sadapi paravarti saṃvedanaṃ pratyakṣam | ata eva
nā'numeyam | tadasiddhau tatkāryā'siddheḥ | nanvatiśayānāṃ kāṣṭhāprāptiḥ
viṣayatvāt | sarvasya ca sadasadrūpatayā tadanupapatteḥ | pratyakṣasyeva cetareṣāmapi
pramāṇānāṃ kramā'kramau na kalpete na ghaṭete | prameyaṃ ca na nirūpyate |
evaṃ tāvadanupapattipramāṇādāru pariśodhitam | jñānamapi pariśodhyate |
tathā hi—
sarvādṛśāmanyavittamindriyāṇāṃ na gocaraḥ |
ata eva na sarvajñajñānakāryaṃ prasiddhyati ||
na tāvadanatīndriyadṛśo'smadādeḥ sadapi paravartti sarvasaṃvedanaṃ pratyakṣa
gamyam | nanu yathā jātamātrasya bālasya smitaruditādikāryadarśanādapratyakṣā
api harṣaviṣādādayaḥ prāgbhavīyaścā'nubhavo'numīyate tathopadeśādilakṣaṇa
kāryadarśanātparavṛttisarvaviṣayaṃ vijñānamanumāsyata ityata āha—ata eva
cātyantā'pratyakṣatvādeva nā'numeyam | nanūktaṃ kāryadarśanādanumeyamityata āha—
tatsarvajñajñānā'siddhau pratyakṣeṇa tatkāryā'siddheḥ | na jātvaparidṛṣṭadhūmaketano
dhūmaṃ tatkāryamavaiti | smitaruditādayastu mānasapratyakṣasamadhigamanīyaśokādi
kāryatayā svātmani pratītā iti vaiṣamyam | upadeśastu manvādīnāṃ carakā
dīnāṃ vā vedāt upadeśāntaraprabhavapravṛttiprasūtādanvayavyatirekā'vagamādvā | na
ca vaidyacarako padeśānāmādirasti | na khalu sarvāsāmauṣadhīnāṃ tatsaṃyogānāṃ ca
sahasreṇāpi puruṣāyuṣairāgamā nadhīnaḥ śaktaḥ kartuṃ kaścidanvayavyatirekau |
caityavandanādyupadeśastu vyāmohādvipralambhādveti niravadyam | paroktāstu
hetavo'dhastādeva nirastā iti nā'tra tirohitamiva kiṃcit |
samprati svāyambhuvā'bhimataṃ sārvajñaṃ nirasitumupanyasyati—nanu sā'ti
śayānāṃ kāṣṭhāprāptiḥ parimāṇānāmupalabdhā, tadyathā kuvalā''malakabilveṣu
mahattvasya sā'tiśayasyā''kāśe kāṣṭhāprāptiḥ | yata ākāśaṃ paramamahadbha
[p.146]
parimāṇānāmupalabdhā, sā'tiśayaṃ ca jñānam, ataḥ kvacitkāṣṭhāprāptaṃ
sarvaviṣayamiti | ucyate—
kāṣṭhā yadyavadhiḥ kāmaṃ paraṃ yasmādasambhavi |
kāryyadravyairanekānto guṇaiśca garimādibhiḥ ||
yadi yataḥ paraṃ nāsti sā kāṣṭhā, bhavatu tatprāptiḥ | na ca tayā sarva
viṣayatvasiddhiḥ | bhūyiṣṭhaviṣayatvaṃ tu bhavet | pārthivasyeva golakasya pārthi
vāntarā'pekṣaṃ bahutarā''kāśā'vakāśavyāpitvaṃ na sarvavyāpitā | atha yataḥ
paraṃ na sambhāvyate, kāryyadravyairvyabhicāraḥ | na hi sā'tiśayā api ghaṭādayaḥ
parimāṇataḥ parā'sambhavanīyā'tiśāyinaḥ | anyā'navakāśaprasaṅgāt | ekena
sarvavyāpteḥ | na ca guṇadharmmo'yamiti sāmpratam | tadgatānāṃ garimādīnāṃ 
vati | sā'tiśayaṃ cedaṃ vijñānamupalabhyate | tattadekadvibahuviṣayādivyāptyā
tadidamapi kvacitkāṣṭhāprāptaṃ yuktam | iyameva cā'sya kāṣṭhā''ptiḥ yadvi
ditasamastaveditavyasya tadabhāve'vyavasthānamiti | yathāha bhagavān patañjaliḥ
tatra niratiśayaṃ sarvajñavijñānamiti | atrottaramāha | ucyate—
kāṣṭhā yadyavadhiḥ kāmaṃ paraṃ yasmādasambhavi |
kāryadravyairanekānto guṇaiśca garimādibhiḥ ||
tāmimāṃ kāṣṭhāṃ vikalpya dūṣayati—yadi yataḥ paraṃ nāsti sa kāṣṭhā,
bhavatu tatprāptirna tayā sarvaviṣayatvasiddhiḥ | sarvameva vijñānaṃ svahetusamāsā
ditajanmabhedaṃ svagocarā'vadhi nā'tivarttate ityatra na vipratipadyāmahe | no
khalvetāvatā sarvaviṣayavijñānasiddhirityarthaḥ | bhūyiṣṭhaviṣayatvaṃ tu bhavet
prājñānāṃ ṣaṭpramāṇīnipuṇānāṃ vijñānasya pārthivasyeva golakasya sakalajīva
lokā'dhiṣṭhānasya gaṇḍaśailaśilādipārthivāntarā'pekṣaṃ bahutarā''kāśā'vakāśa
vyāpitvaṃ, na punaḥ sarvavyāpitā |
kalpāntaramāśaṅkate—atha yataḥ paraṃ na sambhāvyate sā kāṣṭhā, yathā
vyomnaḥ paramamahatparimāṇaṃ paramāṇūnāṃ pārimāṇḍalyam | na hyābhyāṃ paraṃ
mahīyaḥ kṣīdīyo vā sambhavati | taddūṣayati—kāryadravyairvyabhicāraḥ | taṃ
darśayati—na hi sā'tiśayā api kuṭakumbhamaṇikādayaḥ parimāṇataḥ parā
'sambhavanīyarūpadeśā'tiśāyinaḥ | kasmāt ? anyā'navakāśaprasaṅgāt |
sparśavatā gaganaparimāṇena ekenaiva dravyeṇa sarvavyāpteḥ sparśavatāmanyeṣāmanava
kāśaḥ syāt | prathamaṃ guṇatve satīti viśeṣaṇānna kāryadravyairvyabhicāra iti
[p.147]
tadavasthā'sambhavāt | sarvairguruttvavadbhirekakāryyārambhā'bhāvāt | prayatna
viśeṣāccā'ntikadūraprāptirmanuṣyavātahariṇaharipatatriṇām | na ca tasyā'
sambhavanīyaparāvastho'tiśayaḥ | anantatvānnabhasaḥ | keṣāṃcinniratiśaya
prāptānāṃ pātaprasaṅgāt | niravaśeṣagantavya deśaprāptyā hi prayatno nira
tiśayaḥ syāt | tadanantatayā kuto'sya niratiśayatā ? tasmātprayatnenaivā'sā
dhāraṇaguṇena buddhiguṇena vā vyabhicāraḥ | aiśvaryyaphalasya dharmasya na sā
kāṣṭhā | tadvadaiśvaryyajananāddharmasyāpi | īśvaraiśvaryyasya dharmmaphalatvā'bhāvāt |
kleśakarmmavipākā''śayā'parāmarśādanekaiśvaryyaprasaṅgādaiśvaryyavyāghātāt | api
ca—
tatrāha—na ca guṇadharmo'yamiti sāmpratam | kutaḥ ? tatkāryadravyagatānāṃ
garimādīnāṃ tadavasthāyāḥ kāṣṭhāyā asambhavāt | evaṃ hi garimā nā'vastho
dayaṃ paśyedyadi sarvaireva garimaguṇaśālibhiḥ sambhūya paramagarimaguṇamekaṃ
kāryamārabhyate | na tvetatsambhavatītyāha—sarvairgurutvavadbhirekakāryārambhā
'bhāvāt | athā'sādhāraṇye guṇatve satīti viśeṣaṇānna vyabhicāraḥ | tatrāha—
prayatnaviśeṣāccā'ntikadūraprāptirmanuṣyavātahariṇaharipatattriṇām | na ca
tasya viśeṣasyā'sambhāvanīyaparā'vastho'tiśayaḥ | kasmād ? anantatvādganta
vyasya nabhasaḥ | keṣāṃcinniratiśayaprayatnānāṃ pāta prasaṅgāt | niravaśeṣa
gantavyadeśaprāptyā hi prāptaprāpaṇīyatayā gatihetuḥ prayatno niratiśayaḥ
syāt | tadanantatayā kuto'sya niratiśayatā ? tasmātprayatnenaivā'sādhāraṇa
guṇena buddhiguṇena vā vyabhicārastadavastha ityarthaḥ |
yadyucyeta dharmāt khalvaśuklādakṛṣṇāt jñānavairāgyaiśvaryāṇi jāyante, 
tatra yathā dharmaviśeṣajanmanī vairāgyaiśvarye niratiśayameva tajjanmavijñānamati tatra
vyabhicāra iti, tatrāha—aiśvaryaphalasya dharmasya na sā'sti kāṣṭhā tadvadaiśvaryā
dijananāddharmmasyāpi tadvattayā vyapadeśa iti | kutaḥ punaraśuklakṛṣṇasya na sā
kāṣṭhe ? tyata āha—īśvaraiśvaryasya dharmaphalatvā''pātāt | aśuklā'kṛṣṇau
dharmau yogināṃ bhāktasyaiśvaryasya na punarniratiśayasya | tat khalvanupacaritaṃ
bhagavati parameśvare eva | na ca taddharma evaṃ tajjñānamapi | tadayamasiddho hetuḥ |
dṛṣṭāntaśca sādhanahīnaḥ | kutaḥ punaraiśvaryaṃ parameśvarasya na dharmajanitam ?
[p.148]
nā'sambhāvyaparā'vasthaṃ nabhaḥ parimitaṃ yadi |
athā'nantatayā tattvaṃ na tveṣāṃ parimāṇatā ||
ityata āha—kleśakarmavipākā''śayā'parāmarśāt | yathāhā'trabhagavān
patañjaliḥ kleśakarmavipākāśayairaparāmṛṣṭaḥ puruṣaviśeṣa īśvara iti |
avidyā'smitārāgadveṣā'bhiniveśāḥ kleśāḥ | kuśalā'kuśalāni karmāṇi |
tadvipākā jātyāyurbhogāḥ | āśerate ityāśayāḥ karmavāsanāḥ dharmadharmma
viṣayatvāt | tathāpi muktātmabhyaśca prakṛtilayavaidehyaprāptebhyaśca kleśādi
bhiraparāmṛṣṭebhyo'sya bhaga vato na bheda ityāśaṅkyoktaṃ puruṣaviśeṣa iti |
muktātmanāṃ khalu pūrvā bandhakoṭiḥ, uttarā ca videhaprakṛtilayānāṃ sanatkumāra
prabhṛtīnāṃ samasti | tasya tu bhagavato'yameva viśeṣastebhyo yataḥ kleśādīnā
miha vaikalpikatā iti tatra kuto'sya dharmmaviśeṣajanitamaiśvaryam ? ityarthaḥ |
yastu vaighātyāt vṛddhavacanamatikramya parameśvaraiśvaryasyāpi dharmajanitvama
bhyupaiti taṃ pratyāha—anekeśvaraprasaṅgāddhetoḥ sarveṣāmaiśvaryavyādhātāt |
ayamarthaḥ | dharmajanitatve hīśvaratvasya ya eva tadvān sa eveśvaro bahavaśca
tadvanta iti tadvaśād bahava īśvarāḥ prasajyeran | tatraikasyaiśvaryeṇa sarveṣāṃ samaṃ
nyūnaṃ vā aiśvaryam | nyūnatve ca yadevā'tiśāyi tadeva tatsyāditi bhāktamaiśvarya
mitareṣām | tacca svābhāvikatvādupapadyate | svabhāvasyā'vyavasthānāt |
nanu dharmajatve tasyā'vyavasthiteḥ sāmye'pi vā avirodhena saṃbhūyeśate pariṣadvat
pratyekaṃ vā ? pūrvasminkalpe pariṣadiva na kaścidīśvaraḥ syāt, saṃbhūyeśatvāt |
uttaratra tvekenaiveśanāyāḥ kṛtatvādanyaḥ kiṃ kurvannīśaḥ syāt ? na hīśanāma
kurvannīśaḥ, na ca svatantrāṇāmekā'bhiprāyasaṃbhavaḥ | tataśca viruddhā'bhiprāyatve
sati na kasyacitkāryasyotpādaḥ | utpāde vā parasparaviruddhasvabhāvasamaliṅgi
tamaparyāyeṇa jagadupalabhyeta | karmmavadaiśvarya karmajanitamityapyāgamaviruddham |
dharmajasyāpyaparyāyeṇa śruteriti | yacca kāṣṭhāprāptirupalabhyate parimāṇā
nāmityuktaṃ, tadvikalpya dūṣayati—api ca—
nā'saṃbhāvyaparāvasthaṃ nabhaḥ parimitaṃ yadi |
athā'nantatayā tattvaṃ na tveṣāṃ parimāṇatā ||
parimitaṃ cedākāśamiti tato'dhikaṃ kimiti na saṃbhāvyate | sā'
tiśayaparimāṇadarśanādeva kuvalā''malakavilvādiṣu saṃbhāvanīyametat | iyato'pi
nabhaso bhaviṣyati kiṃcidadhikaparimāṇamiti śaṅkate—anantatvānnabhaso'sambhā
[p.149]
anantatvāditi cet | kimidamānantyam ? aparicchinnatā | nanveṣa
parimāṇā'bhāvaḥ | etāvattarhi parimāṇam | īdṛśīṃ ca kāṣṭhāprāptimīśvara
jñānasya vadannajñānamāvirbhāvayati | so'yaṃ śāntikarmmaṇi vetālodayaḥ |
sarvakāryyāṇāṃ kartṛtvādeva tarhi sarvajñatvam | upādānopakaraṇasampradānaprayo
janābhijñā hi kulālādayaḥ karttāraḥ | sarveṣāṃ ca kā ryāṇāmīśvaraḥ karteti
