samastakāryyopādānādyabhijñaḥ | tathā ca sarvajña iti | kimete svakāryasya
niravaśeṣopakaraṇavedinaḥ katipayopakaraṇavedino vā ? na tāvadaśeṣopakaraṇa-
vanīyametat | śaṅkāṃ nirācikīrṣuḥ pṛcchati—kimidamānantyam ? śaṅkitā
brūte | aparicchinnataivā''nantyam | naitasyā anyadānantyaṃ nāma kiṃcit |
utaram—nanveṣa parimāṇā'bhāva eva vacanavyaktyantareṇā''śritaḥ | tadeva
sphuṭayati—etāvattarhi parimāṇaṃ tadviparītatā tu tadabhāvaḥ | astu tarhi
vyomavadanantamīśvarajñānaṃ saivā'sya kāṣṭhāprāptiḥ kiṃ naḥ parimāṇagrahaṇene ?
tyata āha īdṛśīṃ ca kāṣṭhāprāptimīśvarajñānasya vadannajñānamīśvarasyā''vi
rbhāvayati | ātmīyasyaiva vijñānasya sākalyenā'parijñānāt | jñāne vā
kuto'syā''nantyaṃ sākalye paricchedanāntarīyakeṇa iyattā'vadhāraṇenā'pāsta
tvāt | so'yaṃ śāntikarmaṇi īśvarajñānasyā''nantyena vijñatvasamarthanalakṣaṇe
īśvarasyaiva vastvajñānalakṣaṇopeto vetālodayaḥ |
saṃprati naiyāyikāḥ pratyavatiṣṭhante—sarvakāryāṇāṃ kartṛtvādeva tarhi
sarvajñatvam | sarvakāryāṇāṃ tanubhuvādīnāmupalabdhimān kartā pratīyamāno'nta
rbhāvitasarvajñatva eva pratīyate pradīpa ivā'ntarbhāvitaprabhāvitānaḥ, svapramāṇā
dityarthaḥ | nanu bhavatu kartṛtā, sarvajñatā tu kasmādi?tyata āha—upādā
nopakaraṇasaṃpradānaprayojanā'bhijñā hi kulālādayaḥ karttāraḥ | upādānamiha
paramāṇujāticatuṣṭayam | upakaraṇaṃ samastakṣetrajñasamavāyinau dharmā'dharmau |
saṃpradānaṃ kṣetrajñāḥ, yānayaṃ bhagavān svakarmabhirabhipraiti | prayojanaṃ sukhaduḥkho
pabhogaḥ kṣetrajñānāṃ | kulālādikartṛvyāpāraviṣayastūpādānādiḥ prasiddha eva |
etaduktaṃ bhavati | ye yatkartāraḥ te tadupādānādyabhijñāḥ kulālādayaḥ | sarveṣāṃ
ca kāryāṇāmīśvaraḥ kartteti samastakāryopādānādyabhijñaḥ | tathā ca sarvajña
iti |
tametaṃ paratantrasiddhāntaṃ sanidarśanamupanyasya dūṣayitumupakramate—kimete
kulālādayaḥ svakāryasya niravaśeṣopakaraṇādivedinaḥ katipayopakaraṇādivedino
[p.150]
vedinaste | adṛṣṭasyā'jñānāt | nāpi sampradānaprayojanaviśeṣavedinaḥ |
tasyā'nekasyāpi sambhavāt tanmātrajñāne na sarvajñatāsiddhiḥ | bālonmattāda
yaśca sarvakāryyāṇāṃ na prayojanādivedinaḥ | sādhyaṃ cetkartṛtvaṃ tatkathamananta
rbhāvitasarvajñatvaṃ hetumantareṇa seddhumarhati |
nanu siddhameva, sanniveśādimatāṃ buddhimatpūrvakatvāt |
vā ? na tāvadaśeṣopakaraṇādivedinaste | kutaḥ ? adṛṣṭasya sarvotpattimatāṃ
nimittahetoraparijñānāt | nāpi saṃpradānaprayojanaviśeṣavedinaḥ | tasya
saṃpradānabhedasya devadattādeḥ prayojanabhedasya madhūdakādidhāraṇāderanekasyāpi
saṃbhavāt | athopakaraṇādimātravijñānaṃ na samastopakaraṇādijñānaṃ kulalādīnāṃ
tarhi tenaiva nidarśaneneśvarasyāpi tadupakaraṇādimātrajñānaṃ tanmātrajñāne ca na
sarvajñatāsiddhiḥ | katipayajño hi tathā sati syāditi |
na ca tanmātrajñānapi īśvarasya bālādivadityāha—bālonmattādayaśca
svakāryāṇāṃ na prayojanādivedino, nirabhiprāyāṇāṃ tatra pravṛttidarśanāt | na
ca kulālādayo nidarśanaṃ na bālādaya ityatra niyamaheturasti | sādhyaṃ
cettatkartṛtvaṃ tatkathamantarbhāvitasarvajñatvaṃ hetumantareṇa seddhumarhati ?
atra codayati—nanu siddhameva | kutaḥ ? saṃniveśādimatāṃ buddhimatpūrva
katvāt | trayo hi khalu jagati bhāvā bhavanti | prasiddhacetanakarttṛkāśca
yathā prāsādā'ṭṭālagopuratoraṇādayaḥ | prasiddhatadviparyayāśca yathā paramāṇvā
kāśādayaḥ | saṃdigdhatatkartṛkāśca yathā tanutarumahīmahīdharādayaḥ | tatra
vivādādhyāsitā upalabdhimatkartṛkāḥ kāryatvādacetanopādānatvādvā, yatkārya
macetanopādānaṃ vā tatsarvamupalabdhimatkartṛkaṃ yathā prāsādādi, tathā ca tanu
bhuvanādayaḥ, tasmātte'pyupalabdhimatpūrvakā iti | uktahetudvayopalakṣaṇārtha
saṃniveśādimattvagrahaṇam | itarathā guṇakarmaṇoravyāpanena hetorekadeśā'siddhatva
prasaṅgāt | na ca pṛthivyādīnāṃ kāryatvamasiddhaṃ mahattve sati kriyāvattvena vā
sā'vayavatvena vā vastrādivattatsiddheḥ | yadi manyetabuddhimattvamātre sādhye
siddhasādhanam, abhimataṃ hi pareṣāmapi karmajatvātkāryajātasya karmmaṇaścetana
hetutvāttatkāraṇatvaṃ jagataḥ | sarvajñapūrvakatve tu na tena hetorvyāptirdṛṣṭacarī |
daṣṭāntaśca sādhyahīnaḥ | kulālādināmasarvajñatvāt | viruddhatā ca hetoḥ |
asarvajñapūrvakatvenaiva kumbhādāvutpattimattvasya vā'cetanopādānatvasya vā vyāpte
rupalabdheḥ | na copalabdhimatpūrvakatvamātraṃ sādhanaviṣayaḥ | tadviśeṣasya tu
[p.151]
sarvajñapūrvakatvasyā'tadviṣayasyāpi tataḥ siddhiriti sāmpratam | tathā hi |
yadyasau viśeṣo na sādhanavyāptaḥ kathamatastatsiddhiḥ, siddhatvātkathamaviṣayaḥ,
viṣayaścetkathaṃ na svāśrayāttaddoṣagatiṃ spṛśe di ? ti | tadayuktam | sāmānya
mātravyāptāvapyantarbhāvitaviśeṣasyaiva sāmānyasya pakṣadharmatāvaśena sādhyadha
rmiṇyanumānāt | itarathā sarvā'numānocchedaprasaṅgāt | tathā hi vahnyanumāna
mapi na vahnisāmānyamātraviṣayaṃ, tasya prāgeva siddhatvāt | nāpi tadviśiṣṭa
girigocaro, vahnitvasāmānyasya tatsambandhā'bhāvena tadviśeṣatvā'nupapatteḥ |
itarathā gotvasamavāyādiva gāvaḥ śābaleyādayaḥ parvato'pi vahnitvasamavāyā
dvahniḥ prasajyeta | asti ca vahnitvena gireḥ saṃyuktasamavāyaḥ sambandha iti cet,
tarhi nā'pratipadya parvatasaṃyuktaṃ vahniviśeṣamasau śakyapratipattiriti vahni
viśeṣāsyāpyanumānam | tathā na pūrvoktadoṣaprasaṅgaḥ |
evamindriyā'numāne'pi sa eva darśanīyaḥ | tathā hi | tatrāpi nendriya
karaṇikā kācit kriyopalabdhā | na khalu cchidādayaḥ kriyā indriya
sādhanāḥ, vraścanādīnāmanindriyatvāt | na ca vraścanādisādhanāḥ sambhavanti
rūpādiparicchittilakṣaṇāḥ kriyāḥ | tasmādyathaiva kriyātvasāmānyasya satyapi
karaṇamātrā'dhīnatvāvyāptatve pakṣadharmmatāvaśādindriyalakṣaṇakaraṇaviśeṣasiddhiḥ
tathehāpi satyapyutpattimattvasyā'cetanopādānatvasya copādānopakaraṇasaṃpradāna
prayojanajñakartṛmātravyāptatve vivādādhyasiteṣu pakṣadharmmatāvalādviśiṣṭasya tasya
siddhiḥ | anyathā sāmānyasyāpi vyāpakā'bhimatasyā'siddhiḥ syāt, nirviśeṣa
syā'sambhavāt, viśeṣasya cā'nyasyā'nupapatteḥ | asarvajñasya cā'trādṛṣṭādibheda
vijñānarahitasyā'dhiṣṭhātṛbhāvā'sambhavāt | tena yadyapyupalabdhimatkartṛkatvaṃ
vyāptiviṣayastathāpi tadviśeṣasya pakṣadharmmatābalāt pratilambha iti viśeṣa
viṣayamanumānam | na coktadoṣaprasaṅgaḥ | tasya sādhyadṛṣṭāntayorddharmmavikalpā
dutkarṣā'pakarṣā'nuyogasya sarvā'numānasādhāraṇyenā'numānamātraprāmāṇyapratikṣepa
hetutvāt | tatsiddhaṃ vivādā'dhyāsitā upalabdhimatkartṛkā iti |
atrepsitaprasiddhyāyattasādhanaṃ śabdoktaṃ tadāyattopādānādiviṣayatvāt
yasya yadāyattamupādānādiviṣayatvaṃ tattatprasiddhyāyattasādhanaṃ yathā dhūmo
vahnyāderddeśaviśeṣasiddhyadhīnopādānādiviṣayatvantadadhīnasiddhiriti | na cā'sya
sakalakṣetrajñasamavāyidharmmā'dharmajñānakāraṇā'bhāvena tadajñānam | tatsamavetānāṃ
jñānacikīrṣāprayatnānāṃ nityatvāt | na ca buddhitannityatvayoḥ kaścidvirodhaḥ |
[p.152]
buddheranityāyāstatra tatropalabdhervirodha iti cet ? tarhi toyādiparamāṇa
bopi na rūpādimantaḥ prasajyeran | pārthivānāṃ rūpādīnāṃ kāryāṇāmupalabdheḥ,
iha ca teṣāmakāryatvāt | himakarakādau rūpādidarśanāt kāryadravyagatānāṃ ca teṣāṃ
kāraṇaguṇaprakrameṇa bhāvāt | pāthasīyānāmapi rūpādimattā | sada kāraṇatayā
ca tadgatāni rūpādīni nityānīti cet | ihāpi kāryatvenā'cetanopādānatvena
vā samastaviṣayajñānā'numānāt tasya ca sada kāraṇatayā nityatvasiddheḥ
samānatvāt parapuruṣasamavetāvapi dharmmā'dharmāvadhiṣṭhātuṃ śaknoti sambandhāt |
na hi sākṣātsaṃyogasamavāyāveva sambandhau, saṃyuktasaṃyogisamavāyasyāpi tadbhā
vāt | prayuktāḥ khalvīśvareṇa paramāṇavaḥ taireva kṣetrajñāḥ tatsamavetau dharmādharmā
viti, saṃyuktasamavāyo vā kṣetrajñairīśvarasya saṃyogāt | anumānāt saṃyogasiddheḥ |
īśvaraḥ kṣetrajñasaṃyukto mūrttadravyasaṃyogitvāt yo mūrttadravyasaṃyogī sa kṣetrajñasaṃyuktaḥ
yathā ghaṭādiḥ | sa cāsya saṃyogī'nyatarobhayakarmasaṃyogānāṃ tatkāraṇā
nāmabhāvādajanyaḥ | kāryārambhā''bhimukhyameva dharmmā'dharmayoḥ pravṛttiḥ | sā
ca deśakālādivadīśvarapratnamapyapekṣate | na caivaṃ vācyaṃ dharmmā'dharmmau paramāṇūnvā
svadharmā nupagṛhītān kathamīśvaraḥ prayatnena prerayatīti | viṣavidyāvidā tadīya
dharmmā'nupagṛhītasya viṣaśakalasya preraṇadarśanāt | jñānecchāprayatnānāṃ ca tadvartināṃ
nityatvasiddhau kṛtamasya śarīragrahaṇena | tanmātrādeva paramāṇūnāmadṛṣṭādīnāṃ ca
pravṛttau śarīrapṛthivyādilakṣaṇasya kāryasyotpatterupapatteḥ, na caitadvācyaṃ neśvaraḥ
karttā pṛthivyādīnāṃ, prayojanā'bhāvādanityaṃ ceśvaravijñānaṃ vijñānatvāditi |
īśvarasya tajjānasya ca dharmmiṇo'prasiddhau hetorāśrayā'siddhatvāt | tatsiddhau
vā karturnityajñānasyeśvarasyā'numānādeva dharmmigrāhiṇaḥ siddhestadvirodhānna
tadāśrayamanumānam ātmānamāpādayitumarhati | tatkimīśvarajñānā'nityatva
grāhiṇo'numānasya viśeṣaviṣayatayā balīyastvaprasādhanaprayatnena ? mūla evāsya
vihitaḥ kuṭhāraḥ | na cā'jñātarūpāṇāmindriyamanasāṃ vijñānotpattiṃ prati
sādhanabhāvasya ca vatsavivṛddhinimittasyā'cetanasya kṣīrasya svātantryeṇa
pravṛtteśca | vanaviṭapināṃ ca vinā prayatnamutpādasya ca darśanādvyabhicāra iti
sāmpratam | sarveṣāmeva teṣāṃ vivādāspadatvena pakṣapakṣe nikṣepāt | na ca
śaśaśṛṅgā'stitā, tadanupalabdhivirodhāt | aparokṣadarśanā narhatayā bhagava
tastadvirodhā'nupapatteḥ | anyathā sarvā'numānocchedaprasaṅgāt | śṛṅgasya tu
śaśaśirovarttino gavādigatasyeva darśanārhasyā'nupalabdhinirākṛtasya sādhanārha
[p.153]
vārtametat |
tvāt | na ca pakṣeṇa vyabhicāraḥ | sarvatra tasya sulabhatayā sarvānu'mānoccheda
prasaṅgāt | vyatirekī vā hetuḥ kāryamātraṃ pakṣīkṛtya prayoktavyaḥ | sa caivaṃ
prayogamārohati | nityetaratsamastasarvajñapūrvakamutpattimattvādacetanopādāna
tvādvā | yatpunarna sarvajñapūrvakaṃ na tadutpattimat acetanopādānaṃ vā | yathā
paramāṇvāda | na cedaṃ tathā tasmātsarvajñapūrvakamiti | sarvajñapūrvakatvena ca paraṃ
pratyasiddhena kāryamātrameva paṭhyata iti bhavati pakṣaḥ | yadi punaścetanā'nadhiṣṭhi
tāni svaṃ kārya kuryuḥ tato yatra kvacanā'vacchinnāni kārya janayeyuriti na
deśakālapratiniyataprasavaṃ kāryamupalabhyeta | hetusamavadhānajananatayā kārya na
pratyekaṃ kāraṇairjanyata iti cetsatyaṃ, samavadhānameva tu kāraṇānāmanyonyam 
kutaḥ ? kādācitkaparipākādadṛṣṭaviśeṣāditi cet, sa khalvayamapyacetanaḥ
kathaṃ yathāvatkāraṇāni saṃnidhāpayet | no khalu yatra kvacanā'vasthitāni
daṇḍādīni vinā kumbhakāraprayatnamadṛṣṭaviśeṣādeva parasparaṃ saṃnidhīyante, sanni
hitāni vā kāryasyeśate | tasmāt kāraṇasamavadhānavyāptatvāt kāryotpāda
nasya tasya copalabdhimatpūrvakatvā'bhāve'nupapatteḥ vyāpakanivṛttyā vipakṣātkā
ryotpāde nivartamāne upalabdhimattvena vyāpyata iti pratibandhasiddhiḥ | na
ceśvarajñānamapratyakṣamindriyārthasaṃnikarṣā'nutpādāditi sāmpratam | lokasya
hi pratyakṣasya tallakṣaṇam | asya hi nikhilā'rthasākṣātkāritvaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ vedi
tavyam | atra cā''mnāyo bhavati 
etasya vā akṣarasya praśāsane gārgi
dyāvāpṛthivī vidhṛte tiṣṭhataḥ, 
dyāvābhūmī janayan deva eka iti |
smṛtiśca bhavati |
ajño janturanīśo'yamātmanaḥ sukhaduḥkhayoḥ |
īśvaraprerito gacchetsvargaṃ vā śvabhrameva vā ||
na ceśvarajñānasya śarīrendriyā'pekṣayotpādaḥ | asti hi śrutiḥ
apāṇipādo javano grahītā paśyatyacakṣuḥ sa śṛṇotyakarṇaḥ | sa vetti viśvaṃ
na hi tasyā'sti vettā tamāhuragryaṃpuruṣaṃ mahāntam ityādikā | tadanumānā
''gamābhyāṃ siddhamīśvarasya kartṛtvaṃ sarvajñatvaṃ ceti |
tadetaddūṣayati—vārttametat | idamatrā''kūtam | na tāvatpakṣadharmatāva
śāttadadhiṣṭhānaviśeṣo yugapadasaṃkhyeyasthāvarādilakṣaṇakāryadarśanādakhilaviṣaya
nityavijñānamātraśālī ṣaṅguṇa īśvaraḥ seddhurmahati, na hyetāvatā viśvaṃ kārya
[p.154]
mupajāyate | na jātūpādānādijño'pi kumbhakāraḥ kumbhamacikīrṣuścikīṣurvā
tadupādānādiṣvalasatayā'prayatamānaḥ kumbhā''rambhāya kalpate | tasmāt jñāna
cikīrṣāprayatnasamudāyajanmā kāryopādo na jñānamātrād bhavitumarhati |
mā bhūdārdrendhanadahanajanmā dhūmaḥ kevalā''rdrendhanādvahnervā pṛthagjanajñāna
vilakṣaṇamīśvarajñānamasahāyameva viśvasmin kārye paryāptamiti cet ? hanta
bhoḥ kimasya jñānenāpi svarūpā'tiśaya evā'syāḥ sahāyastatra tatra kārye
vartsyatīti na kiṃcitkāryamasahāyādekasmāditi cet | nanu tasyaiva sta eva
kṣetrajñagatau dharmā'dharmau, santi ca paramāṇavaḥ sahāyā iti kimasya jñānena ?
avijñātāste na pravarttanta iti jñānameṣi vyamiti cet | athā'vijñātāste
kasmānna pravarttante ? tathā'nupalambhāt kumbhādyupāyeṣviti cet | tarhi
jñānavaccikīrṣāprayatnāvapyāstheyau tatrāpi ca jñānaṃ cikīrṣāviśeṣe upayujyeta |
sa ca prayatnabheda iti prayatnahetubheda eva sākṣātkārodaye heturiti kathaṃ tamanta
reṇa tatsambhavaḥ ? na hi vahnimantareṇa tuṣaprakṣepaphūtkāramātrādevaudanaṃ
siddhyati |
yadi manyeta sta eva cikīrṣāprayatnāvapi bhagavatīśvare iti ? atha
kimetau nityāvanityau vā ? nityau cetkṛtamīśvarasya jñānena cikīrṣā
prayatnotpādā'nupayoginā | tayornityatayā svotpādopayogā'napekṣaṇāt | na
ca prayatnavat jñānamapi sākṣātkāryotpādanāṅgam | prayatnabhedena khalvayama
dhitiṣṭhatyupādānādi, na tatsamaye jñānasya kaścidupayogaścikīrṣāyā vā
niṣpāditakriye karmmaṇyaviśeṣādhāyinaḥ sādhanasya sādhananyāyā'tipātāditi
dattajalañjālirīśvasya sarvajñatā | athā'nityau, tatastayorutpattikāraṇaṃ
vaktavyam | nanu nityajñānamevotpattimūlakāraṇamasti | tataḥ kimapareṇa
kāraṇena ? aho vata pramādo yadayamātmamanaḥsaṃyogaviśeṣā'samavāyikāraṇau
icchāprayatnau tamantareṇa jñānamātrādeva bhavata ityapi vaktumadhyavasitaḥ | tathā
satyayamataṇḍulamapi maṇḍaṃ sādhayedeva |
santyevā'sya muktātmamanobhiḥ saṃyogāstena tatsahāyastatra cikīrṣā
prayatnapracayaṃ yugapatprasūte tatkāryopajananāyeti cet ? nanu manāṃsi tatsaṃyo
gāśca kimanadhiṣṭhitāni cikīrṣāprayatnau prasuvate adhiṣṭhitāni vā ? yadya
nadhiṣṭhitāni tatastaireva vyabhicāraḥ | adhiṣṭhānaṃ tu prayatnamantareṇā'yukta
meva | prayatnāntareṇa cikīrṣāntarajanmanā tadadhiṣṭhānam | anāditvācci
kīrṣāprayatna-pravāhasyeti cet ? yathā'yamutpannasaṃjihīrṣuḥ īśvaraḥ kṣetrajñeṣu
[p.155]
sanniveśādimatsarvaṃ buddhimaddhetu yadyapi |
prasiddheḥ sanniveśāderekakāraṇatā kutaḥ ||
pratibandhavṛttidharmmādharmanicayeṣu jagati ca paramāṇvavasthāmāpanne nirvyāpāraḥ
kiyantamapi kālaṃ sthitvā yadā cikīrṣati prayatate vā tadā tasya cikīrṣā
prayatnayorjanayitavyayorna sthitaṃ cikīrṣāntaraṃ vā prayatnāntaraṃ samastīti kuto
manasāṃ tatsaṃyogānāṃ vā'dhiṣṭhānami?tyanadhiṣṭhitebhyo manastatsaṃyogebhyo
na prayatnacikīrṣe utpadyeyātām | utpadyamāne vā kathaṃ kāryatvamacetanopādā
natvaṃ vā na hetuṃ vyabhicārayataḥ | na ca savitṛprakāśavadīśvarasya jñānamātra
micchāmātraṃ prayatnamātraṃ tattadbhāvabhedopadhānāttadviṣayaṃ bhavatītyapi sāmpratam |
parasparāśrayadoṣaprasaṅgāt | tattadbhāvopadhāne tattadviṣayatve ca tattadupadhānam |
upadhīyamānā'nadhiṣṭhāne tadupadhānasya kāryasyā'nutpādāt | tasya cā'dhiṣṭhā
nādutpatteriti | jñānānityatvapakṣe ca dūṣaṇamupariṣṭātpravedayiṣyate |
so'yamīdṛśo viśeṣo vicārā'sahaḥ kathaṃ pakṣadharmmatābalādapi sādhya
dharmiṇyupasaṃhniyate ? na hi svayamanupapadyamānamanyasyopapādanāyā'lam | kā
tarhi sāmānyasyā''numānikasyā'sati viśeṣe gatiḥ ? kimavaśyaṃbhavitavyamasya
gatyā ? nanu pramāṇasiddhamupapādanīyaṃ yasyā'numopapadyate yat nāmā'nupapadya
mānam astu tasya paramanupapattireva | nanu pramāṇasiddhamanupapannamiti viprati
ṣiddham | evaṃ tarhi pramāṇasiddhipratighāta evāstvavipratiṣedhāya, na punaranupapanna
syopapattiḥ | katamattarhi sādhanasyedaṃ dūṣaṇam ? na dūṣaṇam sādhanasya tasya nāma
vādinaḥ yaḥ sasahāyaniḥsahāyanityā'nityatvādiviśeṣaparihārāya gaganakusumāya
māne kṣityādivarttini buddhimatpūrvakatvasāmānye sādhanā'narhe sādhanaṃ prayojayati |
na ca sambhavati tādṛśo viśeṣo yamāsthāya kṣityādau buddhimatpūrvakatvasāmānyaṃ
vyavatiṣṭheta | na caivaṃ sarvā'numānocchedaprasaṅgaḥ | sambhavadviśeṣaviṣayatvādi
tareṣāmanumānānām | na hi naṃ sambhavati parvatanitambavarttivahniviśeṣo'
nudbhūtarūpasparśo vā taijasaścakṣuravayavo rūpopalabdhisādhanam | kāraṇānāṃ
ca citrarūpasaṃsthānasāmarthyānāṃ tatra tatropalabdheḥ | tasmādyathā kaścinniśā
tamasimacchedyamākāśaṃ prati vyāpārayanpuruṣo nigṛhyate na punarasimākāśaṃ vā
tadvatkāryatvamacetanopādānatvaṃ vā tarkasahāyaṃ labdhaparipākā'dṛṣṭavatkṣetrajña
paramāṇvādisaṃyogakāraṇamātraviṣayamīśvare sarvajñe iti tadidamuktaṃ
vārttametaditi |
yattu vṛddhairdūṣitaṃ tadatiprasiddhatayopekṣya dūṣāṇantaramāha—
saṃniveśādimatsarvaṃ buddhimaddhetu yadyapi |
prasiddheḥ saṃniveśāderekakāraṇatā kutaḥ ||
[p.156]
tatra vṛddhā dūṣayāṃvabhūvuḥ | kimutpattimattvamātramatra hetuḥ kriyate kiṃ vā
tadviśeṣaḥ ? yaddṛṣṭerakriyādarśino'pi kṛtabuddhirupajāyate | etaduktaṃ bhavati |
ta eva khalūtpattimanto'cetanopādānabuddhimatpūrvakā ye buddhimadanvayavyatirekā
'nuvidhāyibhāvā'bhāvāḥ pramāṇenopalabdhāḥ, prāsādā'ṭṭālakagopuratoraṇādayaśca
tathā, na tu tanubhuvanādayaḥ | na khalu tadbhāvā'bhāvayorbuddhimadbhāvā'bhāvā'nu
vidhānaṃ kadācidapyupalabdham | ghaṭāderutpattimatastathopalambhādanutpattimataśca
gaganāderanupalambhādutpattimattvasya ca tanubhuvanādāvapyabhedāt tathābhāva iti
cet ? tatkimidānīṃ mṛdvikārasya ghaṭādermanuṣyakāryatvamupalabdhamiti śakramūrdhā
'pi tatkāryaḥ | na ca mṛdvikāratvaṃ saṃsthānaṃ vā bhidyate | tadvidhasaṃsthānasyāpi
mṛdvikārasya manuṣyanirmāṇadarśanāt | tādṛśatve'pi tasya tadanvayavyatirekā
'nuvidhānā'darśanāt na tatkāryatvamiti cet | na | ihāpi sāmyāt |
atrāpi hi yasya yasya buddhimadbhāvā'bhāvā'nuvidhānamasti tasya tasya sarvasyā
'stu nāma tatpūrvatā prāsādādeḥ, na punastadvidhasyāpi bhuvanāderbhavitumarhati |
tasya tadanvayavyatirekā'nuvidhānā'bhāvāt | nanvayaṃ yo'sau dhūmaviśeṣo vahni
bhāvā'bhāvā'nuvidhāyibhāvā'bhāva upalabdhaḥ sa sarvo vahnipūrvo'stu na tu
parvatanitambavartī tatheti kathaṃ tato vahniranumīyate | sāmānyavadviṣayatve'pi tu
kāryakāraṇabhāvā'vadhāraṇasyehāpi sāmyam |
tatkimidānīṃ manuṣyakāryāḥ kuṅyādaya upalabdhā iti śakramūrddhāpi 
tathāstu ? kasyacidviśeṣasya kuṅyādibhyaḥ śakramūrddhādeḥ sambhavānnaivamiti
cet | sa tarhi prāsādādibhya saṃbha vati tanubhuvanādāvapīti na viśeṣaḥ | na
tvayamevaṃvidho dhūmaviśeṣāṇāṃ vahnyanvayavyatirekā'nuvidhāyināmasti parvata
gatadhūmātsvarūpeṇa viśeṣaḥ | ata eva na pāṇḍudravyamātrātkumudakapotāderanu
mānam | dhūmatvasāmānyasyaiva tebhyo bhedā''pādakatvāt | na ca hetusamavadhānaṃ
cetanamantareṇā'sambhavi | adṛṣṭa paripākādeva kādācitkāddeśakālabhedasamā
sāditajanmanastadupapatteḥ | tataśca kāryaniyamasiddheḥ | na khalu niyataprakāra
sāmarthyebhyaḥ kāraṇebhyaḥ kāryamaniyamenotpattumarhati | anyathā kartṛcaitanye
'pyayaṃ durvāraḥ prasaṅgaḥ | tasyāpi kāraṇasāmarthyā'nurodhena niyojakatvāt |
na ca cetanamantareṇa kumbhādikāraṇāni na pravarttante iti pṛthivyādikāraṇairapi
na pravarttitavyam | yathā hi dehavatprayatnamantareṇa kumbhādikāraṇeṣvapravartamāne
[p.157]
dṛṣṭā prekṣāpūrvaprāsādādisanniveśavailakṣaṇye'pi cetareṣāṃ tarugirisāgarā
dīnāṃ yadyapi sanniveśamātrasāmānyāt, tathā sati vibhakteśatayā svārthe
parā'nugrahe vā duḥkhottarasargadarśanāt prayojanā'bhāvanirākṛtā'pi caitanyamātra
siddhiḥ syāt ekatvaṃ tu tasya kutastyam ? na hi ghaṭarathādaya ekabuddhimatpū-
ṣvapi pṛthivyādikāraṇāni na dehavataḥ prayatnamapekṣante svapravṛttiṃ prati tathā
cetanamātraprayatnamapi nā'pekṣiṣyante, ki tvadṛṣṭaparipākavatkṣetrajñasaṃyogā deva
pravartsyantīti kiṃ tadabhijñena kalpitena ? tasmādviśiṣṭamutpattimattvamasiddham |
pṛthivyādiṣu utpattimattvamātraṃ tvaprayojakaṃ viśeṣaprayuktavyāptyupa
jīvitvena svābhāvikapratibandhavaikalyāt | svābhāvikapratibandhavataśca hetora
numānā'ṅgatvāt | itarathopādhyāyadarśanāderapi śiṣyādyanumāpakatvaprasaṅgāt |
api ca prekṣāvatāṃ pravṛttiḥ prayojanavattayā vyāptā | na ceśvarasya prekṣāvato
jagannirmāṇe prayojanaṃ paśyāmaḥ | prāptanikhilaprāṇīyasya prāptavyā'bhāvāt |
antataḥ krīḍāsādhyasyāpi sukhasyā'vāptatvāt | nāpi kāruṇyena parārthaṃ
pravartate | sukhamayajīvalokasarjanaprasaṅgāt | duḥkhamayī tu sṛṣṭirbahula 
mupalabhyate | na ca dharmmā'dharmmasahāyasya nirmāṇavaicitryamiti sāmpratam |
adharmasya jagadduḥkhā'hetoḥ kāruṇikā'nadhiṣṭhānā'nupapatteḥ | anadhiṣṭhitasya
vā kāryakaraṇe hetorvyabhicāraprasaṅgāt | na ca duḥkhā'nubhavanaṃ vairāgyotpādana
dvāreṇā'pavargopayogīti parahitakāmyayā tadīyamadharmamadhitiṣṭhatīti
sāmpratam | īśvarā'dhīnatvād duḥkhotpādanasya tasya ca tatra vaimukhyena 
tadanutpāde tadatyantavimokṣalakṣaṇasyā'pavargasya phalataḥ prāpteḥ | na cā'prayojana
mapi svabhāvādeva bhāvayatīti yuktaṃ, prekṣāvattvavyāghātāt | tathā ca tatpraṇī
teṣvāgameṣvanāśvāsa iti tatropekṣāṃ darśayitvā dūṣaṇāntaramāha—
dṛṣṭā prekṣāpūrvaprasādādisaṃniveśavailakṣaṇye'pi cetareṣāṃ tarugiri
sāgarādīnāṃ yadyapi saṃniveśamātrasāmānyāccaitanyamātrasiddhiḥ syāditi
pareṇa sambandhaḥ | tathā sati vibhakteśatayā svārthe parā'nugrahe vā'sati
kasmānna parārthā'nugraha ? ityata āha—duḥkhottarasargadarśanāt | prayojanā
'bhāvanirākṛtāpi caitanyamātrasiddhiḥ syād yadyapi ekatvaṃ tu tasyeśvarasya
kutastyaṃ ghaṭarathādīnāṃ kartustathā darśanāditi cet tatrāha—na hi ghaṭarathādaya
ekabuddhimatpūrvakāḥ |
[p.158]
rvakāḥ | īśvarapūrvakatvena tatheti cet sādhyaṃ taccetanamātranāntarīyakatvātsa
nniveśasya keṣāṃcittathā darśanāt anyatrāpi tathābhāva iti cet | dṛṣṭānta
sādhyābhyāmanekāntaḥ | sarvasādhyatve dṛṣṭāntā'bhāvaḥ | dvikartṛkatve ca ghaṭā
derbhuvanādyapi tatheti īśvareśvaraprasaṅgādanavasthā | ata eva na dvitīyā'nu
mānam | ekā'numānamapi tarhi mā bhūt, tathāpyanavasthāyāstulyatvāt | atha
na dvyadhiṣṭhātṛkā ghaṭādayaḥ sarvamekādhiṣṭhātṛkamiti | na sarvajñatvasiddhiḥ |
śaṅkate—īśvarakartṛkatvena sarvasya kāryajātasya tathaikakartṛkatvamiti
cet sādhyamīśvarapūrvakatvaṃ sarvasya kāryajātasya tatkathaṃ tadeva sādhanatayopādī
yata ityarthaḥ | punaḥ śaṅkate—taccetanapūrvakatvamātranāntarīyakatvāt saṃniveśasya
keṣāṃcid ghaṭarathādīnāṃ tathā darśanādanyatrāpi kṣityādau tathābhāva iti siddha
mīśvarapūrvakatvam | tathā caikakartṛkatvamiti cet | nirācaṣṭe—dṛṣṭānta
sādhyābhyāmanekāntaḥ | dṛṣṭānte hi ghaṭādāvekaḥ karttā kulālādiḥ, sādhye ca
kṣityādau īśvara iti vyabhicārānnotpattimattvamacetanopādānatvaṃ vā cetanā
tmānaṃ karttāraṃ tanubhuvanādīnāṃ sādhayitumarhati | anekakartṛkasya dṛṣṭānta
sādhyayordarśanādityarthaḥ |
yadi tu sarvameveśvarapūrvakatvena sādhyeta tato ghaṭādīnāmīśvarapūrvaka
tvena pakṣavinikṣepāt dṛṣṭāntā'bhāva ityāha—sarvasādhyatve dṛṣṭāntā'bhāvaḥ |
na ca vyatirekī hetuḥ sambhavati | kādācitkā'dṛṣṭaparipākādeva deśakālā
dipratiniyatakāryotpādopapattyā vyatirekā'vyabhicārasyā'niścayāditi
bhāvaḥ | yathā caitattathopapāditamadhastāt |
api cā'stvīśvaraḥ karttā ghaṭādīnāṃ tathāpi pratyakṣaparidṛṣṭakulālā
dirapi kartteti dvikarttṛkatve ca ghaṭāderbhuvanādyapi tathā tenaiva nidarśanena
dvikartṛkatvamitīśvarasyeśvaraprasaṅgādanavasthā |
etaduktaṃ bhavati | yathā pratyakṣaparidṛṣṭakulālādikartṛke'pi ghaṭādau
tāvataivopapadyamānotpāde cetanāntaramīśvarastatkarttāraṃ kulālamadhiṣṭhātuṃ
kalpate tatheśvaramadhiṣṭhātumīśvarāntaram, evaṃ tamityanavastheti | ata eva
codayati—anavasthāprasaṅgādeva na dvitīyeśvarasyā'numānam | tato na dvikartṛ
kateti cet | pariharati—ekā'numānamapi tarhi mā bhūt | kasmāt ? tathā
ekānumāne'pyanavasthāyāstulyatvāt | labdhaparipākā'dṛṣṭavatkṣetrajñasaṃyogādeva
kṣityādilakṣaṇakāryotpattāvekasyāpīśvarasyā'numāne tulyaivā'navasthetyarthaḥ |
śaṅkate—atha na dvyadhiṣṭhātṛkā ghaṭādayaḥ kiṃ tvekaikakulālādyadhiṣṭhā
tṛkāḥ sarve | tataśca neśvarāntarā'numānaṃ na cā'navastheti | nirākaroti—na
[p.159]
rathāṅgādyavayavā nānātakṣanirmitā api dṛśyante jagati prāya upakāryopa
kārakāḥ, evaṃ ca rathāṅgādyekatakṣapūrvakaṃ dṛṣṭam | tasmājjagadapyekakartṛpūrvaka
miti | tadetad vyabhicāri | nānātakṣapūrvakasyāpi darśanāt |
hetvabhāvapratikṣiptaṃ na kāryyamanumīyate |
heturna cetanāmātraṃ prekṣā tvanupapattikā ||
na hi karmmāśayā'bhāve tasya śarīrendriyayogo buddhiraiśvaryyaṃ vā sambha
vatīti pramāṇaviruddhastadupagamaḥ | hetvabhāve kāryyā'nupapatteḥ | upapattau vā
na saṃniveśāttadanumānam | karmmāśayapūrvakatve vā tajjñānamanyataḥ syāt |
sarvajñatva siddhiḥ | yadi hi sarvakāryāṇāmekaḥ karttā syāttato'jñasya tattvā
'nupapatteḥ sarvajñatā syāt | atha punarekaikakāryamekaikena karttā janyata iti yo
yajjanayati sa tatkāraṇamātrajña eveti na sarvajña ityarthaḥ |
yadapi kaścit pramāṇayet yatparasparopakāryopakārakatvena vyavasthitaṃ
tadekakartṛkaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ, yathā rathā'vayavā ekatakṣapūrvakā dṛṣṭāḥ, tathā ca tanu
bhuvanāni |
atrāpi samasti kathaṃcit parasparopakāryopakārakabhāva ityatrāha
rathādyavayavā nānātakṣanirmāpitā api dṛśyante jagati prāya upakāryo
pakārakāḥ | sarva jagat parasparopakāryopakārakaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ, tataḥ kimevaṃvidhaṃ
rathāṅgādyekatakṣapūrvakaṃ dṛṣṭam ? tasmājjagadapyekakartṛpūrvakaṃ tathā ca jagatkarttā
sarvajña iti |
dūṣayati—tadetad vyabhicāri | kutaḥ ? nānātakṣapūrvakatvasyāpi
darśanāt |
hetvabhāvapratikṣiptaṃ na kāryamanumīyate |
heturna cetanāmātraṃ prekṣā tvanupapattikā ||
īśvarasya kleśakarmavipākāśayā'parāmarśā'bhyupagamāt | na hi karmā'
'śayā'bhāve tasya śarīrendriyādiyogo buddhiraiśvaryaṃ vā sambhavatīti pramāṇa
viruddhastadupagamaḥ | pramāṇavirodhamāha—hetvabhāve kāryā'nupapatteḥ | na hyasti
sambhavo vahnirnāsti samasti ca dhūma iti | karmāśayahetavaśca śarīrādayastada
bhāve na bhavitumīśate ityarthaḥ | upapattau vā hetvabhāve kāryotpādasya
bhavatpakṣe'pi na saṃniveśāt tasya buddhimato'numānaṃ, kāraṇamantareṇāpi
kāryotpatteḥ | yastu kaścidvaiyātyādīśvare'pi karmā''śayaparāmarśamabhyupagacchet
[p.160]
nanvata eva hetvabhāve kāryyā'bhāvāt tadanumānam ? anyathā dūṣaṇamapi na
syāt | vaktavyo vā viśeṣo yato dūṣaṇaṃ na sādhanamiti | ucyate | pratyakṣeṇa
vilakṣaṇā hyapratyayapūrvāḥ sanniveśā ataddhetavaḥ syuḥ | na caivaṃ buddhiranu
meyā, dṛṣṭasālakṣaṇyenā'numānāt | api ca tulyatve'pi pratibandhamātrād dūṣaṇaṃ
sādhanamapi, sādhanaṃ tu nirastapratipakṣaṃ sādhyasiddhāviti | na ca buddhimātraṃ
taṃ pratyāha—karmāśayapūrvakatve vā tajjñānamanyataḥ syāt yena karmmāśayenā
'sya śarīrādayo janayitavyāḥ sa tāvadavijñāto'nadhiṣṭhitaśca cakṣurādyupa
jananāya na paryāpta iti jñātavyaḥ | na ca tajjanyena śarīrādinā, itaretarā
'śrayaprasaṅgāt na ca prāgbhavīyena śarīrāntarādinā | janmasamaye tasyā'ti
vṛttatvāt | na ceśvarāntaramabhyupeyate paraiḥ | tadupagame vā tatrāpi durvāraḥ
prasaṅgaḥ | codayati—nanvata eva hetvabhāve kāryā'bhāvādeva tasya buddhima
tastanubhuvanādihetoranumānam | anyathā yadi hetvabhāve'pi bhavetkāryaṃ tato
dūṣaṇamapi hetvabhāvapratikṣiptaṃ na kāryamanumīyate ityetat na syāt |
vaktavyo vā viśeṣastādṛśaḥ, yato dūṣaṇaṃ na tu sādhanamiti | ubhāvapi hi
sādhanadūṣaṇavādinau kāraṇā'bhāve na kāryamiti vaktuṃ pravṛttau | tat kathama
nenaikaḥ paryanuyujyate ? vadanti hi vṛddhāḥ yaścobhayoḥ pakṣayordoṣo na tatraika
ścodyo bhavatīti |
svapakṣe viśeṣamādarśayan pariharati—ucyate | pratyakṣavilakṣaṇā
hyapratyayapūrvakāḥ kṣityādisaṃniveśāḥ ataddhetavo'pratyayavaddhetavaḥ syuḥ | atha
yathā prāsādādīnāṃ saṃniveśāḥ pratyakṣeṇa vilakṣaṇāḥ kṣityādisanniveśebhya
upalabhyante tathā buddhiralaukikebhyo vilakṣaṇā anumānādupalabhyata ityata
āha—na caivaṃ buddhiranumeyā | kutaḥ ? dṛṣṭasālakṣaṇyenā'numānāt | etaduktaṃ
bhavati | pratyakṣamindriyajanmanidarśananirapekṣamupajāyamānamadṛṣṭacaramapi
trailokyavilakṣaṇamapyarthaṃ svalakṣaṇaṃ gocarayituṃ pārayatyeva | anumānaṃ vyutpattya
pekṣitayā vyutpatteśca pūrvadarśanā'dhīnatayā dṛṣṭasalakṣaṇameva gocarayenna tu
vilakṣaṇam | adṛṣṭaliṅgasambandhaṃ hi tat | tadiha buddhiranumīyamānā yathā
darśanamanumīyeta | sā cā'nityā śarīrātmamanaḥsaṃyogahetuḥ | asarvaviṣayopa
labdhā'smadādāviti tadvidhaivā'numātavyā | tathā ca nityatvasarvaviṣayatvā
divyāhatiriti | na caivaṃ dahanādyanumāneṣu prasaṅgaḥ | na khalu tatra mahānasā
didṛṣṭadahanavilakṣaṇaḥ parvatanitambavartī dahane'numīyate | na ca deśabhedasta
[p.161]
sanniveśaheturvinyāsaprayojanavicāranirṇayātmikā prekṣā | na ca sā tatra
sambhavati, svārthaparārthā'bhāvāditi | nanu mā bhūtsarvajño niyoktā, dharmmajña
stvivaṣyatām | na hi rūpataścakṣurādīnāṃ viṣayaniyamo nirūpyata ityuktam |
kālatastu niyame yadā varttamānatvaṃ tadā'tiśayavaccakṣurādiviṣayabhāvo na 
viruddhaḥ | ātyantike cā 'varttamānatve'tyantā'sattvaṃ khapuṣpādivadityuktam |
dvailakṣaṇyāya paryāptaḥ, anyatvāt | kathaṃ tarhi rūpādyupalabdherindriyā'numānam ?
na hi tatra daṇḍādikaraṇasālakṣaṇyaṃ, rūpādiparicchittikaraṇānāmindriyāṇā
manumānā'gocaratvāt | rūpādiparicchittyanyathānupapattiprabhavā khalviyamarthā
pattirindriyagocarā, sā ca sambandhadarśananirapekṣayā na dṛṣṭasālakṣaṇyama
pekṣate | na caivaṃ buddhimatpūrvakatve'pyarthāpatti, ranyathā'nupapadyamānasya kasya
cidapyadarśanāt | tanubhuvanādeścā'dṛṣṭavatkṣetrajñaparamāṇusaṃyogādutpatteritya
sakṛdāveditam | tadevamasti sādhanadūṣaṇasya viśeṣaḥ |
api cā'viśeṣe'pi na vaitaṇḍikaḥ śakyo nirākarttuṃ yaḥ svapakṣasthā
panāhīnaṃ parapakṣamāhantītyāha—api ca tulyatve pratibandhamātrād dūṣaṇaṃ
sādhanamapi sapratipakṣamapi | sādhanaṃ tu nirastapratipakṣaṃ sādhyasiddhau sādhana
tāyāmavatiṣṭhate na tu sapratipakṣaṃ pratibandhamātrāditi |
tadevaṃ pūrvārddha kārikāyā vyākhyāya paścārddhaṃ vyācaṣṭe—na ca buddhimātraṃ
sanniveśahetuḥ, api tu vinyāsaprayojanayoryau vicāranirṇayau tadātmikā
prekṣābuddhiviśeṣa iti yāvat | prekṣaiva tarhi bhavatu bhagavatīśvare, ityata āha—na
ca sā ca sambhavati, svārthaparārthā'bhāvāt darśitaśca tadabhāvaḥ purastāt |
sampratyanyathā pratyavatiṣṭhate paraḥ—nanu mā bhūtsarvajño niyoktā
pratyakṣeṇa, dharmajñastviṣyatāṃ, yathāha—
heyopādeyatattvasya sābhyupāyasya vedakaḥ |
yaḥ pramāṇamasāviṣṭo na tu sarvasya vedakaḥ ||
kīṭasaṅkhyāparijñānaṃ tasya naḥ kvopayujyate |
dūraṃ paśyatu vā mā tattvamiṣṭaṃ tu paśyati ||
pramāṇaṃ dūradarśī ced va yaṃ gṛdhrānupāsmahe || ityādi |
nanvayaṃ dharmo rūpapiśeṣā'bhāvāt kathaṃ pratyayapūrva ? ityata āha—
na hi rūpavataścakṣurādīnāṃ viṣayaniyamo nirūpyata ityuktam | nanvetadapyuktaṃ
[p.162]
tatra kurvityartho dharmmaḥ, yāgāderviṣayasya dharmmatvenopagamāta | utpādyatvātta
syā'pratyakṣatvamiti cet | utpannasyā'stu | nanūtpannamapi svarūpeṇa dharmaḥ,
śreyaḥ sādhanatvena tu taccā'tīndriyam | kiṃ punaridaṃ śreyaḥ sādhanatvam ? yadi
tāvacchaktiḥ sā cā'tīndriyeti | uktamatra nendriyāṇāṃ parimāṇato vā 
viṣayaniyamaḥ sunirūpitaḥ | atha tadā'sattvāt | kālāntarabhāvitvāt
śreyasaḥ | bhavatu, anyathā dharmatvahāneḥ | śreyaḥsādhanaṃ tu varttamānamiti
varttamānakālaviṣayameva cakṣurādīti | na ca dharmo vartamāna ityata āha—
kālatastu niyame yadā dharmmasya varttamānatvaṃ tadā'tiśayavaccakṣurādiviṣaya
bhāvo na viruddhaḥ | yadyucyeta na kadācidapyayaṃ vartamāna iti, tatrāha—
ātyantike cā'varttamānatve atyantā'sattvaṃ khapuṣpādivat ityuktam |
nanu kurviti niyogo dharmaḥ, na caiṣa vartamānaḥ | karoṣītyaviśeṣa
prasaṅgāt | vartamānā'vasthastu na niyoga iti na dharma ityata āha—tatra
kurvityartho dharmmaḥ | kasmāt ? yāgāderniyogaviṣayasya dharmmatvopagamāttatraiva
dharmmatvaprasiddheḥ | śaṅkate—utpādyatvādavartamānatvāttasyāpyapratyakṣatvamiti
cet | nirākaroti—utpannasyāstu | punaḥ śaṅkate—utpannamapi yāgādi na
svarūpeṇa dharmmaḥ, śreyaḥsādhanatvena tu taccātīndriyam | tadetad dūṣayituṃ
vikalpayati—kiṃ punaridaṃ śreyaḥ sādhanatvaṃ ? yadi tāvacchaktiḥ sā cā'tīndri
yeti | dūṣayati—uktamatra nendriyāṇāṃ parimāṇato vā viṣayaniyamastu
nirūpita iti | atha sādhanasya śreyasā saṃbandhaḥ śreyaḥsādhanatvaṃ, kṛttaddhita
samāseṣu saṃbandhābhidhānaṃ tvatalbhyāmiti vārttikakāravacanāt | saṃbandhaśca
saṃbandhidvayā'dhīnanirūpaṇo nā'ntareṇa saṃbandhinā śakyo nirūpayitumityasati
śreyasi kālāntarabhāvini kathaṃ tatmabandho yāgādīnāmpratyakṣamīkṣyeteti
śaṅkate—atha tadā yāgādisamaye'sattvāt | kuto'sattvami?tyata āha kālā
ntarabhāvitvādāmuṣmikasya svargādeḥ śreyasaḥ | uttaram—bhavatu kālāntarabhāvi
śreyaḥ, tatsādhanatvaṃ tvatiśayo yāgādisamaveto yaḥ sa tatkālo yo yāgādi
kālaḥ | na khalu bhinnakālayorādhārā''dheyabhāvaḥ sambhavati | tatsambhave
tattvameva kutaḥ ? ityata āha—anyathā'samavāyādatiśayasya yāgādīnāma
śreyaḥ sādhanatvāddharmmatvahāneḥ | tasmādyadā tadā bhavatu, śreyaḥ sādhanatvaṃ tu varta
mānamiti nā'kṣāṇāmaviṣayo dharmmaḥ śreyaḥsādhanarūpaḥ |
[p.163]
nā'kṣāṇāmaviṣayaḥ | nanu prāganuṣṭhānāddharmajñānamiṣyate'nuṣṭhātṛbhiḥ | tathā
ca bhaviṣyadvā 'kṣāṇāmaviṣayaḥ | naivā'dhiṣṭhātṝṇāṃ pratyakṣaṃ dharme brūmaḥ, api
tūpadeṣṭuratiśayavataḥ | sa cā'tiśayaḥ svābhāviko mahattvamiva vyomna
upadeśāntarajaḥ kriyānibandhano vetyadoṣaḥ |
ucyate—
atulyakālaṃ sāmarthyaṃ karmmaṇaḥ kṣaṇabhaṅginaḥ |
cirakālaphalāyā''hurna tadadhyakṣamīkṣate ||
pralīyamānakarmmalabdhopajanaḥ | pūrvā'parāṅgopapāditā'vasthāntaro deśa-
etaduktaṃ bhavati | bhavatu nāma śreyaḥsādhanatvaśabdaḥ saṃbandhavacano na
punarddharmmaśabdo'pi śreyaḥsādhana eva | tasya laukikaparīkṣakairavivādaṃ prayo
gāt | atiśayavacca yāgādyeva tatsādhanamiti ta eva dharmmapadāspadam | te ca
tadbhājo vartamānā iti teṣāṃ tadvatāṃ pratyakṣateti |
codayati—nanu prāganuṣṭhānāt dharmmajñānamiṣyate'nuṣṭhātṛbhistajjñā
nasyā'nuṣṭhānā'ṅgatvāt | tathā ca bhaviṣyadvā'kṣāṇāmaviṣayaḥ | pariharati—
naivā'nuṣṭhātṛṇāmasmadādīnāṃ pratyakṣaṃ dharmaṃ brūmaḥ, kiṃ tūpadeṣṭuratiśayavataḥ | yaḥ
khalu pareṇā'nuṣṭhīyamānaṃ yāgādipratyakṣeṇa śreyaḥsādhanaṃ paśyati | kutaḥ punarasyā
'yamatiśaya ? ityata āha sa cā'syātiśayaḥ svābhāviko mahattvamiva
vyomna upadeśāntarajaḥ kriyānibandhano vā kasyavidāptopadeśādaṣṭāṅga
yogamavagamya tadanuṣṭhānāttatparipāke sati tāṃ tāṃ yogabhūmimadhirūḍhasya saṃprajñāta
samādhiprabhāvādevā'yamatiśayo jāyate | tasyā'cintanīyasāmarthyatvāt |
yathāha—ko hi yogaprabhāvādṛte agastya iva samudraṃ pivatu, sa iva daṇḍakā
'raṇyaṃ sṛjatīti | so'yamubhayavidho'tiśaya upāyapratyaya upeyapratyayaścetyā
khyāyate yogācāryairiti | tasmādadoṣaḥ |
tadevaṃ pratyavasthitaṃ paraṃ nirākaroti—ucyate |
atulyakālaṃ sāmarthya karmmaṇaḥ kṣaṇabhaṅginaḥ ||
cirakālaphalāyāhu rna tadadhyakṣamīkṣate |
tadetad vyācaṣṭe—āśutaravināśitvāt karmaṇaḥ pralīyamānakarmma
labdhopajanaḥ | karmā''nantaryamapi tasya nāsti, kimaṅga punastatkālatetyāha —
[p.164]
kālādyāsāditapariṇatibheda ānantaryamapi karmmaṇo'tivartamānaḥ ko'pyati
śayaḥ karttari karmmaṇaścirabhāvinaḥ phalasya sādhanaṃ kathyate | tadasau na
karmmakāle vartamānaḥ | svakāle vartamāno'pi karmmaṇo'tivṛttatvānna karma
sambandhitayā'dhyakṣamīkṣate | taduktaṃ 
phalasādhanarūpeṇa tadānīṃ yena
nāstyasāviti | karmmakāle tasya bhāvitvaṃ tatkāle ca karmmaṇo'bhāvaḥ |
kathaṃ tarhi karmmaṇaḥ sādhanatvaṃ tadanyasamavāyi ? tadāhitatvāt | na hi sva
samavāyyeva sādhanaṃ bhavatīti kaścinniyamaḥ | kāryyā'numeyaṃ tadyatra kāryyāyā'laṃ
tatraiva yuktaṃ tadāhitatvāttasya śaktiḥ | na caupacārikaḥ karmmaṇaḥ sādhana-
pūrvaparāṅgā'gnyanvādhānabrahmaṇatarppaṇādinā pūrvopapāditā'vasthāntaraḥ |
etāvatā'pyasau nā'tiśayaḥ, api tu deśakālādyavasthādyapekṣā''sādita
pariṇatibhedaḥ | so'yamuktakāraṇakrama ānantaryakarmaṇo'tivarttamānaḥ kiṃ punaḥ
samānakālatāṃ ko'pi karmmaṇo'tiśayaḥ | nanu nirādhārasya kāryasyā'nupapatte
rutpattidharmmā kimādhāro'yamatiśaya ? ityata āha—kartari nityātmani |
tat kiṃ kartṛsthaḥ karttureva na kiṃ tu karmmaṇaḥ nanu karmaiva phalāya coditaṃ
tatsādhayatu kimantargarbhiṇā'pūrveṇe ? tyata āha—cirabhāvinaḥ phalasya sādhanaṃ
kathyate vṛddhaiḥ | na hi ciravinaṣṭaṃ karmā'nupajanitā'tiśayabhedaṃ cirabhāvine
phalāya kalpate ityarthaḥ bhavatvevaṃ prakṛte kimāyātami?tyata āha—tadasau
na karmmakāle varttamānaḥ | atha sarvadaiva kimavarttamānaḥ ? tathā ca gagana
kamalinīkalpaḥ prasajyetetyata āha—svakāle varttamāno'pi | atha mā bhūdasau
karmakāle, tatkāle, tu karmma kasmāt ? ityata āha—āśutaravināśitayā
tatkāle karmaṇo'tivṛttatvāt | tasmātkarmatadatiśayayorasamānakālatayā
'tiśayavato'tyantā'varttamānatayā vartamānaviṣayaniyataṃ pratyakṣaṃ na tadgocarayi
yitumarhatītyāha—na karmasaṃbandhitayā'dhyakṣamīkṣate | taduktaṃ phalasādhanarūpeṇa
tadānīṃ yena nāstyasāviti | tadeva sphuṭayati-karmmakāle tasyā'tiśayasya
uktakālakramasya bhāvitvaṃ tatkāle ca karmaṇo'bhāvaḥ |
codayati—kathaṃ tarhi karmmaṇaḥ sādhanatvaṃ tadapūrvamanyasamavāyi ?
pariharati—tadāhitatvāt | na hi svasamavāyyeva sādhanamiti kaści
nniyamaḥ | nanu tathāpyātmasamavāyitvādātmana eva kasmānna bhavatī ? tyāha—
kāryā'numeyaṃ tadapūrvaṃ yatra ca kāryāyā'laṃ tatraiva yuktam | ayamarthaḥ | yadi hi
tadrūpamanyanirapekṣameva gamyate tato yatpratyāsannamavagamyate tasya syāt yathā
ghaṭasya śauklyaṃ, na tvetattathā, api tu karmmaṇi karaṇatvena phalāya codite
[p.165]
bhāvaḥ | kāṣṭhādiṣu tathaiva siddheḥ | nanu na tadastīti manyante | pramāṇā'
bhāvāt | na ca śrutā'rthāpattiḥ pramāṇam | phalasambandhe codanāprāmāṇyavi
ghātāt | asiddho hi sa tadā codanāto'rthāpattitaḥ siddhiṃ samāsādaya
tītyarthāpattikāraṇaḥ syāt | anyathā karmmaphalasambandhasiddherarthāpattiprasaṅgaḥ |
siddhe tasminkimanyadapekṣya tadupakalpyate ? na hi nirapekṣā | apekṣayāmapi
yāgaphalasambandhapratītāvarthāntaraṃ cedapekṣeta kathaṃ tadapratipattau tatpratipattiḥ ?
tadupapattyaitatkalpyamānaṃ pratipattidaśāyāṃ karmmasaṃbaddhamevā'vagatam | na hyanya
saṃbaddhametatphalasaṃbandhamupapādayati karmmaṇaḥ | tathāpratīyamānamapi cetkarmmā
'pahāyā'nyena saṃbadhyeta svarūpādeva hīyeteti karmmaṇyeva tat | nanu prathamaṃ
pratīyatāṃ nāma karmmasaṃbandhaḥ, vāstavastasya tena saṃbandho vaktavyaḥ | itarathā
tatpratītermithyātvaprasaṅgādityata āha—tena karmmaṇā''hitatvāttasya karmmaṇaḥ
śaktiḥ |
aparaḥ kalpaḥ—kasmādanyasamavāyi tadapūrvaṃ na tu karmmasamavāyi
kalpyate ? ityata āha—kāryā'numeyaṃ tattadyatra kāryāyā'laṃ tatraiva yuktaṃ,
na khalu tadāśutaravināśi karmmādhāraṃ tadvināśe vinaṣṭamāmuṣmikāya phalāya
kalpate | tasmānnityātmasamavāyītyarthaḥ | yadyātmasamavāyi tattasyaiva śaktiḥ
na karmmaṇa ityata āha—tadāhitatvāttasyaiva śaktiḥ | na cetthamaupacārikaḥ
karmmaṇaḥ sādhanabhāvaḥ, kāṣṭhādiṣu tathaiva siddheḥ | na khalu kāṣṭhādīnāmapi
sākṣādvyaktisādhanatā, kiṃ tvauṣṇyā'pekṣasya vahnisaṃyogapracayabhedasya | atha ca
kāṣṭhaiḥ pacantīti mukhyaiva sādhanatā kāṣṭhānāmagnitanḍulasamavāyyapi vahni
saṃyogastatpūrva iti tathā yāgādīnāmapi | kṛtopapādanaṃ caitad niyoga
parīkṣāyām |
nanu na tadapūrvamastīti manyante devānāṃ priyāḥ | kutaḥ ? pramāṇā
'bhāvāt | na ca śrutā'rthāpattirevā'tra pramāṇamiti kathaṃ pramāṇā'bhāvaḥ ?
ityata āha—na ca śrutā'rthāpattiḥ pramāṇam | kasmāt ? karmmaṇaḥ phala
saṃbandhe codanāprāmāṇyavighātāt | asiddho hi sa tadā'pūrvakalpanāyāḥ
prāvakarmmaphalasaṃmbandhaścodanāto'rthāpattito'pūrvakalpanāyāṃ satyāṃ siddhi
māsādayatīti tadbhāvabhāvādarthāpattikāraṇo'rthāpattipramāṇakaḥ syāt | anyathā
'napekṣācchabdāt karmaphalasaṃbandhasiddhereva hetorarthāpattiprasaṅgaḥ | uttaram siddhe
sati karmaphalasaṃbandhe tatpratītyā kimanyadapūrva vinā'nupapadyamānamapekṣya tadapūrva
mupakalpyate ? na hyatyathā'nupapadyamāne nirapekṣā pratītiḥ kalpanāyai prabhavati |
yadyucyeta yāgaphalasambandhapratītireva nopapadyate kimanyenā'nupapadyamāneneti ?
[p.166]
nā'pratipannapadārtho vākyārtho yataḥ pramāṇāntaraṃ cet apekṣate anapekṣatvaṃ
vyāhanyeta | api ca | śrutaṃ vākyamanupapannaṃ sā'pekṣatvāt viśvajitā
yajete tivat adhyāhāryyanirdeśāt | artho vā'nupapannaḥ | pūrvatra vākyaśeṣa
kalpanā | na cā'nupapattiḥ | svargakāmo yajete ti parisamāptervākyaśeṣā
'nupapatteścā'pūrvaṃ kṛtveti prāgyāgādapūrvotpattiprasaṅgaḥ | yāgenā'pūrvaṃ kṛtveti
dviruccāraṇaṃ yajeḥ, pūrvaśca doṣaḥ | uttaratrāpi śrutatayā cedanupapannaḥ, pūrva eva
vikalpaḥ | svabhāvāntareṇā'nupapattau śrutasiddhyarthamapūrvakalpanetyasamīcīnam |
tatrāha—apekṣāyāmapi yāgaphalasaṃbandhapratītau arthāntaraṃ cetsvotpattāvapakṣeta,
tadanupapannam, kathaṃ hi tasyā'rthāntarasyā'pratipattau tatkarmaphalasaṃbandhasiddheḥ
pratītiḥ | nā'pratipannapadārtho vākyārtho yataḥ pramāṇāntaraṃ cedapekṣate na
tvarthāntaraṃ tatrāha—anapekṣatvaṃ vyāhanyeta | api cā'nupapattirapi ki
vākyasyā'tha tadarthasya ? iti vikalpayati api ca śrutaṃ vākyamanupapannaṃ
sā'pekṣatvāt |
kutaḥ sā'pekṣatvam ? ityata āha—viśvajitā yajetetivadadhyāhārya
nikṣepāt svargakāmadasya vākyekadeśasyā'dhyāhāryatvaṃ tatsamudāye vā vākye
upacaritamadhyāhāro vā'syāstīti adhyāhāri svargakāmadaṃ, tasyā'nirdeśātsā
pekṣatvamiti dvitīyapakṣamāha—artho vā'nupapannaḥ | evaṃ vikalpya dūṣayati—
pūrvatra vākyaśeṣaparikalpanā syāt | na hi vākyamaparipūrṇamatajjātīyenā'rthena
pūrayituṃ śakyamāpa tu śabdenaiveti laukikīsthitiḥ | tadetadvākyaṃ nopapadyata
ityaṅgīkṛtyoktam | paramārthatastu—vā'nupapattiḥ | kutaḥ ? darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ
svargakāmo yajeteti padaiḥ svārthā'bhidhānadvāreṇa vākyārthapratīteḥ parisamāpteḥ |
yaśca vṛddhairvākyaśeṣaḥ parikalpyate'pūrva kṛtveti so'pyanupapanna ityāha—
vākyaśeṣā'nupapatteścā'pūrva kṛtvetyapūrvakatvāt | svargakāmo yajeteti hi
vākyādayamarthaḥ pratīyate yena kenacidapūrvaṃ kṛtvā yajeta svargakāma iti | tathā
sati prāgyāgādapūrvotpattiprasaṅgaḥ | yadi punarapūrvakaraṇe'pi yāgeneti
sambadhyeta tataḥ phale'pi tatsaṃbandhanīyamiti dviruccāraṇaṃ yajeḥ pūrvaśca doṣaḥ |
na tāvadyenaiva yāgenā'pūrva kriyate tenaiva paścātphalamapi, karmmaṇo viramya
vyāpārā'nupapatteḥ | arthādyāgāntareṇa | tathā ca prāgyāgātphalasādhanādapūrvo
tpattiprasaṅga iti pūrvoktadoṣaḥ | uttaratrāpi ca śrutatayā cedanupapanno
vākyārthaḥ pūrva eva vikalpaḥ | tena saha tulyadoṣatvāt | tenātrāpi ca
śābdatayaivārthāntarakalpanīyaṃ yataḥ | yadi tu tattvaṃ parityajya svabhāvāntareṇā
[p.167]
'nupapattirāsthīyeta tatrāha—svabhāvāntareṇā'nupapattau śrutasiddhyarthamapūrvasya
kalpaneti vṛddhavacanamasamīcīnaṃ syāt | so'yaṃ devānāṃpriyaiḥ praṇīto
dūṣaṇagaṇo devacūḍāmaṇibhireva pariharaṇoya ityācāryeṇāpi nodadhāri,
tatra ke vayaṃ te tava mameti tatropakṣīṇamativibhavā iti |
api ca pauruṣeyatve sati vedānāṃ sarvajñapraṇītatayā tadanuvidhāyināṃ
tebhyaḥ pratipattirbhavedapi, na punarasti pauruṣeyatā | nanu vākyamātraṃ puruṣā
'dhīnavacanam | tathā hi durbhaṇatvena varṇā'nupūrvyabhedasaṃbandhena viśiṣṭena vā
viṣayā''nayanādikāryeṇa samānātmānaḥ pauruṣeyā api padakramāsteṣu teṣu
bauddhādyāgameṣvapi pratipannapauraṣeyatveṣu dṛśyante | yacca yasmādabhinnasvabhāvaṃ
na tattato bhinnahetukaṃ yathaikasmātkalamā'ṅkurādabhinnasvabhāvaṃ kalamāṅkurāntaraṃ
tatsamānahetukaṃ, tathā laukikībhyaḥ padānupūrvībhyo'bhidyamānasvabhāvā vaidikāḥ
padasaṃniveśā iti svabhāvahetuḥ | bhinnakaraṇatvāddhi svaviruddhabhinnasvabhāvatva
vyāptātsvabhāvaviruddhopalabdhānniḥsvabhāvatvaṃ vyāvarttamānaṃ kāraṇā'bhedena
vyāpyata iti pratibandhasiddhiḥ |
atrocyate | kiṃ vedavākyānāṃ puruṣapraṇītatvamātra sādhyate āho
svit svatantrapuruṣapraṇītatvami?ti | pūrvasmin siddhasādhanam | na khalu
vayamapi nityatve'pi varṇānāmānupūrvīviśeṣopahitasīmapadamabhedavākyānāma
pauruṣeyatā mā''cakṣmahe | tathā hi | varṇānāṃ nityatayā vibhutayā ca deśataḥ
kālato vā na saṃbhavatyānupūrvyamityabhivyaktyāśrayaṃ tadvaktavyam | sā ca puruṣa
prayatnayoniriti kathaṃ tadbhedopahitamaryādānāṃ gavādipadānāṃ vedavākyānāṃ
ca pauruṣeyate ? ti | atha svatantrapuruṣapraṇītatvaṃ mā niṣādādivākyavat |
prācetasaḥ khalvatrabhavānādikaviḥ svatantraḥ, tadvadvedavākyeṣvapi kaścido
śvarādiriti sāmpratam | anaikāntikatvāt | svatantraprācetasa puruṣapraṇī
tatvā'bhāvāt |
syādetat | na svatantrapuruṣapraṇītatvaṃ vedavākyānāṃ sādhayāmaḥ, api
tu svatantrapuruṣapūrvakatvam | asti cā'smadādipuruṣapraṇāteṣvapi māniṣādā
dipadasaṃniveśeṣu pāramparyeṇa purāṇasya kaveḥ svātantryamiti nānekānta iti |
tadanupapannam | puruṣapraṇītatāmātreṇa kāraṇā'bhedena laukikātpadasaṃniveśā
dvaidikānāmabhedopapattāvapratītasya vedeṣu puruṣasvātantryasyā'prayojanakasya
[p.168]
kalpanānupapatteḥ tatsamānasanniveśatvasya puruṣapāratantryeṇā''tyantike nāsti
kaścidvirodhaḥ | yena tadvipakṣādātyantikapāratantryādvyāvarttamānaṃ puruṣa
svātantryā'prayojanakasya kalpanā'nupapatteḥ | tatsamānasanniveśe ca puruṣa
pāratantryeṇā''tyantikena nāsti kaścidvirodhaḥ | yena tadvipakṣādātyantika
pāratantryādvyāvartamānaṃ puruṣasvātantryeṇa vyāpyeta | tena yathā samānamaitrī
janmasvapi maitrītanayeṣvannapānaviśeṣopayoganimittā śyāmatā keṣāṃcideva
na sarveṣāmiti tattanayatayā na śyāmatā'numānamevaṃ vilakṣaṇaprayatnasthānā
'bhighātādijanmavākyaniṣpattestadbhedā'bhedā'nuvidhāyibhedā'bhedo yujyate na
punaḥ puruṣapāratantrye tantrīkartumutsahate | so'yaṃ sandigdhavyatirekatayā'taikāntiko
hetvābhāsaḥ |
yastu darśanakathantāsaṃbodharahito yathādarśanapūjakaḥ pramāṇayet dṛṣṭāni
hi bhāratādivākyāni svatantrapuruṣapraṇītānīti vedavākyānyapi vākya
tvāttathāvidhānīti, sa vaktavyaḥ, vivādādhyāsitaṃ vedā'dhyayanamadhyanāntara
pūrvakaṃ vedādhyayanatvādasmadādivedādhyayanavat | naivaṃ bhāratādiṣu asyā'nu
mānam | tatra dṛḍhatareṇa kṛṣṇadvaipāyanādismaraṇenā'numānasya bādhena vidheya
tvādanutpādāt | vede tu kartṛsmṛtidraḍhimā'sambhavātkāṭhakādisamākhyātasya
pravacanamiti vacanopapatterabādhitaviṣayasyā'numānasyā'pratyūhamutpatteḥ | na ca
bhavadanumānamasya viṣayabādhe prabhavati | tasya sāmānyatodṛṣṭasaṃbandhātsā
mānyaśāstrasyaiva viśeṣaśāstrād durbalatvāt | tathāha—
prasiddhā'vayavaṃ yatsyādviśeṣe vā pratiṣṭhitam |
tatsādhyāṅgakasāmānyadṛṣṭāttu balavattaram || iti |
syādetat | śabdānāmarthapratyāyanasya puruṣasaṅketā'dhīnajanmatvātta
tsambandhā kenacillaukikasaṃvyavahārārtha śabdānāmarthaiḥ sambandhān kṛtvā 
vedaḥ praṇītaḥ | na cā'rthapratyāyanaṃ prati śabdānāṃ svābhāvikī śaktiḥ saṅketena
vyajyata iti sāmpratam | yadi hi satyapi saṅkate kaścid dhvaniḥ kaṃcanā'rtha
nā'vabhāsayet tataḥ kasyacit saṅketā'bhivyaktasāmarthyasya svābhāvikamupa
padyeta vācakatvaṃ, na tvetadasti | saṅketamantareṇa dhvanerartharūpasyā'pratipāda
katvāt | sati tu saṅkete sarvasmādapi sarvārthapratyayotpattidarśanāt | tada
yuktam | antareṇāpi saṅketamanādivṛddhavyavahāradarśanādabhivyaktasāmarthyebhyaḥ
śabdebhyo'rthapratipatterupapatteḥ | sambandhakalpanāyāmarthāpattiparikṣayāt | na ca
vyavahāradarśanameva saṅkoca iti sāmpratam | na khalvetadanena śabdenā'yamarthaḥ
[p.169]
ucyate—
upadeśo niyojyārthakarmmāprasthitacodanā |
prathito guruvaidyādau nitye'pi na na kalpate ||
yadyapyājñā'bhyarthanā vede'nupapannāḥ, upadeśastu yujyate | so'pi
tadvadeva preraṇātmakaścaturtho loke prajñāyate | tathā hi—ājñā'bhyarthane hi
pratyetavya ityevamākāram, api tu śabdoccāraṇasamanantarapravṛttinivṛtti
harṣādyābhāsam | tacca svapravṛttyādinibandhanā'rthabhedaviṣayaprakhyā'numāna
 dvāreṇa śabdasya tatra kāraṇatāyāṃ pramāṇam | na ca vyutpadyamānāstadā saṅketaṃ
saṅketasya karttāraṃ vā pratiyanti | na ceha saṅketaṃ tasya ca karttāramantareṇa kasya
citparidṛśyamānasyā'nupapattiḥ | tato nūnaṃ svabhāvataḥ śabda evā'rthabhedapratyayasya
heturityadhyavasyati | na ca gavādiśabdānāmaśvādau devadattādiśabdānāṃ ca
tatra tatra saṅketā'dhīnatayā pravṛttergotvādāvapi gavādiśabdāḥ saṅketā''yatta
pravṛttaya iti śakyā'numānam | anādivṛddhavyavahāraparamparā'bhivyaktasvābhāvi
kā'rthapratyayahetubhāvānāṃ svārthavṛtteḥ saṅketā'bhāvena saha sambhavādavirodhāt |
yathādarśanapūjakaṃ prati tu śakyamevaṃ pratyavasthātum | vivādā'dhyāsitāḥ pumāṃso
gavādipadebhyo gotvādiṣu saṅketā'nāyattapratipattayo gavādipadebhyo gotvādi
pratipattimattvādasmadādivaditi | vipañcitaṃ caitadvṛddhairiti diṅmātramatra
sūcitaṃ, kṣantavyaṃ ca kiñcidudgranthā'bhidhānaṃ sūribhityuparamyate |
|| iti pūrvakaṇikā samāptā ||
siddhāntamupakramate—ucyate |
upadeśo niyojyārthakarmā'prasthitacodanā |
prathito guruvaidyādau nitye'pi ca na kalpate ||
tatra yattāvaduktam 'apauruṣeye praiṣādirnṛdharmo nā'vakalpata iti | tena
yadyājñā'bhyarthanā'nujñāḥ pratiṣidhyante tadanujānīmahe eva, upadeśapratiṣedhaṃ tu
na mṛṣyāmaha ityāha—yadyapyājñā'bhyarthanānujñā vede'nupapannāstasyā'pauruṣeyatvena
cetanasya niyokturabhāvāt | upadeśastu yujyate | tasyā'pauruṣeye'pi sambha
vāt | na hyasau niyojakārthakarmmeti vakṣyati, yena cetanakartṛkaḥ syāt | na
cāsau na laukiko'preraṇātmako vā, yenā'vidhiḥ syādityāha—so'pi
tadvadevā'jñāvadeva preraṇātmakaścaturtho loke prajñāyate | nanu ca yadi preraṇātmakaḥ
kathamājñādibhyo bhidyate ? bhidyate cetkathaṃ preraṇātmakaḥ ? ityata āha—
tathā hi | ājñā'bhyarthane hi niyoktrarthamanādṛtaniyojyaphalaṃ karma gocarayataḥ,
[p.170]
niyoktrarthamanādṛtaniyojyaphalaṃ karmmagocarayataḥ | niyojyārthaṃ tūpadeśaḥ |
anujñā tu yadyapyevaṃ kvacittathāpi pravṛttapuruṣaviṣayatvānnopadeśaḥ | niyojyā
rthakarmmagocaramapravṛttapravartanamupadeśamācakṣate dhīrāḥ | na hi gāmabhyāja,
māṇavakamadhyāpaya, kuru yathā'bhimatamityupadeśapratītiḥ | nāpi bhaikṣyaṃ caret,
jvaritaḥ pathyamaśnīyādityājñādi pratītiḥ | bhūyasā caiṣa pauruṣeyeṣu kāmārtha
śāstrādiṣvājñādibhiranārūṣito loke prajñāyate | gopālādivacaḥsu ca
mārgā''khyānapareṣu anena pathā gaccheti | na hi hīnasyājñānātmā rthe karmaṇya-
niyojyārthaṃ tu karmopadeśo gocarayati | tadanenaivopadeśo niyājyā'rthakarmeti
kārikārtho vivṛtaḥ |
etadukta bhavati | ājñā'bhyarthanopadeśāḥ karmaṇi pravṛttijananena tadgo
carayanto bhavanti preraṇātmatayā samānāsteṣāmājñā'bhyarthanābhyāṃ gocarī
kriyamāṇaṃ karmā'nādṛtaniyojyaprayojanamājñāpayiturabhyarthayamānasya vā prayoja
nāyā'vakalpate | upadeśagocarastu karmā'nādṛtopadeśakaprayojanamupadeṣṭa
vyārthamevetyayamājñā'bhyarthanābhyāmupadeśasya bhedaḥ preraṇātmakatvaṃ ceti niyojyārthaṃ
karma yasyopadeśasya na tu niyoktrartha sa tathokta ityakṣarayojanā |
nanu yadi niyojyārthakarmopadeśaḥ hantā'nujñā'pyupadeśaḥ prasajyeta |
so'pi hi kvacinniyojyā'rthakarmetyata āha—anujñā tu yadyapyevaṃ kvacittathāpi
pravṛttapuruṣaviṣayatvānnopadeśaḥ | niyoktrathakarmāpi anujñā bhavatyeva | tathā
hi | bhṛtyasya prabhave hitaṃ cikīrṣorapyasti hitakāmitayā sahasā kurvataḥ
prabhorabhyanujñeti kvacidgrahaṇam | anenā'prasthitacodaneti vivṛtam | aprasthi
tasyā'pravṛttasya puṃsaḥ prasthāpanā cādanetyakṣarayojanā | lokaprasiddho'yamīdṛśa
upadeśa iti kuta ? ityata āha—niyojyā'rthakarmagocaramapravṛttapravarttana
mupadeśamācakṣate dhīrāḥ | na cā''khyānamātramavagatirapyājñādivilakṣaṇopa
deśālambanā'stītyāha—na hi gāmānayetyājñāyāṃ māṇavakamadhyāpayetyabhyartha
nāyāṃ kuru yathehitamityanujñāyāmupadeśapratītiḥ | nāpi bhaikṣyaṃ carejjvaritaḥ
pathyamaśnoyādityājñādipratītiḥ | na cā'syopadeśasya kvacitkathaṃcidavagatiḥ,
bhūyasā prakāreṇa sarvatrā'vagaterityāha—bhūyasā caiva pauruṣeyeṣu kāmā'rtha
śāstrādiṣvājñādibhiranārūṣitā loke prajñāyate | śāstrakārā hi naisargika
vainayikabuddhyatiśayasaṃpannā laukikāḥ na kevalaṃ śāstreṣu nikṛṣṭabuddhivacaneṣva
pīti prasiddhataratvamāha—gopālādivacassu ca mārgā''khyānapareṣvanena pathā
gaccheti | athaitadājñādīnāmanyatamatkasmānna bhavati ? ityata āha—na hi
[p.171]
bhyarthanānujñānamapravṛttasya | ata eva copadeśaḥ | ityājñādibhyo bhedena tasya
pratītatvāttasya jñānamupadeśa iti tato bhinna upadeśaśabdena darśitaḥ | bhavatu
tato bhinnaḥ, kaḥ punarasau ? nanūktamupadeśa iti viśiṣṭasya śabdasyo
ccāraṇam | yadyevabhavyutpannasyāpi pravṛttiprasaṅgaḥ | ucyate—
viśiṣṭaḥ puruṣārthasya śuddhasyopāyamāha yaḥ |
puruṣārtho yadā yena yo nareṇā'bhikāṅkṣyate ||
puruṣārthasyopāyasanavagatamavagamayannutkarṣādviśiṣṭaḥ śabda uktaḥ |
anyathā sarva eva śabdaḥ śabdāntarād bhinna iti aviśeṣaṇameva syāt | ato
hīnasyā'jñānātmārthe karmaṇyabhyarthanā'nujñānamapravṛttasya | yata eva tad gopāla
vaco'ta eva prasaktānāmājñādīnāṃ pratiṣedhādanyatropākhyānādāvaprasaṅgācchi
ṣyamāṇe saṃpratyaya ityāha—ataevopadeśastata eva cā'yamapauruṣeye'pi
sambhavatīti satrakāreṇa darśita ityāha—iti tasmādājñādibhyo bhedena
labdhapratītitvāttasya jñānamupadeśa iti tata ājñādibhyo bhinna upadeśo
darśitaḥ | bhavatu tato bhinnaḥ, kaḥ punarasau ? samānā'samānajātīyavyavaccheda
kamasya vācyamityarthaḥ |
nanu coktaṃ bhāṣyakṛtā upadeśa iti viśiṣṭasyoccāraṇaṃ viśiṣṭasyeti
vadatā śabdasya rūpagato viśeṣa uktaḥ | sa ca pratyakṣaśabdavarttitayā pratyakṣa
iti prathamaśrāviṇo'pi pravṛttiprasaṅga ityāha—yadyevamavyutpannasyāpi pravatti
prasaṅgaḥ | na pratyakṣa vyutpattimapekṣata ityarthaḥ | ucyate—
viśiṣṭaḥ puruṣārthasya śuddhasyopāyamāha yaḥ |
puruṣārtho yadā yena yo nareṇā'bhikāṅkṣyate ||
śabdagato viśeṣaḥ pratiśabda samavāyādaprayojaka ityarthagatena hetunā
viśeṣeṇa viśiṣṭaḥ śabda uktaḥ | sa cā'yamarthagato viśeṣa ityāha—puruṣārtha
syopāyamanavagatamavagamayannutkarṣādviśiṣṭaḥ śabda uktaḥ | anyathā sarvaḥ
śabdaḥ śabdāntarādbhadyata ityaviśeṣaṇameva syāt | anavagatamityanujñāyā
vyavacchinatti | yataścā'rthaviśeṣeṇopadeśatā śabdasya tasya ca nā'navagata
saṅgatinā puruṣeṇa pratītiḥ ato'pi vyutpatterapekṣetyāha—ato nā'viditā
rthasya prathamaśrāviṇaḥ puruṣasyāpadeśātpravṛttiḥ nanūpadeśo vidhiḥ, sa cā'rtha
bhedā'bhidhāyakaḥ śabda iti kvacitkvaciduccāraṇamāha śabdasyoccāraṇa
miti | kvacidartham, vidhyuddeśenaikavākyatvāditi | kvacidvacanaṃ codaneti |
[p.172]
nā'viditārthasya pravṛttiḥ | pradarśanārthaṃ cedam | ato'rthaśabdā'bhidhoccāra
ṇādijñānaṃ ca karmmakartṛkāraṇabhāvasādhanenopadeśaśabdenocyate preṣaṇādivat |
tairapi hi yathāvivakṣitamarthādayo nirdiśyante | arthastarhi pravarttakaḥ | satyaṃ,
śabdo'pi, tatpratipādanāt | kathaṃ tarhyaṅgopadeśaḥ ? apekṣito hyarthaḥ
puruṣā'rthaḥ | anyathā grāmādirapi na tathā syāt | apekṣitaścopadiṣṭa
sādhanā'nugrahaḥ | nanu liṅādibhyaḥ pravarttanā'vagamyate | parāhataṃ cedam
kriyāyāḥ pravartakaṃ vacanamiti | kvacit jñānaṃ śāstraṃ śabdavijñānādasanni
kṛṣṭe'rthe vijñānamiti | vārtikakāraścā'bhidhābhāvanāmāhurityabhidhāmiti |
ata āha—pradarśanārtha cedaṃ, viśiṣṭaḥ śabdo vidhiriti | ato'rthaśabdā'bhi
dhoccāraṇādijñānaṃ ca karmakarttṛkaraṇabhāvasādhanenopadeśaśabdena yathāyathamucyate |
teṣu teṣu śāstrapradeśeṣu codanāvidhiśāstraśabdā upadeśaśabdā evetyarthaḥ |
atraiva laukikaṃ nidarśanamāha—preṣaṇādivat | kathametannidarśana mi?tyata
āha—tairapi hi preṣaṇādiśabdaiḥ karmakaruṇasādhanairyathāvivakṣamarthādayo nirdi
śyante | yadyarthabhedā'bhidhāyitvena utkarṣaḥ tarhi prāptā'prāptavivekenā'rtha eva
pravartako na śabda iti codanālakṣaṇo'rtho dharma iti śāstrakṛdvavacanavyāghātaḥ
prasajyata ityāha—arthastarhi pravartakaḥ |
pariharati—satyaṃ, śabdopi pravartakastatpratipādanāt | ayamabhisandhiḥ |
na tāvatsākṣādapekṣitopāyatā'pi pravṛttihetuḥ, tasyāḥ pravṛtteḥ prāgabhāvādapi
tu svajñānena pravarttayatīti tatra śabdenaiva janyata iti yathā'rthastadviṣayatvātpra
vartakastathā śabdo'pi tajjanakatvādityaviśeṣaḥ | tathā ca na vyāghātaḥ |
etena pūrvārdhaṃ kārikāyā vyākhyātam | atra ca puruṣārthasyeti puruṣo niyojyo
'bhimataḥ śuddhasyeti niyojyārthasyaiva na niyoktrarthasyetyarthaḥ | nanu yadi puruṣā
rthopāyā'bhidhāyī śabda upadeśaḥ, kathaṃ tarhyaṅgopadeśaḥ ? na khalvaṅgāni
puruṣārthasādhanāni, api tu kratvarthāni | pariharati—apekṣito hyartho'tra
puruṣārtho, na hi jātyā kaścitpuruṣārtho vyavasthitaḥ | anyathā grāmādirapi
na tathā syāt | tasyāpi svayamasukhavat kadācitkathaṃcidiṣyamāṇatvāccenna
tarhi jātyā puruṣārthatvam | yathāha nyāyavādī 'so'yaṃ prāṇyartho'parisaṃkhyeyaḥ
prāṇabhṛdbhedasyā'parisaṃkhyeyatvā diti | na cāṅgakāryaṃ prati jātyaivā'pekṣā
nāsti puruṣāṇāmityāha—apekṣitaścopadiṣṭasādhanasya śreyaḥsādhanā'nugrahaḥ |
ananugrahe sādhanavaiguṇyena śreyaso'jananāt |
[p.173]
upadeśo niyojyārthakarmā prasthitacodaneti | nedaṃ parāhatama | tathā hi—
puṃso neṣṭābhyupāyatvāt kriyāsvanyaḥ pravarttakaḥ |
pravṛttihetuṃ dharmaṃ ca pravadanti pravartanām ||
pravṛttisamartho hi kaścidbhāvā'tiśayo vyāpārā'bhidhānaḥ pravarttanā |
sā ca kriyāṇāmapekṣitopāyataiva | na hi tathātvamapratipadya tatra pravarttate
atra codayati—nanu liṅādibhyaḥ pravartanā'vagamyate | anyathā
tacchrāviṇaḥ pravṛttyanupapatteḥ | na punaḥ puruṣārthopāyatā | na khalu bhūtārthapratītiḥ
pravṛttihetuḥ | parāhataṃ cedamupadeśo niyojyārthakarmā'prasthita pracodaneti | yadi
hi niyojyaprayojanaṃ karmabhūtamāhuḥ kathamasāvapravṛttapravarttanā ? seyaṃ parāhatiḥ |
nedaṃ parāhataṃ parāhatiparihāreṇa sarva parihṛtaṃ bhavatītyāśayaḥ | kutaḥ ?
tathā hi—
puṃso neṣṭā'bhyupāyatvāt kriyāsvanyaḥ pravarttakaḥ |
prekṣāvatāṃ hi kāyavacanaceṣṭe prayatnapūrvike, prayatnaśca rāgapūrvaḥ, sukhā
nuśayī ca rāgo na duḥkhe nāpyasukhe bhavitumarhati, dveṣādupekṣaṇācca | kriyā
ca duḥkhetyanubhavo lokasya | sā yadi sukhasādhanamapi na syānnā'syāṃ sukhārthinaḥ
pravartteran | caite upāyāḥ sākṣāt sukhe pravarttitumīśate iti prekṣāvadvyāpāro
paramaprasaṅgaḥ | tasmād duḥkharūpāsu kriyāsu sukhasādhanataivecchopahāramukhena
pravarttayati | svavijñānena cā'sāvicchāṃ pravṛttihetumupaharatītyuktam | yadi
punaḥ śabdaḥ sukhasādhanatāṃ na pratipādayenna kriyāyāṃ puruṣaṃ pravarttayet | nanu
tathāpīṣṭasādhanatāmātraṃ śabdena pratipāditaṃ, kutaḥ punarataḥ pravarttanāpratyayaḥ ?
ityata āha—
pravṛttihetuṃ dharmaṃ ca pravadanti pravarttanām ||
tad vyācaṣṭe—pravṛttisamartho hi kaścidbhāvā'tiśayo vyāpārā'bhidhānaḥ
pravarttanā | ayamarthaḥ | na vayaṃ parispandamevaikaṃ vyāpāramācakṣmahe, yenā'spanda
nātmanaḥ samīhitasādhanatāyā apravarttanātmatvena paryanuyujyemahi, api tu
bhāvadharma eva kaścitsamīhitasādhanā'nuguṇo vyāpārapadārthaḥ | tadyathā ātmano
buddhyādijananapravṛttasya manaḥsaṃyoga evā'yaṃ bhāvadharmaḥ | tadvadatrāpi spandasta
ditaro vā bhāvadharmaḥ pravṛttijananā'nukūlatayā vyāpāraviśeṣaḥ pravartanā | na
cā'sau śabdastadbhāvanā cetyāha—sā ca kriyāṇāmapekṣitopāyataiva | kutaḥ ?
[p.174]
kaścit | yāpyājñā dibhyaḥ pravṛttiḥ sāpi kathaṃ cidapekṣitanibandhanatvamupā
śrityaiva | anyathā'bhāvāt | nanu karttavyamiti pratipatteḥ pravṛttiḥ | kathaṃ
na hi tathātvamapekṣitopāyatvamapratipadya tatra kriyāsu pravarttate kaścit |
nanvanapekṣitopāye'pyājñayā rājñaḥ pravarttante prekṣāvanta iti kathamapekṣitopāya
taiva pravṛttihetuḥ ? ityata āha—yāpyājñādibhyaḥ pravṛttiḥ sā'pi kathaṃ
cidapekṣitanibandhanatāmupāśrityaiva | yadyapyājñādayo niyoktrarthakarmāṇastathāpi
tadviṣayasaṃpādanasya kathaṃcinniyojyā'pekṣitanibandhanatvamapyasti | tathā hi |
tatsaṃpādanaparitoṣita ājñāpayitā samīhitamasmai prayacchati ahitaṃ vā na
vidhatte | tadidamapekṣitamupāśritya bhṛtyasya pravṛttiḥ | kutaḥ punaretadi?tyā
ha—anyathā tadanupāśraye pravṛtterabhāvāt na khalu yasya yo na hitā'hitaprāpti
parihārābhyāṃ yatate sa tadājñāmanurudhyate | na caitāvatopadeśasyā''jñādibhira
viśeṣaḥ, teṣāṃ niyojakā'rthasamuddeśena pravṛtterānuṣaṅgikatvānniyojyārthasyopa
deśasya tadvaiparītyāt | tasmālliṅādibhyaḥ pravartanā'vagatirupapannā'nupapannā ca
parāhatiriti |
atra kecidāmnāyaṃ prati śrāddhamāninaḥ prāhuḥ | nanu karttavyamiti
pratipatteḥ pravṛttiḥ | idamākūtam | kāryadarśanonneyavyāpṛtayaḥ khalvamī
liṅādayaḥ | karyaṃ ca pravṛttilakṣaṇaṃ vṛddhānāṃ liṅādiśravaṇasamanantaramupalabhyate |
tacca buddhipūrvakaṃ, svatantrapravṛttitvāt asmatpravṛttivat | anumitā ca
buddhirasmatpravṛttihetubuddhigocaracāriṇī pravṛttihetubuddhitvāt asmatpra
vṛttihetubuddhivaditi | tasyāśca viṣayaṃ svayameva cakṣurunmīlya piṇḍi
karogaṃ parityajya paryālocayantaḥ śabdavyāpārapuruṣāśayatatsamīhitatatsā
dhanatāvyudāsena karttavyatāmeva pratipadyāmahe | tathā hi | stanapānādāvapi
na jātu samīhitopāya ityeva pravṛttāḥ smaḥ, kiṃ tu karttavyametaditi | atīte
varttamāne'nāgate ca sāmudravidākhyāte etatte bhavitetivatkarttavyatātmanā
pravṛtteḥ | tadavagame ca tadātmani pravṛtteḥ karttavyataiva pravṛttiheturiti liṅādi
śravaṇā'nantarā pravṛttiḥ karttavyatā'bhidhānameva liṅādīnāmāpādayati | tathā
ca viditasaṅgatitayā liṅādayo vede'pi tāmevā'bhidadhate | etāvāṃstu bhedo
laukikavacasaḥ pramāṇāntarā'pekṣapravṛttitvādapekṣitopāyatāpratītipuraḥsaratvācca
duḥkharūpakriyākarttavyatā'vagaterapekṣitopāyatāṃ tatpramāṇabhūtāmantarbhāvya kartavya
tā'vagatiḥ | apauruṣeyastu vedo na svārthe pramāṇāntaramapekṣata ityapekṣito
[p.175]
hi tathā pratipadyamāno na pravartteta ? yo hi svarṇamupalabhya mṛttiketyāha
kastasyottaraṃ dadyāt ? kaḥ punarayamarthaḥ karttavyamiti ? na kaścit |
pratibhā | kā punariyam ? niyatasādhanāvacchinnakriyāpratipattyanukūlā prajñā 
pratibhākriyāviṣayatve hi kriyāpratītiḥ syāt | sā ca pravṛttihetuḥ |
pāyatā'vagamanirapekṣa eva karttavyatā'vagamahetuḥ | tathā ca sā vedādavagamya
mānā'nuṣṭhātāramadhikāriṇaṃ niyojyamapekṣate, na punaḥ sādhyamasyā eva,
tādrūpyāt | niyojyaśca yatra sādhyaviśiṣṭo bhavet tatra sādhyabuddhiryathā
svargakāmo yajeteti | yatra tvasau nimittavānyathā yāvajjīvamiti niṣidhya
mānakriyākarttā vā yathā na kalañjaṃ bhakṣayediti tatra bhagavānāmnāya eva
nirastā'khiladoṣāśaṅko bhāvārthaviṣayaṃ nañarthaviṣayaṃ vā kārya hitā'hita
sādhanatā'vagamanirapekṣo'vagamayanpravṛttiṃ nivṛttiṃ vā vidhata iti kimapekṣito
pāyatayeti |
nanvapekṣitopāyatāmantareṇa karttavyamiti śataśo'pyabhidhīyamānaṃ na
pravṛttaye kalpata ityata āha—kathaṃ hi tathā pratipadyamāno na pravartteta ? śabda
stāvatkartavyatāyāṃ viditasaṅgatistāmavagamayati | tathā naimittikaniṣedhā'
dhikārayorasau pratīyamānā na śakyā neti vaktum | na cā'pekṣitopāyatā
nivṛttau tasyā api nivṛttiriti sāmpratam | sā hi tasyāṃ na pramāṇam |
na caikapramāṇanivṛttau pramāṇāntareṇa pratīyamānaṃ tadeva nivarttitumarhati | na
hi saṃtamase dravyaṃ cakṣuṣā na gṛhyate iti na tvacā vyavasthāpyate | na ca
nimittāntaraṃ naimittikā'pūrvaṃ na karttavyaṃ prekṣāvatā | asukhatve sati hitā'hita
prāptiparihārā'nupāyatvāt kākadantaparīkṣāvadityanumānaṃ sambhavati |
āmnāyajanmanā kartavyatābodhena bādhitaviṣayatvāt | tadāha—yo hi svarṇa
mupalabhya mṛttiketyāha kastasyottaraṃ dadyāt ? tasmādanapekṣādāmnāyāt
karttavyatā'vagatā'nuṣṭhāna mākṣipatyeva | anyathā tattvā'nupapatteḥ | tasyā
stannāntarīyakatvāt | tasmātsūktaṃ kathaṃ hi tathā pratipadyamāno na pravartteta |
nanu yadi na hitasādhanatā karttavyatā tarhi tadarthatattvaṃ vācyam, na ca
tadastītyāśayavānsiddhāntī pṛcchati—kaḥ punarayamarthaḥ karttavyami?ti |
tatrāntare pūrvapakṣyekadeśī prāha—na kaścit | nanvevamasadarthā karttavyatāva
gatirapramāṇam | apramāṇaṃ ca na vyavahārāya kalpata ityata āha —pratibhā |
nanu vyabhicārādarthā niścayātmakamanaikāntikaliṅgaprasūtaṃ saṃvedanaṃ pratibheti
[p.176]
apramāṇaṃ na vyavahārāṅgam | saṃśayaviparyayau hi tathā | na hīdamitthamanena
kartavyamiti anupajātapratibhābhedaḥ pravartate pratyakṣādyavagate'pyarthe | tatra hi
pramāṇakāryyasamāptiḥ | pratibhānetro hi loka itikartavyatāsu samīhate |
pratibhāvidaḥ | na cā'niścayātmakaṃ vijñānaṃ vyavahārāṅgaṃ bhavatītyāśayavānāha—
kā punariyam ? ekadeśina uttaraṃ—niyatasādhanā'vacchinnakriyāpratipattyanu
kūlā prajñeti | na hi te pratibhāvidaḥ, ye saṃvedanamaniścayātmakaṃ pratibhāmācakhyuḥ |
saṃśayo hi saḥ | vayaṃ tu sādhyasādhanetikarttavyatā'vacchinnāyāḥ kriyāyāḥ prati
pattāvanukūlāṃ tatpratipattyā kārye'nuṣṭhānalakṣaṇe karttavye sahakāriṇo karttavya
miti prajñāṃ pratibhāmadhyagīṣmahi | evaṃ tarhi kriyai'vāsyā viṣaya iti na
nirviṣayetyata āha—pratibhākriyāviṣayatve hi kriyāpratītiḥ syāt | sā ca
traikālyā'vacchinnakriyāgocarā | yathā pacati pakṣyatyapākṣīditi | iyaṃ
punaratītā'nāgatavarttamānānāmanyatamenāpyanavacchinnamadbhutārthamiva pratibhāsa
yantī prajñā | na punarasyāḥ paramārthasadviṣayaḥ, kālatrayarahitatvāt khapuṣpavat |
na khalu kuryāmiti varttamānākhyā, karomītyaviśeṣaprasaṅgāt | nā'nāgatā,
kariṣyāmīti | nā'tītā, akārṣamiti | tadeṣā na pramāṇa masadviṣayatvānna
saṃśayaḥ ekāntā'vasāyānna viparyayaḥ sarveṣāṃ sarvatra vyavahāradarśanāt | ata eva
cittavṛttiṃ tāmimāmācakṣate vikalparūpāmācāryāḥ śabdajñānā'nupātī vastuśūnyo
vikalpa iti vadantaḥ |
nanūktamapramāṇaṃ pratibhā kathaṃ vyavahāraṃ pravartayedi?ti, ata āha—sā
ca pravṛttihetuḥ | kuta etadapramāṇaṃ na vyavahārāṅgami?ti | saṃśayaviparyayau
hi tathā | pratibhā punastābhyāmanyā vyavahāraṃ pravartayatītyabhiprāyaḥ | tatki
manyatāmātreṇa pravṛttihetuḥ ? naivamiti, tadanvayavyatirekā'nuvidhānātpravṛti
bhāvā'bhāvayoḥ | tatra vyatirekaṃ tāvadāha—na hīdamitthamanena kartavyamityanupa
jātapratibhābhedaḥ pravartate pratyakṣā'vagatepyarthe | nanu pratyakṣādereva hānopādāno
pekṣāḥ phalamiti kimarthaṃ pratibhā ? ityata āha—tatra hi pramāṇakāryasamāptiḥ |
pramāṇānāṃ hānādilakṣaṇaṃ kāryaṃ tatpratibhābhede sati samāpyate nā'nyathā | tena
hānādikriyāsu pravṛttāni pratyakṣādīni kāṣṭhānīva pāke jvalanaṃ pratibhārūpama
pekṣamāṇānyapi nā'kāraṇamityuktaṃ bhavati | anvayamidānīmāha—pratibhānetro
hi loka itikarttavyatāsu samīhate | itikarttavyatāsvanuṣṭhāneṣu kāyavacana
ceṣṭāsu samīhate prayatate kāryamiti pratibhāsādhano hi loka ityarthaḥ | tadanena
kriyāpratipattāvānukūlyaṃ pratibhāsu darśitam | nanvayaṃ vikalpaḥ, sa ca śabda
[p.177]
kiṃ punarasyā nimittam ? śabdaḥ | tatra vyutpannānāṃ sākṣādbhāvanāmukhena
vā'prasiddhaśabdanibandhanavyavahārāṇāṃ bālādīnāmanādiśabdabhāvanā | anyathā
ādyakaraṇavinyāsādyasambhavāt | yathoktam |
ādyaḥ karaṇavinyāsaḥ prāṇasyordhvaṃ samīraṇam |
sthānānāmabhighātaśca na vinā śabdabhāvanām ||
jñānā'nupātīti tadvidaḥ | na ca pratibhā śabdajñānamanupatati | bālādīnāṃ
tadabhāvena tadanutpādādapravṛttiprasaṅgāt | tasmānnimittāntaraṃ vācyam, na ca
tadastītyāśayavānāha—kiṃ punarasyā nimittam ? uttaraṃ—śabdaḥ | tatra ye
tāvad vyutpannāsteṣāṃ pratibhāyāḥ sākṣādbhāvanāmukhena vā'prasiddhaśabdanibandhana
vyavahārāṇāṃ bālādīnām |
nanviyaṃ ṣaṭvabhyāsā''darapratyayayornirbhāvanānā'sati pūrvā'nubhave bhavitu
marhati, anubhavo'pyaviditasaṃbandhaśabdavijñānajanmā nāsti | nā'smina janmani
bālādīnāṃ prāgbhavīyā tu bhāvanā sarvasaṃskāracchidaḥ prayāṇāt dhvaṃsatesmeti
na sampratitanīṃ pratibhāṃ bhāvayet, bhāvane vā jātyandhavadhirādayastajjanmā'nu
bhūtāniva janmā'ntarā'nubhūtān rūpaśabdādīnapi vyācakṣīrannityata āha—
anādiśabdabhāvanā'vaśyā'bhyupetavyā | kutaḥ ? ādyakaraṇavinyāsādīnāmanyathā
'saṃbhavāt | yathoktaṃ pratibhāvidbhiḥ |
ādyaḥ karaṇa vinyāsaḥ prāṇasyordhvaṃ samīraṇam |
sthānānāmabhighātaśca na vinā śabdabhāvanām ||
jātamātraḥ khalvayaṃ bālakaḥ pitrā mukhahute madhusarpiṣī jihvayā leḍhi,
so'yamādyaḥ karaṇavinyāsaḥ | prāṇāṃścordhva samīrayati yacchvasatītyucyate |
api codīritena vāyunā hṛdayādīni sthānānyabhihanti yataḥ śabdabheda
āvirasti | tadetatsarvaṃ prāgbhavīyaśabdabhāvanāvijṛmbhitam | atra prayogaḥ,
bālānāmevaṃvidhā ceṣṭā karttavyatā'vagatipūrvikā svatantracetanapravṛttitvādyā
yā svatantracetanapravṛttiḥ sā sā kartavyatā'vagatipūrvikā yathā'smadādīnām |
tathā vivādādhyāsitā kartavyatā'vagatiḥ śabdayoniḥ karttavyatā''kāratvāt
yo ya evamākāraḥ sa sarvaḥ śabdayoniryathā'smadādīnām | sa ca śabdaḥ
sākṣādanupalabhyamāno bhāvanāmukhenā'dhikaraṇasiddhāntanyāyena karaṇabhāva
māpadyate | bhāvanā ca yathākāryadarśanonneyā yāvatkāryadarśanaṃ vyavasthāpyata iti
na jātyandhabadhirādīnāṃ janmāntarā'nubhūtarūpādivyākhyānaprasaṅgaḥ |
[p.178]
vicchinnapratītīnāmapi ca padārthānāṃ kiṃ kathamanusandhīyeta, vicāra
vikalā'pratyavamṛṣṭapūrvaśaktiḥ samāviṣṭapūrvaśaktipratibhāsevodīyamānopaśleṣaṃ
vyutpannānāmapi śabdabhāvanāyonirasti pratibhetyāha—vicchinnaprati
pattīnāmapi ca padārthānāṃ kiṃ kathamanusaṃdhīyata iti vicārakalpanāpratyavamṛṣṭa
pūrvaśaktiḥ samāviṣṭapūrvaśaktipratyavabhāsevodīyamānopaśleṣaṃ vidadhāti |
ayamarthaḥ | saṃtāpataptatanavo hi śītahradadarśanamātrādapsu mimaṅghavaḥ pravarttante,
nāntarā vicārayanti | dṛśyamānahradagatā āpastāpamapanetuṃ samarthāḥ sati
śiśirabhāve toyatvādyatoyaṃ śiśira tattatsarvaṃ tāpā'panodanasamarthaṃ yathā
gaṅgātoyam, vayaṃ ca taptāstadiha tatpraśamanāya nimajjāma iti | na ceteṣāṃ
parasparopaśleṣamantareṇa karttavyatā | na vāsyāmasatyāṃ svatantracetanapravttiḥ |
na copaśleṣe samudācaradvṛtti kiṃcitpramāṇamasti | tasmādasaṃviditarūpā
śabdabhāvanaiva idamitthamanena karttavyamiti pratibhāheturāstheyaḥ | na caivaṃ vācyaṃ,
prāgbhavīyaṃ tajjanmajaṃ vā yatra yat pratibhāti tatra tadbhāvanābījamastu, kiṃ
śabdene?ti | ānumānikā'nubhavajanmano bhāvanāyā bhūtārthagocaratvena
karttavyatā''kārapratītyakāraṇatvāt | karttavyatākārarahitasya cā'tropaśleṣa
mātrasya pratīteḥ | atra kiṃ kathamanusaṃdhīyate iti vicāravikaleti anumāna
bījabhūtānāṃ dharmisvarūpadarśanataddharmasaṃdehajijñāsāpakṣahetudharmaparāmarśapramāṇānta
rābādhānāmabhāvaṃ sūcayati | apratyavamṛṣṭapūrvaśaktiriti vyāptismaraṇā
'bhāvamāha | na jātu gṛhītavyāptirapi tasyā asmarannanumātumarhati |
nanu na yadi pūrvaśaktiparāmarśaḥ kimiti tāpadūnaṃ śiśarahradā'vagāhane
pravarttayenna dahanajvālākalāpā''liṅgane ? etannirākaroti—samāviṣṭapūrva
śaktipratyavabhāsānnedamanumānaṃ yena pūrvaśaktiparāmarśamapekṣate, api tu svakā
raṇātpūrvaśaktyākāropajāyamānā kvacitpravarttayati kvacinna | nanu yadi
pūrvaśaktyavabhāsā pratibhā tarhi pūrvaiva śaktirasyā viṣaya iti na nirviṣayeti,
etannirākaroti—iveti | na tu saiva | tathā hi | karttavyatāvabhāsā pratibhā
pūrvaśaktimapi tadavacchedakatayā gocarayati, kartavyatā ca na paramārthasatīti
tadavacchedena pratibhārūḍhenātmanā śaktirapyavastusatī | svarūpeṇa tu sattvama
prayojakaṃ pratibhāyāmanārohāt | seyamevaṃvidhā pratibhā bhāvānāmupaśleṣaṃ
vidadhāti | kutaḥ ? tādarthyātpravṛttyarthatvādasyāḥ |
nanu bhavatu pravṛttyarthā bhāvanā ; upaśleṣavidhānādityata āha—anupa
śleṣe tadabhāvāt | viśiṣṭārthapratipattinibandhano vyavahāro'navicchinna
[p.179]
vidadhāti | tādarthyādanupaśleṣe tadabhāvāt | durjñānopāyeṣu ca prājñairapi
svaraviśeṣāśrayabhedeṣu pakṣiṇāṃ pūrvaṃ śabdabhāvanā'nugamātpratyutpannaśabdavyāpārā 
pratibhā pravṛttinibandhanaṃ, nopāyajñānam | upadeśā'sakṛddarśanayorabhāvāt |
āhāraviśeṣapratipattiśca tajjātīyānāmaviditaprītyupāyatvena prathamajñāna
nibandhanā na bhavet | jātibhedā'bhivyaktapratibhābhedānāṃ plavanādikriyāyāṃ
pravṛttiścā'jñātopāyānāmekajātīyānāṃ ca rāgādimayī pravṛttiḥ, śāśvatika-
pratipattipadārthamātrānnotpattumarhatītyarthaḥ | api ca yatprājñānāmapi pramāṇaire
kāntaduṣkaraṃ tanmandadhiyāmapi pakṣiṇāṃ pratyutpannaśabdavyāpārābhāve'pi
pūrvaśabdabhāvanā'nugamalabdhajanmapratibhābhedavaśādīṣatkaramiti pramāṇebhyo'pi
sāmarthyā'tiśayaṃ pratibhāyā darśayati—durjñānopāyeṣu ca prājñairapi svaraviśeṣā'
'śrayabhedeṣu pakṣiṇāṃ pūrvaśabdabhāvanā'nugamādviprakṛṣṭaśabdavyāpārā pratibhā
pravṛttinibandhanam |
nanūpāyajñānameva pramāṇabhūtamasti, tannaḥ pravṛttinibandhanamiti kiṃ
pratibhayā ? ityata āha—nopāyajñānaṃ pravṛttinibandhanam | kutaḥ ? upadeśā
'sakṛddarśanayorabhāvāddhi kāraṇam upāyajñānaṃ kāraṇadvayanivṛttau nivarttate dhūma
iva dhūmadhvajanivṛttāvityarthaḥ | atha kulāyanirmāṇe kalaviṅkānāṃ svarabheda
vikāre ca madhau puṃskokilānāmupāyajñānabījamasakṛddarśanamadhyāropyetetyāha—
āhāraviśeṣapratipattirasmadādibhiretadādaraṇīyamiti pratibheti yāvat | tajjā
tīyānāṃ tajjātiryeṣāṃ teṣāṃ samānajātīyānāmutpannamātrāṇāmasakṛddarśanā'bhāvā
daviditaprītyupāyatvena prathamajñānanibandhanā na bhavet | prathamagrahaṇenā'sakṛddarśanā
'bhāvaṃ sūcayati |
nanvanāditvātpretyabhāvasya punaḥ samānā'samānajātīyavividhadehopadhāna
samadhigatatattajjātiniyatatattadbhāvanānicayasyaikasyāmapi jātau samasta
pratibhodayaprasaṅgaḥ, kāraṇasannidhānādityata āha—jātibhedā'bhivyakta
pratibhābhedānām | etaduktaṃ bhavati | yena karmāśayena yadupāttaṃ janma sa
tajjanmocitāmeva bhāvanāmabhivyanakti, na jātyantarocitām | tasyāstatrā
'nupayogāt | abhivyaktabhāvanābhedaḥ pratibhābhedamutpādayannabhivyanakti | utpa
ttau satyāṃ pratīteḥ plavanādikriyāpravṛttiścā'jñātopāyānāṃ jātamātrāṇāṃ cakra
vākādīnāṃ na bhavet | ekajātīyānāṃ ca pārāvatīragādīnāṃ rāgadveṣamayī
pravṛttirjātamātrāṇāmajñātasukhaduḥkhopāyānāṃ na bhavet | śāśvatikavirodhinā
mahinakulādīnāṃ dveṣavatī pravṛttirna bhavet |
[p.180]
virodhānāṃ dveṣapravṛttiḥ | ato neṣṭā'bhyupāyamātraṃ pravṛttihetuḥ, api tu prati
bhetyādarśayan vṛddhāḥ | yathā'vocan—
upaśleṣamivā'rthānāṃ sā karotyavicāritā |
sārvarūpyamivāpannā viṣayatvena varttate ||
sākṣācchabdena janitā bhāvanā'nugamena vā |
itikarttavyatāyāṃ tāṃ na kaścidativarttate ||
pramāṇatvena tāṃ lokaḥ sarvaḥ samanugacchati |
vyavahārāḥ pravarttante tiraścāmapi tadvaśāt ||
 pūrvapakṣyekadeśī prakṛtamupasaṃharati—ato neṣṭā'bhyupāyatā pravṛttiheturapi
tu pratibheti | etaccā'darśayan vṛddhāḥ | yadavocan—
upaśleṣamivārthānāṃ sā karotyavicāritā |
avicārite kiṃ kathamanusaṃdhīyata iti vicāravikaleti vyākṛtam | na
cāsau vyatiriktaviṣayetyāha—
sārvarūpyamivāpannā viṣayatvena varttate |
vicchinnapratipattīnāṃ tāpahradatoyādīnāṃ sahasopaśleṣaṃ vidadhāti | teṣāṃ
sārvarūpyamāpanneva na tvāpannā | teṣāṃ tadārohiṇāṃ vastusattāyā vicārā'saha
tvāt | tasmātpratibhaiva tādrūpyamāpannā viṣayatvena vartate | na tadvyatirikto
'sti viṣayaḥ | etadapi ca vicchinnāpratipattīnāmapi bahupadārthānāṃ samāviṣṭa
pūrvaśaktipratyavabhāsevodīyamāneti vyākhyātam | na ceyamākasmikī, na vā
pramāṇabhūtā'pi na vyavahārāṅgamityāha—
sākṣācchabdena janitā bhāvanā'nugamena vā |
iti kartavyatāyāṃ tāṃ na kaścidativarttate ||
apramāṇabhūtāmapi ca nirviṣayatayā vyavahārān pratyantaraṅgatayā
cā'vyabhicāritayā ca |
na kevalaṃ prekṣāvatāṃ manuṣyāṇāmapi tu—
pramāṇatvena tāṃ lokaḥ sarvaḥ samanugacchati |
vyavahārāḥ pravartante tiraścāmapi tadvaśāt ||
tadudāharaṇapracayamātyantikaprasiddhikhyāpanāyāha anyathā
[p.181]
svaravṛttiṃ vikurute madhau puṃskokilaḥ katham |
jantvādayaḥ kulāyādeḥ karaṇe śikṣitāḥ katham ||
āhāraprītividveṣaplavanādi kriyāsu ca |
jātyanvayaprasiddhāsu prayoktā mṛgapakṣiṇām ||
ucyate—
na nirālambanaṃ jñānaṃ kriyāsādhanayogini |
sāmānye'sminviprakṛṣṭe yacchabdavadupāyadhīḥ ||
khā khalu pratibhā''lambanavatī na vā ? ālambanavatī cettadevā'va
boddhavyaṃ pravṛttinibandhanaṃ, na ca tadanyadīhitopāyatāyāḥ |
svaravṛtti vikurute madhau puṃskokilaḥ katham |
jantvādayaḥ kulāyādikaraṇe śikṣitāḥ katham ||
āhāraprītividveṣaplavanādikriyāsu kaḥ |
jātyanvayaprasiddhāsu prayoktā mṛgapakṣiṇām ||
tatra siddhāntī pūrvapakṣye kadeśinamanantaratayā nirākaroti—
ucyata iti |
na nirālambanaṃ jñānaṃ kriyāsādhanayogini |
sāmānye'sminviprakṛṣṭe yacchabdavadupāyadhīḥ ||
yadyapi pratibhā nirviṣayetyuktaṃ, tathāpi kadācididamabhyupagatasiddhānta
mātraṃ syānna tāttvikamiti dūṣaṇā'bhidhitsayā vikalpitam | sā khalu pratibhā
ālambanavatī na vā | tatra prathamaṃ pakṣamāśrityāha—ālambanavatī cettadeva
vā bodhyaṃ pravṛttiheturna pratibhā | nanvetāvatāpi kimabhimatasiddhiḥ ? ityata
āha—na ca tadanyadīhitopāyatāyāḥ | etaccopariṣṭānnipuṇataramupa
pādayiṣyate | tathā cā'bhimatasiddhiḥ |
dvitīyaṃ pakṣamavalambate paraḥ | nanu na vijñānaṃ vākyaviṣayaṃ sambhavati |
vikalpā'sahatvāt | nirākārasya vā vijñānasya tadviṣayaḥ sākārasya vā |
na tāvannirākārasya | viṣayalakṣaṇā'yogāt | tathā hi yadi satta yā
viṣayaḥ sarve'rthāḥ sarveṣāṃ sattāmātrasyā'viśeṣādviṣayā iti sarvasarvajñatāpattiḥ |
athotpādakatvādviṣayatvaṃ tacca kasyacidevārthasya kasyacideva pratipattuḥ kasya
cideva pratyayaṃ pratīti nā'tiprasaṅgaḥ, tathāpi cakṣurādīnāmapyutpādakatvādvi
ṣayatvaṃ prasajyeta, vijñānasamaye ca tadutpādakasyā'tītatvāt | kṣaṇikatvena
kāryakāraṇayorayaugapadyāt | vartamānatājñānā'bhāvaprasaṅgaḥ | emasāmagrī
[p.182]
pratibandhena vā varttamānaviṣayatve netrādīnāmapi tadekasāmagrīniveśinā
vijñānakālānāṃ grahaṇaprasaṅgaḥ |
syādetat | pratyayaḥ svakāraṇā''sāditasāmarthyabhedaḥ kacidevā'rtha
varttamānamavabhāsayati na sarva, sa hi tasya mahimeti na netrādiviṣayatā |
yathā''haviṣayagrahaṇaṃ dharmo vijñānasyeti | atraitadālocanīyaṃ, kiṃ viṣayaḥ
punarayaṃ sāmarthyabhedaḥ svakāraṇā'dhīnajanmā vijñānasye ?ti | arthaviṣaya iti
cet ? kimayamasya nirvarttyo yathā saṃyavanasya piṇḍaḥ, vikāryo vā yathā'vaghā
tasya vrīhayaḥ, saṃskāryo vā yathā prokṣaṇasyolūkhalādiḥ prāpyo vā yathā
dohanasya payaḥ ? kimanenā'nabhyupetā''ropaṇena ? arthaprāptau tu vijñānaṃ
samarthamiti brūmaḥ | kā punariyaṃ prāptiḥ ? pramitirarthadharmaḥ kaṃcideva pramātāraṃ
prati dvitvādisaṃkhyevā'pekṣābuddhimantaṃ prati buddhidhvaṃsācca dhvaṃsate dvitvādika
mivā'pekṣābuddhivināśāditi cet | na tāvadunmīlitalocanā dvi tvādika
miva taddharma pramitimīkṣāmahe | api cā'tītānāgatayorapramitiprasaṅgaḥ | na
hyasti sambhavaḥ, apratyutpanno dharmī dharmaśca tasya pratyutpanna iti |
yadyucyate, na vijñānādanyo'rthaprakāśaḥ kiṃ tu vijñānaprakāśa eva saḥ |
vijñānaprakāśaśca vijñāna meva | kathamanyaprakāśo'nyasyeti cet | svabhāva
bhedāt | rūpādayo hi svapratyayādupajāyamānā nātmanaḥ parasya vā
prakāśakāḥ, jñānaṃ tu svapratyayasamāsāditajanmā''tmaparaprakāśanaparamārtha na
jātu tadarthamantareṇa dṛṣṭarūpa paścādyena sambadhyate paraśuriva śuddhaḥ svakāraṇā
'dhīnajanmā paścācchidayā, kiṃtvarthasahitameveti na paryanuyogamarhati | na hi
paraśuḥ svakāraṇādayomayastattve kiṃcidapekṣate paryanuyujyate vā | svabhāvabhūtaṃ
cā'sya paraprakāśanamaprakāśananivṛttyā kathaṃcitkalpitabhedaṃ phalamiti svapara
prakāśanasāmarthyamasāmarthyanivṛttyā kathaṃcitkalpitavyatirekeṇa pramāṇamiti
na nirviṣayaṃ sāmarthyamiti |
tatra brūmaḥ | kaḥ punararthaḥ svaparaprakāśanaparamārthaṃ vijñānami?ti |
yadi prakāśanasvabhāvaṃ, siddhametat | atha parasparasambandhasvabhāvaṃ, kaḥ punareṣāṃ
sambandhaḥ ? viṣayaviṣayibhāvaḥ sambandha iti cet | tasyā'rthajñānasamavāyena
nānātvamanekavṛttitayā prasajyeta | tathā ca te eva tattvam | atītā'nāgatā
'rthavṛttitā ca kathamasyetyabhihitaprāyam | vijñānamātrā''śrayatve tu kathama
[p.183]
syā'rthā'nvayaḥ ? tasmādātmaparaprakāśanaparamārtha vijñānamiti varṇayatā
balādvijñānatādātmyaṃ nīlāderabhyupagataṃ bhavati | tathā ca nirākāraṃ vijñānaṃ
prati viṣayalakṣaṇā'yogāt sākārasya viṣayo'rtha iti |
api ca vijñānaprakāśa eva cedarthaprakāśastatastadbhedenā'rthabhedo
vyavasthāpanīyaḥ | tasya ca nirākāratayā viśeṣasyā'bhāvāt idaṃ nīlasya
saṃvedanamidaṃ pītasyeti vibhāgā'bhāvādarthakriyāviśeṣā'rthino na pravartteran |
ākārabhedavattve tu vijñānasya nīlasārūpyaṃ vyavasthāpanahetutvāt pramāṇaṃ
saṃvidrūpatā tvasya nīle'rthe vyavasthāpyamānatvāt phalam | yadyapi sārūpyaṃ
vijñānaṃ cetyekameva tattvaṃ tathāpi kathaṃcidekavyāvṛttiniṣṭharūpā'vagāhana
svabhāvairvikalpairvyāvartyabhedā''ropitabhedaṃ pramāṇaphalabhāvamaśnute | yadāha—"na
hi vittisattaiva tadvedanā yuktā, tasyāḥ sarvatrā'viśeṣādaviśeṣaprasaṅgāt |
tāṃ tu sārūpyamāviśatsvarūpavantaṃ ghaṭayediti | tatsārūpyatadutpattibhyāṃ ca
viṣayatvena nendriyādiṣu tatprasaṅgaḥ | arthā''hitā''kāravedanameva cā'rthavedanaṃ
jñānākārāśca varttamānā iti varttamānatā'pyupapanneti | yathāha—
bhinnakālaṃ kathaṃ grāhyamiti ced grāhyatāṃ viduḥ |
hetutvameva tad yuktaṃ jñānā''kārā''rpaṇakṣamam || iti |
tathāpyuktaṃ syādarthavedanamiti | tasmātsākārajñānagocaro'rtha iti
sautrāntikāḥ prapedire |
tadidamanupapannam | tathā hi | sākāraṃ vijñānamarthaviṣayamityanubhavādvya
vasthāpyate'numānādvā | na tāvadanubhavaḥ svākāramātraparyavasāyī nīlamaparaṃ
gocarayati | nīlamiti hi pratibhāso na punarnīle iti | na ca bhinnaṃ sadvi
ṣayo bhavitumarhatītyuktam |
syādetat | dvividho hi viṣayaḥ pramāṇānāṃ, grāhyo'dhyavaseyaśca |
tadyathā pratyakṣasya grāhyaḥ kṣaṇa eko'dhyavaseyaśca pravṛttiviṣayaḥ santānaḥ |
anyathā'rthakriyā'rthinaḥ pramāṇamavarttakaṃ syāt | pravṛttiviṣayopadarśanaṃ hi
pravartakatvaṃ prāpakatvaṃ ca pramāṇānām | na ca grāhyakṣaṇa eva pravṛttiviṣayāstasya
tadānīmatītatvāt | santānastu syāt | na cā'sau grāhya ityadhyavaseyatva
mabhyupetavyam | tathā'numānavikalpasyāpi grāhyaṃ sāmānyamanyavyāvṛttirūpam |
adhyavaseyastu pravṛttiviṣayo'rthakriyākṣamaḥ | pravṛttiviṣayā'pekṣaṃ hi prāmāṇyaṃ
tayoḥ | yathāha—
na hyābhyāmarthaṃ paricchidyā'dhyavasāyapravartamāno'rthakriyāyāṃ
visaṃvādyate iti | tadevaṃ bāhyamagrāhyamadhyavaseyatayā viṣaya iti tatrā'dhya
vasāyārthā'parijñānāt vikalpo'dhyavasāyaḥ | tatrānumānaṃ vikalparūpatvātta
[p.184]
dviṣayaṃ tu vikalpajananāditi cet | nanu vikalpo'pi svā''kāraviṣayatvātkathaṃ
bāhyamadhyavasātumarhati ? yadi manyeta na vikalpānāṃ viṣayaḥ svākāro'pi
tu saṃvedanasya | pratyakṣasya hi vikalpaviṣayo yo vikalpyate | na ca svarūpaṃ
vikalpyate | tasya sarvato vyāvṛttasyā'bhilāpasaṃsargayogyatvā'bhāvādviśada
pratibhāsatvācca | tasmādātmanyavikalpāḥ svā'vabhāsaṃ bāhyatayā vikalpayanta
statra tatra pravartayanti svavyavahārārthinaḥ | pāramparyeṇa ca bāhyādutpattestatprati
bandhāttatprāpterna visaṃvādayanti lokamiti | yadāha—svapratibhāse'narthe
'rthādhyavasāyena pravṛttiriti | āha anarthe svabhāsamarthamadhyavasyatīti nirvaca
nīyametat | nanvarthamāropayantīti kiṃ vikalpasya svarūpā'nubhavā''ropa uta
vyāpārāntaraṃ svarūpā'nubhavaḥ ? na pūrvaḥ kalpaḥ | anubhavasamāropayorvika
lpā'vikalparūpatayā draṃvakaṭhinavattādātmyā'nupapatteḥ | vyāpārāntaratve kramaḥ
samānakālatā vā | na tāvat kramaḥ | kṣaṇikasya vijñānasya kramavadvyāpārā
'yogādakṣaṇikavādināmapi buddhikarmaṇorviramya vyāpārā'yogānna kramavadvyā
pārasambhavaḥ | anubhavasamāropau samānakālāviti cet | bhavatu samāna
kālatvaṃ kevalamātmabhāvā'vasthita eva vedyaḥ | parabhāvavedane svarūpavedanā'nu
papatteḥ | tathā cātmajñānasya grāhyagrāhakā''kārā'nubhūto'rthaśca samāropitaḥ |
na tvātmā'vedyamāne samāropito nārthaḥ samāropyamāṇaḥ pratyakṣavedyaḥ | sa ca
samāropaḥ satā'rthasyā'sato vā grahaṇameva | na ca jñānā'tiriktasya grahaṇa
sambhavatītyuktam | svapratibhāsasya bāhyādbhedā'graho bāhyasamāropastato
bāhye pravṛttiriti cet | sa kiṃ gṛhyamāṇe bāhye na vā ? na tāvad gṛhyamāṇe |
uktaṃ hyetat na tadgrahaḥ sambhavatīti | agṛhyamāṇe tu bhedā'graheṇa na
pravṛttiniyamaḥ syāt | anyeṣāmapi tadānīmagrahaṇāt | anyatrāpi pravṛtti
prasaṅgāt | tasmānnā'nubhavavyavasthāpyo nāpyanumānavyavasthāpyaḥ | tadapi
pratyakṣavadeva grahītumadhyavasātuṃ vā bāhyaṃ nā'rhatītyuktaprāyam | na ca tādṛśo
bāhyapratibandhastadātmā tatkāryo vā heturapyasti | nanvayamasti | ye
yasminsati kādācitkāste sarve tadatiriktahetusāpekṣā yathā vicchinnā''gama
vacanapratibhāsapratyayāḥ santānāntarāpekṣāḥ | tathā ca vivādādhyāsitāḥ
satyapyālayavijñānasantāne ṣaḍapi pravṛtti pratyayā iti svabhāvahetuḥ | yaścā
'sāvālayavijñānasantānā'tiriktaḥ so'rtho vāsanāparipākapratyayakādācitka
tvāt kadācidutpāda iti cet | nanvekasantatipatitānāmālayavijñānānāṃ
tattatpravṛttivijñānajananaśaktirvāsanā | tasyāśca svakāryajananaṃ pratyābhimukhyaṃ
paripākastasya ca pratyayaḥ svasantānavarttipūrvakṣaṇaḥ | santānāntarāpekṣā'nabhyupaga
[p.185]
māt | tathā ca sarve'pyālayasantānapatitāḥ paripākahetavo, na vā kaści
dapi | viśeṣā'bhāvāt | yo yathā'bhipretaḥ paripākahetuḥ kṣaṇa ālayasantā
napatitastathā sarve'pītyaviśeṣaḥ | kṣaṇabhedācchaktibhedastasya ca kādācitka
tvātkāryakādācitkatvamiti cet | nanvevamekasyaiva nīlavijñānajananasāmarthya
tatprabodhajananasāmarthya cati kṣaṇāntarasya tanna syāt | sattve vā kathaṃ
kṣaṇabhedācchaktibhedaḥ ? ityālayasantānavartinaḥ sarve samarthā iti samarthahetu
sadbhāve vā kāryakṣepā'nupapateḥ | svasantānamātrā'dhīnatve niṣedhyasya kādā
citkatvasya viruddha sadātanatvam, tasyopalabdhyā ca kādācitkatvaṃ nivartta
mānaṃ hetvantarāpekṣatve vyavatiṣṭhata iti pratibandhasiddhiḥ | na ca santānāntara
nimittatvaṃ sarveṣāṃ pravṛttivijñānānāṃ vijñānavādibhiraṅgīkṛtamapi tu kasya
cidevā''gamavacanapratibhāsasya vijñānasya | api ca santānāntaranimittatve
tasyāpi sadā sannidhānānna kādācitkatvaṃ syāt | na khalu sāndratarasya
santānasya kādācitkatvasya deśato viprakarṣasambhavo, vijñānavāde vyatirikta
deśā'bhāvāt amūrttatvācca vijñānānāmadeśātmakatvāt | nāpi kālataḥ |
apūrvasattvaprādurbhāvā'nabhyupagamāt | tasmādanumānamasti bāhyasādhanamiti
