tadidamanupapannam | svasantānamātraprabhavatve'pi pratyayakādācitkatvopapattau
sandigdhavipakṣavyāvṛttikatvena hetoranaikāntikatvāt | bāhyanimittatve'pi
kathaṃ kadācinnīlasaṃvedanaṃ, kadācitpītasaṃvedanaṃ, bāhyanīlasannidhāne svakāraṇa
nibandhanaḥ śaktibhedo'pi bhaviṣyati | santānino hi kṣaṇabhedahetavaste ca
pratikārya bhidyanta eva santāne sannidhānā'sannidhānābhyāmiti cet | atha
pītasannidhāne kimiti nīlādijñānaṃ na bhavati pītajñānaṃ vā bhavati ?
sāmarthyādasāmarthyāccetarasminniti cet | kutaḥ punarayaṃ sāmarthyabhedaḥ ?
svahetubhedāditi cet | evaṃ tarhi kṣaṇānāmapi svakāraṇabhedanibandhanaḥ
śaktibhedo bhaviṣyati | santānā hi kṣaṇabhedahetavaḥ pratikāryaṃ bhidyanta eva |
na ca santāno nāma kaścideka utpādakaḥ samasti, yadabhedāt kṣaṇā na
bhidyeran | nanūktaṃ na kṣaṇabhedā'bhedābhyāṃ śaktibhedā'bhedau, bhinnānāṃ kṣaṇānāme
kasya sāmarthyasyopalabdheḥ | anyathaika eva pūrvakṣaṇo nīlavijñānajananaḥ syānna
kṣaṇāntarāṇīti na nīlākārāṇi vijñānāntarāṇyutpadyeran, yadi bhinnānāṃ
naikaṃ sāmarthyam | nanu nīlasantānināmapi mitho bhinnānāṃ naikamasti nīlā
kārādisāmarthyamiti sannidhāne 'pi nīlāntarasya na nīlavijñānamutpadyata |
santānibhede'pi śaktyabheda iti cet | kṣaṇabhedena kimaparāddhaṃ ? yena tasminnapi
na bhavati | atha santānibhede śaktibheda eva, tathā ca kāryabhedaḥ | na hi yade
[p.186]
nirālambanaṃ tu jñānamayuktameva |
kasminnīlākāraṃ jñānaṃ jñānāntaramapi nīlākāraṃ tadeva bhavitumarhati | tada
smākaṃ kṣaṇabhede'pi samānam | anyatrā'bhiniveśāt | api cotpattisā
rūpyābhyāṃ viṣayatve cakṣurālokasaṃskārāṇāmapi yathākramaṃ rūpagrahaṇaṃ prati
niyamaspṛṣṭatājñānajanakatvasārūpyaistadutpattyā ca nīlavadgrahaṇaprasaṅgaḥ |
atyantasārūpyādvā viṣayatve nīlavijñānasamanantarapratyayasya nīlārthādapi
sadṛśatarasya nīlajñānaviṣayatvā''pattiḥ | satorapyutpattisārūpyayoradhyavasāyā
dviṣayabhāvaḥ sa cārtha eva nānyatreti cet | na | uktottaratvāt | nivedita
metadadhastāt | yathā nā'dhyavasāyagocaro bāhya iti |
tadanena prabandhena vedyatvalakṣaṇo hetuḥ samarthito vijñānavādinām | sa
caivaṃ prayogamārohati | yadvedyate yena vedanena tatastanna bhidyate yathā jñānasyā
''tmā, vedyante ca nīlādaya iti viruddhavyāptopalabdhiḥ | niṣedhyabhedaviruddhaḥ
khalvabhedastena vyāptaṃ vedyatvaṃ bhinnasya sā''kāranirākāravijñānapravedanīyasyā
'nupapatteriti prapañcitamadhastāt | tasmādviruddhā'bhedavyāptavedyatvopalabdhirūpā
'nupalabdhireva bhedā'bhāvasādhanīti sthitā buddhīnāṃ bāhyā'nālambanatvasiddhiḥ |
ataścaitadevaṃ sahopalambhaniyamo jñānasyārthena | tathā hi | yadyena
niyatasahopalambhanaṃ tattato vyatiricyate yathaikasmāccandrād dvitīyaścandraḥ |
niyatasahopalambhanajñānamartheneti vyāpakaviruddhopalabdhiḥ | abhedo hi sahopa
lambhaniyamena vyāptaḥ | na khalu nīlapīte bhinne niyamena sahopalabhyete |
tadviruddhaścā'yaṃ niyamaḥ svaviruddhamaniyamaṃ nivarttayaṃstadvyāptaṃ bhedamapi nivartta
yati | tadvyāpakaviruddhopalabdhyā bhedā'bhāvasiddhiḥ | yathāha sahopalambha
niyamādabhedo nīlataddhiyoriti | kathaṃ tarhi jñānātmakātpratipatturarthasya
bhedabuddhirnīlametaditi ? balavadvedyatvasahopalambhaniyamā'numānena bādhitā
bhrāntireva | yadāha bhedaśca bhrāntivijñānairdṛśyetendāvivadvyam iti |
api ca yaḥ pratyayaḥ sa sarvo bāhyā'nālambanaḥ | yathā svapnapratyayaḥ,
pratyayaśca vivādādhyāsita iti svabhāvahetuḥ | nirālambanaṃ vijñānaṃ tanmadhya
pātinī ca pratibheti sāpi tathetyata āha—nirālambanaṃ tu jñānamayuktameva |
idamatrā''kūtam | naitannirālamba natve pramāṇaṃ bhavitumarhati | tathā hi |
kimanubhavasiddhasya nīlādervedyasya tattvaṃ hetuḥ kriyeta, aho svidanyasya ? tatra
yadyanubhavasiddhasya tato'nahaṅkārāspadamasitādirūpaṃ sthūlaṃ nīlādivicchinnaṃ
vedyamanubhūyate, nāsyā''ntarajñānasvabhāvatā śakyasādhanā | yathāhācāryaḥ sa
[p.187]
bahirdeśasambandhaḥ pratyakṣamupalabhyate iti | athā'nyasya | tasyā'siddhaṃ
vedyatvam | jñānākāratve cāsya na kācitkṣatiḥ | yadyucyeta nā'nubhūtatvamātrasya
hetutvamapi tvanubhūtā'vasitatvasya | avasāyaśca svabhāvata ekavyāvṛttiniṣṭha
rūpā'vagāhī na manāgapi vyāvṛttyantaramavagamayati | mā bhūdiha sadabhilāpa
vikalpaparyāyatā sarveṣāṃ gavādiśabdavikalpānām | vyāvarttyabhedā'nuvidhāyinaśca
vyāvṛttibhedāḥ | tadihā'vedyavyāvṛttikṛtavedyatvā'vagāhī vikalpo nā'bāhya
vyāvṛttikṛtaṃ bāhyatvaṃ vigāhata iti yadeva kiṃcitpāṃsulapādakena hālikenāpi
nīlādervedyatvamanubhūtāvasitaṃ tad bāhyatvanirāsāya samarthamiti na virodhaḥ |
tadasat | na khalvekā'vasāyāgocaratayā virodhamāpādayāmo, 'pi tu yadbāhyatvaṃ
sthūlatvaṃ cā'nubhūtāvasitaṃ nīlādestābhyāṃ virodhī | tathā hi | jñānakāratve
nīlasya nānādigdeśavyāpitā sthūlatvaṃ vicchinnadeśatā ca bāhyatva na
syātām | na khalvekatvena grahaṇā'grahaṇe eva paraṃ virudhyete yena vijñānadeśe
nīlādeḥ sthūlasya grahaṇādevā'virodhaḥ syādapi tu taddeśatvā'taddeśatve'pi |
tathā hi | bhinnadeśayorapi parasparaparihāreṇa sthitirasti vicchinnā'vabhāsa
jñānakāraṇaśaktibhirbhinnadeśatvam | tatra pūrvadigbhāgasthitānnīlādvicchinnamava
bhāsate pratyakṣeṇa, na tu parasparātmakamanubhavā'nusāriṇā niścayena yathā'vasī
yate tathā'navasāyātparasparavicchedena cā'vasāyāt | dṛśyamānaṃ cā'nubhavā'nu
sāriṇā niścayena yathā'vasīyate tathaiva sadasati bādhake | yadi ca pūrvadikka
mitaradikkaṃ gṛhyeta naiva digbhinnabhāvadarśanaṃ bhavedasti ca tat | tasmāddigantara
sanniviṣṭaṃ rūpamitaradikkaṃ na gṛhyate | tathā sati yathā nīle pītamadṛṣṭamiti
nīlaparicchede tadabhāvo vyavacchidyate tadvacca pītādivatpūrvadikke rūpe taditara
dikkaṃ rūpamadṛṣṭamiti pūrvadikkarūpaparicchede tadabhāvo vyavacchidyate | itarathā
tādūpyameva na paricchinnaṃ syāt | tadabhāvavacca pūrvadikkā'bhāvavantastaditara
digbhāgavarttinaḥ svabhāvād vyavacchettavyāḥ nīlaparicchedeneva nīlā'bhāvavantaḥ
pītādaya iti siddhastaddeśatvā'taddeśatvayoḥ parasparaparihārasthitilakṣaṇo
virodhaḥ | yatra caivaṃ vidhyārvirodhaḥ tatra mānā'bhedo yathā nīlapītayoḥ |
evaṃvidhaścā'nubhūyamāne nīle sthūle virodha iti vyāpakaviruddhopalabdhiḥ |
pratiṣeddhavyaḥ khalu bhedo virodhā'bhāvena vyāptastadviruddhaśca virodha iti | tena
yāvaduktaṃ bhavati nīlaṃ vā neti tāvaduktaṃ bhavati nīlaṃ sthūlamiti | na ca
nānaikajñānātmeti yuktam | ekatvasya nānātvavirodhāt | na ca nānāvijñānā
nīti sāmpratam | pratyekaṃ svā'vabhāsamātrā'vasāyitve nānāvijñānānāṃ sthūlā
bhāsā'nubhavā'bhāva prasaṅgāt | na ca nānākārā'nubhavavāsanājanmā vikalpa
[p.188]
stadanubhavākārasaṅkalanātmakasthūlākārabhāgbhavitumarhati | tasyāpyekatve'nubhava
vattadābhāsatvā'nupapatteḥ | nānātve vā sthūlābhāsā'bhāvaprasaṅgāt | vikalpa
viṣayatvena ca viśadābhasatā'nupapatteḥ | yadāha | na vikalpānubandhasya spaṣṭārtha
pratibhāsateti | na cā'nubhūta sthūlatvaṃ yena tadupādhirvikalpe vispaṣṭābhāsaḥ 
syāt | tasmādarthe vijñāne ca sthūlatvā'nupapatterasadetadabhyupagantavyam | na ca
tadvijñānaṃ sambhavati | sadasatorekatvā'nupapatteḥ | etenedamiti vicchinnā'va
bhāso vyākhyātaḥ | tasmādanicchatā'pi vijñānānāṃ manovedyatvamabhyupaganta
vyam | tathā ca vedyatvamabhedasya na vyāpyamiti viruddhavyāpakopalabdhirasiddhā
nā'bhedopasthāpananena bhedaṃ niṣeddhumarhati | nanu na parapratibandhamātrādarthasiddhiḥ,
uktaṃ ca sākāramanākāraṃ ca vijñānamanātmabhūtavedanāsamarthamiti | tatkimi
dānīṃ samarthanīya eva sthūlā'nubhavo nāsti vā ? na tāvannāsti, nīlavatta
syāpyanubhavāt | tadapahnave cā'nirmūlatayā nīlā'nubhavasyā'pahnavaprasaṅgaḥ |
tathā cāsya kuto bhedo vā ? athāsti na cā'samarthanīyaḥ | na ca jñānā''kāraḥ |
kā tarhi tasya samarthanā ? ata evā'nātmanaḥ prakāśanavijñānaṃ svakāraṇā''sā
ditā'dṛṣṭāntasiddhasvabhāvabhedaṃ na kathantāspadaṃ bhavitumarhati | na khalvanubhavo
dṛṣṭāntamapekṣate | mā bhūdbhūmyādāvasādhāraṇagandhādiguṇā'nanubhavanam | kaḥ
punarasyā'nātmasambandha ? iti cet | athā'nyatrāpi sambandhasya kaḥ samba
ndhibhyāṃ sambandhaḥ ? rūpeṇaiva sa tatheti na sambandhyantaramapekṣate | na jātu
sambandhināvantareṇā'sau nirūpyate | na ca jātu tasminsati sambandhināva
sambandhinau bhavatastena sambandhisa mbandhanaparamārthaḥ | sambandhasambandhinau
tathotpannau sambandha iti cet | nanu sthūlā'nubhavo'pi na sthūlamantareṇa
nirūpyate | na ca tasminsati sthūlamananubhūtaṃ bhavatīti rūpeṇaiva so'pi sthūla
prakāśanaparamārthaḥ sambandha iva vā tathotpannau sambandhināviva vā sambandhana
paramārthau | seyaṃ samarthanā | yadyevamiyamanubhavamātrasyaivā'nātmagrāhitāmāpāda
yati, na hi nīlādyanubhave'pi nīlādimantareṇa nirūpyate | na ca tasminsati
nīlādayo nā'nubhūtā iti sthūlā'nubhavavadanātmano nīlāderanubhavaḥ svabhāvata
eva na sambandhāntareṇeti tattvam | atadātmano'pi vedyatvopapatteranaikāntikaṃ
vedyatvam | evaṃ sahopalambhaniyamo'pyanaikāntikaḥ | svakāraṇā'dhīno hi tādṛśaḥ
svabhāvabhedo vijñānasya yenātmā'nātmanorasāveka evopalambhaḥ | na ca svabhāvaḥ
kathantāgocara ityuktam | etenaiva pratyayatvamapi pratyuktam | svapnādipratyayānāṃ
ca samāropitabāhyaviṣayatvātsādhyavihīnatvaṃ dṛṣṭāntasya | tathā ca viruddho
hetuḥ |
[p.189]
api cā'siddhaḥ sahopalambhaḥ | na hi ya eva nīlasyopalambhaḥ sa eva
tadupalambhasyeti niyamaḥ samasti | na khalvayamanubhavagocaraḥ | tathā hi |
visphā ritākṣaḥ pratipattā nīlamidamiti paśyanna tadaiva tadanubhavamapi paśyati |
nīlapravaṇo hi tadanubhavitā nā'nu bha vapravaṇo'nubhūyamānopādhitayā'nubhavamapi
paricchinatti | na khalvanubhavā navabhāse nubhūyata iti bhavati | na hyasti sambhavo
daṇḍo na gṛhyate gṛhyate ca daṇḍīti | tasmādviśeṣaṇatayā'nubhavagrahaṇe'pyanubhūyamāna
pravaṇo'nubhaviteti siddhaḥ sahopalambhaniyama iti cet | naitat | na hyanubhūyate
nīlamityanubhavo'pi tu nīlamiti | asatyanubhavaprakāśe viṣayā na prakāśeran |
tadadhīnaṃ hi prakāśaṃ teṣām | na khalu na līno nīlā'nubhavastānalamavabhā sayitum |
na hyanubhavādanyo viṣayā'vabhāsaḥ, yamayamanavabhāsamāno'pīndriyādivaśādadhīto
'pi tu paraprakāśanaparamārtho'nubhava eva tatprakāśaḥ | tathā ca yadi nilīyate
tannilīnasvabhāvatvādviṣayānapi nā''bhāsayediti nā'nubhavo na viṣayā
bhāsanta ityāyātamāndhyamaśeṣasya jagataḥ | tasmādanubhavaprakāśā'yattaprakāśatva
meṣitavyaṃ viṣayāṇām | api caikasminnarthe sphuṭā'sphuṭā'vabhāso yugapadupalabhyate
pratipattṝṇām | na cā'sāvarthadharmaḥ sphuṭatvādirekasminyugapadviruddhadharmasamā
veśā'sambhavāt | vijñānabhedastūpapadyata iti vijñānasya dharmaḥ | tathā ca tajjñāna
manubhavasiddham |
tadevamanubhavādeva svasaṃvedanasiddhau yo nāma svasiddhāntābhyāsā''hita
vyāmoho vipratipadyate taṃ pratyanumānamupanyasyate | yadyatprakāśā''yattaprakāśaṃ
tattasminprakāśamāna eva prakāśate, yathā daṇḍaprakāśā''yattaprakāśo daṇḍī |
pratyayaprakāśāyattaprakāśaśca pratyetavyo rūpādiriti svabhāvahetuḥ | na ca
pratyayaprakāśā''yattaprakāśatvaṃ rūpādīnāmasiddham | uktaṃ hi 'tadanāyattatve
prāptamāndhyameva jagato'śeṣasyeti | na ca svasaṃvedane svātmani vṛttivirodhaḥ |
na hi saṃvedanasya svarūpādanyā vṛttirasti | yataḥ svātmani kartṛkarmabhāvo
virudhyeta | saṃvedanameva tvaparādhīnaprakāśamāvirbhūtasvabhāvamātmasaṃvedanamācakṣate |
na ca svabhāvena bhāvo virudhyate | tasmādātmasaṃvedanasiddheḥ siddhaḥ sahopalambha
niyama iti |
tadanupapannam | hetvasiddheḥ | na hi nolādayo'nubhavaprakāśā''yatta
prakāśāḥ kiṃ tvanubhavā'dhīnaprakāśāḥ | evaṃ cennetrādibhiranekāntaḥ | kiṃ
punarnīlādīnāmanubhave āyattatā ? nanvanubhavaprakāśe'pi kimāyatate ? yadi
[p.190]
na kiñcinnā'tiriktaṃ vacaḥ | anubhavaprakāśā''yattaḥ | prakāśa iti cet | nanu
sa eva tadadhīna ityuktaṃ bhavati, taccā'yuktam | na hyātmanyāyatate, bhedā' 
śrayatvāttadbhāvasya anubhava evātmanaḥ parasya ca saṃbandhitayā'nubhavabhedo
'nubhavaprakāśo nīlaprakāśaḥ, tathā cā'nubhavaprakāśādhīnatā nīlaprakāśasyeti
cet | evaṃ tarhi svaprakāśā'dhīnasya nīlasambandhitetyuktaṃ syāt | tatraitāva
dvaktavyam | bhavatu svaprakāśaḥ kutastu nīlasambandhite?ti | svabhāvāditi cet |
kṛtaṃ tarhi svaprakāśena, svabhāvata eva tatsambandhopapatteḥ | etāvāṃstu viśeṣaḥ,
tavāsau kalpitā tatsambandhitā, mama tu pāramārthikī, yāṃ kila jñeyā'bhivyakti
riti jñātateti vā karmateti vā''cāryāḥ pracakṣate | nanu karmateti ceyamarthadharmaḥ,
kathamarthadharmo'yamatītādiṣu sambhavatītyuktam | neyamarthadharmaḥ, kiṃ tu jñāturātmano
jñeyasambandhabheda eva jñātatā | sa ca jñātṛdharmo jñāyamānārthā'vacchinno jñeyaṃ prati
guṇabhāva ātmanaḥ sukhādivatpramitsā'napekṣa samanantarapratyayotpannamānasapratyakṣa
vedanīya ekasamaveto'pyubhayā'dhīnanirūpaṇatādyāśrayaḥ śaktiriva sambandhaḥ
sambhavati | nanvayamanubhava eva | tathā hi | so'pi vijñeyaviśeṣapratibandha
vyavahārā'nuguṇaḥ puṃso dharmabheda ākhyāyate | satyamīdṛśaḥ | na tvasau sambandhaḥ |
vyāpāro hi sa ātmanaḥ | tasya tu phalaṃ sambandha ātmano jñeyapravaṇatā
lakṣaṇaḥ | kutaḥ punaranubhavā'vagatiḥ ? asmādeva tu sambandhalakṣaṇātphalāt |
yadāha 'jñāte tvanumānādavagacchatīti | ātmanaḥsaṃyogaviśeṣādeva kādāditka
phalasambhavānnā'nubhavā'numānamiti cet | tatkimidānīmātmanaḥ saṃyogaviśeṣo
jāta ityātmanā'rtha prati guṇabhūtena bhavitavyam ? kriyā hi guṇapradhānabhāvamā
pādayati bhāvānāṃ pākādiṣu tathopalambhāt caitraudanādīnām | nanu kriyopa
dhānanibandhanaścetsambandhaḥ kathamapratītāyāṃ kriyāyāṃ śakyaḥ pratipattum ?
pratītā cetkriyā na tarhi sambandhātphalabhūtāttatpratītiḥ | naitadasti | kriyā
mantareṇa hi na sambandhaḥ, na punaḥ kriyāpratītiṃ vinā na tatpratītirapratītā
yāmapi kriyāyāṃ sambandhapratīteḥ | yathā''gneya iti śabdā'vagatād dravyadevatā
sambandhādanumīyate yajistathotpannavijñā nādarthaṃ prati guṇabhūtamātmānaṃ manasaiva
sahasā'vagamya taddhetuṃ saṃvedanakriyāmanumimīte | ātmamanaḥsaṃyogamātrāttada
nupapatteḥ | yathāha—pūrva sā gṛhyate paścād jñānaṃ tajjñātatāvaśāditi |
sphuṭatvamapi jñeyatāviśeṣa eva na saṃvedanaviśeṣaḥ | tathā hi | sāmānya
viśeṣatadvatāṃ saṃvedyatā sphuṭatā, tadviparītatvamasphuṭatā, yato dūre vṛkṣa iti
[p.191]
tasya jñeyā'vabhāsakāryalakṣaṇatvāt |
viṣayā'bhāvācca vyutpattyasambhave tadanapekṣa sya pravṛttiḥ syāt |
pratiyanti na punaraśoka iti vā sahakāra iti vā | pratyāsīdantastu malaya
mārutā''kampamānanavapallavamānandaniṣpandamadhupamālānipīyamānamadhuramadhurasa
stabakakadambakaṃ raktā'śokapādam | tasmājjñeyatāyāmayaṃ viśeṣo na jñāne
iti siddhaṃ parāñcaḥ pratyayā na pratyañca iti |
prayogastu vivādā'dhyāsitāḥ pratyayāntareṇaiva vedyāḥ pratyayatvāt ye ye
pratyayāste sarve pratyayāntaravedyāḥ yathā na pratyayāntareṇaiva vedyāḥ | avidyamā
nasyā'vabhāse'tiprasaṅgāt jñāyamānasyaivā'vabhāso'bhyupeyaḥ | tathā ca vijñānasya
svasaṃvedane tadeva tasya karma kriyā ceti viruddhamāpadyeta | yathoktam—
aṅgulyagraṃ yathātmānaṃ nā''tmanā spraṣṭumarhati |
svāṃśena jñānamapyevaṃ nātmānaṃ jñātumarhati || iti |
yatpratyayatvaṃ vastubhūtamavirodhena vyāptaṃ, tadviruddhavirodhadarśanātsvasaṃve
danānnivarttamānaṃ pratyayāntaravedyatvena vyāpyata iti pratibandhasiddhiḥ | evaṃ
prameyatvaguṇatvasattvādayo'pi pratyayāntaravedyatvahetavaḥ prayoktavyāḥ | tathā ca
na svasaṃvedanaṃ vijñānamiti siddham |
asiddhaśca sahopalambhaniyamaḥ | phalabhedasamunneyaṃ ca vijñānamabhinnaṃ
sanna phalabhedāyā'lamiti vināpi nīlādyābhāsaṃ svabhāvata eva bhedātpratikarma
vyavasthāpyate | tadevamasati bādhake'nubhava evātmagrāhī bāhyasadbhāve pramāṇaṃ,
na pramāṇāntaramatrā'nusaraṇīyam | na hi kariṇi dṛṣṭe cītkārena tamanumimate
prekṣāvantaḥ | prameyaparīkṣā tūpekṣitā vistarabhayāt | tadanena prabandhenokto'
bhisandhirācāryasya nirālambanaṃ jñānamayuktamevetyavadhārayataḥ |
nirākaraṇāntaramāha—tasya jñeyā'vabhāsakāryalakṣaṇatvāt | jñeyā'vabhāso
jñeyatā'rthasya tayā'tyantaparokṣaṃ vijñānamanumātavyaṃ, tatra pramāṇāntarābhāvādi
tyuktam | nirālambanatve tu jñeyā'vabhāsa eva nāstīti kena jñānamanumīyeta ?
yasya nirālambanatvaṃ sādhyeta | tadanena dharmiṇaḥ sādhyadharmeṇa virodho darśitaḥ |
tathā cā''śrayā'siddhatvaṃ hetoriti bhāvaḥ | api ca vyutpannasambandhaḥ śabdaḥ
pratibhāṃ bhāvayet | vyutpattiścā'rthena saha śabdasya na vijñānena kāryeṇa tasyā
''nantyād vyabhicārācca | sāmānyasya ca pratibhātvasyā'kāryatvātkārakasya
[p.192]
nanu kārakasaṃsargiṇī kriyā ālambanamiṣṭaiva | na | tasyā apavarttaka
vacanajanmanyapi prakhyāyāṃ sāmyālliṅādiyuktebhyo vacanebhyaḥ pratibhābhedāda
pravṛttiprasaṅgāt |
nanu nā'pravartakamanivartakaṃ vā vacaḥ samasti | vaiyarthyāt | kartavyatā
paryavasāyinī hi vākyapravṛttiḥ | kathaṃ tarhi bhūtā'nvākhyāyino vedāntā
laukikavacāṃsi ca ? kartavyatābhedāt pravṛ ttinivṛttipratipattiviṣayatvāt |
sarvā hi karttavyatā bhedavatī | vedānteṣu tāvadātmatattvapratipattikarttavyatā |
loke cedamiha nidhyādi pratipattavyamiti vākyaparyyavasānam |
svakārye vyutpattyanapekṣatvāt | arthaśca nirālambanatvā'bhyupagame nāstīti
vyutpatterasambhavāttadanapekṣasya ca prāk pravṛttiḥ syā'nna cāsāvastītyāha—
viṣayābhāvācca vyutpatterasambhave tadanapekṣasya pravṛttiḥ syāt |
kaścidāha—nanu kārakasaṃsargiṇī kriyā''lambanamiṣṭaiva, tathāpi na
bhavadabhimatopāyatā sidhyatīti bhāvaḥ | dūṣayati—na, tasyāḥ kāṣṭhaiḥ pacaya
tītyatrā'pravarttakavacanajanmanyapi prakhyāyāṃ sāmyātpravṛttiḥ syādapravṛttau vā
kāṣṭhaiḥ pacediti liṅādiyuktādapi vākyātpratibhāyā abhedādapravṛttiprasaṅgāt |
parastvapravarttakavacanajanmanītyamṛṣyamāṇa āha—nanu nā'pravarttakama
nivarttakaṃ vā vacaḥ samasti | kutaḥ ? vaiyarthyāt | parapratyāyanāya hi vacana
muccārayanti prekṣāvantaḥ | tadeva ca paraḥ pratyāyayitavyo yatprayojanavat |
na ca bhūtākhyānaṃ tathā | tasmātpravarttakaṃ nivarttakaṃ vā vaco nā'nyā
gatirastītyarthaḥ |
nanu bhūtānvākhyānamapi dṛśyate loke vede ca nidhimāneṣa bhūbhāgaḥ,
sadeva somyedamagra āsīditi, atrāha—karttavyatāparyavasāyinī hi sarvā
vākyapravṛttiḥ | yadyevaṃ kathaṃ tarhi bhūtānvākhyāyino vedāntā laukikāni ca
bhūyāṃsi vacāṃsi ? uttaram—karttavyatābhedāt | pravṛttinivṛttipratipatti
viṣayatvāt | etadeva sphuṭayati—sarvā hi karttavyatā bhedavatī | tameva
bhedamāha—vedānteṣu tāvadātmapratipattiḥ karttavyatā | ātmā jñātavya iti hi
pratipattirātmani vidhīyate, tatparatvaṃ ca vedāntānām | loke'pi cedamiha
nidhyādi pratipattavyamiti vākyaparyavasānam | dūṣayati—naitatsāram | idamatra
vikalpanīyam | ātmapratipattikarttavyatāparatvamupaniṣadām | tisraḥ khalvimāḥ
pratipattayaḥ sambhavanti | śrutamayī, cintāmayī, sākṣātkāravatī, ceti | tatra
[p.193]
naitatsāram | śabdātpratipatteru tpatteḥ punastatrā'vyāpārāt | karmma
phalasambandhabodhavat | niścayo'pi yadi śabdāt, saṃjāta eva, anyataścettadā
prāmāṇyam | arthaparatāpi na dṛṣṭārthatvātsvādhyāyā'dhyayanavidheḥ karmmavat |
aviśiṣṭastu vākyārtha iti nyāyāt | anyathā savidhikasyaiva vivakṣitārthatva
prasaṅgāt | na ca puruṣārthatvāya vidhiḥ prārthyate | jñānasya jñeyā'bhivyakti-
śrutamayīmadhikṛtyāha—śabdātpratipatterutpatteḥ punastatrā'vyāpārāt | na śruta
mayī vidheyā | śabdādvidheyamavagamyate uttarakālā pravṛttirvidhiprayojanaṃ na
punaḥ śabdā'vabodha eva, ātmāśrayadoṣaprasaṅgāditi bhāvaḥ | karmaphalasaṃbandha
bodhavat yathā karmavidhivākyaṃ na karmaphalasaṃbandhabodhe vidhītaramapekṣate tathopa
niṣado'pi nā''tmabodhe ityarthaḥ | śaṅkate—niścaya ātmani | vidheḥ phalaṃ
vikalpya dūṣayati—yadi śabdāt saṃjāta evā'nyataścet pramāṇāntarāt tatsā
pekṣatayā'prāmāṇyaṃ śabdasya | atha vidhiṃ vinā vedāntānāṃ śabdasvarūpaparatā
syāt svarūpaprāthamyāt | yadā tu vedāntairātmā jñātavya iti vidhīyate tadā
tairnā'vivakṣitārthaiḥ śakyamātmajñānaṃ kartumiti vivakṣitā'rthamarthyate vidhi
rityāha—arthaparatā'pi na vidheḥ prayojanam | kutaḥ dṛṣṭārthatvāt svādhyāyā
'dhyayanavidheḥ | upaniṣadāṃ karmavidhivat | aviśiṣṭastu vākyārtha iti
nyāyāt | prayojanavadarthā'vabodhanaṃ hi dṛṣṭaṃ prayojanamadhyayanavidheḥ | akṣara
grahaṇārthatve tvadṛṣṭārthatā syāt | yathā karmavidhīnāṃ vinaiva vidhyantareṇā'rtha
paratvamautsargikamevaṃ, vedāntānāmapītyarthaḥ | yadi punarna svādhyāyavidhidṛṣṭārtho
yadi vā nautsargikamarthaparatvaṃ vacasāṃ tatra savidhikamevā'vivakṣitārtha syādi
tyāha—anyathā savidhikasyaiva vivakṣitārthatvaprasaṅgāt | yadyucyeta vidhyabhāve
hi dhātvartha eva bhāvanābhāvyaḥ, samānapadopādānatvāt bhavedvidhau sati tadati
krameṇā'samānapadopādānopyārthavādiko'pyapunarāvṛttirūpaḥ puruṣārtho labhyata
iti vidhiḥ prārthyata ityata āha—na ca puruṣārthatvāya vidhiḥ prārthyata iti |
kutaḥ ? jñānasya jñeyā'bhivyaktiphalatvāt | tasya ca tannāntarīyakatayā
vidhimantareṇāpi prāpaṇāt | nanvapunarāvṛttiḥ phalaṃ, śrūyate hi vacanaṃ 'na sa
punarāvarttate iti, na cā'sau vidhiṃ vinā śakyā pratipattumityatrāha—phalānta
rā'nabhyupagamāt | apunarāvṛttiriti hi mokṣa ucyate | na cā'sau sādhya iti
vakṣyate | api ca pratipattividhiparatve vedāntānāṃ brahmasvarūpasattāyāmaprā
māṇyaprasaṅgaḥ | na hyasau vidhinā'pi tadviṣayo nityatvāt, yena vidhirapekṣyeta
pratipattiviṣayo hi vidhiḥ pratipattiviṣayamātmatattvamapekṣate | tathā ca
[p.194]
phalatvātphalāntarā'nabhyupagamāt kriyāparatvācca vidhervastusvarūpasattāyā
avivakṣitatvāttatpratipattyarthamanuṣṭhānaṃ gamyeta nātmatattvā'vabodhaḥ syāt |
ataḥ śrūyamāṇo vidhirevaṃjātīyeṣvavivakṣitaḥ | nityaprāpteḥ |
nanu vidhirahitasyā'nuvādarūpasya sākāṅkṣasya prāmāṇyamalabhyam |
atha keyamanuvādatā ? pramāṇāntarā'dhigatārthatā, āhosvidbhūtārthatā ?
pramāṇāntarā'vigatārthatā cedvidhiśūnyamapyatādṛśamapyapauruṣeyaṃ nā'nuvādaḥ |
bhūtārthatā cet kutaḥ sāpekṣatā ? yati laukikavacasāṃ pramāṇāntarā'dhigata
gocaratvād bhūtārthānāṃ sāpekṣatvaṃ dṛṣṭam oṣadhapānādiṣu, vidhayo'pi lauki-
tathātmaśabdaḥ pramāṇamiti cet | na | ātmatattvamantareṇa samāropeṇāpi
pratipatterupapatteḥ | evaṃ ca tatparatvā'vabhāvādvedāntānāṃ brahmavidyātvaṃ prasiddhaṃ
bādhyetetyetadāha—kriyāparatvācca vidhervasturūpasattāyā avivakṣitatvāt tatpra
tipattyarthamanuṣṭhānaṃ gamyeta nātmatattvā'vabodhaḥ syāt | nanvātmā jñātavya iti
śrutirasti, na cāsau pramādaḥ | tulyaṃ hi sāmpradāyikam, tadekavākyatā ca
vedāntānām, ato niṣprojanatayā nā'vajñānamarhatītyetadupasaṃhāravyājena nirā
karoti—ataḥ śrūyamāṇo vidhirevaṃjātīyakeṣu vidhānā'narheṣvavivakṣitaḥ,
nityaprāptatvāt | siddhā hi vedāntebhyo vidhi vināpi brahmasvarūpapratipattiḥ |
na hyasti sambhavo viditarūpasaṅgatiḥ śabdanyāyavidvedāntebhyo'rtha na pratipadyata
iti prāptatvāt, evaṃjātīyakeṣu śrūyamāṇo vidhiravivakṣitaḥ | na ca pramādo'nu
vādato'pyupapatteḥ | nanu mā bhūtpratipattividhiḥ phalaṃ, prāmāṇyāyaiva tu vidhirā
śrayiṣyate, anyathā tadayogāditi codayati—nanu vidhirahitasyā'nubādasvarūpasya
tata eva sākāṅkṣasya prāmāṇyamalabhyam, anapekṣalakṣaṇatvāttasyā,'nyathā
smṛterapi prāmāṇyaprasaṅgāt | tadetadvikalpayati—atha keyamanuvādatā ?
pramāṇāntarā'dhigatārthatā''hosvidbhūtārthatā ? dūṣayati—pramāṇāntarā'dhi
gatā'rthatā cet | vidhiśūnyamapi tarhi bhūtārthamatādṛśaṃ pramāṇāntaraviṣayīkṛtā
rthamapauruṣaiyaṃ nā'nuvādaḥ | jñānapūrvako hi paścādvādo'nuvādaḥ | apauruṣeye tu
jñātuḥkarturabhāvānna jñānapūrvaka iti nā'nuvādaḥ | sāpekṣatvenetyanuṣajyate | pauruṣeyaṃ
hi vacaḥ pramāṇāntareṇārthaṃ pratītya nirmīyamāṇaṃ pramāntarāpekṣaṃ syānna tvevama
pauruṣeyamityarthaḥ | dvitīyapakṣamāśrityāha—bhūtārthatā cetkiṃkṛtā sāpekṣatā ?
bhūtārthatvasāpekṣatvayorna pratibandho'sti kaścidityarthaḥ |
śaṅkate—yadi laukikavacasāṃ pramāṇāntarā'dhigatagocaratvād bhūtārthānāṃ
sāpekṣatvaṃ dṛṣṭamiti vaidikānyapi bhūtārthāni sāpekṣāṇi | tatrottaram—
auṣadhapānādiṣa vidhayo laukikāstathā sā'pekṣā iti vidhimadvaidikamapekṣeta |
[p.195]
kāstatheti vidhimatadapyapekṣeta | athā'poruṣeyatvānnā'pekṣate tato'pauruṣeyatva
mitatvāpyapārtham | atha pramāṇāntarasaṃbhavād bhūtārthasya sā'pekṣatā, na vidhi
manaḥ | vidhyarthe tadasaṃbhavāllaukikasyāpi tadanapekṣatāprasaṅgaḥ |
puruṣabuddhipramaṇatvādviniyogasya tatsāpekṣatayā tatra sāpekṣatvam apau
ruṣeye vacasi | so'pi na kevalaṃ buddhyantaranirapekṣaḥ, mānāntaraviṣayo'pi |
tarhyapauruṣeyavacananimittā'napekṣa iti yato'sābapekṣatvakāraṇaṃ na mānāntarā'
sambhavaḥ | pauruṣeyatā ca sāpekṣatve hetuḥ | niyoganiṣṭhe'pi laukike vacasi
sāpekṣatvānna pramāṇāntarasaṃbhavaḥ | tathā ca sāpekṣatve pratyakṣā'numānayoḥ paraspara-
atha vidhimadvaidikamapauruṣeyatvānnā'pekṣate tato'pauruṣeyatvamitaratrāpi vaidika
bhūtārthe'pyapārthamiti | atha pramāṇāntarasaṃbhavād bhūtārthasya vaidikasya sāpekṣatā na
vidhimataḥ | kutaḥ ? vidhyarthe tadasaṃbhavāt | pramāṇāntarāsaṃbhavāt | uttaraṃ
laukikakasyāpyauṣadhādividhestadanapekṣatvaprasaṅgaḥ | yadi manyeta śabdānāmautsa
rgikī niyogārthatā tadarthasvārthatā vā | tanna | tadarthasvārthāni dravyaguṇakarma
vacanāni na svārthaṃ kevalameva niyogena ghaṭayitumīśata iti viniyogamantarā
śrayante | so'yamajahatkriyākārakasaṃsarga eṣāmarthasya niyogasaṃbandha iti vaidikī
sthitiḥ | utsargasyā'napoditasya tādavasthyāt ato'pauruṣeye niyoga
viniyogayorabuddhipūrvakatvādanapekṣatvaṃ śabdasya | lokastu pramāṇenopalabhyā'rtha
manyatropalabdhaṃ vā samāropya vacanāni racayati | yathā jvaritaḥ pathya
maśnīyādvi, mokṣamāṇo nairātmyaṃ bhāvayedi'ti | na ca niyogaḥ pramāṇāntara
viṣaye samāropaviṣayo vā, anyatrā'nupalabdheḥ | tadviṣayastu viniyogaḥ pramāṇā
ntaragocaraḥ samāropagocaro vā saṃbhavati | yathopalambhanaṃ ca vivakṣā, yathāviva
kṣitaṃ vacanaracanetyautsargikīṃ niyogaparatāmapahāya lakṣaṇayā viniyogaparatā
mavalambate, pauruṣeyaṃ ca vastutaḥ sāpekṣamiti codayati—puruṣabuddhiprabhavatvā
dviniyogasya sāpekṣatayā tatra sāpekṣatvam | apauruṣeye tu so'pi viniyogo'pi na
kevalaṃ niyogo buddhyantaranirapekṣaḥ | puruṣabuddhiprabhavatvaṃ cā''ptavākye viniyo
gasya jñeyatayā'nāptavākye cā''ropitatayā'vagantavyam | pariharati—mānāntara
viṣayo'pi bhūtarūpo'rtho niyoga ivāpauruṣeyavacananimittā'napekṣaḥ | yato'pauru
ṣeyatā'napekṣatvakāraṇaṃ na mānāntarā'sambhavaḥ | pauruṣeyatā ca sāpekṣatve hetuḥ |
atraivā'nvayavyatirekau darśayati—niyoganiṣṭhe'pi lokavacasi sāpekṣatvāt |
ayamarthaḥ | yadi na laukikaṃ vacanaṃ niyoganiṣṭhaṃ bhavet tato vaidikamapi na
[p.196]
viṣayasaṃbhavātsāpekṣatvātpramāṇatvahāniḥ | ataḥ pramāṇāntaropalabdhivivakṣā
puraḥsarapravṛttitvādvidhimaditaratvādvā vacanaṃ sāpekṣam | atathābhāvādvaidikamubhaya
mapyanapekṣamiti caturasram | tena pramāṇatvārthamapi na vidhivivakṣā |
syāt, lokāvagatasambandhasya śabdasya vede'pi bodhakatvāt | agṛhītasambandhasya
tu bodhakatve prathamaśrāviṇo'pyarthapratyayaḥ prasajyeta | tasmādyadyapi pareṣāṃ rāddhānte
na niyoganiṣṭhatā pauruṣeyāṇāṃ vacasāmapi tu viniyogapradhānatā tathāpyāpādya
niyoganiṣṭhatvametaduktamiti mantavyam |
na pramāṇāntarasambhavaḥ sāpekṣatve hetuḥ | satyapi tasmin vaidikasya
viniyogasyānapekṣatvāt | atraiva prasaṅgāntaramāha—tathā ca sāpekṣatve pratyakṣā
numānayoḥ parasparasya viṣayasambhavātsāpekṣatvātpramāṇatvahāniḥ | etadupa
saṃharati—ataḥ pramāṇāntaropalabdhivivakṣāpuraḥsarapravṛttitvādvidhimaditaradvā
puruṣavacaḥ sāpekṣam | atathābhāvāttu vaidikamubhayamapyanapekṣamiti caturasram |
prakṛtamupasaṃharati—tena pramāṇatvārthamapi na vidhivivakṣā |
kaścidāha satyamapauruṣatvādanapekṣatā, tadeva tu pramāṇāntarā'gocarārtha
tayā sidhyati | na khalvanālikhya buddhāvartha tatpratyāyanāya vacanaṃ racayanti
laukikāḥ | na ca pramāṇāntarāgocaro'rthaḥ śakyo buddhāvālikhitum | autsa
rgikī ca niyogaparatā śabdānāṃ, neyamasatyapavāde śakyā nihnotum |
āmnāye niyogaśca pramāṇāntarā'gocaraḥ śabdaikasamadhigamya iti tadarthatvā
dapauruṣeyatā''mnāyasya | pauruṣeyatve hi mukhyā niyogārthatā hīyeta | ata eva
vākyatvādayopyāgamavādhitaviṣayā nāmnāyasya pauruṣeyatāṃ sādhayitumīśate |
tataścā'napekṣatā | na ceyaṃ vidhā jvaritaḥ pathyamaśnīyā'dityādiṣu sambhavati,
spaṣṭadṛṣṭena pauruṣeyatvena mukhyamapi niyogārthatvamapodya lakṣaṇayā viniyoga
paratvā''pādanāt | yadi punarāmnāyasya bhūtārthatā bhavettadā tasya pramāṇāntara
yogyatayā śakyavacanatvena poruṣeyatā durvārā bhavediti pratyatnena bhūtārthatāma
panīya kāryaparatvamāmnāyasya vyutpādyata iti |
tadanupapannam | sidhyedayaṃ manoratho yadi tathāvidho vidhirāmnāya
gocaraḥ syāt, na tvayaṃ tadgocaraḥ saṃbhavatītyupapāditaṃ niyogaparīkṣāyām |
api cā'yamaupaniṣado'pi puruṣo na pramāṇāntaragocara iti kathamupaniṣadāmapi
pauruṣeyatā durvārā ? tathāvidhasyānyasya siddharūpasya pramāṇāntaragoca
ratvamupalabdhamityasyāpi siddhatayā tadarthatva miti cet | evaṃ tarhi kriyātmanaḥ
[p.197]
nanvanyadevedaṃ śabdaprabhavādātmatattvagocaraṃ jñānaṃ vidhīyate | na hi
śābdajñānaparivedyaṃ brahmasvarūpam | vākyalakṣaṇo hi śabdaḥ pramāṇaṃ, padārtha
saṃsargātmā ca tadarthaḥ, pratyastamitā'khilabhedaprapañcaṃ cātmatattvaṃ, tatkathamasya
gocaraḥ ? tasmātpralīnasakalā'vacchedollekhamadvaitatattvā'vabhāsātmakaṃ jñāna
manyadeva śābdādvidhīyate |
vārtametat | na khalu phalāṃśo vidhigocaraḥ | niṣprapañcātmatattvā'va
bhāsaśca phalameva na tato'nyadabhīpsyate | mokṣa iti cet | tato'vyati
rekāt | saprapañcātmatattvāva'bhāso hi saṃsāraḥ | niṣprapañcātmāva'bhāso
kāryasya mānāntaragocarateti kāryatayā niyogasyāpi tadarthatvamiti tadarthasyāpi
vedasya pauruṣeyatā durvārā bhavet | svarūpeṇa vā tasya pramāṇāntarā'gocaratve
aupaniṣadasyā''tmano'pi taditi sarva samānamanyatrābhiniveśāt | tadāstāṃ
tāvat |
evaṃ tāvanna śrutamayī pratipattirvidhātavyetyuktam | nanu mā nāmeyaṃ
vidhāyi, sākṣātkāravatī tu vidhāyiṣyate iti codayati—nanvanyadevedaṃ
śabdaprabhavādvijñānādātmatattvagocaraṃ jñānaṃ vidhīyate | kasmātpunarātmatattva
gocarattvaṃ na śabdasyetyata āha—na hi śābdajñānaparivedyaṃ brahmasvarūpaṃ, vākya
lakṣaṇo hi śabdaḥ pramāṇaṃ, padamātrasyā'pramāṇatvāt | tathāpi kim ? ityāha—
padārthasaṃsargātmā ca tadarthaḥ, saṃsargaśca saṃsṛjyamānā'nekārthatantratvādeko'pi
nānātvādūṣitaḥ | na cā''matattvamevaṃbhūtamityāha—pratyastamitā'khilabhedaprapañcaṃ
cātmatattvaṃ, tatkathamasya gocaraḥ ? upasaṃharati—tasmātpralīnasakalāvacche
dollekhamadvaitatattvāvabhāsanātmakaṃ jñānamanyeva śābdādvidhīyate |
etad dūṣayati—vārttametat | na khalu phalāṃśo vidhigocaro, yathāha
maharṣiḥ—tasya lipsā'rthalakṣaṇā niṣprapañcātmatattvāvabhāsaśca phalameva |
niṣprapañceti vigalitanikhiladuḥkhatvamāha | prapañco hi duḥkhaṃ, tattvagrahaṇe
nā''nandātmatāṃ sūcayati | bahulamānandaśruteḥ phalatvameva darśayati | na
tato'nyaddhīpsyate vigalitaduḥkhatvādānandātmatvācca |
codayati | mokṣa iti cet | śrūyate hi na sa punarāvarttate iti |
pariharati—na | tato niṣprapañcā''tmatattvā'vabhāsādavyatirekāt | avyati
rekameva darśayati—saprapañcātmā'vabhāso hi saṃsāraḥ | niṣprapañcātmā'va
bhāso mokṣaḥ | saṃsāraviruddhasvabhāvo mokṣa iti tatsvabhāvakathanāya saṃsāro'pi
rdāśataḥ | na cā'sau pravṛttiviṣayo'pi, siddhatvādityāha—svātmani sthitiḥ |
[p.198]
hi mokṣaḥ svātmani sthitiḥ | anyathā kāryyatvādamokṣāt | bandhahetuśca
karmmādiprapañco'vidyā, taducchedaśca vidyaiva | yadi ca na kathaṃcidapi śabda
jñānaviṣayo brahma kathaṃ tajjñānavidhiḥ śakyapratipattiḥ ? viṣayataśca jñāna
nirūpaṇaṃ, siddhaśca tannirūpayati, tatsiddhiśca pramāṇāntarā'bhāvāt syāt |
tasyāpi cedaviṣayaḥ tadasiddherjñānaviśeṣā'pratipattervidhyapratipattiḥ | atha kathaṃ
cidviṣayaḥ, na tarhi śābdajñānā'napekṣatā | pratipatti karttavyatāparatvācca
vacanasyedṛktā nā''gamārthaḥ syādityuktam | nāpyārtho'rthaḥ | adhyāropeṇāpi
pratipatteḥ saṃbhavāt | ato na niyogā'nupraveśena vastutattvaṃ prakāśyate | na hi
anena parā'bhimatā mokṣāvasthā vyudastā veditavyā | tadvyudāsahetavaśca brahma
siddhau prapañcitāḥ | yadi tu na svātmani sthitiḥ, api tu parā'bhimato mokṣa
statrāha—anyathā kāryatvādamokṣāt | athā'vidyocchedaḥ kasmānna bhavati ?
ityata āha—bandhahetuśca karmādirūpaḥ prapañco'vidyā, nā'nyā | tataḥ kiṃ ? tadu
cchedakaśca vidyaiva | niṣprapañcātmatattvāvabhāsa ātmatattvameva saḥ, sa eva ca
mokṣa iti na pravṛttiviṣayaḥ | atraiva dūṣaṇāntaramāha—yadi ca na kathaṃcidapi
śabdajñānaviṣayo brahma, kathaṃ tajjñānavidhiḥ śakyapratipattiḥ ? kimiti na śakya
pratipattiḥ ? ityata āha—viṣayataśca jñānanirūpaṇam | co hetvarthe |
nirūpyatāṃ viṣayeṇa, ko doṣa ? ityata āha—siddhaśca viṣayo jñānaṃ nirūpa
yati so'pi sidhyatu, kā kṣatiḥ ? ityata āha—tatsiddhiśca pramāṇāntarā'
bhāvācchabdādabhyupetavyā | tasyāpi cedaviṣayaḥ tadasiddherviṣayā'siddherjñāna
viśeṣā'pratipattervidhyapratipattiḥ |
śaṅkate | atha kathaṃcittadviṣayaḥ | yadyapi tatsvarūpaṃ na śabdagocarasta
thāpi bhedaprapañcavilayadvāreṇa nirūpaṇādviṣayabhāva ityarthaḥ | pariharati—na
tarhi śabdajñānā'paricchedyatā | prathamapakṣoktaṃ dūṣaṇamatrāpi yojayati—
pratipattikarttavyatāparatvācca vacanasya, īdṛśaṃ pratipattavyamitīdṛktā nā''gamārthaḥ
syādityuktaṃ, yatparaḥ śabdaḥ sa śabdārthaḥ | pratipattikarttavyatāparaścenna pratyeta
vyatvapara ityarthaḥ | syādetat | karttavyatāyāḥ pratipattigocaratvātpratipatteśca
pratipādyaviṣayatvātkarttavyataiva pratipattiṃ tadviṣayaṃ cā''kṣepsyati, tataśca
pratyetavyatattvaniścaya ityata āha—nāpyārtho'rthaḥ | kutaḥ ? adhyāropeṇāpi
pratipattisaṃbhavāt | satyaṃ, pratyetavyamākṣipati pratipattiviṣayā karttavyatā, na
tu tasya tathātvamapi, samāropeṇāpi pratyetavyopapatterityarthaḥ | idaṃ dūṣaṇapamu
[p.199]
tatra śabdasya prābhāṇyam | evamanapekṣatayā prāmāṇyasiddheḥ kāryyā'nvayarahita
bhūtādikamapyavagamayitumalamāmnāya ityasamañjasametat | yadi kāryyarūpa eva
vedārthaḥ kathaṃ tarhi mantrā'rthavādāḥ sopaniṣatkāḥ ? yasmād bhūtādikamarthaṃ
codanaiva gamayati | katham ? kāryyamarthamevamavagamayantī bhūtādikamapi
gamayati |
saṃharati—ato na niyogā'nupraveśena vastutattvaṃ prakāśyate | na hi tatra vastu
tattve śabdasya prāmāṇyam |
upasaṃharati—evaṃ bhūtārthatve'pyanapekṣatayā prāmāṇyasiddheḥ kāryā'nvaya
rahitaḥ svarūpeṇā'pi bhūtādikamarthamavagamayitumalamāmnāya iti | codanā
hīti bhāṣyagranthasya nibandhanakṛto vyākhyānamasamañjasamityāha—ityasa
mañjasametat | sarvanāmaparāmṛṣṭaṃ nibandhanakṛto vyākhyānamāha—yadi kāryarūpa
eva vedārthaḥ kathaṃ tarhi mantrā'rthavādāḥ sopaniṣatkā bhūtārthā'bhidhāyina ityā
śaṅkyā'vatāritaṃ codanā hīti, tasyā'rthamāha—yasmād bhūtādikamartha codanaiva
gamayati | yadi bhūtādirarthaścodanāyā na sa tarhi kāryarūpo vedārthaḥ, na cā'na
pekṣetyāśayavānpṛcchati—katham ? uttaraṃ—kāryamavagamayantī bhūtādikamapi
gamayati | dvividho hi śabdaḥ kāryā'bhidhāyī liṅādiḥ, bhūtādyabhidhāyī ca
laṅādiḥ | tatra liṅādestāvatkāryā'bhidhāyitvādeva tatparatvam | laṅādayastu 
bhūtādikāryaparatayā tadanvitamevā'bhidadhati | tataśca teṣāmapi kārye tātparyā
tkāryārthatvaṃ siddham | tathā ca bhūtādyabhidhāyināmapi nirapekṣatvam | yathāha—
nā'nyatkiṃcanendriyamiti | idaṃ ca vyākhyātam, na ca pramāṇāntaragocaro'ya
mityādinā | etaduktaṃ bhavati na kāryasambandhinā rūpeṇa pramāṇāntareṇā'va
gamyata iti | tadidamasamañjasam | bhūtārthaparatve'pyanapekṣatvena prāmāṇyopapatteḥ |
syādetat | vṛddhavyabahārā'bagamyaḥ śabdā'rthasaṃbandhaḥ | sa ca pravṛttyātmakaḥ |
pravṛtiśca kāryā'vagamapūrvikā prekṣāvatām, ataḥ kārye eva sarve sākṣāttadanvita
svārthatayā vā labdhavyutpattayaḥ śabdā iti kāryarūpa eva vedārtha iti brūmo na
punaḥ prāmāṇyāyeti | tadapyuktam anyathāpi vyutpatterupapatteḥ | tathā hi |
indramitraḥ pratipannadevadattānandanibandhanaputrajanmā devadattagṛhādeva vārttāhareṇa
saha devadattābhyāśamāgataḥ paṭavāsopāyanā'rpaṇapuraḥsaraṃ diṣṭyā devadatta vardhase,
te putro jāta iti vārttāharavyāhārasamanantaramunmīlatpulakakapolamutphullalo
canayugalamatismeraṃ mukhamahotpalamavalokya devadattamutpannapramodamanumimīte,
tatpramodahetuśca putrajanmavijñānam, anyasya tadānīmanupalabdheḥ | vacanoccāraṇā
[p.200]
anye tu śābdajñānasaṃtānavidhānamicchanti | tasya saṃhṛtanikhila
bhedollekhaviśuddhaviśadajñānaphalatvāt | atrāpi nātmarūpā'vagatiḥ | jñāna
prakarṣaścā'bhyāsanibandhanaḥ prathita iti na codanārthatā | tasmād dṛṣṭārtheṣu
karmmasu viśeṣavidhayaḥ | upaniṣadātmatattvaṃ tvanapekṣavidhyantarādvākyātpratīyate |
alaṃ vā gurubhirvivādena |
nantaraṃ ca tadbhāvādvacanasya tatra hetutāmavagacchati | na cā'samarthasyā'sāviti
sāmarthyamavaiti | evaṃvidhaviṣaye harṣajanakaṃ hetvantaraṃ śaṅkamānā jananījāra
śaṅkayā''tmano'pi brāhmaṇyaṃ prati saṃdihānā nā'dhikārabhājo brāhmaṇocitāsu
kriyāsviti kṛtaṃ mīmāṃsā'bhyāsapariśrameṇa teṣām | api cā'vyutpannakāṣṭha
śabdārtho vyutpannavibhaktyarthaśca varttamānā'padeśe'pi devadattaḥ sthālyāmodanaṃ
kāṣṭhaiḥ pacatīti pacatyarthe karaṇaṃ paśyati, tasya kāṣṭhaprātipadikārthatāṃ ca
pratipadyata iti siddhaṃ vivādādhyāsitaṃ vacanaṃ kāryā'saṅgatā'rthagocaraṃ tatra
pramājananāt yadyatra pramājananaṃ tattadgocaraṃ yathārūpādigataṃ cakṣurādīti |
samprati cintāmayīṃ pratipatiṃ nirācikīrṣustāmupanyasyati—anye tu
śābdajñānavidhānasantānavidhānamicchanti | na ca tatphalarūpaṃ yena na vidhīyate,
api tu phalasyā''tmatattvasākṣātkārasya sādhanam, ato vidhātavyamityāha—
tasya saṃhṛtanikhilabhedollekhaviśuddhaviśadajñānaphalatvāt | saṃśayādiparyudāso
viśuddhiḥ | sākṣātkāro viśadatā | tatra tāvatpūrvoktaṃ doṣamāsañjayati—
atrāpi nā''tmarūpā'vagatiḥ, pratipattividhiparatvādvedāntānāmiti | bhāva
nā''dheyaviśadābhatvaṃ ca vijñānānāmanvayavyatirekasiddhamiti prāptatvānna
śābdajñānasamānajñānasantāno bhāvanāparanāmā vidheya ityāha —jñānapraka
rṣaścā'bhyāsanibandhanaḥ prathita iti na codanārthatā yuktā |
nanu tasmācchānto dāntaḥ samāhito bhūtvā''tmanyevā''tmānaṃ paśyet
ityādivākyānyātmadarśanaṃ śāntatādiguṇopetaṃ vidadhatyeva, na cā'vidhīyamāne
ātmadarśanāśrayiṇo guṇā vidhātuṃ śakyante iti śāntatādiguṇavidhānāyā''tma
darśanamapi vidhātavyamityata āha—tasmād dṛṣṭārtheṣviva karmasu viśeṣavidhayaḥ,
upaniṣadātmatattvaṃ tvanapekṣitavidhyantarādvākyātpratīyate | idamatrākūtam |
guṇavidhayo hi bhāvārthasiddhimapekṣate na punastadvidhānamapi tena, yathaiva rāgataḥ
prāpte dravyā'rjane brāhmaṇādīnāṃ pratigrahādayo niyamyante tathehāpi nityānanda
mayamātmatattvaṃ vigalitanikhiladuḥkhā'nuṣaṅgaṃ sākṣāccikīrṣoranvayavyatirekā
[p.201]
bhavatu vā pratipattikarttavyatā niyogadvāreṇātmatattvāvagatirlokavacāṃsi
bhūyāṃsi bhūtā'nvākhyānānītyuktam |
nanu vaiyarthyamatroktam | pratipattividhāvapi tulyametat | bhūtārthaprati
patteranatirekāt | athā'viparītātmapratipattireva | sā bhūtā''khyānaparyya
vasāne'pi tulyā | astu tarhi pravṛttiparyyavasāyitvameva sarvavacasāṃ bhūto'pyarthaḥ
kathyate | tenā'nyatra pravṛttiṃ kartuṃ tatraiva vā, anyathārthābhāvāt | na | kutuhala
nivṛtterapyarthātvāt | tathā hi | ajihāsitā'nupāditsitadūradeśarājasaṃjñā
caritagocarāḥ kutūhalināṃ dṛśyante praśnāḥ | kutūhalanivṛttiparāṇi ca tajjñānāṃ
'vagatadhyānasādhanasyā'tmadhyāne pravṛttasya śāntatvādividhiriti | tatasti
sṛṇāmapi pratipattīnāṃ vidhānā'narhatvādātmatattvapratipādanaparatvaṃ hi
vedāntānāmupapāditam | sarva caitad brahmasiddhau kṛtaśramāṇāmanāyāsamadhigama
nīyamiti nehā'smābhirupapāditam |
idānīmabhyupetyāha—alaṃ vā gurubhirvivādena | anuttarārhatāsāmyā
daupacārikaṃ gauravam | bhavatu pratipattikarttavyatā niyogadvāreṇātmatattvāvagateḥ,
lokavacāṃsi tu bhūyāṃsi bhūtā'nvākhyānānītyuktam | tathāpyapravarttakavacana
janmanyapi prakhyāyāṃ sāmyāditi hetuḥ siddho bhavatītyarthaḥ | nanu nā'pravarttakaṃ vaco,
yato vaiyarthyamatroktam | pariharati—pratipattividhāvapi tulyametat | kutaḥ ?
bhūtārtha prattipatteranatirekāt | yadi bhūto'rthaḥ prayojanā'bhāvānnā''khyāta
vyastarhi pratītirapi tadviṣayā na vidhātavyā | na hyetasyāḥ pratyetavyavyāpte
ranyat phalamastītyarthaḥ | athā'viparītā''tmapratipattirevārthaḥ pratipattividheḥ |
pratipattividhipakṣe tāvadayamartho na bhavatītyuktam | abhyupagamya parihārāntara
māha—sā prattipattirbhūtākhyānaparyavasāne'pi tulyā |
punaścodayati—astu tarhi pravṛttinivṛttiparyavasāyitvameva sarvavacasāṃ
bhūto'pyarthaḥ kathyate | tenā'nyatra pravṛttiṃ kartuṃ tatraiva vā, anyathā'rthābhāvāt |
idamatrā''kūtam | sarvo hi śabdaḥ parārthamuccāryate prekṣāvadbhiḥ, paraśca tame
vā'rtha pratipadyate yastena pratipitsitaḥ, yastasya heya upādeyo vā, upekṣaṇīya
pratyāyane prekṣāvattvavyāghātāt | viśeṣatastu vedarāśeḥ svādhyāyā'dhyayanavidhi
samayasamāsāditapuruṣārthaśeṣabhāvasya | tena bhūtārthapratipatterbhavatu tatra śabdasya
kāraṇatvaṃ, kiṃ tu śabdasya pravṛttinivṛttiparatvamiti | pariharati—na kutūhala
nivṛtterapyarthatvāt | etadeva darśayati—tathā hi | ajihāsitā'nupāditsitadūra
[p.202]
vacāṃsi | aṭavīvarṇakādayaḥ pravṛttinivṛttiśūnyā bhāratādiṣu bhūtaparyyavasāyino
rūpādeva prītihetavaḥ, prītyutpattyarthaṃ ceṣṭākhyānāni na pravṛttaye nivṛttaye vā
śroturityalamatiprasaṅgena |
śiśuśakuntādīnāṃ yathā viprakṛṣṭaḥ śabdo nimittam bhāvanā'nugamāt
pratibhāyāḥ, tathā tādṛśamevopāyajñānaṃ pravṛttinivṛttimukhena kiṃ neṣyate ?
jātiviśeṣādayaśca śabdabhāvanāyā ivopāyajñānabhāvanāyāḥ prabodhakāḥ |
deśarājasaṃjñācaritādigocarāḥ kutūhalināṃ dṛśyante praśnāḥ | kutūhalanivṛtti
parāṇi ca tajjñānāṃ vacāṃsyaṭavīvarṇṇakādayaśca pravṛttinivṛttiśūnyā bhāratādiṣu
bhūtaparyavasāyino rūpādeva prītihetavaḥ | prītyutpattyartha ca iṣṭā ''khyānāni na
pravṛttaye nivṛttaye vā śroturityalamatiprasaṅgena | idamatrākūtam | prekṣāvanto
na pravṛttinivṛttiviṣayamevā'rthamavagamayanti, api tu prayojanavantam | prayojana
vattā ca kvacidarthasvarūpapratyayottarakālābhyāṃ pravṛttinivṛttibhyāṃ parisamāpyate,
kvacidarthasvarūpapratyayenaiva | taduktam—kutūhalinivṛtterapyarthatvāditi | evaṃ
brahmasvarūpamapyapahatapāpmajñānamāgamaupaniṣadaṃ svayameva puruṣārtha iti svādhyāya
vidhyavirodhānnopaniṣadāṃ pravṛttinivṛttiparateti sāmpratam | tatsiddhaṃ kriyā
sādhanayoginī samā'nyasminniti na pratibhāyā ālambanam | na ca nirālamba
neyam | tatpāriśeṣyāt hitopāyatāviṣayeti |
yaduktaṃ svaraviśeṣādāśrayaviśeṣāt śiśuśakuntādīnāṃ pūrvapūrvaśabdabhāva
nā'nugamādviprakṛṣṭaśabdavyāpārā pratibhā pravṛttinibandhanaṃ nopāyajñānamiti,
tatrāha—śiśuśakuntādīnāṃ yathā viprakṛṣṭaḥ śabdo nimittaṃ bhāvanānugamātpra
tibhāyāḥ, tathā tādṛśamevopāyajñānaṃ pravṛttinivṛttinibandhanaṃ bhāvanā'nugama
mukhena kiṃ neṣyate ? kiṃ viprakṛṣṭaśabdena ? upāyajñānasyeva pravṛttihetutayā
anyatra svā'nubhavasiddhatvāt | yathā caitattathopariṣṭādvakṣyate | yadi manyeta
upāyajñānā''hitā cedbhāvanā pravṛttihetuḥ athā'nubhūtacaraśakuntajanmano
manuṣyasyāpi kimiti na tayā pravṛttiḥ ? ityata āha—jātiviśeṣādayaśca
śabdabhāvanāyā ivopāyajñānabhāvanāyāḥ prabodhakāḥ | atha śabdabhāvanāpi
kimiti na tathā ? jātiviśeṣādṛṣṭāditi cet | upāyajñānabhāvanāyāmapi
tulyametadityarthaḥ |
[p.203]
nanu pratibhālambanasya svarūpato'niṣpatteḥ śabdajñānākāreṇaiva nirū
paṇā | na ca pratyutpannaḥ śabdo'sti | tadasyāśrayaṇīyā bhāvanā | naitatsāram |
aniṣpattirhi avartamānatvādvā'nekārthasamāhārādekātmabhāvādvā pratibhā
lambanasyocyate | śabde'pyasau tulyā | so'pi tarhyatīto varṇasamāhārātmako
naikaḥ samasti | bhedadarśanā'bhāvāt, abhedadarśanāccetyuktam |
atra codayati—nanu pratibhālambanasya samīhitopāyasya kriyāviśeṣasya
svarūpato'niṣpatternā'sāvabalambanam | na ca nirālambanaṃ jñānamityuktaṃ bhavataiva,
ataḥ śabdajñānākāreṇaiva nirūpaṇā | na ca pratyutpannaśiśuśakuntādīnāṃ
śabdo'sti | tadavaśyā''śrayaṇīyā'pūrvaśabdabhāvanā | pariharati—naitatsāram |
vikalpā'nupapatteḥ | tatra tāvadvikalpayati—aniṣpattirhyavartamānatvādvā'ne
kārthasamāhārādekātmabhāvādvā pratibhālambanasyocyate | varttamānaviṣayaṃ
khalūpāyajñānaṃ na pravartayati pravṛttaviṣayatvāt | ato'nāgataviṣayameṣitavyam |
tathā cā'sattvaniṣpattiḥ pratibhālambanasya | atha vā pratibhāviṣayasya padārtha
saṃsargātmakatvāttasya caikasyā'nupapatteḥ parasparavyavacchinnakriyākārakā'nekārtha
samāhāramātratvāt | na cā'nekātmakamekaṃ bhavitumarhati, anekeṣāmapyekatva
prasaṅgāt | tathā ca na saṃsargaḥ | nānātve ca saṃsargiṇa eva | tathā ca na saṃsarga
ityantābhāvo niṣpattirityarthaḥ | tadetadubhayaṃ parapakṣe tulyamityāha—śabde'pyasau
tulyā | so'pi hyatīto varṇasamāhārātmako naikaḥ samasti |
nanu varṇavyatiriktaḥ sphoṭātmā śabdaḥ parairabhimataḥ, sa caika iti sa
yuktaḥ pratibhālambanamiti śaṅkāṃ nirācikīrṣuḥ sphoṭanirākaraṇaparāṇi
siddhavadbhāṣyākṣarāṇi paṭhati—bhedadarśanā'bhāvādabhedadarśanāccetyuktaṃ bhāṣya
kṛtā | varṇebhyo bhidyate iti bhedaḥ sphoṭa ucyate | taddarśanā'bhāvāt | bheda
darśanā'bhāvāt | na jātu gauriti caturthyāmapi buddhau kramabhedavadanubhavakarmato
'praśliṣṭavarṇatrayā'vagāhinyāmanyo varṇebhyaḥ kaścidābhāsate | yadyucyeta
jarā rājeti tattve'pi varṇānāmarthadhībhedādanyo varṇebhyo vācakaḥ, sa ca sphoṭa
iti, tatrāha—abhedadarśanācca | bhidyanta iti bhedāḥ, na bhedā abhedā varṇāsteṣāṃ
darśanāt | etaduktaṃ bhavati, na tu jarā rājeti pañcamyorapi buddhyorvarṇā'ti
riktau parasparavibhinnavācakau cakāstaḥ, kiṃ tu ta eva varṇāḥ kramabhedavadanubhavā
hitasaṃskārabhedā bhāsante, te ca sphoṭadhībhedamādhāsyanti | avaśyābhyupe
tavyatvādanavayavapadavādināmapi saṃskārabhedasyeti diṅmātramatra sūcitamācārya
matamanavarttamānaiḥ
[p.204]
nanvevaṃjātīyakeṣvevā'bhedavastubuddherekaśabdātmakamupāgaman | na hyavastuṣu
saṃsargasamūhātyantā'sadādiṣu saṃsargiṣu vā nānātmasvekavastupratibhāsaḥ saṃbhavati
vinaikasya śabdātmanaḥ pratyāsāt pariṇāmādvivarttādveti | na | anyathā siddheḥ |
punaḥ prakārāntareṇa paraḥ pratyavatiṣṭhate—nanvevaṃjātīyakamevābhedavastu
buddherekaśabdātmakamupāgaman dhīrāḥ | tathā hi | kriyākārakaguṇaguṇiprayojana
prayojaniprabhṛtiṣu saṃsargiṣu samūhiṣu vā śiṃśapā'śokakiṃśukādiṣu saṃsargaḥ samūha
ścaiko vastubhūtaścakāsti, cakāsati vā śaśaviṣāṇā''lātacakrādayo'vastu
bhūtāḥ | saivaṃvidheṣvekabuddhirvastubuddhiścaikamanavayavaśabdātmānamevā'valambate |
atha saṃsargādaya eva tadvanto vā ekabuddhervastubuddheśca kasmānnā''lambanam ?
ityata āha—na hyavastuṣu saṃsargasamūhā'tyantā sadādiṣu vā nānātmasvekavastu
pratibhāsaḥ saṃbhavati | saṃsargādayo hi na paramārthasantaḥ saṃbhavanti | tathā hi
prasaṅgasādhanam | yadanekavṛtti tannānā tadyathā bhinnabhājanagatāni tālaphalāni |
anekavṛttiśca parābhyupagataḥ saṃsargādiriti svabhāvahetuḥ | nānātvā'bhāvāccā
'nekavṛttitvābhāva ityatiprasaṅgaviparyayo vyāpakā'nupalabdhiḥ | na caikatvā'neka
vṛttitvayoravirodhātsaṃdigdho vyatireka iti vācyam | avirodhā'siddheḥ | tathā
hi | ekasya sattvaṃ virodhyabhāvena vyāptaṃ tadviruddhaśceha virodhī vyāpakavirodho
palabdhyā bhāvikamekatvaṃ nivarttayati | tathā caikatvā'bhāvātsaṃsargiṇa eva na
saṃsargaḥ tathā hi ekasminsaṃsargiṇi paricchidyamāne tadabhāvo vyavacchidyate |
tadabhāvavantaścā'nye saṃsargiṇa iti te'pi vyavacchidyante | anyathā'sau saṃsargī 
paricchedyo na syāt | tathā tatsaṃsargisambandhe ca svabhāve saṃsargasya paricchidya
māne tatsaṃsargisvabhāvā'bhāvo vyavacchettavyaḥ | tadabhāvabantaśca saṃsargyantara
sambandhasvabhāvā iti te'pi vyavacchettavyāḥ | tadavyavacchede sa eva tatsvabhāvaḥ sa
evā'tatsvabhāvaśceti viruddhamāpadyeta | so'yaṃ taddeśatvā'ttaddeśatvalakṣaṇaviruddha
dharmādhyāsaḥ | tadāha deśakālā'vasthāviśeṣaniyataikasaṃsargavyavacchinnasvabhā
vāntaravirahādanekavṛtterekasya taddeśādiviśeṣavattā'nyena yoga iti | athai
kasminneva saṃsargiṇi gṛhyamāṇe tadgataḥ saṃsargo'pi gṛhyeta, tenaiva svabhāvena
saṃsargyantareṣvapyastīti tānyapi grahītavyāni | na ca tāni gṛhyante, iti
saṃsargo'pi na gṛhyeteti so'yaṃ grahaṇā'grahaṇalakṣaṇo virodhaḥ | tathā nānā
saṃsargiṣu varttamānaḥ saṃsargo bhāgaśo vartate, kārtsnyena vā ? na tāvadbhāgaśo,
nirbhāgatvāt | sabhāgatve vā'navasthāpātāt | nāpi kārtsnyena, saṃsargyantare
tadabhāvaprasaṅgāt | na ca prakārāntaramasti, tayoranyataraniṣedhasyā'nyataravi
[p.205]
saṃsargastāvadvākyārthalakṣaṇaḥ kārakopasarjanakriyāpradhāno'tulyapradhānasaṃsargī |
dhināntarīyakatvāt | tasmānna saṃsargasamūhau paramārthasantau | etenā'vayavisā
mānyādayaḥ prayuktā veditavyāḥ | śaśaviṣāṇādeścā'sattvaṃ prasiddhataramevetya
bhiprāyaḥ |
na caiṣa saṃsargādibādhakaprakāraḥ pade niravayave samastītyāha—vinaikasya
śabdātmanaḥ pratyāsātpariṇāmādvivartādvā | pratyāso'dhyāsaḥ | yathā vastutaḥ
svacchadhavale lākṣārasāvasekatiraskṛtadhavalimni sthavīyasi sphaṭikamaṇau
tanīyastayā lākṣālakṣaṇadravyāgrahaṇāllākṣāguṇā''ropeṇā'ruṇaḥ sphaṭikamaṇi
riti matiradhyāsaḥ | tathā nānāpadārtheṣu tiraskṛtabhedeṣu tasya sphoṭātma
nastāttvikamekatvamāropyaiko vākyārthaḥ saṃsargātmeti | samūhiṣu ca kiṃśukā
diṣu ca tiraskṛtabhedeṣu vanamiti vā samūha iti vā padasya tāttvikamekatva
māropyaikatvabuddhiḥ | evaṃ śaśaviṣāṇādiṣvasadbhūteṣu śaśaviṣāṇamityādipada
gatasattvā'dhyaropeṇa pratītiriti pratyāsārthaḥ | pariṇāmo'pi yathaikaṃ suvarṇatattvaṃ
kaṭakamukuṭakarṇikāṅgulīyādibhedena vipariṇamate tathā śabdatattvamapi nānā
padārtharūpeṇeti | etasmiṃśca darśane bhedānāṃ kathaṃcitpadatattvādbhinnānāṃ
pāramārthikatvam | evaṃ sphuraṇamātreṇa cā'sya pakṣasyopanyāsaḥ | vivartastu
yathā mukhamekamanekeṣu maṇikṛpāṇadarṣaṇādiṣu sadbhūteṣu vivarttamānaṃ tacchāyāpattau
vibhinnavarṇaparimāṇadeśaṃ cakāsti, na tu tatra tattvato bhinnavarṇaparimāṇadeśāni
mukhāni santi, evamanādyavidyāvāsanopadhānavaśātpadatattvamekamaneka
padārthātmanā prathate, na tu padārthāstato vibhidyamānātmānaḥ paramārthataḥ
santīti | ete pratyāsapariṇāmavivarttā matabhedena mantavyā iti |
pariharati—na | anyathāsiddheḥ | tāmevāha—saṃsargastāvadvākyārtha
lakṣaṇaḥ kārakopasarjanakriyāpradhāno'tulyapradhānasaṃsargī | atredamākūtam |
yattāvadidamuktam anekapravṛttitvānnānātvamiti, tadanekavṛttitvasyai katvenāpi
saha saṃbhavātsaṃdigdhavyatirekatayā nānātvena pratibandhāsiddherayuktam | na
caikasya nānāvarttinastaddeśatvā'taddeśatvalakṣaṇo virodhaḥ saṃbhavati | saha
saṃbhavāt | yuktametat | yadvastuni paricchidyamāne tadabhāvo vyavacchidyata iti
svā'bhāvavyavacchedarūpatvena bhavānāṃ tadavyavacchede paricchedā'nupapatteḥ, bhāva
paricchedātmakatvāt tadabhāvavyavacchedasya | atha saṃsargāntaraṃ kasmādvya
vacchidyate ? tadabhāvavattvāditi cet | tadeva kutaḥ ? pratyakṣeṇa tādātmyenā
'pratibhāsanāt | anyathaikasaṃsargivatsaṃsargyantaramapi pratheta | yathāha tatparicchi
[p.206]
natti tato'nyad vyavacchinnati | ta eva tarhi parasparā'bhāvātmāno na ye
pratyakṣeṇa kadācidapi parasparasaṃkīrṇāḥ pratyavabhāsante | saṃsargaḥ punarayaṃ nānā 
saṃsargiṣvabhinnaḥ pratyakṣeṇa na virodhamadhirohati |
syādetat | amūlyadānakrayī khalvayaṃ saṃsargo nāsti, yato na
vijñāneṣvākāramarpayati, pratyakṣatāṃ tu svīkartumicchatīti | tadayuktam |
nirākāravijñānavedyatvavyavasthāpanena nīlādīnāmapyamūlyadānakrayitvenā
'sattvaprasaṅgāt | na ca saṃsargiṇaḥ saṃsargaṃ jñātumutsahate | na cāyaṃ saṃsargibhyo na
bhidyate | viruddhadharmāspadatvāt | na khalvayaṃ saṃsargiṣu parasparaṃ vyāvarttamāne
ṣvanuvarttamāno'nuvarttamāneṣu ca nivarttamānastadātmā bhavitumarhati, trailokya
syaikyaprasaṅgāt | tenāstu taddeśatvatadabhāvayorvirodho, na punastaddeśatvā
'taddeśatvayorbhavitumarhati | na hi nañaḥ prayogamātrādvirodhā'vagatiḥ | mā
bhūtputro'putra iti nānātvamekasyāpi putrasyeti |
syādetat | yenaiva svabhāvenaikasminsaṃsargiṇi saṃsargo varttate kiṃ tenaiva
saṃsargyantare'pi kiṃ vā svabhāvāntareṇa ? iti | na tāvatsvabhāvāntareṇa |
svabhāvenopādeyabhede bhāvabhedaprasaṅgāt | tenaiva cetsaṃsargiṇāmatyantābhedaprasaṅga
iti | tatra vaktavyam | atha rūpaṃ yena svabhāvenopādeyaṃ rūpāntaraṃ janayati kiṃ
tenaiva svā''lambanaṃ vijñānaṃ svabhāvāntareṇa veti tulyamevaitat | yadyucyeta,
svabhāvo vā sāmarthya vā na rūpādatiricyate, tadeva tu svapratyayā'dhīnajanma
bhedamubhayajananamekaṃ pratyakṣeṇā'vasīyamānaṃ na bhedamarhati | na ca kāraṇābhedaḥ
kāryā'bhedamāpādayati, anyatvāt | tatkimidārnī nānāsaṃsargiṣu eko na
bhāsate pratyakṣeṇa saṃsargaḥ svabhāvāsaṃsargiṇaḥ, yena te tadabhedādabhinnā bhaveyuḥ |
pratītyasamutpādamātraṃ kāryakāraṇabhāvo na bhāvikaḥ kaścidubhayāśrayaḥ,
tenā'nyasya kāraṇasyā'bhede'pi na kāryābheda iti cet | nanu saṃsargibhiḥ saṃsarga
syāpi na kaścidaparasaṃsarga iti tadabhede'pi na saṃsargiṇāmabhedo'nyatvāt |
asaṃbandhaḥ saṃsargaḥ kathaṃ teṣāmeva ? iti cet | athā'saṃbandhe kimiti rūpameva
pratītya rūpavijñāne samutpadyete na punā rasādikamapi ? svabhāvabhedāditi
cet | so'trāpi tulyaḥ | tasmādvirodhā'siddheḥ saṃdigdhavyatirekatvādanekaṃ
vṛttitvaṃ nānātvaṃ pratyasādhanāṅgam | na ca grahaṇā'grahaṇalakṣaṇavirodhaḥ saṃsarge
saṃbhavati | sakalasaṃsargigra haṇā'dhīnanirūpaṇasya tasyā'grahaṇe grahaṇādeva
[p.207]
samūho'pi nāntareṇa tadbhāvanimittamekaṃ samūhinām | tatra tadupā
dhirekaprakāśaḥ | śaśaviṣāṇādayo'pi jātyantaraparidṛṣṭavasturūpeṇotprekṣyamāṇā
tadgrahaṇe vā grahaṇādeveti | vṛttivikalpaśca sākalyavaikalyalakṣaṇo'trānavakāśa
eva | nānātve kāryasamavāyinī sākalyavaikalye nā'bhinne tattve saṃbhavaḥ |
tasmātsvabhāvato vartate saṃsargiṣu saṃsarga ityetāvad vaktumīśmahe, na punaḥ
kārtsnyabhāgābhyāmiti | etena samūho vyākhyātaḥ, pratyākhyātāśca katipaye
avayavisāmānyanirākaraṇahetavo na sarve vistarabhayāt |
tadevaṃ pratyakṣasaṃsargaḥ saṃsargidvayā''dhāra eko guṇaguṇinorjātijātimatoḥ
kriyākārakayoravayavā'vayavinordravyayoranubhavasiddho vinā bādhakaṃ na pratyā
khyānamarhati | vākyārthalakṣaṇastu devadattaḥ sthālyāmodanaṃ pacatītyādiryadyapi
pratisaṃsargidvayaṃ bhidyate tathāpi kārakāṇāṃ kriyārthatvāttasyāḥ pradhānatvena
vākyārthabhūtāyā ekatvāttatpradhānaḥ saṃsargo'pyeka uktaḥ | nānākārakoparakta
kriyārthatve'pi ca vākyasya padārthamātrasyā'nyataḥ prāpteḥ prāptā'prāptavivekena
saṃsargo vākyārthaḥ | tadidamuktaṃ, kārakopasarjanakriyāpradhāna iti | yatra tarhi
kriyādvaya pratīyate yathā nijabhujavīryamāsthāya śūrānādiśūro jayati, tatra
kathaṃ vākyārthasyaikatvam ? ityata āha, atulyapradhānasaṃsargī | atulyo'ta
eva pradhānaṃ saṃsargī yasya sa tathoktaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati | na kriyetyeva
vākyārtho'pi tu pradhānamiti | jayārthaṃ ca jigīṣorādiśūrasya nijabhuja
vīryā''śrayakriyetyapradhānatvānna vākyārthaḥ | vijayakriyaiva tu prādhānyāttathā |
samūhamupapādayati—samūho'pi nāntareṇa tadbhāvanimittamekaṃ samūhi
nām | samānadeśakālāḥ khaluḥ kiṃśukādayaḥ samūha iti vā vanamiti vā
vyapadiśyante, tena so'pi samūhināṃ saṃbandhinaikena deśena kālena samūhabhāva
nimittena vinā na bhavatīti | tatra samūhiṣu tadupādhirekadeśakālopādhireka
prakāśa ekaviṣayaḥ prakāśa ityarthaḥ | śaśaviṣāṇādayo'pi gavādijātyantara
paridṛṣṭaviṣāṇavasturūpeṇotprekṣyamāṇāḥ samāropyamāṇā vastvātmakāḥ
prakāśante | tenā'nyathāpi siddheḥ saṃsargādiṣvekapratyayapratyavabhāsasya naika
śabdātmapratyāsādikalpanā yuktā | api cā'nyathaikasya śabdātmanaḥ pratyāsena
bhinneṣvabhedakalpanāyāmekavākyagamye'ne katrāpyekatā syāt | yathā śveto
dhāvatītyekasmādeva vākyādarthadvayamavagamyate, śukro nirṇenektīti kauleyaka
ito drutaṃ gacchatīti | tadetadarthadvayamekavākyasamadhigamyaṃ tatpratyāsādabhinna
meva bhāsetetyarthaḥ |
[p.208]
vastvātmakāḥ prakāśante | anyathaikavākyagamye naikavākyatā syāt | na ca
tatra śabdabhedaḥ | rūpā'bhedāt | arthabhedādbheda iti cet | kathamanyathā pratīto
'nyathā | arthabhedā'numānāditi cet | prāktarhyarthabhedābhedāvabagamyete
paścācchabdabhedābhedāviti na śabdapratyāsattinimitto'rtheṣvekatā'vabhāsaḥ |
samūhaikaśabdayoścā'viśeṣaḥ | aviditasamūhaśabdasya ca tattadavabhāso na dūrataḥ
nanvatrāpi pratyartha śabdabheda ityata āha—na ca tatra śabdabhedaḥ kutaḥ ?
rūpā'bhedāt | na hi nirṇejane gatau vā vākyasvarūpasya bhedakaṃ pratipādyāmahe |
śaṅkate—arthabhedādbheda iti cet | uttaram—kathamanyathā ekatvena pratīto
'nyathā nānā ? punaḥ śaṅkate—arthabhedānumānāditi cet | yathā jñātā
sthirā'vagamyamānāpi pratyakṣeṇā'numānenā''śutaravināśinī niścoyate evaṃ
pratyakṣā'vagataikyamapi vākyamarthabhedānnānā niścīyata iti | pariharati—
prāktarhyarthabhedā'bhedāvavagamyete, paścācchabdabhedā'bhedāviti na śabdapratyā
sattinimitto'rtheṣvekatā'vabhāsaḥ | agṛhītabhedaḥ khalvarthaḥ śabdagatā'bhedādhyava
sāyādabhinno bhāsate | na hi jātu śuktitvapratipattau rajatasamāropo gavati |
arthabhedaścetprāk śabdabhedā'bhedābhyāṃ gṛhīto nā'rthasya śabdavartyabhedaḥ śakya
samāropa ityarthaḥ | doṣāntaramāha—samūhaikaśabdayoścāviśeṣaḥ | yadi
śabdaikatvasamāropādbhinnānāmapyarthānāmekatā tato yathaikamākāśamityabhinna
syaikatvamanaupādhikamavagamyate tathā samūha ityatrāpi nopādhyapekṣamabagamyeta |
upādhyapekṣatve caikamākāśamityapi na śuddhamavagamyetetyaviśeṣaḥ prasajyate |
kvacitpāṭhaḥ samūhaikaśabdayoścā'viśeṣa iti | tasyārthaḥ | yathā bahūnāṃ
dhavādīnāṃ samūha ityekaśabdanibandhanamekatvamavabhāsate tathā vṛkṣā ityatrāpye
katvamavabhāseta | na caitadasti | kiṃ ca yadi śabdapratyāsakṛtamekatvam
aviditaśabdaviśeṣo na rūpata eva ekamarthamavagacchedityāha—avidita
samūhaśabdasya ca tadavabhāso na dūrataḥ syāt | api cā'bhāve'śabda iti ca
bhāvaśabdapratipattervivakṣitārthahāniḥ | śabdā'dhyāse khalvabhāva iti śabda
evādhyasya pratipattavyaḥ sa ca bhāva iti vivakṣitārthahānamevamaśabda iti ca
śabda ityarthaḥ | vivarttapariṇāmayorapyete doṣāḥ prasañjanīyāḥ |
eṣa tvadhiko'pi pariṇāmavivarttayordoṣa ityāha—vivarttapariṇāma
yostu sarvasyaikatvādavāntaraikatvaṃ naikaśabdālambanam | ye hi parasparavibhinnānāṃ
śabdānāmadhyāsamāhusteṣāmavāntaraikatvaṃ gauriti vā'śva iti copapadyetaiva |
ye punarabhinnasya śabdabrahmaṇo vivartaṃ vā pariṇāmaṃ vā'rthamācakṣate teṣāṃ sarvatra
[p.209]
syāt | abhāve śabda iti ca bhāvaśabdapratītervivakṣitārtha hāniḥ | vivartta
pariṇāmayostu sarvasyaikatvādavāntaraikatvaṃ naikaśabdālambanam | vastvavastu
vibhāgaśca na syāt | atha tulye'pi śabdavivarte'nyanibandhano'yaṃ vibhāgaḥ |
na, śabdavivartanibandhanaḥ | śaśaviṣāṇā'lātacakrādiṣu vastvākāranirūpaṇā |
parākrāntameva ca bahudhā svayūthyairityalamatiprasaṅgena | tasmādīpsitasyo
pāyatā kartṛpravṛttihetuḥ |
nanu karttavyatāvagamātpravarttate, saiva tathā'stu | kā punariyam ?
karttavyataiva | kena pramāṇenā'vagamyate | nendriyairadhyakṣamīkṣyate | tatpuraḥ-
śabdabrahmaṇo'viśeṣādavāntaraikatvaṃ padārthānāṃ vākyārthānāṃ ca kutastyamityarthaḥ |
kiṃ ca vastvavastuvibhāgaśca na syāt | na hyasmindarśane samasti tatprameyaṃ na
yacchabdabrahmaṇaḥ pariṇāmo vā vivartto vā | tathāvidhaṃ ca sarvaṃ sadeveti nā'satpra
meyatetyarthaḥ | atra śaṅkate—atha tulye'pi śabdavivartte'nyanibandhane'nādyavidyā
vāsanāvaicitryanibandhano'yaṃ vibhāgaḥ | uttaram—na, śabdavivarttanibandhanaḥ |
ayamabhisaṃdhiḥ | anubhavasiddho nā'sati bādhake'nādyavidyānibandhanaḥ śakyo
vaktum | śabdabrahmaṇyapi tathāprasaṅgāt | tacca śabdavivarttanibadhanastasya
tadrūpatayā niṣedhātmatvā'yogāt | tasmātpāramārthika eveti yuktam | yathāha
nyāyavādī satsaditi gṛhyamāṇaṃ yathābhūtamaviparītaṃ ca tattvamiti | nanvastu
tāttviko vibhāgo bhāvā'bhāvayoḥ | abhāvānāṃ punaranadhigatasvalakṣaṇabhedānāṃ
kutaḥ parasparavibhāgaḥ ? ityata āha—śaśaviṣāṇā'lātacakrādiṣu vastvākāra
nirūpaṇā | vibhāga ityanuṣaṅgaḥ | yadyapi śaśaviṣāṇā'lātacakrādyabhāvānāṃ na
svarūpeṇa bhedastathāpyavacchedakabhāvā'dhīnanirūpaṇatvādbhāvānāṃ ca svarūpabhedā
ttadupādhirabhāvānāmapi parasparavibhāga ityarthaḥ | sphoṭasvarūpanirākaraṇaprayatnā
bhāvaṃ prati kāraṇamāha—parākrāntameva cā'tra bahudhā svayūthyairbhāṣyakāravārtti
kakāraprabhṛtibhirityalamatiprasaṅgena |
prakṛtamupasaṃharati—tasmādīpsitasyopāyatā karttuḥ pravṛttihetuḥ | evaṃ
pūrvapakṣaikadeśini niraste samprati paramapūrvapakṣī brūte—nanu karttavyatā'va
gamātpravarttate, saiva tathā'stu | dūṣayati—kā punariyaṃ karttavyatā ? na nirā
lambanaṃ jñānam, na ca śreyaḥsādhanatā tvayeṣyata iti bhāvaḥ | pūrvapakṣiṇa uttaram—
karttavyataiva | yadābhāsaṃ vijñānaṃ tadālambanam, karttavyatā'vagamaśca karttavyatā'
'bhāsa iti karttavyataivā'syālambanamityarthaḥ | siddhāntī pṛcchati—kena pramāṇenā
'vagamyate ? karttavyatā'vagamo hi pramāṇasya kāryamasati kāraṇe pramāṇe na
bhavitumarhatītyarthaḥ | sa evaikagranthyenāha—nendriyairadhyakṣamīkṣyate | nanu mā
[p.210]
saratvānnetaraiḥ | satyam | na śabdātirekibhiḥ | nanu śabdo'pi saṃvidapekṣaye
tarasiddhimapekṣate | nanu niyogataḥ, śabde'pi tatsambhavāt | nanvitaretarāśrayaḥ
syāt | na | anāditvācchabdabhāvanāyāḥ | na khalu śabdaḥ sākṣādeva karttavya
tāpratibhāmāvirbhāvayati viprakṛṣṭaḥ, api tu bhāvanā'nugamamukhena | tatra
viprakṛṣṭaśabdabhāvanādvārasiddheḥ karttavyatātmani paratra pravṛttiprasavonnīta
pratyaye nā'sukarā pratyutpannaśabdasaṃvit | uktaṃ ca |
sākṣācchabdena janitā bhāvanānugamena vā |
itikarttavyatā yā tāṃ na kaścidativarttate ||
nāmendriyāṇi pravarttipata, kasmātpunarnānumānādigocaraḥ karttavyatā ? ityata
āha—tatpurassaratvānnetaraiḥ | anumānādayo hi pratyakṣapurassarāstatkāryatvā
nnā'sati pratyakṣe pravarttante ityarthaḥ | pūrvapakṣī brūte—satyaṃ, na śabdā'tireki
bhirgamyate | karttavyatāśabdena tu gaṃsyata ityarthaḥ | siddhāntī brūte—nanu śabdo'pi
saṃvidapekṣayā saṃbandhajñānā'pekṣayā prathamaśrāviṇo'pratīteḥ itarasiddhimitareṇa
karttavyatāyāḥ siddhimapekṣate | pūrvapakṣī tvāha—nanu niyogataḥ pramāṇāntarādeva
siddhimarthasyā'pekṣate | kutaḥ ? śabdataḥ siddhe'pi tatsambhavātsaṃbandhajñānasaṃbhavāt |
siddhāntī brūte—nanvitaretarāśrayaḥ syāt | śabdataḥ siddhau satyāṃ saṃbandhavijñānaṃ
saṃbandhavijñāne ca sati śabdataḥ siddhiriti | pūrvapakṣiṇa uttaram—anāditvā
cchabdabhāvanāyāḥ | yathā hi bījādaṅkuro'ṅkurādbījamiti bījāṅkurajātyo
ranyo'nyāśrayatve'pi vyaktibhedād vyavasthā | na hi yasyā bījavyakteryā'ṅku
ravyaktirupajāyate tasyā evā'ṅkuravyakteḥ saiva bījavyaktirapi tu tajjātīyāyā
anyasyāḥ | iyamasyāścānyā janiriṣyate | tena yatrā'nyonyāśrayaṃ na tatra
janyajanakabhāvaḥ | yatra ca tadbhāvo na tatrā'nyonyāśrayatvamiti | tathehāpi
śabdapravṛtteḥ karttavya vigamena bhāvanā prāgbhavīyā | tasyāśceha janmani
karttavyatā'vagame tatra viditasaṃbandhasya śabdasya pravṛttiḥ, tataḥ pratyutpannaśabdataḥ
karttavyatā'vagamaḥ, tato bhāvaneti | tadidaṃ śabdapravṛttikartavyatā'vagatirbhāvanā
cakramaniśamāvarttate iti netaretarāśrayatvam |
nanu bhāvanāyāṃ kiṃ pramāṇam ? ata āha-na khalu śabdaḥ sākṣādeva
karttavyatāpratibhāsamāvirbhāvayati, yato viprakṛṣṭaḥ, api tu bhāvanāmukhena,
evaṃ ca sati śabdaikasamadhigamye'pi karttavyatātmani nāsukarā saṃvidityāha—
tatra viprakṛṣṭabhāvanādvārasiddheḥ karttavyatātmani svajñānena pravṛttihetau paratra
pravṛttiprasavonnītapratyayenā'sukarā pratyutpannasya varttamānasya śabdasya saṃvid
jñānamiti | uktaṃ ca |
sākṣācchabdena janitā bhāvanā'nugamena vā |
itikarttavyatā yā tāṃ na kaścidativarttate || iti |
[p.211]
karttavyatāvagamādeva pravṛttiḥ, nepsitopāyatāyāḥ | nityeṣvapya
pravṛttiprasaṅgāt | īpsitopāyatve vā nityatvavyāghātaḥ |
ucyate—
pūrvaśabdairvyavahṛtirvyutpatteranapekṣaṇam |
bhavedviśeṣahetuḥ ko bījamarthena tu śrutau |
kā punariyaṃ śabdabhāvanā ? śabdaviṣayā śabdajñānabījamuta śabdā
'bhihitā'rthajñānabījam | yadi śabdaviṣayā tairevā'naṣṭasmṛtisaṃskāraiḥ
prāgbhavīyaiḥ śabdairvyabhicāraḥ syāt | vyutpattiśca tadā nā'pekṣyeta | śabdā
'bhihitā cettathāpi pūrvasyāṃ satyāṃ sa eva prasaṅgaḥ | atheyameva | viśeṣa
heturvaktavyo, yat tulyeṣu pratyayapāṭavā'bhyāsā''dareṣu śabdārthasaṃbandhaviṣayeṣvartha
kṛtavyākhyānametat | idaṃ ca vedavādibhiravaśyābhyupetavyaṃ na punarabhi
matasādhanatvam, avyāpakatvādityāha—karttavyatā'vagamādeva pravṛttirnepsi
topāyatāyāḥ | nityeṣvapyapravṛttiprasaṅgāt | atha nityamapi samīhitopāyaḥ
kasmānna bhavati ? ityāha—īpsitopāyatve vā nityasyā'nityatvaprasaṅgaḥ |
kāmyatayā puruṣecchātantratvenā'vaśyakarttavyatā'nupapatteḥ | tadetad dūṣayati—
ucyate |
pūrvaśabdairvyavahṛtirvyutpatteranapekṣaṇam |
bhavedviśeṣahetuḥ ko bījamarthena tu śrutau ||
bhavanā'nugamenetyuktam, tatra bhāvanāṃ vikalpayati—kā punariyaṃ śabda
bhāvanā ? śabdaviṣayā vācakatvaviśiṣṭaviṣayā śabdavācakatvajñānabīja
muta śabdābhihitā'rthajñānabījam, karttavyatāmātrajñānasya vā bījaṃ karaṇam |
bhāvanāvijñānayoḥ kāryakāraṇabhūtayoḥ samānaviṣayatvāt | atra prathamaṃ
kalpamadhikṛtyāha—yadi śabdaviṣayā tairevā'naṣṭasmṛtisaṃskāraiḥ prāgbhavīyaiḥ
śabdairvyabhicāraḥ syāt | smṛtijanakaḥ saṃskāro na vegādirityarthaḥ | sa ca
vācakatayā śabdaḥ smaryata iti vyutpattiśca nāpekṣyeta | tathā ca prathama
śrāviṇo'pyarthapratyayaprasaṅgaḥ | tadidamuktaṃ pūrvaśabdairvyavahṛtirvyutpatteranapekṣaṇaṃ
bhavediti | kalpāntaraṃ śaṅkate—śabdā'bhihitā'rthaviṣayā ced, bhavatu, tathāpi
vācakaśabdaviṣayāpyasti bhāvaneti tasyāṃ pūrvasyāṃ satyāṃ sa eva prasaṅgaḥ |
athaivamarthaviṣayaivāsti bhāvanā na pūrvaśabdaviṣayaḥ | uttaraṃ—viśeṣaheturvaktavyo
yattulyeṣu pratyayapāṭavā'bhyāsā''dareṣu śabdārthasaṃbandhaviṣayeṣvartha eva bhāvanā
bījaṃ tatpratyayapāṭavādayo na tu śabdasaṃbandhayoḥ | trīṇi kāraṇāni bhāvanāyāḥ
samastavyastāni | tathā hi | sakṛdanubhūteṣu pratyayapāṭavād bhāvanā bhavanti |
[p.212]
eva bhāvanābījaṃ na śabdasaṃbandhayoḥ | prāyaṇaṃ duḥkhaṃ vocchettṛ jātyantaravyavadhānaṃ
vā śabdasaṃbandhayorbhāvanābījānāṃ nā'rtheṣu | teṣvapi ca bhāvanānugamaścet,
jātyantarabadhirāstajjanmā'nubhūtāniva rūpaśabdaprakārān janmāntarā'nubhūtānā
cakṣīran | athāpi pūrvā'bhyastasmṛtyanubandhājjātasya harṣabhayaśokasaṃpratipatteḥ
kāsāṃcidbhāvanānāmanugamo na sarvāsām | vyutpannavadavyavahārādbālasya |
yathā durjanavacanajanite duḥkhe mandasyāpi pratyayasyā'bhyāso bhāvayati bhāvanām,
yathā jaḍasya śāstrā'bhyāsaḥ, tathā sakṛnmandamanubhūyamāne'pi pratyayādarād bhavati
bhāvanā | yathā devahrade mahācaitryāṃ budbudavatkiṃcidutpannaṃ kanakanalina
darśanādīni vyastāni samastāni cohanīyāni | tadetattritayamapi saṃskāra
kāraṇaṃ śabdārthasambandheṣu tulyamiti kathamartha eva saṃskāro na tu sambandha
śabdayoriti | tadidamuktaṃ viśeṣahetuḥ ko bījamarthena tu śrutau | yadi manyeta
arthavacchabdasambandhayorapyutpannaiva bhāvanā, sā tu prāyaṇena vā duḥkhena vā
garbhavāsajanmanā jātivyavasthānena vā vinaṣṭā na smṛtiṃ janayatītyata āha—
prāyaṇaṃ duḥkhaṃ vocchetṛ jātyantaravyavadhānaṃ vā śabdasambandhayorbhāvanābījānāṃ
nārtheṣviti | viśeṣaheturvaktavya ityanuṣajyate | bhāvanārūpāṇi bījāni
smṛterityarthaḥ | na jātu kāraṇā'niyame kāryaniyamaḥ saṃbhavati, tasyā''kasmi
katvaprasaṅgāditi bhāvaḥ | yadi tu kaścidvaiyātyādabhyupaiti, śabdārthasambandheṣu
bhāvanā'nuvartata iti, taṃ pratyāha—teṣvapi cā'viśeṣeṇa bhāvanā'nugame
jātyandhabadhirāstajjanmā'nubhūtāniva rūpaśabdaprakārān janmāntarānubhūtān
ācakṣīran |
tatra codayati—athāpi pūrvābhyastasmṛtyanubandhājjātasya harṣabhaya
śokasaṃpratipatteḥ kāsāṃcid bhāvanānāmanugamo na sarvāsām | asyārthaḥ |
nikāyaviśiṣṭābhirapūrvābhiḥ śarīrendriyabuddhivedanābhirabhisambandho janma,
tatkarttā jātastasyā'bhipretaviṣayaprārthanāyāṃ tatprāptau sukhā'nubhavo harṣo'niṣṭa
viṣayasādhanasannipāte tajjihāsorhānyaśakyatā bhayam | iṣṭaviṣayaniyoge
sati tasyeṣṭasya prāptyaśakyasya prārthanā śokaḥ | tadeteṣāṃ harṣabhayaśokānāṃ
saṃpratipattiranubhavaḥ pūrvābhyastasmṛtyanusambandhād bhavati | pūrvasmin janmani
abhyastasyā'na rthasya smṛtyanubandhāt | ekaviṣayo vā ekā''kāraviṣayo vā
anekavijñānotpādo'bhyāsaḥ, tatkarmā'bhyastaṃ yathā devadatto'nenā'bhyastaḥ
śālayo vā'bhyastā iti gṛhītinirodhe tadanusaṃdhānaviṣayaḥ pratyayaḥ smṛti
ranubadhyate'nenetyanubandhaḥ smṛteranubandho bhāvanā | tasmātpūrvā'bhyastasmṛtyanu
bandhāddhetorjātasya kumārasyā'satīndriyaviṣayasaṃprayoge harṣabhayaśokasaṃpratipatteḥ
[p.213]
bhavatu, kiṃ tu
pūrvasiddhivyapekṣatvānmānaṃ tatra na kiṃcana |
siddhe sāmānyato vṛttirviśiṣṭe liṅgavākyayoḥ ||
na tāvalliṅgādijaṃ jñānaṃ tatra pramāṇam | saṃvitprabhavatayā pūrvasiddhi
vyapekṣatvena | nāpi bhāvanājaṃ, tannimittajñānā'pekṣatvena | nāpi liṅgavākyaje |
tayoḥ sāmānyarūpaprasiddherviśiṣṭāvagāhitvenā'prasiddhapadārthavākyārthapratītyanu-
prāgbhavīyānāṃ kāsāṃcittadīyabhāvanānām anugamaḥ | āsīcca pūrva śarīrā
ntarasambandhaḥ | harṣādisaṃpratipattirasiddheti cenna | smitaruditādimattvenaitadanu
mānāt tathā hi | harṣādimadātmavatī bālyāvasthā smitaruditādimattvād
yauvanāvasthāvat | evaṃ bālyāvasthā smṛtimadātmavatī harṣādimadātmavattvāt
yauvanāvasthāvat | tathā bālyāvasthā saṃskāravadātmavatī smṛtimadātmavattvād
yauvanāvasthāvat | tathā ca bālyāvasthā'nubhūtacarānubhavavadātmavatī saṃskāra
vadātmavattvādyauvanāvasthāvat | tathā bālyāvasthā'nubhavakāle śarīrasambandha
vadātmavatī anubhavavadātmavattvād yauvanāvasthāvat iti prasaṅgātparaloka
siddhirapi sūcitā | tadevaṃ kāsāṃcidanugamo na sarvāsāṃ bhāvanānām |
kutaḥ ? vyutpannavadavyavahārād bālasya | kāryadarśanonneyā hi bhāvanā
yāvatkāryadarśanaṃ vyavatiṣṭhata ityarthaḥ |
evaṃ siddhāntaikadeśino dūṣaṇe parihṛte pūrvapakṣavādinā paramasiddhāntī
dūṣaṇamāha—bhavatu, kiṃ tu
pūrvasiddhivyapekṣatvānmānaṃ tatra na kiṃcana |
siddhe sāmānyato vṛttirviśiṣṭe liṅgavākyayoḥ ||
tad vyācaṣṭe—na tāvalliṅgādijaṃ tatra jñānaṃ pramāṇaṃ na padaṃ pratyayo vā
tatra karttavyatāyāṃ pramāṇamiti | kena hetunā ? prathamaśrutebhyo liṅgādibhyo
'nutpādātkarttavyatā jñānasya saṃvitprabhavatayā mānāntarataḥ pūrvasiddhivyapekṣatvena |
nāpi bhāvanājaṃ tannimittajñānāpekṣatvena, yadākāraṃ hi vijñānaṃ bhāvanayā
janayitavyaṃ tadākāramevānubhavaṃ svakāraṇamevāpekṣate mānāntarataḥ, na ca
tadastītyuktam nanu liṅgavākye karttavyatāvagāhinī bhaviṣyataḥ, tayoḥ pramā
ṇāntarā'navagatārthagocaratvādanyathā prāmāṇyā'nupapatterityata āha—nāpi
liṅgavākyaje | hetumāha—tayoḥ sāmānyarūpaprasiddheḥ viśiṣṭā'vagāhitvena
gṛhītasambandhaṃ liṅginaṃ gamayati, sambandhaśca pakṣavyāpakatve sati svasādhyena
niyamaḥ | na cā'gṛhīte dṛṣṭāntadharmivartini sādhyadharme tena saha hetorniyamaḥ
[p.214]
papattyā sāmānyarūpaprasiddhinibandhanapramāṇā'pekṣāyāṃ tadabhāve prāmāṇyā'bhāvaḥ |
yathā ceha janmani tathā pūrvasminpūrvatare'pi sā'pekṣiteti pramāṇā'bhāvaḥ |
tatra kiṃ vikalpamātramevedamanādiśabdavāsanānibandhanaṃ syāttacca purastātpra
tyuktam |
śakyasādhanaḥ | na cā'pūrvavastusattāsādhane pakṣadharmatā | tathā hi | kadā
citsattāyāṃ sādhyāyāṃ bhāvadharme vā heturucyetā'bhāvadharmo vā, prakārāntarā'
bhāvāt | tatra bāhyā apyāhuḥ |
nā'siddhe bhāvadharmo'sti vyabhicāryubhayāśrayaḥ |
dharmo viruddho bhāvasya tatsattā sādhyate katham || iti |
tasmādanumānaṃ sāmānyarūpeṇa yo vahniḥ prasiddhaṃ tena viśiṣṭaṃ vastu
parvatādikamavagāhate | vahnibhāvā'bhāvasādhāraṇena rūpeṇa vā yaḥ prasiddhaḥ
parvatādiḥ sādhanāśrayastameva vahniviśiṣṭamavagāhate | tadanena niyamātpakṣa
dharmatārūpasambandhadvayasaṃvittāvanumānapravṛttirnā'nyatheti sūcitam | evaṃ vākya
mapi sāmānyaprasiddhakriyaṃ padārtha sāmānyarūpaprasiddhenaiva padārthāntareṇa kāraka
rūpeṇa viśiṣṭamavagāhate | tadanayorviśiṣṭaviṣayatā, na punaraparidṛṣṭacaravastu
sadbhāvaviṣayatetyuktaṃ bhavati | kuto vākyamevaṃvidham ? ityāha-aprasiddha
padārthavākyārthapratītyanupapattyā padārthasyaivetarapadārthaviśiṣṭasyaiva vākyārthatvāt |
agṛhītasambandhasya cā'padārthatvāt | ataḥ sāmānyarūpaprasiddhinibandhanasya ca
karttavyatāyāṃ pramāṇasyā'bhāvālliṅgavākyayorviśiṣṭāyāṃ tasyāṃ prāmāṇyā
'bhāvaḥ |
nanūktaṃ kathaṃ śabdapravṛttikartavyatā'nubhavabhāvanācakramaniśamāvarttata iti,
kimatra mānāntareṇa ? ityata āha—yathā ceha janmani tathā pūrvasminpūrvatare'pi
sā'pekṣiteti pramāṇā'bhāvaḥ | atredamākūtam | prāgbhavīyabhāvanājanitakarttavya
tāvagamād bālasya svayaṃ pravṛttau siddhāyāmanāditāparihāraḥ syāt, saiva
tvasiddhā | tathā hi | yadviṣayā'nubhavabhāvitā yā bhāvanā sā tatraiva smṛti
mādhatte, na tvarthāntare'pi, atiprasaṅgāt | na ca karttavyatāsāmānyamapi viśeṣa
pravṛttihetuḥ | na ca yatkriyābhedagocarā prāgapi bhave karttavyatā'vagatāttatkriyā
gocaraiva bālena smṛtā kriyāntare pravṛttimutpādayitumarhati | nāpi kriyāntare
smaryate | na khalu pāṭaliputraupalabdhasya prāsādasya śilāhrade smarannabhrāntaḥ |
ataḥ stanapānādigocarakarttavyatā'vagatau bhāvanāya anyatkāraṇaṃ vaktavyam, na
ca tacchakyamiha janmani sarveṣu janmasviti pramāṇā'bhāvaḥ | apekṣitopāyatāṃ
stanapānādeḥ pratyabhijñāsiddhayā tajjātīyatayā bālo'pyanumimīte | tatra
viṣayasyāpyasiddhau satyāṃ vikalpamātramevedamanādiśabdavāsanānibandhanaṃ syāt |
[p.215]
api ca—
kartturiṣṭā'bhyupāye hi karttavyamiti lokadhīḥ |
viparīte tvakarttavyamiti tadviṣaye tataḥ ||
yaḥ khalu pratyayo yamarthamanveti sa tasya viṣayaḥ śukla iva śuklapratya
yasya | apekṣitopāyatāṃ tadviparītatāṃ cā'nuyātau karttavyamakarttavyamiti
pramitipratyayau laukikānām | tena tayorgocarāviti gamyate |
tacca purastātpratyuktaṃ na nirālambanaṃ jñānamiti | tatpāriśeṣyātkarturīpsito
pāyataiva pravṛttihetuḥ karttavyatājñānasyā''lambanam |
api cā'nvayavyatirekābhyāmapi saivā''lambanamityāha—
kartturiṣṭā'bhyupāye hi karttavyamiti lokadhīḥ |
viparīte na karttavyamiti tadviṣaye tataḥ ||
nanu kṛtivyāpyatadviparītatāgocarau karttavyatvā'karttavyatvapratyayau kathaṃ
samīhitatadviparītasādhanate gocarayitumarhataḥ ? na ca sādhanatākarttavyatayostā
dātmyam, siddhasādhyatvena dahanatuhinavatsvabhāvavirodhādityata āha—yaḥ khalu
pratyayo yamarthamanveti sa tasya viṣayaḥ śukla iva śuklapratyayasyā apekṣito
pāyatā tadviparītatāṃ cā'nuyātau karttavyamiti pratyayau pravṛttinivṛttihetū lauki
kānām | tena te apekṣitopāyatātadviparītate tayoḥ karttavyamakarttavyamiti
pratyayayorgocarāviti gamyate anvayavyatirekābhyām | idamatrākūtam | satyaṃ,
kṛtisādhyatāmātra karttavyatā | na tu tanmātraṃ hetuḥ, samīhitasyāpi karttavyatayā
taddhetutvaprasaṅgāt | na khalu phalaṃ karttavyamiti pravṛttigocarastatsādhanaṃ tu
syāt | na cā'nyasya karttavyatā'nyatra pravartayati | atiprasaṅgāt | sādhanatayā
nā'tiprasaṅga iti cet, tarhi prāptā'prāptavivekena sādhanatvameva pravṛttihetuḥ,
na tu tanmātramapi tu kartturiṣṭā'bhyupāya ityupapādayiṣyati svayamevā''cāryaḥ |
tasmātkartturiti vacanādācāryasya karttavyataikārthasamavāyinī samīhitasādhanatā
pravṛttihetuḥ karttavyatājñānālambanamabhimatā | na cā'nayorbhinnakālayorai
kādhikaraṇye kaścidvirodha iti upapādayiṣyāmaḥ | na ca karttavyatāmātropa
yoginī samīhitasādhanatā pramāṇāntaḥpātinī na pravṛttihetuḥ | karttavyataiva
taddhetuḥ | sā ca kvacitsādhanatayā'vagamyate, kvacicchabdatastenā'satyāmapi
samīhitasādhanatāmanityeṣu karttavyatā'vagamyamānā pravṛttiheturiti sāmpratam |
[p.216]
tasmātkarttavyatāpi nā'nyā kartuḥ samīhitopāyatāyāḥ | yathoktaṃ
tena karttavyatāmātraṃ pravṛttiheturityuktam | api ca bhavatu pramāṇāntaḥpātinī,
tathāpyasti hi kiṃcitpramāṇaṃ yadabhāvātprameyaṃ nivarttate, yathā kṛtakatva
bhāvānāmanityatve pramāṇam | atha ca tadabhāvādanityatvaṃ nivarttate, naiva hyasti
saṃbhavaḥ akṛtakaścā'nityaśca bhāva iti | tathehāpi kṛtivyāpyatā kāryatā, kṛtiśca
puruṣaprayatnaḥ, sa cecchādveṣajīvanayoniḥ svayoninivṛttau na bhavati, dhūma iva
dhūmadhvajanivṛttau | na ca hitāhitaprāptiparihārā'sādhanasādhaneṣu naimittika
niṣedhā'pūrveṣvivā'saṃbhavaḥ | dveṣajīvane tvanāśaṅkanīye evā'nupādeyatvād
vidheyaviṣayatvācca | nanvetadanumānaṃ, taccā'pūrvakarttavyatāvidhāyini pari
panthini nityatayā'napekṣapramāṇye sati śabde bādhitaviṣayatayā nodetumarhati |
abādhitaviṣayatvamapi hi pakṣadharmatāvadanumānasāmagrīniviṣṭamiti tadvidaḥ |
tatkiṃ sahasrasaṃvatsare satre sahasrahāyanajīvina eva santvadhikāriṇaḥ ? sahasra
saṃvatsaranirvartanīyametadāmnāyate satram | sahasrasaṃvatsarajīvināṃ pusāmasaṃbha
vātsaṃvatsaraśabdo jaghanyapravṛttirddivaseṣviti cet | na asaṃbhavasyā'siddheḥ |
āmnāyata eva hi mānāntaranirapekṣātsahasrasaṃvatsarasatravidhāyinaḥ saṃbhaviṣyanti
sahasrāyuṣaḥ puruṣāḥ | syādetat | vivādādhyāsitāḥ puruṣāḥ na sahasrāyuṣaḥ
puruṣatvādasmadādivadityanumānādasaṃbhava iti | nanvāgamavādhitamanumānaṃ nodī
yata ityuktam | satyam, pravṛtta āgamo'numānaṃ bādhate, tatpravṛttireva tu smārita
padārthā'nvayayogyatā'vadhāraṇapuraḥsarā, tadavadhāraṇasamaye ca bādhitaviṣaya
manumānaṃ pravṛttaṃ satrakarttavyatāsahasrasaṃvatsarāṇāmanvayamayogyatvānnirudhya saṃvatsara
śabdaṃ jaghanyavṛttimāpādayatīti cet | na | ihāpi samānatvāt | na khalū
pekṣaṇīye naimittikaniṣedhā'pūrve prayatnaṃ prati prādhānyenā'nvayamarhataḥ | na caika
śabdavācyayoranvayo na yogyatāmapekṣata iti yuktam śabdā'vadhāraṇasamaye
yogyatāhīnayoranyayā'navadhāraṇenaikaśabdavācyā'nvayā'nupapatteḥ | kāmādhikāre
kāmā'nukūlatayā kṛtyanvayopapattiriti cet | kimāyātaṃ naimittikaniṣedhā
'dhikārayoḥ ? tatra tacchabdapratyabhijñānātkāmasyā'śruterapūrvamātrakarttavyatā
'vagatiriti cet | tarhi karttavyatāśarīraniveśinā'kāmenāpi bhavitavyaṃ
niyojyeneva nimittavattā niṣidhyamānakriyākartrā vā śrutenāpi | tathā ca na
niyogamātrakarttavyatā | naimittikaniṣedhā'dhikārayostanmātratve ca karttavyatā
hānirniyojyā'bhāva iveti kartturapekṣitopāyatā karttavyatā, tadviparītatā
cā'karttavyateti siddham | tadetadupasaṃharati—tasmātkarttavyatāpi nā'nyā
[p.217]
karttavyasya sukhaṃ phalamiti | kathaṃ tarhi nityeṣu nityatā karttavyatā vā |
yathāśakti cāṅgopasaṃhāraḥ | tathā hi | yadi tāvatputrādīpsitaṃ tatropeyanityatā
vihanyeta | tadicchāyā anityatvāt | tatra yāvajjīvamityanarthakam |
nāpyadhikāriviśeṣaṇatayārthavat | arthasiddheḥ |
atha, mataṃ sarveṣāṃ sarvadā sarvamīhitaṃ phalaṃ bhaviṣyatyupāttadurita
nirharaṇam | bhavedevaṃ nityatā, phalavaśena na śabdāt | tathā hi | phalasya
nityasamīhitatvāttadvaśenā'vaśyakarttavyatā vāstavī | śabdastu nityasamī
hitopāyapratītimātropalakṣaṇaḥ | tatrā'sati śabdavyāpāre tato nirhṛtyartha
kṛcchādivatsarvāṅgopasahārāprasaṅgo'nyathā phalā'bhāvāt | śābde hi nityatve
samahitopāyatāyāḥ | atraiva bhāṣyagranthaṃ paṭhati—taduktaṃ karttavyasya sukhaṃ
phalamiti |
atra codayati—yadi kartuḥ samīhitasādhanataiva karttavyatā, kathaṃ
tarhi nityeṣu nityatā karttavyatā vā ? na hyetannityaṃ samīhitasādhanaṃ bhavatīti
bhāvaḥ | kvacitpāṭhaḥ kathaṃ tarhi nitye'pyanityatā karttavyatāyā iti, sa ca
sugama eva | yadi ca samīhitasādhanatā tato yathāśakti cāṅgopasaṃhāraḥ
kathamiti cetyanukṛṣyate | tadabhāve na nityatetyarthaḥ | nanu samīhitasādhana
tvanityatvayoḥ ko virodhaḥ ? ityata āha—tathā hi | yadi tāvatputrādīpsitaṃ
tatropeyanityatā vihanyeta | kutaḥ ? tadicchāyā anityatvāt | bhavatu
anityatvaṃ ko doṣaḥ ? ityata āha—tatrā'nityatve sati yāvajjīvamityana
rthakam | nāpyadhikāriviśeṣaṇatayā'rthavat | arthasiddheryāvatpadasyānarthakyaṃ
sthitameva | yāvajjīvanamātrasyāpyadhikṛtaviśeṣaṇatvaṃ na saṃbhavati, vyavacchedyā
'bhāvāt | na hyajīvato'dhikāraprasaktistenārthasiddheranarthakatvamityarthaḥ |
atha matam, sarveṣāṃ sarvadā sarvamohitaṃ phalaṃ bhaviṣyati upāttaduḥrita
nibarhaṇam, tataśca nityasamohitasādhanatvānnityatvamupapatsyata iti | nirā
caṣṭe—bhavedevaṃ nityatā | kiṃ tu phalavaśena, na tu śabdāt | sphuṭayati—
tathā hi | phalasya nityasamīhitatvāttadvaśenā'vaśyakarttavyatā vāstavī |
śabdastu samīhito pāyapratītimātropakṣoṇaḥ | nanu nityatayā naḥ prayojanaṃ,
sā ca phalato'pi labdheti kṛtaṃ śabdenetyata āha—tatrā'sati śabdavyāpāre
nityatāyāmenonirhṛtyarthakṛcchādivatsarvāṅgopasaṃhāraprasaṅgaḥ | kutaḥ ? anyathā
'nupasaṃhāre sarveṣāmaṅgānāṃ prāyaścittavadeva phalā'bhāvāt | na caiṣa śabde'pi
[p.218]
paripūrṇāṅgaṃ sarvadā cetyasaṃbhavādyathā śaknuyādityupapadyate | itarathā
'ṅgāmnāyasyā'vikalpyatvādyo yadeti viśvajidādivatsyāt | na hi sarveṣāṃ
samīhitopāyo viśvajiti paṅgvādayo'dhikāriṇaḥ | nānuṣaṅgikamīpsitam,
pramāṇā'bhāvāt | arthāpatterakriyāyāṃ pratyavāyenāpi kṣayāt | smṛtivākya
śeṣābhyāṃ ca tatkalpanāyā eva yuktatvāt | smṛtitaśca punastadarthāvagamāt |
nityatve prasaṅga ityāha—śābde hi nityatve paripūrṇāṅgaṃ sarvadā cetyasaṃbhavāt
śabdenaivā'vaśyakarttavyatā'bhidhāyinā sarvāṅgopasaṃhṛtau satyāmanupapadyamānena
yathā śaknuyādityupapadyate | atha vāstavenāpi nityatvena kimiti nopapadyate ?
ityāha—itarathā tu vāstave nityatve nityavadaṅgā''mnāyasyā'vikalya
tvādyo yadeti viśvajidādivadupalabhyeta |
dṛṣṭāntaṃ vivṛṇoti—na hi sarveṣāṃ samīhitopāye viśvajiti
paṅgvādayo'dhikāriṇaḥ | paṅgvādaya ityasamādheyāṅgavaikalyaṃ sūcayati |
idamatrākūtam | śābdamaṅgāmnāyaṃ śābdenaiva nityatvena śakyaṃ vyavasthāpayituṃ
na vāstavena, tasya prameyatvādaṅgāmnāyasya ca pramāṇatvātpramāṇatantratvātprameya
vyavasthiteḥ | nanu yāvajjīvaṃ darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ yajeteti yāvajjīvopa
bandhānnityakarttavyatāpratipādanaparaṃ śāstram, tataśca nityakarttavyatā śābdī
bhaviṣyatīti tatparācca śāstrādānuṣaṅgikaṃ sarvadā sarvajanasamīhitaṃ duritakṣaya
lakṣaṇaṃ bhavatocyata ityata āha—nānuṣaṅgikamīpsitam, pramāṇā'bhāvāt | na
khalūpātaduritanibarhaṇakāma iti yāvajjīvavākyaśeṣamadhīyate | nāpyarthāpattiḥ |
kutaḥ ? arthāpaterakriyāyāṃ pratyavāyenāpi kṣayāt |
nanvevamubhayathā'pyupapatteḥ kasya kalpanaucityam ? ityata āha—
smṛtivākyaśeṣābhyāṃ ca tatkalpanāyā evā'karaṇe pratyavāyakalpanāyā eva
yuktatvāt | tatra smṛtiḥ akurvanvihitaṃ karmetyādikā | vākyaśeṣo'pi yo
ha vai darśapūrṇamāsayājī sannamāvāsyāṃ vā paurṇamāsīṃ vā'tipātayetsa svargā
llokāccyavate iti | tathā hi | yāvajjīvopabandhayordarśapūrṇamāsayoḥ
kimupāttaduritakṣayaḥ kalpyatām, utā'karaṇe pratyavāyaprāptiḥ ? iti saṃdehe
yo ha vai iti vākyaśeṣātsmṛteścā'karaṇe pratyavāya iti nirṇayaḥ | api
ca laukikyā api smṛtestadarthā'vagamāt | tathā hi | yadavaśyakarttavyamiti
loke vidhīyate yacca niṣidhyate yathā grāmāntarā''nītānāṃ rājāśvādīnāṃ
nīrājanādi, bhujagāṅgulidānādi vā tadakaraṇe | tatkaraṇaṃ ca pratyavāyo
bhavatīti pratipadyante laukikāḥ | tadevamanādilaukikasmṛtidraḍhimalabdha
[p.219]
pratiṣiddhasya ca niyogato'karttavyasya kriyāyāmiva niyogataḥ karttavya
syā'kriyāyāṃ pratyavāyo yuktaḥ | tathā niyogopapatteḥ | pratyavāyabhayāt
īpsitopāyeṣvicchayā pravṛtteḥ | tathā ca yādṛśī pravṛttirnna tādṛśyabhyu
dayāya | nityepsitopāyanityatvaphalavattvayathāśaktyanuṣṭheyatvānāṃ cāsaṅgatera
karaṇe pratyavāyakalpanā | tasmānnityepsitopāyāni na nityāni | pravṛttiśca
teṣvakaraṇe pratyavāyakalpanāt | duḥkhātmakatvācca karmmaṇaḥ ānuṣaṅgi-
vyutpattirityavaśyakarttavyatā'karaṇe pratyavāyakalpanābījena punarupāttadurita
nirharaṇakalpanāyā ityāha—smṛteśca punastadarthā'vagamātpratiṣiddhasya
niyogato'kattavyasya kriyāyāmiva niyogataḥ karttavyasyā'kriyāyāṃ pratyavāyo
yuktaḥ | na cātra na saṃśayo yena vākyaśeṣādibhyo nirṇaya ityāha—tathā
niyogopapattestadakaraṇe pratyavāyakalpanāyāmeva niyogasyā'vaśyabhāvasya
karttavyatāyā upapatteḥ | kutaḥ ? akaraṇe pratyavāyabhayādopsitopāyatvenā
'vaśyaṃbhāvaḥ pravṛtterityāha—īpsitopāyeṣvicchayā pravṛttericchāyāścā'vaśyaṃ
bhāvāditi bhāvaḥ | nanu nepsitopāyatāmātramapi tu nityepsitopāyatvam,
tataścā'vaśyaṃbhāvaniyamaḥ pravṛttestaddhetoḥ ityata āha—tathā ca yādṛśī
pravṛttirna tādṛśyabhyudayāya | tathā hi | nitāntasamīhiteṣvapi kadācidāla
syādudāsate tadarthinastadupāyeṣu, na tvakriyamāṇe pratyavāyahetāviti sarva
janīnametadityarthaḥ | parapakṣe virodhamādarśayati—nityepsitopāyanityatva
phalavattvayathāśaktyanuṣṭheyatvānāṃ ca kā saṅgatiḥ ? viruddhaṃ khalvetannityepsito
pāyanityatve phalavatva yathāśakyanuṣṭheyatvaṃ ceti | tathā hi | sadātanāṅga
sākalyavattā hi sādhananityatā, sā yathāśakyanuṣṭhayatvena katipayāṅgavaikalyena
virudhyate | tathā tadeva phalavatvena nityepsitopāyanityatvaṃ virudhyate | tathā
hi | īpsitaprāptāvicchā nivarttate | na nivarttate cet na nūnaṃ tadīpsitam |
icchānivṛttau ca na nityamīpsitam, soyaṃ phalavattvepsitatvayovirodhādvirodhaḥ |
tasmācchabdāvagatanityatvanirvāhāyā'karaṇe ca pratyavāyakalpanaiva jyāyasau |
upasaṃharati—tasmānnityepsitopāyatayā na nityāni karmāṇi | pravṛttistu
teṣvakaraṇe pratyavāyāt | nanu nityeṣu keṣucidupāttaduritakṣayo'pi phalaṃ
śrūyata ityata āha—duḥkhātmakatvācca karmaṇastadānuṣaṅgikāt kāryavirodhinaḥ
pāpmanaḥ kṣayātpravṛttirna tacchābdaṃ phalaṃ karmaṇyeva svabhāvato duḥkhātmani
vidhīyamāne duḥkhavirodhitvātpāpmanastatprakṣayasya havirvikārādivadānuṣaṅgi
katvādityarthaḥ |
[p.220]
