kātkāryyavirodhinaḥ pāṣmanaḥ kṣayāditi | bhāvanāyāśca sādhyatvāt
agnihotramitivannirddeśārthatvādvipariṇamyāpi saṃbhavāttṛtīyānirddeśā'virodhaḥ |
vārttametat | tathā hi |
nityaṃ nityehitopāye yāvajjīvamiti śruteḥ |
aṅgānyato yathāśakti na vā sarvāṇi cā'nyathā ||
na tāvadiṣṭasādhanatve'pi nityatvavihatiḥ | nityeṣṭasyaiva sādhyatva
kalpanāt duritanirharaṇasya | sukhe taddhetau vā kadācinmādhyasthyameveti
duḥkhataddhetubhyāṃ tu sarvadodvijamānasya tannivṛttau sarvadākāmaḥ, hitamapi ca
yadi nityānāṃ karmāṇāṃ nā'bhimatasādhanatvaṃ kiṃ tu tānyeva karttavyatayā
codyante tadā teṣāṃ karaṇavācinī tṛtīyā darśapūrṇamāsābhyāmityanupapannetyā
śaṅkyāha—bhāvanāyāśca sādhyatvāt | ayamarthaḥ | yadyapi yajerneṣṭaṃ prati
sādhanatā | yadyapi ca pratyavāyaprāgabhāvo na sādhyastathāpi bhāvanaiva sādhyā
bhaviṣyati | yadyapi bhāvanāvyāpyatayā tāṃ prati kamatvānna karaṇatvaṃ yajestathāpi
phalapakṣe yathā bhāvanāvyāpyasya sādhanāvacchedakatayā karaṇabhāvastathehāpi
bhaviṣyatīti |
parihārāntaramāha—agnihotramitivannirddeśārthatvādvipariṇamyāpi saṃbha
vāttṛtīyānirdeśā'virodhaḥ | ayamarthaḥ | yathā'gnihotraṃ juhuyātsvargakāma
ityagnihotramiti tṛtīyārthe dvitīyā tathā darśapūrṇamāsābhyāmiti tṛtīyā
dvitīyārthe bhaviṣyati | nirdeśārthatvāditi | nirddeśyata itinirdeśaḥ karmarūpā
rthastadarthatvādvipariṇāmastṛtīyāyā ityarthaḥ | pariharati—vārtametat |
tathā hi |
nityaṃ nityehitopāye yāvajjīvamiti śruteḥ |
aṅgānyato yathāśakti na vā sarvāṇi cānyathā ||
na tāvadiṣṭasādhanatve nityatvavihatiḥ, nityeṣṭasyaiva sādhyatvakalpanād
duritanirharaṇasya viśvajidādibhyo vailakṣaṇyam | pravṛtteravaśyaṃbhāvāyāha—sukhe
taddhetau vā kadācinmādhyasthyamityālasyādibhyo duḥkhataddhetubhyāṃ tu sarvado
dvijamānasya tannivṛttau sarvadā kāmaḥ | nanu hitakāmanāsamaye nā'sāvastīti
[p.221]
prepsatastasya pratibandhakṣaye samīhā'styeva | tasmādiṣṭanibandhanaḥ pāpmanaḥ
kṣayaḥ sadā samīhitaḥ | nityānāṃ karmmaṇāṃ phalanityatvādeva yāvajjīvamiti
śabdāvagatāditi na nityatvavirodhaḥ | anitye hi sādhye sā vihanyeta |
yathāśaktyupasaṃhāraśca nityatvādevā'hīnāṅgasya nityaṃ kriyāsaṃbhavāt | na cedaṃ
vāstavaṃ nityatvaṃ, na hi phalavādino yāvajjīvaśabdaṃ nāmananti phalaviśeṣa
śabdaṃ vā'dhīyate, kiṃ tu yājjīvaśruteravagatanityatvāstata evā'rthāpattyā
phalabhedaprayogabhedau kadācidunnayanti | anitye sādhye paripūrṇāṅge ca prayoge
yāvajjīvaśabdādhigatanityakarttavyatā'nupapattiḥ | na hi kadācidīpsitopā
yātmikāyāṃ karttavyatāyāṃ paripūrṇāṅgasādhanātmikāyāṃ ca yāvajjīśabdo'nveti |
nityatvācca kalpitaḥ phalabhedo na tadeva bādhate | kathaṃ tarhi nityepsitopā
yatvānnityatvamuktam ? śabdā'vagatasyaiva tasyā'vighātaḥ śabdasāmarthyalabhyenaiva
tenokto yathāśakti prayogeṇa tantreṇa ca | tata evā'nayormānāntaraṃ mṛgyam |
kathaṃ sarvadā ? ityata āha—hitamapi ca prepsatastayeṣṭasya pratibandhakṣaye prati
bandhyate'neneti duritaṃ pratibandhastasya kṣaye samīhā'styeva | tasmānnityepsi
topāyatvaphalavattvayoravirodha ityāha—tasmādiṣṭanibandhanaḥ pāpmanaḥ kṣayaḥ
sarvadā samīhitaḥ | nityānāṃ karmaṇāṃ phalanityatvādeva yājjīvamiti śabdā'va
gatāditi na nityatāvirodhaḥ | yadanupapattyā yatkalpyate na tena tadeva bādhyata
ityarthaḥ | na ca tasya svabhāva eva karmaṇo nityatāvirodhīti vyatirekamukhena
darśayati—anitye sādhye sā vihanyeta | idaṃ nityaṃ samīhitam | yathāśaktya
nuṣṭheyatvasyā'virodhamāha—yathāśaktyupasaṃhāraśca nityatvādeva | kutaḥ ? ahī
nāṅgasya nityaṃ kriyāsaṃbhavo yataḥ | na cedaṃ vāstavaṃ nityatvaṃ yena prameyaṃ sadaṅga
vidhaṃ na vyavasthāpayet, api tu śabdameva kutaḥ ? na hi phalavādino yāvajjī
vaśabdaṃ nāmananti, phalaviśeṣaśabdameva vā'dhīyate | yataḥ śabdāvagatānnityatvā
nnaivaṃvidhaṃ phalaṃ kalpayeran | kiṃ tu yāvajjīvaśruteravagatanityatvāttata evārthā
pattyā phalabhedaprayogabhedāvunnayanti | arthāpattiṃ darśayati—anitye sādhye
paripūrṇāṅge ca prayoge yāvajjīśabdā'vagatanityakarttavyatā'nupapattiḥ na hi kadā
cidīpsitopāyātmikāyāṃ karttavyatāyāṃ paripūrṇāṅgasādhanātmako yāvajjīva
śabdo'nveti | nityatvācca kalpitaḥ phalabhedo na tadeva vādhate api tu tada
dhīnā''tmalābhatvāttadanuguṇātmūlābhatvāttadanuguṇātmatayā vyavatiṣṭhate | yadi
śabdā'vagamyameva nityatvaṃ kathaṃ tarhi nityepsitopāyatvānnityatvamuktam ?
pariharati—śabdā'vagatasyaiva yāvajjīvamiti śabdāvagatasyaiva karmanityatva
syā'vighātaḥ śabdasāmarthyalabhyenaiva tena nityasamīhitopāyatvenokto yathāśakti
prayogeṇa tantreṇa ca |
[p.222]
kathaṃ cedaṃ nityatvaṃ karmaṇo yadi nityasamīhitasādhanatva mi?ti nityā'nuṣṭhā
nādetadakaraṇe pratyavāyānnityakriyā śabdādeva | naitat | śabdādeva cedasya
karttavyatā kimarthamakaraṇe pratyavāyakalpanā ? śabdādeva pravṛttisiddheḥ | na ca
śabdaḥ kārakatvena pravṛttiheturityuktam jñāpakatve cākaraṇe pratyavāyajñāpanānni-
prasaṅgāt kiṃcidāha—tata eva yato yāvajjīvaśrutiviśiṣṭayā liṅā
nityepsitamiha phalaṃ kalpitaṃ tata ubhayorapi kāmyanityayoḥ karmaṇoḥ kāma
nākṛtapravṛttitvenā'gṛhyamāṇaviśeṣatayā sādhāraṇatayā tantramanuṣṭhānamasmadabhi
mataṃ sidhyati |
mīmāṃsakaikadeśinastu kāmye kriyamāṇe pravṛttau niyogāt kāmanāyā
balīyastvānnityasyaikadeśakālakartṛkatayā prāsaṅgikīṃ siddhiṃ manyante | tadi
damasāmpratama | yadi hi naisargikī kāmanibandhanā pravṛttirniyoganibandhanāyāḥ
pravṛtterbalīyasī syāt tato bhavanmatena pratyagrakleśadhanavyayaparāṅmukhā nitya
naimittikeṣu pravarttayatyapi niyoge na pravartteran | tathā niṣiddhebhyo hiṃsādibhyo
na nivartteranniti nityanaimittikaniṣedhagocarāḥ khalu vidhayo dattajalāñjalayaḥ
prasajyeran | syādetat | kevalā kāmanā vidherdurbalā, vidhyanugṛhītā tu
valīyasī nā'nugrahārthā bhaveta | na hi nityanaimittakeṣu vidhinā pravṛttau karttavya
tāyāṃ kāmanāyāḥ kaścidupayogaḥ | nāpi dṛṣṭārtheṣu kāmyeṣu tadā vidherupayogaḥ |
tasmānna kāmye'nuṣṭhīyamāne prāsaṅgikatvaṃ nityasya | tasmādasmanmatāvalambanena
tantrataiva ramaṇīyeti |
kvacit pāṭhaḥ na tu tata eveti | asyārthaḥ na tu nityasamīhitopā
yatvādyathāśaktiprayogācca nityatvakalpanā, kiṃ tu śrutādeva nityatvādetadubhaya
miti | anyatheti yathārthā syāditi | tayośca nityasamīhitopāyatvaṃ
yathāśaktyanuṣṭhānayormānāntaraṃ mṛgyam | yadi punaryāvajjīvaśrutipratipannanitya
tvopapādanārthaṃ nityasamīhitaphalakalpanāyāmapi vāstavī nityatā na śābdīti
manyeta tatrāha—kathaṃ cedaṃ vāstavaṃ nityatvaṃ karmaṇo yadi nityasamīhitasādha
natvamiti | nityā'nuṣṭhānātparasyāpyettattulyamityāha—akaraṇe pratyavāyā
ditarasyāpyanuṣṭhānamiti tulyam | śabdapūrvakatvena tatkalpanāyāmaṅgīkriya
māṇāyāṃ nā'śabdanityatvamubhayoḥ parasyā''tmanaśca | para āha—nā'karaṇe
pratyavāyādasmanmate nityakriyā kiṃ tu śabdādeva | pariharati—naitat | śabdādeva
cedavaśyakarttavyatā kimarthamakaraṇe pratyavāyakalpanā ? śabdādeva pravṛttisiddheḥ |
nanu śabda eva svarūpeṇa pravarttayiṣyati mā ca bhūdakaraṇe pratyavāya ityāśaṅ
kyāha—na ca śabdaḥ kārakatvena pravṛttiheturityuktam | jñāpakatve cā'karaṇe
[p.223]
tyanuṣṭhāpayatīti vāstavameva syāt | tasmādavyavasthitavidhitattvānāmīdṛśā
nānāvidhāḥ samullāsāḥ | kṛcchaviśvajitostu sadā sarvasya samīhitasādhana
yorapi sarvasya sarvadeti śabdasyā'bhāvātsarvāṅgaprayogaḥ | nanu phalavattve śaktya
nusāra eva na saṃbhavati, vaiguṇyātphalābhāvaprasaṅgāt kimidaṃ vaiguṇyam ? coda
nārthatvahāniḥ, na tarhi vaiguṇyam, yāvajjīvike yathāśaktyaṅgopete'pi codanā
rthatvāt acodanārthatve vā śakteraphalatve'pi sarvāṅgopasaṃhāro'nyathā codanārtha
nivṛttyabhāvācceti | tadā kriyāvyatiriktasādhyābhāvānnāṅgāpekṣā | anaṅgatva
meva prāptam, karmmaṇo vijñātopāyatvāt | kriyā'pi vidheyarūpeṇa jñātasvabhāvā
codanārthanirvṛttaye apekṣate'ṅgānīti cet, sarvāṅgopasaṃhāraprasaṅgaḥ | athā''
khyātānāmarthaṃ bruvatāṃ śaktiḥ sahakāriṇīti yāvajjīvaśruteḥ śaktiścoda
nārthaḥ | phalavādino na sā daṇḍavāritā | kiṃ cedaṃ rūpaṃ codanāyāḥ | yadi
pratyavāyajñāpanānnityamanuṣṭhāpayatīti vāstavameva nityatvaṃ syāt | upasaṃharati—
tasmādavyavasthitavidhitattvānāmīdṛśā nānāvidhāḥ samullāsāḥ |
yattu viśvajidādivadityuktam, tatra vaidharmyamāha—kṛcchaviśvajitostu
sadā sarvasya samīhitasādhanayorapi sarvasya sarvadeti śabdasyā'bhāvatsarvāṅga
prayogaḥ | codayati—nanu phalavattve śaktyanusāra eva na saṃbhavati, vaiguṇyāddhetoḥ
phalā'bhāvaprasaṅgāt | sādhanāddhi phalenotpattavyam, | aṅgasākalyavacca
sādhanaṃ nāṅgavaikalye sati tattve vyavasthātumarhatītyarthaḥ | pariharati—kimidaṃ
vaiguṇyam | ? yadyucyeta codanārthatvasya hānistatrāha—na tarhi vaiguṇyam |
kutaḥ ? yāvajjīvike yathāśaktyaṅgopete'pi codanārthatvāt | yāvajjova
śabdasyaiṣa mahimā yatkatipayā'ṅgavikalamapi sādhanaṃ phalāya kalpate ityarthaḥ |
na cedevaṃ paramate'pi tulyaḥ prasaṅga ityāha—acodanārthatve vā śakteraphalatve'pi
sarvāṅgopasaṃhāraḥ | kutaḥ ? yāvajjīvaśabdā'nādare yo yadetyupabandhātsāṅga
syā'phalasyāpi codanārthatvatkiñcidaṅgavaikalye codanārthanirvṛttyabhāvāt |
yadyucyeta tadā kriyāvyatiriktasādhyā'bhāvānnāṅgāpekṣā sādhyā'bhāvasūcana
paraśca yāvajjīvaśabda iti | dūṣayati—anaṅgatvameva tarhi yuktaṃ karmaṇaḥ |
kutaḥ ? vijñātopāyatvāt | śaṅkate—kriyāpi vidheyarūpeṇā'jñātasvabhāvā
codanārthanirvṛttaye'pekṣate'ṅgānīti cet | yadyapi kriyāsvarūpaṃ laukikaṃ
laukikopāyasādhyaṃ ca, tathāpi codasāgamyena rūpeṇālaukikatvāt sāṅgasya
codanārthatvādaṅgānāmapyanuṣṭhānaṃ yuktamityarthaḥ | nirācaṣṭe—sarvāṅgopa
saṃhāraprasaṅgaḥ | āśaṅkya sāmyamāha—athā''khyātānāmarthaṃ bruvatāṃ śaktiḥ
sahakāriṇīti yāvajjīvaśruteḥ śaktiścodanāyāḥ | phalavādino'pi sā na
[p.224]
darśapūrṇamāsasaṃjñakaṃ tadutpattivākyā'vagatamityanautpattikānāmabhāvaḥ |
athānyanna taccheyaḥsādhanatvāt | yathoktam śreyaḥsādhanatā hyeṣāṃ nityaṃ
vedātpratīyateiti | tasyā'yamabhyupāya iti hi teṣāmupadeśa' iti ca | nā'pya
karaṇe pratyavāyahetutā, asato hetutvā'nupapatteḥ |
daṇḍena vāritā | api ca kiṃ cedaṃ rūpaṃ codanārthaḥ, karmasvarūpaṃ hi devatoddeśena
puroḍāśatyāgātmakam, tacca mānāntarātsamadhigamyamiti na codanārthastatra
pramāṇamiti bhāvaḥ |
nanu bhavatu karmasvarūpaṃ mānāntaravedyaṃ yatpunaretasyāṣṭasu kapāleṣu
saṃskṛtapuroḍāśadravyatvamagnidevatātvaṃ ca tadāgneyo'ṣṭākapāla ityetasmā
dutpattivākyādanyato'śakyāvagamamiti | na cotpattivākyā'vagatavividha
viśeṣaṇe karmaṇi nyūnā'tirekakaraṇāya pramāṇāntaraṃ prabhavati, tatkaraṇe tatsva
rūpagrahaṇaprasaṅgādanyatastadavijñānādityāśayavānāśaṅkate—yadi darśapūrṇamāsa
saṃjñakam | nirākaroti—tadutpattivākyā'vagatamityanautpattikānāmabhāvaḥ
prasajyeta | yanmātrameva hi dravyadevatādikamaṅgamutpattivākyā'vagataṃ tāvato
'śakyahānatvāttaddhāne karmasvarūpahānaprasaṅgāt | mā bhūtparityāgaḥ, anyāni
yāni punaraṅgānyutpattikarmasaṃbandhīni sannipatyopakārakāṇyavadhātādīnyārā
dupakārakāṇi ca prayājādīnyanautpattikānyāgneyādyutpattyasaṃbandhīni teṣāma
bhāvaprasaṅgaḥ | na khalu teṣāṃ prahāṇe karmasvarūpaṃ nirapekṣotpattivākyāvagataṃ
vidyate | athā'nyanna taccheyaḥsādhanatvāt | yathoktaṃ śreyaḥsādhanatā hyeṣāṃ
nityaṃ vedātpratīyate' tasyāyamabhyupāya iti hi teṣāmupadeśa iti ca |
yatpunaruktaṃ yāvajjīvopadhānālliṅo'vaśyakarttavyatā karmaṇo'vagamyate |
lokaśca tadavaśyakarttavyamavagacchati yadakaraṇe pratyavāyo bhavati, bhayāddhi
yādṛśī pravṛttirna tādṛśī lobhāt, tadakaraṇanimittapratyavāyameva kalpayiṣyāmo
na punarupāttaduritakṣayalakṣaṇaṃ phalam | tathā satyakurvanvihitaṃ karmetyādibhūyāṃsi
vacāṃsi dharmaśāstrakārāṇāṃ vākyaśeṣaśca yo ha vetyupapatsyate ityata āha—
nāpyakaraṇe pratyavāyahetutā | asato hetutvā'nupapatteḥ | ayamarthaḥ | satyaṃ
karaṇenā'karaṇaṃ nivartate | kimiti punastannivarttanīyam | pratyavāyahetu
tvāditi cet | tadasat | kutaḥ ? asato hetutvā'nupapatteḥ | na ca karaṇena
bhāvarūpeṇa pratyavāyā'bhāvaḥ prāgabhāvarūpaḥ śakyopajanaḥ | nanu ca nityā
'karaṇe tatkāle'nyānyeva karmāṇi kānicidantato nimeṣādīnyapi tāni
pratyavāyaṃ janayanti | nityakriyāyāṃ tu santyapi tāni na janayantītyevaṃ
[p.225]
tulye ca sādhyatve nā'nupapattiḥ prayājādīnām |
na hi svarūpe kārye vā'nupayogyaṅgam | prayukterapyevaṃvidhasyā'
bhāvāt | na ca duḥkhatvātkarmaṇa ānuṣaṅgikātpāpakṣayātpravṛttiraśāstrīyatve
kimiti na kalpyate ? na śakyaṃ kalpayitum | tathā hi | yāvajjīvaṃ
juhuyāditi homā'nuraktāyāṃ bhāvanāyāṃ puruṣaṃ pravarttayatīti pravarttanā, sā
cā'nvayavyatirekābhyāmapekṣitopāyataiva pravṛttiviṣayasya nivarttanā vā'nartha
sādhanatā nivṛttiviṣayasya | tadyatra pravarttayati yataśca nivarttayati tasyaiva
hitopāyatvamahitopāyatvaṃ ca pratipādayati | na punaranyasya nityasamaye
kriyamāṇasya karmaṇaḥ pratyavāyahetutāmavagamayitumutsahate | śrutisaṃbhave
lakṣaṇāśrayaṇā'yogāt |
yaccoktaṃ bhayāt pravṛttirdhruvabhāvinī na tathā lobhāditi | satya
metat | vāṅmanaḥśarīraceṣṭābhirdaśavidhābhirniṣiddhatvena vivekinā parihriya
māṇābhirapi daivāddivāniśamupagatābhirādhīyamānātpāpmano vividhaduḥsaha
duḥkhabījādvibhyatastatpratyakṣalakṣaṇasamīhitasādhane'pi dhruvapravṛttayo bhavantyeva |
na ca kvacitsamīhitasādhanatā, kvacidaparāmṛṣṭeṣṭasādhanatvaṃ liṅartha iti
yuktam | aikātmye saṃbhavatyavyāpyatvādarthavaicitryasyā'vyavasthāprasaṅgāt | na
ca smṛtivirodhaḥ | samīhitasādhanatve'pi nityānāṃ yadā na tāni kriyante
tadā duritānyakṣīṇāni prāyaścittāntareṇa kṣeṣyanta iti nā'nupapattiḥ |
vākyaśeṣo'pi sandehe'pekṣito nirṇayahetutāṃ pratipadyate | apekṣitopāyatve
vidhāvupāttaduritakṣayapratyavāyā'bhāvasyā'sandehānnityānāṃ na vākyaśeṣāpekṣā
'stīti arthavādamātraṃ vākyaśeṣa iti kṛtaṃ vistareṇa |
kiṃ cā'sminmate tulye ca sādhyatve prayājādīnāṃ darśapūrṇamāsayośca
tṛtīyāyā api dvitīyārthe vyākhyānādbhāvanāyāṃ vā kathaṃcitkaraṇabhāvenopa
pādanātprayājādīnāmaṅgatvā'nupapattiḥ | viniyojakapramāṇābhāvāt | karttavyasya
hītikarttavyatākāṅkṣasya vacanaṃ prakaraṇamācakṣate | tadiha darśapūrṇamāsayo
rnityayorniṣphalatvena prayājādīnāṃ ca yāvaddarśapūrṇamāsau karttavyatayā
prayājādīn nāpekṣete tāvatprayājādayo'pi kimiti darśapūrṇamāsau apekṣante ?
iti na prakaraṇaṃ viniyojakaṃ bhavitumarhati | na ca prakaraṇavyatiriktameṣāṃ prayo
jakaṃ pramāṇāntaraṃ pratijñāyate prajñāyate vā | na ca dvārakāryābhāve'ṅgatā yuktā |
tasyāstadvyāpyatvena tannivṛttau nivṛtterityāha—na hi svarūpe kārye vā'nu
payogyaṅgam |
[p.226]
taptaśilārohaṇādivat | pāpakṛtatve ca puruṣakāravaiyarthyāt | atatkṛtatve vā
nāśā'bhāvāt | śāstrīyeṇa tūpāyena vipācane na doṣaḥ |
yadi tarhi phalavatyapi na sarvāṅgopasaṃhāraḥ kāmye'pi tathā syāt |
yāvajjīvaśruteritaratraivamiti cet | ihāpi svargakāmaśruterastu | yathaiva
yāvajjīvamaśakyamahīnāṅgaṃ tathā sarveṇa svargakāmena tatra yatthā yadā yāvanti
śaknoti tadā tāvatāmupasaṃhāraḥ, tathā yo yāvanti śaknoti sa tāvantyupa
saṃhariṣyati | aṅgavidhivirodhāddhi svargakāmaśrutirarthimātraviṣayā viśeṣa
manurudhyate śaktyapekṣāyāṃ tvaṅgavidhyavirodhe nityavanna viśeṣānurodhahetuḥ |
nanu mā bhūdaviniyojakam, prakaraṇādiprayuktireva tu vidherākṣepā'paranāmā
liṅgasaṃkhyādīnāmiva prayājādīnāmaṅgatvaṃ gamayiṣyatītyata āha—prayukte
rapyevaṃvidhasyā'bhāvādevaṃvidhasyeti darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ sādhyatvena tulyasyāpi
niyuktasya na prayuktirupādānaṃ saṃbhavati viniyogā'nusāritvādupādānasya |
ataeva nodyantamādityamīkṣeta, suvarṇa bhāryamityevamādayo viniyogā'bhāvāt
kratuvidhinā'nākṣipyamāṇāḥ kratvarthā na bhavanti | na ca duḥkhatvātkarmaṇa
ānuṣaṅgikāt pāpakṣayātpravṛttiḥ aśāstrīyatve taptaśilārohaṇādivat |
api ca tasya nityakriyājanmano duḥkhasyā''kṣiptapāpakṛtatve vidhānamanarthakam,
nityānāṃ karmaṇāṃ prāgbhavīyapāpaparipākādevotpādātpuruṣakāravaiyarthyāt |
atatkṛtatve vā nāśābhāvātsvaphalavirodhitvātpāpasya | nanu yadyakṛtena vidhinā
nāśyate pāpmā kathaṃ tarhi tvanmate'pi nityātkarmaṇo vinaṅkṣyatītyata āha—
śāstrīyeṇa tūpāyena vipācane na doṣaḥ prāyaścittairiva | pācanaṃ phalajananā'bhi
mukhībhāvaḥ, tasyaikāntikī nivṛttirvipācanaṃ ciraṃbhāvārthaḥ | śāstragamyo'yamartho
na paryanuyogamarhati, taptaśilārohaṇādi tu na śāstrayuktigocara iti bhāvaḥ |
atha codayati—yadi tarhi phalavatyapi na sarvāṅgopasaṃhāraḥ, kāmye'pi
tathā syāt | codaka evā''śaṅkya pariharati—yāvajjīvaśruteritaratra nitye
eva na sarvāṅgopasaṃhāra iti cet | ihāpi svargakāmaśruterastu | ekagranthenāha—
yathaiva yāvajjīvamaśakyamahīnāṅgaṃ tathā sarveṇa svargakāmena yatra yathā nitye
yadā yāvanti śakroti tadā tāvatāmupasaṃhāraḥ tathā kāmye'pi yo yāvanti
śaknoti sa tāvantyupasaṃhariṣyati |
syādetat | aṅgānāṃ nityavadvidhānāt vyavasthāyāṃ ca tadanupapatternārthi
mātramadhikṛtya vidherupapattiḥ | arthiśrutistu sarvāṅgopasaṃhārasamarthaviṣaya
tvenāpyupapannā | na hyasau na svargakāmastasmātkāmyeṣu yo yadetyeva yuktamityata
[p.227]
atha matam anuparodhe svargakāmaśruteraṅgavidhaya evoparudhyerannaviśeṣapravṛttā
yathāśaktivyavasthāyām | nitye'pi tulyam | yāvajjīvaśruterananyagatitvāt |
tatraivaṃkāmaśrutistu sarvāṅgopasaṃhārasamarthe'pyupapannaiva | tatrā'virodhāt kāṃsya
bhojinyāyena tadanurodhenāṅgivṛttiḥ | satyamavirodhena darśitastu svargakāma
śruterviṣayasaṃkocalakṣaṇo virodhaḥ | tatrā'pradhānavirodhe pradhānā'nurodhaḥ śreyān,
āha—aṅgavidhivirodhāddhi svargakāmaśrutirarthimātraviṣayāpi viśeṣamanu
rudhyate | śaktyapekṣāyāṃ tvaṅgavidhyavirodhe ca nityavanna viśeṣā'nurodhaheturasti
tasyāḥ | asyārthaḥ | sati virodhe'ṅgavidhibhiḥ svargakāmaśrutiraviśeṣapravṛttā
viśiṣṭaviṣayā vyākhyeyā, nityavattu yo yāvanti śaknotīti śaktyapekṣāyā
maṅgavidhīnāmavirodhena svargakāmaśruteḥ prasthitāyāḥ saṃkoco nyāyyaḥ | atho
cyeta, viṣayasaṃkocalakṣaṇastāvaduparodho'ṅgavidhīnāṃ svargakāmaśruteśca tulyasta
trāṅgavidhīnāṃ bahutvāttadanurodhena varaṃ svargakāmaśrutireko'varudhyatāmiti |
śaṅkate—atha matamanuparodhe svargakāmaśruteraṅgavidhaya uparudhyeranna
viśeṣapravṛttā yathāśaktivyavasthāyāmiti | pariharati—nitye'pi tulyamiti |
tatrāpyekaiva yāvajjīvaśrutiraṅgavidhayaśca bahava iti bhāvaḥ | syādetat |
yadi nitye yadā yāvantyaṅgānīti śaktyapekṣā nāśrīyeta tato yāvajjīvaśruti
ratyantaṃ bādhyeta | na hyasti saṃbhavo yāvajjīvaṃ yajeta sarvāṅgopasaṃhāreṇeti |
asti tu saṃbhavaḥ svargakāmo yajeta sarvāṅgopasaṃhāreṇeti | nahi sarvāṅgopasaṃhāra
samarthasya svargakāmasya svargakāmapadamavācakamityāha—yāvajjīvaśruterananya
gatitvāt | tatraivaṃkāmaśrutistu sarvāṅgopasaṃhārasamarthe'pyupapannaiveti |
nanu bhavatu samartho'pi svargakāmastathāpi tatpadamaniyatavṛtti sat kathama
pradhānairaṅgavidhibhirniyamyate ? ityāśaṅkyāha—tatrā'virodhāt kāṃsyabhoji
nyāyena tadanurodhenāṅgividhivṛttiriti | ayamarthaḥ | kāmipadaṃ hi kāmo
pādhinā pratipuruṣavṛttyaṅgavidhivirodhena samarthe niyamyamānamapi virudhyeta
kāṃsyabhojinyāyena | tadyathopādhyāyaḥ pradhāno nityaṃ tattatpātrabhojī śiṣya
stvapradhānaḥ kāṃsyapātra eva bhuṅte, tatra pravṛttilāghavādekasminpātre upādātavye
yadi pradhānopāyā'nurodhādyatkiṃcitpātramupādīyeta śiṣyasya bhojanaṃ na
saṃpadyeta | kāṃsyapātre tūpādīyamāne nā'niyatapātrabhojanamupādhyāyasya bhojana
virodhaḥ | tenā'virodhe pradhānamapi guṇā'nurodhānniyamyataeveti tadanurodheneti
aṅgavidhyavirodhenāṅgividhivṛttiriti | tadetad dūṣayati-satyamavirodho
darśitastu svargakāmaśruterviṣayasaṃkocalakṣaṇo virodha iti | nā kāṃsyapātra
bhojinyāyasya viṣayaḥ | tathā hi | tatrā'niyatapātrabhojano'pyupādhyāyo na
[p.228]
lokavat | yathā hi brāhmaṇānbhojaya nānāvidhopakaraṇenā'nneneti yathāśaktyu
pakaraṇavyavasthānopakaraṇā'nurodhena brāhmaṇaniyamaḥ | evaṃ cā'ṅgavidhīnāmapi
nā'tyantasāpekṣatvam | tataḥ kāmyeṣvapi yathā śaknuyādityevāstu, na yo yadā
śaknuyāditi, availakṣaṇyāt | naitatsāram | ekaprayogavidhivairūpyaprasaṅgādaneka-
sahasaiva sarveṣu pātreṣu bhuṃkte yena niyatapātrabhojinā śiṣyeṇa virudhyeta, kāma
śrutistu kāmasaṃbandhamātralabdhavṛttiḥ sakṛdeva samarthamasamartha vā'rthimātramupasthā
payantī samarthe'rthini vyavasthāpyamānā kathaṃ nāṅgavidhibhiruparudhyeta ? iti |
nanu bhavatu virodhastathāpi bhūyastvādaṅgaviṣayo balīyaso bādhiṣyanta
ityāśaṅkyāha—tatrāṅgapradhānavirodhe pradhānā'nurodhaḥ śreyān lokavaditi |
sarvathā sāmye hi bhūyastvaṃ balīyastve hetuḥ, iha kāmaśrutirekāpi prādhānyād
bhūyaso'pyapradhānatayā'ṅgavidhīn vyavasthāpayitumarhatyevetyarthaḥ |
lokavaditi dṛṣṭāntaṃ vyācaṣṭe—yathā brāhmaṇān bhojayā'nena nānā
vidhopakaraṇenā'nneneti yathā brāhmaṇaśaktyupakaraṇavyavasthānopakaraṇā'nurodhena
brāhmaṇaniyamaḥ | yadā tu kāmaśruterasaṃkoco nāṅgavidhīnāṃ yo yāvantyaṅgāni
samartha upahartuṃ sa tāvadbhirupetaṃ pradhānaṃ nivarttayatīti vyavasthāpanā tadā
pradhānavidhiraṅgāni kānicitkadācidapekṣate kadācinneti naikānto'ṅgavidhya
pekṣā bhavati, yathā keśaśmaśru vapate na veti vidhipratiṣedhasāmarthyalabdhajanmani
vikalpā'vagame sati naikāntataḥ pradhānavidhiraṅgaṃ vapanamapekṣetaiva kiṃ
tvapekṣetāpi tathaite sarve'pi kāmavidhayo'ṅgānīti siddhaṃ bhavatītyāha—evaṃ
cā'ṅgavidhīnāmapi nātyantasāpekṣateti | evaṃ ca sarveṣāmeva nityakāmyavidhī
nāmavairūpyaṃ syādityāha—tataḥ kāmyeṣvapi yathā śaknuyādityevāstu, na yo
yathā śaknuyāditi, availakṣaṇyāt |
tadetad dūṣayati—naitatsāram | ekaprayogavidhivairūpyaprasaṅgāditi |
ayamarthaḥ | sakṛtpravṛttā khalviyaṃ kāmaśrutirarthimātraviṣayā yadi sarvānevā'
viśeṣeṇārthino'ndhādīn vikalā'vayavāṃścopasthāpayaittadā svargakāmopahita
maryādo'yaṃ prayogavacano nayanavantaṃ svargakāmaṃ pratyājyāvekṣaṇaṃ vidadhāti andhaṃ
ca svargakāmaṃ prati sa eva tadeva na vidadhātīti vairūpyaṃ prasajyeta | na hyandhādi
padānyarthipadena saha samabhivyāhṛtāni, yena vyavasthā syāt | nanu naikaśaḥ
prayogavacanāṅge pravarttate yena tasya vairūpyaṃ syāt kiṃ tvekaphalasambandhānarthā
nekapradhānopakāra saṃbandhāṃścaikasya karturekavyāpāratāmāpādayati, tathā ca
na prayogavidhiraṅgāni prāpayati kiṃ tarhi prāptānyaṅgānyabhisamīkṣyopasaṃharati
[p.229]
prayogakalpanāprasaṅgāt | kevalapadārthavyavahārābhāvādviśeṣaśruteravirodhācca |
sa eva prayogavidhistasyaivā'ṅgasya prayogaṃ tameva pratividadhāti na vidadhātīti 
syāt | kalpanāgauravaṃ ca, sāmarthyabhedena prayogabhedāt | padasya ca padāntara-
ekaprayogamāpādayatīti yāvat | tena yathā śrutyādiprāpitānyaṅgāni tadanu
sāreṇaiva prayogavacanenopasaṃhriyante tathā''khyātānāmartha bruvatāṃ śaktiḥ saha
kāriṇīti sruvā''jyāvadānavatkartṛsāmarthyasyāpi prāpakatvādandhasvargakāma
kartṛsāmarthyā'nusāreṇā''jyāvekṣaṇamātravarjitamaṅgajātamupasaṃhariṣyati | cakṣu
ṣmantaṃ tu prati tatsahitamiti ko virodhaḥ ? yadi cakṣuṣmantameva pratyupasaṃharet
nopasaṃharecca tato bhavetprayogavidhivairūpyalakṣaṇo virodha ityāśaṅkyāha—
anekaprayogakalpanāprasaṅgāditi | mā prāpadvirodhalakṣaṇaṃ vairūpyaṃ samarthā
'samarthārthisamanvayavyavasthāpanayā tattadaṅgopādānaparivarjanā''pāditaprayoga
bhedarūpaṃ tu prayogavairūpyaṃ kalpanāgauravāpādakaṃ prayogavacanaḥ pratipadyata evetyarthaḥ |
nanu ca prāmāṇikaṃ kalpanāgauravamapi na doṣamāvahati | asti cā'tra
pramāṇaṃ svargakāmaśruterasaṅkocaḥ sāmānyena pravṛttāyāḥ | aikarūpye tu prayogasya
saṅkoce virodhaḥ syādityāśaṅkyāha—kevalapadārthavyavahārā'bhāvādviśeṣa
śruteravirodhācca | ayamarthaḥ | pratyāyanāya padānyuccārayanti prekṣāvantaḥ, sa
eva ca tairartho bodhayitavyo yo bubhutsitaḥ paraiḥ tameva ca pare bubhutsante yo
vyavahārāṅgam, na cā'viśiṣṭapadārthasādhyo'sti kaścidvyavahāraḥ, tena yadyapi
padātpadārthamātramavagamyate tathāpi tanmātraṃ na vyavahārāṅgamityaparyavasyat
padārthāntaraṃ viśeṣamapekṣamāṇaṃ yatpadāntareṇa yogyamupanīyate padārthāntaraṃ tada
vacchedamanumanyatetarāṃ prayoge, na tu prati kṣepakaraṇamanyathā vākyārthapratyayā
'bhāvāttaducchinnapadaśaṅkyamatyantaṃ mūkaṃ jagatprasajyeta | tadiha svargakāma
śrutirapi lokā'nusāreṇa svārthamātre paryavasyantī padāntareṇa vākyāntareṇa
vā yogyena ya eva viśeṣaḥ kaścidupanīyate tamanumanyamānā na saṅkocena
hūyate | na hi tathā sarvāṅgopasaṃhārasāmarthyaviśeṣaṇe'rthini labhyamāne na labdhaḥ
svārthaḥ kāmipadasambandhopahitasīmnastasyāpi tattvādityaprāmāṇikatvāt
kalpanāgauravaṃ doṣāyeti |
ekaprayogavidhivairūpyaprasaṅgādityuktam, tadvispaṣṭayati—evaṃ hi sa eva
prayogavidhistasyaivrāṅgasya prayogaṃ tameva pratividadhāti na vidadhātīti
syāditi | anekakalpanāprasaṅgāditi sphuṭayati—kalpanāgauravaṃ cā'ṅga
sāmarthyabhedena prayogabhedāditi |
[p.230]
sambandhe viśeṣaparatvaṃ sarvatragamiti na kadācidanyāyakalpanā | nitye'pi tarhyeva
mevāstu jīvanyadā śaknuyāt | yāvadityanupapannamiti cet | aniyatanimitteṣu
katham ? api ca sātatyena homaprasaṅgaḥ | atha kālaviśeṣopādānā'nurodhānna
tathā | hantā'ṅgavidhyanuvidhāyinyapi yāvajjīvaśrutiḥ sāmarthye sambandhamanvetī
tyaṅgavidhyanurodhādyadeti syāt |
atha mataṃ—jīvannapi sāṅga eva karmmaṇyadhikriyate nāṅgaśūnyaṃ vidhāna-
kevalapadārthavyavahārā'bhāvādviśeṣaśruteravirodhāditi yaduktaṃ tatspaṣṭayati
—padasya ca padāntarasambandhe viśeṣaparatvaṃ sarvatragamiti na kadācidanyāya
kalpaneti | nanu yadi padasya padāntarasambandhe viśeṣaparatvamaviruddhamiti svarga
kāmapadaṃ samartha viṣayamiti yo yadeti sambandhaḥ, hanta tarhi jīvanamapi
sāmarthyenā'vacchinnaṃ nitye'pi yo yadetyupabandhamāvedayiṣyatītyāha—nitye'pi
tarhyevamevā'stu jīvanyadā śaknuyāditi |
śaṅkate—yāvadityanupapannamiti cet | ayamabhisandhiḥ | ekaikatra cetane
kāmanāniveśādekasminnapi samarthe svargakāmaśruteḥ samavetā'rthatvānna virodhaḥ,
iha tu yāvacchabdasya jīvanakālavyārptthatvāt yadā śabdasya kālaikadeśavyavasthā
pakatvādvyāptiviruddhatvādasti nāstītivadasaṅgatiriti | pariharati—
aniyatanimitteṣu kathamiti | nā'yamekānto, yāvacchabdā'nanubandheṣu naimittikeṣu
grahoparāgādisnāneṣu yadā śaknuyādityupabandho nopapanna ityarthaḥ |
idānīṃ tāvacchabdopabandhe'pi yadā śaknuyāditi sambandhayituṃ prasaṅgamā
pādayati—api ca sātatyena homaprasaṅgaḥ | yāvajjīvetpadaṃ ca jīvanā
vacchinnakālavyāpanārthamiti yāvadayaṃ prāṇiti tāvadanena hotavyamityanapekṣite
prātaḥsāyaṃniyame nirantarameva jagataḥ sarvo'pi pānāhāravyavahāro vipadyeteti |
atha kālaviśeṣopādānā'nurodhānna tathā, tatrāha—hanta kālaviśeṣopādāna
vatsarvāṅgavidhyanuvidhāyinyapi yāvajjīvaśrutiḥ sāmarthye sambandhamanvetītyaṅga
vidhyanurodhādyadeti syāt | ayamarthaḥ | yathā sāyaṃprātaḥsamayavidhisāmarthyā
tsāyaṃprātaḥsamayā'vacchinnameva jīvanaṃ yāvadityupabadhyate tathā sarvāṅgavidhi
sāmarthyāttadekavākyatayā sarvāṅgopasaṃhārasāmarthyasamayāvacchinnameva jīvanaṃ
yāvadityupabhantsyante | tataśca nitye'pi kāmyavadeva yadā śaknuyādityupa
bandhasiddhiriti |
atha mataṃ jīvannapi sāṅga eva karmaṇyadhikriyate, nāṅgaśūnyaṃ vidhānam,
sāṅgasyaiva tasya karaṇabhāvenā'vagamāt | nanu tathāpi kāmyānnityasya ko
viśeṣaḥ ? ityāśaṅkyāha—adhikṛtasya tu prayogo yāvadadhikāraṃ nāṅgānu
[p.231]
madhikṛtasya tu prayogo yāvadadhikāraṃ nāṅgā'nurodhena nivarttate | yathāsambhavaṃ
tu adhikārāyattatvāttasya | na ca kāmye'pi prasaṅgaḥ | kāryaniṣṭhatvātkāmā
dhikārasya prayoganiṣṭhatvācca naimittikasya | sādhanāpekṣatvāt kāryyasya,
rodhena nivarttate | ayamabhisandhiḥ | yadyapyaṅgavidhyekavākyatābalānni
khilāṅgopasaṃhārasamarthasya jīvato'dhikāro vidheravagatastathāpi sāmarthya
mālocya puruṣaḥ pravṛtto'nantaraṃ tu yadi kaścitpramādataḥ katipayāṅgakaraṇā'
samarthaḥ samarthaścetarāṇi sarvāṇyeva karttuṃ tatra kimeṣo'samarthaḥ katipayeṣvaṅgamiti
sarvathaiva tataḥ prayogāduparamatām ? atha yatrāpyasamarthastadaṅgasahitamapyadhikāra
pratītisāmarthyādanutiṣṭhatu ? āhosvidaśakyakatipayāṅgavarjitamitarāṅgagrāma
sahitaṃ pradhānamprayuṅktām ? iti yathāsambhavaṃ tvadhikārāyattatvāttasyeti |
ayamarthaḥ satyapyadhikārapratyaye śakyakaraṇā'nupapatteḥ sarvathoparame cādhikārā'ṅga
virodhādadhikārā'tikramaprasaṅgenā'ntimapakṣaparigrahaḥ | aśakyāṅgavarjitetarāṅga
sahitapradhānānuṣṭhānaṃ khalvadhikārānurodhādayaṃ prayogavidhiravalambate iti |
nanu kāmyepyetatsarvaṃ samānamityāśaṅkyāha—na ca kāmye'pi prasaṅgaḥ,
kāryaniṣṭhatvātkāmā'dhikārasya prayoganiṣṭhatvācca naimittakasya | ayama
bhiprāyaḥ | kāmye hi svargakāmo yajeteti sādhyasvargaviśiṣṭo niyojyastadeva
kāryaṃ pratipadyate yatsvaviśeṣaṇā'nukūlam, sādhyasyaiva sato viśeṣaṇatvāt,
tadananukūlatve tu kāryasya, na tatra svargakāmo niyojyaḥ syātkāryaṃ ca kṛtya
vacchinne nāpratīyamānāyāṃ kṛtau śakyanirūpaṇam | na hyanavagataviśeṣaṇo
viśiṣṭamavagacchati | kṛtivyāpyaṃ hi pradhānaṃ kāryamucyate | prādhānyaṃ ca taduddeśena
kṛteḥ pravṛttiḥ | kṛtiścaivaṃvidheṣu vākyeṣu cetanavyāpāraḥ prayatnaśabdavedanīya
evā''stheyaḥ | cetano hi niyojyastadeva kāryaṃ pratipadyate yatra kārye tiraścīna
mātmānamavagacchet | na cā'nyavyāpāravyāpye tathā'vagantumarhati | prayatnaśca
pratyayavanna viṣayamantareṇa śakyo nirūpayitum, viṣayaśca tasya prāyeṇa bhāvārthaḥ |
tadālambanasyaiva prayatnasyā'pūrvābhidhānakāryoddeśena pravṛtteḥ | na khalu loke
'pyalabdhapākarūpabhāvārthaviṣayaḥ paktuḥ prayatna odanoddeśena pravartitumarhatīti |
tataśca kṛtimantareṇa kāryasyā'nirūpaṇāt kṛteśca bhāvārthaṃ vinā'pratīteḥ
kṛtipraṇāḍikayā kāryasya bhavati bhāvārtho viṣayaḥ, tadbandhanatvāt | ṣiñ
bandhane ityasmādviṣayapadavyutpatteḥ | tadevaṃ kāmopāyasya kāryasya viṣayabhāvamupa
gacchan bhāvārthe nā'nupāyaḥ pārayati gantu mityupāyatāmeva purodhāya
viṣayatā'vagamyate bhāvārthasya kāmyeṣu | upāyatā ca nikhilāṅgasaṃpādito
[p.232]
yāvadaṅgaṃ ca sādhanabhāvā'vagateḥ sarvāṅgopasaṃhāreṇaiva kāryyasiddheḥ | nanu ubhaya-
pakāratayā nā'nyathā, militānāṃ ca sarveṣāmaṅgabhāvaḥ | pratyekaṃ tadbhāve'
ṣṭavidhadoṣanidānavikalpā''patteḥ | tatrā''gantuke'pyakriyamāṇe samastā'ṅga
janyasya karaṇopāyasyā sampatterupāyatvā'bhāvād viṣayiṇo'dhikārasyā'
pratīterna yadbalena hīnāṅgaṃ kāmyaṃ karma saṃpādayati, api tu sarvopasaṃhārāya
ghaṭate | samarthastu kvacitprayogādeva nivarttate | nitye tu nāyaṃ prakāraḥ
saṃbhavī | na hi tatra sādhyaviśiṣṭo niyojyaḥ, yenā'nupāyaṃ kāryaṃ kāryatayā
nāvagacchet | tenā'nyataḥsiddhanimittāvacchinno niyojyaḥ kṛteḥ praṇāṅikayā
bhāvārthamātraviṣayameva tāvatprathamaṃ kāryamavagacchati | pratipannasaviṣayakāryastu
kṛtyupahitasvarūpatvātkāryasya kṛteśca kriyātvena karaṇanāntarīyakatvātkāryaṃ
prati karaṇamapekṣamāṇaḥ sannidhānādviṣayībhūtasya bhāvārthasya karaṇatāṃ prati
padyate | na ca kṛtinivṛttasya bhāvārthasya kathaṃ tāmeva kṛtiṃ prati karaṇatvamiti
vaktavyam | tadavacchinnāyāstadapūrvaviśeṣeṇa kṛtibhāvālloke tathā pratīteḥ |
yathāccheturudyamananipātanalakṣaṇayā kriyayā vyāpyamānamapi vraścanaṃ tāmeva
prati sādhanaṃ bhavati, vraścanaviṣayayorudyamananipātanayośchidārūpatvaprati
lambhāt | na hi loṣṭasamāśraye udyamananipātane anāhitadvaidhībhāve chideti
nirūpayanti | tena yadyapi bhāvārthoṃ vyāpyate kṛtyā tathāpyapūrvā'rthapravṛttayeti
tāṃ prati na bhāvārthaḥ karma, kiṃ tu karaṇamapūrvaṃ tu pradhānaṃ karma, taduddeśena kṛteḥ
pravṛttatvāt | tadevaṃ nitye'pi viṣayabhūtasya bhāvārthasya paścātkaraṇatvapratī
teravagatasāṅgapradhānakaraṇatvastadanuṣṭhānasamarthaḥ pravṛttaḥ kutaścinnimittādyadi
kiṃcidupahartu na pārayati tathāpyadhikārapratīteḥ pratītyanubandhībhūtabhāvārtha
mātraviṣayā'vagamanopāyānnoparantumarhati, kiṃ tu kiṃcidaṅgahīnaṃ prayogamanu
tiṣṭhati | tadidaṃ prayoganiṣṭhatvaṃ kāryaniṣṭhatvapratipakṣatayopanyastam |
yadi kāmā'dhikārasya kāryaniṣṭhatvaṃ tataḥ kiṃ bhavati ? ityata āha—
sādhanā'pekṣatvātkāryasya, yāvadaṅgaṃ ca sādhanabhāvā'vagateḥ sarvāṅgopasaṃhāre
ṇaiva kāryasiddherna kāmye prasaṅgaḥ | nanūbhayatrāpi kāmye naimittike cā'dhikāra
eva karttavyaḥ | niyogasyaiva kāryasya vidhipratyayādavagatestasyaiva pradhāna
tvāt | tatkarttavyatāyāścā'nuṣṭhānamantareṇā'nupapatteranuṣṭhānasya ca puṃsaḥ
kartṛtāmantareṇā'sambhavāt kartṛtāyāścā'dhikāriṇāṃ karmaṇi svāmitāmanta
reṇā'siddheradhikāritāyāśca niyojyatāṃ pradhānībhūtakāryaṃ niyogaṃ pratyātmana
stiraścanabhāvaboddhṛtāṃ vinā'paryavasānāt niyojyo'pekṣitaḥ | sa cā'viśiṣṭo
na śakyaḥ pratipattumiti tadviśeṣaṇākāṅkṣāyāṃ kvacitsiddhameva viśeṣaṇaṃ
[p.233]
trāpyadhikāra eva karttavyaḥ | satyam | ekatra nimittaparpyanto'paratraphalaparyyantaḥ |
tatra nimittaparyyante nimittavato'dhikṛtasyā'prapuñjānasyā'dhikārā'ti
krāntiriti yathāsaṃbhavaṃ prayogaḥ | phalaparyyante tvaṅgavaikalye phalā'nupapattiḥ |
phalakāmitā'dhikāraheturnivarttate ityadhikārā'bhāvānnādhikārā'tikrānti-
jīvanādi svīkaroti, kvacicca kāmādipadā''pāditasādhyabhāvaṃ svargādi | na
ca niyogaviśeṣaṇasya sādhyatā niyogasya sādhyatvamupahanti, niyogasādhyatva
nimittatvānniyojyaviśeṣaṇasādhyatvasya | na ca nimittaṃ vidhirapabādhataiti
nyāyāt | tenobhayatra nitye kāmye ca niyogasyaiva karttavyatvātkāryaniṣṭhatvā
dityaviśeṣa ityarthaḥ |
samādhatte—satyamekatra nimittaparyanto'paratra phalaparyantaḥ | ayamarthaḥ |
nimittaparyantā'dhikārāt nimittasya cā'sādhyatvāt nimittavānnānyopāya
tayā kāryamavabudhyate, kiṃ tarhi bhāvārthaviṣayameva | tasyāṃ ca daśāyāṃ prati
pattyanubandhitayā bhāvārthaḥ pratīyamānastadanurañjakatvenaiva paramavatiṣṭhate
nopāyatayā | tataśca naimittikā'dhikārasya bhāvārthamātraviṣayatā na tūpāyī
bhūtabhāvārthaviṣayatā | tataśca yāvadbhāvārtharūpabhāvitvādadhikārā'vagatestanmā
treṇa ca nimittasya nimittatāparyavasānānnimittaparyanta ityuktam | kāmādhikāre
tu kāmopāyasyaiva kāryasya pradhānasyā'vagateḥ phalaparyanta ityuktam | yadyevaṃ
tataḥ kiṃ sidhyati ? ityata āha—tatra nimittaparyante nimittavataḥ sarvāṅgopa
saṃhārasamarthasya pravṛttasya daivātkiñcidaṅgamupasaṃharttumapārayato'pi bhāvārthamātra
viṣayatvānniyogasya tasya ca kiñcidaṅgahānādapracyute radhikṛtasyā'prayuñjā
nasya prayogamananutiṣṭhato'dhikārā'tikrāntiriti yathāsambhavaṃ prayogaḥ |
phalaparyante tvadhikāre'ṅgavaikalye sati na sādhanatvam, sāṅgasya sādhanabhāvāt
asādhanātphalā'nutpatterutpattau vā'tiprasaṅgāt phalakāmitā nivarttate |
nanu na pratyātmavedanīyā phalakāmitā'ṅgahānau na nivarttata iti śakyaṃ
vaktumityāśaṅkyāha—adhikāraheturiti | ayamarthaḥ | phalakāmitā khalū
ktena mārgeṇa karaṇabhūtasya bhāvārthasyā'dhikāraviṣayabhāvamāpādayantyadhikāra
hetuḥ | sā cā'ṅgavaiguṇye sati kevalasya bhāvārthasyopāyabhāvāt viṣayā'bhāve 
na viṣayiṇo'dhikārasyāpi nivṛtteḥ satyapi nādhikārahetutayā'stīti phala
kāmitā nivarttata ityucyate iti | tasmādadhikārā'bhāvānnā'dhikārā'ti
krāntidoṣa iti na yathā kathaṃcitprayogaḥ |
[p.234]
doṣa iti na yathākathaṃcitprayogaḥ | evaṃ na sātatyena prayogo nā'rthā'va
ruddheṣu yathāśakti copasaṃhāro nityeṣu na kāmyeṣviti sarvaṃ caturasram |
nedaṃ caturasram | idamaṅga bhavānvyācaṣṭāṃ kiṃ śaktyapekṣo'dhikāro
nanu yadi naimittikeṣvaṅgavaiguṇyeṣvadhikārā'napagamo bhāvārthamātraviṣaya
tvā'napāyāt tathā sati yāvajjīvamagnihotraṃ juhuyāditi sāyaṃprātaḥkālayo
ranaṅgatvena tadabhāve'pyadhikārasyā'napetatvānmadhyandinādikāle'pyajuhvadadhi
kāramatikrāmediti nirantarameva homaprasaṅga ityāśaṅkyāha—evaṃ sati na
sātatyena homaprasaṅgaḥ | idamatrākūtam | yadyapi naimittikeṣu prathamaṃ bhāvārtha
mātrameva viṣayastathāpi kāryasya kṛtyupahitatvena karaṇā'pekṣāyāṃ bhāvārthaḥ
paścātkaraṇaṃ bhavati | labdhakaraṇatvasya cā'ṅgāpekṣāyāmaṅgānyupatiṣṭhante, tena
sāṅgopasaṃhāraṃ prati svābhāvikasāmarthyaśālina eva paścādadhikārā'vaga
mādapratisamādheyā'ndhatvādidoṣāṇāmapekṣamāṇādyaṅgavati atryārṣeyāṇāṃ ca
tryārṣeyacaraṇavatyanagnyāhitānāṃ cā''havanīyādibhāji madhyandinasamayavarttināṃ
ca puṃsāṃ sāyaṃprātaḥsamayā'ṅgasaṅgini nā'dhikāraḥ karmaṇi, yathāvihita
karmopasaṃhāraṃ prati nijasāmarthyā'bhāvāt | yeṣāṃ punarājānika masti sāmarthya
yathā daridrāṇāṃ pratisamādheyabādhiryādīnāṃ ca teṣāṃ satyapi daridrāṇatve
satyapi ca vādhiryādāvastyeva dhanasādhane śravaṇādisādhane ca karmaṇyadhikāra
iti tadakaraṇe bhavatyadhikārā'tikramo nityeṣu na tu kāmyeṣu | svābhāvika
sāmarthye satyapi tadānīntanena pratisamādheyenāpyasāmarthyenāṅgavaikalye sati
sādhanatvā'pāyena sādhanībhūtabhāvārthaviṣayasyā'dhikārasya nivṛtteḥ | nanu
viśeṣaṇeṣu bhavatu bhāvārthamātrameva viṣayaḥ kāryasya, tathāpi tasyāmapi
kāryatvena karaṇā'dhīnotpattitvāt pratisamādheyenāpyasāmarthyenāṅgā'nanuṣṭhāne
sati tadvikalpādanutpatteḥ kathamanapāyo'dhikārasya nāpāyastasya yāvadviṣayaṃ
pratyabhijñānādviṣayasya bhāvārthasya katipayāṅgavaiguṇye'pyanapetatvānnijasya ca
sarvāṃṅgopasaṃhārasāmarthyasya ca tadanapāye'napeto'dhikārastadānīntanamāgantukaṃ
katipayāṅgā'nuṣṭhānasāmarthyamabalokya yāvadupasaṃharaṇīyāṅgasaṃpādyatāmevātmano'
vagamayati | na hyasti sambhavo'dhikārā'vagatirananuṣṭhānaṃ ceti | tasmādadhi
kārā'vagatisāmarthyāttasyāṃ daśāyāṃ yathāśaktyupasaṃhniyamāṇāṅgā''dheyopakā
ratāmevā'nyathānupapatteḥ svakaraṇasya kāryamavalambate | so'yamaṅgavaikalyād gauṇaḥ
śāstrārtha ityucyate | autsargikī tu sakalāṅgagrāmasaṃpādanīyakaraṇopakāritaiva
[p.235]
yathādhikāraṃ ca prayogaḥ, āhosvicchrutyapekṣo'dhikāro, 'dhikṛtasya tu
yathāśakti prayogaḥ ? pūrvasminkalpe yathaiva kālaviśeṣayukte karmmaṇi
tatkālajīvino'dhikṛtā'nyasya tadviśiṣṭakarmmopasaṃhārā'sāmarthyāt | anyathā
'nyadāpi akurvato'dhikārā'tikrāntiḥ | paścime tu nāṅgaśaktiradhikāraṃ
nityā'dhikārasyeti sarvāṅgopasaṃhāro mukhyaḥ śāstrārthaḥ | tasmātsūktaṃ na
sātatyena prayogo nā'rthāvaruddheṣu kāleṣviti | nāpi tasyaiva homasyā''hārā
dyavaruddheṣu kāleṣu yathā satatahomo na satataṃ sāyamādikālamapekṣate, kiṃ tarhi
arthā'viruddhakālā'valambanam, anyathā saṃtatatvā'bhāvapraṅgāt, jñāyata evaitatsatataṃ
hotavyaṃ na tatsāyamādisamayā'pekṣamiti, tatheha home sāyamādisamayavidhānā
ttasya ca madhyandinādisamaye'vaśyamanupasaṃhāryatvāt | tasmānna sātatyena homo
yathāśakti copasaṃhāro nityeṣu na kāmyeṣviti sarva caturasram |
tadekadeśimataṃ dūṣayituṃ vikalpayati—nedaṃ caturasram | idamaṅga bhavān
vyācaṣṭāṃ ki śaktyapekṣo'dhikāro yathādhikāraṃ ca prayogaḥ, āhosvityaṃcchrutya
pekṣo'dhikāro'dhikṛtasya tu yathāśaktiprayogaḥ ? iti | ayamarthaḥ | aṅgopadeśa
sāmarthyādaśrutamapi sarvāṅgopasaṃhāranijasāmarthyamanupādeyatvādadhikāraviśeṣaṇa
tāmāpadyata iti tadapekṣo'yamadhikāro bhavati | yathādhikāraṃ ca prayoga ityekaḥ
kalpaḥ | aparastu yāvajjīvaśrutimātrā'pekṣo'dhikāro nāṅgopasaṃhārasāmarthya
mapekṣate, tasyā'śrutatvāditi | tatra prathamaṃ kalpaṃ śaktyapekṣo'dhikāra iti
dṛṣṭāntadvayenopapādya dūṣayati—yathaiva kālaviśeṣayukte karmaṇi yāvajjīvaṃ
darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ yajetetyevamādau tatkālajīvanādhikṛtādanyasyā'tatkāla
jīvasya tatkālaviśiṣṭakarmopasaṃhārasāmarthyā'bhāvāt | avaśyaṃ ca tadāstheya
mityāha—anyathā'nyadāpi pañcamyādāvapi akurvato'dhikārā'tikrāntiḥ
syāt | yathā rājasūye rājatvamanyasya saṃpādayitumaśakteradhikāraṃ viśinaṣṭi |
na jātu brāhmaṇavaiśyau rājatvamātmanaḥ saṃpādayitumarhataḥ, yena rājasūye rāja
kartṛke'dhikriyeyātām | tathā'ṅgodeśā'pekṣatvādadhikārasya tatsāmarthya
viśiṣṭajīvanamadhikārakāraṇamiti | bhavatvevaṃ tathāpi ko doṣaḥ ? ityata
āha—kutaḥ prathame kalpe tādṛśasyāṅgopasaṃhārā'samarthasyā'dhikārā'tikrāntiḥ |
dvitīyaṃ kalpaṃ śrutyapekṣo'dhikāra iti dūṣayati—paścime tu nāṅgaśaktira
dhikāraṃ viśinaṣṭīti sātatyaprasaṅgah | asyārthaḥ | yadā hi śrutyapekṣo
'dhikārastadā jīvanamātrasya śrutatvāttanmātramevā'dhikāriviśeṣaṇamiti
homādisātatyaprasaṅgaḥ | kiṃ ca kāmye cāṅgahānādananuṣṭhāne'dhikārā'ti
kramaḥ syāt | kiṃ ca paṅgvādeścā'dhikāraprasaṅgaḥ, na hyasau na svargakāmo
na cā'jīvanavān |
[p.236]
viśinaṣṭīti sātatyaprasaṅgaḥ | na brūmo'ṅgaśaktiradhikāraṃ viśinaṣṭīti, kiṃ
tvaṅgaśaktyapekṣa evā'dhikāra iti | tādṛśasyaiva tu kasyacidaṅgasyā'saṃbhave na
prayogo nivarttata iti | vaiguṇyāccāpi phale'dhikārahetureva nāstītyuktam |
ko'yamasaṃbhavaḥ ? tadupasaṃhārāsāmarthyam, na tarhyadhikṛtastadviśeṣaṇatvāttasya |
athā'nupasaṃhāro'naṅgatvameva | kādācitkamasāmarthyamasaṃbhavo na tenā'dhi-
atraikadeśī gūḍhā'bhiprāyaḥ prathamaṃ pakṣamāśrityāha—na brūmo'ṅgaśaktira
dhikāraṃ viśinaṣṭīti, kiṃ tvaṅgaśaktyapekṣa evā'dhikāra iti | kimiti
tarhyaṅgavaikalye naimittikaṃ prayujyate ? ityāśaṅkyāha | tādṛśasyaiva hyadhikāriṇaḥ
kasyacidaṅgasyā'sambhave'pi na prayogo nivarttata iti sāmarthyasyā'dhikāri
viśeṣaṇasya bhāvārthamātrasyā'dhikāriviṣayasya ca tadānīmapi naimittikeṣu
bhāvādityarthaḥ |
kāmye tarhi hīnāṅge prayujyamāne samarthasyādhikārā'tikrāntiḥ
prasajyetetyāśaṅkyāha—vaiguṇyāccapi phale'dhikārahetureva nāstītyuktam |
ayamarthaḥ | vaiguṇye satyavidyamānaphalatvaṃ kāmye karaṇatvamiti yāvat |
karaṇībhūtasya bhāvārthasya viṣayatvaṃ vaiguṇye cā'karaṇatve viṣayatvā'bhāvā 
dadhikārā'vagamavilaya iti nā'nanuṣṭhāne'dhikārā'tikrāntirityuktaṃ kārya
niṣṭhatvātkāmyasyetyanena | tadetadvikalpya dūṣayati—koyamasambhavo yadi
tadupasaṃhārā'sāmarthyaṃ na tarhyadhikṛtaḥ, tadviśeṣaṇatvāttasya | ayamarthaḥ | yadyaṅgo
pasaṃhārā'sāmarthyamaṅgasyā'sambhavastatkāraṇatvenopacārāducyate, tadā sāmarthyasyā
dhikāriviśeṣaṇasyā'sambhavādanadhikṛtaḥ kimiti naimittikamaṅgavikalaṃ
prayuñjīta | athā'nupasaṃhāro'naṅgameva | atha samartho'pi nopasaṃharati
kiṃcidaṅgamanupasaṃhāra evā'ṅgasyā'bhāva ucyate tadā na nūnametadaṅgam,
na hyasti sambhavo yadvidvān samarthaḥ kiñcidaṅgamapahāya pradhānaṃ prayuṅkta iti
seyamubhayataspāśā rajjuḥ |
sāmpratamekadeśī svā'bhiprāyamāviṣkaroti—kādācitkamasāmarthyama
sambhavo na tenā'dhikārātparyudāso dravyāpratisamādheyā'ṅgavaikalya iva | idamatrā
kūtam | dvaidhaṃ khalviha pumāṃso vyavasthitāḥ | kecidasamarthāḥ kecicca tirohi
tasāmarthyāḥ | tatra ye tāvadasamarthā andhapaṅgvādayaḥ śūdrādayaśca teṣāmanadhikāra
eva, sāmarthyasyā'dhikṛtaviśeṣaṇasyā'bhāvāt | ye'pi samarthāḥ kutaścinni
[p.237]
kārātparyyudāso dravyāpratisamādheyāṅgavaikalyavat | satyam, na śūdravadekāntato
na tvadhikārahetuvigame'dhikāro na nivarttate vītāyāmiva phalecchāyāṃ
mitādabhibhūtasāmarthyāsteṣāmadhikṛtaviśeṣaṇasya sāmarthyasya sadbhāvātko'
dhikāravilayahetuḥ ? na khalvabhibhūtasāmarthyo bhavatyasamarthaḥ | na ca kārya
vyavaseyatvātsāmarthyasya kāryā'bhāve tadapramāṇamiti vācyam | pratītacara
sāmarthyasya kāryakāriṇaḥ paścādakaraṇe punaśca karaṇe kiṃ sāmarthyavināśo
tpādau, āhosvittirobhāvā''virbhāvau ? iti viṣaye paraiva kalpanā
jyāyasī, lāghavāt, na pūrvā | tadā hi samarthyarūpadharmakalpanāyāṃ gaurava
prasaṅgaḥ | tataśca sāmarthyasyā'dhikṛtaviśeṣaṇasya vidyamānatvādupakrame prayogasya
sādhveva cā'dhikṛta iti yathāśakti naimittikamārabheta samāpayecceti | atraiva
nidarśanamāha—dravyā'pratisandheyāṅgavaikalyavat |
taddūṣayati—satyaṃ na śūdravadekāntato na tvasyā'dhikārahetuvigame'
dhikāro na nivarttate | ayamatrā'bhisandhiḥ | idamatra bhavān vyācaṣṭāṃ
yadetatsāmarthyamadhikṛtaviśeṣaṇamucyate tat kiṃ sākṣātsāmarthyasya śruterāho
svidaṅgopadeśabalāt ? tatra tāvatprathamaḥ kalpo'nupalabdhinirākṛta eva |
dvitīye tu kalpe'ṅgopadeśasāmarthyādyathāviniyogamadhikāravyavasthiterayama
dhikāravidhiḥ sāṅgasya pradhānasyā'nuṣṭhānamākṣipan karttāramantareṇa tadanupapatteḥ
karttāramākṣipati | na cā'sāmarthyasya kartṛtvaṃ kārakaviśeṣasya tadbhāvācchakti
mataśca kārakatvāt | kriyānimittaṃ hi kārakaṃ, nimittaṃ ca nāma taducyate
yasmin sati naimittikaṃ bhavatyevetyupariṣṭādvakṣyate | na ca dravyasvarūpamātre
sati kriyā bhavatyeva | tasmācchaktimadrūpakārakatvā'dhyavasānam | yathā na
dravyamātraṃ kārakaṃ tathā na śaktimadapi | abhibhūtaśakterapi śaktimataḥ kriyāyā
anupajananāt | tataścodbhūtaśaktereva sahakārisamavadhānaśālinaḥ kartṛtvaṃ
kārakatvaṃ ceti ramaṇīyam | na cā'dhikāramantareṇa kartṛtvaṃ na ca niyojya
mantareṇā'dhikāra iti tadāvirbhūtasāmarthyaṃ kartṛtāpraṇāḍikayā niyojyatā'
vasthāmupagatamanupādeyaṃ sanniyojyaviśeṣaṇatāmanubhavati | yathā cā'samartha
vadabhibhūtasāmarthyo'pyaniyojyo nā'dhikṛtaśca | taduktaṃ na tvasyā'dhikāra
hetutvavigame'dhikāro na nivarttata iti | na sāmarthyamātramanuṣṭhānā'nupayogi
sadapyadhikāramanuvarttayati, kiṃ tarhi yadanuṣṭhānaheturudbhavaviśiṣṭam, tasyai
vā'dhikārahetutvāt | taccedvigatamadhikāro'pi nivarttataeveti na kasya
cidaṅgasyopasaṃhārasāmarthye tirobhāve'pyaprayuñjānasya naimittikā'dhikārā'
tikrāntirityarthaḥ | api ca jāteṣṭau bhavatāṃ saṃvalitā'dhikārapakṣe janmavataḥ
[p.238]
kāminaḥ | anyathā'dhikṛtasya samarthasya svargakāmasya kādāvitkāṅga-
putrasyendriyādikāmasya pituradhikārāditarakāmyavatkaraṇībhūtasyaiva bhāvārthasya
viṣayabhāvāttanmātraviṣayatve kāmyatvā'nupapatternaimittikasya ca karaṇībhūta
bhāvārthaviṣayatve'pyavirodhāt yathāśaktiprayogā'nupapattirityāstāṃ tāvat |
athocyeta adhikārā'vagamaḥ pravṛttihetutvātpravṛtteḥ pūrvamupayujyate, na
pravṛttāvupajātāyām, na jātūpajanitakumbhaḥ kumbhakāraḥ kumbhakaraṇāya ghaṭate |
tadudbhūtasāṅgapradhānakaraṇasāmarthyo'dhikṛtaḥ sanyadi kutaścitpramādato'ṅgaikadeśe
na svasāmarthyamutpaśyati tathāpi tadā'nadhikṛto na virantumarhatītyata āha—
vītāyāmiva phalecchāyāṃ kāminaḥ | idamatrākūtam | asti hi kiṃcinni
mittikāraṇamapi yadupajanitakāryaṃ nivarttamānaṃ kāryamapi nivarttayati | yathā'
pekṣābuddhirdvitvasaṃkhyānimittamutpāditadvitvā nivarttamānā dvitvamapi nivartta
yati | samavāyasya samavāyikāraṇeṣu sarvatra prāyeṇeyameva gatiḥ | tadihāpi
svargakāmaḥ pravṛtto'pyuparatakāmo nā'dhikārī kevalaṃ śiṣṭavigarhaṇabhiyā'
nadhikṛto'pi karma samāpayati | tathā nimittavānapi tirohitasāmarthyo'sanna
nadhikārītyakurvannādhikāramatikrāmet | śiṣṭagarhaṇabhayāttu samāpanaṃ kāmye'pi
samānamiti na naimittikasya virodhāyā'lamiti |
yadi ca samarthasya pravṛttasya paścātsāmarthyā'bhibhavena pravṛttyuparame'
dhikārā'tikrāntirnaimittike kāmye'pi prasajyetetyāha—anyathā'dhikṛtasya
samarthasya svargakāmasya kādācitkā'ṅgavaikalye phalā'nutpattāvadhikārā'
tikrāntiḥ syāt | ye punarāhuḥ | ekāṅgavaikalye'pi prayogasya pratyabhijñā
nātpravṛttasyāpi nā'dhikāravigamaḥ, na khalu bhagnaśṛṅgo gauriti | tān prati
kāmye'pi samānamityuttaram | prasiddhataratvācca tadupekṣya dūṣaṇāntaramāha—
aprāptakālaṃ cā'kurvato'dhikārā'tikrāntiḥ syāditi cenā'nuṣajyate |
aprāptakālaṃ ca pañcamyāmiṣṭiprayogamakurvato'dhikārā'tikrāntirbhavedityarthaḥ |
athāpi syāt kimityevamaprāptakālamananutiṣṭhannadhikāramatikrāmati | na
hi nimittānantarameva naimittikaṃ karttavyamiti pramāṇamasti, kiṃ tarhi nimitte
satīti sattāmātramavagamyate | śāstrānmīmāṃsitvā vyavasthāpitamidaṃ hi
jāteṣṭau | evaṃ hi śrūyate | vaiśvānaraṃ dvādaśakapālaṃ nirvapetputre jāte iti |
tatra saṃśayaḥ kiṃ putrajanmā'nantarameva nirvaptavyam, uta kṛte jātakarmaṇi ? iti |
kiṃ prāptam | putrajanmā'nantaramiti | kutaḥ ? jāta iti nimittaśruteḥ |
[p.239]
śaktivakalye phalā'nutpattāvapyadhikārā'tikrāntiḥ syāt aprāptakālaṃ
cā''kurvataḥ |
athā'dhikṛto'pi jātaputravatkālaśauce kālamupasaṃhṛtya kurvannā'dhikāra
matikrāmati nimittānantaryyasyā'śabdārthatvāt | kālasaṃpattāvapyanyāṅga-
satyasminnakriyāyāṃ tadvirodhaprasaṅgāditi prāpte ucyate | kṛte jātakarmaṇīti |
kutaḥ ? iṣṭibhāvanāyāmasyāṃ bhāvyākāṅchāyāmarthavādato bahavaḥ khalviha
bhāvena sambandhuṃ samarthāstejasvitendriyāvitvādayaḥ putragāminaḥ śrūyante |
tatteṣāmeva rātrisatranyāyena phalatvakalpanā | yadi ca jātamātre eva kumāre
iṣṭiriyaṃ nirupyeta tato'kṛtajātakarmaṇaḥ stanyadānaniṣedhādiṣṭikriyāyāṃ ca
kālakṣepāt kumāravipattiprasaṅgāttadguṇavidhānārthamiṣṭiranarthikā syāditi
na jātamātre iti nirūpitam |
tathedamaparaṃ cintyate | avagatametannā'kṛte jātakarmaṇīti | idamidānīṃ
sandihyate kiṃ jātakarmā'nantarameva nirvaptavyam, āhosvidūrdhva daśarātrāt ?
iti | kumāramaraṇabhayātkāraṇājjanmā''nantaryamatikrāntam | kṛte tu jāta
karmaṇi putrajanmano nimittasya sanidhānāditi kramakāraṇā'bhāvājjātakarmā'
nantarameveti prāpte brūmaḥ | śucinā kamaṃ karttavyamiti śaucasyāpyadhikāra
kāraṇatvādānantaryasya ca jātakarmaṇaiva vadhitatvāt sati bhāvamātratayā
nimittabhāvā'balambanāddaśarātrasya parastādapi tadupatteratīte daśarātre iti
siddhāntaḥ |
punaścintyate daśarātrā'nantaramiyamiṣṭiḥ, utā'māvasyādikāla
pratīkṣā ? iti | tatrā'tyantavyavadhāne sati nimittasya nimittabhāvaḥ śruta
ekāntato bādhitaḥ syāditi daśarātrā'nantarameveti prāpte'bhidhīyate | kāla
pratīkṣeti | tadavacchinnasya nimittasya nimittabhāvānna jātvatikrānte daśarātre
darśapūrṇamāsakālāvacchinnasadbhāvo jātaputro jātaputratāmativarttate | tadevaṃ
jāteṣṭinyāyena nimittānantaryasyā'śābdatvāt pañcamyāmiṣṭimaprayuñjāno'pi
nā'dhikāramatipatatītyāha—athā'dhikṛto jātaputra iva kālaśauce kālaṃ ca
śaucaṃ ca nimitte putrajanmanyupasaṃhṛtya yathā jātaputro daśarātrasya parastādamā
vāsyāyāṃ paurṇṇamāsyāṃ vā jāteṣṭiṃ kurvannā'tipatatyadhikāraṃ tathā jīvanavānapi
nimitte jīvane darśapūrṇṇamāsakālamupasaṃhṛtya kurvannā'dhikāramatikrāmati,
nimittānantaryasyā'śabdārthatvāt | atrottaram—kālasaṃpattāvanyāṅgasaṃpatti
mapekṣya kurvastathā syāt | nā'dhikāramatikrāmedityarthaḥ |
[p.240]
saṃpattimapekṣya kurvastathā syāt | athāṅgabhūtakālāvacchinnameva jīvanamadhi
kārahetuḥ, anyāṅgeṣvapi tulyam | api ca paripūrṇāṅgo'dhikṛtaḥ kimaṅga
vikalaṃ prayuṅkte ? asaṃbhavāditi cenna | kalpanātrayopapluteḥ | tathā hi |
karmmaparyyantāpekṣitvādvā niyogasyā'dhikarānirvṛtteraprayogaḥ, adhikāra
vaśādvā yathāsaṃbhavamadhikāṅgopadeśā'nurodhādvā | yadā tādṛśasya saṃbhavaḥ
athāṅgamapi kālo nimittamavacchindannaṅgatāmupayāti, nā'navacchinda
nniti nimitte kālopasaṃhāro nāṅgopasaṃhāra ityāha—athā'ṅgabhūtakālāva
cchinnameva jīvanamadhikārahetuḥ | atrottaram—anyāṅgeṣvapi tulyam | mā nāma
bhūvannaṅgāni sākṣānnimittāvacchedakāni, aṅgopadeśavalasamāsāditanirṇayaṃ tu
sarvāṅgopasaṃhārasāmarthyamudbhūtaṃ nimittamavacchettuṃ pārayatīti tulyatā'ṅgāntarāṇāṃ
kālenetyabhiprāyaḥ |
evaṃ tāvaditarāṅgavirahavaccoditakālavirahe'pyanuṣṭhānaprasaṅgamāpādya
samprati kiñcidaṅgavaiguṇye'pi prayogapratyabhijñānaṃ yaduktaṃ tadapi dūṣayannāha—
api ca paripūrṇāṅgo'dhikṛtaḥ kimaṅgavikalaṃ prayuṅkte ? vidhigamyo'yamartho
nātra pramāṇāntaraṃ kramate | vidhinā ca yāvadaṅgagrāmasahitaḥ prayogo'va
bodhitastatrā'ṅgatuṣatyāge'pi tatprayogatvamanavabodhitaṃ vidhinā | puruṣasya
pratibhāmātraṃ na prāmāṇikamiti bhāvaḥ |
atra śaṅkate—asambhavāditi cet | śaṅkāṃ nirākartumasambhavaśabdārthaṃ
vikalpayati—na | kalpanātrayaparipluteḥ | tisraḥ khalviha kalpanāḥ paripla
vante | pariplutirupapattiḥ | saha vaikagranthena svā'nuguṇāṃ tāvatkalpanā
mādito darśayati—tathā hi | kramaparyantāpekṣitvādvā niyogasyā'dhikārā'
nirvṛtteraprayogaḥ | viniyogopādānā'vagataśeṣabhāvaṃ padārthā'vagame śabdā
bhihitaṃ ca tatkramamanaṅgamapyapekṣamāṇaḥ svotpattau niyogo yāvajjīviko
nā'ṅgahānāvutpattumarhatīti | sarvathaiva prayogā'bhāvo'saṃbhavaśabdārtha iti
codakā'nuguṇāṃ kalpanāmāha—adhikāravaśādvā yathāsambhavam | ayamarthaḥ |
yadyapyayaṃ paripūrṇṇāṅgo'pyadhikṛtastathāpi tadadhikārapratītisāmarthyāttatpravṛttau
hīnāṅgamapi samāpayati | kvacidaṅgakaraṇe sāmarthyā'bhāve saṃpūrṇāṅgaprayoga
karaṇāditi | so'yamasambhavaḥ | kalpanāṃ tṛtīyāmāha—adhikāṅgopadeśā'nu
rodhādvā yadā tādṛśasya sambhavaḥ adhikaṃ tatkālaśakyāṅgāpekṣayā yada
śakyamaṅgaṃ taducyateḥ | yadā sāṅgamupasaṃhartuṃ śakyate tadā tasya sambhavaḥ |
[p.241]
tatrobhayā'nugrahādānanta'ryasyā'śabdārthatvājjātaputreṣṭivadyadeti yuktam | aṅga 
śaktyā vā kālavadadhikārāvacchedādaprayogaḥ | na caika eva vidhisteṣāmeva
prayojakaścā'prayojakaśca | api ca kimarthaṃ vikalāṅge pravarttate ? adhi
kāranirvṛttaya iti brūmaḥ | kimadhikāranirvṛttirnāṅgāpekṣā phalanirvṛ
ttiriva ? yadyevamanaṅgatvameva | sāpekṣā cedyathāśaktyaṅgasādhyaiveti vaikalye
tatrā'nantarā'bhihitāṃ tāvatkalpanāmupapādayati—tatrobhayā'nugrahādā
nantaryasyā'śabdārthatvājjāteṣṭivadyadeti yuktam | asyārthaḥ | adhikāra
pratīteradhikāṅgopadeśasya cā'nugrahādyadeti yuktam | nanvevaṃ nimittā'nantara
makaraṇānnimittatvavyāhatirityāśaṅkyāha—ānantaryasyā'śabdārthatvājjāta
putreṣṭivaditi | vyākṛtametadyuktam | nanu yathāsambhavamaṅgānuṣṭhānaṃ tathā
kāmyanaimittikayoraviśeṣa iti dūṣaṇam | yadi punaraṅgopasaṃhārasāmarthya
syā'nupādeyatayā kālavadadhikārāvacchedakatvānnāṅgavaiguṇye kālā'bhāvaiva
kriyā, na cā'vaiguṇyaṃ yāvajjīvaṃ kasyacidapi sambhavati | tataśca
yāvajjīvasarvāṅgasamavetaṃ prayoktavyamityayamartho viruddhatvānna sambhavati | tato'
śakyārthavidhānāt jvaraharatakṣakacūḍāratnālaṅkāropadeśavadaprāmāṇikatvaṃ
śāstrasyeti sarvadaivā'prayoga ityāha—aṅgaśaktyā ca kālavadadhikārāvacche
dādaprayogaḥ | nanu yāvajjīvamupadeśasāmarthyātkimiti viguṇaḥ prayogo'pi
na kalpate ? ityāśaṅkyāha—na caika eva vidhisteṣāmeva prayojakaścā'
prayojakaśca | evaṃ hi sa evaikopi vidhistānyevā'ṅgānyākṣipati pratikṣi
pati ceti vyāhatamāpadyate | tasmādātyantika eva prayogā'sambhavo'sambhava
vādinā'bhihitaḥ | so'yamātmīya eva bāṇo bhavantaṃ praharatītyāgatamityarthaḥ |
evaṃ tāvadyāvadadhikārapradhānaṃ nikhilāṅgagrāmasahitaṃ prayojyatayā'vagataṃ
tāvadeva prayoktavyam | na cā'nyūnaprayogastu prātibha ityuktam | samprati
nyūnaprayogasya niṣprayojanavattāmapi pratipādayati—api ca kimartha vikalāṅge
pravarttate | prekṣāvatāṃ hi pravṛttiḥ prayojanavattāvyāptā prayojanavattābhāve na
bhavati, śiṃśapāvattvamiva tarutvā'bhāva ityarthaḥ |
atraikadeśī prayojanamāha—adhikārā'nirvṛttaye iti brūmaḥ | etaduktaṃ
bhavati | yamarthamadhikṛtya pravarttate tatprayojanaṃ prayujyate'neneti vyutpattyā,
kāryaṃ cā'dhikṛtya lokaḥ pravarttate, kārya cā'dhikāraḥ, tadayaṃ vikalāṅge'pi
karmaṇi kāryanirvṛttaye pravarttate iti | tadetad dūṣayati—kimadhikāra
nirvṛttirnāṅgāpekṣā, phalanivṛttiriva | nanu mā bhūdadhikāranirvṛttiraṅgāpekṣā,
ko doṣa ? ityāśaṅkyāha—yadyevamanaṅgatvamevāṅgairvināpi karaṇopakārasya
[p.242]
vṛthā ceṣṭā syāt | atha yathāśaktyaṅgasādhyeti | na kāryyaniṣṭhatā
viśeṣāyālam | api cā'dhikārātphalanirvṛttiḥ, tatra yathāśaktyaṅgopete'
dhikṛtasya tādṛśādevā'dhikāranirvṛttimukhena tannimittamiva | atha na tādṛśe ?
adhikāranirvṛttirapi na syāt | paripūrṇāṅge'dhikṛtasya vikalāṅga-
kāryasiddheriti bhāvaḥ | sāpekṣā cedyathāmnātāṅgasādhyaiveti vaikalye vṛthā
ceṣṭā syāt | śaṅkate—atha yathāśaktyaṅgasādhyeti | nirākaroti—na
kāryaniṣṭhatā viśeṣāyā'lam | etaduktaṃ bhavati | kāryaniṣṭhatāyāṃ satyāṃ
kāmye'pyupakaraṇībhūtabhāvārthasya viṣayatvātsakalāṅgagrāmajanitopakārasya ca
bhāvārthasya karaṇabhāvādekāṅgavaikalye ca tadanupapatterviṣayā'bhāvādadhikāra
pravilayaḥ | naimittikeṣu tu bhāvārthamātrasya viṣayabhāvādvaikalye'pi ca viṣaya
bhāvopapatterapralīno'dhikāra iti kāmyanaimittikayoryo viśeṣaḥ sa nopapadyate |
yathāśaktyaṅgasādhye'dhikāre'dhikāraścedaṅgavikalādapi pradhānādbhavati nūna
maṅgahīnamapi karaṇamakaraṇādanupapatteḥ | karaṇaṃ cetkathamaviṣayaḥ, viṣaya
ścetkathamanadhikāraḥ, anadhikāraścetkathaṃ kāmyanityayorviśeṣaḥ ? iti |
yadyucyeta naimittike bhāvārthamātrasya viṣayabhāvādaṅgavaikalye'pi tadastītyanapeto'
dhikāraḥ svasiddhyartha yathāśaktyaṅgajanitopakārameva svakāraṇamākṣipati
aṅgabādhāyāmivā'vādhitāṅgamātrāhitopakāramiti | tadasat | adhikāra
pravilayāt | na hi viṣayamātramayamadhikārasya vyavasthāpanāyā'pekṣate, api
tvadhikāriviśeṣaṇamapi | āvirbhūtasarvāṅgakaraṇasāmarthyaścādhikārī nāṅga
sāmarthyā'bhibhave'dhikāravānityuktamityuparamyate |
evaṃ puruṣapravṛttiṃ paryanuyujyā'dhikārotpattiṃ prati paryanuyogamāha—api
cā'dhikārāt phalanirvṛttiḥ kāmye'pi karmaṇīti bhavanmate siddham | astu
vā ko doṣaḥ ? ityāśaṅkyāha—tatra yathāśaktyaṅgopete'dhikṛtasya tādṛśā
deva yathāśaktyaṅgopetādevādhikāranirvṛttimukhena tatphalaṃ saṃpadyate | atraiva
dṛṣṭāntamāha—nimittamiva | yathā nimittaṃ vikalāṅgamapi naimittikaṃ
prayojayedadhikāranirvṛttimukhena nimittabhāvena saṃpādyate tathā phalamapītyarthaḥ |
atha na tādṛśe'ṅgahīne kāmye'dhikāraḥ | evaṃ tarhi kāmyavannaimittike
vikalāṅge'nadhikāranirvṛttirapi na syāt | yataḥ paripūrṇṇāṅgā'dhikṛtasya
vikalāṅgaprayoge karmāntaraprayoga iva | yathaiva khalvagnihotre'dhikṛtaścaityavandanāṃ
kurvannā'dhikāraṃ saṃpādayati evaṃ sāṅge tasminnadhikṛto'ṅgahīnaṃ nirvarttayanna
kṛtādhikāra ityarthaḥ |
syādetat | agnihotre'dhikṛtaścaityaṃ vandamāno nā'dhikāraṃ saṃpādayati |
adhikāraviṣayakaraṇasya homasyā'nanuṣṭhānāt | juhvatyuttarāṅgahīnamapyadhikāra
[p.243]
prayoge karmmāntaraprayoga iva aṅgapradhānabhedo'pyatrā'nupakārako'dhi
kāravidhiviṣayatvenā'bhedāt | bhede cā'naṅgatvaprasaṅgaḥ | aṅgapradhāna
bhāvasya cāsaṃpradhāryyatvāt |
viṣayasaṃpattestathābhūtasyaiva homasyā'dhikāreṇa karaṇabhāvā''kṣepādadhikāraṃ
saṃpādayatītyayamaṅgapradhānayorviśeṣo yatpradhānā'bhāve nā'dhikāro bhavati
aṅgābhāve ca sati pradhānaṃ bhavatītyāśaṅkyāha—aṅgapradhānabhedo'pyatrā'nupa
kārakaḥ | kutaḥ ? ityāha—adhikāravidhiviṣayatvenā'bhedāt | yadyapi
niyogasya bhāvārtha eva pratipattyanubandhitvādviṣayastathāpi sāṅgapradhāne
prayojakatvādaṅgānāṃ pradhānasya ca niyogasya viṣayatvenā'bheda yuktaḥ | yadi
punaretadbhayādadhikāraniyogasyā'ṅgeṣu prayojakatvaṃ nā''sthīyeta tato bhede
cā'pradhānasya prayojyatvenā'ṅgānāṃ cā'prayojyatvena viṣayatvā'viṣayatva
lakṣaṇe'ṅgīkriyamāṇe'naṅgatvaprasaṅgaḥ | yena yena vinā'yaṃ kāryasvabhāvo
'dhikāro notpadyate tattadātmopapattināntarīyakatvādākṣipati, nākṣipati
cedaṅgāni tarhi tairapi notpadyeta, tataścā'naṅgatvaprasaṅga iti bhāvaḥ |
kiṃ cā'syaikadeśino mate'ṅgapradhānabhāvasya cāsaṃpradhāryatpādanupakāraka
iti sambandhaḥ | idamatrā''kūtam | kāryarūpamapūrva pratyayārthaḥ | kāryaṃ ca
kṛtiṃ puruṣaprayatnaṃ prati yaduddeśyaṃ pradhānam, tacca yathā 'darśapūrṇṇamāsābhyāṃ
svargakāmo yajete tyetasminpratīyate tathā samidādivākyeṣvapīti kāryāntara
viṣayakāraṇānāṃ samidādīnāmadhikāraṃ pratyai damarthyā'nupapatteḥ kāryasya ca
samidādiviṣayasya 
kāryameva hi tanna syādyadanyārthamiti nyāyādadhikāra
kārya prati tādarthyā'bhāvādaṅgapradhānabhāvā'nupapattiḥ |
syānmatam, na hi kāryamityeva nā'nyārthamapi tu yadanuṣṭheyam | anuṣṭheyaṃ
taducyate yatpuruṣeṇa mamedaṃ kāryamiti nirapekṣaṃ kāryamavagamyate | paramā'pūrva
ca tanna jātu tasyā'nyārthapravṛtteḥ kṛtivyāpyatāpratītiḥ | anyasya pradhānasyā'
navagamāt | nanu phalamīpsitamavagamyate na prathamataḥ pratītena kāryeṇa
niyojyaṃ vinā paryavasyatā viśiṣṭaniyojyā'pekṣiṇā svargakāma iti niyojya
viśeṣaṇatayā grahaṇānna phalapratipādanaparaṃ tatpadaṃ tadeva tu nirapekṣaṃ kāryamavagataṃ
sādhyasvargaviśiṣṭena nā'nutpādayatsvargamutpadyata iti svargopāyatāmaśnuta iti
nirapekṣamaparakāryā'pagame sati phalā'vagatirna punaḥ phalā'vagatau satyāṃ
kāryā'vagatiḥ | yataḥ sā'pekṣatvena paramā'pūrvamanuṣṭheyaṃ syāt | tadeva tu
nirapekṣaṃ prayojanaṃ satparamā'pūrvamabhidadhāti liṅ svasannidhisamāmnānaiḥ svayaṃ
prayojanībhūtairavaghātādibhiḥ samidādibhiśca prayojanibhiranvitamabhidhate iti
[p.244]
satyapi codanārthā'nirvṛttervṛthā ceṣṭāprasaṅga ityuktam |
kaścāyabhadhikāro yasya nirvṛttiḥ ? niyogaścet na nirvṛttirvi-
tadidaṃ grāhakagrahaṇamabhidhīyate | tadevaṃ sā'dhikāravidhisannidhisamāmnāteṣu
samidādividhiṣu tadupagṛhītaviṣaye ṣu tataḥ paramā'pūrvalakṣaṇakāryapratyabhijñānānna
drāgityeva kāryāntarabuddhirudayamāsādayati | paścāttu kathamapi samidādayaḥ
kriyārūpatayā viśarāravaḥ parasparamasaṃbhavantaḥ prokṣaṇādivadapūrvasādhanībhūtadravya
saṃskārādidvāreṇā'pūrvaviṣayā'nanupraveśinaḥ paramā'pūrveṇa grahīṣyante iti
prasiddhataraidamarthyānāmavāntarā'pūrva kārya samidādīnāmavakalpyamānaṃ samidādi
gatā''khyātā'bhidheyamapi paramā'pūrvapāratantryeṇā'bhidhīyamānatvātkāryamapya
nanuṣṭheyamityupapanno'ṅgabhāvaḥ kāryaviṣayāṇāmapi samidādīnām |
atrocyate | adhikāravākyeṣu tāvadamī liṅādayo nirapekṣakāryā'bhi
dhānaviditaśaktayo na samidādivākyeṣvanyaniṣṭhaṃ kāryamabhidhātumīśate | yadi
paramavahantyādivatkimādhikārā'pūrvasyaite'nuvādā, utā'pūrvāntarā'bhidhyāyinaḥ ?
iti padāntaramavaśiṣyate na punarliṅgādyabhidheyasya svapradhānasya kāryasya
prādhānyaparityāgo yuktaḥ | nanūktamadhikārasamāmnātāḥ khalvamī samidāda
yastenaivā'dhikāravidhinā'bhidhānata evā''pāditaidamarthyā na svaniṣṭhakārya
viṣayatāṃ pratipattumarhanti, prakṛtā'pūrvaidamarthyavirodhāt | na cā'vahantyādi
vatprakṛtā'pūrvaviṣayā'nupraveśinaḥ, tataśca nā'nuvādāḥ | na ca svaniṣṭhakāryā
'bhidhāyīni tatpadānītyabhidheyaśūnyatayā pramattagītāni prasajyeranniti varaṃ
prādhānyamātratyāgena kāryā'bhidhāyitvamastīti |
satyamuktam | ayuktaṃ tu tat | na hi sā'dhikāro vidhiḥ saṃnidhi
samāmnātamātrameva gṛhṇātimātraṃ hī ? jyotiṣṭomavidhirdvādaśopasattām
api tu yadgrahaṇārham | na ca svaniṣṭhakāryaviṣayāḥ samidādayo grahaṇamarhanti |
nanu grāhakavirodhātprādhānyaṃ samidādiviṣayasya niyogasya nāsti | atha
prādhānyapratītivirodhād grāhaka eva kimiti na parihriyate ? tatra śrutimātrā
deva niyogaprādhānyapratīternirapekṣatvādvākyāntarā''locanayā grāhakagṛhīteḥ
sā'pekṣatayā prādhānyapratīterbalīyastvena rāttrisatrādivatsvatantrā'dhikārakalpanāyāṃ
satyāṃ nā'ṅgabhāvaḥ prayājādīnāṃ pradhānabhāvaśca darśapūrṇṇamāsādīnāmupapa
dyeta iti suṣṭhūktamaṅgapradhānabhāvasya cāsaṃpradhāryatvāditi |
abhyupetyāṅgapradhānabhāvaṃ dūṣaṇāntaramāha—satyaṅgapradhānabhāve naimitti
kasyā'naṅtve'sya prayoge codanārthā'nirvṛttervṛthā ceṣṭāprasaṅga ityuktam | kiṃ
[p.245]
cāritā | niyojyatā cet sāpi niyogādeva nirvṛttā | svāmitā cet
na tatkṛ taṃ kiṃcidabhuñjānaḥ svāmī, ityubhāvapyadhikārau kāryyaniṣṭhau
syātām | tasmānna kāryyaniṣṭhatvaprayoganiṣṭhatvābhyāṃ kāmyanaimittikā'dhi
kārayorbhedaḥ śakyaḥ samādhātumityanyathā samādheyaḥ |
taducyate—
jīvanādernnimittasya sāmyātkālo viśeṣakaḥ |
nimittārthastatra jāte karmmaṇo'vaśyakāryyatā ||
cā'yamadhikāro vivāraṃ na sahate yasya nirvṛttiraṅgavikale'pi naimittikasya
prayoge tvayocyata ityāha—kaścā'yamadhikāro yasya nirvṛttiḥ ? tvayocyata
iti śeṣaḥ | ekadeśī svaparibhāṣayā''ha—niyogaścet | dūṣayati—
tannirvṛttirvicāritā | uktaṃ hi 
niyogasya na kāryatetyatra 
viṣayaḥ kāryo
na niyojyasya niyoktṛtvaprasaṅgāditi | bhāṣāntareṇā''ha—niyojyatā cet
pariharati—sāpi niyogādeva nirvṛttā bhāvārthaviṣayaṃ niyogamavabuddhyamāna
evāyamadhigataniyojyabhāvo na tadadhigamāyā'nuṣṭhānaṃ yāvadgacchediti |
vyarthīkṛtaparibhāṣo laukikamadhikārapadasyārthamāha—svāmitā cet | īśvara
vacano hyayamadhikṛtaśabdo loke prasiddha iti bhāvaḥ | etaddūṣayati—na
tatkṛtaṃ kiṃcidabhuñjānaḥ svāmī, evaṃ hi naimittikaṃ karma saṃbhavati puruṣasya
svāminaḥ, yadi tatkarma tasyopakāre varttate | dvayī ceyamupakārajātihiṃtaprāpti
rahiṃtanivṛttiśca | na ca hitā'hitaprāptiparihārā'nuyāyi tvanmate naimittikaṃ
karma kalpate | yaducyate naimittikādanyapūrvapuruṣaguṇo jāyate, jāyatāṃ nāma na
punarayamadhikāraḥ svayamasukharūpatvādataddhetutvācca | yadi tvetadbhayānnaimitti
kasyāpi puruṣopakārakatvamaṅgīkriyeta tata ubhāvapyadhikārau kāryaniṣṭhau
syātām | itiśabdastasmādarthe | prakṛtamupasaṃharati—tasmānna kāryaniṣṭha
tvaprayoganiṣṭhatvābhyāṃ kāmyanaimittikādhikārayorbhedaḥ śakyā'dhyavasānaḥ |
svamatapātanikāṃ karoti—tasmādanyathā samādheyaḥ | taducyate |
jīvanādernimittasya sāmyātkālo viśeṣakaḥ |
nimittārthastatra jāte karmaṇo'vaśyakāryatā ||
yadi śrutimātrā'pekṣo'dhikāro bhavet tato'ndhaśūdrādīnāṃ kāmye
naimittike cā'viśeṣeṇa prasajyeta, traivarṇṇikānāṃ cā'gnihotraṃ satatamakurvatā
madhikārā'tikrāntiḥ syāt | na tvasmākaṃ śrutyapekṣo'dhikāraḥ kiṃ tvaṅgo
[p.246]
padeśā'nurodhena śaktyapekṣaḥ | tathā cā'śaktānāmandhaśūdrādīnāṃ traivarṇṇikānāṃ
ca madhyandine sāyamādisamayamupasaṃhartumasamarthānāṃ nā'dhikāraḥ | nanvevaṃ
kāmyavannaimittikeṣvapyaṅgopadeśā'nurodhena yadetyupapadyeta |
tatrocyate | idaṃ tāvadatra bhavān pṛṣṭo vyācaṣṭām, yadetatkāmye
sarvāṅgopasaṃhārasamartho'dhikārīti, tatkiṃ svargakāmavatsākṣācchabdā'vagatam,
utāṅgopadeśā'nurodhāt ? tatra na tāvatsvargakāmapadamiva sarvāṅgopasaṃhāra
samarthapadamadhīyate samāmnātāraḥ | athāṅgopadeśā'nurodhādasamartha prati
tadupadeśā'nupapatteḥ | ākhyātānāmarthaṃ bruvatāṃ śaktiḥ sahakāriṇīti | yathā
sruveṇā'vadyatītyatrā'śrutamapi dravyādi yacchakyata ityupabandhātsambandhyate evama
śrutamapi sarvāṅgopasaṃhārasāmarthyamanupādeyaṃ sadadhikṛtaviśeṣaṇatayā sambhantsyate |
yuktaṃ kāmye tatra sarvāṅgopasaṃhārasāmarthyaviṣayāyā arthāpatteḥ paripanthyabhāvenā'
pratyūhamupapatteḥ arthipadasya ca samarthe'pi sambhavādavirodhāt | na khalu
samarthaḥ svargakāma ityuktam | iha tu nimittaśrutivirodhaḥ | tathā hi |
yāvajjīvaṃ juhuyāditi yāvadupasaṃbaddhaṃ jīvanaṃ karttavyatāyāṃ nimittam |
yasmin sati naimittikaṃ bhavatyeva bhāktamitaradityupariṣṭādupapādayiṣyate |
tena jīvane sati karttavyameva na na karttavyamiti pratīternimitte sambandhā'vaśya
karttavyatā''pattiḥ, nimittavadaṅgopadeśā'nurodhāt | tadupasaṃhārasāmarthyaviśeṣa
udbhavasamādhau khyātaḥ pratīyate | sa cā'nitya iti tannāntarīyakatvāda
vaśyakarttavyatā''patati | na cāsti kaścidapi puruṣadhaureyastathāpi vidhau
daivasaṃpanno yo yāvajjīvaṃ sāyaṃprātarapratyūhamavikalāṅgamagnihotramapi prayoktuṃ
samarthaḥ | antarāyāṇāṃ vyādhitodapramādādīnāmavaśyambhāvaniyamāt | tatsa
habhuvāṃ ca duḥkhadaurmanasyādīnām | tadanayārnimittopadeśasarvāṅgopasaṃhāra
sāmarthyayorvirodhānnimittasya ca śrautatvena tadanuguṇaṃ sāmarthyamupavarṇanīyam |
nanvaṅgānyapi śrutānyeva kimatastānyapi nityavacchrutānīti bahūnāṃ śrutīnāma
nurodhena varamekā nimittaśrutiḥ tyajedekaṃ kulasyā'rthe itivatparityajyatām |
tatkimiyaṃ nimittaśrutistapasvinī pramattagītameva api tu mukhyaṃ parityajya
gauṇaṃ nimittabhāvaṃ yasminsatyeva nā'satītyevaṃ bhūtamabalambate ?
atrocyate | na tāvatsākṣādaṅgaśrutayo nimittaśrutivirodhinyaḥ | na
hi tasya tasyāṅgasya kriyā nimitte sati na sambhavati | nanu nityavacchru
tāni na cāṅgānīti virodhaḥ | satyaṃ nityavacchrutāni na tvaṅgavidhayaḥ
pradhānavidhinirapekṣā aṅgānuṣṭhānaṃ prayoktumīśate | tathā hyaṅgabhāvanā
[p.247]
yastāvadyathoktāya prayogāyaikāntato'samarthaḥ sa dūraparyyudasta evā'dhi
kārāt | na tvasau tādṛśe niyojyatayā pratyetuṃ pāryyate | tena śaktyaṅgo-
bhāvyā''kāṅghā svavākye bhāvyamapaśyantī vidhyāpāditatvena cā'puruṣārthā
dvācyārthātpracyāvitā puruṣārthātmakaṃ bhāvyamapekṣate, pradhānabhāvanā ca nimitta
vatyavaśyakarttavyatāgocaro labdhanityasamīhitasādhyatatsādhanā svasādhanopakāra
hetumapekṣate | tadiyamaṅgabhāvanā karaṇopakāralakṣaṇaṃ bhāvyamāsādya pradhāna
bhāvanā ca karaṇopakāraṃ prāpya nirvṛṇutaḥ | tataśca pradhānavidhyākāṅghitopa
kārasādhaneṣvaṅgeṣu pradhānā'dhikṛtā'dhikāraprāptilabdhānuṣṭhāne'pyapradhānānu
rodhena nivṛttirvyavasthāpanīyā | tadvirodhe tu tadadhikṛtā'dhikārabhāvenā'nuṣṭhā
nā'nupapatteraṅgavidhīnāṃ tattvaṃ hīyeta | na ca nimittasambandhādavaśyaṅkarttavyaṃ
pradhānaṃ kādācitkenāṅgasākalyena sambandhārham, nityā'nityasambandhavirodhāt |
nanu pradhānamapi kathaṃ nimittena saṃbhantsyate ? na hyagnihotramaṅgahīnamapi
yāvajjīvaṃ śavayasaṃpādam | antarāyāṇāmapi saṃbhavāt | yadyucyeta vidhi
sāmarthyādanuṣṭhātrā nipuṇena bhavitavyam | tadaṅgasākalye'pi tulyam |
maivam | yasya hi nimittasambandhastadavaśyakarttavyam, sa ca pradhānasyeti tadeva
tathā | tatra ca svarūpeṇā'nyā'sattvādanuṣṭhāturnaipuṇyamapi sambhavati na
tvaṅgeṣu nimittaśrutiḥ | yatasteṣāmapyavaśyakarttavyatā syāt | pradhāna
sambandhātteṣāmapyavaśyambhāva iti cet, nā'vaśyakarttavyatopahitamaryādena
pradhānavidhinā vāstavamaṅgasākalyasyāvaśyambhāvamananurudhyamānena yadā 
yāvantyaṅgānītyupabandhāt | tadevaṃ nimittaśrutiḥ pradhānavākyasthā prathamameva
svaviṣayaṃ pradhānena sahaikalolībhāvamupanayantī bhūyasīrapyaṅgaśrutīḥ svā'nuguṇa
tayā vyavasthāpayantī naitābhirjadhanyavṛttitāmāpādyate | na caivamitarāṅgavaika
lyavatkālāpagame'pi pradhānaprayogasambhavānna tasya hi guṇatve'pi nimittā'nu
praveśo'pi vakṣyate | na cāndhaśūdrādīnāmadhikāraḥ | yato yāvajjīva
magnihotraṃ juhuyāt iti bhāvanālabdhasādhyatatsādhanā kathamiti svakaraṇo
pakārajanakākāṅkṣā aṅgopadeśairnirākāṅkṣīkriyate | tatra sarvāṅgopadeśā
nurodhādasamarthasyānuṣṭhānā'sambhavāt samarthasyāpi cā'nubhūtatadbhāvasya tadanupapatteḥ
sarvāṅgopetapradhānā'nuṣṭhānagocarasāmarthyodbhūtiradhikārī kāmyavaditi sāmarthya
tadudbhūtiścā'dhikṛtaviśeṣaṇatayā'vagamyate | tatra nimittaśrutivirodha iti
tadavirodhāyā'vaśyā'baheye'nyatarasyā'nudbhavamātraṃ hīyatāṃ nāṅgasāmarthyam,
ubhayahāniprasaṅgāditi nā'ndhaśūdrādīnāmadhikāro nāpi śakyapratikāravyādhī
nāmanadhikāro naimittika iti siddham |
[p.248]
padeśāvapekṣya samarthasya tāvadadhikārapratītiḥ | sā ceyamadhikārapratīti
rnneti paryyavasitā anityatvātsāmarthyasyaanyathā'dhikṛtasya sarvāṅgopasaṃhārā'
bhāve nā'nuṣṭhānaṃ yathāsaṃbhavam anyathā'nyādṛśe'dhikārāttādṛśaprayogasyā'
śāstrīyatvānnānuṣṭhānamanadhikārāt | ataḥ punarapi śaktyapekṣā'dhikāraprati
pattiparyyavasānāya | śaktirhi pramāṇaikadeśa ityuktam | na prayoganiśca-
tadidamāha—yastāvat yathoktāya prayogāyaikāntato'samarthaḥ śūdra
paṅgvādiḥ sa dūraparyudasta evādhikārāt | kutaḥ ? ityāha—ta tvasau tādṛśe 
niyojyatayā pratyetuṃ pāryate | tena śaktyaṅgopadeśāvapekṣya samarthasya tāvadadhikāra
pratītistāvaditi | sphoṭayati—sāceyamadhikārapratipattirnaimittike neti
paryavasitā anityatvātsāmarthyasyā'naimittikasya cā'vaśyakāryatvāt avaśyama
navaśyambhāvayośca vipratiṣedhāditi bhāvaḥ | nanu bhavatu sarvāṅgopasaṃhāra
sāmarthyamanityaṃ tathāpi tenaivā'dhikārapratipattiḥ paryavasyatu, samarthastvadhikṛtaḥ
kutaścitpratibandhātkiñcidaṅgamupasaṃhartuṃ tadānīmaśakto'pi yathākathaṃcitpra
yokṣyata ityetannirākarttumāha—anyathā'dhikṛtasya sarvāṅgopasaṃhārābhāve sati
nā'nuṣṭhānaṃ yathāsambhavamanyatheti | yadyetāvatyadhikārapratyayaḥ paryavasye
dityarthaḥ | kasmādityāha—anyādṛśe'dhikārātsarvāṅgopapanne tatsamarthasyā
'dhikārāt tādṛśaprayogasya nyūnāṅgasya prayogasyā'śāstrīyatvānnānuṣṭhānam,
nyūnāṅgasyā'nadhikārāt |
etaduktaṃ bhavati | adhikārapratītyā saha pravṛtteḥ saṃpratipattau satyāṃ
śāstrīyatvam, iha tvaṅgasākalyavaikalyalakṣaṇe viruddhadharmasaṃsargādviṣayabhede'pi
pratipattau satyāṃ prātibhatvaṃ prayogasyetyupasaṃharati—ataḥ punarapi śaktyapekṣā
'dhikārapratītiparyavasānāya | śaktyapekṣeti yā'sāvaṅgopadeśāsāmarthyalabdhā
'śaktiśrutistasyā yadetyupabandhasya nityaśrutivirodhādupabandhāntarāpekṣopa
bandhāvaśaktiriti śaktyapekṣetyuktam | śaktyā ca viṣayeṇa viṣayiṇīṃ
śaktiśrutiṃ lakṣayati | yathā dhūmādagniḥ pratīyate iti dhūmena dhūmajñānalakṣaṇā |
atha kasmādadhikārapratipattiparyavasānāya śaktiśrutirapekṣyate | yāvatopabandha
viśeṣaśūnyāyāmadhikārapratipattau paryavasitāyāṃ paścādyathāśaktiprayogaṃ śakti
reva niścāyayiṣyatītyata āha—śaktiḥ śaktiśrutiḥ pramāṇaikadeśaścodanaika
deśa ityuktamācāryaiḥ | tathā ca tadantarbhāvenaivā'dhikārapratītiparyavasāna
mityarthaḥ | yadi tu na codanaikadeśaḥ syātprameyamātraṃ śaktirna prayoganiścayāya
kalpeteti śeṣaḥ | idamatrākūtam | tattvaniścayo hi pramāṇasya, kāryaprameyā
[p.249]
yāya, ananyapramāṇakatvāttasya | sā kālato'ṅgato vā''śrīyeta | tatrāṅgataḥ
saṃśraye śabdā'vagatānāmaṅgāntarāṇāṃ hānācchabdavirodhaḥ | kālatastu
kālāntarāṇāmaśabdārthatvānna kaścidvirodhaḥ | sa cā'yamavirodho nirnnimitte
'dhikāre idamanenedṛśenetyetāvanmātrasya śabdārthatvāt | sannimitte tu nimitta
śrutivirodhaḥ | saṃprāpte ca tasminkāryyasya tannimittatvavyāghātāt |
itaratrāpi svargakāmaśrutivirodha iti cet | uktam | na ca puruṣaviśeṣanirddeśo-
ca śaktiriti kāraṇavirudhopalabdhyā prayoganiścayo nivarttata iti | atha
śaktijñānameva pramāṇaṃ satprayogaṃ niścāyayet | kiṃ codanaikadeśatvena
śaktiśruteḥ ? ityāha—ananyapramāṇakatvāttasya prayogasya na śaktijñānaṃ
pramāṇamiti |
saṃprati pūrvapakṣī yasminsatyeva nā'satīti nityavadaṅgopadeśā'nurodhā
nnimittabhāvaṃ manyamāno vimṛśati—sā ca śaktiḥkālato vā'ṅgato vā''śrīyate
kālādveti | sarvāṅgopasaṃhārasāmarthyamapekṣya yadā śaknuyāditi yāvat |
evaṃ vimṛśyāṅgata iti pratipakṣanirākaraṇapūrvakaṃ kālāditi pakṣamavadhārayati—
tatrāṅgataḥ saṃśraye śabdā'vagatānāmaṅgāntarāṇāṃ hānācchabdavirodhaḥ | kāla
tastu kālāntarāṇāmaśabdārthatvānna virodhaḥ | nityavadaṅgānāmupadeśādanya
tamatyāge tadvirodhaḥ | yadetyupabandhe tu kālāntarāṇāmanupadiṣṭatvānnopadeśa
virodha iti kāmye naimittike ca yadetyeva yuktam |
pariharati—sa cā'yamavirodho nirnimitte'dhikāre | kutaḥ ? idaṃ
bhāvyamanena karaṇenedṛśenetikarttavyatāviśeṣavatetyetāvanmātrasya śabdārthatvānnā
vaśyakarttavyatā pratīyata ityarthaḥ | sannimitte tu nimittaśrutivirodhe kasmā
tsamprāpte ca tasminna kāryasya tannimitatvavyāghātātsamprāpta iti prakṛti
pratyayābhyāmanupādeyatāṃ nimittatāṃ ca kathayati | caśabdo'perarthe virodha
dyotanaḥ | sati bhavatyeva na bhavatīti nimittārtho na bhavadukta iti vakṣyate |
na cāṅgavidhivirodha iti prapañcitam iti bhāvaḥ |
pārśvasthaḥ siddhāntinaṃ paryanuyuṅkte—itaratrāpi svargakāmaśrutivirodha
iti cet | samarthamasamarthaṃ cā'rthimātraṃ pratyasaṃkuñcitavṛttirarthiśrutiḥ samartha
prati niyamyamānā pīḍyataityarthaḥ | samādhatte—uktamiti | kevalapadārtha
vyavahārā'bhāvādityādinā prāk | arthipadasya cā'dhikārivacanatāmabhyu
petyedamuktam | paramārthatastu nedamadhikāripratipādanaparam, api tu bhāvanā
kāṅkṣitabhāvyaviśeṣaparam | arthāttvadhikāripratipattirityupariṣṭādihaiva vakṣyate
[p.250]
'tra vivakṣitaḥ, sādhyatvāt | na ca sātatyena prayogaḥ | nimitte kāla
viśeṣavataḥ karttavyatā'vagamāt | kālaviśeṣeṇa tasyā'rthādviśeṣaṇāt |
tasyā nimittā'nupraveśitvāt | taduktaṃ 
na kālo guṇo nimittaṃ hyeta
diti | na guṇa evetyarthaḥ | natvaṅgāntarāṇyevam, asāmyādvacanavyaktibhedāt |
uktaṃ ca bhāvanāviveke, tato
'dhikāripadā'bhāvātkutastadvirodhiteti iti
darśayati—na ca puruṣanirdeśo'tra vivakṣitaḥ, sādhyaparatvāt |
evamuktāṃ kārikāmuktvā kārikārthapātanikāṃ karoti ca kiṃcit—na
ca sātatyena prayogaḥ | atraiva śaṅkate—yadi nimittaśrutivaśādaṅgahīnamapi
yathāśakti prayujyeta tataḥ kālasyāpi sāyamāderaṅgatvena vidhānāttadabhāve
madhyandinādau jīvanasya nimittasya vidyamānatvādagnihotraprayogaprasaṅgaḥ |
sāyamādyaṅgāpagamena vā tadā tadaprayoge 'ṅgāntarābhāvepyaprayoga iti kāmya
vadyadevetyevaṃ prāptamiti | tadanena nirākriyate—na ca sātatyena prayoga
iti | nimitte kālaviśeṣavataḥ karttavyatāvagamāt | etaduktaṃ bhavati |
prayogāṅgamapyayaṃ kālo'nupādeyatvādaṅgopasaṃhārasāmarthyamiva niyojyaṃ
viśinaṣṭi | tena tadabhāvānmadhyandinādau nā'dhikāre viṣṇukramaṇādisāma
rthyā'bhāvādiva paṅgvādīnāmiti | nanvaṅgatayā kālaḥ śrūyate nādhikṛtavi
śeṣaṇatayā | tathā hi | apravṛttapravartantātmako vidhiḥ pravartyapuruṣaviśeṣa
samadhigatasāṅgapradhānatayā, tatkathaṃ tadvato'dhikārāvagamaḥ ? ityata āha—
kālaviśeṣeṇa tasyārthādviśeṣaṇāt | yadyapyaṅgatvena śrūyate kālastathāpyadhi
kṛtaviśeṣaṇam | tathā hyapravartapravartanātmako vidhiḥ pravartyapuruṣasamadhigata
sāṅgapradhānaviṣayo'nupādeyavastvavacchinnaṃ pravartyapuruṣaviśeṣaṃ kalpayitumudyataḥ
tasya viṣayaṃ cā'nupādeyatvavidheyatvalakṣaṇayoḥ prāptyāptibhyāṃ virodhādvi
śeṣaṇatayā svīkartumasamartho yathāviniyogamadhikārāvagamācca tannāntarīyaka
sāṅgapradhānopasaṃhārasāmarthyamanurudhyamāno'nupādeyāṅgasāmarthyaṃ ca nimittaṃ
cāṅgamapi pravartyapuruṣaviśeṣaṇatayā gṛhṇāti | na ca kālasyetarāṅgagrāma
vatsvakriyopasaṃhārasāmarthyamapi sambhavati kāryāṅgatvātsāmarthyasya kāryasyopa
saṃhāralakṣaṇasya tadanupādeyā'nupapatteḥ | kāle hi karma kriyate na karmaṇi
kālaḥ | tasmātkālaviśeṣeṇāṅgenāpi satā vidhiḥ pravartyapuruṣaviśeṣā'pe
kṣāyāmarthāttadviśeṣaṇāttadvataḥ karttavyatā'vagamaḥ | tataśca na sātatyena prayoga
iti | kutaḥ ? ityata āha—tasyāpi nimittā'nupraveśitvānnimitta
sambandhaścaikaṃ niyojyā'vacchedalakṣaṇo yathā sāmānyaguṇayorekavyaktisamavāyaḥ
[p.251]
upādeyāni hyaṅgāntaṇyanupādeyaṃ nimittam | ebhiretāni vā kuryyā
dityaṅgeṣu vacanavyaktiḥ | asmin satīti nimitte | tato'ṅgānta
rāṇi nālaṃ nimittamanupraveṣṭum | kālastvanupādeyaḥ samānavacanavyakti
raṅgamapi sannimittamanupraviśatīti yuktam | atha nimittopasaṃhāra eva
kiṃ neṣyate ? kāmye'pi tatkālecchayā | nanu prayojanametat ? satyam |
sambandho gauḥ śukla iti sāmānādhikaraṇyahetuḥ | ācāryasyāpi tatrabhavata
etadabhimatamityāha—taduktaṃ na kālo guṇo nimittaṃ hyetaditi | nanu
prayogāṅgaṃ kāla iti svatantrasiddhāntabhaṅgaḥ prasakta ityāśaṅkyāvyācaṣṭe—na
guṇa evetyarthaḥ | nigittatvaṃ ca nimittasambandhād bhāktaṃ na punastattvameva,
tatkalpanāyāṃ pramāṇā'bhāvāt | nanu yadyaṅgamapi kālo nimittā'nu
praveśī kasmānnāṅgāntarāṇyapyevam ? ata āha—na tvaṅgāntarāṇyevam |
kutaḥ ? asāmyānnimittena | tadeva kutaḥ ? vacanavyaktibhedāt | kiṃ
punaḥ kāraṇaṃ vacanavyaktibhede ? ityata āha—upādeyāni hyaṅgāntarāṇi
kālāt anupādeyaṃ nimittam, tato vacanavyaktibhedaḥ | taddarśayati—ebhire
tāni vā kuryāditi aṅgeṣu vacanavyaktiḥ | aṅgaiḥ khalu karaṇopakāre kriya
māṇe'kṛtāni na santīti tānyapi kiyantaeva | tadidamupādeyatvamaṅgānām |
tena tatrobhayī vacanavyaktirupapannā | etairetāni puruṣaprayatnā'gocare
tvasminsatīti nimitte vacanavyaktiḥ | tato vacanavyaktibhedādaṅgāntarāṇi
nā'laṃ nimittamanupraveṣṭum | kālastvanupādeyo yatastasmānnimittasamāna
vacanavyaktiḥ kālo'ṅgamapi sannimittamanupraviśatīti yuktam | yathā
anupādeye nimitte karma vidhīyate tathā kāle'pi na tvaṅgāntareṣviti nimitta
vatkālo'dhikṛtaviśeṣaṇaṃ nāṅgāntarāṇīti siddham |
atra pārśvasthaścodayati—atha nimittopasaṃhāra eva kiṃ neṣyate ?
prathamaṅgatvena sambadhyate kālaḥ paścāttvarthānnimittamanupraviśatīti kimanayā
paramparayā ? yatsāmānyena yāvajjīvamiti śruteḥ prasṛtaṃ jīvanaṃ śrutyaivā'yaṃ
kālavidhiḥ kālaviśeṣe upasaṃhariṣyati | neyaṃ kālaśrutiraṅgaśrutirapi tu
nimittaśrutirevāstvityarthaḥ |
pārśvasthāntaraṃ pariharati—kāmye'pi tatkālecchayā | ayamarthaḥ |
yadīyaṃ paurṇṇamāsyāmamāvāsyāmamāvāsyāyāmiti nimittaśrutiḥ syāt
tatkāmyena na sambadhyeta, na caikameva vākyaṃ tasyaiva nimittatāmaṅgatāṃ ca
bodhayituṃ pārayati vairūpyāt | nimittatayā cobhayasambandhe kāmye'pi
yathāśakti prayogaprasaṅgaḥ | asambandhe vā kāmyasyā'niyatakālatvā''pa
ttiriti |
[p.252]
hetustu yāvajjīvaśruteranatiriktārthatvam | prakaraṇino'pyaṅgārthatvaṃ ca |
tadevamavirodhādanimitte kālataḥ śaktyāśrayo yadeti, nimittavati tu nimitta
virodhātkālavata evāṅgato yatheti |
tadetad dūṣayati—nanu prayojanametat | ayamabhisandhiḥ | yadi
pramāṇapratyālocanayā kāmyasyā'niyatakālatvaṃ bhavati bhavatu kā naḥ pīḍā
prāmāṇikānām ? na jātu prayojanā'nuvarti pramāṇam, pramāṇavaśavarttitvā
ttasyeti |
paramasiddhāntī brūte—satyam, na prayojanādhīnaṃ pramāṇamityarthaḥ | hetustu
yāvajjīvaśruteranatiriktārthatvam idamatrākūtam | tatra nāma sāmānyaśruti
rviśeṣe upasaṃhriyate yatra sā viśeṣaśāstrādatiriktā'rthā bhavati, tadyathā
puroḍāśaṃ caturdhā karotīti sāmānyaśrutirāgneye upasaṃhriyate āgneyaṃ caturdhā
karotītyanena, tatra yadyāgneyaṃ caturdhā karotītyetāvanmātraṃ na śrūyate anāgne
yasyāpi prasajyeta tadubhayamarthavat | yadi tu paurṇṇamāsyāmamāvāsyāyāṃ vā
nimitte karma kriyamāṇaṃ tarhi nā'jīvato nāpyayāvajjīvaṃ syāt | nimitte
satyavaśyakarttavyatvāt naimittikasyeti yāvajjīvaśruteranatiriktārthatvaprasaṅgaḥ |
athocyeta yadi yāvajjīvaṃ darśapūrṇamāsābhyāmiti na paṭhyate tataḥ samudāya
dvayasya pratyekaṃ nimittasambandhātpratyekamadhikāraviṣayatvaṃ prapadyeta | tanmā'tra
prasāṅkṣīditi yāvajjīvaṃ darśapūrṇṇamāsābhyāmiti vākyārthaḥ | anena hi
yāgaṣaṭkasyaikā'dhikāraviṣayatvamāpādyata iti | tathāpi yāvajjīvamiti
vyartham, darśapūrṇṇamāsābhyāṃ yajetetyetāvanmātrameva paṭhitavyam | na cātra sa
svargaḥ syāditi svargakāmā'dhikāraḥ | svargakāmo yajetetyanenaiva tasyā'
vagateḥ | na ca yāvajjīvamagnihotramityatrāyaṃ parihāraḥ saṃbhavati | agni
hotraṃ juhotītyutpannasyā'gnihotrasya sāyaṃprātaragnihotraṃ juhotīti sāyaṃprātarla
kṣaṇanimittavidhāne sati yāvajjīvamagnihotraṃ juhuyāditi vākyasya vaiyarthya
prasaṅgāditi | hetvantaraṃ samuccinoti—prakaraṇino'pyaṅgārthatvaṃ ca |
saṃsādhyasādhanā khalviyaṃ pradhānabhāvanā svasādhanā'nugrahā'pekṣā yadyadaṅge
saṃnidhyānupatitaṃ tatsvasādhanā'nugrāhakatayā gṛhṇātīti | tatprakaraṇe ca
kālavidhivākyaṃ śrutaṃ svagocarasyāṅgatāmavagamayati na nimittabhāvam,
prakaraṇino'ṅgā'napekṣitatvāt | tasmātkālavidhivākyaṃ prakaraṇasahāyaṃ
kālasyāṅgatāyāṃ pramāṇaṃ na nimittatāyāmiti ramaṇīyam |
[p.253]
kiṃ punaridaṃ yadviruṇaddhi ? kālataḥ śaktyāśrayam | kasya ceti
kuto gamyate nimittanaimittikabhāvaḥ ? kathaṃ cā'sya naimittikasambandhaḥ ?
yadi tāvadyasmin sati | ko virodhaḥ ? na hi kālato'pi śaktyāśraye
tasminnasati tatkriyā satyeva | atha tasmin sati kriyate eva | syād
virodhaḥ | na tvīdṛśo nimittārthaḥ | heturhi nimittam | na cā'vaśyaṃ
hetavaḥ kāryyavantaḥ | kāryyantu nā'sati hetau | tasmānna virodhaḥ |
sahetukaṃ prakṛtamupasaṃharati—tadevamavirodhādanimitte kālataḥ śaktyāśrayo
yadeti, nimittavati tu nimittavirodhāt kālavata evāṅgato yatheti |
sugamam |
evaṃ pūrvārddha kārikāyā vyākhyāya paścārdha vivarītukāmastadavatārayituṃ
nimittamākṣipati—ki punarityādinā | kimākṣepe | tatra kiṃ punaridaṃ
tannimitaṃ yadviruṇaddhi kālataḥ śaktyāśrayamiti sparūpākṣepaḥ, kasya ceti
saṃbandhyantarākṣepaḥ kuto gamyate | nimittanaimittikabhāva iti pramāṇākṣepaḥ
kathamiti | tatra svarūpākṣepaṃ vibhajate—yadi tāvadyasmin sati | ko
virodhaḥ kālataḥ | śaktyāśrayeṇeti śeṣaḥ | avirodhaṃ darśayati—na hi
kālato'pi śaktyāśraye tasminnasati tatkriyā, api tu satyeva sā |
athocyate | tasmin satyeva kriyate nā'satīti nā'vadhāryate, api tu
kriyate eva na na kriyate iti bhavatyakriyāyāṃ nimittavirodha ityāha—atha
tasmin sati kriyataeveti | dūṣayati—syādvirodho na tvīdṛśo nimitta
śabdasyārthaḥ | heturhi nimittam | tataḥ kim ? na cāvaśyaṃ hetavaḥ kārya
vantaḥ, tataśca pūrva eva nimittārthaḥ satyeva nā'satīti | tathā ca na
virodha ityāha—kāryaṃ tu nā'sati hetau, tasmānna virodhaḥ | idamabhi
saṃhitam | lokaprayogā'vadhāraṇādhīno hi śabdārthasaṃbandhaḥ | hetuśabdaścā'
vivādaṃ yasmin satyeva nā'sati kārya tasmin prayujyate | na khalu kusūlasthaṃ
bījamajanayadaṅkuraṃ hetutayā na pratiyanti laukikāḥ | tena yathā vyāpyavyā
pakabhāva ubhayāśrayopi vyāpakasaṃbandhitayā vyāpye bhāva eva'nābhāva iti
vyāptasaṃbandhitayā ca nā'nyatreti nirūpyate tathā kāryakāraṇabhāvo'pi
kāryasya kāraṇe satyeva nā'sati bhāva iti kāraṇasyāpi kāryātprāgbhāvo
nābhāva iti nirūpaṇīyaḥ | tataścāyaṃ nimittaśabdaḥ | tasmātso'tadartha
iti | kasyetyākṣiptaṃ vibhajate—yadi karmaṇo nimittaṃ tataḥ karmapravṛtte
rvidhivaiyarthyam, sakalavacovaiyarthyaṃ ca |
[p.254]
yadi karmaṇo nimittaṃ tata eva karmmapravṛttervidhivaiyarthyam | anirū
pitādapi nimittānnimittavaddarśanāt puruṣakārā'bhāvāt | na tasya nimittā
dutpattiḥ | nityatvāt | tasya tasmin sati na bodhaḥ | vākyādeva
tatsiddheḥ | karmavaiyarthyam | sati bhavatyeveti | satyeva tasmin karmmaṇaḥ
phalamiti cet | virodhābhāvaḥ sarvāṅgopasaṃhāreṇa | athā'dhikārasya |
na, niyogādadhikāraḥ | sa eva niyogasambandhastannimittaḥ | yadi tarhi
nanu kimiti vacovaiyarthyam ? yāvatā vaco nimittamavagamayiṣyati
tato nimittāvagamātpuruṣaḥ karmaṇe ghaṭiṣyate ityāśaṅkyāha—anirūpitādapi
nimitānnimittavaddarśanātpuruṣakārā'bhāvāt | ayamabhiprāyaḥ | tannāma
hetubhūtamapiśabdena jñāyatāṃ yadajñānamanupāttaṃ satkāryāyā'paryāptam | yathā
yathāsvamupādeyāni dravyaguṇakarmāṇi karmaṇaḥ na cedamupādeyaṃ jīvanādi |
tasmātsattāmātreṇaiva karmaṇo heturiti na puruṣakārastannirūpaṇaṃ cā'rthavaditi |
atha vidhernimittamityanuṣajyate | dūṣayati—na tasya nimittādutpattiḥ |
kutaḥ ? nityatvātsvābhāvikatvāt | śreyaḥ sādhanatā hi karmaṇo vidhiḥ |
na cā'sau nimittena jīvanādinā karmaṇyādhīyate, api tu svakāraṇādeva
śreyaḥ sādhanamutpadyate karmaṇaḥ | na ca jīvanādi karmanimittamityuktamitya
bhiprāyaḥ | nanu mā bhūdvidhestadvodhasya bhaviṣyatītyata āha—tasya tasmin
sati na bodhaḥ | kutaḥ ? vākyādeva tatsiddheḥ | atha phalasya nimitta
mityanuṣajyate | dūṣayati—karmavaiyarthyam | yataḥ sati nimitte phalaṃ bhava
tyeveti | punaḥ śaṅkate—satyeva tasminnimitte karmaṇaḥ phalamiti cet |
na karmā'pekṣā nimittabhāvaṃ vihanti, mopaghāni tathā sarvatreti bhāvaḥ |
