satyamevam, abhimataṃ tu nāstītyāha—virodhābhāvaḥ sarvāṅgopasaṃhāreṇa | nigada
vyākhyātam | athā'dhikārasya nimittamiti saṃbandhaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati |
yeyaṃ karmaṇi svāmitā karmaitadasya tadabhimatasya janakamiti puruṣasya pratītirasau
nimittāditi | nirākaroti—nimittātkiṃ tu niyogādadhikāraḥ | niyogamava
gacchat kārye bhāvārthe viṣaye tiraścīnamātmānaṃ niyojyaṃ pratipadyamānaḥ puruṣo
niyogaviṣaye bhāvārthe svāmitāmātmano niyogasāmarthyādeva vijānātīti
kiṃ nimitena ? ityarthaḥ | satyam, niyojyatā bhāvārthaviṣayāṃ svāmitāmākṣi
pati, saiva tu niyojyatā niyogasaṃbandhaḥ puṃso na nimittamantareṇa bhavati,
tatra nimittasya sadbhāvo bhaviṣyatītyāśaṅkate—sa eva niyogasaṃbandhastanni
mittaḥ | dūṣayati—yadi tarhi tannimittako niyogo na niyogānnimittā'va
gatiḥ | ayamabhisaṃdhiḥ | jīvanādi nimittaṃ niyogasaṃbandhaṃ puṃso vadaṃsta
[p.255]
tannimittako niyogo na niyogānnimittā'vagatiḥ | prāksiddhanimittāpekṣaḥ sa
śabdenocyata iti sāpekṣatvam |
atha niyojyaviśeṣaṇasyā'rthānnimittabhāvastasmin satyadhikārāt |
na cātra pratyakṣādi kramate | nāpi śabdaḥ | vidhipradhānatvāt | tasya ca
bhāvaviṣayatvāt | tasya cāṃśatrayā'tirekāt | tatra vidhīyamānasya karmmaṇaḥ
phale vidhānāt tatra vidhau nimitte ubhayavidhau vākyabheda iti |
nnimittaṃ niyogameva brūte | na hi kāryamityevā'vagamyate, api tu mamedaṃ
kāryamiti | evaṃ ca svasambandhikāryapratipattireva niyogapratītiḥ | ato
nimittā'vagatipuraḥsaraṃ niyogasaṃbandhaṃ bruvāṇo niyogameva tatpuraḥsaramāha |
tataśca na niyogānnimitā'vagatiḥ syāditi | mā bhūtko doṣaḥ ? ityata
āha—prāksiddhanimittāpekṣaḥ sa śabdenocyate iti sāpekṣatvam | nimittaṃ
hi niyogasya kārakaṃ vā syād jñāpakaṃ veti tṛtīyasya prakārasya nimittatā
yāmabhāvāt tatkārakatve karmānuṣṭhānavaiyarthyād jñāpakatvamavaśiṣyate | tathā
ca prāksiddhenaiva nimittena niyogasyā'vagamitatvāttadapekṣamabhidhānaṃ śabdasye
tyaprāmāṇyaprasaṅgaḥ |
athocyeta na vayaṃ nimittā'vagamānniyogā'vagatiṃ brūmahe, yadevamupā
labhyemahi | kiṃ tu śabda eva nirapekṣo jīvanavato niyogamavagamayati |
niyogena niyojyaviśeṣaṇasyaiva prathamamapekṣitatvāt jīvanādestadavacchedakatvena
sambandhaḥ | niyojyaviśeṣaṇameva tu jīvanādi nimittamityucyate | tanni
yojyaviśeṣaṇe satyevā''tmasaṃbandhitayā niyogapratīteḥ karmaṇi ca svāmitā'
vagamāt karturadhikārasiddherityāha—atha niyojyaviśeṣaṇasyārthānnimita
bhāva stasmin satyadhikārāt | arthānninittatvaṃ svargakāmanāyā api
saṃbhavati | na ca sarvāṅgopasaṃhāreṇa virodhastathā yāvajjīvamityatrāpi
syādityarthaḥ |
evaṃ kasya ceti vibhajya kuto veti vibhajate—na cā'tra nimittabhāve
jīvanādeḥ pratyakṣādi kramate | nanu mā nāma kramantāṃ pratyakṣādīni, śabdastu
kraṃsyate ityaha āha—nāpi śabdaḥ | kutaḥ ? vidhipradhānatvāt | tathāpi
kuto na nimittaviṣayatvam ? ityata āha—tasya ca vidherbhāvaviṣayatvā
dbhāvaśabdaśca ṇyantādbhavatererajityacivyutpatterbhāvanāmāha | yadyucyeta bhavatu
bhāvanāviṣayo vidhistatsaṃbandhāttu nimittamapi gocarayan tatra śabdaṃ pramāṇa
[p.256]
ucyate | yatra jāte karmmaṇo'vaśyakarttavyatā tannimittam | katham ?
yasmin sati bhavatyeva sa heturmukhyaḥ, itarastu bhaktyā | tadavasthābheda
sambandhāt | gauṇamukhyayośca mukhye saṃpratyaya ityāvaśyakasiddhiḥ | tannimitta-
yiṣyatītyata āha—tasya cāṃśatrayā'tirekāt | bhāvanāsaṃbandhe siddhe
nimittasya codanārthatvam sa eva tu sādhyasādhanetikarttavyatā'tirekādasiddha
ityarthaḥ |
kathaṃ cāsyeti vibhajate—tatra nimitte vidhīyamānasya karmaṇaḥ phale
nityasamīhite vidhānāttatra phale vidhau nimitte vidhānādityanuṣaṅgaḥ | ubhaya
vidhau tūbhayasmin phale nimitte ca karmavidhau vākyabheda iti | itiścā''kṣepa
parisamāptau |
evamākṣipyottaratayottarārdha kārikāyā avatārayati—ucyate | yatra
jāte karmaṇo'vaśyakarttavyatā tannimittam | jāte ityanupādeyatāṃ nimitta
bhāvopayoginīṃ sūcayati | karttavyatāyāṃ nimittamityāha karmaṇāṃ karttavyateti |
yasmin sati bhavatyeva na na bhavati tannimittamityavaśyagrahaṇena darśayati |
evaṃ ca vadatā karttavyatārūpavidhiviśeṣaṇatayā nimittaṃ saṃbadhyata iti vākyārthā
'nvayaprakāro'pi nimittasyārthātsūcita iti | pūrvoktāni prāyeṇa pṛcchati—
katham ? uttaram—yasmin sati bhavatyeva sa heturmukhyaḥ | nanu kāryama
kurvāṇo satyeva nā'satīti vivakṣitvā hetuśabdaṃ laukikāḥ prayuñjate, tatra
kathamasau mukhyo hetuḥ ? ityata āha—itarastu bhaktyā lakṣaṇayā | satyama
kurvatyapi kārya dhūmādilakṣaṇaṃ vahnyādau hetuśabdaḥ prayujyate | na tvetāvatā'sau
mukhyā'rthaḥ | na khalvayaṃ hetuśabdo yadyat kāryaṃ janayati tatra sarvatra viśeṣeṇa
pravarttamāno janayati pravartitumarhati | na jātu daṇḍaviśeṣaṇā'dhīnapravṛtti
daṇḍipadaṃ daṇḍavirahiṇi caitrādau varttate janayatīti sa evocyate yasmin sati
kāryaṃ bhavatyeva na tu yasminsatyeva taddhi karoti na karoti ca | karaṇaṃ
copādhiḥ pravṛttau hetuśabdasya katham ? yat kariṣyatyakārṣīdvā na tatra hetuśabdaḥ
pravarttate, avarttamānasyopādherasattvāt | asataśca pravṛttinimittatve'ti
prasaṅgāt | tasmādyasmin sati bhavatyeveti tatrā'yaṃ nimittaśabdo mukhyo
bhākta itaratreti sāmpratam | lakṣaṇāyāṃ bījamāha—tadavasthābhedasaṃbandhāt |
bījādaya eva hi parasparasamavadhānasamāsāditā'vasthāviśeṣā niyamena kārya
śālinaḥ | yadāhurbāhyā api 
na kiṃcidekamekasmātsāmagryā sarvasambhava
iti | tasmādavasthāviśeṣā vā 'vasthāviśeṣaśālino vā nimittapadā'
[p.257]
sāmarthyādeva | nimittameva hi tanna syād yasmin sati na kriyeta |
avaśyakarttavyatā ca karmmaṇaḥ pratītā nopasaṃhāryyanikhilāṅgasya
kalpate, na kadācidahitopāyasyetyato yathāśaktyaṅgasamavetaṃ sadā samabhilaṣi
topāyaṃ karmmeti gamyate | nimittatā ca karttavyatāyā vidhirūpāyāḥ |
tasmin sati tatkurvato yathākathaṃcitsadā samīhitamenaḥkṣayaṃ sādhayatyeva |
bhidheyāḥ | yatra tu so'vasthāviśeṣo na varttamānastatra so'bhūdbhaviṣyati veti
kathaṃcidavasthābhedasaṃbandhāllākṣaṇika ityarthaḥ | nanu bhavatu bhāktam, tathāpi
sa eva hetuḥ kimiti na ? ityata āha—gauṇamukhyayośca mukhye saṃpratyaya iti |
tasmādāvaśyakasiddhiḥ karttavyatāyāḥ | kutaḥ ? nimittasāmarthyādeva | sāmarthya
meva vyatirekamukhena darśayati—nimittameva hi tanna syād yasmin sati na
kriyeta | tataśca siddhaḥ sarvāṅgopasaṃhāravirodhānnaimittike yathāśaktiprayogaḥ |
sadā samīhitopāyatā cetyāha—avaśyakarttavyatā ca karmaṇaḥ pratītā nopa
saṃhāryā'khilāṅgasya kalpate | na kadācidīhitopāyasyetyarthādyathāśaktyaṅga
samavetaṃ sadā samabhilaṣitopāyaṃ karma naimittikamiti gamyate | kasya nimitta
miti yaduktaṃ tatrottaramāha—nimittatā ca karttavyatāyāḥ phalakarttavyatāyā
vyavacchinatti vidhirūpāyā iti |
nanu tasya nityatvādityuktam, tatrāha—tasminsati kurvato yathā kathaṃ
citsadā samīhitamenaḥkṣayaṃ sādhayatyeva | idamākūtam | satyaṃ kartṛsamīhito
pāyatā karmadharmo vidhiḥ | sa ca svakāraṇādutpatsyamānasya svābhāvikastathāpi
nimitte satyavaśyakarttavyatvādyathā kathaṃcit kriyamāṇaṃ kartuḥ samīhitamupāttadu
ritakṣayalakṣaṇaṃ phalaṃ sādhayatyeva | etaduktaṃ bhavati | nimitte sati sva
kāraṇebhyaḥ karmopajāyamānamavikalāṅmitarathā karttuḥ samīhitopāyatāṃ
karttavyatāmāsādayati | nā'satīti bhavati karttavyatāyāḥ kartṛsamīhitasādhana
tāyā vidhernimitta śravaṇāditi |
yaduktaṃ nimittabhāve pramāṇābhāva iti, tatra śabdavyatiriktānāmabhāvaṃ
mṛṣyāmahe, na tu śabdasya | nanu vidhipradhānaḥ śabdo vidhau tadgocarabhāvanāyāṃ
tadaṅgeṣu vā tatsaṃbandhātpramāṇam | na ca nimittameṣāmanyatamam, kathaṃ tatra
codanātvena śabdaḥ pramāṇam ? ityata āha—bhāvavyatireke'pi ca codanābhūta
śabdaprameyatvaṃ nimittasyeti saṃbandhaḥ | bhāvaśabdenāṃśatrayopetāṃ bhāvanāmāha |
nanu nimittasyā'nupādeyatvādavidheyasya vākyabheda prasaṅgāccā'nu
[p.258]
bhāvavyatireke'pi ca śabdaprameyatvam | avidheyasyāpi vidhivadvidhyanupraveśā 
dvidhiviśeṣaṇatvāt | ata eva cāsya karmmaṇā saha sambandhe vikalpo'navakāśa
iti caturasram |
nanu niyojyaviśeṣaṇaṃ jīvanādi svargādivanmanyante, niyogena
niyojyasya prathamamapekṣitatvāt | na hyanāśritaniyojyaviśeṣo niyogo loke
ddeśyasya kathaṃ codanābhūtaśabdaviṣayatvam ? ityata āha—avidheyasyāpi
vidhivat | ayamabhisaṃdhiḥ | nāstyayaṃ niyamo yadvidheyameva soddeśyaṃ codanārtha
iti | vidheravidheyasya cā'nuddeśyasya cā'codanārthatvaprasaṅgāt, api tu
yaccodanā bodhayati nimittamiti vidhivattadapi tadarthaḥ | nanu vidhi vidheyaṃ
vā bodhayan śabdaścodanātvena pramāṇam, na ca nimittaṃ vidhirvidheyaṃ vā, tatkathaṃ
tatra codanātvena śabdaḥ pramāṇam ? ityata āha—vidhyanupraveśāt | sa
evā'vidheyasya kutaḥ ? ityata āha—vidhiviśeṣaṇatvāt | ayamabhisaṃdhiḥ |
vaiśvānaraṃ dvādaśakapālakarmma, tatkarttavyatā putrajanmani satyasati cā'viśeṣeṇa
prasaktā na cā'vaśyabhāvinī syāt | anena ta putrajanmanā nimittena jāte
eva nā'jāte karttavyataiva nā'karttavyateti vyavasthāpyamānā nimittamavidheya
mapyanuddeśyamapi svayamanuddeśyā vā'vidheyā ca viśeṣaṇatayā gṛhṇātīti
viśeṣaṇā'bhidhāne copapannaṃ śabdasya codanātvamiti |
kathaṃ cā'sya naimittikasaṃbandha iti yaduktaṃ tatrottaramāha—ata eva
cāsya karmaṇā saha saṃbandhe vikalpo'navakāśaḥ | yata evā'vidheyasya
viśeṣaṇatayā vidhyanupraveśitvena śabdaprameyatayā vidhisaṃbandhaḥ ata eva
vidheyena karmaṇā saha nimittasya saṃbandhe yo vikalpaḥ ki nimittoddeśena karma
vidhīyate, āhosvitkarmoddeśena nimittamiti, tatra yo doṣaḥ prasañjitaḥ
sa sarvo'navakāśaḥ | vidheyasambandhanibandhano vikalpaprasaṅgo nā'vidheya
saṃbandhe bhavitumarhatītyartha iti | tasmāccaturasram |
yaduktaṃ prathamata eva jīvanādirnimittatayā saṃbadhyata iti, tatpratyācakṣāṇa
āha—nanu niyojyaviśeṣaṇaṃ jīvanādi svargādivanmanyante | na mananamātrā
dvastusiddhiriti hetumupanyasyati—niyogena niyojyasya prathamamapekṣitatvāt |
ayamarthaḥ | kāryaṃ hi kṛtyadhīnasattvamananuṣṭhīyamānaṃ na tattve paryavasyati,
anuṣṭhānaṃ ca na karttāramantareṇa, karttṛtā ca nādhikāraṃ vinā, adhikāraśca
[p.259]
dṛśyate | tadabhāve pratipattireva na syāt | tadviśeṣaṇameva tu sa tasmin
satyadhikārānnimittatvenā'vatiṣṭhate | svargādi tu sādhyatayā kāmyatvena |
naitatsāram | viśeṣaṇā'nupapatteḥ | na hi yāvajjīvanaṃ tāvatā
viśiṣṭaḥ kaścitkadācidvā yo niyujyate nānāvasantakālaviśiṣṭo vā |
mamedaṃ kāryamiti boddhāraṃ niyojyamapekṣya bhavatīti kāryaboddhā niyojyabhedo
na niyogena kāryeṇā'nanyaprayuktā 'nuṣṭhānenāntaraṅgatayā nimittānniyojya
syaiva prathamapekṣitatvādviśeṣaṇamantareṇa ca niyojyabhedā'siddhirnimittasyā'nu
pādeyatayā tadupapatterniyojyaviśeṣaṇatayaiva tāvatprathamaṃ saṅgatirna tu nimittata
yeti | loke'pi caitat siddhamiti tatpūrvakatvādvede na kathaṃ sā setsyati ?
ityāśayavānāha—na hyānāśritaniyojyaviśeṣo niyogo loke dṛśyate |
nimittamantareṇa punarloke vede ca yathāyathaṃ niyogo dṛśyate evetyarthaḥ | nanu
nā'darśanamātrādvyāvṛttirityata āha—tadabhāve niyojyaviśeṣasya pratipattura
bhāve pratipattireva na syāt | taduktamācāryeṇa na hi devadattaṃ yajñadattaṃ vā'nuddiśya
liṅādayaḥ pravarttantaiti | nanu yadi nimittamapyadhikṛtaviśeṣaṇaṃ tato nimitta
tvenā'saṃbandhānna naimittikatvaṃ karmaṇa iti svasiddhāntavyākopaḥ prāpta ityata
āha—tadviśeṣaṇameva tu sa tasmin satyadhikārānnimittatvena avatiṣṭhate |
jīvanāderadhikṛtaviśeṣaṇasyāpi satastasmin satyadhikārādarthānnimittatvama
stīti tadyogānnaimittikaṃ karmeti kutaḥ siddhāntavyākopa ityarthaḥ | nanvīdṛśaṃ
svargāderapi nimittatvamiti kāmyasyāpi naimittikatvaprasaṅga ityata āha—
svargādi tu sādhyatayā kāmyatvenā'vatiṣṭhata ityanuṣaṅgaḥ | ayamabhisadhiḥ |
satyapyadhikṛtaviśeṣaṇatve svargāderna nimittabhāvaḥ | kamiyogā''pāditena
sādhyābhāvena virodhānnimittatvasya ca siddhatayā sahaikārthasamavāyaniyamā
dvyāpakaviruddhopalabdhyā vinivṛtteḥ | jīvanādestu siddhatve nimittatvama
vihitamiti siddhaḥ kāmyanaimittikaviveka iti |
tadekadeśimataṃ siddhāntī dūṣayati—naitatsāram | kutaḥ ? viśeṣaṇā'
nupapatteḥ | tāmevāha—na hi yāvajjīvanaṃ tāvatā viśiṣṭaḥ kaścitkadā
cidvā yo niyujyate nānāvasantakālaviśiṣṭo vā | ayamarthaḥ | jīvanaṃ
nāma prāṇadhāraṇamucyate | tatpadaṃ ceha yāvacchabdasamabhivyāhṛtaṃ yāvatāṃ vā
prāṇināṃ jīvanāni yāvanti vā yāvatkālaṃ vā puṃsa ekasya jīvanamabhidadhīta |
tatra prathamamadhyamayoḥ kalpayordūṣaṇaṃ—na hi yāvatāṃ puṃsāṃ yāvanti vā jīvanāni
[p.260]
na jīvanamātraṃ vasantamātraṃ vā viśeṣaṇam | avācyatvājjīvanasya | yāvadvīpsa
yorananvayācca | na ca prāptayoranuvādaḥ | jīvato'pi svargakāmasyaivā'dhikāre
yadā śaknuyādityupabandhādvirodhā'bhāvāt | kramaparyantā'pekṣitvānniyaugasya
nā'ṅgahānam | tasmādyāvadvīpsārthā'nanvayādyathā na kartṛviśeṣaṇaṃ jīvanama-
saṃbhavanti, jātyabhiprāyamekavacanam, tāvatā viśiṣṭaḥ kaścidekaḥ puruṣaḥ
saṃbhavati | tṛtīyakalpadūṣaṇām—kadāciditi | na hi kālatrayaviprakīrṇṇa
jīvanavānekadā puruṣaḥ saṃbhavī, saṃbhave vyabhicāre vā'rthavadviśeṣaṇam asaṃbhave
kathaṃ bhavet ? iti nānāvasantakālaviśiṣṭo vā | kadāciditi vākyānta
reṇa hi jyotiṣṭomasya kālasaṃbandhe vihite vasante jyotiṣā yajeteti
nimittārthā śrutirityāśayaḥ | athocyeta na yāvadvīpse adhikṛtaviśeṣaṇe
api tu jīvanavasantau, tayoścā'sti saṃbhava ityata āha—na jīvanamātraṃ vā
vasantamātraṃ vā viśeṣaṇam | tatra jīvanamātrasyā'viśeṣaṇatve hetumāha—
avācyatvājjīvanasya | satyamasti saṃbhavo na tu vyabhicāraḥ | no khalva
jīvato niyojyatā saṃbhavati, yā jīvanena vyavacchidyeta, tataścāsya vyavacche
dyā'bhāvādviśeṣaṇatvenā'vācyatvaṃ jīvanasyetyarthaḥ | jīvanavasantasādhāraṇaṃ ca
hetvantaraṃ samuccinoti—yāvadvīpsayorananvayāccaikapuruṣasaṃbandhitayā ekadā'dha
stādasaṃbhava uktaḥ tato'nanvayādyāvadvīpsayoḥ pramattagītatvaprasaṅgādityarthaḥ |
syādetat | yadyapi śābdamadhikṛtaviśeṣaṇatvaṃ jīvanavasantayoḥ tathāpyartha
nimittatvamapyasti | yadā yadā nimittasadbhāvastadā tadā bhāvyaṃ naimittike
neti prāptāveva yāvadvīpsārthāvanūdyete iti kuto'nanvayaḥ ? ityata āha—na
ca prāptayoranuvādaḥ | kutaḥ ? jīvato'pi svargakāmasyevā'dhikāre yadā
śaknuyādityupabandhāt | nanvārthenāpi nimittatvenā'vaśyakarttavyatāpratīteryadā
śaknuyādityupabandho virodhādanupapanna ityata āha—virodhā'bhāvāt | etaduktaṃ
bhavati | adhikāriviśeṣaṇadaśāyāṃ tāvajjīvanādīnāṃ yadetyupabandhena na
virodho'sti, yadi paramārthena nimittabhāvena vaktavyaḥ | sa ca nityavadāmnā
tanānāṅgopadeśa balalabdhanikhilāṅgopasaṃhārasāmarthya virodhāt ātmānameva
tāvadāsādayituṃ nārhati kimaṅga punarvirodham ? atastadanuguṇatayā vyava
tiṣṭhate | na cā'dhikāravākyavirodhaḥ | na hi tannimittaśrutyanapekṣamavaśya
karttavyatāmavagamayati | na ca tāmantareṇa nopapadyate yenādāvevaṃ kalpayet |
tasmāddhirodhā'bhāvātkramaparyantā'pekṣitvamapratihataṃ niyogasyeti yadā śaknu
[p.261]
vācyatvāt, vasante vasante iti ca na kālārthasaṃyogastathā nā'dhikāraviśeṣaṇe
api | ato nimittameva tannimittam | tathā hi—
tayorananvayaḥ | nimittameva hi tanna syādyadi tasminsati na kriyeta |
nimittavāṃśca niyogo yasya tasyetyadhikāriviśeṣasiddhernā'pratipattiḥ | tena
nimittaṃ sadadhikāriviśeṣaṇaṃ nā'dhikāriviśeṣaṇaṃ sannimittam | śābda
tvācca nimittabhāvasya | liṅgādayo hi dahedbhinne ārabhamāṇa iti
tatra tatra smṛtestadabhidadhati | tatra śabdārtha parityajyā'nyaparaḥ śabdasāmarthyā-
yādityupabandhānnāṅgahānam | tadidamuktam—kramaparyantāpekṣitvānniyogasya
nāṅgahānam |
api ca | tvayā'pyetadavaśyāṅgīkaraṇīyamityupasaṃhāramukhenāha—tasmā
dyāvadvīpsārthā'nanvayādyathā na kartṛviśeṣaṇaṃ jīvanamavācyatvāt vasante vasante
iti ca na kālārthasaṃyogastathā nā'dhikāriviśeṣaṇe api | yathā jīvanaṃ
tavāpi na kartṛviśeṣaṇaṃ yāvacchabdā'nanvayādvyavacchettavyā'bhāvenā'vācya
tvācca tathā vasante vasante iti na kāryaḥ saṃyogaḥ, vīpsāyā ananvayā
dityarthaḥ |
svamatamupasaṃharati—ato nimittameva nimittaṃ nā'dhikāriviśeṣaṇaṃ
prathamamityarthaḥ | kasmāt ? tathā hi | tayoryāvadvīpsayorananvayaḥ | kasmātpu
narnimittatve satyananvayaḥ ? ityata āha—nimittameva hi tanna syādyasminsati
na kriyeteti | uktametatprāk | nanūktaṃ niyogena niyojyasya prathamamapekṣaṇā
diti, tatrāha—nimiktavāṃśca niyogo yasya puṃsastannimittamasti tasya karma
karttavyatetyadhikāriviśeṣaṇasiddhernā'pratipattiḥ | asti tāvanna prathamamityu
pariṣṭādihaiva vakṣyāmaḥ | nimittaśruterevārthādadhikāraviśeṣaṇalābha iti
pratipattipratilambhānnā'pratipattiḥ karttavyatāyā ityarthaḥ | tenaitaduktaṃ bhavatī
tyāha—tena nimittaṃ prathamamavagataṃ sadadhikāriviśeṣaṇaṃ nā'dhikāriviśeṣaṇaṃ
prathamamavagataṃ sadarthānnimittamiti |
nanu yāvadvīpsārthayorananvayājjīvanavasantayorastu nimittabhāvaḥ, na tu
gṛhadāhādipvetadasti pramāṇamityata āha—śābdatvācca nimittabhāvasya |
śrutireva mūlaṃ nimittatvā'vagamyasya, saukaryamātreṇa tu yāvadvīpse apyupanyaste
ityāśayaḥ | śābdatvaṃ darśayati—liṅādayo hi dahedbhinne ārabhamāṇa iti
tatra nimittasmṛtestannimittamabhidadhati | dahediti liṅ, bhinne iti nimitta
[p.262]
ttatpara itaro'rthāditi śabdavṛttyanusāro'nyāyyaḥ | yathā ca nimittaṃ
sadadhikāriviśeṣaṇamevaṃ phalaṃ sadadhikāriviśeṣaṇaṃ nā'dhikāriviśeṣaṇaṃ sat
phalamityuktaṃ bhāvanāviveke |
saptamī | darśapūrṇṇamāsāvārabhamāṇa iti lakṣaṇahetvoḥ kriyāyā iti hetau
lakāraḥ | yāvajjīvamityatrāpi yāvati vindajīvoriti ṇamulpratyayo yadyapi
sākṣānnimittesmṛtastathāpi samānakartṛkadhātvarthāntarasambandhe smaraṇājjīvanasya
homādidhātvarthāntarasambandhamāha | na cāsau nimittanaimittakabhāvādanya iti
ṇamulo nimittabhāvaḥ | tathāpi kimati nā'dhikaraṇaviśeṣaṇaparatā nimitta
śabdasya ? na khalvabhidhīyamānaparataiva sarvatretyata āha—tatra śabdārthanimitta
bhāvaṃ parityajyā'nyaparaśabdārthabhūtā'dhikāriviśeṣaṇaparo nimittaśabdaḥ, sa ca
śabdārthabhūto nimittabhāvo'rthādanenāyuktapakṣasvī kāro darśitaḥ |
yuktaparityāgaṃ darśayati—na punastatparaḥ śabdārthībhūtanimittabhāvaparaḥ |
adhikāriviśeṣaṇaparastvarthāditi | anena prakāreṇa śabdavṛttyanusāro'nyāyya
tayā'narha ityarthaḥ | kvacitpāṭhaḥ—tatra śabdārtha parityajyā'nyaparaḥ śabda
sāmarthyāttatpara itaro'rthāditi | tasyārthaḥ | vimarśa eva vimṛśyate—kiṃ
śabdārthanimittabhāvaṃ parityajyā'nyaparo'dhikāriviśeṣaparaḥ sāmarthyāttu tatparo
nimittapara utā'dhikāraviśeṣo'rthānnimittaparastu śabdaḥ ? iti vimṛśya
nimittamavadhārayati—śabdavṛttyanusāro nyāyyaḥ | nābhidheyaparatve sambhavatya
nyaparatā nyāyyā | abhidheyatā ca nimittasya varṇṇitā | tasmānnimittapara
taiva sādhvītyarthaḥ | nanvevaṃ sati svargakāmapadamadhikāriviśeṣaṇapratipādanaparaṃ
prasajyeta, na hyetadbhāvyatayā svarga bodhayati, api tu puruṣaviśeṣaṇatayetyata
āha—yathā ca nimittaṃ sadadhikāriviśeṣaṇamevaṃ phalaṃ sadadhikāriviśeṣaṇaṃ
nādhikāriviśeṣaṇaṃ sat phalamityuktaṃ bhāvanāviveke | bhāvanā hi kasya
cidutpādanā, tenāsāvapi kiṃcidutpādyaṃ bhāvamantareṇāśakyā nirūpayitum |
na ca samānapadopāttaṃ dhātvartha bhāvyatayā svīkartumarhati | vidhyāpāditatvena
puruṣārthabhāvyāpekṣaṇāt | dhātvarthasya ca duḥkhatayā tadvaiparītyāttataḥ pracyāvitā
prathamataramapekṣitatvādbhāvyasya svargakāmapadasya daṇḍī praiṣānanvāhetivadviśeṣaṇa
pradhānatāmāpādayatītyupapāditaṃ bhāvanāviveke |
[p.263]
yadyapi sa pūrvamapekṣitaḥ, pratipattitvasya paścādeva, anuṣṭhānāpekṣatvāt,
tathā ca kramaparyyantātparā nirūpaṇeti |
nanvevaṃ vidhimati sarvatreṣṭā'nubandhādutpatticodanā'bhāvaḥ | tathā ca
syādetat | yathā prathamamapekṣitatvādbhāvyasya svargakāmapadaṃ tatparamevaṃ
niyogena niyojyasya prathamapekṣitatvānnimittaśrutayo'pi niyojyaparāḥ
prasajyeran, vaktavyo vā viśeṣa ityata āha—yadyapi so'dhikārī niyojyaḥ
pūrvamapekṣitaḥ pratipattistvasyā'dhikāriṇaḥ sāṅgapradhānapratīteḥ paścādeva |
idamatrākūtam | na hi prathamamapekṣita ityeva prathamaṃ pratīyate | api tu
yacchakyapratipatti | śakyapratipatti ca bhāvyaṃ, na ca sā tādṛśā'dhikāri
viśeṣaṇamiti | kutaḥ ? anuṣṭheyajñānā'pekṣitvādadhikāraviśeṣasya | no
khalvarthimātraṃ nimittamātraṃ vā'dhikāriviśeṣaṇatāṃ pratipadyate | mā bhūt
paṅguśūdrādīnāmadhikāraḥ, kiṃ tu yathāviniyogamadhikāravyavasthiternikhilāṅgo
petapradhānopasaṃhārasamartho'dhikāriviśeṣaḥ | tathā ca paṅguśūdrādīnāmana
dhikāraḥ | sāmarthyaṃ ca tadgocarā'dhīnanirūpaṇakramaparyantāṅgagrāmopetaṃ ca
pradhānaṃ tadgocara iti tajjñānamapekṣate | tataśca prathamamapekṣito'pi niyojyo
na drāgityevā'vagantuṃ śakyata iti bhāvanākarttavyatā nimittaphalaṃ karaṇeti
karttavyatāṃ parijñāya paścānnirūpyata iti yuktam | maharṣerapi kramalakṣaṇā'nantara
madhikāralakṣaṇaṃ praṇayata etadeva saṃmatamityāha—tathā cā'syādhikāraviśeṣasya
kramaparyantātpradhānāṅgagrāmagocaravicārātparā vicāraṇāpi | etena jñāpyajñāpa
kabhāvasambandhānnirūpyasyā'vasaralakṣaṇaṃ sambandhaṃ ye nā'dhikāralakṣaṇasyācakṣate
te nirastā veditavyāḥ | pūrveṣāmadhikāragocaravicāradvāreṇa jñāpakatvādetasya
jñāpyatvāt | na caikenaiva lakṣaṇārthena jñāpakena bhavitavyamiti kaścinniyama
heturasti | tadāhuratrabhavanto vārttikakāramiśrāḥ kārye jñāne'dhikāraḥ
syāt | kramaparyantāṅgagrāmasahitaṃ ca pradhānaṃ kāryamuktamiti sarvaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ
sthitaṃ tāvat | samīhitasādhanatāyāmapi vidhau yadā śaknuyādityupabandhaḥ
kāmye yathā śaknuyāditi tu naimittika iti samīhitasādhanataiva tatra
vidhiriti |
tatra codayati—nanvevaṃ vidhimati sarvatreṣṭā'nubandhādutpatticodanā
bhāvaḥ | ayamarthaḥ | samīhitasādhanatā cedvidhiḥ sa ca liṅādyartha iti
yadāgneyo'ṣṭākapāla ityādiṣūtpatticodanāsvapi vidhipratipattestasya ca
samīhitasādhanātmakatvāttata eva phalā'vagaterddarśapūrṇṇamāsābhyāṃ svargakāma
itivadadhikāracodanātvādutpatticodanā'bhāvaprasaṅga iti |
[p.264]
parasparā'napekṣatvātsannidhāvapi na phalāryena punaḥ śrutiriti sarvatra
karmmabhedaḥ syāt | kratvarthaśca na syāta | dadhnā juhotīti ca na guṇaphalasaṃbandhaḥ
syāt | na cā'nyat sukhādiṣṭam, tacca svarga iti svargā'varodhātpaśvādyava
kāśo durlabhaḥ | upādānādvā te'pi syuḥ | nityastu svargaḥ syāt |
bhavatūtpatticodanābhāvaḥ, kimetāvatā ? ityata āha—tathā ca parasparā'
napekṣatvātsannidhāvapi na phalārthena punaḥ śrutiriti sarvatra karmabhedaḥ syāt |
ayamarthaḥ | utpattivākyānāṃ khalu karmasvarūpapratipādanaparāṇāṃ tadrūpasyā
'nyotpāde svābhāvyādutpādyamākāṅkṣitam, karmaphalasambandhapratipādanaparāṇāṃ ca
vākyānāṃ svarūpaprajñāpanamapekṣamāṇānāṃ parasparasambandhasambhavādutpattivākya
vihitakarmasannidhau tasyaiva karmaṇaḥ phalasambandhārthena punaḥ śrutiḥ phalavākya
upapadyata iti na karmāntaraṃ kalpyeta | yadā tu samīhitopāyataiva vidhistadā
tata evotpādyapratilambhānna tasya phalvākyā'pekṣā'sti | phalavākyamapi ca
tadvākyopāttaphalā'varuddhaṃ karmaphalāntarasambandhāyanā'pekṣituṃ kṣamata iti karmāntara
meva phalasambandhāya kalpayatīti sarvatra karmabhedaprasaṅga iti | prasaṅgāntaramāha—
kratvarthaśca na syātprayājādi | na jātu śrutyupāttasamīhitaphalapratilambhe
prakaraṇalabdhakaraṇopakāralakṣaṇaphalasaṃbandhasaṃbhavaḥ | śruteḥ prakaraṇād balīya
stvāditi bhāvaḥ |
prasaṅgāntaraṃ samuccinoti—dadhnā juhotīti ca guṇasya dadhnaḥ samīhi
tena phalena saṃbandhaḥ syāt | iha hi prāptā'prāptavivekena dadhnendriyakāmasye
tyatra naitāvatā homo niṣphalastasyāpi svavākyasamadhigataphalatvena tadvattvāt |
na ca prakṛtātkarmāntaraṃ dadhi phalaviśiṣṭaṃ vidhīyata iti sāmpratam | na khalu
tadānīmeva tasyaiva karmaṇaḥ phaloddeśena vidheyatā dadhividhau coddeśyatā
saṃbhavati | tasmātprakaraṇalabdhahomāśrayasya dadhnaḥ phalasambandhaprasaṅga iti
yuktam | kiṃ ca samīhitasādhanatā cedvidhiḥ samīhitaṃ ca puṃsāṃ sukhameva na
duḥkhaṃ nāpyasukhaṃ dveṣaṇādupekṣaṇācca | taccā'navacchinnaṃ svarga eva | avacchede
laukikaṃ syāt | na ca citrayā yajeta paśukāma ityatra yajeteti śruterevā'va
'cchinnaṃ paśvādibhiḥ samīhitamavagamyate | na ca paśukāmapadasambandhādavaccheda
stasya padāntaraparyālocanayā vākyasamadhigamyatvātsvargasya ca śrutimātrātpratī
terbalīyastvāt | svapadopāttasamīhitarūpabhāvyalābhena ca bhāvanayā'napekṣi
tatvāt | yathāśrutapuruṣaviśeṣaṇatvena vyavasthānaṃ na bhāvyaparatvamiti svargāva
rodhātpaśvādīnāṃ citrādiṣvasaṃbandhaprasaṅga ityāha—na cā'nyatsukhādiṣṭam |
tataḥ kiṃ ? tacca svarga iti svargā'varodhātpaśvādyavakāśa durlabhaḥ | athā'nya
dicchatyanyatkarotīti virodhaḥ | na tāvatpuruṣaviśeṣaṇaparatāmātrapareṇa paśu
[p.265]
tasmātkimityavaśiṣṭaṃ bhāvanāyāḥ ? puruṣārthataiva viśeṣaṇam | tathā'pi
ca nimitte karmmaiva sādhyam | anyathā nimittaphalayorupādeye vākyabhedaḥ |
jīvanena homo homena phalamiti vairūpyāt | dvayoruddeśyayorekavākyā'sa-
kāmapadenāsyā'nvayaḥ | tatra yadi phalatayā nā'nviyāt pramattagītaṃ prasajyeta |
tasmātphalaparametadityāha—upādānādvā te'pi paśvādayaḥ syuḥ | nityastva
vaśyaṃbhāvī tu svargaḥ syāttathāpi samuccayo'niṣṭaste prasajyata iti bhāvaḥ |
svapakṣamupasaṃharati—tasmātkimityaviśiṣṭaṃ bhāvanāyāḥ ? kimiti bhāvanā
bhāvyamupalakṣayati | tadyadā samīhitasādhanatā na vidhirna puruṣārthataiva viśeṣaṇaṃ
na puruṣārthenaiva viśeṣyate api tvanyenā'pītyarthaḥ | kvacitpāṭhaḥ—puruṣārthataiva
viśeṣaṇamiti | tatra kebhyaścitpadebhya uttaram | nanu bhavatpakṣe'pi bhāvanaiva
vidhivadbhāvyamupasthāpayati paśvādyavakāśo durlabha iti padāni tebhya uttaraṃ
puruṣārthenaiva viśeṣaṇam | ayamarthaḥ | asmanmate hi bhāvanāsvābhāvyādbhā
vyamātramevā'vagatamiti tadviśeṣāpekṣāyāmaṇuguṇatayā puruṣārthenaiva paśvādinaiva
kimaṃśo viśeṣyata iti yuktam | tvanmate tu bhāvyaviśeṣaṇā'pekṣaiva nāsti |
svargasyaiva tadviśeṣasya pratītiriti | tathā ca yaduktaṃ nityasamamīhitopāyo
naimittikakarmeti tadapi nirastamityāha—tathāpi ca kimaṃśasyā'viśiṣṭatve
nimitte karmaiva sādhyaṃ na puruṣārthaḥ | naimittikakarmabhāvanā hi bhāvyākāṅkṣā
puruṣārtha ca bhāvyamādātumaśaktā dhātvarthameva svapadopāttamādāya nirvṛṇotī
tyarthaḥ | yadi punaḥ puruṣārtho'syā bhāvyo bhavati tataḥ ko doṣaḥ ? ityata
āha—anyathā nityatvasamīhitasādhanatve nimittaphalayorupādeye ca karmaṇi
vākyabhedau | vacanavyaktibhedāt | tameva darśayati—nimitte sati karmaiva
vidheyaṃ bhāvanābhāvyatayā'vasīyate iti | īdṛśaṃ hi tatra vacanaṃ vyajyate,
jīvatve hi sati nimitte homaḥ karttavya iti | yadā tu phale karma vidhīyate
tadā phalasya nimittavadasiddhatvātsādhyatvena pratīteḥ, tatra karma cetsādhyaṃ tadobha
yorasaṃbandhādekasya tvitarārthatvānneṣṭaṃ paraniṣpattaya iti karmaṇaḥ phalaṃ prati
karaṇatvena saṃbandhādīdṛgvacanaṃ vyajyate homena phalamiti | tadidaṃ vairūpyam |
ato vākyabhedaḥ syāt | etanmā bhūdvairūpyam, yatā phalamuddiśya karma vidhāsyata
ityata āha—dvayoruddeśyayorekavākyāsamavāyāt | lokā'dhīnā'vadhāraṇā hi
vākyagatiḥ | loke ca pratyuddeśyaṃ vidhivākyaparisamāptiḥ | na khalu yenaiva
prayatnenaikamarthaṃ vidhātumekamuddiśāmastenaivā'paramapyuddeṣṭumīśmaha iti svā'nubhava
siddhametat | tasmād dvayoruddeśyayornaikavākya -samavāyasaṃbhava ityarthaḥ |
[p.266]
mavāyāt | na hyekasmādaśubhakṣayo'kriyāyāmapāyaśca sulabhaḥ | evaṃ cā'naṅga
tvameva prajñātatvātkarmmarūpasya, na yathāśaktyupādānamiti |
ucyate—
utpattiviniyogārthaprayogā'dhikriyātmakaḥ |
sarvo vidhistatparatāviśeṣānna vibhajyate ||
ekasyaiva jñāpakatvavirodhāt nimittasya vidhiviśeṣaṇatvamapi na saṃbhavatītya
bhimāna ākṣepturiti mantavyam | na caivaṃ vākyabhedaprasaktiḥ | idaṃ cā'paraṃ
prasajyata ityāha—na hyekasmādaśubhakṣayo'kriyāyāmapāyaśca sulabhaḥ | yadā
nimitte karma vidhīyate na tadā phale vidhātuṃ śakyate | na ca tatsvarūpamātra
vidhānaṃ prekṣāvatpravṛttiheturiti tadakriyāyāmapāyaḥ kalpanīyaḥ, tadbhayātpravṛtti
siddheḥ | yadā tu phale vidhīyate tadā tadeva duritaṃ kṣiṇotīti tadarthamaneka
kalpanāgauravaprasaktiriti nimittena karmaiva bhāvanābhāvyaṃ na samīhitamapi
ityākṣepaḥ | eṣa cākṣepāntaramapi prasūta ityāha—evaṃ cā'naṅgatvameva naimitti
kasya nā'ṅgasambandha ityarthaḥ | kutaḥ ? prajñātatvātkarmasvarūpasya | upakāraka
tayā khalvaṅgatā vyāptā, sā copakāratā svarūpe kārye vā syāt | na
tāvatkārye kriyāyā upakārakatve naimittikasya karmaṇaḥ kāryā'bhāvādityuktam,
pāriśeṣyātkarmasvarūpajanakatvamaṅgatvaṃ vācyam, na ca tatsaṃbhavati, prajñātatvā
tkarmaṇaḥ | upāyato rūpataścopakārakatāvyāptā'ṅgatā tadabhāve na bhaviṣyatī
tyarthaḥ | na kevalamanaṅgatvamidaṃ cā'paramāpatitamityāha—na yathāśaktya
ṅgopādānaṃ naimittikakārye'stīti | pariharati—
utpattiviniyogārthaprayogādhikriyātmakaḥ |
sarvo vidhistatparatāviśeṣānna vibhajyate ||
nāpekṣitopāyatāyāmapi vidhau utpattyādyabhāvaḥ, sahasaṃbhave nā'vi
rodhāt | sarvasyaiva vidherutpattyarthaviniyogārthaprayogādhikriyātmakatvāt |
nanvasminpakṣe prayogātmakatvamanupapannam, karttavyatā'vagamo hyanuṣṭhāpayati,
neṣṭasādhanatāvagamaḥ | na ceṣṭasādhanataiva karttavyatā, kṛtisādhyatā hi
socyate | sādhanatātra siddhatā, na ca svabhāvaviruddhayordahanatuhinayoriva
siddhatāsādhyatayorekatvaṃ śakyasādhanam, trailokyasyaikyaprasaṅgāt | api ca
karttavyatā cedabhimatasādhanatā abhimatamapi karttavyatvamavivādamityabhimata
sādhanaṃ syāt | nidāghasamayasaṃbandhimadhyandinā'rkavikīrṇakharatarakaranikara
[p.267]
neṣṭasādhanatāmātraṃ vidhirapi tu karttuḥ | evaṃkāraṃ ca pravarttanāpratyayaḥ |
nipātā''hitaduḥsahasaṃtāpadūnatanavo hi pihitagaganamaṇḍalāṃ balāhakonnatimā
kasmikīmambhaḥsaṃsāragambhīrāmabhinavatamālamālābhāmatirucirāṃ rocayante, na
ca karttavyatāmasyā manyante | na cā'vagrahaviśoṣitasasya sīmānaḥ kṛṣībalā
yajamānasamāpitāmabhimatavarṣopāyabhūtāmapi kārīrīṃ karttavyatayā'vagacchanti |
tasmādvirodhād vyabhicārācca nā'bhimatopāyatā karttavyatā |
na cetkarttavyatā kathamanuṣṭhāpayati ? iti na prayogātmako vidhirityata
āha—neṣṭasādhanatāmātraṃ vidhiḥ, api tu kartuḥ | asamarthaḥ | na vayamiṣṭa
sādhanatāmātraṃ kāryatāṃ brūmahe, yadevamupālabhyemahi, kiṃ tu karturyadiṣṭasādhanaṃ
yattasya karttavyam, akarttavye karttustattvā'nupapatteḥ | na ca sādhyatāsiddhatayo
rvirodhaḥ, bhinnakālatvāt | na khalu pravṛtteḥ pūrvaṃ karmaṇaḥ kṛtatā kriyamāṇatā
vā, kiṃ tūparitanasya kriyamāṇatā tadanantarasya kṛtatā | śabdaśca traikālyavastu
viṣayatvena śaknoti tasya siddhatāsamayavarttinīṃ sādhanatāṃ tatpūrvakālabhāvinīṃ
ca kṛtivyāpyatāmidānīmasatīmapyabhidhātum | yadeva ca kṛtivyāpyaṃ tadeva ca
kṛtisādhanam, itikarttavyatāsādhanatayoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyamiṣṭasādhanaṃ kartta
vyamiti | tadevaṃ karturiṣṭasādhanatāṃ vidhiṃ vadatāṃ karttavyataikārthasamavāyisamī
hitasādhanatvamuktaṃ bhavati, na karttavyatāmātram, phale'pi karttavyatāyā aviśeṣeṇa
vidhitvaprasaṅgāt | na ca phalakarttavyatā śabdagocaraḥ | svayameva hi jānanti
karttavyaṃ puruṣāḥ phalam, na ca śabdaḥ phale puruṣasyotsāhamutpādayet, na ca
tadīhāgocarastatsādhanaṃ tu syāt, na ca tadbhāvenā'jñānaṃ pravṛttiheturiti
tajjñānameva vidhiḥ |
kiṃ kāraṇaṃ punarevamabhyupeyata ? ityata āha—evaṃkāraṃ pravarttanā
pratyayaḥ | idamākūtam | odanakāmaḥ pacet, na sarpāyā'ṅgaliṃ dadyādityupa
deśaśravaṇasamanantaramupadeṣṭavyasya puṃsaḥ pāke pravṛttimuragāṅgulidānodyatasya
ca tato nivṛttimupalabhya bālā evamavadhārayanti nūnamasya pravṛtihetugocaraḥ
pratyaya upajāta iti | tathā hi | pravṛttiriyamasya puṃsaḥ kāraṇavatī pravṛtti
tvātpravṛttivat | pratyayapūrvikā ceyaṃ svatantrapravṛttitvādasmatpravṛttivat |
karttavyatākarttavyatvaikārthasamavāyihitā'hitasādhanatvagocaraśca vivādādhyāsitaḥ
pratyayaḥ pravṛttihetupratyayatvāt asmatpravṛttihetupratyayavat | tathā hi | stana
pānādau karttavyataikārthasamavāyinīṃ hitasādhanatāmavagamya pravṛttosmi, dahanasaṃ
parkāccā'karttavyataikārthasamavāyinīmahitasādhanatāmavetya nivṛttosmīti
[p.268]
anyathā pravṛttihetutvābhāvāt | svato'nyato vā siddhe ataevā'napekṣitavyāpāre
samīhitasādhane'pyapravṛtteḥ | taduvataṃ 
śāstraphalaṃ prayoktari tallakṣaṇatvā—
diti | prayoktṛphalasādhanatālakṣaṇatvācchāstrasyeti | tataśca vidhiparyyāyā
cchāstrādanuṣṭhānaṃ karmmaṇo'vagamyate | adṛṣṭārthasya ca śāstrā''yattā'nuṣṭhānasya
svarūpaviśeṣo'pi | prāgvidheraprāpteḥ | apekṣite cā'pekṣitasādhanatvādviniyogaḥ |
pravartyaśca kaścidadhikārī svāmī yasya tadiṣṭasādhanam | sarvatrāpi ca pratīya-
karttavyatvaṃ kṛtyarhatvaṃ tadavirodhi cā'karttavyatvam adharmavat | evaṃvidhe eva
ca hitā'hitopāyatve tādarthyātpravarttanānivarttanājñāne gocarayataḥ | te ca
prāgasatī śabdajñānānantaramupajāyamāne tajjñānameva kāraṇatayā svīkurutaḥ |
tasmādevaṃ yataḥ pratyayo bhavati na tvanyathā, pravarttanāpratyayaśca pravṛttibījaṃ tasmā
nneṣṭasādhanatāmātraṃ vidhirapi tu kartturiti siddham |
anyathākāraṃ kasmānna bhavati ? ityāha—anyathā vidheḥ pravṛttihetutvā'
bhāvāt | kutaḥ ? svataḥ puruṣaprayatnamanapekṣya tatkṣaṇasphuṭatkamalakuṅmalaparima
lodgāriṇi nāsā'gramadhirohati mātariśvani anyato vā pratyatnavadvaiṇikavādya
mānavallakīvipañcyamāne pañcamadhvanau narāntaraśravaṇapathavarttini siddhe'ta
evā'napekṣitavyāpāre samīhitasādhane'pyapravṛtteḥ | atra bhavato maharṣerapyetadabhi
matamityāha—taduktaṃ śāstraphalaṃ prayoktari tallakṣaṇatvāditi | kathamidamane
noktam ? ityāha—prayoktṛphalasādhanatālakṣaṇatvācchāstrasya | nanu śāstrasya
tallaṇaṃ na vidherityāśaṅkāmapanayannupasaṃharati—tataśca vidhiparyāyācchāstrāda
nuṣṭhānaṃ karmaṇo'vagamyate, tataśca siddhaḥ prayogavidhirityarthaḥ | astu tarhi
prayogātmakatāmātraṃ kimutpattyarthatayā ? ityāha—adṛṣṭārthasya śāstrā''ya
ttānu'ṣṭhānasya svarūpaviśeṣo'pyavagamyate | kutaḥ ? prāgvidheraprāpteḥ | vini
yogārthatāṃ darśayati—apekṣite cā'pekṣitasādhanatvādviniyogaḥ | liṅādayaḥ
khalu vidhau smaryante, vidhiḥ karturapekṣitopāyatā, tataśca te dhātoḥ pare
śrūyamāṇāḥ svārthasya bhāvanāyāḥ samīhitaṃ bhāvyamupanayanti, dhātvarthasya ca
bhāvanā'nurañjanasya samīhitā'rthapravṛttabhāvanābhāvyasya samīhitāṃśasādhanatvaṃ
samīhitabhāvanāsādhanatvaṃ vā tadrūpasya tasya svato'lābhāt śrutyaivā'bhidadhatīti
viniyogārthatvam | phalatadbhāvanopāyatvapratipādanameva hi tādarthyapratipādana
miti bhāvaḥ | adhikriyātmakatāṃ darśayati—pravartyaśca kaścidadhikārī svāmī
yasya tadiṣṭasādhanamiti | pravarttanātmako hi vidhirvivicyamānaḥ karturabhi
matasādhanātmā sthāpitaḥ | sa ca pravarttanīyaṃ karttāraṃ yāgādyupāyajanitasama
bhilāṣe phalaviśeṣaśālinamadhikāriṇaṃ karmaṇi svāminamadhikurvannadhikāra
ityucyate | anadhikurvataḥ karturityabhāvāt | samīhitasādhanatāmātrasya
[p.269]
māne'smiṃścatuṣṭaye aidamparyya bhidyate | tenotpattyādividhivākyabhedaḥ | taccai
damparyya prāptyupapadaviśeṣasaṃbandhāt | kvaciccatuṣṭayaparatā; yathodbhidā
yajeteti | anyataḥ kasyacidalābhāt | phalapadasaṃbandhācca | kvacittriparatā,
yathā jyotiṣṭomavākye | utpatteranyato lābhāt | kvacid dviparatā, yathā
pravarttanātmakatvā'nupapattervidhitvavyāhṛtiprasaṅgāt | nanu sarvasya vidheścātū
rūpyādutpattyādīnāṃ mā bhūtparasparavirodhaḥ, ekaikasmādvākyātkārtsnyena
cātūrūpyasya vidheranugamānnotpattyādividhivākyabheda ityata āha—sarvatra ca
pratīyamāne'smiṃścatuṣṭaye aidamparya bhidyate tenaivotpattyādividhibhedaḥ | yadyapi
sarvasya vidheretaccatuṣṭayātmakatā tathāpi vākyānāṃ vaicitryātsāmarthyā'bhedena
catustridvyekaparatvādutpattyādividhivākyabheda ityarthaḥ | tadeva vaicitryamaidamparya
nimittamādarśayati—prāptyupapadaviśeṣasaṃbandhādbhidyata ityanuṣaṅgāccaturṇāmanyata
masyā'prāpteḥ svargakāmādyupapadaviśeṣasaṃbandhāccetyarthaḥ | tannimittānaidamparya
bhedānudāharati—kvaciccatuṣṭayaparatā yathodbhidā yajeteti | kutaḥ ?
anyataścaturṇṇāmapi vidhirūpāṇāṃ madhye kasyacidapyekasya lābhāt | nanu
yajeteti karmarūpasya ca samīhitasādhanatārūpavidhyabhidhāyinā ca pratyayena
samīhitaṃ prati viniyogasya cā'vagamādutpattiviniyogaparatvamupapannam,
anupapannaṃ tvadhikāraparatvam, tatsvarūpā'navagamāt | na ca pravarttanātmakena
vidhinā''kṣiptaṃ pravarttyamātramadhikārīti sāmpratam, sāmānyamātrasya tadbhāvā
'bhāvāt | uktaṃ hi devadattaṃ vā'nuddiśya liṅādayaḥ pravarttante iti |
paśukāmapadaṃ tadviśeṣāyā'laṃ bhāvanā''kṣiptābhavyaparatvāt | na caikasyobhaya
paratvā'saṃbhavaḥ | tathā ca tadabhāve na prayogātmakatā vidherityata āha—
phalapadasambandhācca | paśukāma iti phalapadam, tacca vidhyupahitāyā
bhāvanāyā bhāvyasamarpaṇamapyadhikāriviśeṣā'pekṣāyāmarthādadhikāriṇamapyupa
nayati | evaṃ ca karttṛviśeṣasyā'bhimatasādhanatā buddhisiddhā | tadevaṃ karttāramadhi
kāriṇaṃ prayojayanti prayogātmakatāmapi labhante iti siddhaṃ ca catuṣṭayaparatvam |
triparatā yathā jyotiṣṭomavākye | utpatterajātakarmarūpajñānasyā
'nyataḥ somena yajetetyato vākyāt lābhāt | kvacid dviparatā adhikāra
viniyogaparatā, yathā darśapūrṇṇamāsavākye | utpatteryadāgneyo'ṣṭākapāla
ityādibhyaḥ prayogasya ca paurṇṇamāsyāṃ paurṇamāsyā yajetetyevamādibhyo'nyataḥ
siddheḥ | kvacittriparatā kvacid dviparatā veti kvacitpāṭhaḥ | tatra triparateti
matamupanyasya dviparatā veti vāśabdena nirasya dviparatā 'svīkṛtetyarthaḥ | kva
[p.270]
darśapūrṇamāsavākye | utpattiyogayoranyataḥ siddheḥ | kvacidekaparatā, yathā'gni
hotraṃ juhoti paurṇamāsyāṃ yajeteti | na ca phale viniyogaḥ | anirddiṣṭaphala
viśeṣasyā'śakyapratītitvāt | tadanirddeśe'dhikāriviśeṣā'pratyayānnādhikā
ro'pi | ata eva na prayogaḥ | na hyapratiṣṭhiteṣṭasādhanabhāve'nuṣṭhānapratyayaḥ
pratitiṣṭhati nā'nirūpitā'nuṣṭhātṛkaḥ | tasmātphalavākyādeva prayogapratyayaḥ |
tenā'gnihotraṃ juhotīti karmmasvarūpaparatā vidheḥ | itare tvaṃśāḥ pratītā
cidekaparatā yathā'gnihotraṃ juhotītyatra utpattiparasya paurṇamāsyāṃ paurṇa
māsyā yajeteti prayogaparatā | nanu kathamagnihotravākyasyotpattiparatā ?
yāvatā kartṛsamīhitopāyatāṃ vidhimabhidadhatā ākhyātena samīhitaṃ prati
homasya viniyogaḥ kartuścādhikārastannibandhanaśca prayoga ukta evetyata
āha—na phale viniyogaḥ | kutaḥ ? anirddiṣṭaphalaviśeṣasyā'śakyapratītitvāt |
idamatrākūtam | satyaṃ vidhisvābhāvyātsamīhitamātraṃ phalam, natvetāvatā
viniyogo'nuṣṭhānāṅgaṃ seddhumarhati | anenaitaditi hi sa syāt | na cā'sau
samīhitabhedā'bhidhāyipadamantareṇeti | ata eva nā'dhikāraparatā'pītyāha—
tadanirddeśe phalaviśeṣā'nirdeśādadhikāriviśeṣasya tatphalakāmasyā'pratyayāt
nā'dhikāraḥ | prayogaparatāṃ niṣedhati—yato na viniyogo bhūtvā'dhikāraḥ
ataeva na prayogaḥ | kasmāt ? na hyapratiṣṭhiteṣṭasādhanabhāve karmaṇi vini
yogā'bhāve satyanuṣṭhānapratyayaḥ pratitiṣṭhati nā'nirūpitā'nuṣṭhātukaḥ | ani
rūpitā'dhikāriviśeṣo'nuṣṭhānapratyayaḥ pratitiṣṭhatīti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ |
tasmātphalavākyādeva prayogapratyayaḥ | upasaṃharati—tenā'gnihotraṃ juhotīti
karmasvarūpaparatā vidheḥ | nanu ca vidhisvābhāvyāditare'pi sāmānyataḥ pratīya
mānā nā'vivakṣāmarhanti kiṃ tu tatsāmarthyādeva piṇḍapitṛyajñavadviśiṣṭatāṃ
gamayiṣyantītyata āha—tvaṃśāḥ pratītā apyavikṣitāḥ | yuktaṃ piṇḍapitṛyajñe
viśeṣāṇāmanyato'siddhesteṣāmabhāve ca vidhestattvā'nupapatteḥ tadviśeṣā'bhidhā
yiśabdakalpaneti, iha tu sāmāmnātebhya eva vacanāntarebhyo viniyogādhi
kriyāprayogāṇāṃ prāptervidhisvābhāvyātpratītānāmavivakṣaiva yukteti bhāvaḥ |
prayogamātraparatāmudāharati—paurṇṇamāsyāmiti tvananyaparatvātprayogabhedaparatā |
na khalvetat utpattiparam, utpatteryadāgneyo'ṣṭākapāla ityādibhyo'vagateḥ |
nāpi viniyogā'dhikāriparatvam, anayordarśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ svargakāmo yajetetyata
eva siddheḥ | prayogo'pi yadyapi tadvākyādeva sidhyati tathāpi na tadbhedaḥ
seddhumarhati | tathā hi | darśapūrṇṇamāsābhyāmiti vacanaṃ ṣaṇṇāmapi yāgānāme
kaprayogatāṃ gamayet, asmiṃśca paurṇṇamāsyāmiti vacane kālaviśeṣe paurṇamāsyāṃ
[p.271]
apyavivakṣitāḥ | paurṇamāsyāmiti tvananyaparatvātprayogabhedaparatā | saṃmārga
prokṣaṇādau tūtpattiviniyogaparatā | vrīhyādimukhena prakṛtayajikaraṇāṃśaprāptau
paurṇṇamāsīsaṃjñakakarmatrayavidhipare amāvāsyāyāmiti cā'māvāsyākāle'māvā
syā'bhidhānakarmatrayavidhipare kālabhedataḥ prayogabhedapratīteḥ prayogāvacchedaka
tvāt | kālasya prayogabhedaparataivā'nanyalabhyatvāt tadbhedasyotpattivākyāccā
''gneyādisvarūpamātratrayāṇāṃ kālayogopakaraṇībhūtānāṃ karaṇānāṃ tu tadyoge
tadaṅgānāmapi tatsahitānāṃ karaṇatvādvidhiparatāṃ prakārāntareṇāha—saṃmārgapro
kṣaṇādau tu utpattiviniyogaparatā |
ayamabhisaṃdhiḥ | daśāpavitreṇa grahaṃ saṃmārṣṭi vrīhīn prokṣatīti saṃmārgaḥ
prokṣaṇaṃ ca vidhīyate, na ca tatsvarūpamanyataḥ prāptamiti tatprajñāpanādutpatti
paratā vākyasya | na ca prokṣaṇāderapekṣitaṃ pratyanyato vākyādaidamarthyamavagamyate
iti viniyogaparatāpi | na ca sahasaiva vidhidarśanātsvargādi sākṣādapekṣitaṃ 
gamyate | yacchaknuyādityupabandhāddhi yadapekṣitaṃ prati yasya sāmarthyamupalabhyate
tena tatsādhanīyam | iha vrīhīn prokṣatīti vidhyupahitā prokṣaṇabhāvanā
dvitīyāśrutyāpāditaprādhānyān brīhīnāpātataḥ sādhyatayā svīkarttuṃ pravṛttā
satī sākṣādapekṣitatvaṃ teṣāmanāsādayantī paramparayā'pi puruṣārthamarthayate |
tadyadi paramparayā'pi tannāpnoti tato viparivṛttya saktūn juhotītivadvini
yogabhaṅgena prokṣaṇādyeva sākṣādapekṣitaṃ kalpayet | asti tu pāramparyeṇā'pe
kṣitapratilambhaḥ | tathā hi | brīhibhiryajeteti prakṛtayāgā'nuvādena sādhanatayā
brīhayo vidhīyante | na ca sākṣādyāgasādhanatvaṃ teṣāmupapattimat | utpattiśiṣṭa
puroḍāśā'varodhāt | āgneyādīnāṃ ca somena yajetetivad brīhidravyaviśiṣṭa
karmāntaravidhigauravāt | tasmātprakṛtā''gneyādisādhanībhūtapuroḍāśaprakṛtitayā
teṣāṃ vidhānamiti sāmpratam | tataśca phalavadyāgasādhanībhūtapuroḍāśadravya
prakṛtitayā brīhīṇāmapekṣitatvādvidhisāmarthyād vrīhisvarūpā'tilaṅghanena
lakṣaṇayā phalavadyāgasaṃbandhinā rūpeṇa saṃbadhyamānāḥ prokṣaṇādayā saṃskārā
brīhīn prokṣatītyevamādibhireva vākyairapekṣite viniyujyante | evaṃ ca yaveṣvapi
prokṣaṇādaya aupadeśikā eva | phalavadyāgasādhanībhūtapuroḍāśaprakṛtitvasyo
bhayatrāviśeṣāditi |
atha prayogaparatāpi kasmānna bhavati ? ityata āha—brīhyādimukhena
[p.272]
karaṇavidhinaivā'nuṣṭhānasiddheḥ | adhikṛtasya cā'dhikārapratyayāt | samidā
diṣūttpattiparatāmeva kecinmanyante | prakaraṇonnītaśrutinibandhanatvādvini
yogasya | śrutireva tviha viniyojikā prakaraṇonneyā | vidhistvayameva vini-
prakṛtayajirūpakaraṇāṃśaprāptau karaṇavidherevā'nuṣṭhānasiddheḥ | paurṇṇamāsyāṃ
paurṇṇamāsyetyamāvāsyāyāmamāvāsyayā yajetetyanena paurṇṇamāsyeti cā
māvāsyayeti ca karaṇībhūtasya prayogabhedaścodyate | na cā'nitikartavyatākasya
tadbhāva iti karaṇamanuṣṭhāpayatyetikarttavyatākamevā'nuṣṭhāpayati brīhyā dimukhe
neti saṃnipatyopakārakāṇāṃ dvāraviśeṣaṃ kathayati | atha kasmādadhikārapatā na
bhavati ? ityata āha—tadadhikṛtasya karaṇā'dhikṛtasyā'dhikārapratyayāt |
phalavākyaṃ hi phalaṃ sādhane viniyojayatphalārthinaṃ karttāraṃ tatsādhane'dhikaroti |
sādhanā'nupraveśinyaścetikarttavyatā iti phalavidhivākyasāmarthyādeva sādha
nā'dhikṛtasyā'dhikārastaditikarttavyatāsvapi tāsāmabhāve tatsādhanatā'nava
gateriti bhāvaḥ |
atraikadeśimatamupanyasyati—samidādiṣūtpattiparatāmeva kecinmanyante |
kutastarhi teṣāṃ viniyogaḥ ? ityata āha prakaraṇonnītaśrutinibandhanatvādvi
niyogasya | ayamarthaḥ | darśapūrṇṇamāsābhyāṃ svargakāmo yajetetye
tatpradhānabhāvanāyā labdhasamīhitasādhyatatsādhanāyāśca sādhanopakārā
kāṅkṣāyāṃ vacanamupapadyamānaṃ yadeva tatsannidhātumupanipatati samidādi ana
vāptasamāhitabhedaṃ tasyaiva svaviṣayasādhanopakārakatvādaidamarthyāpādakaṃ vacana
mupakalpyaikavākyatāṃ bhajate iti prakaraṇonnītaiva śrutiśca viniyojikā na
punaḥ samidho yajatītyādikā | na hyasyāḥ śruteḥ pradhānaidamarthyavagamyate samidā
dīnām | tasmādagnihotraṃ juhotītivadutpattimātraparatā samidādivākyānā
miti kecinmanyante |
atha prakaraṇameva kasmānna sākṣādviniyojakam ? ityata āha—śrutireva
tviha viniyojikā sākṣānna prakaraṇamityavadhāraṇā | idamatrākūtam |
karttavyasya hītikarttavyatākāṅkṣasya vacanaṃ prakaraṇam, tacca tadaidamarthyāpādanā
tmakam, aidamarthyaṃ ca viniyogaḥ, yattvanupapadyamānamupapādakaṃ kalpayettadekavākyatāṃ
ca gacchettadupapādakamiti lokasiddham | na ca viniyoganirūpaṇārthena
pramāṇāntareṇa vā vacanamanupapannārthamekavākyatāmupaityapi tu vacanāntareṇaiva |
ata eva vacanasya paripūrṇārthaucitye vacanamadhyāharanto dṛśyante śābdā iti |
yadi śrutireva viniyojikā na prakaraṇaṃ kimiti tarhiṃ śruterbhedena prakaraṇākhyaṃ
[p.273]
yīganibandhanaḥ | nanu śrutyādayaśca viniyojakāḥ, kimatra vidhinā ? | naitat |
tādarthyaṃ hi viniyogastacca vidheḥ | śrutyādayastu saṃbandhamātrahetavaḥ | saṃbandha-
pramāṇamupanyastaṃ maharṣiṇā ? ityata āha—prakaraṇonneyāyāṃ | yadyapi śrutitvenā'
bhedaḥ tathāpi pratyakṣaśrutiḥ śrutirityucyate, prakaraṇonneyāyāṃ śrutau tanmūlatvā
tprakaraṇaṃ viniyojakamucyate iti bhedaḥ |
tadidamekadeśimatamupanyasya dūṣayati—vidhistvayameva viniyoga
nibandhanaḥ | tuśabdaḥ pūrvapakṣaṃ vyāvarttayati | na prakaraṇonnītaśrutirviniyojikā
samidādīnām, api tu tadvidhiḥ samīhitaṃ pratyaidamarthyamāpādayati teṣām |
na cā'gnihotraṃ juhotītivatsamīhitabhedā'nupādānāt aviniyojakatvam,
tadanupādāne'pi vidhisvābhāvyādeva samīhitaṃ pratyaidamarthyā'vagamāt tasya ca
prakaraṇena viśeṣaṇāt | asati vidhau samānapadopādānatvena dhātvarthasyaiva
bhāvanābhāvyatvaprasaṅgātsamīhitaṃ pratyaidamarthyā'nupapatteḥ | agnihotrotpatti
vākye tu vidhisvābhāvyātsatyapi samīhitamātralābhe'nyata eva tadviśeṣa
pratīterna viniyogaparatetyuktam | ataḥ samidādivākyānāmutpattiviniyoga
paratvamityarthaḥ | atra codayati—nanu śrutyādaya eva viniyojakāḥ, kimatra
vidhinā ? athātaḥ śeṣalakṣaṇamityupakramya śrutyādīnāmeva tallakṣaṇatvena
nirdeśādityarthaḥ | pariharati—naitat | tādarthya hi viniyogaḥ | tataḥ kiṃ ?
tacca vidheḥ | akiṃcitkarāstarhi śrutyādaya ityāha—śrutyādayastu saṃbandha
mātrahetavaḥ | nanu caidamarthyameva sambandha ityata āha—saṃbandhaścāśrayāśrayi
bhāvādiranekavidhaḥ, śeṣatvalakṣaṇastu vidhigamya evetyāha—pārārthya tu vidheḥ |
yadyevaṃ kathaṃ tarhi śrutyādayo viniyojakā ? na jātu saṃbandhamātrapratipādakatvaṃ 
viniyojakatvam, api tu atyantapārārthya pratipādanamityarthaḥ |
uttaraṃ—vidhinā pratipādyamānapārārthyo'vaghātādiryasya brīhyādeḥ
śrutyādibhiḥ saṃbandhitayā'vagamyate so'vaghātādistadarthovrīhyādyartha iti
pratyayāt | idamatrākūtam | śrutyādayo na pārārthyamavagamayanti | prayājaśeṣeṇa
havīṃṣyabhidhārayatītyatra mā bhūtprayājaśeṣo guṇabhūto mā ca bhūvan pradhānahavīṃṣi,
vibhaktyoḥ karaṇakarmatvā'bhidhāyitvena tadāpādakatvāt | yadā tu vidhya
dhīnaṃ pārārthya tadā'bhighārayatīti vidhānādabhighāraṇasya samīhitaṃ
prati tādarthyapratipādanāt prayājaśeṣasya ca prayājaidamarthyāpannasyā'tra haviṣṣu
śeṣabhāvā'bhāvāt pratipattivirodhāt | svarūpalakṣaṇāyāścā'pradhānībhūta
[p.274]
ścāśrayāśrayibhāvādiranekavidhaḥ | pārārthyaṃ tu vidheḥ | kathaṃ tarhi śrutyādayo
viniyojakāḥ ? vidhinā pratipādyamānapārārthyo yasya śrutyādibhiḥ saṃbandhi
tayā'vagamyate tadartha iti pratyayāt | na cobhayalakṣaṇamapi śeṣalakṣaṇamuktam |
vidhestadviṣayatvāt | yā ca svarūpaparatā sā bhedalakṣaṇe cintitā | tadāya
ttatvādbhedasya | svarūpaparo hi vidhirbhinatti karmma |
vibhaktyarthā'nurodhena pradhānībhūtaprakṛtyarthā'nyathābhāvasyā'nyāyyasya prasakteḥ |
samāpitaprayājasya tu tatropayuktasya taccheṣasya homamātrā'virodhino rocanī
yatvena samīhitatvāddhaviṣāṃ ca vākyāntareṇa snehanārthopastaraṇā'bhighāraṇa
vidhānāt punarabhighāraṇasyā'dṛṣṭārthatvaprasaṅgāt asamīhitatvāt prayājaśeṣaṃ
haviṣṣu kṣārayatīti vacanārthaḥ saṃpadyate | tataśca śrutyādayaḥ svasāmarthyātsaṃbandhaṃ
pratipādayanto vidhipratipāditaṃ pārārthya viśeṣe vyavasthāpayantaḥ pārārthya
prati pramāṇamucyante ityarthaḥ | maharṣiṇāpyathā'taḥ śeṣalakṣaṇamityupakramya
karmāṇyapi jaiminiḥ phalārthatvāditi karmaṇaḥ śeṣabhāvaṃ vidhivibhaktihetukaṃ
varṇayatā śrutyādīni copāriṣṭātpārārthyapratipādakānyupapādayatobhayavidha
lakṣaṇaṃ kevalā dividhibhaktiḥ vidhisahitaśrutyādibhirapi śeṣalakṣaṇamuktam |
kathamavāntarabhede'pi lakṣaṇaikatvam ? ityāha—vidhestadviṣayatvāt | yadāpi
śrutyādigamyaṃ śeṣatvaṃ tatrāpi vihita eva tadityuktamityarthaḥ | na ca svarūpa
paratvādayo'smābhirutprekṣitāḥ, ki tu bhagavato maharṣerapi saṃmatāḥ, tadvicāraṇā
dityāha—yā ca svarūpaparatā sā bhedalakṣaṇe cintiteti | kutaḥ ? tadāya
ttatvāt svarūpaparatvād bhedasya | kutastadāyattatva ?-mityata āha—svarūpaparo
hi vidhirbhinatti karmabhāvanām | ajñātasvarūpajñāpanaṃ hi svarūpaparatvaṃ, na
ca tadanyena jñāpite saṃbhavatīti jñāpyamānaṃ tato bhinnaṃ vyavasthāpyate, anyathā
bhedā'bhāvāditi bhāvaḥ |
samprati svarūpaparatāhetukāṃ bhāvanābhedacintāmādarśayati—tatparatā
cetyādinā, vyāpriyata ityantena | asti jyotiṣṭomena svargakāmo yajeteti |
tatraivamāmnāyate somena yajeta, dakṣiṇāni juhoti, hiraṇyamātreyāya dadātīti |
tatraivaṃ cintyate, kiṃ svargakāmo yajeteti bhāvanāyāmekasyāṃ dānādayo dhātvarthā
vidhīyante uta pratidhātvartha bhāvanābhedaḥ ? iti | kiṃ prāptam ? ākhyāta
vācyatvādbhāvanāyāstasya ca sarvatraikatvādvācakabhedādṛte vācyanānatvā'yogāt
atiprasaṅgāt dhātvarthānāṃ ca nānādhātuvācyānāmapi bhāvanāto'nyatvāt
[p.275]
tatparatā caikaśabdopādāne'pi bhāvanātmani prakṛtipratyayayoḥ paurvāparyya
niyamātprakṛtyarthāpekṣeṇa pratyayenā'rthasyā'bhidhāyāḥ kṛtā'nubandhe pratīyamāne
śabdāntaratvācchuddhe'labhyamāne dhātvarthaviniyogaparatvānupapatterekasyāṃ bhāvanāyāṃ
tadbhede'pi bhāvanābhedā'nupapatteḥ svargakāmo yajeteti bhāvyaviśeṣopādānādya
jiparasyā''khyātasya viśadatarabhāvanā'bhidhāyitvāt tadvihitabhāvanā'nuvādena
dānādayo dhātvarthā viniyujyante | na cotpattiśiṣṭayāgā'varodhāddānānāma
viniyoga iti uktam, dadyāt juhuyādityādiṣu tadākhyātapratyabhijñānairbhāvanā
tadbhāvasyā'śakyā'pahnavatvāt | tathā ca tasyāṃ dānādīnāṃ viniyogā'vagamāt
sakaladhātvarthaviśiṣṭā bhāvanaikaiveti prāptam |
evaṃ prāpte ucyate—ekaśabdopādāne'pi bhāvanātmani prakṛtipratyayayoḥ
paurvāparyaniyamāddhetoḥ prakṛtyarthā'pekṣeṇa pratyayenā'rthasya bhāvanāyā abhidhāyā
yeyamabhidhā tasyāḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati | yadyapyayamākhyātapratyaya eka eva
tathāpi tattaddhātuparaḥ prayujyamānaḥ tattaddhātvarthā'nuraktaṃ svārtha mabhidadhāno
yajeta dadyāt juhuyāditi parasparavyāvṛttāḥ prakṛtyarthabhedabhinnā bhāvanā
darśayati | kathamanyasya dhātvarthasya bhedo bhāvanābhedahetuḥ ? iti cet, na |
tasyāstadviṣayatvena tadadhīnanirūpaṇatvāt | audāsīnyapracyutyupalakṣyamāṇātma
vyāpāro hi bhāvanā āśutara vināśitayā'nyonyaṃ ca tadbhedāttu bhidyamānā
api na svarūpato nirūpyante | api tu tadviṣayadhātvarthopadhānena | na ca
prakṛtyarthanirapekṣaḥ kevalaḥ svā'rthamabhidhatte kevalasyā'sādhutayā prayogā'narha
tvāt | prakṛtiparastu svā'rthamabhidhattadarthoparaktaṃ bhinnamevā'bhidadhātī ti
bhāvanābhedasiddhiḥ | tasmāt kṛtānubandhe kṛtā'vacchede pratyayārthe pratīyamāne
kṛtaśabdāntaratvādanubandhā hi dhātvarthā bhāvanāyāḥ | te bhinnadhātugocara
tvādbhinnāḥ santo bhāvanāmapi bhindantītyarthaḥ |
evaṃ hi bhāvanā pratidhātvarthaṃ na bhidyeta, yadi bhāvanābhidhāyī pratyayaḥ
prakṛtiparastāṃ śuddhāmabhidadhyāt, na caitadastītyāha—śuddhe bhāvanātmanya
labhyamāne sati dhātvarthaviniyogaparatvā'nupapatterhetorekasyāṃ svargabhāvanāyāṃ
nānādhātvarthaviniyogā'nupapatteḥ | na jātu yāgāvacchinno bhāvanābhedo
dānā'vacchinno bhavitumarhati | tataśca yaduktam ekadeśimate'pi pūrvapakṣiṇā
[p.276]
nānādhātvarthaviniyogaparatvānupapattau naikasyāṃ bhāvanāyāṃ nānādhātvarthavini
yojakā'bhāvātkṛtsno veda ekaṃ vākyamekaṃ cā'pūrvamiti | samidādiṣu
tvananyaparatvāt |
tisraṃ āhutīriti tu svarūpaparatve'pi saṃkhyāto bhedaḥ | akāraṇaṃ
tadapyasmadupanyastapūrvapakṣanirākaraṇānnirastaṃ bhavatītyāha—naikasyāṃ bhāvanāyāṃ
nānādhātvarthaviniyogāt | kṛtsnaṃ caikaṃ vākyam evaṃ cā'pūrvamiti karmabhāvanā
bhedā'nuvidhāyitvādapūrvabhedasyeti bhāvaḥ |
evaṃ śabdāntareṇa karmabhedaṃ pratipādyedānīmabhyāsenāha—tatra samidho
yajatītyādiṣu pañcakṛtvo'bhyasto yajatiśabdaḥ, tatra kimekā karmabhāvanā
āhosvit pañcaiveti ? kiṃ prāptam ? dhātvarthā'nubandhabhedena bhāvanā
bhedā'bhidhānāt dhātvarthasya ca dhātubhedaṃ vinā bhedā'nupapatteḥ samidho yajatīti
prathamabhāvanāvacanena vihitā bhāvanā viparivarttamānoparitanairanūdyate | na ca
prayojanā'bhāvādananuvādaḥ | pramāṇasiddhasyā'prayojanatvasyā'paryanuyojyatvāt
anekā'pūrvakalpanāprasaṅgādekā'pūrvā'vāntaravyāpāramekaṃ karmeti |
evaṃ prāpte'bhidhīyate—samidādiṣu tvananyaparatvād bhedaḥ | idamatrā
kūtam | parasparā'napekṣāṇi samidādivākyānīti sarvāṇyeva prāthamyārhāṇyapi
yugapadadhyayanā'nupapatteḥ krameṇā'dhītāni | na tvayameṣāṃ prayojakaḥ kramaḥ |
parasparā'napekṣāṇāmekavākyatve hi sa tathā syāt | atha prāthamyā'bhā
vātprāptamityeva nāsti, kasyā'nuvādaḥ ? kathaṃcitparivṛttimātrasyautsargi
kā'pravṛttapravarttatālakṣaṇavidhitvā'pavādasāmarthyā'bhāvāt | guṇaśravaṇe hi
guṇaviśiṣṭakarmavidhigauravabhayātkarmā'nuvādā'pekṣāyāṃ viparivṛtterupakāraḥ |
yathā dadhnā juhotyagnihotraṃ juhotīti vihitahomā'nuvādena dadhividhipare
vākye | na ca guṇādbhedaḥ | samidādipadānāṃ karmanāmadheyānāṃ guṇavacana
tvā'bhāvāt nāmadheyānāṃ ca yajisāmānādhikaraṇyena tattvāttatpāratantryeṇa
vyavasthānāt neha karmabhedasādhanatvamiti na saṃjñāto vidhibhedaḥ | tasmādanya
paratvābhāvādajñātakarmapratipādanaparatvātkarmāṇyayamabhyāso bhāvanā'nubandha
bhūtāni bhindāno bhāvanāmapi bhinattīti siddham |
samprati saṅkhyāto bhedaḥ pratipādyate | tatra tisra āhutīrju hotītyetadu
dāharaṇam | tatrā'yaṃ vicāra | kimāhutibhāvanekaiva vidhīyate uta tisraḥ ? iti |
[p.277]
tarhi tat | na | tasminnasati saṃkhyāyāmabhyāsenāpi saṃpatteḥ |
ki prāptam ? juhotītyasya vidhāyakatvāttasya caikadhātvarthaviṣayabhāvanā'bhi
dhāyitvādabhyāsasya cā'bhāvattisra iti cā''hutīriti ca bahutvasaṅkhyāyā
juhotītyetadvihitaikahomābhyāsenā'pyupapatteranubandhabhedā'bhāvāt ekaiva bhāvaneti
prāptam |
evaṃ prāpte'bhidhīyate—tisra āhutīriti svarūpaparatve'pi saṅkhyāto
bhedaḥ | etadākūtam | bhavedetadevaṃ yadi juhotītyetāvanmātraṃ vidhāyakaṃ syāt |
api tu vākyārthasya vidhiviṣayatvāt padārthamātreṇa vyavahārāt padārthā'bhi
dhānadvāreṇa vākyameva vidhāyakam | tisra āhutīrjuhotītyetacca bahutvasaṅkhyā
yuktamanyato'pratītaṃ karmasvarūpaṃ vidadhad bahutvasya nānātvaikārthasamavāyaniya
mādekasmiṃśca tadanupapatterabhyāsā''śrayaṇe ca juhotīti homenā'bhyāsalakṣaṇā
patteranubandhabhūtān homān bhindattadgocarāṃ bhāvanāmapi bhinattīti siddhaḥ
saṅkhyāto bhedaḥ |
bhāṣyakāreṇa tu durupapādatayā bhītiṃ manyamānena saptadaśaprājāpatyān
paśūnityudāhṛtamiti tadapyupanyastam | tatredaṃ cintyate | kiṃ prajāpatirdevataiṣā
miti prājāpatyā uta prajāpatirddevatā'syeti ? tatra yadi kṛtaikaśeṣasya taddhi
totpattiryathā pūrvaḥ pakṣastathā sati saptadaśapaśūnāmeko devatāsambandha itye
katvādyāgasyaikaiva bhāvanā | atha kṛte taddhite paścādekaśeṣaḥ, yathā paraḥ pakṣaḥ,
tathā sati prajāpatipaśusambandhānāṃ bahutvāttābanto yāgā iti tāvanta eva
bhāvanābhedā bhavanti | tatra kiṃ prāptam ? kṛtaikaśeṣasya taddhitasambandha iti |
tathā satyekapūrvaṃ kalpyetaikatvāt karmaṇaḥ | karmabhede tu bahūnyapūrvāṇi
kalpyeranniti |
evaṃ prāpte'bhidhīyate—saptadaśeti svarūparatve'pi saṅkhyāto bhedaḥ |
idamatrākūtam | autsargikatvādekatvasaṅkhyāyāḥ prajāpatirddevatā'syetyapi
sphurati, anekā'pūrvakalpanābhayāccaiṣāmityapi | tadasminsaṃśaye prakṛtivadvikṛtiḥ
karttavyetyarthāpattiparikalpitādvākyaśeṣānnirṇaye sati asyetyetadeva yuktaṃ naiṣā
miti | tathā hi | prakṛtāvagnīṣomīye paśāvekādaśā'badānāni vihitāni,
tatra cāvattahṛdayādyavadānadvāreṇa paśoḥ sādhanatvamiti sthitam, tadiha prakṛti
vaditi vacanena prāpayitavyam, tatra yadi saptadaśapaśudravyaka eko yāgaḥ
tadā'yamekādaśako'vadānagaṇa ekasminyadi kriyeta sa evaiko yāgasādhanamiti
ṣoḍaśa paśavo vyarthā bhaveyuḥ | atha vyārājya kriyeta tathāpyekādaśako'vadā
nagaṇa ekādaśapaśūn vyāpnotīti ṣaṭ pare vyarthāḥ syuḥ | na ca pratyekaṃ sarveṣu
[p.278]
guṇo'pi pūrvasaṃyogāt viniyogamātraparatāṃ nivārayaṃstatparatāmevā''
vahati |
paśuṣvavadānagaṇa iti yuktam | yāgasādhanībhūtasyā'vadānavidhānāt | iha ca
saptadaśānāṃ sādhanatvaṃ na pratyekaṃ tadabhāvāt | pratipaśu tu yāgabhede pratyekamava
dānagaṇa upapadyata iti taddhitāntasya paścādekaśeṣa iti saṅkhyāto'nubandhabhedā
dbhāvanābheda iti siddham | nanu yadi svarūpaparatve'pi saṅkhyāta eva bhedaḥ,
akāraṇaṃ tarhi svarūpa paratvam | uttaram—na, tasmin svarūpaparatve asati
saṅkhyāyām abhyāsenāpi sampatteryatparaḥ śabdaḥ sa śabdārtha iti svarūpaparatvaṃ
cenna syāt na svarūpaṃ śabdārtho bhavet | tataścā'bhyāsenāpi saṅkhyā sampadyeta |
homāvacchinnabhāvanāsvarūpapratipādanaparatve tu tisra āhutīrjuhotītyasya
tasyaivā'bhidheyatvāt tatparatvācchabdasya abhyāsasya ca tato'nyatvāt tallakṣa
ṇāyāṃ svarūpaparatvaṃ sati sambhave śabdo na jahyāt | tasmātsvarūpaparatvasyā'
bhyāsalakṣaṇanirākaraṇadvāreṇāsti saṃkhyāhetuke'pi bhede na rūpayoga ityevārthaḥ |
pūrvakarmā'sambhavino guṇādapi bheda ityāha—guṇo'pītyādinā''vaha
tītyantena | idamāmnāyate | tapte payasi dadhyānayati sā vaiśvadevyāmikṣeti |
atra vaiśvadevyāmikṣeti ca dravyadevatāsambandhā'numito yāgo vidhīyate | tada
nantaraṃ cedamāmnāyate vājibhyo vājinamiti | tatra sandihyate | kiṃ pūrvasmi
nkarmaṇi vājinaṃ guṇo viniyujyate, uta karmāntaraṃ dravyadevatāviśiṣṭaṃ vidhī
yate ? kiṃ prāptam ? dravyadevatāntaraviśiṣṭakarmāntaravidhau vidhigauravaprasa
ṅgātkarmāntarā'pūrvakalpanāgauravaprasaṅgācca na karmāntaram, api tu pūrvasmin
karmaṇi vājinadravyaniyoga iti | na cotpattiśiṣṭā''mikṣāguṇā'varodhāttatra
vājinamalabdhā'vakāśaṃ karmāntaraṃ gocarayatīti sāmpratam | ubhayorvākyayoḥ
samasamayaṃ pravṛtteḥ āmikṣāvājinayorutpattau samaṃ śiṣyamāṇatvāt na utpatti
śiṣṭatvamāmikṣāyāḥ sambhavati | tataḥ kathametayā virudhyate vājinam ? na ca
vaiśvadevītyatra śrauta āmikṣāsambandho viśveṣāṃ devānāṃ vājibhyo vājinamiti
vājinasambandhādvākyagamyād balīyāniti vācyam | ubhayorapi padāntara
sāpekṣapratītayorvākyagamyatvasyā'viśeṣāt | na khalu vaiśvadevītyukte vinā''mi
kṣāpadamāmikṣāmadhyavasyanti śrotāraḥ | bhavatu vā śrautatvaṃ tathāpi vājinam
iti padaṃ vājamannamāmikṣā tadeṣāmiti vyupattyā tatsambandhino viśvān
[p.279]
saṃjñā'pi pūrvabuddhivicchedena sahasradakṣiṇatvādiguṇaviniyogamātra
virodhena apūrvabuddhiprasūtā ca tadevānayati |
devānāha | tataścā''mikṣāsambandhopajīvanena viśvebhyo devebhyo vājinaṃ vidhī
yamānaṃ nā''mikṣayā bādhyate api tu tayā saha samuccīyata iti na karmāntaram,
kiṃ tu pūrvasminneva vājinaguṇaviniyoga iti prāptam |
evaṃ prāpte'bhidhīyate—guṇo'pi pūrvasaṃyogāt viniyogamātraparatāṃ
nivārayaṃstatparatāmābahatīti | idamatrākūtam | bhavedetad yadi vaiśvadevoti
taddhitaśrutyā nā''mikṣā'bhidhīyeta, taddhitasya tvasyeti sarvanāmārthe smaraṇātsaṃ
nihitasya ca viśeṣasya sarvanāmārthatvāt tatraiva taddhitasyāpi pravṛttirna viśveṣu
deveṣu na satsambandhe nāpi tatsambandhamātre | nanvevaṃ tarhi kasmādveśvadevīśabda
mātrādevā''mikṣāṃ na pratīmaḥ, kimiti vā''mikṣāpadamapekṣāmahe ? taddhitāntasya
padasyā'bhidhānā'paryavasānānna pratīmaḥ, tatparyavasānāya cāpekṣāmahe | avasitā
bhidhānaṃ hi padaṃ samarthamarthadhiyamādhātuṃ saṃnihitaviśeṣā'bhidhāyi tatsaṃnidhima
pekṣamāṇaṃ tatsaṃnidhāpakamāmikṣāpadamapekṣata iti | kuta āmikṣāpadādeva vaiśvadevī
padādāmikṣāpratyayaprasaṅgaḥ, kuto vā tadanapekṣā ? ataśca satyāmapi padāntarā
'pekṣāyāṃ yatpadameva padāntarā'napekṣamabhidhatte tatpramāṇabhūtaprathamabhāvipadāva'ga
tatvācchrītaṃ balīyaśca | yattu paryavasitā'bhidhānapadā'bhihitapadārthā'vagamanaṃ
ca ramapratītabāvayā'bagamyaṃ durbalaṃ ceti taddhitaśrutyā'vagatāmikṣālakṣaṇaguṇā'va
rodhātpūrvakarmā'saṃyogi bājinaṃ tatra svaviniyogaṃ vinivārayat svagocara
karmāntarasvarūpavidhānaparatāmevāvahati | na ca nityā'vagatanirapekṣasādhana
bhāvā''mikṣā bājinena saha vikalpasamuccayau sahate | na cā'śvatve niruḍha
vṛttirvājiśabdaḥ kathaṃcidyogena sāpekṣavṛttirviśveṣu deveṣu vyākhyātumucitaḥ |
prāmāṇike ca vidhikalpanāgaurave abhyupetavya eva na tvaviṣayatvātpramāṇasyeti
siddho guṇādbhedaḥ |
uddeśakramasyā'vivakṣitvāt tadanurodhega samprati saṃjñābhedamāha—saṃjñā
pītyādinā, nayatītyantena | athaiṣa jyotirathaiṣa viśvajyotiretena sahasra
dakṣiṇena yajeteti | tatra saṃdehaḥ | kiṃ yajeteti saṃnihitajyotiṣṭomā'nuvādo'yaṃ
sahasradakṣiṇalakṣaṇaguṇavidhānāyā'tha vā guṇaviśiṣṭaṃ karmāntaraṃ vidhīyate ?
iti | kiṃ prāptam ? jyotiṣṭomasya prakaraṇād guṇamātravidhau lāghavāt
jyotiḥśabdasya ca vasante vasante jyotiṣeti jyotiṣṭome prayogadarśanāt
nāmaikadeśena ca nāmopalakṣaṇasya loke siddhatvāt bhīmasenopalakṣaṇabhīmaśabda
[p.280]
prakaraṇāntaramapi anyabuddhivicchedāttātparyyāyaiva vyāpriyate |
vat, athaśabdasya cā''nantaryārthatvāt asambandhe ca tadanupapatterdvādaśaśatadakṣi
ṇāyāścotpattyaśiṣṭatvāt tathā ca samaśiṣṭatayā tathā sahā'sya vikalpo
papatteḥ prakṛtasyaiva yāgasya sahasradakṣiṇalakṣaṇaguṇavidhānārthamanuvādo'yaṃ na
karmāntaramiti prāpte ucyate—
saṃjñā'pi pūrvabuddhivicchedena sahasradakṣiṇatvādiguṇaviniyogamātraviro
dhenā'pūrvabuddhiprasūtā ca tadevā''nayati | ayamarthaḥ | bhavetpūrvasmin guṇavini
yogo yadi tadeva prakaraṇaṃ syāt, vicchinnaṃ tu tat | tathā hi | saṃnidhāvapi
pūrvā'sambaddhārtha saṃjñāntaraṃ pratīyamānaṃ pūrvakarmmabuddhiṃ vyavacchinatti, apūrvabuddhiṃ
ca prasūta iti lokasiddham | na jātu devadattāya dehi gām atha devāya svarṇa
miti devaśabdādapi devadattaṃ svarṇabhājamavagacchanti janāḥ | tathā copariṣṭā
dyajeteti śrūyamāṇamapyasambaddhārthaśabdavyavāyāt tadbuddhimanādadhattatra guṇavini
yogamātrāsamarthaṃ karmāntarameva guṇaviśiṣṭaṃ vidhatte | na caikatrā'nupapattyā kathaṃ
cillakṣaṇayā jyotiḥśabdo jyotiṣṭome pravṛtta ityatrāpi sati sambhave tatraiva
pravarttanīyaḥ | na jātu gaṅgāyāṃ ghoṣa ityatra gaṅgāśabdo jaghanyavṛttiriti
gaṅgāyāṃ matsya ityatrāpi tathā | evaṃ cātha'śabdo'dhikārārthaḥ prakaraṇāntara
tāmeva dyotayiṣyatītyeṣaśabdaścā'dhikriyamāṇaparāmarśāyopapadyata iti siddhaḥ
saṃjñāntarādbhedaḥ |
samprati prakaraṇādbhedamāha—prakaraṇāntaramiti | kuṇḍapāyināmayane
māsamagnihotraṃ juhutīti samāmnāyate | tatraivaṃ vicāraṇā, kimayaṃ naiyamika
magnihotramanūdya māsalakṣaṇo guṇo vidhīyate, uta karmāntarameva ? tatrā'gni
hotraśabdasya naiyamikā'gnihotravacanatvātkarmāntare cā'prasiddhestadvācakatve
cānyāyyā'nekārthatvāpatteḥ lakṣaṇāyāṃ ca sati sambhave śrutiparityāgaprasaṅgāt
juhutīti ca homatvasāmānyamātrā'nubādasambhavena viśeṣākāṅkṣāyāmagnihotreṇa
viśeṣaṇe'pi haviṣevārtteravākyabhedāt juhutīti ca kartṛbahutve'pi karmaṇastāda
vasthyena pratyabhijñānā'vināśāttatrāpi ca naiyamike yāvajjīvaśrutivirodhena
māsapadārthe'nvayā'narhatayā sāyaṃprātarnirvarttanīyakāmyāgnihotrā'nubādena
tadabhyāsasya māsavidhi riti prāpte ucyate | prakaraṇāntaramanyabuddhivyavacchedā
ttātparyāyaiva vyāpriyate | idamatrākūtam | syādetadevaṃ yadi karmaṇi kālo
[p.281]
sannidhī tu tadavicchedānna tātparyyamityavicchedaḥ | evaṃ ca karmmaṇo
vidhīyeta | na tvasāvanupādeyatayā vidhānamarhati, kiṃ tu kāle karma vidheyam,
vidheyaṃ cet kathaṃ nā'nyat ? aprāptagocaratvādvidheḥ | sāyaṃprātaragnihotraṃ
juhotītyatrāpyayaṃ prasaṅga iti cet na | tatprakaraṇavarttitayā sphuṭatareṇā'gni
hotrapratyabhijñānenaitsargikakarmasvarūpaparatā'pavādena sāyamādiviniyogamātra
paratvāttasyāḥ | asya tu prakaraṇāntarasamāmnāyena tadbuddhivicchedādapavādā
'bhāvenautsargikā'prāpteḥ karmasvarūpaviṣayatvasyā'panetumaśakyatvāt nāmnaśca
vidhiviṣayā 'vacchedakatayā vidhīyamānabahukartṛkapūrvā'parabhūtā'khyātā'rtha
pratyabhijñāpanā'sāmarthyātadanurodhena vyavasthitermukhyatayā ca paraśabdasya paratra
vṛtteranupapatteranurūpakarmāntarā'pekṣitanaimittikā'gnihotradharmasaṃpattyai jaghanyā'pi
vṛttirupapatsyate | tasmātprakaraṇāntaramapi vidhīyamānāt karmaṇo yadanya
dvihitaṃ tadbuddhivicchedāddhetostātparyāya tadeva karmasvarūpaṃ paraṃ pradhānaṃ yasya
vidhivākyasya tatparaṃ tasya bhāvastattvaṃ tasmai vyāpriyate iti siddhaṃ karmāntara
tvamiti |
yatra tu na prakaraṇāntaratvamevaṃvidhe viṣaye tatra na karmasvarūpe tātparyam,
api tu vidhiyogamātraparataivetyāha—sannidhau tadavicchedāt pratyabhijñānā
'vicchedāt na tātparya na svarūpaparatvamityavicchedo viniyogamātraparatvam |
tadyathā aveṣṭiṃ prakṛtyedamāmnāyate etayā'nnādyakāmaṃ yājayediti | tatra saṃdehaḥ |
kimaveṣṭiḥ karmāntarametatphalaṃ prati vidhīyate uta phale viniyogamātrametat ?
iti | kiṃ prāptam ? phalasyoddeśyatayā tadvidhānā'nupapattestatra karma vidhā
tavyam | dvayoruddeśyayormithaḥ saṃbandhā'bhāvāt | vidhīyate cet karmāntaramiti
prāpte'bhidhīyate | satyamapratyabhijñāyāmevamiha tvaveṣṭisaṃnidhau tatpāṭhāt
sphuṭataraṃ tatpratyabhijñānamapavādakamautsargikakarmasvarūpaparatvaṃ vighaṭayadvi
niyogamātraparatāmāpādayatīti siddhamasya prakaraṇāntarapratyudāharaṇatvamiti
darśitā ṣaṭpramāṇā karmasvarūpaparatāyām |
yadyepā karmasvarūpaparatvapratipādanī kathaṃ tarhi bhedo lakṣaṇārthaḥ ? ityata
āha—evaṃ ca karmaṇo vidheyatvaṃ tadbheda upoddhātaḥ | yadyapi bheda eva
lakṣaṇārthaḥ, tathāpyasyaiva svarūpaparatvapratipādanamantareṇā'śakyā pratipatti
rityuktam | ataḥ prakṛtabhedasiddhyarthamupodghātaḥ ṣaṭprāmāṇyāḥ svarūpaparatvaṃ
bodhayannadhikaraṇasiddhāntanyāyena bhedapratipādyata iti bhedo'vilakṣaṇārthaḥ |
[p.282]
vidheyatvaṃ tadbhedopoddhātaḥ | viniyogaparatā tu tṛtīye vakṣyate | na hi śrutyādi
mātrāttādarthyamityuktam | na cā'nāśritā'nuṣṭhānaṃ tat | anuṣṭhānaṃ ca vidheḥ |
tadapi tarhi vivakṣitam | satyam | prayogavidhestatsiddherviniyogamātraparametat |
evaṃ tāvadutpattyarthatāṃ vidherdvitīyādhyāyagocaraṃ darśayitvā idānīṃ
viniyogārthatāṃ tṛtīyādhyāyaviṣayamādarśayati—viniyogaparatā tu vidhe
stṛtīyalakṣaṇe cintyate | nanu cintyetaivaṃ yadi vidhirviniyojakaḥ syāt | na
tvayaṃ tathā, śrutyādīnāmeva viniyojakatvāt | uktaṃ hi śrutyādīni ca
viniyoge kāraṇānītyata āha—na hi śrutyādimātrāttādarthyamityuktam |
na khalvatra saṃbandhamātraṃ cintyate'pi tu pārārthyam, yathāha athātaḥ śeṣalakṣaṇaṃ,
śeṣaḥ parārthatvāditi | na ca tacchrutyādimātragocaro grāmaṃ gacchan vṛkṣamūlā
nyupasarpati | prayājaśeṣeṇa havīṃṣyabhidhārayatītyādiṣu teṣāṃ dṛṣṭavyabhicāra
tayā parārthatvaṃ prati prāmāṇyā'bhāvāt | tathā ca brīhīnavahantītyādāvapi
bhāvanā svabhāvena bhāvyamapekṣamāṇāpi samānapadopādānā'vaghātatyāgena brīhi
rūpaṃ gṛhṇīyāt | davīyasī tvasyā'pūrvasādhanabhāvāvasthā tapasvinīti kartṛ
samīhitasādhanatātmā vidhiravahantyādau pratīyamānaḥ samīhitaṃ bhāvyamupasthā
payatsaṃnihitamapyasamīhitatvādavaghātaṃ hitvā vrīhīnupasarpayati | tānapi
copasṛtya tadrūpasyāpyasamīhitatvāttanmātre'paryavasyatsamīhitopāyakratusādhanī
bhūtapuroḍāśaprakṛtitvā'vasthāmasyā'vaśyamālambate, tatsādhanatvaṃ cā'vaghā
tasyā'bagamayati | evaṃ ca yaveṣvapyaupadeśika evā'vaghātaḥ siddho bhavati |
sādhanībhūtapuroḍāśaprakṛtitvasyobhayatrā'viśeṣāt | tadvidhau sati tādarthyaṃ na
śrutyādīnāmityuktamadhastāt ityarthaḥ |
nanu mā bhūcchrutyādimātrāttādarthyaṃ pradhānavidhirevatvavaghātādiṣu pratya
bhijñāyamāno viniyogasiddhyanyaprayojanatvā'bhāvātsākṣādvidhyaviṣayānapyapūrva
sādhanībhūtakratvapekṣitānantarādṛśyamānatuṣavimocanalakṣaṇakāryatayā tadviṣayā
'nupraveśino'vaghātādīnaidamarthyaṃ nayan tatsiddhārthatayopādīyamānatayā vidhāsya
tīti kṛtamavaghātādiṣvivāntaravidhineti śaṅkāmapanetumāha—na cā'nāśritā
'nuṣṭhānaṃ tattādarthyam | na khalvavaghātādaya ityeva pradhānārthāḥ kiṃ tu tadarthama
nuṣṭhīyamānāḥ | kasmāt ? tādarthyamanuṣṭhānamātraṃ śritāḥ |
astvevaṃ tathāpi prakṛte kimāyātam ? ityāha—anuṣṭhānaṃ ca vidheḥ |
sākṣādavahantiviṣayā'vāntaravidheḥ | atredamākūtam | apūrvavidhipakṣe hi
sarveṣāṃ prakriyā ghaṭitāpi, na tvapūrvavidhiriti niveditamadhastāt niyoga
[p.283]
yathoktam evaṃ satyaṅgavidhiranuvādaḥ, na cā'vihitamaṅgaṃ bhavatītyaṅgatvāya
vidhātavyamiti |
parīkṣāyām | karturapekṣitopayatayā tu vidhau sarvamidamindrajālapratimam |
na hi pradhānavidhipratyabhijñānamavahantyādiṣu samidādiṣviva samasti | na
khalu ya eva darśapūrṇamāsayoḥ samīhitaṃ phalaṃ prati sādhanatāvidhiḥ sa evā'va
hantyādīnāmapi samīhitaṃ phalavatkaraṇopakāraṃ prati sādhanatā vidhiḥ saṃbhavati |
parasparaparihārasthitilakṣaṇavirodhāt | tadyadi pradhānavidhirabhyupeyetā'va
hantyādiṣu tato'vahantyādīnāmāgneyādīnāmiva pradhānabhāvāpatterna pradhānārthatā
lakṣaṇaṃ śeṣatvaṃ syāditi tatsiddhyarthamavāntaravidhiravaghātādiṣu samidādiṣvi
vā'bhyupagantavyaḥ | evaṃ nā'bahantyādīnāmapi sākṣādvidhigocaratayā vidheyatvaṃ
na punaḥ kathaṃcidvidhisiddhyarthatayopādīyamānatayā kalpitaṃ bhaviṣyati | na ca
yasya siddhi reva prayojanaṃ sa viṣaya ityapi yuktam, asmanmate viṣayaikadeśe'pya
pravṛtteḥ viruddhatayā'sya tallakṣaṇatvā'yogāt | tathā hi | pradhānasyāpi na
vidhisiddhimātramapi prayojanam, apūrvā'vāntaravyāpārasya tasya phalasiddhi
prayojanatvātprāgeva punarevakārārthaḥ | tasmāt vidhiviṣayatayā samidāderava
ghātādīnāmaviśeṣe'pyapūrvāntaravyāpārayogalakṣaṇo viśeṣo'vagantavyaḥ | ida
meva ca viśeṣamāha sma maharṣiḥ tāni dvaidhamiti | tenā'nuṣṭhānā'vagamādvidhi
mantareṇa ca tadanupapatteravaghātādiṣu cā'vāntaravidhisiddhiḥ |
yadyevaṃ tadapyanuṣṭhānamapi tarhi vivakṣitaṃ, tathā ca brīhīnavahantītyādiṣu
vyavasthitādviparītatā vyāhanyeta | uttaram—satyam | prayogavidheḥ paurṇamāsyāṃ
paurṇamāsyāṃ yajetetyādestatsiddheḥ anuṣṭhānasiddheḥ viniyogamātraparametat
brīhīnavahantyādivākyam | mātragrahaṇaṃ ca śaṅkitatvāt prayogaṃ vyavacchinatti, na
punarutpattim | vyavasthā pitatadvidhiparatvavyāhatitādavasthyāt | edaduktaṃ bhavati |
sarvasyaiva vidheścātūrūpyānnotpattiviniyogaparatā'pi tu vidhivākyanāntarīya
katayā prayogaḥ pratīyamāno'nyatastatsiddheravivakṣito'sti | nanu nāsti, asti
cetkathamavāntaraśrutyādīni ca viniyoge kāraṇānītyata āha—na hi śrutyādi
mātrāttādarthyamityavidhau na pramāṇamiti siddhāvavaghātādiṣvavāntaravidhiḥ |
tatrā'vahantyādau satyabāntaravidhāvanyataḥ prayogasiddheḥ tadavivakṣetyatrā''vārya
vacanamupanasyati—yathoktam evaṃ hi satyaṅgavidhiranuvādaḥ prayogasyeti
tasyā'nyataḥ siddheravadhātādyaṅgaviniyogāya tvavāntaravidhirāsthātavya iti |
[p.284]
tatra śrutyādinibandhanatvādviniyogasya pravarttanamātratvādvidherdravyādiṣveva
śeṣatvamiti vidhiraviniyojaka iti pūrvapakṣayitvā pravarttanārūpamapyaviniyo
jakatve durllabhamiti | asādhakaṃ tu tādarthyāditi vakṣyamāṇarītyā viniyoga
śaktiruktā |
ācāryavacanameva paṭhati—na cā'vihitamaṅgaṃ bhavatīti aṅgatvāya
vidhātavyamiti | tatra bhagavatī maharṣerathātaḥ śeṣalakṣaṇamityupakramya prathamameva
bādaryadhikaraṇaṃ praṇayato vidhyadhīnameva pārārthyaṃ lakṣaṇārthaśca saṃmatamityāha—
tatretyādinoktetyantena | atra darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ svargakāmo yajetetyevamādyamudā
haraṇam | iha hi svargastatkā śca yāgaścā'vagamyate | teṣāmanyonyasya śeṣa
śeṣibhāvosti na veti vicāryate | tatra śrutyādinibandhanatvādviniyogasya |
tathā hi brīhīniti dvitīyāśrutirīpsitatamatvaṃ brīhīṇāmabhidadhatī tādarthya
prokṣaṇalakṣaṇasya saṃskārasyā'vagamayati | evamekahāyanyeti cāruṇayeti ca
dravyaguṇayoḥ kriyāṃ prati tādarthyaṃ karaṇavibhaktiriti yujyate dravyaguṇasaṃskārāṇāṃ
śeṣatvaṃ pramāṇasadbhāvāt | yāgasvargatatkāmānāṃ tu śeṣatvena śrutyādīnāmanya
tamatpramāṇamasti |
nanu mā bhūvannanyāni pramāṇaṃ vidhistu bhaviṣyatītyata āha pravarttanā
mātratvādvidheḥ | vidhiśravaṇasamanantaraṃ puruṣapravṛttidarśanāt | pravarttanāmātraṃ
vidhirityasti pramāṇam, na punarayaṃ viniyojaka iti | tasmād dravyādiṣveva
śeṣabhāvo na svargayāgapuruṣeṣviti vidhiraviniyojaka iti pūrvapakṣayitvā
pravarttanārūpamapi aviniyojakatve durlabhamityasādhakaṃ tu tādarthyāditi vakṣya
māṇanītyā viniyogaśaktiruktā vidheḥ | edaduktaṃ bhavati | satyaṃ pravarttanārūpo
vidhiḥ | tadrūpataiva tasya viniyojakatvamantareṇa durlabhā | na khalu kartrapekṣito
pāyatvamantareṇa prayojakatvamiti niveditamadhastāt, vakṣyāmaścātraiva vyāca
kṣāṇāḥ svargakāmādhikaraṇamasādhakaṃ tu tādarthyāditi | evaṃ cetsvargakāmo
yajeteti liṅo vidhyuparaktā bhāvanā gamyamānā taduparāgavaśādeva samānapado
pādānamapi duḥkhākaraṃ karmakartṛsamīhā'nāspadatvādapahāya bhinnapadopādānamapi
puruṣaviśeṣaṇamapi samīhitatatvādbhāvyasvarga gṛhṇīyāt | labdhasamīhitabhāvyā
copāyamapekṣamāṇā vidhivaśādeva samīhitopāyatāyāṃ karma niveśayatīti kamaṇaḥ
phalārthatvaṃ viniyogaṃ ko'nyo vidhervidhātumarhati | tadidamuktaṃ karmāṇyapi
jaiminiḥ phalārthatvāditi | sa eva ca kartṛsamīhitopāyatārūpaḥ phalagocarāṃ
kartṛsamīhāmudvahan phalasya cā'kartṛtāyāṃ samīhā'nupapattestādarthyamavabodhayati |
na jātu kaściccetanaḥ kāmayate svarga ātmānaṃ labhatāmiti, api tu madanu
[p.285]
vidhiścedviniyojako'kāraṇaṃ śrutyādīnītyāśaṅkyottaratra śrutyādi
sāpekṣatvamabhihitam |
caturthe tvanuṣṭhānāṅgatā'ṅgaparimāṇacintā |
katham ? kratuvidhyanuṣṭhānā'dhīnaḥ kratvarthaḥ, puruṣecchānibandhanapravṛttiḥ
kūlo bhavediti | anukūlavedanīyataiva hi kāmyānāṃ kāmyatā, pratikūlasvarūpa
vedanīyayordviṣopekṣā''spadatvāt | ataḥ phalasyāsti puruṣaṃ prati aidamarthyam |
taduktaṃ phalaṃ ca puruṣārthatvāditi | evaṃ sa eva vidhiḥ kartraṃśamudvahan yasyaiva phalaṃ
prati prādhānyam adhikāritā puruṣasya tasyaiva tadupāye karmaṇi kartṛtayā
kārakatayā karma prati pārārthya pratipādayati | tadetaduktaṃ puruṣaśca karmārthatvā
diti | tasmānmaharṣerapi saṃmatametaditi siddham |
nanvevametenaivādhikaraṇenā''rabdhena viniyogasya vicāritatvāt
lakṣaṇārthaparisamāptervyartha uttaraḥ prapañca ityāśaṅkāmapanayati—vidhirityā
dinā'bhihitamityantena | ayamarthaḥ | satyamanena vidhinibandhano viniyogo
vicārito na tvetāvatā lakṣaṇārthaparyavasānam | uparitanasyāśaṅkottaratvāt |
tathā hi | vidhiścet viniyojakaḥ tata eva viniyogasiddheḥ śrutyādīnāṃ ca
sambandhamātrahetutvāttādarthya prati vyabhicārādakāraṇaṃ tānīti śaṅkitam |
tatrottaram | satyaṃ, viniyojako vidhistathāpi nā'sahāyo viniyoktumutsahate |
na jātu viniyuñjāno'pi vidhiḥ prokṣatīti ca brīhīniti siddhā'nuguṇatvaṃ
śrutimantareṇa brīhiṣu prokṣaṇaṃ yajeteti ca paśukāma iti vidhiḥ pūrvasiddhā'nu
gaṇatvamantareṇa paśuphalayāgam abhighārayati ca prayājaśeṣeṇeti viparītamapi
śrutimantareṇa prayājaśeṣe'bhidhāraṇaṃ viniyoktumarhati | tasmādviniyojako'pi
śrutyādīnsahāyatayā'pekṣata iti tadapekṣottareṇa prapañcena pratipāditeti siddha
marthavattvam |
evaṃ tṛtīye śrutyādisahāyaviniyogaṃ vyutpādya tadaṅgabhāve siddhe
caturthe'nuṣṭhānagatāṅgaparimāṇacintā | ayamarthaḥ | pravarttanātmakatvādvidheranuṣṭhā
pakatvaṃ prasiddhaṃ lokavedayoriti tatsvarūpamavicāraṇīyam | ato'nuṣṭhāna
gatā'ṅgaparimāṇacintā'dhikriyate kiyantyaṅgāni vidhinā'nuṣṭhāpyante kiyanti
nimittāntarādityetāvatāpi vidhyadhīnamanuṣṭhānaṃ cintitaṃ bhavatyeva |
nanvathātaḥ kratvarthapuruṣārthayorjijñāseti pratijānānaḥ sūtrakāraḥ kratvartha
puruṣārthacintāmadhyāyārthamāha nā'nuṣṭhānagatāṅgaparimāṇacintāmityāśaya
[p.286]
puruṣārthaḥ | tatra kasya kratuvidhito'nuṣṭhānam, kiyatyanuṣṭhāpanavyāpāraḥ kratuvidheḥ,
kasya vā'napekṣitatadvidhinimittāntarataḥ puruṣasyeti prayojakā'prayojakacintā'pi
kratvanuṣṭhānavidhivyāpāraparimāṇamavadhāryyate |
tathāhi | āmikṣārthatvena cāturmmāsyaprayogavidhirddadhyānayanamanuṣṭhā-
vānākṣipati—katham ? uttaram—kratuvidhyadhīnā'nuṣṭhānaḥ kratvarthaḥ |
puruṣecchānibandhanapravṛttiḥ puruṣārthaḥ | kratunā hi vidhiviṣayeṇa viṣayī
vidhirūpalakṣyate tenārthyamānāni anuṣṭhāpyamānānīti tasyārthaḥ | tato vidhya
dhīno'nuṣṭhāno bhavati kratvarthaḥ | evaṃ puruṣeṇāpi svasaṃbandhe nimitāntaraṃ vā
vidheranyadupalakṣyate tenārthyamānānyanuṣṭhāpyamānānīti tasyārthaḥ | tataḥ
puruṣecchānibandhanapravṛttiḥ puruṣārthaḥ |
sūtrapadārtha vyākhyāya cintāyāstadārūḍhatāmāha—tatra kasya kratu
vidhito'nuṣṭhānam ? | etadeva sphorayati—kiyatyaṅgajāte'nuṣṭhāpanavyāpāraḥ
kratuvidheḥ kasya vā'napekṣitatadvidhinimittāntarataḥ puruṣasyā'napekṣitatā |
tadvidhiviṣayaṃ nimittāntaraṃ puruṣasaṃbandhi tasmāt | ato na pratijñāsūtra
vyādhāta ityarthaḥ | nanu mā bhūtpratijñāsūtravyāghātaḥ, prayojakā'prayojakacintā
tvanuṣṭhānagatāṅgaparimāṇacintayā kathaṃ na viruddhyate ? ityata āha—
prayojakā'prayojakacintayāpi kratvarthā'nuṣṭhānavidhivyāpāraparimāṇamevā'va
dhāyate | kratvanuṣṭhāne yo vidhiḥ tasyeyati vyāpāro neyatīti vyāpāra
parimāṇam, tadevā'vadhāryate ityarthaḥ | tadavadhāraṇameva sopapattikamudāharati—
tathā hīti | cāturmāsyeṣu vaiśvadeve samāmnāyate | tapte payasi datyānayati sā
vaiśvadevyāmikṣā vājibhyo vājinamiti | tatrete dve karmaṇī ekatrā''mikṣā
havirviśvedevā devatā, anyatra tu havirvājinaṃ vājino devatā | iha hi dadhyā
nayanamavagatam, tatra saṃśayaḥ | kimayaṃ cāturmāsyaprayogavidhirubhayārthamapi
dadhyānayanaṃ prayojayati, āho svidāmikṣārthamiti ? tadarthamayaṃ kāraṇasaṃśayaḥ
kimāmikṣā dadhisaṃyuktaṃ paya eva, kiṃ vā'rthāntaraṃ payasaḥ ? iti | kiṃ
prāptam ? rūpasaṃsthānavyapadeśaliṅgabhedātseti ca sarvanāmno'thaiṣa jyoti
ritivadvakṣyamāṇā'pekṣayā'pyupapattervājinabadarthāntaramāmikṣā dadhisaṃyuktā
tpayasaḥ | dadhyānayanātpunaretadubhayamapi vājinamāmikṣā ca niṣpadyate | dvayamapi
ca cāturmāsyaprayogavacanagṛhītakarmadvayasādhanamiti mithunamapi cāturmāsya
vidhiraviśeṣeṇa prayojayatīti prāptam | evaṃ prāpte'bhidhīyate | āmikṣārtha
tvena cāturmāsyaprayogavidhirdadhyānayanamanuṣṭhāpayati, na vājinārthatayā tadarthā
'nuniṣpannasyaiva tadanupraveśitvāt | ayamabhisandhiḥ | syādetadevaṃ yadyāmikṣā
[p.287]
payati na vājinārthatayā, tadarthā'nuniṣpannasyaiva tadanupraveśitvāt |
evaṃ krayārthānayananāntarīyakasya saptamasya padasya | tathā puroḍāśa-
dravyaṃ na kṣīrajātīyakaṃ bhavet, satyapi tu rūpādibhede rasataḥ payastvajāteḥ
pratyabhijñāyamānatvāt | āmikṣāpayorūpasaṃsthānavyaṅgyā hi jātistadbhede
bhidyate | iyaṃ punā rasaviśeṣavyaṅgyeti tadbhedā'bhedānuvidhāyinau bhedābhedau
bhavitumarhataḥ | ata eva satyapi vārisauvīrayo rūpādyabhede rasabhedājjāti
bhedamadhyavasyanti, śrutayośca payasoḥ sāndrasāndratarayorapi rasataḥ pratyabhijñā
nānnābhinnajātīyatāmavadhāratanti laukikāḥ | ekajātīyatve'pi ca liṅgādi
bhedopapattistathā cā''mikṣā paya iti payo vaiśvadevaṃ vaiśvadeve payasi ca
dadhyānayanaṃ viniyuktamityāmikṣāyāṃ viniyuktam, viniyogānusārī ca prayoga
iti cāturmāsyaprayogavidhirāmikṣārthatvena dadhyānayanamanuṣṭhāpayati, na
vājinārthatvena | tasya bhinnarasatayā payaḥ pratyabhijñānā'nāspadatayā jātyanta
rasya dadhyānayanaviniyogagocaratvāt | āmikṣārthadadhyānayanamanuniṣpannasya
tasya tadanupraveśitvāt na pṛthak prayuktiriti cāturmāsyavidhyanuṣṭhāpanavyāpāra
parimāṇameva tena siddhaṃ bhavati |
udāharaṇāntaraṃ samuccinoti—evamiti | jyotiṣṭome śrūyate |
aruṇayaikahāyanyā piṅgākṣyā gavā somaṃ krīṇāti, ṣaṭpadānyanuniṣkrāmati
saptamaṃ padaṃ gṛhṇāti | yadi havirdhāne pravarttayeyuḥ tarhi tenākṣamupāñjyuriti |
tatra sandehaḥ | so'yaṃ jyotiṣṭomaprayogavidhiḥ krayārtha cāñjanasādhanasaptama
padapāṃsugrahaṇārtha ca kimekahāyanyānayanamanuṣṭhāpayati, atha krayārthameveti |
kiṃ prāptam ? ekahāyanyānayanādubhayaniṣpatteḥ ṣaṭpadānyanuniṣkrāmatītyasya
krayavacanaṃ padapāṃsuvacanaṃ cāntarā samāmnānādubhayatra viniyogā'viśeṣā
dubhayārthaḥ prayojaka iti prāpte ucyate | krayārthā''nayananāntarīyakasya
saptamapadasya tadanupraveśitvāditi pūrveṇa pṛthaganuṣṭhānamiti ca pareṇa sambandhaḥ |
etaduktaṃ bhavati | sidhyedayaṃ manoratho yadi pāṃsugrahaṇavākye nirapekṣaikahāyanī
śrūyeta | kiṃ tu ekahāyanyā somaṃ krīṇātītyetadvākyagatāyāstasyāḥ sanni
dhānāt buddhau viparivarttamānāyāḥ pāṃsugrahaṇavākyena sambandha upajīvanīyaḥ |
sannihitā ca somakrayārthamiti tannāntarīyakatayā saptamasya padasya krayārthā
nayanā'nupraveśitvānna pṛthaganuṣṭhānaṃ jyotiṣṭomavidhinā prayujyate iti
vidhyanuṣṭhānaparimāṇasiddhiḥ |
udāharaṇāntaramāha—tatheti | sto darśapūṇṇamāsau, tatredamāmnāyate
kapāle śrapayatīti, puroḍāśakapālena tuṣānupavapatīti | atra bhavati vicāraṇā
[p.288]
śeṣasyaiva kapālasya tuṣopavāpamukhena tatsaṃsparśāt uttarārddhasya caikadeśatvādu
pāttaukadeśyupādeyasyaiva tatsambandhānna pṛthaganuṣṭhānamiti |
kimayaṃ prayogavidhiḥ puroḍāśārthaṃ ca tuṣopavāpārtha cā'viśeṣeṇa kapālasya
prayojakaḥ, uta puroḍāśārthasyaiva ? iti | tatra viniyogā'nusāritvātprayojaka
tvasya viniyogasya ca kapāle śrapayatītyanena puroḍāśe ca puroḍāśakapālena
tuṣānupavapatītyanena tuṣopavāpe cā'viśeṣādubhayasādhāraṇasya caikenāpi
puroḍāśena vyapadeśasambhavādubhayārthamapi kapālaṃ prayujyata iti prāpte'bhi
dhīyate | puroḍāśaśeṣasyaiva kapālasya tuṣopavāpamukhena tatsaṃsparśānnopavāpārtha
pṛthaganuṣṭhānamiti pareṇa sambandhaḥ | idamatrākūtam | upapadyetaitat yadyupavāpa
vākye śrapaṇavākyavat kevalakapālavacanamaśroṣyata, purāḍāśasambandhastu śrūyate
puroḍāśakapāleneti | na cāsya bhaviṣyatā puroḍāśena sambandho'nyastā
darthyāt | evaṃ cet puroḍāśaśeṣasyeva kapālasya tuṣopavāpe viniyogaḥ |
na ca viniyuktaviniyogalakṣaṇaḥ pratipattivirodhaḥ | puroḍaśakapālaśabdena
kapālasvarūpasya lakṣaṇāt | na ca kapālāntare prasaṅgaḥ | tādarthyena svā'dhi
ṣṭhānasyaiva sambandhino lakṣyamāṇatvāt | na khalu gaṅgāyāṃ ghoṣa ityatra
gaṅgāśabdaḥ taṭākatīraṃ lakṣayati | ata evāha kapālasyeti | kapālāntare
mā prasāṅkṣīditi śaṅkayā puroḍāśaśeṣasyaivetyavadhārayati | tathā ca bhaviṣyatṣu
roḍāśārthameva prayogavidhinā kapālasyopadāpitasya tasyaiva tuṣopavāpa
mukhenāpi pradhānasaṃsparśo na punarapuroḍāśārthasyāpi kapālamātrasya | taramānna
pṛthaganuṣṭhānamityatrāpi prayogeyattā siddhā |
udāharaṇāntaramāha—uttarārdhasya ceti | darśapūrṇamāsayoḥ śrūyate
uttarārddhāstviṣṭakṛte samavadyatīti | tatredaṃ cintyate | kiṃ prayogavidhinā
sviṣṭakṛdijyārthamuttarārthamapi prayujyate kiṃ vā''gneyayāgārtha prayuktādeva
puroḍāśādapi sidhyati ? iti | kiṃ prāptam ? āgneyasya puroḍāśasya devatā
ntarasambandhā'nupapatteḥ śiṣṭācāravirodhāt | na khalvanyārthaṃ kalpitamanyasmā
dupakalpayanti śiṣṭāḥ | arthasya cā'rthadvayanāntarīyakatayā tadākṣepasāmarthyāt
sviṣṭakṛdijyārthaṃ dravyāntaraṃ prayojayantīti prāpte'bhidhīyate—uttarārddhasya
caikadeśatvādupāttaikadeśyupādeyasyaiva tatsambandhānna pṛthaganuṣṭhānam | ayamarthaḥ |
uttarārddhāditi śruteravayavabhāginīyamijyā pratīyate | na cā'vayavabhājaḥ
kṛte'vayavinamupādadate laukikāḥ | na khalu modakaśakalādāne preṣito vaṭu
rmodakāya ghaṭate, na cāghaṭamānāyāsmai preṣayitā kupyati | pradhānayāgārthaptupāttasya
hyavadānamātranivarttitapradhānasyotarārdhe'vadānīyatayā'pekṣite sviṣṭakṛdijyā
[p.289]
arthabādatve hi juhvādyaṅgakratuprayogavidhinibandhanānuṣṭhānatvaṃ parṇamayī
tvādīnāṃ phalavidhau tadabhāvaḥ | ato'rthabādaḥ kratvarthaḥ phalavidhiḥ puruṣārthaḥ |
satyapi ca vidhyadhīnā'nuṣṭhānatve parṇamayītvasya kratuprayāgavidhyaviṣaya-
vidhoyamānā tadarthā, na tvijyārthamuttarārdham | ata eva tadāvinījyā
nā''cāravirodhamābahati | satyapi vā tadvirodhe śrutirācārād vatīyasīti
sthitam | tasmātmadhānārthaprayogavidhinopātenaikadeśinā puroḍāśenopādeya
syottarārdhasyejyāsambandhānna pṛthaganuṣṭhānamiti siddhaṃ prayogaparimāṇam | itiḥ
sagāptau |
yadi prayogavidhivyāpāraparimāṇā'vadhāraṇaṃ lakṣaṇārtho hantā'saṅgataṃ
tarhi phalavidhyarthavādanirūpaṇamatretyata āha—arthavādatve hi juhvādyaṅgakratu
prayogavidhinibandhanā'nuṣṭhānatvaṃ parṇamayītvādīnāṃ phalavdidhau tadabhāvaḥ |
yadi punarjjahvādyāśrayaṃ parṇamayītvaṃ phalāya bodyeta tasminsati tadabhāvaḥ
kratuprayogavidhinibandhano'nuṣṭhānatvā'bhāvaḥ | iha khalvaprakaraṇādhīnamasti
vacanaṃ yasya parṇṇamayī juhūrbhavati na sa pākaṃ ślokaṃ śṛṇotīti | tatra saṃdehaḥ |
kiṃ phalavidhiḥ, utā'rthavādaḥ ? iti | yathā yasya jīrṇṇe bhojanaṃ bhavati na
tasya rogā jāyante ityato jīrṇṇe bhojanasya rujā'bhāvavājatyamavagamyate
tathaitasmādapyapāpaślokaśravaṇaṃ prati kāraṇatvamavagamyate parṇṇamayītvasya |
tacca sarve rocayante | tasmāddhinā 'pi karmisaṃbandham apāpaślokaśravaṇasya
phalatvaṃ pratīyate iti phalavidhiriti prāpte 'bhidhīyate | naivaṃkātīyakasya
phalavidhitvam, na hyanvayamātrātkāryakāraṇabhāvā'vagatiḥ, vyabhicārāt | kiṃ
tu yadyasminsati sambhavati satyapi tadanye tadabhāve na bhavati tattasya
kāryamiti prāmāṇikāḥ | jīrṇṇabhājanādivānayaṃ tu pramāṇāntarā'nusārāt
tathā | na ceha tadapyarita, viparītarayāpi saṃbhavāt na cā'vyabhicārikratu
pārārthyāya labdhasamīhitāyā juhvāḥ parṇamayītvasaṃbandhavidhiṃ pratyasti
