saviśeṣarūpā | sāmānyarūpā tu puruṣecchayetyetāvatā puruṣārthaḥ | idamatrārthatattvama
bhisaṃhitamācāryeṇa | ubhayī hi puṃsāṃ kāmanā aśātopāyaviśeṣā tadviparītā
ca | tatrā'jñātopāyaviśeṣā samuddiṣṭakāmyamātrameva prayatnaṃ prasūyate | sa
ca prayatnaḥ phalamātrakāmanāyā niṣphalakarttavyatāmātraṃ tadupāyaviśeṣānava
cchedāt sāmānyarūpā pravṛttirucyate | uddeśyamātraviṣayatvāt | na cāsau
nirviṣayaḥ | etāvatyeva phale pravṛttiḥ | na khalvātmakāyavacanaceṣṭāviśeṣāḥ
phalarūpagavagāhante | yathoktamāpāryeṇa 'phalena sākṣātkaraṇe kartuśceṣṭā
phalārthina, iti | tādṛśaśca phalapramā phalānubandhāt tadupāyamātre pramāṇa
[p.290]
tvātpuruṣārthatvam | tadvyāpāraparimāṇaṃ caivameva cintitam | pradhāne tu
yadyapi vidhitaḥ pravṛttirviśeṣarūpā sāmānyarūpā tu puruṣe setyetāvatā puruṣārthaḥ |
aṅgāpekṣā tu karaṇajñānātparācīnā tajjñānaṃ tadvidhiriti sāmānyatopi
pravṛttestadāyattatvādaṅgaṃ kratvarthaḥ | idaṃ tarhi katham prayojakaḥ kaścitpuruṣārtho-
mādhatte | so'pi cā'nadhigataviśeṣaṃ tadupāyakarttavyatāmātraṃ prayatnasāmānyaṃ
prasūyate | na ca karttavyatāmātraṃ kāgavyatiriktaṃ nāstīti vaktavyam |
prativiṣayaṃ satyapi kāme prekṣāvatā karttavyatā'nabagamāt | na ca mānarāpratyakṣa
vedanīyā kāmanā'stīti śakyā dambharahitairabhidhātum | tadviparītā
tūpāyaviśeṣaviṣayatvāduddiṣṭaphalaviśiṣṭameva prayatnaṃ janayati | tatra
sāmānyarūpā pravṛttirūpā prāgeva vidheriti phalapravṛttiriva na vaidhī, viśeṣarūpā
tu na vidhiṃ vineti vaidhī | syādetat | icchā''pagāsrotasā pratikṣipyamāṇaḥ
phale tadupāyaviśeṣā'parijñānasetunā pratibaddhavṛttirayaṃ cetanastadviśeṣamātra
mavabodhyatām tatastadevecchāsrotaḥ setubhedādupāyaviśeṣe pravṛttiviśeṣaṃ prasoṣyata
iti kṛtaṃ pradhānavidhiviṣayeṇa prayogavidhinā | nanvevaṃ tadeva pradhānaparyanta
māgatamaṅgaviśeṣā'parijñānā'varuddhaṃ tatparijñānamātrādaṅgeṣvapi pravartakamityaṅga
gocaro 'pi na prayogavidhiḥ syāt | atha kāmanayā niḥsāmānyarūpā 'pi
pravṛttiraṅgeṣu na prāksiddhā vidheḥ | tatkimiyamupāye viśeṣarūpā pravṛttiḥ
siddhacarī ? na ca sāmānyasiddhau viśeṣasiddhiranyatvāt | tasmātsatyapi
phalatadupāyatadaṅgeṣvicchāhetusāmānyapravṛttiviṣayatvena tulyatve phale sāmānya
rūpaiva pravṛttirna pravṛttyantaramasti | sā ca vidheḥ pūrvamutpanneti na vaidhī |
tadupāye tu sāmānyarūpecchā hetuḥ | pravṛttirvidheḥ pūrva yadyapi vidhitastu
viśeṣarūpā tadbodhe sati bhavet | tadabhāve tu satyāmapīcchāyāmasambhavāt |
na ca vidhimātramapi pravṛttihetuḥ | anicchato vidhīnāṃ śatenāpyapravṛtteḥ |
tena pūrvotpannecchāsahakārī vidhiḥ pradhāne pravṛttiviśeṣaheturiti pūrvotpanne
cchā'dhīnapravṛttiviśeṣatvaṃ pradhānaṃ puruṣārthaḥ | aṅgeṣu tu yadyapi vidhiricchā
sahakārī pravṛttiviśeṣahetuḥ, asatyāmicchāyāṃ prayatnā'nupapatteḥ | asati ca
tasmin kāyavacanaceṣṭayoranupajananāt | hetvabhāve kāryā'bhāvāt
tathāpyaṅgajijñāsācikīrṣayorupāyavidheḥ pūrvamasambhavāt vidhinibandhanatva
miti taddhetuḥ | sāmānyarūpā pravṛttirapi tannibandhaneti vidhyadhonapravṛtti
tvāt kratvarthamiti | so'yamaṅgānāmupāyādviśeṣaḥ | tadetadāha—aṅgāpekṣā
tu karaṇajñānātparācīnā, tajjñānaṃ ca vidheriti na kevalaṃ viśeṣataḥ sāmānyato
'pi pravṛttestadāyattatvāt vidhyāyattatvādaṅgaṃ kratvartham |
[p.291]
'prayojakaḥ kratvartha iti ? kimatra katham ? prayojyau hyatra kratvarthapuruṣārthā
vuktau | tatraivaṃ vaktavyam, prayojyaḥ kaścitpuruṣārthaḥ kaścit kratvartha iti | naiṣa
doṣaḥ | puruṣārthaḥ kratvartha iti samānādhikaraṇasamāsaḥ | puruṣārtha iti vigrahāt |
etaduktaṃ bhavati | kasyacitkaścitpuruṣaḥ kiṃcitprayojyamityarthaḥ |
kratuvidherapi kiṃcitprayojyaṃ kiṃ cidaprayojyam, yathā''mikṣāvājinadvāreṇa
atra codayati—idaṃ tarhi bhāṣyakāroktaṃ katham ? prayojakaḥ
kaścitpuruṣārtho'prayojakaḥ kratvartha iti | abhiprāyamajānanaḥ pṛcchati—
kimatra katham ? codakaḥ svā'bhiprāyaṃ sphorayati—pradhānavidhitaditara
nimittaprayojyau hyatra kratupuruṣārthāvuktau | tatraivaṃ vaktavyam, prayojyaḥ
kaścitpuruṣārtha ityādi | pariharati—naiṣa doṣaḥ | prayojakaḥ kaścidityatra
bhāṣyagranthe puruṣārthaḥ kratvarthaśceti samānādhikaraṇasamāsaḥ | puruṣārtho
viṣaya āśrayaḥ kāmanimittayoḥ kraturarthaḥ viṣayaḥ kratuvidheriti vigrahāt |
tadeva prakaṭayati—etaduktaṃ bhavati | kasyacitkarmaṇaḥ kāmyasya naimittikasya
vā kaścitpuruṣaḥ kāmī vā nimittavānvā prayojakaḥ | tadeva kriyāsama
bhihāreṇa śiṣyahitāya sphuṭayati—kiṃcitkarma kāmyaṃ vā naimittikaṃ vā
kasyacitkāmino vā nimittavato vā prayojyamityarthaḥ | aprayojakaḥ
kratvartha iti kaścicchabdamantarbhāvya vyācaṣṭe kratuvidherapi kiñcit prayojyaṃ
yathā ''mikṣādvāreṇa dadhyānayanam, kiṃ cidaprayojyaṃ yathā vājinadvāreṇa dadhyā
nayanam | anena bhāṣyakārīyā vacanavyaktirvyākhyātā | sa tu puruṣecchā'
dhīnapravṛttiḥ puruṣārthaḥ, kratuvidhyadhīnapravṛttiśca kratvarthaḥ, ityetadeva sthitam |
kecidāhuḥ yasyaupādānikameva śeṣatvaṃ puruṣārthaḥ | kratuvidhyadhīnapravṛttiśca
kratvarthaḥ ityetadeva sthitam |
kecidāhuḥ, yasyaupādānikameva śeṣatvaṃ puruṣārthaḥ yathā yāgasya phalaṃ
prati phalasya vā'dhikāriṇamprati | na hyatra śrutyādīnāmanyatamamasti
pramāṇam | sādhyasvargaviśiṣṭasya tu yāgaviṣayo niyogaḥ pratīyamāno
'nyathānupapatteryāgasya svargasādhanatātmikāṃ svargasya cā'dhikāribhogyatātmikāṃ
śeṣatāmākṣipati | tādṛśaśeṣatā puruṣārthatā sati tu śrutyādiviniyoge
yasyaupādānikaṃ śeṣatvaṃ sa kratvarthaḥ, yathaikatvādiḥ | saṃkhyā hi saṃkhyeyamava
cchinatti svabhāvata iti vastusāmarthyalakṣaṇena liṅgena paśusvarūpasambaddha
mekatvamadhikārasannidhisamāmnānamātrāccādhikāraidamarthyamāpannamadhikārata eva |
tata ātmaidamarthyanirvāhāyā'dhikāra evākṣipati karaṇībhūtapaśusaṃbandhamasye
tyaupādānikaḥ śeṣabhāvaḥ | sati ca viniyoga iti kratvarthatāmevā''vahati
[p.292]
dadhyānayanam | tatra yasminprītiriti phalāṃśe'nuṣṭhānaṃ na vidhita iti
darśitam | prayojanaśyenādiphalasyā''narthatvam | punaḥ kratvaṅgatvena tadaṅga-
saṃkhyāyām | tadevaṃ yasyaupādānikamevaidamarthya sa puruṣārthaḥ, sati tu viniyoge
kratvartha iti | tayośca śrutyarthābhyāṃ lakṣaṇā'bhidhitrāyedaṃ sūtraṃ pravataṃ yasgi
nprītiriti | tān pratyāha—yasmin prītirati phalāṃśe 'nuṣṭhānaṃ na vidhita
iti darśitam | na khalu nyāyalakṣaṇapare śāstre sati sambhave padārthamātra
lakṣaṇamucitam | na copapadyate | na ca svargayāgayoradhikārisvargayośca
 śeṣaśeṣatvamaupādānika sambhavati | niyogākṣepaḥ kilopādānamucyate, anupa
padyamānaścākṣipati yathā svargayāgādhikāriṇāṃ saṅgatigantareṇāpi niyogasya
 nā'nupapattistathopapāditaṃ niyogaparīkṣāyām | vidhiśrutireva tu svargakā
mādiśrutisahitā svargayāgapuruṣāṇāṃ yathāyathaṃ viniyogiketi viniyoga
vyāpāraṃ vidhervyutpādayatā darśitam | tasmādyathoktasmābhiḥ kratugurupārthatvaṃ
tadeva jyāyaḥ |
sūtrārtha ucyate | jyotiṣṭomena svargakāmo yajetetyatra bhāvyaḥ
svarga upanītaḥ, tatra saṃdehaḥ kiṃ phalāṃśe'pi bhāvanāyā vidhiranuṣṭhāpayati
puruṣam, kiṃ vā na ? iti | tatra bhāvanāviṣayatvādvidherbhāvanāyāścāṃśatraya
vihīnāyā ananuṣṭheyatvādvidhātumaśakteraṃśatrayasahitāyāṃ ca bhāvanāyāṃ sādha
netikarttavyatāṃśavatsādhyāśo 'pi vidhīyata iti sādhanatadaṅgavat phale'pyanuṣṭhāpa
yati vidhiḥ puruṣamiti vidhyadhīnā'nuṣṭhānatvāt phalasya na puruṣārthatvamiti
prāpte'bhidhīyate | satyamaṃśatrayopetā bhāvanā vidhīyate | tathāpyapravṛtta
pravarttanātmako vidhiḥ pravṛttamupanītamapi parityakṣyati | uktaṃ hi tadgaṇāstu
vidhīyeranna cedanyena śiṣṭā iti | pravṛttaśca puruṣaḥ phale | na khalvicchā
hetoḥ sāmānyapravṛtteranyā phale pravṛttirityuktamadhastāt | tadupāye tu yadya
pīcchā hetuḥ sāmānyapravṛttiḥ prāgvidherasti tathāpi viśeṣarūpātmikā kāya
vacanaceṣṭāviśeṣavidheḥ prāgaprāpteti vidhīyate | phale tu pravṛttatvānna pravartyate
vidhineti siddhamasya puruṣārthatvam |
vicāraprayojanamāha prayojanaśyenādiphalasyā'narthatvam | yadi hi
phalāṃśasyā'nuṣṭhānaṃ codyeta yathā pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ tataḥ karttavyatayā karturapekṣito
pāyatayā ca gamyamāno'bhicāro nānā'rthaḥ | atha punaḥ phalasya na vidhīya
mānatvaṃ yathottaraḥ pakṣaḥ tathā sati na karttavyatā'vagama iti na hisyāditi
akarttavyatā nirapabādhā pravarttate | akarttavyaṃ ca duḥkhaphalamityanarthatvaṃ
śyenādiphalasya siddhaṃ bhavati |
[p.293]
tvena ca kratuprayogavidhiviṣayatvā'viṣayatve śeṣalakṣaṇena | kratvarthapuruṣārthau
vigaktau kratuśeṣatvā'śeṣatvavicāreṇa | parastu siddhaekaśeṣatve kratuvidhi
prayogavidhivyāpāraparimāṇamātravicārāt | ata evoktamatikrāntastṛtīya
viṣaya iti |
pañcame tu kiṃ cintyate ? nanu kramaḥ kena pramāṇena ? śrutyādibhiḥ |
nanu yadi kratupuruṣārthatvamukhena vidhivyāpāraparimāṇamiha cintyate
asaṅgatā tarhi godohanādiṣu viniyogapradhānavicāraṇetyata āha—punaḥ
kratvagaṅgatvena godohanadravyārjanādestadaṅgatvena va saṃkhyādeḥ kratuprayogavidhi
viṣayatvāviṣayatve darśite bhavataḥ | yathāsaṃkhyamatra neṣyate |
kecidāhuḥ, śrutyādipramāṇā'tiriktamupādānamevaikatvādīnāṃ vini
yojakamiti, tānpratyāha—tadapi śeṣalakṣaṇena śrutyādinetyarthaḥ | tathā hi |
godohanadravyārjanādau vidhivibhaktireva puruṣārthatvaśrutiḥ, ekatvādīnāṃ ca
karaṇābhūtapaśubandhatṛtīyāśrutiḥ | nanviyaṃ prātipadikāduccarantī tadartha
mātragatamekatvamabhidhātumutsahate na karaṇagatam, tatkimasyā na karaṇatvamarthaḥ ?
bhavatu tathāpi tadāpi prātipadikārthamātragatamevābhidhattām | nanu kimete
asaṃbaddhe eva mithaḥ karaṇaikatve prāk satyamiti vadato yugapat dvayābhidhāna
paratvam na khalu samarthopyakṣaśabdaḥ sakṛdeva parasparāsabandhā'nekārtyaparaḥ
prayujyate loke | na kadācidakṣamānayetyukte sahasaivā'kṣatrayaṃ pratiyanti
laukāḥ | kiṃ tu prakaraṇādivaśādekameva, tadabhāve vā saṃdihate | akṣāviti
vā akṣāniti vā dvau trīnakṣānpratipadyanta iti cenna | ekaśeṣeṇa saṃbandhā
'vagateḥ | ihāpyekaprātipadikārthasamavāyalakṣaṇaḥ karaṇaikatvayorasti saṃbandha
iti cetkimayamabhidhānavṛttau nimittaṃ na vā ? no cetkiṃ tadabhidhānena ?
asatkalpatvānnimittatve tu karaṇasyaiva tadekatvam | ekasya vā karaṇatvamiti
tṛtīyāśrutilabhyatvānnaupādānikaṃ śeṣatvamiti siddham | tadiha sāmyavaiṣamya
vicārātpūrva kratvarthapuruṣārthau vibhaktau kratuśeṣatvā'śeṣatvavicāreṇa | paratastu
siddha ekaśeṣatve kratuprayogavidhivyāpāraparimāṇamātravicārāddadhyānayanādau
tu kratvarthatvapuruṣārthatvamukhena prayogeyatteti | yata eva ca naupādānikamapi
tu śrutyādilakṣaṇamekatvādeḥ śeṣatvam ata eva sāmyavaiṣamyavicāropakrame
bhāṣyakāra āha atikrāntastṛtīyaviṣaya iti | tārtīyapramāṇaviṣayatveneti
tṛtīyaviṣayatā bhavati nānyathā | yathā ca tadviṣayatvaṃ tathoktamityarthaḥ |
samprati pañcamādhyāyacintāmāripsustadviṣayaṃ tāvadākṣipati—pañcame
[p.294]
naitatsāram | codanālakṣaṇo'rtho dharmmaḥ, sa ca caturavastha evetyuktam |
kramaśca tadatiriktaḥ, na hyeṣa prayogavidherviṣayo'nanuṣṭheyatvāt | aśabdārtha-
tu kiṃ cintyate ? catasraḥ khalvimā vidheravasthāstāsāṃ dvitīyatṛtīyacaturthā
dhyāyaistisra utpattiviniyogarūpā nirūpitāḥ, ṣaṣṭhena cā'dhikāro nirūpa
yiṣyate | tatkimaparamavaśiṣyate yatpañcamādhyāyagocaraḥ syādityāśayaḥ |
samādhātā tu vakṣyamāṇā'bhisandhirāha—nanu kramaḥ | hanta bhoḥ sthavīya
etat krama iti | na punarayaṃ pramāṇagocaraḥ | catasṛṇāmapi vidhyavasthānā
'nāspadatvādaśābdatvātpratyakṣādīnāṃ ca davīyastvāt | na ca pramāṇarahitaṃ
mīmāṃsāyāmādriyante vṛddhā ityāha—kena pramāṇena ? samādhāturnigūḍhābhi
saṃdhirāha—śrutyādibhiḥ | vaṣaṭkartuḥ prathamabhakṣa iti prathamapadamaindravāyavāgrā
nityagrahaṇaṃ gṛhapatiṃ dīkṣayitveti ktvāśrutirarthādayaśca vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇakāḥ
pramāṇaṃ krama ityarthaḥ | ākṣeptā svābhiprāyamāha—naitatsāram | codanā
lakṣaṇo'rtho dharmaḥ, sā ca vidhyabhidhāyinītvādvidheśca cātūrūpyāccatura
vasthaivetyuktamadhastāt | satyamuktam, kimetāvatā ? ityata āha—kramaśca
tadativṛttātmā | kutaḥ ? na hyeṣa prayogavidherviṣayaḥ | tatra vivaraṇakṛto
hetumāha—ananuṣṭheyatvāt | anuṣṭhānaṃ hi prayogaḥ, tadvidhiranuṣṭhānaṃ vyāpāraṃ
tadāveśādvā kārakaṃ gocarayet | na ca kramo vyāpārastatkārakaṃ vā vyāpāra
kārakā'nuṣṭhānapracayalabhyatvādasya | nanu na tarhi vidhirekādaśaprayājā
nityekādaśasaṃkhyāyāḥ | iyamapi kramavatpadārthā'nuṣṭhānapracayalabhyaiveti na
vyāpāro na ca kārakam | nātraikādaśasaṃkhyā vidhīyate prayājādīnāmapi tu
tallakṣaṇastadabhyāsaḥ | sa ca paunaḥpunyena vyāpāra eva | na ca paurṇṇamāsyā
miti prayogavidhiḥ prayogamanuṣṭheyaṃ vidhatte, kiṃ tu tadā karmeti nā'nuṣṭheyagocaro
vidhirananuṣṭheyaścāyaṃ tārtīyaḥ pāñcamikaśca kramaḥ | tasmānna prayogaviṣayaḥ |
nibandhanakṛto hetumāha—aśabdārthatvāt | idamatrākūtam | yadyapi
bhāvārthaviṣayo vidhiḥ, tathāpi na sākṣād gavārthe prerayati niyojyam, api tu
svātmani | idameva cāsya svātmani prerakatvaṃ yattiraścīnatayā tadavabodhaḥ |
puruṣasya tadavabodhavattaiva cāsya niyojyatā | sa tu vidhiḥ kāryasvabhāvo
bhāvārthaviṣayo 'vagamyamāno bhāvadravyaguṇasaṃkhyākramādinā sarveṇa prayojanenā
'nvitaḥ svaśabdena prayojanaṃ sadabhidhīyate | bhāvādyabhidhāyinaśca śabdāḥ
svārthāstadaidamarthyāpannānevācakṣate | tadidaṃ grāhakagrahaṇamucyate | tataścā
[p.295]
tvāt kramavadvyatirekeṇedantayā'nirūpaṇāt | padārthā hi krameṇā'nuṣṭhīyante
na tu kramaḥ | nāpi viniyogavidherutpattividhervā | ata eva nādhikāravidheḥ |
prerayannapyayaṃ vidhirbhāvārthādiṣu tairvinā svasiddhimanāsādayaṃstayā va vinā
''tmasiddhatvā'bhāvena sādhyatvā'nupapatteḥ sarvāneva tānākṣipati | tadidameṣāṃ
vidhiviṣayatvam | yathāha yattu tatsiddhyarthamupādīyate tadvidheyamiti tatraiva vyava
hāra iti | tadeva ca tena svasiddhyarthamākṣeptavyaṃ yatatsiddhyartha tatra tatsiddhyartha
yadvidhinā''tmedamarthyamāpāditam, taccā''pāditaṃ yattatsaṃnidhisamāmnātam |
tatra vaṣaṭkartuḥ prathamabhakṣa ityevamāderadhikāravidhisannidhau svaśabdena samarpitasya
padārthabhūtasya kramasya bhavatu vidheyatā tārtīyasya, pāñcamikasya tvaśabdārthasya
gṛhapati dīkṣayitvetyevamādestadaidamarthyābhāvātkathaṃ tatsiddhyarthatayopādānam ?
na ca dīkṣayitveti ktvāśrutiḥ pūrvakālatāmabhidadhāti, mukhaṃ vyādāya svapi
tītyatra vyabhicārāt | svāpītarakālameva hi mukhavyādānam na ca ye mukhavyā
dānānnidrāṇasya parāñcaḥ kṣaṇāstānvivakṣitvā vyākhyeyā lokādhīnāvadhāra
ṇatvācchabdārthasaṃbandhasya, vyādānasya tu purastāttaneṣvapi nidrāṇāṃ kṣaṇeṣu mukhaṃ
vyādāya svapitīti sorastāḍaṃ krandanto 'pi prayogasyā 'nivṛtteḥ | samānakartṛ
kataivā'vyabhicāriṇī ktvārthaḥ | samānakartṛke 'rthe varttamānācca dhātorvidhīya
mānaṃ ya eva pūrva prayujyate tatraiva vatvāpratyayaṃ prayuñjate laukikāḥ | yathā
prayogaṃ cā'rthapratyayo yathāpratīti cā'nuṣṭhānamiti kutaḥ pūrvakālārthatābhimānaḥ
prayogopādhirityaśabdārtho'yaṃ kramaḥ, tathā cā'vidheya ityananuṣṭheyatvāditi
yuktam |
mā nāma bhūtkramasya vyāpāravatsvato 'nuṣṭheyatvam, anuṣṭheyāvacchedaka
tayā tu bhaviṣyati | tathā hi | vyāpārāḥ krameṇāva'cchidyante tena
tadavacchedakatayaikatvādivatkramo'nuṣṭhāsyata ityata āha—kramavadvatirekeṇedantayā
anirūpaṇāt | anuṣṭheyāvacchedakamanuṣṭheyam, taccā'nuṣṭheyam yaccoditam,
samudāyinaśca padārthāścoditā naiṣāṃ samudāyastavacchedaśca kramastadāśrayaṇā
tsamudāyinaḥ padārthānnanuṣṭheya ityarthaḥ | nāpi tasya svato 'nuṣṭheyatetyāha—
padārthā hi krameṇā'nuṣṭhīyante na kramaḥ | padārthā iti bahuvacanaṃ bahutvaikārtha
samavetasamudāyāśrayatvaṃ kramasya sūcayati | tena na svato nāpi parataḥ kramasyā
'nuṣṭheyatvamiti na prayogavidhigocaraḥ krama iti siddham |
nanu mā vidadhyātprayogavidhiḥ, utpattividhirniyogavidhirvāṃ vidhā
[p.296]
aniyojyatdāt | syācca tāvacchautre kramaniyame tatparo vidhirarthādiṣu
tatparavidhyasaṃbhavaḥ | ucyate | prayogo vidhyartha ityuktam | kramabhedaśca
prayogaviśeṣaḥ | tathā hi | krameṇa padārthā anuṣṭhīyante ityucyate |
syati | na khalvetāvanuṣṭhānagocarau svarūpasaṃbandhaparatvādityata āha—nāpi
viniyogavidherutpattividhervā viṣaya ityanuṣajyate | kutaḥ ? ata eva ana
nuṣṭhetvādeva | ayamabhiprāyaḥ | sarva evāyaṃ vidhiścatūrūpaḥ, tadabhidhānameva
tvidamparatayā bhidyata ityuktam | ataścotpativiniyogayorapi prayogavyāpta
tvātkrame ca prayogasyā'bhāvāditi vyāpakanivṛttyā vyāptayorutpattinivṛtti
riti | athādhikārasya viṣayaḥ kasmānna bhavati ? ityata āha—nā'dhikāra
vidheḥ | atrāpi viṣaya ityanupajyate | kutaḥ ? aniyojyatvāt, mā
nāma bhūdanuṣṭheyagocaro 'thikāravidhiḥ niyojyaṃ gocarayati, cetanaśca,
niyojyaḥ | kramasya caitanyamiti ca sthavīyaḥ | na ca nimitatvaṃ prāga
siddheḥ | tasmāt anuṣṭheyatvādapadārthatvatvādaniyojyatvācca na vidhiviṣayaḥ
krama iti | nanvapadārthātvādityasiddho hetuḥ, dīkṣayitveti ktvārthā
tvātkramasya | tathā ca tatparo vidhirityata āha syācca tāvacchrota
kramaniyame tatparo vidhiḥ arthādiṣu tatparavidhyasambhavaḥ | ayamarthaḥ |
vyabhicārāt pūrvakālatā na ktvārtha ityuktam | tathā cā'padārthatvāditi nā
'siddho hetuḥ | abhyupagamyāpyucyate | śrautavyatiriktaḥ pāñcamikaḥ krama
iti dharmiviśeṣaṇānna bhāgāsiddho 'padārthātvāditi hetuḥ | arthādiṣu
kramavyāpakatvāditi |
evamākṣipya samādhatte—ucyate | prayogo vidhyartha ityuktam |
tataḥ kimityata āha—kramabhedaśca prayogaviśeṣaḥ | ayamarthaḥ | krameṇa hi
prayogo viśeṣyate, prayogāntarādvyāvartyate | tathā hi | ta eva padārthāḥ
kramabhedena prayujyamānāḥ prayogakramabhedadhiyamādadhati varṇṇā iva tattve'pi
gaveveganadīdīnajarārājetipadabhedabuddhim | anyathā 'rthadhībhedo na syāt |
asati tu hetubhede kāryabhedasyā''kasmikatvaprasaṅgāt iti |
nanūktaṃ padārthā vidhīyante na tatsamudāyaḥ | padārthānuṣṭhānānniṣpanno hi
saḥ | taṃ ca śritaḥ kramo na padārthānpratyekaṃ teṣvanirūpaṇādataḥ padārthaviṣayo
vidhirna kramaṃ gocarayati | na cāyaṃ śabdārtho yena kāryaidamarthyāpattyā
tatsiddhyarthatayopādīyamānatayā vidheyaḥ syādityata āha—tathā hi | krameṇa
[p.297]
padārthā anuṣṭhīyanta ityucyate | idagatrākūtam | yattāvaducyate na kramo
nāma padārthavṛttiḥ pratyekaṃ teṣvanirūpaṇāt iti, tadayuktam | saṃyogavibhāgā
vayavidvitvādisaṃkhyādīnāṃ pratyekamanirūpyamāṇānāmapi padārthavṛttivarśanāt |
athā'nupalambhāt | tadayuktam | saṃyogādivadeva padārthāśritasyaṃ tasyopa
labdheḥ | tathā hi | saṃyogādayaḥ sadvitīyaṃ padārthamāśrayanto naikatra nirūpyante
na ca samudāyāśrayāḥ | na ca bhavataḥ samūhaḥ saṃyukta iti pratyayavyapadeśau,
api tvanenaitatsaṃyuktamiti vā saṃyukte ete iti vā | samudāyaikārthasamavāyastu
saṃyogādīnām | tebhyo'pi ca kramasya pūrvā'paratvā'paranāmnaḥ pratyekapadārthā
''dhāratāviśeṣaḥ | tathāpi tu dvitīyapadārthajñānādhīnānirūpaṇamātreṇa
saṃyogāditulyatā kramasya | tena bhavatyapi tadrūpatayā sa dvitīyavṛttiḥ pūrvatvā
'paratvarūpeṇa kevalapadārthavṛttirvā | ubhayathā'pi padārthavṛttireva na samudāya
vṛttistathopalambhāt | na hi bhavati karmaṇāṃ samūhaḥ pūrva iti vā para iti
vā | karmaikaṃ karmāntarā'pekṣayā pūrva tadapekṣayā ca karmāntaramaparam | pūrvā
'paratve eva vā'pyavadhimatpadārthanirūpaṇīye tadvitatirvā kramaḥ | tayāśca
śabdakarmādāvupalabdhestadāśritatvamipyate | tena yadyapi pratyekaṃ padārtheṣu
prātisviko vidhiḥ, yadyapi ca prayogavidhiravalambate | sa ca yaugapadyā
nuṣṭhānānupapattestadvatprayogavidhāne jvaraharatakṣakacūḍāratnā'laṅkāropadośavada
prāmāṇyaprasaṅgāt yathā śaknuyādityupādityupasthāpitakramabhedaṃ samuccitapadārthā
nuṣṭhānabhedaṃ vidavatkramamapi vidhatte | na khalvasminnavidhīyamāne etadviśiṣṭa
padārthānuṣṭhānavidhisambhavaḥ |
yadapi matam yata eva so'pekṣipyate vidhinā, ata eva na vidhāyi
ṣyate | na hi yadyadvidhinā'pekṣyate tattadvidhīyata iti kaścinniyamaheturasti |
adhikārasyāpyādhayatvātkartaiva tu tatsiddhyartha vidhīyate | kramabhedasya
tvapekṣitatvādvidhānāṃ pramāturasya ca pratīteranyasya cā'pratīteḥpratītipūrva
katvāccānuṣṭhānasya prekṣāvatāmādriyate vidhiriti |
atrocyate | ki punarvidheyaṃ manyate bhavān yadatra nāsti ? nanūktaṃ
niyāgasiddhyarthatayopādīyamānaṃ taditi | kimidaṃ tatsiddhyarthamiti ?
tatsādhanaṃ cetkayamekatvādino vidhaṃyatvam | na hi tatkārakam | kārakāva
cchedaṃ taditi cet | kramo'pyanuṣṭhayavastvavacchadakatapā'nuṣṭheyasya ca
kārakatayā tādṛśa eva | na caiṣa samudāyāyatta ityuktam | atha na
tatsiddhyarthamiti tatkārakaṃ vidheyam, api tu tadaidamarthyāpannaṃ dvaidhaṃ ca tadvi
[p.298]
niyogata upādānataśca | viniyogatastārtīyātpaśoḥ, upādānataścaturthāt |
tadekatvādīnāṃ tūbhayavidhamapi śeṣatvaṃ śabdārthasya kramastu pāñcamiko na
śabdārtha ityuktam | tatkimidānīṃ “viśvajitā yajeta” ityaśabdārthataiveti
cet | tarhi dīkṣayitvetyatrāpi pūrvakālatāmabhidheyāpi śabdārthaḥ kathaṃciditi
śrautaḥ kramo viśeṣata iti tatsiddhyarthatayopādīyata iti vidheyaḥ | api ca
śabdopātta eva tatsiddhyarthatayopādīyata ityatra ko hetuḥ ? tasyaiva tādarthyā
vagama iti cet | na, tasya tādarthyaṃ kiṃ tatpratipādakaḥ śabdo'vabodhayet,
utānuṣṭheyaikārthābhidhāyī śabdaḥ ? na tāvat pūrvaḥ kalpaḥ | na hi śrutyādi
mātrāt tādarthyamityuktam anuṣṭheyakāryābhidhāyī tu śabdo yadeva tadanantaraṃ
buddhau viparivartate yena prayojanenānvitaṃ svārthaṃ prayojanaṃ yadabhidhatte tena
tatsannidhāpanamātropayogīśabdaḥ sannidhāpanaṃ vā'nyato'pyarthāderiti śabdo
pāttatvaṃ kvopayujyate ? tena nāsya pramāṇāntarasamadhigamyatvādavidheyatvam |
arthādisahāyasya śabdasyaiva prāmāṇyāt | anyathā tvanabhidheyakramavādino
'pyacodanālakṣaṇatvena kramasya śāstravicārānarhatvaprasaṅgāt |
yaccocyate—vidheyatvaṃ prayuktyā vyāptam, sā ca krame nāsti
taduttarakālatvātkramasya | ata eva prayuktyanantaramayaṃ vicāritaḥ | tasmādvi
dheyatvavyāpikā prayuktiḥ kramānnivarttamānā vyāpyaṃ vidheyatvamapi nivarttayati
vṛkṣateva śiśaṃpātvamārādupalabhyamānādekaśilāmayād giripradeśāditi, tada
yuktam | prayuktyanantarakālatvasyāsiddheḥ | vidadhāna eva hi prayogamayaṃ
prayogavidhiryena yena vinā'nyato'prāptena prayogasaṃbandhena na nirvahati tatsarvaṃ
taccheṣatayā vidhatte | evaṃ lakṣaṇakaśca śrutyādisamarpitaḥ krama iti kathaṃ
prayuktyuttarakālaḥ ? tasmādvyāpakānupalabdhirasiddhā | na caturthe svarūpeṇa
prayuktirvicāritā, kiṃ tarhi prayujyamānapadārtheyattayetyuktam | tadvijñānaṃ krama
bheda niyamāvagame hetuḥ | prayujyamānapadārtheyattānavadhāraṇaṃ hīti yāvat | na ca
padārthavatkramo'vagataḥ | sa padārthāntarā'nupraveśe bhajyeta | kramāvadhāraṇena
tu vinā neyattāvagamaprasaṅgaḥ | nāpi hi krameṣviyattā tādavasyāt |
tasmānnā'yaṃ prayogottarakālaḥ kramavicāraḥ, api tu tadiyattā'vadhāraṇottara
kālaḥ | taddhetutvāt | tasmātprayujyamānapadārthāśrayastaccheṣakramo vidheya
śceti sāmpratam | tadidamuktam | tathā hi krameṇa padārthā anuṣṭhīyanta
ityucyate | svarūpeṇā'nanuṣṭheyo'pyaśabdopātto'pi anuṣṭheyāvacchedakatayā
tadvidhau vidheya ityarthaḥ | yathā caivaṃvidhasyāpi vidheyatvaṃ tathopapāditama
dhastāditi bahuvistārādvibhyato'pi vistare patitāḥ smaḥ | tasmādāstā
ntāvat |
[p.299]
tathā ca prayogavidhiviṣayatvānna codanālakṣaṇatvamativartate kramaḥ |
tatra prathamaṃ kramavadvyatirekeṇedantayā'nirūpitatvāt aprayojyatāmaviniyo
jyatāmanutpādyatāṃ ca matvā vidhiviṣayatvamāśaṅkya prayogavidhiviṣayatā
darśitā | uttaravarṇakena tūdāharaṇaśuddhiḥ kṛtā | dīkṣāpadārthasya prāpteḥ |
tathā ca caturvidhavidhivyāpārā'gocaratvādyaccodanālakṣaṇatvamākṣiptaṃ
tatprayogavidhivyāpāraviṣayatāṃ darśayatā parihṛtamityāha—tathā ca prayoga
vidhiviṣayatvānna codanālakṣaṇatvamiti vartate kramaḥ | evaṃ prameya pariśodhya
vicārasvarūpamādarśayati—tatra prathamaṃ kramavadvyatirekeṇedantayā'nirūpitatvāda
prayojyatāmaviniyojyatāmanutpādyatāṃ ca matvā vidhiviṣayatvamāśaṅkya
prayogavidhiviṣayatā darśitā | prathamaṃ darśiteti sambandhaḥ | kṛtavyākhyāna
metadākṣepaparihārayoḥ |
varṇakāntaramāha—uttaravarṇakenetyādinā kṛtetyantena | atra cottara
varṇakenodāharaṇapariśuddhistadvidhau kṛteti prathamamadhyamā'vasānaiḥ granthaprayoja
nam | tatrodāharaṇam—
satre ye yajamānāste ṛtvijaḥ ityāmnāyate |
adhvaryurgṛhapati dīkṣayitvā brahmāṇaṃ dīkṣayati, tata udgātāram, tato
hotāram, tatastaṃ pratiprasthātā dīkṣayitvā gāthino dīkṣayati ityādi
grāvastutaṃ hotā brahmaṇo dīkṣayati brahmacārī vā''cāryapreṣitaḥ ityantam |
tatra saṃśayaḥ kiṃ tattatpadārthaviśiṣṭā dīkṣā vidhīyate uta krama iti ?
tattatpadārthaviśiṣṭadīkṣāvidhānenaivārthalabhyatvātkramasya pṛthaganavagamācca
dīkṣaiva vidhīyata iti prāpte vidheyāntarābhāvātkrama eva vidhīyata ityāha—
dīkṣāpadārthasya prāpteḥ | yājamānatvātsūtre gṛhapatyādīnāṃ codakata eva
prāptā dīkṣeti sā tāvanna vidhātavyā | nanu kramo'pyaśabdārtha iti na
vidhātavyaḥ | uktaṃ hi na ktvāśrutiḥ pūrvakālatāmāha ityata āha—
anabhidheyasyāpi kathañcitpratīyamānasya viśiṣṭakramānuṣṭhānasya vidhyarthatvāt |
ayamarthaḥ | kuta etat ktvāśruterna pūrvakālatā'rtha iti | tadabhāve'pi
prayogadarśanāditi cet, nanvevaṃ sati samānakartṛkatvamapi nārthaḥ syāt |
alaṃ khalūpapadāddhātostadabhāve'pi pratiṣedhamātre prayogadarśanāt | khalūktvā,
alaṃ bhuktvā iti | yadyucyeta na vyabhicārādaśabdārthatvaṃ brūmaḥ | mā
bhūdakṣādiśabdānāmanekārthānāmānarthakyam, api tvananyalabhyaḥ śabdārtha iti
sthitiḥ | prayogopādhilabhyā ca pūrvāparakālatetyuktam | na tvevaṃ sati
samānakartṛkateti saiva vyabhicaratyapi ktvārthaḥ | kaḥ punastarhi prayogopādhiḥ ?
samānakartṛkatve'pi dhātvarthayorya eva pūrvaṃ prayujyate dhātustatraiva ktvāpratyayo
[p.300]
anabhidheyasyāpi kathaṃcitpratī pramānasya viśiṣṭakramānuṣṭhānatva vidhyarthatvāt
kartṛ viśeṣavidhādhvaryusambandhasyānarthapayāt | anekārthavidhito vākyabhedāt |
dīkṣāpadārthasya tu pratipradhātāvṛtteḥ anuṣṭhānaviśeṣavidhirekārthatvāt | tadvidhau
laukikānāṃ nānyatra | satyam, prayoganiyame siddhe syādevaṃ, na tvayamasti |
sa khalvanvākhyānādvā gamyo bhavellokato vā | na tāvat ktvāntaṃ pūrva
prayoktavyamākhyātāditi kasyapidanvākhyānamasti | nāpi ktvāntameva
lokaḥ pūrva prayuṅkte | ākhyātādapi parastātkavīnāmṛṣīṇāmāmnāye ca
ktvāntapadaprayogadarśanāt | tasmādananyathāsiddheḥ samānakartṛkatāvat pūrva
kālatā tadartha iti paramārthaḥ |
mābhūttathā, anabhidheyā'pi kathañcitpratīyamānatayā vidheyā'bhāvā
tsaiva tāvadvidhātavyā, tathā cā'narthako'yaṃ kākadantānāmiva ktvaḥ pūrvakālatā
bhidhānasya vicāra iti |
athocyate | mā vidhāyi dīkṣā, codakaprāptāyāṃ tu tasyāmadhvaryu
prabhṛtayaḥ kartṛviśeṣā vidhāyiṣyante | tataḥ kiṃ kramavidhinetyata āha—
kartṛviśeṣavidhāvadhvaryusambandhasyānarthakyāt | ādhvaryavī hi prakṛtau dīkṣā
'vagateti kimatrā'dhvaryusambandhavidhinā | prāptatvāt | itaśca anekātha
vidhito vākyabhedāt | adhvaryurgṛha | tigadhvayurbrahmāṇamadhvaryurhotāramityaneke'
rthāstadvidhau vākyaṃ bhidyeteti | nanu ca tathāpi pratipadārtha dīkṣā''vṛttau
kuto na vākyabhedaḥ ? ityāha—dīkṣāpadārthasya tu pratipradhānā''vṛtteḥ |
mama dīkṣāsamabhivyāhāro vidhīyete | yajamānasaṃskāro hi dīkṣā | sā
guṇatvāt pratiyajamānamāvartate iti nyāyaprāpto'yamarthaḥ | itthaṃ kramavidhau
na vākyabhado'rthaiṃkatvādityāha—anuṣṭhānaviśeṣavidherekārthatvāt | viśeṣyate
'nenānuṣṭhānamiti anuṣṭhānaviśeṣaḥ kramaḥ | evaṃ tu vadata ācāryasya
saṃyogādivadanekapadārthavṛttikaḥ kramo'bhimata iti |
kaścidāha—adhvaryuvidhāvaniyamaḥ iti | tasyā'rthameke vyācakṣate |
atra hi 
ye yajamānāste ṛtvijaḥ iti ṛtvikkāryaiḥ yajamānavidhānād
ṛtvijo nivṛttāḥ, yajamānā eva tu tattadṛtvikkāryakaraṇāllabdhartvigvya
padeśā na punariha tattvato'dhvaryādayaḥ santi, tena yenava kenacitkvacitkadā
cidādhvaryavaṃ yajamānena kṛtaṃ tenā'dhvaryuvyapadeśabhājā''dhvaryavāntaraṃ karttavya
[p.301]
na pūtaḥ pāvayatīti pratiṣedhāt, itaramanyaḥ, teṣāṃ yato viśeṣaḥ syāditi |
miti na kaścinniyamaheturasti | tenādhvaryavī dīkṣā gṛhapateryena kṛtā, sa
eva brahmādivīkṣāyāganena vacanena niyamyate | evaṃ pratipasyātrādiniyamo
'pīti na vidheyāntarā'bhāvaḥ | tadetadālocanīyam—kathaṃ niyamaheturnāstīti |
nanu yadyatra niyāmakaṃ pramāṇaṃ nāsti tato brahmādayo'pyaniyatāḥ | tathā hi—
ya eva brahmakāryakaraṇād brahmā sa eva hotṛneṣṭrādikāryakaraṇāddhotṛneṣṭrādiḥ |
evaṃ hotrādirbrahmaneṣṭrādikāryakaraṇād brahmaneṣṭrādiḥ | evaṃ neṣṭrādayo
brahmaneṣṭrādayastathā brahmā'pyadhvaryādiriti niyamyamānānāṃ niyamavigayāṇāṃ
cā'vyavasthānātkimāśrayo niyamaḥ kalpayiṣyate | brahmādiṣvadhvaryudīkṣā
vidhinā khalvanye neṣṭrādayo nivarttanīyāḥ | tatra vyāvarttyānāṃ brahmādidīkṣāyāṃ
neṣṭrādīnāṃ vidheyasya cādhvaryoravyavasthānātyaṃkareṇa na śakyā vyāvṛttiḥ
pratipattam | na tpravadhānanakhanirbhedādīnāṃ saṃkareṇa 
brīhīnavahanti iti
niyamaḥ kalpyate | tasmādyasyartvijaḥ karmaṇi prathamaṃ yaḥ pravṛtto yajamāna
stenaiva tadīyaṃ sarvameva samāpanīyam, na yajamānāntareṇartvityāntarakāriṇā
nauvityādaṅgīkarttavyam | avyavasthāyāmanaucityaprasaṅgāt | prakaraṇasāmarthyā
divadaucityamapi hi vākyārthāvadhāraṇopāyaṃ saṅgirante śābdāḥ | ataścau
cityameva niyamaheturastīti niyamato'pi na vidhātavya iti sthavīya etadato
na dūṣitamācāryeṇa |
ye tvanye manyante—dīkṣāyā ādhvaryavītvād gṛhapatyādeścānyeṣāṃ ca
dīkṣāyāmadhvaryuḥ prāpta eva gṛhapatyādiṣu vidhānena tadanyebhyaḥ parisaṃkhyāyate
tatparisaṃkhyāyāgeva ca niyamoktirbhaktyā'nanyanivṛtisāmyaṃ ca bhaktiriti
tannirāsāyāha—na pūtaḥ pāvayatīti pratiṣedhāt | tatra vidhisaṃbhavena
doṣatrayavatī parisaṃkhyoviteti prasiddhataratvādanabhidhāya vacanavirodho darśitaḥ,
hotṛdīkṣā'nantaramadhvaryudīkṣāvidhānāddīkṣito'dhvaryupūtaḥ kathamadhvaryuprabhṛtīn
pāvayeddīkṣayediti | tasmānniṣedhaphalā parisaṃkhyā niṣedhe'nupapannā, phalā'
bhāvādityarthaḥ | na cā'dhvaryudīkṣāyāṃ pratiprasthātā na prāpto yena taṃ prati
prasthātetivacanaṃ pratiprasthātāraṃ vidadhyādityāha—itaramadhvaryumanyaḥ pratiprasthātā
pāvayati vinā'pi vacanāt | ādhvaryate hi karmaṇi prakṛtāvevā'dhvaryupuruṣāḥ
pratiprasthātrādayo vihitāḥ | yadā khalvadhvaryurasamarthaḥ karmāntaravyāsaṅgi
tvātsvātmani kriyāvirodhitvādvā, tadā tadīyaṃ karma tatpuruṣāḥ pratiprasthātrā
dayaḥ kurvantīti sthitam | tadayaṃ svātmani kriyāvirodhādātmānaṃ
dīkṣayitumasamartha iti svapuruṣeṇa dīkṣaṇīyaḥ | svapuruṣaśca tasya
[p.302]
abrāhmaṇasyā''rtvijyā'bhāvāduttarāśramiṇośca | brahmacāriṇaścā''cāryyapara
tantratvāt | arthādiṣu tu yadyapi padārthaparovidhistathāpi tadrūpaprayojanāpekṣī
pratiprasthāteti prāptatvānna vidhātavyaḥ | nanvadhvaryupuruṣā bahava iti
āha—teṣāṃ yato teṣāṃ pratiprasthātṛvidhaye vacanaṃ bhaviṣyatītyata viśeṣaḥ
syāditi | teṣāmadhvaryupuruṣāṇāṃ madhye yato yasya | sārvavibhakti
kastasiḥ | viśeṣaḥ antaraṅgatvalakṣaṇaḥ syātso'nyaḥ pāvayati | ādhvaryave
hi karmaṇi tadasāmarthye santi vihitā bahavaḥ pratiprasthātṛprabhṛtayaḥ uttarottara
bahiraṅgāḥ | teṣāmādyaḥ pratiprasthātā'ntaraṅga iti tanniyamo na vidhātavyaḥ,
prāptatvādityarthaḥ | nanu connetṛdīkṣāyāmadhvaryuprabhṛtīnāṃ pūtatvenā'nava
kāśatvātpuruṣamātraprasaṅge brāhmaṇo niyamyetetyata āha—abrāhmaṇasyārtvi
jyābhāvāt | brāhmaṇasyaiva yājanamasādhāraṇatayā vihitamiti kuto'nyatra
prasajyenetyarthaḥ | atha brāhmaṇaprāptau cāturāśramyasyā'viśeṣaprasakterbrāhmaṇena
saha brahmacāriṇo vikalpyatvādbhikṣuvānaprasthādinivṛttyarthavacanamityata āha—
uttarāśramiṇośca | ārtvijyā'bhāvāditi cehā'nukṛṣyate | na vṛttya
rthamārtvijyam, bhikṣuvānaprasthayostadvṛttiniṣedhāt | nā'nugrahārtham, 
hisā
'nugrahayoranārambhī ityanugrahaniṣedhādinyarthaḥ | na ca brahmacāriṇaḥ svātantryeṇa
pravṛttiniṣedhārthamācāryyapreṣita iti vacanam | vacanāntarasiddhaṃ hi tasyā''
cāryyapāratantryamityāha—brahmacāriṇaścā''cāryyaparatantratvāt | tasmādetebhyo
hetubhyo vidheyāntarā'bhāvātkramasya ca vidhānārhatvāt | uttaravarṇakena
tadvidhau kramasya vidhyartha 
carmaṇi dvīpinaṃ hanti itivadudāharaṇapariśuddhiḥ
kṛteti granthayojanā |
sampratyarthalakṣaṇasya kramasya vidheyatāmāha—arthādiṣvityādinā'pekṣite
tyantena | atrodāharaṇam | 
agnihotraṃ juhoti yavāgūṃ pacati" iti |
tatra saṃśayaḥ—kiṃ homapākayoraniyatakramaḥ, uta pākā'nantaraṃ homaḥ ? tatra
vidhyorhomapākapadārthamātravidhānaparatvātpūrvavacca śruterabhāvāt homarūpayavā
gūpacanaprayojanakriyayośca prameyāntaḥpātitayā kramaniyamaṃ prati prāmāṇyā
nupapatterdravyāntareṇāpi homasyopapatteḥ kramaniyamapramāṇā'bhāvādaniyama iti
prāpte āha—arthādiṣu yadyapi padārthaparo vidhistathāpi tadrūpaprayojanā'pekṣī |
anuṣṭhānaviśeṣaḥ kramaṃ pratipādayati iti pareṇa sambandhaḥ | ayamarthaḥ—
yadyapi kramaviśeṣe na śrutirasti tathāpi homavidhirarūpaṃ homaṃ vidhātuma
samarthastadrūpabhūtadravyamapekṣate—yavāgūṃ pacatīti | niṣprayojanaṃ yavāgūṃ prati
guṇatvena pākavidhānamanupapannamiti yavāgūḥ prayojanāpekṣiṇī | tathā cā'gni
[p.303]
svādhyāyavidhipratyāyitapāṭhakramaniyamaprayojanāpekṣaśca nirjñātaṃ ca kramamanu-
hotraṃ yavāgvā tāṃ ca pākenetyayamarthaḥ siddho bhavatīti | na ca prāgasiddhā
yavāgūragnihotrāya kalpata iti padārthaparo'pi vidhiḥ padārtharūpaprayojanāpekṣī
arthasahakārīti yāvatpaurvāparyaviśeṣamavagamayati na ca niyataṃ prāmāṇyam |
prameyasyā'pi prāmāṇyadarśanāt | yathāhuḥ prāmāṇikāḥ—prameyā ca tulā
prāmāṇyavatī iti | tasmādārtho'pi kramo vidheya iti siddham |
arthādiṣvityādiśabdasūcitaṃ kramaprāptaṃ pāṭhakramasya vidheyatvamāha—
svādhyāya iti | atrodāharaṇaṃ 
samidho yajati, tanūnapātaṃ yajati ityādi |
tatra sandehaḥ—kimete samidādayo niyatakramāḥ prayoktavyāḥ, uta pāṭhakrame
ṇeti ? śrutyarthayorabhāvātpāṭhakramasya prāptatvānnāntarīyakatvenā'varjanīya
tayā'nupraveśādaprayojakatvādaniyatakramā eva samidādayaḥ prayoktavyā iti
prāpte āha—svādhyāyavidhipratyāyitapāṭhakramaniyamaprayojanāpekṣaśca | 
vidhi
ranuṣṭhānaviśeṣaṃ pratipādayati iti pareṇa sambandhaḥ | svādhyāyavidhinā
hi kramavanti vākyāni upasthāpitāni, tadeṣāṃ kramasya sati prayojanasambhave
nā'varjanīyatāmātreṇopādānamaṅgīkartumucitam | sambhavati ca prayogavidhya
pekṣitasamidādikramabhedaniyamaḥ prayojanam | na cātra pāṭhakramasyāsāmarthyam |
tathā hi—krameṇa vākyāni paṭhyante, tenaivā'pratītacarāstadarthā api
prakāśante, kāraṇakramā'nuvidhānāt kāryakramasya, vākyavijñānajanitatvācca
tadarthavijñānasya, yathā'vagamaṃ cā'nuṣṭhāmātprekṣāvatām | tenā'yaṃ pāṭhakramaḥ
svādhyāyavidhinā'pi prayojanā''kāṅkṣaḥ sāmarthyena ca tadasambhavānnirākṛtaḥ
prayogavidhinā samidādikramaniyamalakṣaṇaprayojane vyavasthāpyatāmiti pāṭha
kramasadhrīcīnaprayogavidhividheyakramasiddhiḥ | nānādhikāreṣu tu kramabheda
paṭhiteṣvapyekaprayogavacanasaṃgrahā'bhāvāt asambaddheṣu na kramaḥ sambhavatīti
svā'dhyāyavidhyutpāditāpi prayojanā''kāṅkṣā tadasambhavātpralīyate |
akṣarārthastu svādhyāyavidhipratyāyito yaḥ pāṭhakramaniyamastatprayojanaṃ
ca tadapekṣaprayogavidhiriti | ādiśabdasūcitāmeva pravṛttikramaniyamasya
vidheyatāmāha—nirjñātamiti | vājapeye 
saptadaśa prājāpatyān paśūnālabhate
iti śrūyate | tatra saptadaśaite bhāgā vājapeyāṅgabhūtā bājapeyaprayogavidhyā
pāditasahaprayogāścodakaprāptāścaiteṣu prokṣaṇādayo dharmāste'pi ca dharmi
sahabhuvaḥ sahaiva prayoktavyā naikaikaśaḥ kārtsnyena samāpanīyā iti samadhigatam |
[p.304]
rudhyamānaḥ svāvagataṃ ca sahabhāvagatyantamavādhamāno'nuṣṭhānaviśeṣaṃ pratipādaya-
tatra prathamapadārtho niyamapramāṇā'bhāvādyatra kvacana paśau prayoktavya iti
siddham | dvitīyādiṣu ca padārtheṣu saṃśayaḥ | kimete prathamapadārthapadaniyamena
prayoktavyāḥ, uta prathamapadārthakrameṇa ? iti | tatra pratītipūrvakatvāda
nuṣṭhānasya pratyayasamaye ca prathamapadārthānuṣṭhānavad dvitīyādipadārthānuṣṭhānasya
kramaniyamani pekṣatvādādyavad dvitīyādayo'pi puruṣecchādhīnā'nuṣṭhānakrama
iti prāpte āha—nirjñātaṃ kramamanuruthyamānaḥ prayogavidhiranuṣṭhānaviśeṣaṃ
pratipādayatīti | kasmādanurudhyate ? ityata āha—svā'vagataṃ ca saha
bhāvamatyantamavādhamānaḥ | cakāro hetau | bājapeyaprayogavidhinā hi sarve
padārthāḥ samucinatāḥ badhānaṃ prati prayujyamānatayā saṃnidhāpyante | na ca teṣāṃ
yugapatprayogaḥ sambhavatīti, yathā śaknuyādityupabandhāt | tādṛśamenāśrayate
karma yādṛśo'sau saṃvidhinātyantaṃ vādhyate | na cāsti vidheścodakaprāpteṣu
prokṣaṇādiṣu kiñcitprati prema dveṣo vā, yena kiñcitsaṃnidhāpayed vyavadhāpa
yedvā | tena tulyameva sarveṣāṃ saṃnidhāpanaṃ prekṣamāṇaḥ pariharannapi na vyavadhi
mavarjanīyatayā'bhimanyamānaḥ puruṣecchā'dhīnakramapratyayātparāñcamapi prathama
padārthapravṛttikramaṃ sahakāriṇamāsādya prayogamadhya eva dvinīyādibadārthaprayogaṃ
tattatkrameṇaiva niyacchati pradhānapratyāsattisāmyāya | anyathā vaiṣamyaṃ dharmāṇā
matyantavyavadhimadabhāvaḥ syāt | tathā hi—yataḥ prabhṛti prathamapadārtho'nu
ṣṭhitaḥ, tata eva prabhṛti dvitīyādīnāyanuṣṭhāne ādyapadārthapravṛttiviṣayasamba
ndhinā dharmeṇa dvitīyādidharmānuṣṭhānānāṃ ṣoḍaśabhiḥ paramaśubhirvyabadhānaṃ syāt |
tenā'varjanīyatvāt prayogavidhiranumanumanyate eva | saptadaśāttu paśorārabhya
dvitīyādau padārthe pratilomaṃ prayujyamāne dvāviṃśatā vyavadhānaṃ syāt | eva
manyeṣvapi vaiṣamyaṃ draṣṭavyam | tacca sarvatra samaḥ prayogavidhirnā'numanyate yataḥ
yasmāt svā'vagataṃ ca sahabhāvaṃ pradhānaṃ saṃnidhirnā'tyantaṃ bādhate | anyathā
vaiṣamyeṇa bādhaḥ syāt | tasmādanuṣṭhānaviśeṣaṃ kramaṃ pratipādayati prayoga
vidhiriti prayogakramo'pi vidheya iti siddham |
aparaḥ kalpaḥ—sādyaske śrūyate 
saha paśūnālabhate iti | tasya
somavikāratvātte trayo'pi paśavaḥ somāṅgabhūtā agnīpogīyasabānīyā'nu
bandhyāścodakaprāptāsteṣāṃ tvaprāptaṃ sahatvaṃ vidhīyate | tadvidhānācca prākṛtaḥ
pāṭhakramaḥ bādhito bhavati | savanīyakāle ca teṣāṃ sahabhāvaḥ, parivyāṇā
nantaraṃ ca savanīyakālaḥ 
trivṛtā yūpaṃ parivīya iti vacanāditi sarvamava
[p.305]
gatam | idaṃ tu sandihyate, kimamī savanīyasamaye trayo'pi paśavaḥ prayujya
mānā aniyamena prayoktavyāḥ, āhosvitsavanīyasya parastādagnīṣomīyā'
nubandhyau ? iti | tatra savanīyasamaye trayāṇāṃ sahā'nuṣṭhānaśruteḥ saha
bhāvasya sampādanādarthādāpatati kramaḥ | tanniyame ca parivyāṇā''nantarya
matyantaṃ bādhyate | aniyame tu pakṣe tadastīti nātyantiko bādhaḥ | tasmā
nniyamā'niyamaḥ kramasyā'tra jyāyāniti prāpte punarupadiśyate—
nirjñātaṃ
kramamanurudhyamānaḥ | utpattāveva hi savanīyasya parivyāṇā'nantarakāla
bhāva iva kramo'pi nirjñātaḥ | so'pi balavadbādhakānupanipāte savanīya
śarīrā'bahirbhūto'śakyaḥ parityaktum | nanu sahabhāvavirodho'sya bādhakaḥ,
ata āha—savanīyasthāne sahabhāvavidhānāt savanīyamupasarpataḥ paśū na tu tau
savanīyaḥ | tadautpattikasthānavatā savanīyena saha bhavantau tadavirodhena
svasahabhāvaṃ vyavasthāpayataḥ, na punaḥ savanīyaṃ svasthānāccālayataḥ | tada
virodhenā'pi sahabhāvopapatteḥ | sahabhuvāmevārambhaviṣayatvāt | tadida
mavagataṃ saha paśūnālabhava iti | vidhyavagataṃ sahabhāvagatyantamabādhamāna ekā
rambhaviṣayatvāditi sthānapra masyāpi vidhiviṣayatvamuktam |
tathā'paraḥ kalpaḥ—yatra pradhānadvayaṃ yugapaccoditaṃ yathā 
sārasvatau
bhavata etadvai daivyaṃ mithunam iti | tatra sandehaḥ—kimetayoraṅgānyaniyata
krameṇa prayoktavyāni, uta pradhānaprayogakrameṇa ? iti | tatra pradhānacodanā
'nuvidhānādaṅgacodanānāṃ tasyāśca yugapatpradhānadvayavidhāyinītvena kramā'bhāvāt
pradhānacodanāprayuktā'nuṣṭhānānāmaṅgānāmapi na kramaniyam iti prāpte
punarupasthitam—
nirjñātaṃ kramamanurudhyamānaḥ | strīpuṃsayoḥ sarasvatyāḥ
sarasvataśca khalu yājyānuvākyāyugalayoḥ kramasamāmnānādvā asamāmnāne vā
niyamābhāve'pi yadā yādṛśaḥ pradhānakramo vijñātastamanurudhyamānaḥ prayoga
vidhiraṅgānāmanuṣṭhānaviśeṣaṃ kramaniyamaṃ pratipādayati yatpradhānaṃ prathamaṃ prayujyate
tadaṅgānyapi prathamaṃ prayoktavyānīti |
nanu pradhānabodanā tattatpradhānadvayavidhānātpradhānakramaniyame'pyasamarthā
kimaṅga punastadaṅgānāṃ kramaniyame, ityata āha—svā'vagataṃ ca sahabhāva
matyantamabādhamānaḥ | idamatrā''kūtam | vidhānasya yaugapadyametat na
vidheyasya, aśaktyānuṣṭhānopadeśakatve śabdasyā'prāmāṇyaprasaṅgāt | yathā
śaknuyādityupabandhāt avaśyambhāvini krame pradhānayordvayoranyo'nyaṃ tadaṅgānāṃ
ca pradhānābhyāṃ sahabhāva pratyāsattimātyantikīṃ vidadhat prayogavidhiryāvā
nevā'varjanīyatayā vyavadhirāpatati tāvanmātramanumanyate nā'bhyadhikaṃ vyavadhi
[p.306]
tīti prayogāṅgameva | cintito'sau purastāt | satyam | prayojyeyattā |
aprayojyastukramaḥ prayogaviśeṣa eva | ataḥ pūrveṇā'siddheranuṣṭhānaviśeṣa
cinteyamiti bhidyate lakṣaṇārthaḥ |
tuṣamapi | pradhānakrameṇa cāṅgaprakrameṇa yāvadanujñātaṃ vyavadhānaṃ bhavati |
aniyame tvanujñātā'tiriktaṃ bhavettacca prayogavidhirna mṛṣyati | tataḥ svā'va
gatasahabhāvapīḍā mā pramāṅkṣīditi mukhyakramasahāyaprayogavidherevāṅga
kramaniyamapramāṇamiti aniyame'pi kramo vidheya iti siddham |
tasmāccaturvidhavyāpārāgocaratvāt kramo na codanālakṣaṇa iti
yadākṣiptaṃ tatprayogaviśeṣakatvāt kramasya parihṛtamityupasaṃharati—tasmāt
prayogāṅgameva kramaḥ | nanu prayogāṅgaṃ cet kramaḥ tarhi caturthe prayogacintayaiva
cintita iti nā'dhyāyāntaraṃ prayojayatīti codayati—cintito'sau purastāt |
pariharati—satyam, prayogāṅgaṃ pintitam | kiñcā'sau prayojyā ye
padārthārateṣāmapīyattārūpaḥ prayogāṅgapintitaḥ kramastu na tathetyāha—aprayo
jyastu kramaḥ | avyāpāratvācca yadyaprayojyaḥ kīdṛśastarhi ? ityata āha—
prayogaviśeṣa eva | yathā vā'nena viśiṣyate prayogastathopapāditaṃ purastāt |
upasaṃharati—ataḥ pūrveṇāsiddheranuṣṭhānaviśeṣapinteyam | na caivamephalakṣa
ṇatvamityāha—iti | prayogagateyatāprayogaviśeṣayorbhadāddhetoḥ satyapi prayoga
vidhiviṣayatāyāsturyatve bhidyate lakṣaṇārthaḥ | itarathā tu codanā'rthavidhāra
sāmyena śāstrameko'dhyāyaḥ syāditi | tadanena dvitīyādinā pañcamāntenā
'dhyāyavyūhenotpattiviniyogaprayogarūpavidhyaṃśanirūpaṇapareṇa karttavyatāviṣayaḥ
karma sāṅgamanuṣṭheyaṃ samadhigatam |
athedānīṃ kastasya kartādhikārīti nirūpyate—adhikāro'pi kartṛ
samāhitasādhanātmikāyāḥ karttavyatāyāḥ vidheraṃśa itīha ṣaṣṭhe adhikārāṃśo
nirūpyate vidheḥ | nanu svargakāmapadaśrutereva labdhā svargakāmasya puṃsaḥ kartṛtā
svāmitā ceti kimatra nirūpaṇīyam ? ityata āha—sarvāṅgopasaṃhārasamartha
eva utā'samartho'pīti tadviśeṣaḥ parīkṣyata ityarthaḥ | nanu saṃśayamāpannaḥ
parīkṣāmarhati | na ceha saṃśayaḥ | sāmarthyarahitasyārthino'dhikārasya
mānāntaranirastatvāt | anupalabdhāvyavasthāyāṃ ca saṃśayotpattirityata āha—
śakteḥ śāstrīyatvācchāstrīyāśāstrīyatvābhyām | etaduktaṃ bhavati | svarga
kāmasya karturapekṣitopāyatāmanavagamayat karmaṇaḥ śāstraṃ sāmarthyamantareṇa
kartṛtvā'nupapatteryadi sāmarthyamapi gamayet tatastasyā'pi śāstrā'dhīnajñānatvā
[p.307]
ṣaṣṭhe tvadhikārāṃśo nirūpyate | sarvāṅgopasaṃhārasamarthasyetarasyāpīti
śakteḥ śāstrīyatvācchāstrīyā'śāstrīyatvābhyām | tatra yathaivā'pekṣita
sādhanatvaṃ pravṛttiheturnetarat, tathā śakyatvamapi | śakyatā'pi pravartanātmavidhi
rūpamanupraviśantīṣṭasādhanatāvacchāstragamyā | tathā cā'rthimātrasyeṣṭasādhanam |
śakyaṃ ca śāstrāt pratītaṃ karmma pramāṇāntaravirodhena yathāsambhavaṃ vyava
tiṣṭhata iti sarvādhikāra uktaḥ | pramāṇāntaraviṣayatvāt śavateraprāpteḥ
śāltrasyā'thavattvāt vidhirūpā'nugagatāyā api na śāstrārthatā | vidhistu
darthimātroddeśena ca śāstrapravṛtteraniyamāt sarveṣāmarthināṃ tatkarma śakyamabhimata
sādhanaṃ ceti śāstrāvagamadraḍhimnā devarṣitiryagandhavadhiraśūdrādīnāmapi tatta
daṃśā'nuṣṭhānasāmarthyabādhanācca yo yathā śaknuyāttasya tathā karmābhimatasādhana
miti kalpanāyāgaviśeṣeṇā'dhikāra iti pratibhāti | atha na sāmarthya
śāstragamyamapi tu mānāntarasiddhamupajīvya karturamigatasādhanatāmātramavabodha
yati śāstram, tavastanmānāntaraṃ yamevārthinamupanayati tatraiva śāstraṃ vyavatiṣṭhate,
tadapekṣaṃ hi tadaśāstramiti | tadanena krameṇa śakteḥ śāstrīyatvā'śāstrīyatva
paryavasito vidhāraḥ |
pūrvapakṣaṃ gṛhṇāti—tatra yathaivā'pekṣitasādhanatvaṃ pravṛttihetu netarat tathā
śakyatvamapi | lokata evā'nvayavyatirekābhyāmavagatamiti | śakyatā'pi
pravarttanātmavidhirūpamanupraviśantīṣṭasādhanateva śavayatāyāstadapekṣaṃ śāstrama
pramāṇaṃ syāditi bhāvaḥ | nanu nityabadaṅgāmnānasyā'vikalpatvādaṅgasākalyavat
pradhānasāmarthyamapi vidhigocaraḥ | na ca tatsahasreṇāpi śāstraiḥ tiryagādīn
prati śakyaṃ śavayatāmāpādayitum, ityetannirākartumupasaṃharati—tathā ca—
śāstragamyasamīhitavādhanatā'vacchedyatve niyāmakasya pramāṇasyā'bhāvāt
arthimātrasyeṣṭasādhanam | śakyaṃ ca śāstrāt pratītaṃ karma kevalaṃ kvacit
pramāṇāntaravirodhadarśanādaprāmāṇyabhiyā pramāṇāntaravirodhena yathāsambhavaṃ—
yo yāvanti śaknotyupasaṃharttumaṅgāni tasya tāvadbhirevāṅgaistatpradhānaṃ śakyama
bhimatasādhanamiti vyatatiṣṭhate iti śabdā'vagatā'rthigātradraḍhimnā sarvā'dhikāra
uktaḥ |
siddhāntamupakramate—pramāṇāntaraviṣayātvāttu śakteraprāpteḥ śāstrasyā'rtha
vattvādvidhirūpānugatāyā api na śāstrārthatā | tu śabdātpūrvapakṣaṃ vyāvarttayati
[p.308]
mānāntaragamyaśaktyapekṣaḥ śabdena pratīyate | na caivaṃ sāpekṣatvadoṣaḥ, yathā
mānāntaragocarapadārtharūpā'pekṣitve | anyathā sarvaḥ sarvāṅgopasaṃhārasamarthaḥ
syāt | kuto yathāsambhavaṃ vyavasthā ? pramāṇāntaraviro dhatvāditi cet,
hanta, tarhi śabdasyāprāmāṇyam | lakṣaṇapramāṇāntarasiddho yaḥ padārthasvarūpaviśe
ṣopāyapratipattirananyagocaraḥ śāstraviṣayo'bhyupagamanīyaḥ | tathā ca nāśaktasya
pravṛttiheturakartṛkatvāditi | nā'samathastatra pravartyo gamyata iti samarthā'
dhikāraḥ sati cā'dhikāre sa kasyeti sa eva purastāt pratipāditaḥ | kathaṃ
punastasyā'bhāvaḥ śaṅkitaḥ ? iha kecit |
sādhyatvātkarturākhyātairvyāpārāḥ siddhasādhanāḥ |
prādhānyenābhidhīyante phalenāpi pravarttitāḥ ||
bhavedetadevaṃ yadi śaktiḥ śāstrato'vagamyeta, na tvetadasti | aprāptaprāpaṇaṃ
hi śāstram | na cā'bhimatasādhanatāvatprāmāṇāntareṇā'prāptā śaktiḥ |
tasmādvidhirūpā'nugatāmapi śaviṃta prāptatvānna śāstraṃ gocarayati, kintu tāṃ
mānāntarasiddhāmupajīvya kartṛsamāhitasādhanatāmaprāptāmavagamayatītyāha—
vidhistu mānāntaragamyaśavatyapekṣaḥ śabdena pratīyate | nanvevaṃ sāpekṣatvāda
prāmāṇyaprasaṅgaḥ śāstrasyetyata āha—na caivaṃ sā'pekṣatvadoṣaḥ | yathā
mānāntaragocarapadārtharūpā'pekṣitve svaviṣaye hi mānāntarā'pekṣā prāmāṇyaṃ
vyāhanti na svasāmagryā, atiprasaṅgāt | na ca śakyatā vidhyanugatā'pi
śāstrasya viṣaya ityuktamityarthaḥ | anyathā yadi śaktiviṣayaṃ śāstraṃ syāt
tato niyāmakapramāṇābhāvādarthipadena sahasaiva sarveṣāmarthināmupasthāpanātsarvaḥ
sarvāṅgopasaṃhārasamarthaḥ syāt kuto yathāsambhavaṃ vyavasthā |
pūrvapakṣī śaṅkate—pramāṇāntaravirodhāditi cet | arthipadasamabhivyāhṛ
tena vidhinā sarveṣāṃ tiryagādīnāṃ sāmarthyapratipādanāt tasya ca mānāntara
virodhāt | uttaram—hanta tarhi śabdasyā'prāmāṇyam | lakṣaṇapramāṇāntara
siddho yaḥ padārthasvarūpaviśeṣaḥ śakyatālakṣaṇaḥ tadupāyapratipattiryasya śāstra
viṣayasya so'nanyagocaro'bhyupagamanīyaḥ | tathā ca śakyatāgrāhiṇi niyāmake
pramāṇe sati samarthādhikārasiddhiḥ | yat punarādye vicāritaṃ kimadhikāro'
sti na veti, tasyā'tra saṅgatimāha—sati cā'dhikāre dharmiṇi kasya samartha
syeti tadviśeṣanirūpaṇā ghaṭate nā'nyatheti sa evā'dhikārasadbhāva eva
purastāt pratipāditaḥ | nanu svargakāmo yajetetyevamādibhyaḥ svargakāmādera
dhikāro'vagamyata iti kathaṃ punastasyābhāvaḥ śaṅkito yena'sau vicāraṇīyaḥ
syāt, tatrāha—iha kecit |
[p.309]
kriyāpradhānamekārthaṃ vākyamiti | kriyaiva hi bhāvyate svabhāvasiddhaṃ
dravyaṃ guṇamiti vadantaḥ phalasyā'samanvayaṃ vākye manyante | tathā ca phalā'
bhāvādadhikārā'bhāvaḥ | na hyaphaleṣu karmmasu kaścinniyojyaḥ svāmī vā |
kathaṃ punarasamanvayaḥ phalasya ? karmmaṇaḥ sādhyatvapratīterdvayoḥ sādhyayo
rasamavāyāt | tathā hi | yadi yāgādaya eva kriyāsta eva pūrvā'parībhūtāḥ
pradhānātmanā gamyante | athāpi tadatirekiṇī kriyā sā ca sakarmmikā
bhāvyapradhānā | tathāpi ta eva | kutaḥ ? paurvāparyyaniyamāt | pratyayasya
prakṛtyarthā'bhisambandhisvārthābhidhāyitvātsādhyatvena pratīteḥ | anyathā dravya-
sādhyatvāt kartusakhyātairvyāpārāḥ siddhasādhanāḥ |
prādhānyenā'bhidhīyante phalenāpi pravarttitāḥ ||
vyācaṣṭe—sarvageva hi kriyāprathānamekārthamekaprayojanaṃ vākyaṃ śabdapravṛttya
nusārāt | vastuto'pi kriyaiva hi svabhāvataḥ sādhyarūpā bhāvyate, svabhāvasiddhaṃ
tu dravyaṃ bhūtaṃ bhavyāyeti nyāyāt tāṃ prati guṇabhūtaṃ svargādīti vadantaḥ
phalasyā'samanvayaṃ manyante | phalatvena tu sambandhe svargādestatprādhānyāt
kriyāyā na prādhānyam, ubhayaprādhānye ca ekārthatvaṃ lokā'vagatamapahīyeteti
bhāvaḥ | nanu mā bhūtphalaṃ kiṃ naśchinnam ? ityata āha—tathā ca phalā
bhāvāt adhikārābhāvaḥ | kasmāt ? na hyaphaleṣu karmasu kaścinniyojyaḥ
svāmī vā | tadeva ca tasya svaṃ bhavati yadyasyopakāre vartate | na cā'phalaṃ
tatheti kathaṃ svaṃ bhavati svāminaḥ ? svāmī cā'dhikārītyucyate | na
cā'phalaṃ prayogamapi prekṣāvān pratipadyate na niyojyo'pītyarthaḥ |
nanu bhāvanāpradhānaṃ vākyam, bhāvanāyāśca bhāvyākāṅkṣāyāṃ bhāvyatvena
phalaṃ saṃbhantsyate iti codayati—kathaṃ punarasamanvayaḥ phalasya ? samādhatte—
karmaṇo dhātvarthasya sādhyatvapratīteḥ | atha phalakarmaṇoḥ kasmānna sādhye
karmaṇo ? ityata āha—ubhayoḥ sādhyayorasamavāyāt | ekasmādvākyā
dbhāvayorapi karmaiva sādhyaṃ yuktaṃ, na punaḥ phalamityata āha—tathā hi | yadi
yāgādaya eva kriyāḥ tathāpi ta eva pūrvā'parībhūtāḥ pradhānātmanā gamyante na
tadā bhāvyāntarā'pekṣā'sti | athāpi tadatirekiṇī kriyā sadā sakarmikā,
tasyā bhāvyā'pekṣāyāṃ satyāmapi bhāvyaprādhānyāttathāpi taeva yāgādayo na tu
phalam | nanūbhayabhāvyasambhave ko viśeṣaḥ ? ityāśayavān pṛcchati—kutaḥ ?
paurvāparyaniyamāt | pratyayasya prakṛtyarthā'bhisambandhisvārthā'bhidhāyitvāt
dhātvarthasya ca sādhyatvena pratīteḥ | so'yaṃ phalādviśeṣa ityarthaḥ | anyathā
yadi dhātvartho na sādhyaḥ syāttato dravyaguṇaśabdādaviśeṣāt | siddhārthatvena
[p.310]
guṇaśabdā'viśeṣāt pranabhyupagamācca 
yeṣāmutpattāviti | sākṣātkaraṇā
bhisambandhācca kāṣṭhaiḥ pacatīti | nanu karmmāntarā'bhisambandhānnaivaṃ syāt ?
na | karmmaṇyapi kārakasmṛteḥ kārakatvenaiva kriyā'nvayāt | śabdena bhūtatayo
pādānāt | īpsitatamatvaṃ tu pramāṇāntaraviṣayaḥ | tathā dṛṣṭaphaleṣvastu
phalavattā, yāgādiṣu tu na phalaṃ svargādistathā'samanvayānmānāntarasya
cā'bhāvāt | evaṃ ca tāni dvaidhamityuktam | anyathaikadhyameva syāt |
evaṃ tāvad dravyāṇāṃ karmmasambandhe guṇatvenā'bhisambandho na phalatvena
hi dravyaguṇaśabdā ākhyātātsādhyā'bhidhāyino bhidyante | ākhyātānāṃ
maharṣiṇāṃ yeṣāmutpattau sve prayoge rūpaṃ nopalabhyate tānyākhyātānītyanena
sūtreṇa | na ca tadbhāvanā'bhiprāyaṃ nāmapadārthasya dhātvarthasambandhe dṛṣṭārthatayā
'pekṣitasambandhapratipādanārthatvāt | ataśca sādhyatvaṃ dhātvarthasya sākṣātkaraṇā
'bhisambandhāt kāṣṭhaiḥ pacatīti | na khalvasādhyasya sādhanasambandha upa
padyate | na cā''khyātavācyayāṃ bhāvanāyāṃ karaṇatvaṃ kāṣṭhānāṃ saṃbhavati,
tasyāstvanmate samānapadopātapākakaraṇā'varodhāt | na ca pākā''kṣiptāyāṃ
sambandhaḥ | sākṣātpākasambandhapratīteḥ | na khalu gṛdā ghaṭaḥ kāṣṭheḥ pacatīti
samaḥ sambandhā'vagamaḥ | tasmātkāṣṭheriti sākṣātpākādigambandhāt te eva
svarūpeṇa sādhyā iti sāmpratam |
nanu yatra tarhi kāṣṭherodanaṃ pacatīti karmāntaraṃ pratīyate tatra na dhātvarthasya
sādhyatā syāt, na hyekasminvākye sādhyadvayamupapadyate, iti bodayati—nanu
karmāntarā'bhisambandhānnaivaṃ syāt | samādhatte—na, karmaṇyapi kārakatvasmṛteḥ |
kārakatvenaiva kriyā'nvayāt | ayamabhisandhiḥ | ubhayamastyodanādī kāra
katvamīpsitatamatvaṃ ca | tatra kārakatvenā'nvaye na kriyāsādhyatvahāniriti |
na ca śabdādodanādiḥ pāka iva pacyate ityatra pūrvā'parībhūto'vagamyate, kintu
siddhatayetyāha—svaśabdena bhūtatayopādānāt | nanu yadi na śabdamīpsitata
matvaṃ kutastatpratītiḥ ? ityata āha—īpsitatamatvaṃ tu pramāṇāntaraviṣayaḥ |
tathā ca dṛṣṭaphaleṣu yodanapākādiṣvastu phalavatā, yāgādiṣu punaraśabdārthaḥ
phalaṃ svargādiḥ kutaḥ ? tathā ca phalatvenā'sambandhānna bodanādi tatra bhavitu
marhati | mānāntarasya cā'sambhavāt | evaṃ ca keṣāṃcitkarmaṇāṃ phalānāṃ
sādhyatvena pradhānatvaṃ jyotiṣṭomaprayājādīnām, keṣāṃcicca mānāntarasiddha
phalānāmavadhātādīnāṃ phalaṃ prati guṇatvena tāni dvaidhaṃ guṇapradhānabhūtānītyuktaṃ
maharṣiṇā | anyathā tu sarveṣāṃ phalavattve'bhyupagamyamāne caikarūpameva
syātsarveṣām, phalaṃ prati guṇatvādavadhātādivaditi |
[p.311]
iti yuktam | vastutaśca na sambandhaḥ phalatvena, puruṣaprayatnasya yāgādi
viṣayatvātsvargādyasambandhādyāgādeśca vyāpārāntarā'darśanātkaraṇatvā'nupapatteḥ |
bhūteravyāpāratvāt | śakye ca puruṣaniyogātsvarga kuryādityanupapatteḥ |
avaśyakarttavyatāprasaṅgācca | phalasya cā'vidheyatvopagamāt | yāgādeśca
vidheyatvāśrayaṇāt | pravṛttiviṣaye hi vidhirna sākṣādyāge phale vā | pravṛttiśca
bhāvanā | yadviṣayāyāśca pravṛtterhetuḥ śabdaḥ sa vidhirityucyate | tatrā
khyātopādānā bhāvanā nāstyeva, yā vidhīyeta | satyāmapi bhinnabhāvanāyāṃ
prameyaṃ pariśodhya sūtramatra yojayati—evaṃ tāvat dravyāṇāṃ karmasaṃyoge
guṇatvenā'bhisambandho na phalatvena svargādīnāṃ śabdata iti yuktam | na kevalaṃ
śabdato vastutaśca na sambandhaḥ phalatvena | kutaḥ ? puruṣaprayatnasya bhāvanāyā
yāgādiviṣayatvāddhetoḥ svargādyasambandhāt | na jātu puruṣaḥ svarge vyāpriyamāṇa
upalabhyate, api tu yāge | tataśca yāga evā'sya sādhyo na svargaḥ | atha
puruṣaprayatnasādhyo'pi yāgaḥ phalaṃ prati karaṇaṃ bhaviṣyati, tataḥ sambandha upa
patsyata ityata āha—yāgādeśca vyāpāntarā'darśanāt karaṇatvā'nupapatteḥ |
karaṇatvaṃ hi kriyāyogena vyāptam, sa cā'darśanād yāgādernivṛttaḥ karaṇatva
mapi nivarttayatītyarthaḥ | nanu bhūtireva bhāvyānāṃ yāgādīnāṃ vyāpāraḥ,
tataśca kārakatvaṃ karaṇatvaṃ cetyata āha—bhūteravyāpāratvāt | vyāpāro hi
vyāpāravatsamavāyikāraṇakaḥ, siddhaṃ ca samavāyikāraṇam, asiddhaścedbhāvyo
nā'smin bhūtiḥ samaveti | na khalu gaganakusumādayo vyāpāravantaḥ | siddho
'pi bhavitā na bhavitumarhati | na jātu viyadbhavati | tasmānna bhūtirvyāpāra
iti | api ca bhāvyatve svargasya tatra puruṣo niyojyaḥ | na ca tenā'sau
śakya ityāha—śakye ca puruṣaniyogāt svarga kuryādityanupapatteḥ | api ca
icchātantratve svargādīnām | icchāyāścā'vaśyambhāvaniyamā'bhāvānnā
vaśyakarttavyatāprasaktiḥ | vidhitantratve tvicchāyā atantratvāttasya ca nitya
vidhāyakatvāt avaśyakarttavyatā syādityāha—avaśyakarttavyatāprasaṅgācca |
phalasya cā'vidheyatvopagamāt | tasya lipsā'rthalakṣaṇeti cedevāsau mamai
tatkarttavyamiti | yāgādeśca vidheyatvā'śravaṇāt | yathāha upāyaṃ tu na veda |
atastadākāṅkṣāta idamupadiśyate yāgeneti | yuktaṃ caitadityāha—pravṛttiviṣayo
hi vidhiḥ pravarttanārūpatvānna sākṣād yāge phale vā | pravṛttiśca bhāvanā |
yadviṣayāyāśca pravṛttirhetuḥ śabdaḥ anyanimittatvādicchānimittatvātphale pravṛtte
riti | api ca viṣayarūpāpravṛttirvidhātavyā, na cā'sāvupāyaviṣayāyā
[p.312]
na bhāvanākarmaṇoryāgaphalayorasamavāyaḥ, na vidhisāmarthyāt anyadapi phalam,
tasyāpyevama samavāyāt | vairūpyātsādhanabhāvasya duravabodhatvāt |
nā'rthāpattitaḥ | cīdanālakṣaṇatvahāneḥ | tasyāḥ sādhyatvaparyavasā
nāt | aphalasya karmmaṇo loke sādhyatvā'dṛṣṭeḥ | sādhanatvasya ca sādhyatva
puraḥsaratvādarthāttadākṣepāyogāt | sādhyatvasya purobhāvinaḥ sādhanatvā''
kṣepā'sāmarthyāt | ākṣepe'pi siddhyarthaṃ kartṛrūpasya kāṇatvā'bhāvāt | kathaṃ
anyā phalaviṣayā'stītyāha—dvitīyā ca bhāvanā nāstyeva, yā phalaviṣayā
vidhīyate | bhavatu vā, tathāpi prakṛtaṃ na sidhyatītyata āha—satyāmapi
dvitīyāyāṃ bhāvanāyāṃ na bhāvanākarmaṇoryāgaphalayorasamavāyaḥ | atha mā
bhūd bhinnabhāvanākarmaṇo bhūtena phalena sambandho yāgaśca vidhisāmarthyāttu
viśvajidādivatkalpitena phalāntareṇa saṃbhantsyata ityata āha na vidhi
sāmarthyādanyadapi phalam | kutaḥ ? tasyāpyevaṃ śrutaphalapadāsamavāyāt |
kutaḥ ? vairūpyāddhetoḥ sādhanabhāvasya duravabodhatvāt | na jātu sakṛdeva
śruto yāgaḥ sādhyaṃ sādhanaṃ cetyupapadyate, virodhāt | tasmāt sādhanabhāvo
duravabodhaḥ sādhyatvena śrutasya yāgasyeti |
syādetat | codanātaḥ sādhyatvena patītasya yāgāderarthāpattitaḥ phalaṃ
prati sādhanatvaṃ gamyate, mā bhūdvairūpyam, ityata āha—nārthāpattitaḥ | kutaḥ ?
codanālakṣaṇatvahāneḥ | phalasādhanatā cedarthāpattigamyā codanālakṣaṇatvaṃ na
syāt | tataśca codanaiveti pratijñāvyāghātaḥ | na cā'rthāpatteścodanā
mūlatayā tadviṣayaścodanālakṣaṇa iti sāmpratam | codanāyā yāgādisādhya
mātraparyavasitāyāḥ phalasādhanatāmantareṇopapatterarthāpattigūlatvā'bhāvādityāha—
sādhyatvaparyavasānāt | athā'phalasya sādhyatvaṃ codanāpratītamanupapannamiti
codanaiva sādhyatvapratipādikā'paryavasyanto phalakalpanābījamiti tatrāha—
aphalasya karmaṇo lokasādhyatvenā'dṛṣṭeḥ | yatkila dharmaśāstrakāro niṣedhati
na kurvīta vṛthāceṣṭāmiti | nanu yathā pareṣāṃ sādhanatvena śabdataḥ prati
pannā yāgādayaḥ sādhyatāmākṣipantyātmanaḥ tathā'smanmate'pi sādhyatvena
pratītāḥ sādhanatāmātmana ākṣepsyantītyata āha sādhanatvasya yāgādeḥ
sādhyatvapuraḥsaratvāt | akṛtasya tattvā'nupapatteḥ |
athā'yamākṣepayogaḥ śabdasādhanatvavādino mate | śabdasādhyatvavādināṃ
tu mate sādhyatvasya purobhāvinaḥ sādhanatākṣepā'sāmarthyāt | sugamam | yadi
manyeta sādhyatvamapi siddhiṃ prati kārakatvamantareṇa nopapadyata iti sādhanatva
[p.313]
tarhīṃdānīṃ vidhiḥ ? kimatra katham ? pravarttanāmātraṃ vidhirnna puruṣārthā
narthau spṛśati | kutaḥ ? pratīteravyabhicārācca | tathā ca liṅādibhyaḥ
preraṇā'vagamaḥ | yadyapi ceṣṭasādhanatā pravṛttihetuḥ, śabdastvanāmṛṣṭatadrūpaḥ
pravṛttihetumātramabhiniviśate | tadākārā'vabodhātpraiṣādiṣu ca tadabhāvāt |
pravarttanāmātrasya cā'vyabhicārāt | anekārthatvadoṣāt | na ca tadrūpa
bodhādeva tadbodhaḥ | mohāderapi sambhavāt |
nanvevamapi pravarttanāyāḥ pravarttyaviśeṣaviṣayatvāttasyaiva pūrvamapekṣaṇāt
mākṣepsyatīti, tatrāha—ākṣepe'pi siddhyartha kartṛrūpasya siddhaḥ kāraṇatvā'
bhāvāt | upapāditametat yathā na sādhyaṃ kārakamiti |
abhyupetyocyate, bhavatu kārakam, ata eva cā''kṣipatu tasya tathāpi
siddhiṃ prati kartṛtvāt tadevākṣipenna karaṇatvamityarthaḥ | yadi yāgādīnāṃ
sādhyatvameva na cā'bhimatasādhanatvaṃ kathaṃ tarhīdānī vidhiḥ ? pūrvapakṣī brūte—
kimatra katham ? pravarttanāmātraṃ vidhiḥ, nā'bhimatasādhanatvam, tataśca na
puruṣārthā'narthau saṃspṛśati | kutaḥ ? uttaram—pratīteḥ | nanvabhimata
sādhanatāpi pratīyata ityata āha—avyabhicārācca | pratīteriti vivṛṇoti—
tathā ca liṅādibhyaḥ preraṇā'vagamaḥ | nanvastu, kintvasau samīhitasādhanatā
nibandhana ityata āha—yadyapi ceṣṭasādhanatvaṃ pravṛttihetuḥ śabdastvanāmṛṣṭa
tadrūpaḥ pravṛttihetumātramabhiniviśate | kutaḥ ? tadākārā'vabodhāt | avyabhi
cārāditi vyatirekamukhena vivṛṇoti—praiṣādiṣu ca tadabhāvāt | abhimata
sādhanatvā'bhāvāt | pravarttanāmātrasya cā'vyabhicārāt kvacitpreṣaṇā kva
cidadhyeṣaṇā kvacidabhimataphalasādhanatā liṅartho bhaviṣyati, tatra tatra
pratīterityata āha anekārthatvadoṣāt | na khalu pravarttanāmātratve sambhava
tyanekārthatā yuktā | anekaśaktigauravādityarthaḥ | nanu nā'bhimatasādhanatā
vyabhidharati | abhimatasādhanatā sarvatra praiṣādiṣu sākṣātpāramparyeṇa vā
vidyata ityata āha—na ca tatrā'vabodhādeva abhimatasādhanatā bodhahetu
rityarthaḥ |
atrāntare mīmāṃsakaikadeśī prāha—nanvevamapi pravarttanāyāḥ pravarttya
viśeṣaviṣayatvāttasyaiva pūrvamapekṣaṇāt karaṇādyapekṣayā pūrvatvam | na ca
svargakāmādipadamapekṣāṃ pūrayituṃ na kṣamamityāha—svargakāmādipadānāṃ ca
tatsamarpaṇasāmarthyāt | sa eva pravartya eva taiḥ samarpyate na sādhanākarmaviśeṣaḥ |
karmaṇaḥ paścāttu kartṛtayā sambhantsyata iti bhāvaḥ |
[p.314]
svargakāmādipadānāṃ ca tatsamarpaṇasāmarthyāt sa eva taiḥ samarthyate na sādhana
viśeṣaḥ | kimataḥ ? kāmino'dhikāraḥ pratipanno bhavatīti | evaṃ
nāma, na tu pravṛttisiddhirasati phale | pravṛttyarthaścā'dhikāraḥ | nanvānarthakya
mupadeśasyaivaṃ syāt | satyarthe nā''narthakyamasati tu kimanyat ? ātma
vijñānādhikārasiddhiprayojanatvādvā nā''rthakyam | eṣa khalu puruṣaḥ svabhāvato
rāgādyāviṣṭo dṛṣṭaphalairupāyaiviṣayopārjanaṃ pravarttamānastadāptimanāstatpakṣa
pātī na vigalitaviṣayaprapañcamātmatattvamupadiṣṭaṃ pratyetuṃ paribhāvayituṃ vā
alam | karmmopadeśaistvanuśiṣṭaḥ kṛtakāmanibarhaṇaḥ svābhāvikyāḥ pravṛtte
rnivṛttaḥ śāstrīyāyāṃ karmapravṛttau vyavasthito dāntaḥ kāmairabādhitamanāḥ
pūrvapakṣī vakṣyamāṇā'bhisandhiḥ pṛcchati—kimataḥ ? ekadeśina uttaram—
kāmino'dhikāraḥ pratipanno bhavati | tataśca yajeta svargakāma ityupapannā
saṃgatirityarthaḥ | pūrvapakṣī dūṣayati—na hyaphale'dhikāra ityuktam, abhyupagamyā
'pyucyate—evaṃ nāma, na tu pravṛttisiddhirasati phale | nanu mā bhūtpravṛtiḥ,
adhikārastu sidhyatītyata āha—pravṛtyarthaśnā'dhikāraḥ | pravṛttiścennāsti
vyartho'dhikāra iti | nantānarthakyamupadeśasyaivaṃ syāt | utaram—satyarthe
nā''narthakyam | asati tu kimasyadānarthakyaṃ syāt | athavaivamapyānarthakyaṃ
nāstītyāha—ātmavijñānādhikārasiddhiprayojanatvādvā nā''narthakyam |
kathaṃ punaretatprayojanāḥ sarve vidhayaḥ ? ityata āha—eṣa khalu puruṣaḥ kṣetrajñaḥ
svabhāvato rāgādyāviṣṭaḥ | yathāha bhagavānakṣapādaḥ vītarāgajanmā'darśanā
diti | ato dṛṣṭaphalairupāyaiḥ sevādibhiḥ yadviṣayopārjanastasmiṃstu pravartta
mānastadākṣiptamanāstatpakṣapātī sadaiva tatprayuktaḥ | ato na vigalitaviṣaya
prapañcamātmatattvam upadiṣṭamapi pratyetuṃ niścetuṃ paribhāṣayitu samanaskā
nīndriyāṇi pratyāhṛtyātmanyeva buddhidhārāṃ vidhātumalaṃ, kimaṅga punaḥ
sākṣātkartum |
bhavatvevam, tathāpi kiṃ karmapideśaiḥ ? ityata āha—kamaṃpideśastvanu
śiṣṭastraivarṇṇiko 'ṣṭavarṣādirbrāhmamuhūrttapikramāyāṃ pradoṣaparyavasānāyāṃ prati
vāsaraṃ karmapaddhatau sthito'pi steyadyūtastryabhisaraṇamṛgayādyāhṛtahṛdaya ātmaja
yonijaparijanacintāyāstadanuśāsanāt kṛtakāmanivarhaṇaḥ tataḥ svabhāvikyāḥ
pravṛtternivṛttaḥ parijanacintāyāmiva śāstrīyāyām aharaharbrāhmamuhūrttopakrama
[p.315]
śaknoti tādṛśamapyātmatattvamupadiṣṭaṃ pratyetuṃ paribhāvayituṃ ca | dṛṣṭenaiva
karmmavidhaya ātmajñānādhikāraprayojanāḥ | evaṃ ca śāstratvaṃ niyojyā'nu
śāsanāddhitopadeśādvā | kāme tu sādhye rāgādyabhivyāptāyāṃ svābhāvikyāṃ
pravṛttāvupāyadarśanena svahastadānācchabdaḥ śāstratāmativarttate | na hi tadā
puruṣo'nuśiṣṭaḥ syāt | svacchandaceṣṭāyāṃ tvanujñāyeta | athāpi hita
mupadiśat śāstraṃ tathāpi prakṛtyā duḥkhā'nuṣakteṣu kāmyeṣūpāyadarśanena pravarta
yanna hitopadetī śabdaḥ syāt |
nanvevaṃ jñānavidhirapyanuśāsanamātrameva syāt | na | jñānasya dṛṣṭārthatvāt
svabhāvataśca guṇabhūtatvātsaṃmārgādivat | imameva cārthamamipretya karttavyaśca
pradoṣaparyavasānayāṃ karmapaddhavī vyavasthito dāntaḥ kāmairavādhitamanāḥ śaknoti
tādṛśamapi vigalitaviṣayaprapañcamapi ātmatattvaṃ pratyetuṃ paribhāṣayituṃ ca |
nanu sādhyā kriyā yathā svargādinā sādhyena na sambadhyate tathā ''tmajñānā'dhi
kārasiddhyā 'pi, kaḥ khalvanayārviśeṣaḥ ? ityata āha—evaṃ dṛṣṭenaiva karmavidhaya
ātmajñānā'dhikāraprayojanāḥ | satyam, na śābdaḥ sambandhaḥ pramāṇāntareṇa
tūpanīyamāno na śabdena pratikṣipyate brīhīnavahantītivaditi bhāvaḥ | evaṃ
ca śāstratvaṃ niyojyā'nuśāsanāt pravṛttyantaropadeśena pariṇāmatāpasaṃskāra
duḥkharūṣittābhyaḥ pravṛttibhyo 'rthānnivarttanamanuśāsanaṃ tasmādityarthaḥ |
prakārāntaramāha—hitopadeśādvā | nityānandamayātmajñānā'dhikāra
siddhiprayojanaṃ karmajñānaṃ tasyopadeśādityarthaḥ | itarathā tu na śāstratva
māmnāyasya bhavedityāha—kāme tu kāmyata iti svargādiḥ kāmaḥ tasminsādhye
rāgādyākṣiptāyāṃ svābhāvikyāṃ pravṛttāvupāyadarśanena svahastadānācchabdaḥ
śāstratāmativarttate | icchannapi kathaṃcidupāyā'parijñānānna pravartteta,
upāyajñānādeva pravṛtteḥ | atastādṛśamapi cecchāstraṃ kimanyadaśāstraṃ
bhaviṣyatītyarthaḥ | śāstratāṃ kasmādativarttate ? ityata āha—na hi tadā
puruṣo'nuśiṣṭaḥ syāt | svacchandaceṣṭāyāṃ tvanujñāyeta | evaṃ tāvadahita
nivarttanālakṣaṇaṃ śāstratvaṃ na syādityuktam | athāpi hitamupadiśacchāstraṃ
tathāpi pravṛttyā duḥkhānuṣakteṣu kāmyeṣu svargādiṣūpāyadaśanena pravarttayanna hito
padeśo śabdaḥ syāt |
codayati—nanvevaṃ jñānavidhirapyanuśāsanameva syāt | na hi tatrāpi
svargadivanmokṣaḥ sādhyatayā sambandhumarhati | nāpi mokṣamāṇo 'dhikārītyarthaḥ |
pariharati—na, jñānasya dṛṣṭārthatvāt | taccoktaṃ purastātsvabhāvataśca
[p.316]
sukhavānakarttavyo duḥkhavānityādyuktam | eṣo 'rthaḥ | svagāṃdeḥ kṣaṇikatvā
diduḥkhānuṣaṅgāttadupāyo yāgo duḥkhatayā na karttavya eva syāt | pralīnā'
śeṣaduḥkhaprapañcā'vabhāsaprayojanastu sukhaphalaḥ karttavyaḥ |
nanvevaṃ varṇayatā 'tisṛṣṭa evādhikāro bhavati | satyam | nānyastu
nirūpyate, nā'bhyupagamaḥ | na ca yāgaṃ kuryyādityarthe yathā 'dhikārastatho
pariṣṭādvakṣyate |
atrocyate—
viśeṣā eva śabdārthā dṛṣṭarūpānvaye'pi hi |
na ca pravarttanābodha iṣṭaśaktervinā'rthavān ||
guṇabhūtatvātsaṃmārgādivat | yathā saṃmṛjyamānapadārthatantraḥ saṃmārgaḥ tathā
jñeyatantraṃ vijñānamityetadanubhavasiddhametadityarthaḥ | imameva cārthamabhipretya
karttavyaśca sukhavān akarttavyaśca duḥkhavān ityādyuktamatrabhavatā | nanu
svargādinarakādiphalatve'pi karttavyatākartavyatayorvacanametad bhāṣyakārīya
mupapadyata evetyata āha—eṣorthaḥ | svargādeḥ kṣaṇikatvādiduḥkhānuṣaṅgātta
dupāyo yāgo duḥkhatayā na karttavya eva syāt | pralīnā'śeṣaduḥkhaprapañcā
'vabhāsaprayojanastu sukhaphalaḥ karttavyaḥ |
codayati—nanvevaṃ pāramparyeṇa phalatvāt karmaṇi nā'dhikārā'bhāva
aikāntika ityāha—nanvevaṃ varṇṇayatā'tisṛṣṭa evā'dhikāro bhavati | pari
harati—satyam, nyāyastu nirūpyate nā'bhyupagamaḥ | na vayamabhyupagamamātreṇā
'dhikārā'bhāvaṃ brūmaḥ, api tu nyāyataḥ | sa tu svato niradhikāratvamāpāda
yati karmavidhīnām, nādhikārāntarā'nupraveśaṃ vārayati, tena tadanusāriṇāṃ
taddṛṣṭa evābhyupagamo yujyata ityarthaḥ |
evaṃ pūrvapakṣā''bhāsamupanyasya prathamamevā''narthakyaṃ svā'bhimatamāha—na
ca yāgaṃ kuryādityacā''rtho yathā'dhikārastathopariṣṭādvakṣyate | evaṃ pūrvapakṣa
yitvā siddhāntamāha—ucyate |
viśeṣā eva śabdārthā dṛṣṭarūpānvaye'pi hi |
na ca pravarttanābodha iṣṭaśaktervinā'rthavān ||
[p.317]
na tāvatsāmānyaṃ liṅādyarthaḥ | preṣaṇādhyeṣaṇā'bhyanujñopadeśānāṃ tu
viśeṣāṇāmeva loke pratīteḥ | mohādibhedāntarā'vagateśca | na hi pravṛtti
hetumātrā'vabodhaḥ kvacidasti | sāmānyaśabdādviśeṣapramāṇā'bhāve tanmā
trā'vagatiḥ | ato na tāvanmātraṃ śabdārthaḥ | viśeṣā'nuṣaktameva tu tṛnnā
dīnā miva kartṛtvaṃ tāccholyā'nuṣaktama | anyathā kartṛprayogapratīti
prasaṅgāt | nāpyupādhayo na śabdārthāḥ | apratītānāmabhedakatvāt | anyasya
pravarttanāmātraṃ śabdārtha iti hi manyamānena svargādeḥ sādhyatvaṃ nirāsīti
tadeva tāvad dūṣayati—na tāvatsāmānyaṃ liṅādyarthaḥ | preṣaṇā'dhyeṣaṇā'bhyanu
jñopadeśānāṃ viśeṣaṇāmeva loke pratīteḥ | na khalu liṅa upadeśādiviśeṣa
rahitaṃ pravarttanāmātramavagacchanti | na ca tathā na gauḥ padā spraṣṭavyeti
gotvamātramapi tu arthādiśabdādivā'bhidheyeṣu sandihate | pratipadyamānāstu
tāmupadeśā'nuraktāmeva nā'vagamayanti | na hi jvaritaḥ pathyamaśnīyādityu
padeśapravṛttā pravarttanāmātrā'vagatiḥ tatpūrvā copadeśe pratipattiriti | yadi
pravarttanaiva liṅarthaḥ tadā''kṣepeṇa tadviśeṣāḥ pratīyeran, tata upadeśādibhedavat,
ktravinmohasyāpi cetyavandanādī pravṛttihetutvaprasaṅga ityāha—mohādi
bhedāntarā'vagateśca |
nanu pratīyatāṃ nāma viśeṣā'nuraktā pravarttanā, tathāpi kasmānna tanmātra
marthaḥ ? ityata āha—na hi pravṛttihetumātrā'vabodhaḥ kvacidasti |
sāmānyaśabdatvaṃ hi viśeṣapramāṇā'bhāve gṛhītasambandhasya sāmānyamātrā''va
bodhakatvena vyāptam, tadihoktena krameṇa sāmānyamātrā'vabodhakatvaṃ nivarttamānaṃ
svavyāpyaṃ sāmānyaśabdatāmapi liṅo nivarttayati | tadidamāha—sāmānya
śabdādviśeṣapramāṇā'bhāve tanmātrā'vagatiḥ | kvacicca sāmānyā'vagati
puraḥsaraiva kasyacit viśeṣasya bādhe sati viśeṣā'vagatiḥ | sarvathā
tanmātrā'vagatistāvadasti | upasaṃharati ato na tanmātraṃ śabdārthaḥ |
viśeṣānuṣaktameva tu tṛnnādīnāmiva kartṛtvaṃ tācchīlyā'nupaktam | na cāyama
siddho dṛṣṭānta ityata āha anyathā tācchīlyādayo bhedā yadi nocyeran
kartṛtvamevocyeta, tathā sati tṛjakādivat kartṛmātre prayogaprasaṅgāt | yadapi
kaiściducyate praiṣādayo na śabdārthā upādhayaste, pravarttakatvaṃ tu śabdārthaḥ sarvatrā'
parityāgāditi, tatrāha—nāpyupādhayo na śabdārthā upadeśādayaḥ | kutaḥ ?
apratītānāmabhedakatvāt | upādhitvameṣāmabhyupagacchadbhirapi bhedakatvamabhyu
[p.318]
pratyayahetorabhāvāt | tadantatvāttadvācyaḥ samānaśabdo'yam iti ca
smaraṇāt | gotdādīnāmapyupādhitvāt | sadityeva śabdārthatvaprasaṅgāt |
upetyāpi tūcyate | sāmānyacodanāyā viśeṣaparatvādviśeṣa eva prati-
pagamanīyam, tadabhāve upādhitvā'nupapatteḥ | na cā'gṛhītaṃ bhedakaṃ bhavitumarhati |
na hyagṛhītaviśeṣaṇā buddhirudīyate | nanūpahitābhidhānamupādhipratītimapekṣate,
na tu svapratipādyatāmapyasyetyata āha—anyasya pratyayahetorabhāvāt | upa
hitavācitevā'rthātpratipādyata ityarthaḥ | na kevalamupapattiḥ smaraṇamapi
kātīyamatrāstītyāha—tadantatvāttadvācyaḥ samānaśabdo'yam iti smaraṇāt |
dvividho'pyupādhirupahitasamānādhikaraṇastadvyadhikakaraṇaśca | tadyathā | dṛti
hariḥ paśuḥ, gārgikayā ślāghate iti ca | tatra yaḥ samānaśabdaḥ sa samānā
dhikaraṇopādhiḥ sa tadantavācyaḥ pratyayāntavācyo dṛtiharipaśvādiḥ | na
tvasamānaśabdo'samānādhikaraṇo gārgikayā ślāghate ityādiḥ | na hi
gārgikayeti ślādhādyadhikāravihite buni tadantenā'sagānādhikaraṇaḥ, gārgi
kayā ślādhā'bhidhīyata ityarthaḥ |
syādetat | ananyalabhyaḥ śabdārtha iti sthitiḥ | upādhayaścā'nyato
labhyā na vācyā bhavitumarhanti | kutaḥ ? prayogādeva laukikāt | tathā
hi | dṛtihariśabdaṃ paśāveva prayuñjate vṛddhāḥ nānyatra | tatprayoganiyama eva
kutaḥ ? iti cet | na | anāditvena lokaprayogasyā'paryanuyojyatvāt |
tasmāttaṭasthā evopādhayo na punarabhidhāviniveśina ityata āha—gotvādī
nāmapyupādhikatvāt | sadityeva śabdārthatvaprasaṅgāt | yadyaparyanuyojyādeva
laukikātprayoganiyamādavagateraśabdārthatvam, hanta gotvādīnāmapyaśabdārthatvaṃ
syāt, sattaiva gavādiśabdānāmarthaḥ prasajyeta | sattāmātrapravarttināmapi teṣāṃ
prayoganiyamādeva gotvādiṣūpādhisiddheḥ | tathā cā'nyāyyamanekārthatvaṃ
tanna bhaviṣyati | na vā sattāmapyabhidadhyuḥ, prayoganiyamādeva sāpi
gaṃsyate, tataśca sarveṣāmeva śabdānāmavācakatvaprasaṅgaḥ | anādirapi vṛddha
prayogo'rthapratyayanārthatvāt tatprayuktastadapratyāyane paryanuyojyaḥ | na hi
prayoktavyaḥ śabda ityeva prayuñjate vṛddhāḥ, api tvartha pratyāyayitum | tad
gotvādiṣu gavādiśabdaprayoganiyamastadabhidhāyakatvādupapanno nā'nyatheti |
tarhi samānametat upādhiṣu sarveṣvatyatrā'bhiniveśāt | na ca liṅādiṣu
prayoganiyamo'pyasti | etena paṅkajādiṣu yogarūḍhirūpapāditā | na cā'
samānādhikaraṇeṣūpādhiṣu vācyatvaprasaṅgaḥ | tatra ślāghādivācināṃ śabdā
ntarāṇāṃ sadaiva prayogaprasaṅgāt | na hi gārgikayetyetāvanmātraṃ prayuñjate
[p.319]
pādyaḥ | sa ca yaḥ kaścitsarvo vā kaścideva vā | sarvathā tu viśeṣapratyasta
mayena sāmānyabodho 'rthavān | tatreṣṭatacchaktyanapekṣāṇāmabhiprāyamātrā'nu
vidhāyināṃ tadapekṣāṇāṃ ca tadānukūlyamapekṣamāṇānāṃ pravṛttihetuvaḥ preṣaṇā'
dhyeṣaṇā'bhyanujñā apuruṣabuddhiprabhavāyā na pratipādyāḥ | na mohādayo bodhaviro
dhāt | te hi pratipādyamānā apravṛttihetava iti pravarttanā'vabodho mohapratipāda
nāt vihanyeta | viparyyaya eva syāt | neṣṭatā karmmaṇi, pratyakṣavirodhāt |
iṣṭasādhanatāmapi cenna gamayedapratipādakataiva syāt | sāmānyabodhasya viśeṣa
pratyastamaye'narthakatvānmithyātvāpātāt | tasya ca hetudvayābhāve 'yuktatvāt |
vṛddhāḥ, tanmātrādvā ślāghāṃ pratipadyante | tasmādupadeśādayastacchīlādayaśco
pādhayo vācyāḥ | anyathātiprasaṅgādityabhyupetyāpi pravarttanāmātrā'bhidhāna
mucyate | sāmānyacodanāyā viśeṣaparatvādviśeṣa etra pratipādyaḥ | sa ca yaḥ
kaścidyathā sthālyāmodanaṃ pacediti kācit sthālī sarvo vā, aviśeṣāt |
yathā brāhmaṇo na hantavya iti brāhmaṇamātram | kaścideva yathā puruṣamānaya,
surā boḍhavyeti śūdrapratipattiḥ | sarvathā viśeṣapratyastamayena sāmānyabodho
'rthavān | nirviśeṣasya sāmānyasyā'nupapatteḥ | tadevaṃ prasaktā''jñādivyu
dāsena anyatra ca mohādaprasakterupadeśasyaiva śiṣyamāṇasya sapratyaya ityāha—
tatreṣṭatacchatatyanapekṣāṇāṃ preṣituradhyeṣiturbā'bhiprāyamātrā'nuvidhāyināṃ preṣyā
ṇām adhyepyāṇaṃ vā'vadapekṣāṇāṃ ca tadānukūlyamapekṣamāṇānāmanujñātramātrā
bhiprāyajñānāṃ pravṛttihetavaḥ preṣaṇādhyeṣaṇā'bhyanujñā apuruṣaprabhāvāyāścodanāyā
na pratipādyāḥ | mohādīnāmaprasaktimāha—na mohādayaḥ pravṛttihetavaḥ |
kutaḥ ? bodhavirodhāt | virodhamāha—te hi pratipādyamānā apravṛtti
hetava iti pravarttanābodho mohapratipādanādvihanyeta, viparyaya eva syāt |
nivṛttireva prasajyeta, na jātu prāmāṇikāścaityavandanādau māyāmohavini
rmāṇe'vagate pravartante pravṛttā vā na nivarttante | astu tarhīṣṭasādhanatā pravṛtti
heturityata āha—neṣṭasādhanatā karmaṇi pravṛttihetuḥ | kutaḥ ? pratyakṣa
virodhāt | iṣṭasādhanatāmapi cenna gamayedapratipādakataiva syāt | pratyakṣa
viśeṣapratyastamaye sāmānyasyāpyabhāvādanarthakatvānnirviṣayatvānmithyātvā''
pātāt | bhavatu mithyātvam, ko virodhaḥ ? ityata āha tasya ca hetudvayā
'bhāve kāraṇadoṣabādhakāpratyayābhāve'yuktatvāt | atra ca pāramarṣa sūtraṃ
yojayati—asādhakaṃ tu tādarthyāditi | tadvyācaṣṭe—bhavedasādhakaṃ karma
sādhayitāraṃ nādhigacchet | anadhikāri syāt ityarthaḥ | atraiva hetumāha—
tādarthyāt | vivṛṇoti—pravarttanāmātrārthatvāt vidhiśabdasya prītisādhana
tvā'navagamāt | bhavatvanavagamaḥ, tathāpi kimityasādhakaṃ karma ? ityata
[p.320]
taduktam | asādhakaṃ tu tādarthyāditi | bhavedasādhakaṃ karmma sādhayitāraṃ
nā'dhigacchedanadhikārī syādityarthaḥ | tādārthyātpravarttanāmātrārthatvāt |
śabdasya prītisādhanatvā'navagamāt | prītyarthatvācca puruṣaprayatnānām | aviśeṣe
tu prītisādhanatve siddho'dhikāraḥ | sarvaviśeṣapratyastasamaye cā'sādhakaṃ
vākyaṃ syāt abodhakam | aviśeṣārdhatvātsāmānyacodanāyāḥ |
nanūktaṃ prajñāyate liṅādīnāṃ vyañjanīyā pravarttanā prayoktṛdharma iti |
satyam | ājñādiko nopadeśaḥ | nanūpadeśo'pyanugrahā'bhiprāyātmaka iti
na prayoktṛdharmmatāmativarttate |
naitatsāram | upadeśe praiṣādiṣviva pravṛttihetutvābhāvādabhiprāyasya |
tathā hi—
ājñādau karmmaśakterhi na 'bhiprāyānurodhitā |
upadeśe karmmaśaktimavabudhya pravarttate ||
ājñā'bhyarthanayorhi karmmaṇaḥ sāmarthyamanapekṣamāṇastadviśeṣamanādriya-
āha—prītyarthatvācca puruṣaprayatnānām | prītyabhāvādicchābhāvaḥ sukhā'nu
śāyitvādicchāyāstadabhāve prayatnā'bhāvaḥ, vahnyabhāvaiva dhūmābhāva iti
sāmānyavacanam | aviśeṣe tu prītisādhanatve siddho'dhikāraḥ, sarvaviśeṣa
pratyastamaye 'sādhakaṃ vākyaṃ syādabodhakam, anavabodhakam | kutaḥ ? avi
śeṣārthatvāt sāmānyacodanāyā nirviśeṣasya ca sāmānyasyā'yogānnirviṣa
yatvā''patteḥ | atrāntare pūrvoktamākṣepaṃ smārayati—nanūktamiti | prajñāyate
liṅādonāṃ vyañjanīyā pravarttanā | prayoktṛdharmo na ṇico yathāniyatakartṛ
keti | prayoktuścodanāyāmasaṃbhavānnāsti pravarttanetyarthaḥ | tatrāṃśe siddha
sādhanatāmāha—satyam, ājñādikaḥ prayoktṛdharmo vede nāstyeva, na tūpadeśaḥ |
codayati—nanūpadeśo'pyanugrahā'bhiprāyātmaka iti na prayoktṛdharmatāmati
vartate | tathā hi | maharṣayaḥ svārthamanapekṣamāṇāḥ pareṣāṃ hitā'hitamāpti
parihāralakṣaṇā'nugrahābhiprāyā upadeśaṃ praṇayantītyupadeśo'pi prayoktṛdharma
evetyarthaḥ | pariharati—naitatsāram | kutaḥ ? upadeśe praiṣādiṣviva
pravṛttihetutvā'bhāvāt abhiprāyasya | tathā hi |
ājñādau karmaśakterhi nā'bhiprāyā'nurodhitā |
upadeśe karmaśaktimavabudhya pravarttate ||
ājñābhyarthanayorhi karma svasamīhitasiddhiṃ prati sāmarthyamanapekṣamāṇo
[p.321]
tvam | sarvaśabdānāṃ jñātārthatvaprasaṅgāt |
nanvevaṃ prameyaiva syāccodanā, pramāṇaṃ ceṣyate, codanālakṣaṇo'rtho dharma
ityabhidhānāt | tasmācchabdena pramāṇātmanā'bhidheyaḥ pravṛttipratipatti
kāryonneyaḥ kriyāviṣayaḥ pratipattṛsthaḥ svayaṃsaṃvedyaḥ pravuttiheturathaṃścodanā |
'ta eva tadviśeṣaṃ tasya karmaṇaḥ prayojanaviṣayaṃ viśeṣamanādriyamāṇaḥ tadabhi
prāyā'nurodhādeva kiṃcitsamīhamānastuṣṭo 'yamājñāpayitā kiṃcidabhimataṃ
me kariṣyatīti samīhamāno'bhiprāyānurodhī pravartate | anujñāne'pi yadyapi
kriyāphalāt pravṛttiḥ tathāpyabhiprāyā''nukūlyamapekṣate | upadeśasyājñādibhyo
'bhiprāyānanurodhaṃ viśeṣamāha—upapadeśe tu satyapyanugrahā'bhiprāye karmaṇaḥ
samīhitaśaktyādyabhāve na pravarttate | karmaśaktiṃ tvavabudhyopadeṣṭurapratārakatva
mātreṇa vipralambhanimittā'bhāvamātreṇa hetunā satyārthādvacanādasatyapyanupahā
'bhiprāye vaktuḥ pravarttate | tasmādupadeśapravṛtteḥ pravṛttiviṣayasamīhitasā
dhanatvabhāvānuvidhānādabhiprāyā'nanuvidhānācca nā'nugrahā'bhiprāyaḥ śabdārtho
'bhimatasādhanatayā tu karmaṇaḥ śabdārtha iti sāmpratam |
śaṅkate—tatpratipādanamabhimatasādhanatāpratipādanaṃ nā'nugrahā'bhiprāya
mantareṇa, tasmādabhiprāyaḥ śabdārtha iti cet | nirākaroti—na, madhyastha
syāpi praśnādidarśanāt | prāyeṇa khalu variṃvasitopasanno jijñāsuḥ pṛcchanna
nugrahā'bhiprāyeṇā''cāryeṇopadiśyate | madhyasthena tu jijñāsunā pṛṣṭasya
madhyasyasyopadeṣṭurupadeśadarśanād vyabhicāra ityupadeśo nā'nugrahā'bhiprāyaḥ
iti | mā bhūd vyabhivāraḥ, tathāpi nā'bhiprāyārthatā | na hi yaṃ yaṃ vino
padeśo na bhavati sa sarva upadeśārtho'pi tvananyalabhyaḥ | anyathā sarveṣāmeva
śabdānāmarthajñānā'dhīnaniṣpatitvāt jñānarthatā syādityāha—na caiva tāvatā
śabdārthatvaprasaṅgaḥ |
atha codayati—nanvevaṃ sati samīhitasādhanatāyāḥ pravarttanātve pravarttanaiva
codaneti tasyāḥ śabdārthatvātprameyaiva syāccodanā, pramāṇaṃ ceṣyate, codanā
lakṣaṇo'rtho dharma ityabhidhānāt | upasaṃhāravyājena pūrvapakṣī svapakṣamāha—
tasmācchabdena pramāṇātmanā'bhidheyaḥ pravṛttipratipattikāryonneyaḥ kriyāviṣayaḥ
pratipattṛsthaḥ svasaṃvedyaḥ pravṛttiheturarthaścodanā pramāṇātmaneti na mama pakṣe
pratijñāvyāghātaḥ | pramāṇātmanaiva śabdenā'bhidhānāccodanāyāḥ pravṛttau yā
pratipattiḥ saiva kāryaṃ tadunneya iti sambandhagrahaṇasambhavaḥ kriyāviṣaya iti
kriyāyāmasya pravarttakatvamupapannam | pratipattṛstha iti | pratipattustannimitta
gocarapravṛttipratyayādhāratvāt | svasaṃvedya iti | śabdaprabhavāyāstadālamba
[p.322]
vārttametat | pravttihetorvākyasya codanātvābhidhānāccodaneti
kriyāyāḥ pravarttakaṃ vacanamiti | yatra cā'sya prāmāṇyaṃ śabdasyaiva
tatrāstu kimanenāntargaḍunā ? na hi tatra śabdasya kaścidvirodhaḥ |
padārthāntaravat prāmāṇyamastu, vyartha viśeṣaṇā'bhidhānam | kva
cā'sya prāmāṇyam ? viṣaye cet | na, pramāṇāntarasiddheḥ | apūrda
syārpi ca saṃsargasya lokavattadabhāve'pi pratipatteḥ | adṛṣṭasādhanatve cet |
śabdasyaivāstu | śabdakāryyatve cā'vyutpannaśabdapravṛttiḥ | śabdajñāpyatvamanya
hetukasyā'yuktamityuktam | anumānaṃ ca na svārthapratipattyā | tasyā
nāyāḥ pratipatteḥ svasaṃvedyatvātsā'pi tathetyarthaḥ | dūṣayati—vārttametat |
kutaḥ ? pravṛttihetorvākyasya codanātvā'bhidhānāt | codaneti kriyāyāḥ
pravarttakaṃ vacanamiti vacanā'bhidheyā codanā vacanasya codanātvābhidhānaṃ
bhāṣyakarīyaṃ virudhyata ityarthaḥ | api ca yatra cāsya prāmāṇyamabhidheyasyeṣyate
śabdasyaiva tatrāstu, kimantargaḍunā pramāṇarahitenā'nena ? śabdasyaiva pramāyāṃ
śaktatvāt | tasya tu svarūpeṇāpyapratīyamānatvāt | na hi tatra śabdasya
kaścidvirodhaḥ | yadyucyeta prāmāṇyena śabdā'bhihitasya svākāṅkṣāvaśā
nniyojyā sādhaneti karttavyatā saṃsargāva'gatirnā'nyatheti pramāṇātmanā sā
tvabhidheya eṣitavyetyata āha—padārthāntaravata prāmāṇyamastu, vyartha viśeṣā'
bhidhānam | tathaiva hi kriyā svapadena pramāṇatvenā'nabhihitā'pyākāṅkṣā
vaśātkārakeṇa yogyena saṃnihitenā''tmanaḥ sambandhamavagamayati evaṃ kārakamapi
kriyāyāḥ sthālyāmodanaṃ pacati caitra ityādau, tathā'pamapi gamayiṣyatīti
vyartha pramāṇatvaviśeṣā'bhidhānamityarthaḥ |
evaṃ tāvadavicāritasiddhaṃ viṣayaṃ kṛtvoktam, saṃprati viṣayaṃ vicārayati—
kva cāsya prāmāṇyam ? śaṅkate—viṣaye yāgādau cet | nirākaroti—
na pramāṇāntarasiddheḥ | atha padārthasaṃsarge pramāṇāntarādṛṣṭe, tatrāha—
apūrvasyāpi saṃsargasya tadabhāve lokavatpratīteḥ | loke sthālyāmodanaṃ
pacatīti kriyākārakasaṃsargasya tadabhāve'pi bhavadabhimatacodanārthābhāve'pi
pratipatteḥ | adṛṣṭasādhanatve yāgādīnāṃ śreyaḥ sādhanatve cet | nirākaroti—
śabdasyaivāstu | api cā'rthabhedaḥ śabdena kriyate jñāpyate vā | śabdakāryatve
avyutpannaśabdapravṛttiḥ | śabdajñāpyatvamanyahetukasyā'nyapramāṇakasyāpya
[p.323]
aliṅgatdāt | liṅgaphalatvāt | anavasthānāt | na ca viṣayapratipattyā,
viṣayaśabdebhyastatsiddheḥ | pravṛttyā'pi nātmīyayā | tadabhāvāditaretarā
śrayāt | pravṛttimeye śabdavaiyarthyam | śabdameye vṛthā 'numānam | parapravṛttyā
'nubhitādviditaśabdasaṅgateḥ svayaṃśabdādavabudhya pravarttata iti nobhayavaiyarthya
miti cet | na | parapravṛttyā pravṛttihetumātrā'vagamādviśeṣā'nirdhāraṇāttada-
yuktamityuktam | adṛṣṭavattve yāgādīnāṃ sambandhasaṃvedanā'saṃbhavāt | yadyu
cyeta anumānacodanākhyamarthabhedaṃ gṛhītvā tatra śabdasya sambandho grahīṣyata iti |
tatrāha—anumānaṃ ca na svārthapratipattyā gṛhītasambandham | liṅgadarśanajaṃ
khalvanumānaṃ bhavati, tataśca na pratītirbhavitumarhati | tasyā aliṅgatvāt |
pramāṇaphalayoścaikatvā'nupapatteḥ | atha svapratipattyaikayā liṅgabhūtayā
pratipadya pratipattyantaraṃ codanāviṣayaṃ janayiṣyati, tato bhedāśraye pramāṇaphalatve
upapatsyete ityata āha—anavasthānāt | liṅgabhūtāyāḥ pratipatterapi
kāraṇāntarā'bhāvāt svātmapratipattyantarameva kāraṇaṃ vācyam | evaṃ ca
tasyā apyātmapratipattyantaramityanavasthetyarthaḥ |
yadyucyeta bhāvārthaviṣayā pratipattirliṅgamasya bhaviṣyatīti, tatrāha—
na viṣayapratipattyā codanānumānam | kutaḥ ? viṣayaśabdebhyastatsiddheḥ |
bhāvārtharūpaviṣayapratipattisiddheḥ | ayamabhisandhiḥ | na tāvaddṛṣṭasvalakṣaṇa
metadanumānam | na hi viṣayapratipattyā kvacidapi codanā dṛṣṭacarī |
kasmāt ? sāmānyatodṛṣṭamanumānamindriyādivadrūpādyupalabdhiliṅgakameṣitavyaṃ
bhavatu, taccā'nupapannam | viṣayaśabdebhya eva viṣayapratipattilakṣaṇasya
kāryasyopapatteriti | pravṛtteriti cet, sā 'pyātmīyā parakīyā vā syāt,
tatra prathamaṃ kalpamapākaroti—pravṛttyānātmīyayā liṅgena codanā'numānam |
kutaḥ ? nimittā'bhāvena tadabhāvāt | codanā'vagamanimittatve vā itare
tarāśrayāt | atha pravṛttyanimittāntarajanmanaiva so'rtho'numīyate, tathā ca
netaretarāśrayam | tatrāha—pravṛttimeye vṛthā'numānam | parapravṛttyā'numitād |
viditaśabdasaṅgateḥ svayaṃśabdādavabudhya pravarttataiti nobhayavaiyarthyamiti cet |
śabdasya ca pravṛttiliṅgasya cā'numānasya ca na vaiyarthyamiti cet | nirā
karoti—na, parapravṛttyā kāryeṇa pravṛttihetumātrāvagamāt viśeṣā'nirdhāraṇāt
tanmātrābhidhānapratīteḥ | bhavatu tanmātrapratītiḥ, kiṃ naśchinnam ? ityata
āha—tanmātrapratītyā pravṛttyayogāt | yadi hi svātmani pravṛttihetuviśeṣaḥ
kaścitpratītaḥ syāt sa eva pravṛttidarśanātparatrāpyanumīyate | na ca vidita
saṅgateḥ, śabdāttamavagamya pravṛttirupapadyeta, paramapravṛttimātraliṅgake tasmiṃstaddhetu
[p.324]
bhidhānapratīteḥ | tanmātrapratipattyā pravṛttyayogāt | na hi sarvaḥ sarvasya
pravṛttihetuḥ | ananyahetukasvabhāvatvācca | śabdapūrvayaiva pravṛttyā'numāna
hetukamiti cet | anumānapūrvakameva śabdātpratīteḥ kiṃ nā'numānapramāṇaka
eva ?
api ca nā'dṛṣṭātiriktaḥ pravṛttiheturanumīyate | tasya pūrvasiddhya
pekṣatvāt pravṛtteḥ dṛṣṭairapi hetubhimastadunapatteḥ | pratipatrāśritaiśca yaiḥ
pratyayaviśeṣa āśritaḥ tairālambanaṃ vācyam | anyapramāṇakatvaṃ ca samartha-
tvamātreṇā'numānāt | svarūpā'navagaterna pravṛttihetutā śabdā'vagatasyāpi
bhavitumarhatītyarthaḥ | atha sāmānyamātreṇā'vagataḥ pravṛttihetuḥ kasmānna bhavati ?
ityata āha—na hi sarvasya sarvaḥ pravṛttihetuḥ | na khalvājñādayo viśeṣāḥ sarve
sarvaṃ pravarttayanti, api tu kaścideva tadviśeṣāvasāya eva pravṛttiheturna sāmānya
mātram | tasya sarvatrā'viśeṣeṇa sarveṣāmājñādīnāṃ sarvānprati pravarttakatva
prasaṅgādityarthaḥ | api ca sambandhasaṃvedanā'dhīnapravṛttitvācchabdasya mānā
ntareṇa ca sambandhagrahaṇamantareṇa tadanupapatte | mananyahetukasvabhāvattrācca |
śaṅkate—śabdapūrvayaiva pravṛttyā'numānāt śabdahetuka iti cet | nirā
karoti—anumānapūrvameva śabdāt pratīteḥ kiṃ nānumānapramāṇaka eva |
ayamabhisandhiḥ | yatrārthe yena pratipatrā gṛhītasaṅgatiḥ śabdastameva pratipattāraṃ
tamarthamavagamayati | sa ca sambandhā'vadhāraṇasamayapravṛttaṃ tadarthaviṣayamātmanaḥ
pramāṇaṃ purodhāya pratipattā śabdādarthamavagacchatīti smārakaṃ manyate param |
tadiha yadyanumānāt gṛhītvā pravṛttihetuṃ sambandhinaṃ gṛhītavān nūnamasyā
