apyartho'numānameva purodhāya pratipattavyaḥ syāt | tathā ca tadanuvādatā
liṅaḥ | na ca liṅārthā'vagatinimittapravṛttihetukamanumānaṃ nimittamavagamaṃ
purodhāya varttata iti sāmpratam | pratipatrantarasthaṃ hi tat | na ca pravṛttya
ntarasthavodhaṃ tadviṣayamapi pratipavantarapratipattiṃ punaruktayati, atiprasaṅgāt |
ata evāha | anumānapramāṇaka eveti | nātrā''gamā'vagamagandho'pyastī
tyevakārārthaḥ | pravṛttihetuḥ pramāṇāntaradṛṣṭa eva svātmani paratrā'numātavyo
na punaḥ śabdaikasamadhigamyo'pratītacara ityāha—api ca na śabdādadṛṣṭā
'tiriktaḥ pravṛttiheturanumīyate | tasya tasya pūrvasiddhyapekṣitatvātpravṛtteḥ |
tasya śabdanibandhanapravṛttiliṅgakasyā'numānasya pravṛtterityarthaḥ | mā bhūdanu
mānaṃ śabdāntarapravṛttyanyathānupapattyā tu pravṛttiheturadṛṣṭapūrvaḥ kalpayiṣyata iti
cet, tatrāha—dṛṣṭairapi hetubhistadupapatteḥ | yaccoktaṃ pratipattṛstha iti |
kimayaṃ pratyayo'bhimataḥ, arthāntaraṃ vā tat | ya āhuḥ pratyaya iti, tān
[p.325]
nīyam | arthāntaropagame'pi nityatve sarvaḥ sarvadā sarvatrā'dhikṛtaḥ syāt
apramāṇakaṃ ca tat, śabdātkasyacit kvacinniyogapratīteḥ | anityatve'pi
na śabdaḥ kāryya uktadoṣatvāt | nimittātkāmādvotpattau kṛtaprayogasyāpi
prasaṅgaḥ | yāvannimittamanuvṛttau kṛtaprayogasyāpi prāgupabhogāt pravṛttiḥ
syāt | tasmātpūrva eva vidhiḥ | tatra
yāgā'nurakto vyāpāro dṛṣṭaheturnna yujyate |
tatparatvena śabdena yadā sa pratipāditaḥ ||
pratyāha—pratipatrāśritaśca yaiḥ pratyayaviśeṣaiḥ āśritaḥ tairālambanaṃ vācyam |
nirālambanatvā'nupapatteḥ na ca tadanyadīhitopāyatāyā iti siddhaṃ naḥ
samīhitamiti |
ananyapramāṇakatvaṃ ca samarthanīyam, na ca śakyaṃ svasaṃvedanasiddhatvādanu
meyatvādvā | kalpāntaraṃ vikalpya dūṣayati—arthāntaropagame'pi nityatve
tasyāviśeṣeṇa sakalapuruṣādhāratayā samarthatayā tadviṣayasādhāraṇatayā sadā
tanatayā ca sarvaḥ sarvadā sarvatrādhikṛtaḥ syāt | samarthahetusadbhāve kāryakṣepā
'nupapatteḥ | apramāṇakaṃ ca tat | pramāṇā'bhimatācchabdādeva kasyacit kva
cinniyogapratīteḥ | anityatve'pi tannāntarīyakatvātkāryatvasya śabdakāryo
vā niyogo bhavet, kāmanimittakāryo vā | tatra na śabdakāryaḥ, uktadoṣa
tvāt pramāṇatvādityādinā | nimittātkāmādvotpattau nimittakārakā'vi
nāśeṣpi kāryā'vināśāt | kṛtaprayogasyāpi tadastīti prasaṅgaḥ |
atha na kāmo nimitakāraṇam, api tu kāryaikārthasamavāyikāraṇam,
tadvināśācca kārya vinaśyati | tantuvyatiṣaṅganāśādiva paṭaḥ, tatrāha—
yāvannimittamanuvṛttau kṛtaprayogasyāpi prāgupabhogāt pravṛttiḥ syāt | idama
trākūtam | ātmaviśeṣaguṇatvādayaṃ niyoga ātmanaḥ saṃyogā'samavāyikā
kāraṇako buddhyādivat | na tu kāmā'samavayikāraṇaḥ | nimittaṃ tvasya
kāmaḥ | tathā hitasādhanataiva vidhiḥ | tathāpyasti kiṃcinnamittakaraṇaṃ
yadvināśaḥ kāryavināśahetu, apekṣābuddhivināśa iva dvitvādivināśasya |
tatredamucyate—yāvannimittamanuvṛttau kṛtaprayogasyāpi prāgupabhogātpravṛttiḥ
syāt iti | prakṛtamupasaṃharati—tasmātpūrva eva kartṛsamīhitasādhanartava
vidhiḥ | tatra ca sthite tadanuguṇo darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ svargakāmo yajetetyevamā
dīnāṃ vākyārtho varṇanīyaḥ | paramate tu tadviparītaṃ syādityāha—
yāgā'nurakto vyāpāro dṛṣṭaheturna yujyate |
tatparatvena śabdena yadā sa pratipāditaḥ ||
[p.326]
yadi khalu yāgānurakte vyāpāre yāgaḥ sādhyātmanā bhāvā'nurañjakatvena
gṛhyate tadā sannikṛṣṭastatparaḥ śabdenokta iti iṣṭahetutātmā na tatra vidhyarthaḥ
samaveyāt | tasyā'niṣṭatvāt | tatrā'vivakṣite vidhau saphalasya niṣphalasya
vā loke kathaṃcitpravṛttiḥ syāt | taduktam—asādhakaṃ tu tādarthyād iti |
prītyarthatvātpuruṣaprayatnānām | prītihetutā tu vidhyarthā'samavāye sādhakaṃ
nā'dhigacchediti | athaivaṃ vidhisāmarthyādiṣṭaniṣṭhastathā sati dhātvarthaḥ
karaṇam, tannāntarīyakatāptitaḥ | yadi tu vidhyavivakṣāhetvabhāvānnārthavyāpāra-
yadi hi vyāpārayogaparatayā pratipādyeta śabdena kāryādiriti yathā
prathamaḥ pakṣaḥ, tadeṣṭasādhanatāvyāpārasya vidhipratyayagamyasya vyāhanyeta |
tadetatprapañcayati—yadi khalu yāgā'nurakte vyāpāre yāgaḥ sādhyātmanā bhāvā
nurañjakatvena yāgaṃ kuryādityanena rūpeṇa gṛhyeta tataḥ sa vyāpārastanniṣṭhaḥ
tatparavyākhyeyā'nubhāṣaṇaṃ śabdena liṅokta iti hetoriṣṭahetutātmā na tatra
vyāpāro vidhyarthaḥ samaveyāt | atha yāge'pi sādhyātmake ko vyāpāra
syeṣṭahetutāvirodhaḥ ? ata āha—tasya yāgāderaniṣṭatvāt duḥkhaṃ karme 
tyanubhavo lokasya | nanvasatyāmapyupadeśavivakṣāyāmājñādau yathā puruṣapravṛtti
rloke tathā vede'pi vinā'pyupadeśavivakṣāyā bhaviṣyatītyata āha—tatrā'viva
kṣite vidhau saphalasya niṣphalasya vā loke kathaṃcidanuvidheyapuruṣā''śaya
vaśātpravṛttiḥ syāt | apauruṣaye tu vede puruṣāś'śayā'bhāvāt | tasyā
pravarttakatve'pi vidhyavivakṣāyāṃ na pravṛttiḥ syāditi mantavyam | viśeṣa
vidhirviśeṣāntaraniṣedhahetutvāt | yathā vāmenā'kṣṇā paśyatīti dakṣiṇā
'kṣidarśanapratiṣedho gamyate | tatrāpi pāramarṣa sūtraṃ yojayati | taduktam asādhakaṃ
tu tādarthyāditi | tadvivṛṇoti—prītyarthatvātpuruṣaprayatmānāṃ prītihetutā tu
vidhyarthā'samapāye sāvayitāraṃ nā'dhigacchediti | sugamam | yajeteti
samīhitasādhanatāmātre vidhiravagamyate | tathā yāgasya sādhyatvam | tadyadi
vidhirvivakṣyeta sādhyatvaṃ yāgasya hīyeta | na ca sādhyatvaparityāgena na
vidhirvivakṣitavya iti vinigamanāyāṃ heturasti | dvayoḥ śrutatvenā'viśeṣā
dityata āha—athaivaṃ vidhisāmarthyādiṣṭā'niṣṭastathā sati dhātvarthaḥ karaṇaṃ
tatra nāntarāyakatāptitaḥ | vidhivivakṣāyāmapi yāgasya sādhyatvapratipatti
rupapadyate | tasya prāgasiddheḥ sādhitasyaiva sādhanatvena sādhyatvākṣepāttadbhāvasya
tannāntarīyakatvāt |
[p.327]
stadā yāgasyāniṣṭatvāt tadanyapare pradhānātmano'navakāśo yāgaḥ | prakā
rāntareṇāpi sāvakāśatayā vidheścānavakāśatayā sāmarthyānnāntarīyakatayā
pratipādyamāno viṣayatvaṃ karaṇātmanā 'nveti |
na hīṣṭaparo vyāpārastatparo yāgaparo vā iṣṭaparaḥ | evaṃ tāvadya
jeteti vidhivaśāt phalā'bhisaṃbandhaḥ | yataśca—
bhāvanākarmatā pūrvamicchārūpā'vagamyate |
svargaḥ siddho'pi tadyogādato'nveti phalātmanā ||
yo hi yadicchati sa tat karotīti bhāvanāyāḥ prārambha icchā |
kārikāṃ vyācaṣṭe—yadi tu vidhyavivakṣāhetvabhāvāt sādhakā'bhāvenā
'rthavyāpāraḥ iṣṭā'niṣṭaḥ tadā yāgasyā'niṣṭatvāt yāgādanyadiṣṭaṃ tatpare
vyāpāre pradhānātmanā yāgo 'vikāśo na bhavati | nanvanavakāratvādeva
samīhitaparatā mapodya yāgaḥ pradhānatayaiva saṃbhatsyate'nyathā sādhyatvā'nu
patterityata āha—prakārāntareṇāpi yāgasya sādhyatāyāḥ sāvakāśatayā
vidheścā'navakāśatayeṣṭaparataiva vyāpārasya yuktetyarthaḥ | nanūbhayathā 'pyanava
kāśe vinigamanāyāṃ ko hetuḥ ? ityata āha—sāmarthyāt | anakkāśa
vidhipratītisāmarthyāt nāntarīyakatā vṛttyā pratipādyamāno viṣayatvaṃ yāgaḥ
karaṇātmanā'nveti | yadyapi puruṣaprayatnaḥ samīhitoddeśena pravṛttaḥ tathāpi
yāgādikamagocarayanna samīhitāya kalpata iti nāntarīpakatayā samīhitārtha
pravṛttaprayatnavyāpyatālakṣaṇaṃ sādhyatvamatatpare vyāpāre yāgādeḥ siddhamityarthaḥ |
nanu samīhitapare'pi vyāpāre yāgaparatvaṃ kasmānna bhavati ? ityāha—
na hīṣṭaparo vyāpāraḥ tatparo yāgaparaḥ | yāgaparo vā iṣṭaparaḥ | sakṛcchu
tasyobhayaparatvā'darśanālloke tanmūlatvācchabdārthasambandhapratīteriti upa
saṃharati—evaṃ tāpadyajeteti vidhivaśātphalā'bhisambandhaḥ | na kevalaṃ
vidhivaśātkarmisambandhādapi svargāderbhāvyatetyāha—yataśca |
bhāvanākarmatā pūrvamicchārūpā'pagamyate |
svargaḥ siddho'pi tadyogādato 'nveti phalātmanā ||
bhāvanākarmatāyāḥ pūrvarūpatvamicchāyāṃ darśayati—yo yadicchati sa
tatkarotīti bhāvanāyāḥ prārambha icchā, tayā bhāvanāprayatna ārabhyate,
[p.328]
tathā ca tadbhāvanāsādhyamiti siddho'pi svargādistatkarmmatayā karmi
sambandhādivaudanādistatsādhyātmanaiva bhāvanāyāmanveti nānyathā | taduktam |
pratyarthaṃ cābhisambandhā diti | arthyamānaṃ prati puruṣavyāpāra ābhimukhyena
sambathyate | ato'rthanāyogātsvargādestakarmmatayā 'bhisambandhaḥ | anyathā
'nanvayāt | ataḥ kevalaṃ yajeteti phalāpekṣatvātsvargāderanyathāsamavāyāt |
tathā ca sambandhātkarma svargaṃ prati sādhanatvena gamyate | kathaṃ punaḥ
svargasyānyaviśeṣaṇasya bhāvanā'bhisambandhaḥ, tatsambandhe vā 'nyaviśeṣaṇatvaṃ
kathaṃ samāso vā ? na hi tadabhisambandharahitasyā'nyasambandhasavyapekṣasya
vā bhāvanāsambandhaḥ | guṇabhūtasya na hi samāso yujyate |
naiṣa doṣaḥ |
arthādviśeṣaṇaparaṃ sambandhaṃ yāti daṇḍapat |
tadyogāccā'nyasambandhe na samāsaḥ praduṣyati ||
icchāpūrvakatvātprayatnasya tatkarma icchākarma bhāvanā vadhyamiti siddho'pi
svargādistatkarmatayā icchākarmatayā karmisambandhādadvitīyāsambandhādiva odanā
distatsādhyātmanaiva bhāvanāyāmanveti nā'nyathā 'dhikṛtaviśeṣaṇatayā | atraiva
pāramarṣa sūtraṃ yojayati—taduktaṃ pratyartha cā'bhisaṃbandhāditi | tadvivṛṇoti—
arthyamānaṃ prati puruṣavyāpāra ābhimukhyena saṃbadhyate, ato'rthanāyogātsvargā
destatkarmatayaivā'bhisambandhaḥ | anyathāsiddhatve'nanvayāt | upasaṃharati—
ato na kevalaṃ yajeteti phalā'pekṣatvādvidhyupahitāyā bhāvanāyā api tu
svargādeḥ karmiyogāpāditasādhyabhāvasyā'nyathāsamavāyāt | upasaṃharati—
tathā ca sādhyatvena sambandhātkarmavyāpāraḥ | svargakāma iti hi viśeṣaṇa
śabdātsvargo'vagamyate, sa kartha bhāvanāyāṃ prādhānyena sambandhyate ? satsambandhe
vā bhāvanāyā anyaviśeṣaṇatvaṃ kathaṃ samāso vā ? | sāpekṣatvenā'sāmarthyāt |
asāmarthyamevāha—na hi tatsambandharahitasya puruṣasambandharahitasyā'nyasambandha
savyapekṣasya vā bhāvanāsambandhaḥ | pradhānasya tvanyasambandhasāpekṣasyāpi
samāso dṛṣṭa eva, yathā rājapuruṣaḥ śobhana ityata āha—guṇabhūtasya na hi
samāso yujyate | pariharati—naiṣa doṣaḥ |
arthādviśeṣaṇaparaḥ sambandhaṃ yāti daṇḍavat |
tadyogādvā'nyasambandhe na samāsaḥ praduṣyati ||
[p.329]
na khalu svargakāmā'nvayanirapekṣaḥ svargo bhāvanā'bhisambandhī, kintu
sa eva viśeṣaṇapradhānaḥ sambandhamupāśnute | yathā daṇḍī praiṣānanvāheti daṇḍa
pradhāno daṇḍī | tadabhisambandhī tu tatparasvātmanā avivakṣita eva | evaṃ
ca samāso'pyupannaḥ | svatantrasya hyanyasambandhe tadasaṃbhavāt sāpekṣatvāt
samāso na syāt | tadabhisambandhena tu taddvārā anyasambandhe naikārthyabhāvo-
yadi hi puruṣasambandhanirapekṣa eva svargo bhāvanayā sambadhyeta tataḥ śābdī
puruṣānvayapratītirvirudhyeta | yadi vā puruṣaparameva svargakāmapadaṃ syāt
tato bhāvanayā na svargaḥ sambadhyeta | na ca tadubhayamastītyāha—na khalu
svargakāmā'nvayanirapekṣaḥ svargo bhāvanāsaṃbandhī, yena śābdī puruṣaviśeṣaṇa
pratītirvyāhanyeta | kintu sa eva viśeṣaṇapradhānaḥ sambandhamupāśnute bhāvanā
yām | viśeṣyapradhāne hi svargakāmapade svargaḥ puruṣavaśena bhāvanāyāṃ
sambadhyate, viśeṣaṇapare tvasminnā'yaṃ doṣaḥ | kva viśeṣaṇaparatvaṃ dṛṣṭam ?
ityata āha—yathā daṇḍo praiṣānanvāheti daṇḍapradhāno daṇḍī | yathā
maitrāruṇaḥ praiṣānanvāheti praiṣā'nuvacane maitrāvaruṇasya prāpte daṇḍī praiṣānanvā
heti maitrāvaruṇalakṣaṇaviśeṣyavidhānā'nupapatterviśeṣaṇasya daṇḍasya cā'prāpte
rvidheyatvāt tatparo daṇḍiśabdaḥ, tatheha yajeteti bhāvanākṣepādeva bhāvayituḥ
prāpteravidheyatvāt bhāvyaviśeṣāpekṣatvācca bhāvanāyāḥ svargasya ca puruṣa
viśeṣaṇasyā'prāptarūpatayā kamikarmatayā ca bhāvyatvārhatvādanyataścā'prāpte
statparā svargakāmaśrutiriti ramaṇīyam |
nanu yadi viśeṣaṇaparā svargakāmaśrutiḥ tato viśeṣyasambandhaḥ svargasya
śabdato na pratheta cet nā'sya bhāvanāsambandhaḥ, tatparatvādityata āha—
tadabhisambandhī tu tatparo bhāvanāsambandhaparaḥ, svātmanā vivakṣita eva,
svaparatayā vivakṣita eva | sambandho hyasambandhavirodhī na punaranyaparatā
virodhītyarthaḥ | asamāsaprasaṅgaṃ pariharati—evaṃ ca samāso'pyupapannaḥ |
evaṃ ceti | tadeva vyatirekā'nvayābhyāṃ vivṛṇoti—svatantrasya hi puruṣa
sambandhanirapekṣasyā'nyasambandhe tadasaṃbhavāt puruṣasambandhā'sambhavāt samāso
na syāt | samāsasamaye vā'nyasambandharahito'pi bhāvanāsambandhasāpekṣaḥ
svargaḥ tathāpi sā'pekṣatvātsamāso na syāt | na tvetadubhayamastīti |
anvayamāha—tadabhisambandhena tu puruṣasambandhena taddvārā'nyasambandhe sati
naikārthībhāvoparodho na vigrahavākyārthā'bhidhānasāmarthyoparodha iti | tasmā
dyuktaḥ samāsaḥ | yadi tu viśeṣyā'bhisambandhadvāreṇāpi kriyāmanupraviśa
dviśeṣaṇaṃ sāpekṣamasamarthamiti na samasyeta tatsarvameva viśeṣaṇaṃ nā'sminsamāse
[p.330]
parodha iti yuktaḥ samāsaḥ | anyathā sarvameva viśeṣaṇasamāsaṃ syāt | tasya
kāryamasaṃspṛśato vaiyarthyāt | saṃsparśe vā sā'pekṣatvātsamāso bhavet |
yattvākhyātārthe sādhye sādhyāntarā'samavāya iti | bhāvanāyāṃ tāvadrū
pādeva tatsamavāyaḥ | tasya bhāvyapradhānarūpatvāt | dhātvarthe'pi yadi tātparyyeṇa
bhāvanāyāḥ sādhyatvaṃ tadvidhinā'pāstam | atha bhāvanāviṣayatvamātraṃ
tatkāraṇāntareṣvapi samānam | uktaṃ hi karaṇaṃ khalu sarvatra kartṛvyāpāra
gocaramiti | dravyaguṇaśabdārthasdarūpato 'pi niṣpādyasya sādhanatvāt |
siddhasyopādānā'bhāvāt | śrutyaiva bhāvanāmanurañjayato yāgādestadviśeṣa-
sambhavedityāha—anyathā sarvameva viśeṣaṇamasamāsaṃ syāt | nanu kimitya
samāsam ? bhaviṣyati hi kiṃcidviśeṣaṇaṃ yadviśeṣyamātraparam, tadyathā nīlo
tpalamānayetyata āha—tasya viśeṣaṇasya nīlādeḥ kāryamānayanādikamasaṃpṛśato
vaiyarthyāt | evaṃ hi yāvaduktaṃ bhavati utpalamānayeti tathā ca'viśeṣakara
tvāt kāryavaiyarthya viśeṣaṇarapasyeti utpalasya prasajyetetyarthaḥ | kārya
saṃsparśe vā viśeṣaṇasya sāpekṣatvātsamāso bhavanmate bhavediti |
yacca paricoditam, ākhyātārthe sādhye sādhyāntarasya svargāderasama
vāya iti | tatredaṃ vikalpanīyaṃ—kiṃ bhāvanāyāṃ sādhyāyāṃ sādhyāntarasyā
'samavāya ucyate, āhosvit dhātvarthe sādhye yāgādau ? tatra bhāvanāyāṃ
tāvadrūpādeva tatsamavāyaḥ sādhyasaṃbandhaḥ | kutaḥ ? tasyā bhāvyapradhānarūpa
tvāt | bhāvanā hi sādhyarūpāpi bhavitāraṃ bhāvyamantareṇā'nupapatteḥ
svabhāvādeva bhāvyā'pekṣā | na hyasti saṃbhavo yadutpādanā ca na cā'nya
dutpādyamastīti | tasmātsādhyā'pekṣāyāṃ na sādhyasambandho ghaṭate | atha
dhātvarthe sādhye na sādhyāntarasamavāyaḥ tasyāpi sādhyatvaṃ bhāvanāyāṃ tatpara
tvādvā phalārthapravṛttabhāvanāviṣayatvādvā | na tāvadbhāvanā tātparyarūpeṇa
vidhibodhavirodhādityāha—dhātvarthe yadi tātparyeṇa bhāvanāyā dhātvarthasya
sādhyatvaṃ tadvidhinā 'pāstam | athā'nyārthapravṛttabhāvanāviṣayatvamātraṃ
tatkāraṇāntareṣvapi paraśvādiṣu samānam | bhavanti hi te 'pi dvaidhībhāvo
ddeśapravṛttodyamananipātanalakṣaṇavyāpāraviṣayāḥ | te ca kāraṇam | evaṃ
yāgādayo 'nyārthapravṛttabhāvanāviṣayatayā sādhyā api kāraṇatayā sādhyāntaraiḥ
sambhantsyante vraścanādaya iva dvidhābhavadbhiḥ kāṣṭhādibhiḥ | etacca na sādhita
mihā'nyatra siddhatvādityāha—karaṇaṃ khalu sarvatra kartṛvyāpāragocara iti
bhāvanāviveke |
[p.331]
ṇasya pratīteḥ | dvividhatvaṃ tvapūrvabhāvā'bhāvakṛtam | puruṣaprayatnasya yāgā
viṣayatvaṃ svarge'pi aviruddham, vṛkṣa iva cchedye paraśuviṣayatvam | na ca
yāgāderavyāpāratvam | śabdādeva tatpratīte | jñānapradīpānāṃ cotpatterapi
yacca paricodayā~babhūvuḥ, ākhyātasya karaṇārthatve dravyaguṇaśabdābhyāma
viśeṣaḥ, siddhārthatvāditi, tatrāha—dravyaguṇaśabdārthaviśeṣastu dhātvarthasya
rūpato 'pi niṣpādyasya sādhanatvātprāgasiddheḥ siddhasyopādānā'bhāvāt
godohanādīnāṃ tvanyataḥ siddhārthānāṃ satāmupādānāditi | na kevalaṃ vastutaḥ
śabdato'pyasya sādhyatvenā'vagatiḥ prakṛtyarthatayā niyatapūrvapratītakatvena
svarūpaviniveśena sādhyarūpabhāvanānurañjakatayā tadviśeṣaṇatvāt, śābaleyādaya
iva gotvaviśeṣaṇatvena gāva ityāha—śrutyaiva bhāvanāṃ sādhyarūpāmanurañjayato
yāgādestadviśeṣaṇasya bhāvanāyā viśeṣaṇasya pratīteḥ sādhyatvā'vagamaḥ |
na tvevaṃ dravyādau bhinnapadopādāne tadviśeṣaṇatvaṃ tadviniveśena tadavacchedakatayā
na punarbhāvanātvasāmānyāśrayeṇeti mantavyam | evaṃ ca vyāpārāveśāt
sādhyarūpapratīteḥ sākṣātpacyarthe karaṇabhāvaḥ kāṣṭhāderupapannaḥ kāṣṭhaiḥ paca
tītyādāviti sūcitaṃ bhavati |
yadyapi coditam, sarvā'bhimatasādhanatvena karmaṇo guṇabhāvād guṇapradhāna
bhāvena dvaividhyaṃ hīyeteti, tatpariharati—dvividhatvaṃ tvapūrvabhāvā'bhāvakṛtam |
satyapyapekṣitaparatve yatrāpūrvabhāvastasya prādhānyamāgneyādeḥ samidādeśca |
yatra tadabhāvastasya guṇakarmatā'vaghātādeḥ prokṣaṇādeśca | niyamāpūrva tu
niyamādeva na karmaṇa ityuktaṃ vārttikakṛtā |
yaccoktaṃ vastuto na bhāvyatā svargādeḥ puruṣaprayatnasya yāgādidhātvartha
viṣayatvāditi, tatpariharati—puruṣaprayatnasya yāgādiviṣayatvaṃ svarge'pi
sādhye 'viruddham, vṛkṣe iva cchedye paraśuviṣayatvam | yadi hi sākṣātpuruṣa
vyāpāragocaratayā bhāvyaṃ nedānīṃ lokaprasiddhabhāvyatvānāṃ dvaidhībhāvādīnāmapi
bhāvyatvaṃ bhavet | na hi te'pi sākṣātpuruṣavyāpāraviṣayāḥ | api tūdyamya
mānanipātyamānāḥ paraśvādaya eva | na ca te bhāvyāḥ, api tu tatsādhanam |
paraśuviṣayasyāpi puruṣavyāparasya phalāṃśadvaidhībhāvoddeśena pravṛttastasya bhāvyatvaṃ
svargādīnāmapi samānam | tatrāpi svargādyuddeśena puruṣavyāpārasya yāgādi
viṣayasyāpi pravṛtteḥ |
yaccoktamavyāpārā yāgādayo na karaṇam, na ca bhūtireṣāṃ vyāpāra iti,
tatrāha—na ca yāgāderavyāpāratvam | kutaḥ ? śabdādeḥ sādhanatayā pratīteḥ |
anyathā 'nyathā'nupapattyā tannirvāhāyā'rthāpatyā 'pūrvābhidhānāvāntaravyāpāra
[p.332]
vyāpāratvā'bhyupagamāt | na ca svargī nityo'tyantamasattvāt yenā'śakyaḥ |
upāyājñānāditi cet ? ata eva sa jñāpyate | na cāvaśyakarttavyatā-
parikalpanāt | kathaṃ tadanāśrita uparate'pi karmaṇi tasya vyāpāra iti cet,
tarhi kimidānīmāgneyādyutpattyapūrvāṇyāgneyādibhiḥ karaṇairadhikārā'pūrve
janayitavye na teṣāmavāntaravyāpārāḥ ? na hi tānyāgneyādisamānakālāni,
uparateṣveva teṣu tebhyaḥ paramā'pūrvotpatteḥ, darśapūrṇamāsayośca sahabhuvoḥ
svarūpato darśapūrṇamāsābhyāmityavagatasādhanabhāvanirvāhāya tadabhidhānakalpa
nāyāṃ tādarthyāttadasamavāyitadāśritatayā tadavāntaravyāpāratayā cā'siddha
mavyāpāratvaṃ yāgādīnāmapi | prapañcitaṃ caitannyāyaparīkṣāyām |
na ca bhūrtirna vyāpāro jñānapradīpādiṣu darśanādityāha—jñānapradīpādīnāṃ
cotpatterapi vyāpāratvā'bhyupagamāt tena janmaiva viṣaye buddhervyāpāra iṣyate
ityādinā | nanvasiddhasya kathaṃ bhavanakartṛtvam ? nā'siddham | upādāna
rūpeṇa siddhatvāt | tantava eva paṭotpādanapravṛttāḥ pūrvā'parībhūtasaṃyogaviśeṣa
pracayaśālinastādarthyādupacārāt paṭa ityucyante | teṣāṃ ca taduddeśapravṛtānāṃ
paṭībhavanavyāpārasya bhavanamiti nā'siddhasya vyāpāraḥ | dvividhaṃ ca bhavanāṃ
śāya ? | kiṃ cidbhāvamātrā'dhīnajanma yathā vijñānasya jñeyā'bhivyāptiḥ |
kiṃ ciccotpannānāṃ satāṃ paścātsahakārigrāmasamavadhānotpādanam, yathā cakṣu
rādīnāṃ rūpādijñānam | na hyutpannamityeva cakṣū rūpamīkṣate kiṃ tvarthāllokā
disahakāripratyayasamavadhānāt | na hi nirvyāpārāṇāṃ sādhanatopalabdhā |
tatra yatkārya yeṣāṃ naiyatyādhīnaṃ tasmiñjanayitavye kaścidutpattimānpratyakṣaḥ
parokṣavyāpāro'bhyupetavya iti tadyogātteṣāṃ sādhanatā | yatpunaḥ svabhāva
mātrā''yattaprabhavaṃ kārya na tatra vyāpārāntaraṃ samasti | na ca nirvyā
pārasya sādhanateti janmaiva tatra vyāpāro'bhyupeyate | tatra cā'sya kartṛtva
mupapāditam | na cotpattyanantarameva vyāpārāḥ phalaṃ janayanti | utpanne
vyāpāre pūrvasaṃyoge vibhāgena vināśite satyuttarasaṃyogītpattiriti
sarvamavadātam |
yaccoktaṃ svarga kuryādityaśakyopadeśa iti | tadanupapannam | dvidhā
hi bhavati kriyā'narhatā 'tyantasattvād gaganavad, atyantā'satvādvā tatpuṣpavat |
na caitadubhayamapi svargādau samastītyāha—na ca svargo nityaḥ atyantamasattvā
dyenā'śakyaḥ syāt | nanu mā bhūdasya svarūpā'yogyatā upāyā'jñānāttva
karaṇīyo bhaviṣyatīti codayati—upāyā'jñānāditi cet | pariharati—
ata eva sa jñāpyate vidhinā ataśca jñātopāyaḥ śakyo bhaviṣyatīti |
[p.333]
prasaṅgaḥ | phalāṃśe'nuvādātkaraṇāṃśopasaṃhārācca vidheḥ | dadhnā juhotīti
yathā | phalaviṣayāyā eva ca pravṛtteryyāgaviṣayatvena śabdo heturiti
yāgo vidhīyate | na hyanyā puruṣasya karmmaṇi pravṛttiranyā karaṇe | karaṇa
eva pravṛttaḥ phalapravṛtto bhavati | karmmaṇyeva pravarttamānaḥ karaṇe pravarttate |
tadevamarthā'vagamānna vaiyarthyam | nāpyanyat prayojanam | svagekāmo yāgaṃ
kuryāditi | yāganiṣṭhe śabde tatsamavāyādapramāṇatvācchrutahānādaśruta
kalpanācca | na ca dṛṣṭameva prayojanamātmapratipattiḥ karmmabhiḥ kṛtakāma-
yaccoktamavaśyakartavyatāprasaṅgo vidhyadhīnatvātpuruṣasyeti, tatrāha—na
cā'vaśyakartavyatāprasaṅgaḥ | svargādeḥ phalāśe'nuvādātkaraṇāṃśopasaṃhārācca
vidheḥ | aprāptaprāpaṇasvaraso hi vidhiḥ satyapi bhāvanāviṣayatve tatsamba
ndhena tamaṃśamasyā vidhatte yo na prāptaḥ | prāptaśca phalāṃśaḥ | tasmātta
danuvādenā'prāpte sādhanāṃśe vartate | atraiva dṛṣṭāntamāha dadhnā juhotīti |
yathā homabhāvanāviṣayo'pi vidhistasyā homāṃśamagnihotraṃ juhotītyutpatti
vākyasamadhigatamanūdyopapādaparo bhavati tathā 'yamapotyarthaḥ |
yaccoktaṃ pravṛttiviṣayo vidhiḥ phalaviṣayā cetpravṛttiḥ phalaṃ vidhīyeta
na yāgo, yāgaviṣayā cenna phalamiti, atrāha na vayamācakṣmahe na phala
viṣayeti, kintu taduddeśyatvātkalaviṣayāyā eva pravṛtteḥ phalaparatayā
vyāpyatvena yāgaviṣayatvamaprāptatvācchābdo hetuḥ pramāṇamiti yāgo
vidhīyate | nanvevaṃ yāgapravṛttau vidhīyamānāyāṃ kutaḥ phalasaṃgatiḥ ?
ityatrāha—na hyanyā puruṣasya karmaṇi phale pravṛtiranyā karaṇe | karaṇa
pravṛttyā phalapravṛtto bhavati, tadevā'nena phalaṃ sādhitaṃ bhavati | karmaṇyeva phale
pravartamānastasyā'nyathāsiddhyasaṃbhavānnāntarīyakatayā karaṇe pravartate |
tasmātphalasambandhādādhivayā'bhāvādyadānarthakyamāpāditaṃ pūrvapakṣiṇā tatphala
sambandhopapādanenā'pāstagityupasaṃharati—tadevamarthāvagamānna vaiyarthyam | pūrva
pakṣaikadeśino matamanupapannamityāha—nāpyanyadātmajñānā'dhikārā'nupraveśaḥ
prayojanam | kutaḥ ? bhavanmate svargakāmo yāgaṃ kuryāditi yāgakartavyatā
niṣṭhe śabde tatraiva tātparyagamavāyādapramāṇatvādātmajñānā'dhikārā'nupraveśasya |
tatparatve vā śrutayāgakarttavyatāhānādaśrutātmajñānā'nupraveśakalpanācca | nanu
śabdasamadhigamyatve bhavedayaṃ doṣaḥ, dṛṣṭā tvātmapratipattiḥ, karmaṇāṃ prayojanam,
ityata āha—na ca dṛṣṭameva prayojanamātmapratipattiḥ karmabhiḥ kṛtakāma
nivarhaṇena dāntena kāmairanāgardhitamanasā ''tmatattvasya supratayayatvāt | kṛta
vyākhyānametatpūrvapakṣe | tatra kathaṃ karmavidhayaḥ kāmaṃ nibarhayanti, kiṃ tatkāmo
[p.334]
nibarhaṇena dāntena kāmairanāgarddhitamanasā''tmatattvasya supratyayatvāt | yadi
khalu kāmanibarhaṇāni karmmāṇi taiśca kṛtī na prayojanāntaramapekṣate kāmī |
tathā ca tatpara eva karmmavidhiriti nānyaparatve pramāṇam | na cā'śāstratvam |
śreyo'bhidhāyakatvāt | śreyasaḥ prītirūpatvāt, prīteśca kāmeṣu pratyātma
saṃvedyatvāt | na ca bhogo nibarhaṇam | na hi kāmānāṃ tṛptirasti | atha
pravṛttyantaranivṛttyā, tatrāpi yadi kāmī yāgena kuryyānnā'nyenopāyena kāmaṃ
ko viśeṣaḥ kāmapakṣe cetasaḥ kāmanivṛttāyā pravṛttau kathaṃ ca pakṣe'pyaprāpta
vidhāvanyanivṛttiḥ ? | atha kāmī yāgaṃ kuryyānna kāmam ? durllabhamarthadvayama-
pahāreṇa tadupabhojakatayā, āhosvitpravṛttyantaropadeśena svābhāvikībhyaḥ
pravṛttibhyo nivartanena ? na tāvadādyaḥ kalpa ityāha—yadi khalu kāma
nivarhaṇāni tadupabhojakatayā taireva karmabhiḥ kṛtakāmaiḥ kṛtī puruṣa iti na
prayojanāntaramapekṣate | anākāṅkṣitaṃ ca na vidhinā svīkriyata iti |
tathā ca tatpara eva svavākyopātakarmapara eva karmavidhiriti anyaparatve
pramāṇaṃ kāmanā'napekṣitvāt | asya ca tadvaiparītyāt |
taduktaṃ kāmanāparatve śāstravyāpatiriti, tatrāha—na cā'śāstratvam |
kutaḥ ? śreyo'bhidhāyakaḥ padasamūhaḥ śāstramiti vṛddhāḥ | kāmavidhayaśca
tathetyāha—śreyo'bhidhāyaketvāt nanvāpātata eva kamanīyānāmanarthapariṇāmānāṃ
kena śreyaḥ svabhāvatetyata āha—śreyasaḥ prītirūpatvāt | śreyo hi puruṣapratīti
riti laukikī pratītistadupadeśaḥ śāstramiti laukikameva | na caite paryanuyoginaḥ,
yena na kāmalobhamādriyeran | kāmāstu paśusvargādayo na prītyātmāna ityāha—
prīteśca kāmeṣu pratyātmasaṃvedyatvāt | na ca bhogo nivarhaṇaṃ saṃbhavati |
na hi kāmānāṃ svargādīnāṃ tṛptirasti puruṣasya | kāmānāmiti suhitārtha
yoge ṣaṣṭhī | tadūktaṃ na jātu kāmaḥ kāmānāmupabhogena śāmyatīti |
bhogā'bhyāsamanuvivardhante rāgāḥ kauśalāni cendriyāṇāmiti ca | dvitīyaṃ
kalpamanubhāṣate—atha pravṛttyantaranivṛttyā | dūṣayati—tatrāpi yadi kāmī
yāgena kuryānnā'nyenopāyena kāmaṃ ko viśeṣaḥ kāmākṣepe cetasaḥ kāmanivṛttāyāṃ
pravṛttau ? kathaṃ ca pakṣeṇa prāptavidhāvanyanivṛttiḥ ? na tāvatkāmino vidheḥ
pūrvaṃ kāmopāyānyāgādibhedānaṅgabhedapracayasādhanoparāgānadhigantumīśate
laukikāniva tuṣavimokādihetūnavaghātādīn, yena teṣāṃ laukikaiḥ kāmopāyai
[p.335]
viśeṣataḥ śrautalākṣaṇikādikam | yāgāvidhiparatve na kāmaniṣedhaḥ |
kāmaniṣedhaparatve na yāgavidhiḥ | arthātkāmanivṛttirubhayasyāpi asama
vāyāt | sarvadṛṣṭārthapravṛttivirodhe ca karmmavidhikṛte 'pyapārthakāḥ syuḥ |
dṛṣṭopāyasādhyatvāttadarthasya | svābhāvikatvācca phalopabhogasya svabhāvavāda
evā''mnāyārthaśchadmanā ''śritaḥ syāt | tasmānmahaddaṇḍapadamidaṃ dṛṣṭenaiva
karmmavidhaya ātmajñānādhikāramārohantīti | niyojyā'nuśāsanaṃ śāstra
syeti jñānaniyogo'pi niyojyā'nuśāsanamātramiti na tata ātmapratipattiḥ |
rvikalpe satyavaghātādivatpakṣe prāptiḥ syāt | abhyupetya pakṣe prāptimeta
duktamiti mantavyam | atha tu na kāmasādhanatayā yāgo vidhīyate'pi tu
kāmebhyo nivartayituṃ kāminaṃ yāgaṃ kurvādityetāvanmātramityāha—atha kāmī
yāgaṃ kuryānna kāmam | utaram—durlabhamarthadvayaṃ viśeṣataḥ śrautalākṣaṇikā
dikam | yāgaṃ kuryāditi śraute saṃbhavati, na kāmanivṛtirlākṣaṇikī yuktā
'śrayitumityarthaḥ | atha śakyatāgeva darśayati—yāgavidhiparatve na kāmani
ṣedhaḥ, kāmaniṣedhaparatve na yāgavidhiḥ | pīno divā na bhuṅkte iti
bhojananiṣedhapare vākye 'rthādasya bhojanaṃ doṣā vihitaṃ bhavati tathehāpyarthā
tkāmanivṛttiḥ | kutaḥ ? iha dvayasyāpyasamavāyāt | pīnasya divā
bhojananiṣedho rātrāvabhojane divā bhojananiṣedho rātrāvabhojanena saha na
sambhavatītyarthāddoṣā bhuṅkte ityavagamyate | na punaryāgādayaḥ kāmanivṛttāyāṃ
pravṛttau na saṃbhavantītyarthaḥ | bhavatu vobhayaparatvaṃ tathāpi yāgādividhibhiḥ
sarvā eva dṛṣṭārthāḥ pravṛttaya uparuddhā iti tatsādhyānāṃ brīhyādīnāmasaṃbhavā
dyāgādayo 'pi na nivarteransādhanābhāvādityāha—sarvadṛṣṭārthapravṛttinirodhe ca
karmavidhikṛte 'pyapārthakāḥ syurdṛṣṭopāyasādhyatvāttadarthasya yāgādeḥ | api
ca yāgādīnāmaphalasādhanatve sevādeśca vyabhicārādahetuḥ sukhaduḥkhādireva
svābhāvikalaukāyatikā'bhyupagatāmnāyārtha iti vacanavyaktyantareṇā'bhyupagataṃ
bhavatītyāha—svābhāvikatvācca phalabhogasya svabhāvavāda evā'rthādāmnā
yārthacchadmanā''śritaḥ syāt | upasaṃharati—tasmānmahaddaṇḍapadamidaṃ dṛṣṭenaiva
karmavidhaya ātmajñānā'dhikāramadhirohantīti | api cā'dhikārā'nabhyupagame
niyojyā'nuśāsanaṃ śāstratvaṃ śāstrasya yāgādividheriti tenaiva nyāyena jñāna
niyogo'pi niyojyā'nuśāsanamātmajñānaṃ kartavyamityetāvanmātravidhānapara
miti | tasmātphalaṃ nā'''tmapratipattirabhivyāptiḥ syāt |
[p.336]
jñānasvabhāvāditi cet ? | vidhivaiyarthyāt | avahantyādivaditi cet ?
na niyamārthatvāt | na jñānaviśeṣaniyamaḥ | pakṣe'pyaprāpteḥ | samāropeṇa
ca sambhavāt na tattvaparicchedaḥ | tasmānnā'sādhane dhātvarthe 'vikārasiddhiḥ |
sādhanatvaṃ cāsya vidhirityaktam ||
iti śrīmahāmahopādhyāyamaṇḍanamiśraviracito
vidhivivekaḥ samāptaḥ ||
śaṅkate—jñānasvabhāvāditi cet | nirākaroti—vidhivaiyarthyāt |
punaḥ śaṅkate—avahantyādivaditi cet | nirācaṣṭe—na, niyamārthatvā
davahantyādividheḥ | yadyucyeta ihāpyātmā'vagatāvātmajñānaṃ niyamyata iti,
tatrāha—na jñānaviśeṣaniyamaḥ | kutaḥ ? pakṣe'pyaprāpterjñānānantaraṃ na khalvātma
jñānādātmapratītisaṃbhavaḥ | ātmajñāne ca vidheye viparītasyāpyātmajñāna
tvāttasyāpi vidheyatvasaṃbhavānna tattvaparicchedaḥ syādityāha—samāropeṇa
saṃbhavānna tattvaparicchedaḥ | upasaṃharati—tasmānnā'sādhane dhātvarthe
'dhikārasiddhiḥ | bhavatu phalasādhanaṃ dhātvarthaḥ, sidhyatu cā'dhikāraḥ,
tathāpi kimāyātaṃ vidhāvityata āha—sādhanatvaṃ cā'sya vidhirityuktam ||
ityācāryavācaspatimiśraviracitā vidhivivekaṭīkā
nyāyakaṇikā samāptā ||
